Explanatory Remarks:

Text:

nayamayūkhamālikā
śrīḥ
nayamayūkhamālikā
||prathamādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ||
heṣāravairyaśśrutibrahmanirbharaiḥ strīyāṃ vyanakti svaraso ditāṃ purā|
niśvāsapaṅktiṃ nikhilāmapi trayīmaśvānanaḥ pātusa vaściraṃ hariḥ||
śreyasānnidhimiva śriyaḥpatiṃ prepsatāṃ priyahitādhvadarśinī|
lakṣmaṇāryahṛdayānusāriṇī likhyate nayamayūkhamālikā||
"athātodharmajijñāse"tyārabhya "anāvṛttiśśabdādanāvṛttiśśabdā" ditiparyaṃtaṃ viṃśatilakṣaṇamekaṃ śāstraṃ, tatra karmavicārārthā dvādaśalakṣaṇī devatāvicārārthā caturlakṣaṇī ca vṛttā|brahmavicārārthā caturlakṣaṇyāsbhyate|tatrādau brahmaviṣayavyutpattyabhāvapratipattidausthyamānāntarasiddhatvaphalarāhityarūpatadanārambhahetucatuṣṭayanirīsena tadārambhasamarthanārthatayā śāsropoddhātarūpāṇi samanvayādhikaraṇaparyantāni catvāryadhikaraṇāni|teṣu vyutpattyabhāvaśaṃkānirīkaraṇārthamidamādyamadhikaraṇam||
athāto brahmajijñāsā||1||
brahmavicārātmkamidaṃ śāsramārambhaṇīyaṃ naveti saṃśayaḥ|tadartha brahmaṇi vedāntāḥ pramāṇaṃ naveti saṃśayaḥ| tadarthañca siddharūpe brahmaṇi vyutpattissaṃbhavati naveti saṃśayaḥ|siddhe śabdavyutpattyasambhave brahmaṇi vedāntā napramāṇamiti, vedāntarūpapramāṇamukhena kartavyatayā'bhimato brahmavicāro nakartavya iti, tadarthamidaṃ śāsraṃ nārambhaṇīyamiti, pūrvapakṣe phaphalibhāvaḥ|śabdānāṃ siddhe vyutpattisambhave vedāntānāṃ brahmaṇi prāmāṇyamupapadyata iti, tanmakhena brahmavicārātmakamidaṃ śāsramārambhaṇīyamiti, siddhante phalaphalibhāvaḥ|tatrottamamadhyamavṛddhavyavahārābhyāṃ bālasya kārya eva vyutpattigrahāt upajīvyakāryavyutpattigrahānusāreṇa tadupajīviṣu "putrastejāta" ityādipāścātyavyavahāreṣu kāryapadādhyāhārāvaśyaṃbhāvāt sarve śabdāḥ kārya eva vyutpattimanta iti pūrvapakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - ādyavyutpattigrahasya kāryaviṣayatvaniyame'pi pāścātyeṣu siddhārthavyavahāreṣu na kāryapādādhyāhāraḥ kāryaḥ|tathāhi - kimādyavyutpattigrahakāle gāmānayetyādivākyagatānāṃ sarveṣāṃ padānāṃ kāryavācitvamavagatamiti tadanusāreṇa dvitīyādiprayogeṣu śrutairapi sarvaiḥ padaiḥ kāryasāmānye bodhite tadviśeṣajijñāsayā kāryaviśeṣavācikriyāpādādhyāhāra iṣyate, uta ādyavyavahārasya kriyāpadaghaṭitatvadarśanamātreṇa|nādyaḥ|gopadasamabhivyāhārāt karmakārakasyeva kriyāpadasamabhivyāhārāt kāryasya pratītyupapattyā karmakāraka iva kārye'pi teṣāṃ śakterakalpanīyatvāt|nāpidvitīyaḥ|"daṇḍenakāmānaye" tyādyavyutpattigrahe karaṇaśravaṇe'pi "agre ghaṭaṃ paśye" tyādau karaṇānadhyāhāravat kāryānadhyāhārasyāpyupapatteḥ|anyathā ādyavyutpattigrāhakavākyasya ānayanakāryaghaṭitatatve dvitīyādiprayogeṣvapi tasyaivādhyāhārāpatteḥ| agre kāryāntaraghaṭitavākyaśravaṇāt ānayananiyamasthyajyate cet, kāryavācipadarahitasiddhārthavarṇanaparakāryādyupalambhabāhulyāt kāryaniyamo'pi tyaktavyaḥ|apica siddhārthaviṣayo'pyādyapalayutpattigraho ddaśyate|yathā mātapitradayo bālān śabdārthaṃ grāhayantaḥ śaśipaśuprabṛtīnaṃgulyānirdiśya tattadvācakaśabdān prayuñjate, bālāḥ tathābhūtāṃgulinirdeśasahitaiḥ tatprayogaiḥ śabdārthasambandhaṃ niścinvantītyanubhavasiddhamevaitat|nanu agulinirdeśasāhitaśabdaprayogatarśanamātreṇa kathaṃ bālaḥ asya śabdasya ayamarthaṃ iti niścinuyāt|nahyarthaviśeṣābhimukhāṃguliśasahitaśabdaviśeṣaprayogastayorvācyavācakabhāvapratyāyanārtha iti bālasya vyutpattigraho'sti, navā tathā niyamo'sti, bahujanasamāje caitramapaśyantaṃ tamanvicchintaṃ prati tadabhimakhāṃgulinirdeśasahitacaitraśabdaprayogasya vācyavācakabhāvapratyāyanārthatvābhāvāt|yattu tattadarthānaṃgulyā nirdiśya tattadvācakaśabdeṣu bahuśaḥ prayukteṣu bālānāṃ tattadārthābhimukhāṃgulinirdeśaśabdāviśeṣaprayogasāhacaryadarśanajanitavāsanābāhulyāt tattacchabdaśravaṇānantaraṃtattadarthaviśayasvabuddhyudayena tatratatrārtthena tattacchabdasya vācakatvarūpasaṃbandhaniścayo bhavet,
saṃbandhaṃ vinā śabdādarthabodhasambhavāt saṃbandhāntarābhāvācceti| naitadyuktaṃ - yadi tattadarthaviśayāṃgulinirdeśaśabdaprayogasāhacaryarūpasaṃbandhāntarādhīno bālasya śabdādarthapratyayaḥ, kimarthaṃ
vācakatvarūpasaṃbandhāntarasyāpi kalpanā, sāhacaryasaṃbandhenaiva arthapratyayasiddheḥ|nahi saṃbandhāntareṇa siddhe arthapratyaye tadupapādakatvenānayopi saṃbandhaḥ kalpyaḥ, kalpyamāno'pi na prāṅniṣpannārthapratyayasyopapādakaḥ, jñātohi saṃbandho'rthapratyayaheturbhavati naca paścātsaṃbandhakalpanayā arthapratyayātpūrvaṃ saṃbandhajñānamupapāditaṃ bhavatiti cet, ucyate - ddaśyate tāvanmātṛkṛtāṃgulinirdeśādisahitaśabdaviśeṣaprayogabhūyodarśanavanto bālāḥ tayorvācyavācakabhāvaṃ pratipādya śabdaśravaṇe tadarthaṃ smṛtvā tadanusāreṇe ceṣṭante, arthadarśane tadvācakaśabdaṃ smṛtvā prayuñjate ceti, tena taddarśināmitthamabadhāraṇaṃ jāyate, śabdārthasaṃbaṃdhānabhijñān prati aṃgulinirdeśaceṣṭānaviśeṣasahitaśabdaprayogo vācyavācakabhāvasaṃbandhapratyāyanārthaḥ, ata eva tena bālāḥ pratilabdhavācyavācakabhāvajñānāstadanukūlaṃ vyavahantīti, evamavadhāraṇavanta eva janmāntare śaiśavaṃ pratipadya mātrādibhirvyutpādyanta iti teṣāṃ pūrvajanmasaṃskārānuvṛttyā mātrādikartṛkārthaviśeṣābhimukhaśabdaviśeṣaprayogasya śabdārthasaṃbandhapratyāyanārthatvasmaraṇaṃ bhavatīti tato vācyavācakabhāvarūpasaṃbandhaniśacayaḥ| yadvā pūrvapūrvajanmasu mātrādikṛtāṃgulinirdeśasahitaśabdaprayogādhanivyutpattigrahajanitasaṃskārabalādeva bālānāmuttarottarajanmasu tena vyutpattigrahaḥ, avaśyañca "putrastejāta" ityādivyavahāreṣu vyutpattigrahe'pi janmāntarasaṃskāropajīvanamapekṣitam|nakhalu tatra ādyavyutpattigrahaṃ vadatāṃ śiśacananaṃ|"caitra putraste jāta" iti vākyaśravaṇānantaraṃ caitrasya snānadānādikañca dṛṣṭavato'pi bālasya jātaśśiśuścaitrasya putra iti vā, svataeva jñānamasti|ataḥ putrajanamaśravaṇe hṛṣṭāḥ snānadānādikaṃ kurvantītyetadviṣayajanmāntarasaṃskārodbodhena smṛtvaivasa, jātaśśiśuścaitrasya putraḥ "caitra putraste jāta" iti śabdasyāpi caitraputrajananamarthaḥ, ataeva madadṛṣṭaputrajanmaśravaṇānantarakartavyaṃ snānadānādikamayaṃ karotīti niścināṃtītyevopapādanīyaṃ|putrapādāṃkitapaṭapradarśanapurvake'pi "putraste jāta" iti vākye śrute lāṭadeśe putrapādāṃkitapaṭapradarśanapūrvakaṃ putrajananavārtāṃ vadantītayetadviṣayajanmāntarasaṃskārodbodhāpekṣā'styeva|tasmātsiddhi padānāṃ vyutpattisattvāt vedāntānāṃ brahmaṇi prāmāṇyamupapadyataeveti siddhaṃ|sutrasyatvayamarthaḥ|atha - vedapūrvabhāgārthakarmavicārānantaraṃ|ataḥ - karmavicārasya pūrvavicārasya pūrvavṛttatvādeva hetoḥ|vedottarabhāgārthabrahmavicāraḥ kartavya iti|yadyapi jijñāsāpadaṃ jñānecchāpratipādakaṃ, tathā'pi adhyāhṛtasya kartavyatvasya svataḥ prāptāyamicchāyāmananvayāt tadviśeṣaṇe vicārarūpe jñāne'nvayaḥ, yathā "dadhnā juhoti" tyatra utpattividhiprāptaṃ homaṃ vihāya tadviśeṣaṇe dadhni vidheranvayaḥ|yadyapi vicāre'pi rāgataḥ prāpnuyāt, tathā'pi siddhavyutpattyabhāvaśakayā pratibandhaparihāreṇa taduttaṃbhanārthaṃ tatra kartavyatānvayo yukta eva|nanu vṛttasya karmavicārasya kathaṃ vartiṣyamāṇe brahmavicāre hetutvaṃ|karmaṇāmāvṛttividhānādyavagamya alpāsthirapalatvāvagamanena brahmaprāptyarthopāsanāpekṣitakarmasvarūpapādhigamanena ca hetutvamiticet|tathā'pi bādarāyaṇakartavyabrahmavicāraśāsrapratipādyavirūddhārthābhidhānena jaimininā pravartitasya karmavicārasya hetutvaṃ nayujyate, jaimineḥ brahmasanyāsādyapalāpena tadviruddhārthabhidhāyityavañca bhagavatā bādarāyaṇenaiva "śeṣatvātpuruṣārthavādoyathā'nyoṣviti jaiminiḥ, parāmarśe jaiminiracodanāccāpavadatī" tyādibhissūtrairdarśitamiti cet|maivaṃ|jaiminiranvāruhyavādasteṣusūtreṣu anūditaḥ, ata eva tasya brahmasanyāsābhyupagamaḥ "sākṣādapyavirodhaṃ jaiminiḥ" "tadbhūtasyatunātadbhāvojaiminerapī" ityādisūtrairdarśitaḥ athāpi syāt brahmasanyāsāpalāpābhyupagamādinā jaimineḥ "vipratiṣedhāccāsamañjasa"miti sāṃkhyādipakṣoktadoṣabhāktavameva sūcitam, evamapi jaiminikṛtakarmavicāropajīvane doṣo nāpatati, nahi tatkṛte karmavicāraśāsre brahmasanyāsādinirākaraṇaṃ sūtritamasti, tatobahirvirūddhārthavāditvamasti cedasau nadoṣaḥ| vedapramāṇyapratikṣeptṛbhirapi kṛtasya kāśikāvṛttyādeḥ "yuktiyuktaṃ vaco grāhya natupūruṣagauravami" dityādisūtreṇa vedasya kriyārthatvenaiva prāmāṇyaṃ vaktavyamiti aviśeṣeṇa akāryārthanāmaprāmāṇyamāśaṃkya teśaṣāṃ vidhyekavākyatayā tadarthatvopapādanena prāmāṇyasamarthavanaṃ jaimininā kṛtaṃ, tadapi bādarāyaṇena samanvayasūtre brahmaviṣayāṇā vākyānāmakriyārthānāṃ svātatryeṇa prāmāṇyasamarthanena pratyakhyātam|evaṃ viruddhayoḥ kathamaikaśāsryamiti cet|ucyate - jaimininā svakīye devatādhikaraṇe devatādhikaraṇe devatāsvarūpanirākaraṇaṃ kaṇṭhato na kṛtaṃ|naca ābhiprāyikatatkalpanaṃ yuktam. tīryagadhikaraṇe tena devatābhyupagamarayasphujīkṛtatvena tadvirodhāpatteḥ|tatrāhi "nadevatādevatāṃtarābhāvāditi sūtreṇa devatāḥ karmasu nādhikriyante, indrādidevatānāṃ havistyāgodṛśyedrādidevatāṃtarābhāvāt, svātamane saṃkalpyamānasya dravyasya svatvatyāgāsaṃbhavena yāgādyanivṛtterityaktam|nanu ca tathāpi jaiminibādarāyaṇayoḥ devatānāṃ karmasu guṇatvaprādhānyābhyupagamābhyāmaparihāryo virodhaḥ|tathāhi - jaimininā vipratimā" vityaṣṭāmādhyāyadhikaraṇe "vipratipattau haviṣā
niyamyetakarmaṇastadupākhyatvā"diti sūtreṇa aindrapuroḍāśaṃṣu puroḍāśarūpahavissāmānyādāgneyayāgavidhyantaḥ, natvindrarūpadevatāsāmānyāt sānnāyyayāgavidhyantaḥ|havistyāgarūpasya yāgakarmaṇo haviṣyupalabhyāmānatvāt ddaśyamānatvātsa haviṣā hi tyajyamānena yāgaḥ kriyamāṇo ddaśyate natvapratyakṣayā uddeśyamātrarūpayeti pratipādya yāgasya devatārāghanarūpatvāt devataiva prasannā phaladātrī prathānabhūteti tatsāmānya havassāmānyadbalīya iti śaṅkānirākaraṇārthaṃ sūtritam, "guṇatvena devatāśruti"riti|tayā nāvamikedevatā'dhikaraṇe "devatāvā prayojayedatithivadbhojanasya tadarthatvā"diti sūtreṇa yathā'tithipūjanātmakaṃ yajanamapi devatāprītyarthaṃ saddevatāpradhānamiti pūrvapakṣamuktavā "apivā śabdapūrvatvādyajñakarmapradhānaṃ syāt guṇatve devatāśruti"riti sūtreṇa na devatāpradhānaṃ yajanaṃ, tadeva apūrvadvārā phalajanakaṃ, natu haviṣā prītā devatā, tasyāstu dravyoddeśyatayā yāgopasarjanatvenaiva śrutiriti siddhāntamabhidhāya "atithau tatpradhānatvamabhāvaḥ karmaṇi syāt tasya prītividhānatvā"diti sūtreṇa atithivaiṣamyandarśitam, ātithyaṃ - atithiprītividhānaṃ, yathā'tithiḥ prīyate tathā kartavyatayā vihitamiti tadatitathipradhānaṃ yujyate, karmaṇi tu nāsti prītividhānaṃ, na devatāpradhānaṃ yajanamiti|bādarāyaṇena tu "phalamata upapatte"riti sutreṇa pasmeśvarārādhanarūpatayā sarvaṃ karma parameśvarapradhānaṃ tattatkarmārādhitātparameśvarādeva phalaṃ nāpūrvavyāpārātkarmaṇa iti svamatamuktavā "dharma jaiminirataeve"ti sūtreṇa karmaiva apūrvadvārā phaladāyakamiti jaiminimataṃ pūrvapakṣaṃ nirdiśya "purvantu bādarāyaṇo hetuvyapadeśā"diti sūtreṇa parameśvara eva sarvakarmaphaladātā, "sa evainaṃ bhūtiṃ gamayatī"tyādikarmavidhivākyaśeṣeṣu vāyvādirūpasya parameśvarasyaiva phalahetutvavyapadeśāt, kṣaṇavināśinaḥ karmaṇaḥ phalajanane dvārākāṃkṣāyāṃ vākyaśeṣārpitāṃ devatāprītimapabāya dvārāṃtarakalpanāyogāditi jaiminimatapratikṣepakayuktipradarśanena svamataṃ nirddhāritam|evaṃ parasparaviruddhārthayoḥ kathamekaśāstryamupapadyate|ucyate - nāyaṃ virodhaḥ ekaśāstryavirodhī, anuṣṭhānavirodhābhāvāt, tadaviśeṣe ca vākyārthavarṇanadvaidhībhāvamātrasya pūrvataṃtre'pyadhyāyabhedanadarśanāt|tatrāhidarśapūrṇamāsayoḥ "upastṛṇātyabhidhārayati dvirhaviṣovadyati caturavattaṃ juhoti"ti śrutivākyacatuṣṭayamadhye caturavattavākye avattahomavidhiḥ, tato'rthaprāptamavadānamanūdya vdyavadānavākye haviṣo dviravadātavyamiti vidhiḥ|caturavattavākya tu catuśśabda upastaraṇābhidhāraṇadyavadānavākyagatapracayaśiṣṭasaṃkhyānuvāda iti "saṃyukte tu prakramāttadaṅgaṃ syā"diti pāñcamikādhikaraṇe nirūpitam|vdyavadānavākye dvitvaviśiṣṭahaviravadānavidhiḥ, upastaraṇābhidhāraṇavdyavadānavidhiprāptaṃ caturavattamanudya homamātravidhiriti prakārāntaraṃ "utpattitādarthyāt caturavattaṃ pradhānasye"ti dāśamikādhikaraṇe nirūpitam|tatrādhikaraṇayoḥ parasparavirodhamāśaṃkya ubhayathā'pyanuṣṭhāne viśeṣābhāvannāyāṃ virodho doṣa iti prasādhitaṃ tantraratne|na ca vācyaṃ "yāṃ devatāṃ vaṣaṭkariṣyan syāttāṃ manasā dhyāyediti" vihitaṃ devatādhyānaṃ yāgadeśaṃ bādarāyaṇamate kartavyam, jaimininā tu udāhṛtaṃ devatādhyānavidhānaṃ tattaddevatāvāciśabdadhyānaviṣayamiti, yato vṛttikāreṇa "saṃhitametajjaiminīyena ṣoḍaśalakṣaṇeneti" karmavicārārthayā dvādaśalakṣaṇyā devatāvicārārthayā caturlakṣaṇyā caikatantryaṃ pratipāditamityudāhṛtaṃ bhāṣye, tatra karmavicārabhāge dvādaśānāṃ lakṣaṇānāṃ dharmapramāṇatadbhedataccheṣaśeṣibhāvādyarthabhedassulabhasiddhiḥ|devatāvicārabhāge caturṇāṃ lakṣaṇānāmarthabhedamitthamāhuḥ, "svarūpamādau tadbhedastadupāsanapūrvakaṃ|phalañca devatākāṇḍe devatānāṃtu kathyata" iti| karmadevatopāsanayośca vidyasahakāritvena niśreyasārthatayā brahmavicāreṇa sahaikalatvāt, vyākhyeyavedarūpaviṣayaikyācca, sīmāṃsāpūrvottabhāgayoraikaśāstryaṃ siddhaṃ|ataeva "purāṇanyāyamīmāṃsādharmaśāsrāṅgamiśritāḥ|vedāḥ sthānāni vidyānāṃ dharmasyaca caturdaśa||aṅgāni vedāścatvāro mīmāṃsā nyāyavistaraḥ|purāṇaṃ dharmaśāsrañca vidyāhyetāścaturdaśetyādismṛtiṣu vidyāsthānaparigaṇane mīmāṃsaikavidyāsthānatvena parigaṇitā pūrvottaramīmāṃsā, bhedeṣu saṃkhyā'tirekassyādityalaṃ prapañcena|
saṅgrahakārikāḥ||
siddhaṃ brahma na vedāntāḥ pratipādayituṃ kṣamāḥ|
kārya eva hi śabdānāṃ loke vyutpattirādimā||
tasmādvedākṣaraprāyān vedānatānavalaṃbya vā|
brahmamīmāṃsanaṃ vyarthamiti prāpte'bhidhīyate||
aṃgulyā nirdiśanto'rthān vṛddhāśśabdān prayuṃjate|
vyutpadyante tatobālāste śabdāstatparā iti||
itthaṃ siddhe'pi bālānāṃ lokānubhavasammate|
vyutpattigrahaṇe kasmāt kārye śaktirniyamyate||
uktarūpāṃguleśceṣṭā śakiṃta grāhayituṃ kṛtā|
iti bodhe tu bālānāṃ gatiḥ prāgjanmavāsanā||
tiryañco'pi hi loke puṃdhvaniceṣṭāviśeṣāṇāṃ|
tattatakāryārthatvaṃ vijānate pūrvajanmavāsanayā||
athavā'ṅgulinirdeśatattacchabdaprayogayoḥ|
sāhacaryāttu tadvīkṣājātasaṃskāradārḍhyataḥ||
tattacchabdaśrutau tattadarthabuddhyudaye sati|
tattacchabdārthayogātmā śaktirlokaiḥ pratīyate| iti jijñāsā'dhikaraṇam||1||
janmādyasya yataḥ||2||
bhṛgurvaivāruṇiḥ varuṇaṃ pitaramupasasāra adhīhi bhagavo brahmetyārabhya "yatovā imāni bhūtāni jāyante yena jātāni jīvanti yatprayantyabhisaṃviśanti tadvijijñāsasva tadbrahmeti" śrūyate|kimetadvākyaṃ brahma lakṣaṇataḥ pratipādayituṃ śaknoti uta neti sṛṣṭyādīnāṃ trayānāṃ viśeṣaṇatvena upalakṣaṇatvena ca lakṣaṇatvāsambhavasambhavābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ|viśeṣaṇatvapakṣe brahmaikyaṃ na sidhdyati, viśeṣaṇabhedena viśeṣyabhedāpatteḥ, bhedakaṃ hi viśeṣaṇaṃ, bhedyaṃ viśeṣyam, ato "khaṇḍo muṇḍaḥ pūrṇaśrṛṅgo gauraśvo mahiṣa" ityādiṣviva anivāryo viśeṣyabhedaḥ|"śyāmo yuvā lohitākṣo devadatta" ityatra tu devadattaikyasya pramāṇāntarasiddhatvāt na tatra viśeṣyānekatvaṃ, pramāṇāntarasidhdyabhāve tu tatrāpi viśeṣaṇabhedena tadbhetassyādeva|ato'trānenaiva lakṣaṇena lilakṣayiṣitasya brahmaṇaḥ pramāṇāntareṇa aikyānavagateḥ viśeṣaṇabhedādbahutvamavarjanīyam|tatrāpyekatvādiviśeṣaṇabhedena viśeṣyabahutvapratīteḥ tadanusāreṇa ekamityādinā eko vrīhiritivat sājātyaviṣayatopapattaḥ|navātraiva "tadbrahma"tyekavacanāttadaikyasiddhiḥ, mossvarūpaṃ jijñāsamānaṃ prati "khaṇaḍo muṇḍaḥ pūrṇaśrṛṅgo gau"rityatra vākye gośabdagataikavacanasyeva jātiviṣayatvopapatteḥ|nahyekaeva gauḥ khaṇḍo muṇḍaḥ pūrṇaśrṛṅgaśca bhavati, khaṇḍatvādīnāṃ parasparaviruddhatvāt, tatraikavacanaṃ jātiviṣayañcet, ihāpi sraṣṭṭatvādīnāṃ bhedakasvabhāvānāṃ bhedakasvabhāvānāṃ viruddhatvādekavacanaṃ jātyekaviṣayaṃ vācyamiti tulyam|api ca sraṣṭṭatvasaṃrakṣakatvayossaṃhartṛtvena anayathā'sti virodhaḥ, na hi putramutpādya yatena tatsaṃrakṣakaḥ pitā tasya sahartā'pi bhavati|etena upalakṣaṇatvapakṣo'pi nirastaḥsa, viruddhadharmāṇāmekopalakṣaṇatvāsambhavāt|kiñca ākārāntarāpratipratterapi upalakṣaṇatvaṃ na sambhavati|upalakṣaṇena lakṣyapratipattau hi upalakṣaṇaṃ upalakṣyākāraḥ upalakṣyasya pratipannākāraśceti ākāratrayamapekṣitaṃ , yathā "yatrāyaṃ śarāriḥ pakṣiviśeṣassamitrakedāra" ityatra hi śarārisambandha upalakṣaṇaṃ, mitrasvāmikatvaṃ upalakṣayaṃ, kedāratvaṃ pūrvapratipannākāraḥ|"śākhāgrañcandra" ityatra śākhāgramupalakṣaṇaṃ, kālāviśeṣe śākhāgrordhvadeśasaṃbandha upalakṣyākāraḥ, candrasvarūpaṃ pūrvapratipannākāraḥ|asmin jyotirmaṇaḍale kaścandra iti pūrvapratipattisādhyāyāṃ bubhutsāyāmeva śākhāgrañcandra iti uttarāvataraṇāt pratyakṣapratītaḥ prakṛṣṭaprakāśadirvā pūrvapratipannākāraḥ|nanu ca atrāpi satyajñānādikāvākyataḥ pratipannaṃ pūrvapratipannopalakṣyākāro'stīti cenna|tatrāpi satyajñānādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇānāṃ bhedena viśeṣyabhetāpattyā viśeṣaṇatvāsaṃbhavāt, ākārāntarāpratipattyā upalakṣaṇapakṣasyāpyasambhavāt, "yatovā imānī" tyādivākyapratipannākārāntarasamāśrayaṇe parasparāśrayāpatteḥ, ato
viśeṣaṇopalakṣaṇapakṣadvayasyāpyasambhavāt na brahma pratipattuṃ śakyamiti|evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - lakṣaṇamukhena brahma pratipattuṃ śakyameva|na ca abhede'pi viśeṣaṇabhedāyattaviśiṣyabhedapratītistatpratipattibādhikā, "śyāmo dīrgha" ityādāviva viśeṣyabhede'pi viśeṣyābhedopapatteḥ|nanu viśeṣaṇabhede viśeṣyabheda autsargikaḥ, "khaṇḍo muṇḍa" ityādau darśanāt, "śyāmo dīrgha" ityādau mānāntarasiddhaviśeṣyaikyapratītistadbādhiketyuktamiti cet satyamuktam|duruktantu tat, viśeṣaṇabhede'pi viśeṣyaikyamautsargikaṃ, "khaṇḍo muṇḍa" ityādau viśeṣaṇānāṃ virodhastadbādhakaityeva utsargāpavādavyavasthākalpanaucityāt|kimatra vinigamakamiti cet, aikyasya autsargikatvakalpane lāghavaṃ, bhedasya autsargikatvakalpane gauravamityetadvinigamakaṃ, "śyāmo dīrgha" ityādau śyāmatvādīnāṃ gaurādibhedakatvena viśeṣaṇatvamupapadyate, evaṃ "tadbrahme"tyekavacanāvagataviśeśyaikyamapi
viśeṣaṇabhedāyattaviśeṣyabhedabādhakaṃ, ekavacanasya hi vyaktvaikyaviṣayatvamautsargikam|"khaṇḍo muṇḍa" ityādau tu viśeṣaṇānāṃ virodhāttattyāgaḥ, tatrāpi viśeṣaṇānāṃ viśeṣyabhedakatvaṃ "khaṇḍo muṇḍa" ityādāvautsargikaṃ "śyāmo yuvā'ya"mityādau mānāntaratoviśeṣyaikyāvagatyā tattyāgaḥ iti, viparītotsargāpavādavyavasthākalpanapratikṣepe vyaktyaikyaviṣayatautsargikatvakalpane lāghavaṃ inyāthā gauravamityena vinigamakamanusandheyam|evaṃ lokasādhāraṇamidaṃ vinigamakaṃ, viśiṣyamātra, "yatove"tyādivākyasya yacchabdayogena "sanmūlāssomyemāssarvāḥ prajāssadāyatānāssatpratiṣṭhā" iti śrutyantarasiddhasṛṣṭyādikāraṇānuvādakatvapratipatteḥ śrutyanatrasiddhasarvavijñānapratijñānurodhāt ekakāraṇaviṣayatvāvaśyambhāvena purovādapratipannaukakāraṇaviṣayatva vācyamiti anyadvinigamakaṃ|tathā ca yathā "yadi rathantarasāmā somassyādaindravāyavāgrān grahān gṛhṇīyāt yadi bṛhatsāmā śukrāgrā" niti jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇāmnātarathantarasāmatvabṛhatsāmatvaviśeṣaṇayoḥ tatprakaraṇāmnātagāyatrā'mahīyavādisāmāntaravyāvartakatvaṃ nāsti, evamihāpi sṛṣṭikāraṇatvasya purovādapratipannasthitilayakāraṇatvavyāvartakatvaṃ nāstīti yuktamutpaśyāmaḥ|naca utpādya saṃrakṣitavatassaṃhartṛtvaṃ viruddhamiti śaṅkanīyaṃ, utpādanasaṃrakṣaṇayoriva saṃhārasyāpi kālabhedenāvirodhāt|svayamutpādya saṃrakṣitānāma sasyānāṃ kālāntara lavanadarśanāt, tattatsṛjyakarmānusāriṇa īśvarasya tattatsaṃhāre vaiṣamyanairghṛṇyadoṣānāpattervakṣyamāṇatvācca|evañca upalakṣaṇapakṣe'pi na doṣaḥ|yathā ddaṣṭānte śarāriralakṣaṇaṃ, mitrakedāratvaṃ mupalakṣyākāraraḥ, kedāratvasāmānyaṃ pūrvapratipannākāraḥ, evamihāpi jagajjanmasthitilayakāraṇatvamupalakṣaṇaṃ, tadupalakṣyākāro lakṣmīpatitvaṃ, upalakṣaṇasya janmādikāraṇatvasya anūdyamānasya puruṣasūktarūpapurovādānusāreṇa "hrīścate lakṣmīśca patnyā"viti tatratyamantrapratipanne lakṣmīpatau paryavasānāt|na hi "sanmūlā" iti chandogaśrutireva purovādaḥ, vinigamanāviraheṇa kāraṇavastusamarpakāṇāṃ puruṣasūktādīnāmapi purovādatvānivāraṇāt|brahmatvaṃ pūrvapratipannākāraḥ|taddhi kedāratvaṃ pratyakṣato'nekakedārasādhāraṇyeneva bṛhaterdhātorarthānugamena "yasmādbṛhati brahmayati tasmāducyate paraṃbrahme"ti śrutyā "bṛhattvādbrahmaṇatvācca tadbrahmetyabhidhīyata" iti smṛtyā ca bhagavati puruṣottame'vagamyamānaṃ maṃtrārthavādāvagatanānāvidhamahimaśālibrahmādyanekadevatāsakādhāraṇyena pratipannaṃ, evaṃ lakṣaṇamukhena puruṣottama eva brahmatvaparyavasānāt "nārāyaṇaparaṃbrahma"tyādiśrutiḥ|nanu bṛhatidhātormaninpratyayāntasya bṛhattvamātramarthaḥ, kathaṃ brahmaṇatvamapi tadarthaiti varṇyate|ucyate - brahmaṇatvaṃ hi svasaṅkalpena cetanācetanasthūlapariṇāmahetutvaṃ, tadapi svarūpato guṇato vibhūtitaśca bṛhattvavācinā brahmaśabdena kroḍīkṛtameva|nanu lakṣaṇasya viśeṣaṇopalakṣaṇatvapakṣadvayaṃ parasparaviruddhaṃ kthaṃ paragṛhītaṃ, jñāpyānvayivyāvartakaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ, tadananvayivyāvartakamupalakṣaṇamiti bhedamāhuḥ|satyaṃ|ihāpi upāsanābhedena janmādikāraṇatvasya anyānanvayābhyāṃ pakṣadvayopapattiḥ, yathā śāṃḍilyavidyāyāṃ - "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalā"nityupāsyakoṭāvanvitaṃ tadviśeṣaṇaṃ, vyāhṛtiśarīrakāṅyādityamaṇḍalasthapuruṣopāsanāyāṃ upāsyabrahmasvarūpapradarśakatayopalakṣaṇam|yadi "kāraṇantu dhyeya" ityarthavaśikhāvacanaṃ sarveṣu upāsaneṣu kāraṇatvasya upāssarvopāsanānugatā guṇā viśeṣaṇāni, keṣucidevopāsaneṣu vyasthitā guṇāḥ te tadanyopāsaneṣu upalakṣaṇānīti śiṣyaśikṣārthaṃ janmādikāraṇatve sthitvā pakṣadvayaṃ bhāṣyakārairdarśitaṃ|yadyapi janmakāraṇatvādikamekaikamapyanativyāptaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ bhavitumarhati, tathā'pi brahmaviṣṇurudrāḥ kāraṇapuruṣāḥ parasparabhinnāḥ pratyekaṃ janmādiṣu kāraṇānītyanādi pravādaprāptaśaṅkāvyāvṛttyarthaṃ samuditalakṣaṇāśrayaṇamiti sarvamanavadyaṃ|satyajñānādivākyoktabrahmalakṣaṇāntarasyopayogaḥ tadarthaśca "ānandādayaḥ pradhānasye" tyadhikaraṇe darśayiṣyate||
saṅgrahakārikāḥ||
janmādyasyeti yatastadbrahmeti na lakṣaṇaṃ yuktaṃ|
taccodviśeṣaṇatayā lakṣma brahmāpi bhidyeta||
autsargikaṃ viśeṣaṇamakhilānyaviśeṣadharmajātasya|
vyāvartakamiti hetostadbhede tadviśeṣyabhidā||
upalakṣaṇatā nabhavatyupalakṣyākāravaidhuryāt|
avagatakiñcidrūpe rūpāntarabodhakaṃ khalu tat||
kedāratayā ddaṣṭe dharmiṇi tadboddhṛvākyena|
pakṣī yathā śarāriḥ svamitrakedāratāgamakaḥ||
tasmāllakṣmamukhena jñātuṃ na brahma śakyamuddiṣṭam|
iti saṃkā'pākriyate pakṣadvayamapyurīkṛtya||
śyāmo yuvā lohitākṣo devadatta itīrite|
viśeṣaṇānāṃ bhede'pi viśeṣye nahi bhedadhīḥ||
khaṇḍo muṇḍaḥ pūrṇaśrṛṅgo gaurityatratu bhedadhīḥ|
khaṇḍatvādivirodhena natvatrāsti virodhitā||
upalakṣaṇatā vā'stu pratipanne bṛhattayā|
mantrārthavādādivaśātpratipannāhi tatra sā||
bahuṣu pratipanne tairbṛhatve druhiṇādiṣu|
teṣu brahma viniścetuṃ lakṣaṇoktiriyaṃ śrutau||
yathā sādhāraṇenaiva kedāratvena niścite|
mitrakedāratābuddhirvyaktibhede śarāriṇā||
evaṃ brahmādisāmānyabṛhattvena viniścite|
śrīpatau kāraṇatvena nirāghāṭā bṛrattvadhīḥ||
sarvakāraṇavākyānāṃ vaiśeṣikavacobalāt|
paśucchāganayasthityā tasminneva hi viśramaḥ||
iti janmādyadhikaraṇam||2||
śāsrayonitvāt||3||
yatovetyādivākyaṃ tatpurovādasahitaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ brahma pratipādayatītyuktamanupapannam|brahmaṇo'numānagamyatvena tatra śāsrasya prāmāṇyāsaṃbhavāt ityākṣepikī avāntarasaṅgatiḥ|brahmaṇi śāsrasya prāmāṇyaṃ sambhavati na veti, tasyānumānagamyatvatadagamyatvādābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ|na sambhavati tatra prāmāṇyaṃ, mahīmahīdharādikaṃ sakartṛkataṃ kāryatvāt ghaṭavaditi sāvayavatvānumitakāryatvaliṅkasya naiyāyikoktasya tadanumānasya jāgarūkatvāt| kāryatvaliṅgaṃ sāvayavatvaṃ na sāṃśatvamātraṃ, yena kālaviyadādisāṃśatvavādīnāṃ teṣu vyabhicārassyāt|yadavayavāḥ tataḥ parasmācca puruṣayatnena viyojanārhāḥ tatsāvayavamiti vivakṣitaṃ, atonakvāpi vyabhicāraśaṅkā|evaṃ mānāntaraprāpte'rthe śāsrasya prāmāṇyaṃ na sambhavati|nanu nimittamātreśvarasādhakenānumānena abhinnanimittopādānatvarūpasya śāsrārthasya prāptirnāstīticet - na|anumānavirodhena tasminnarthe śāsrasyāprāmāṇyāt|nanu balavatpramāṇena śāsreṇa virodhāt anumānamevāpramāṇamiti cenna|īśvarānumānamanapekṣya śāsraprāmāṇyasyaiva nirvoḍhumaśakyatvāt "mantrāyurvaidavattatprāmāṇyamāptaprāmāṇyā"diti nyāyasūtrakṛtā śāsrasya prāmāṇyamīśvaranirmitatvenaiva prasādhitaṃ, tatprasādhanaṃ śāsraprāmāṇyasiddhiḥ prāgeva anumāneneśvarasiddhimapekṣta iti upajīvyavirodhāt, upādānatvāṃśe upajīvyataḥ prāptarthatvāt nimittatvāṃśeca śāsramapramāṇam|na ca īśvaranirmitatvamanapekṣya mahājanaparigrahādeva śāsrasya
prāmāṇyamupapadyata iti vācyaṃ|anyaparamparāśaṅkānirākaraṇāya mahājanaparigrahe guṇavadvaktakatvarūpamupadārḍhyāpekṣaṇāt|evaṃ śāsrasya anumānāpekṣapravṛttikatvādeva "tattejosṛjate"tyādivākyaiḥ yadīkṣāpūrvakasraṣṭṭatvarūpaṃ kartṛtvamuktaṃ, tadanumānamūlamiti vyañjayitumeva "anena śuṅgenāpomūlamanviccha adbhissomyaśuṅgena tejomūlamanviccha tejasāsomyaśuṅgena sanmūlamanvicche"ti kāraṇānumānaṃ pradarśya "sanmūlāssomyemāḥ prajāḥ" ityādyuktaṃ|nanu īśvarasya kartṛtvavadupādānatvasyāpi pratīteḥ, nahi kartṛmātraviṣayamiti cenna|ghaṭādiṣu kulālādeḥ yāddaśaṃ kartṛtvaṃ ddaṣṭaṃ, tāddaśakartṛtvādhikaraṇatvenaiva hi īśvaro'numānena sādhanīyaḥ|kulālādiṣu mṛtpiṇḍādisakalocetanaddaṣṭakāraṇādhiṣṭhātṛtvarūpameva kartṛtvanddaṣṭamiti, ddaṣṭānurodhāt tathābhūtakartṛtveneśvarānumānaṃ nimittopādānabhedagarbhaṃ kathamupādānatvāṃśe śāsraprāmāṇyapratikṣepakaṃ nasyāt|tasmāllakṣaṇoktimātreṇa cāritārthyābhāvāt lakṣye tasya lakṣaṇaveśiṣṭye ca prāmāṇyasya vaktavyatvāt|iha ca abhinnanimittopādānatvarūpe lakṣaṇe vedāntānāṃ prāmāṇyāsambhavāt vedāntamukhena kartavyatayā'bhimato brahmavicāro na kartavya iti tadarthamidaṃ śāsrannārambhaṇīyamiti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - mahīmahīdharamahārṇavādīnāmekena kartrā yugapatakṛtatvenaiva sakartṛkatvaṃ nirvāhyamityatra pramāṇannāsti|mahato devālayasya prāsādamaṇḍapagopuraprākāradīnāmiva teṣāṃ krameṇa bahubhiḥ kṛtatvenāpi sakartṛkatvopapatteḥ|pratyuta
mahīmahīdharādikamanekakartṛkaṃ, yāvatsaṃpratipannaikakartṛkaghaṭādibhyo mahākāryatvāt, gopuraprākāravaditi bahukartṛkatvasyaiva anumātuṃ śakyatvācca|na ca lāghavātkartraikyasiddhiḥ, nahi prasiddhasaṃsārivilakṣaṇā bahavaḥ kartāraḥ kalpyante, kintu teṣveva kecitkarmopāsanāviśeṣairupacitapuṇyaviśeṣāḥ kṣityādiṣu kasyacitkasyacitkālaviśeṣeṣu kartāraḥ kalpyante|prasiddhaṃ hi agastyaviśvāmitrādīnāṃ vindhyastaṃbhanamahārṇavaculakīkaraṇasvargāntarasṛṣṭyādikaṃ sāmarthyaṃ, adyataneṣvaṃkurādiṣvapi sūkṣmavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭārthajñānādimanatastatra santo yoginaḥ kartārassambhavanti|ataoḥ kṣityādisakalopādānagocarāparokṣajñānacikīrṣādimataḥ saṃsārivilakṣaṇasya kasyacitkartuḥ kalpana eva dharmikalpanāgauravam|evaṃ ca ghaṭādiddaṣṭāntena kṣityaṅkurādīnāṃ śarīrajanyatvasiddhirapyanivāritā bhavati|kiṃ ca lāghavādekaḥ kartā sidhyan prasiddhasaṃsārivilakṣaṇaḥ sidhdyatīti ko'rthaḥ|kiṃ vastutaḥ ekaḥ kartā sidhdyatītisa uta ekatvaviśiṣṭassidhdyatīti|nādyaḥ|ekatvāviṣayatve vastutaḥ ekaḥ kartā sidhdyatīti, uta ekatvaviśiṣṭassidhdyatīti|nādyaḥ|ekatvāviṣayatve vastuta ekaḥ kartā viṣaya iti nirṇetumaśakyatvāt|na dvitīyaḥ|ekatvasya saṃkhyārūpatve pratyekantadvaiśiṣṭyasatvena tatsiddheḥ bahukartṛtvāvirodhitvāt anekakartṛkatvābhāvaviśiṣṭaṃ sakartṛkatvaṃ sādhyamiti cenna|gopurādiṣu vyabhicārāt, laghuviṣayopanāyakatayā tvadabhimatasya lāghavasya bahuviṣayopanāyakatvāpatteśca|nahyevaṃ saṃpratipannaṃ pramāṇānugrāhakamasti, yadagre vastulāghavasidhdyarthaṃ svānugrāhyapramāṇasya viṣayagauravamāpādayet, tathāsati iṣṭapurovartijñānasya pravattikāraṇatāṃ gṛhṇataḥ pramāṇasya anugrāhako lāghavatarkaḥ, bhramasthale vyadhikaraṇāprakārakajñānasya akalpanayā agre vastulāghavasidhdyarthaṃ bhedāgrahameva kāraṇatāvacchedakatvenopanayet, na tu jñānavitti vedyatayopasthitaṃ vaiśiṣṭyam, ato lāghavātkartraikyasiddhirityayuktameva|etena sādhyamānasya kartuḥ kartṛtopayuktānāṃ aparokṣajñānacikīrṣākṛtīnāṃ lāghavātpratyekamekatvāsiddhau tadāśrayakartraikyasiddhiratyapi nirastam|kartraikyasiddhipakṣoktavikalpamukhadūṣaṇānāmatrāpi satvāt, kasyacijjñānaṃ kasyaciccikīrṣā kasyacitkṛtirityevamāśrayabhedasiddhinivāraṇāya punastadāśrayaikyasyāpi sādhanīyatvāpatteḥ|na hi īśvarajñānādīnāṃ tvanmate nityānāṃ janmajanakabhāvo'sti, yena samānādhikaraṇānāmeva teṣāṃ janmajanakabhāvadarśanāt tadāśrayaikyaṃ sidhyet, evaṃ ca teṣāmekaikatvahetukanityatvāsiddheḥ, evaṃ ca yogināmaparokṣajñānādibhirapi siddhasādhanamanivāryam|na ca kṣityaṃkurādijananapūrvakṣaṇe tatra tatra teṣāṃ sannidhānamaklṛptamiti vācyam|"dharmikalpato varaṃ dharmakalpane"ti nyāyena yogaśāsrādipramāṇakānāṃ teṣāṃ sannidhānamatrasya kalpanaucityāt|nanu anumānena kartṛmātrasiddhau "dyāvābhūmī janayan deva eka" ityādiśrutitastadaikyasiddhirastvaticet, tarhi anumānena apratyākhyātānāṃ bhagavato niratiśayānandādyanantakalyāṇaguṇānāṃ nityavigrahasya "tadaikṣate"tyādiśrutisiddhatattatsṛṭyādisaṅkalpādyanityatvasya ca tatassiddhirnanivāryeti, na nityānuvartamānaśarīrarahitanityāparokṣajñānādiviśeṣaguṇatrayayuktanaiyāyikābhimateśvarasiddhiriti, na tadanumānasiddheśvarānuvādakatvādinā śāskasyāprāmāṇyaprasaktiriti, pramāṇabhūtena śāsreṇa
jagatkāraṇatvasatyasaṅkalpatvādyanantakalyāṇaguṇādhārapurūṣottamābhidhaparabrahmasiddhirapratayūheti siddham||
saṅgrahakārike||
jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ brahma sidhdyatyevānumānataḥ|
ato vicāryā vedāntā na tatsiddhānuvādinaḥ||
naitatsādhvanumānāddhi kartraikyādi na sidhyati|
atastatkāraṇaṃ brahma vedāntaikapramāṇakam||
iti śāsrayonitvādhikaraṇam||
evaṃ mānāntarasiddhimūlāprāmāṇyaśaṅkānimittakaśāsrānārabhaśaṅkānivāritā - atha aphalatvanibaṃdhanatadanāraṃbhaśaṅkaraṇārthaṃ caturthamadhikāraṇam - 
tattusamanvayāt||4||
vedāntānāṃ siddharūpe brahmaṇi mānāntarāpravṛttāvapi prāmāṇyaṃ na sambhavati, siddharūpabrahmaparavākyasya pravṛttinivṛttiparavākyasyeva purūṣārthaparyavasāyitvāsambhavena śrotṛṇāṃ tatra bubhutsā nodetītyabubhutsite śrotṛṇāṃ pramityanudayena teṣāṃ prāmāṇyokternirālambanatvāt| na ca yathā kenāpi hetunā rathyāyāṃ patitasya kirātaissaṃnardhitasya
kirātaputro'hamityabhimanyamā nasya rājakumārasya "na tvaṃ kirātaputraḥ, kiṃ tu rājakumāraḥ, tava
pitā mahārājastarūṇo niratiśayabhogaśāli rājayaṃ paripālayannāsta, iti siddhārtha vākyaśravaṇena harṣo jāyate, evamihāpi tava anyeṣāṃ ca pitṛsthānīyo nikhilaheyapratyanīkasarvajñatvasatyasaṅkalpadayādākṣiṇyamahodāratvādhananta kalyāṇaguṇaśāli bhagavānāsta" iti vākyaśroturmahān harṣojāyate, sa ca tatpāpyupāyamanviṇya taṃ prāpnoti" tyevaṃ siddhārthavākyasyāpi harṣatutvena purūṣārthaparyavasānamupapadhata iti vācyaṃ| tasya harṣasya rā jakumāraharṣasyeva tātkālikatvena mukteḥ sthiraṣurūṣārthatvābhāvāpatteḥ| na ca taṃ prāptasya tadīyaniratiśayānandānubhavassadā vartamānanobhavati, sassthira purūṣārthassyāditi vācyam| parakīyānandajñānasya paraṃ pratyapurūṣārthatvāditi purvapakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - tātkālikaharṣastāvadastyeva| brāhmaniratiśayānaṃda sākṣātkārakṣca brahmaṇā saha bhogasāmyasahitaḥ sthāyī purūṣārthaḥ, natu tatra svakīyatvamapekṣitaṃ, sākṣātkāratvaviśeṣaṇe atītasvakīyasukhādismaraṇasyāpi sukhasākṣātkāravatpurūṣārthatvaprasaṅgāt, taddhiśeṣaṇe tataevātiprasaṅga nivāraṇena svakīyatvaviśeṣaṇānapekṣaṇāditi - 
saṃgrahakārike|
tathā'pi siddharūpe'smin vedāntānāṃ namānatā|
pravṛttyādiparatvasyābhāvādaphalaśālināṃ||
maivaṃ svamandirāntasthanidhibodhakavākyavat|
tāddagbrahmāvaboghitvātte svataḥ phalaśālinaḥ||
iti samanvayādhikaraṇam||4||
evaṃ śāstrānārambhahetuśaṃkācatuṣṭayanirākaraṇakme'dhyāyakamohetuḥ, tatra siddharūpe brahmaṇi vyutpattisamarthanaṃ samanvayādhyāyopayogi, viśeṣaṇā nāmavirodhasamarthanamavirodhādhyāyopayogi, anumānatassiddhinirākaraṇaṃ sādhanādhyāyopayogi,anumanātassiddhau hi anumānasiddhajñānāditrayavattayā saṃravyādipañcakavattayā ca brahmopāsanā siddhet, natvānandādiguṇavattayā| purūṣārthaparyavasānavarṇanantu phalādhyāyopayogīti spaṣṭameva| evaṃ upoddhātādhikaraṇacatuṣṭayena śāsrārambhe samārthite sati īkṣatyadhikaraṇamārabhya śāsraṃ pratāyate||
īkṣaternāśabdaṃ||5||
gauṇaścennātmaśabdāt ||6||

śrutatvācca||12||
śāstra kāṇḍadvikādhyāyapādasaṃgatayassphuṭāḥ| peṭikāsaṃgatirvācyā tathā'vāntarasaṅgatiḥ|| vedārthavicāratvādbrahmakāṇḍasaṅgatiḥ, kāraṇaviṣayatvāt dvikasaṃgatiḥ, kāraṇavākyasamanvaya viṣayatvādadhyāyasaṃgatiḥ,
spaṣṭatarapradhānajīvaliṃgavākyaviṣayatvāpādasaṃgatiriti pañcasaṅgatayassphuṭāḥ| peṭikāsaṅgatiravāntarasaṅgatiśca vaktavyocyate - brahma jagataḥ kāraṇameva nabhavati, kintu pradhānajīvākāśādikamiti śaṅkānirāsena brahmakāraṇatvāyogavyavacchedaḥ, upodghātādhikaraṇacatuṣṭayānantaraprathamapādaśeṣarūpapeṭikārtha iti peṭikāsaṅgatiḥ| kāraṇatvākṣiptāḥ brahmaṇassarvajñatvasatyasaṅkalpatvādiguṇāstatkratunyāyena muktaprāpyā guṇāḥ, tasya mahābhogaśālitvaguṇaḥ nirañcanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti| "bhogamātra sāmyaliṃgācce"ti śrutismṛtyanusāreṇāpi muktaprāpyoguṇaḥ, brahmaṇoniratiśayānandastu muktasya svayamaprāpyo'pi sadā'svādya ityanantakalyāṇaguṇākarasya niratiśayamahānandaśālinaḥ parabrahmaṇaḥ pratipādanaṃ puruṣārthaparyavasāyi bhavatyeveti hi samanvayādhikaraṇe vivakṣitaṃ, tadayuktaṃ, sarveṣāṃ kāraṇa vākyānāṃ pradhānaviṣayatvādityākṣepikī avāntarasaṅgatiḥ| chāndogye śrūyamāṇā "sadevasomyedamagra āsī"dityādirūpā sadvidyā khalu kāraṇavākyeṣu sārabhūtā, sā
tāvanna brahmapratipādinī| kiṃtu sāṃkhyānumānasiddhapradhānānuvādinī, "sadevasomyedamagraāsī"diti pratijñānirdeśena yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vijñātaṃ syāditi dṛṣṭāṃtopādānena anumānasiddhārthānavādakatvasya svayameva śrutyā sphuṭīkaraṇāt| naca acetanasya pradhānasya īkṣaṇaṃ nasambhavatīti tadaikṣate ti śravaṇamanupapannaṃ syāditi vācyam| "vṛṣṭipratīkṣāśśālayaḥ"| vṛṣṭyā modamānāśśālayaḥ kandalantī tyādivyavahāreṣu acetanānāmapi cetanavadupacāradarśanāt, kūlaṃpipatiṣatī tyatra kūlasya patanakāryaunmukhye gauṇevyavahāravat sataḥ tejassṛṣṭikāryaunmukhye gauṇekṣaṇavyavahāropapatteḥ, "tattejaaikṣata"| tā āpa aikṣante ti gauṇekṣaṇaprāyapāṭhena sadīkṣaṇasyāpi gauṇatvanirṇayācceti pūrvapakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - pratijñātāvat pratyakṣādyavagatārthabodhanasādhāraṇī, dṛṣṭāntanirdeśo'pi pratijñātārthasya budyavyārohārthatayā anyathāsiddhaḥ, tatsādhāraṇa eva| anyathā "yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatī"ti ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñā budhdyārūḍhā nasyāt, hetuvacanamātramanumānāsādhāraṇantadiha na śrūyate| nanu annena śuṅgenāpomūlamanviccha adbhissomyaśuṅgena tejomūlamanviccha tejasā somya śuṅgena sanmūlamanvicce ti vākyeṣu annādikāryarūpaliṅgena kāraṇānumānamupanyastaṃ dṛśyate - satyaṃ - upakramopasaṃhārāvabhyāso'pūrvatāphalaṃ| arthavādopapattī ca liṃgaṃ tātparyanirṇaya iti śrutitātparyanirṇāyakaliṅgeṣu parigaṇitamupapattirūpamidaṃ liṃgaṃ natvatra pradhānasādhakatvena sāṃkhyābhimatasya kasyacilliṃgasya nirdeśo'sti, sarve kārye sukha duḥkhamohātmakadravyaprakṛtekaṃ, tatsamanvitatvāt, sāmānyavyāptau ghaṭādivadityādikaṃ hi sāṃkhyānāṃ pradhānasādhakamanumānamabhimataṃ, natadihaśrutyākaṭākṣeṇāpi vīkṣitamasti| gauṇekṣaṇaprāyapāṭhastu nasadīkṣaṇasya gauṇatvāpādakaḥ, sanidhyāmnānalakṣaṇaprāyapāṭharūpapañcamapramāṇasya īkṣaṇaśrutitadvācyekṣaṇaliṃgabādhakatvāyogāt| kiñca "aitadātmyamidaṃ sarva tatsatyaṃ saātme"ti sarvasya prapañcasya sadātmakatvoktiḥ taduddeśena sataātmatvoktiśca acetane pradhāne naghaṭate| evañca sarvasya prapañcasya ātmabhūtaṃ tejaḥprabhṛtiśarīrakaṃ sadeva tatteja aikṣate tyādiṣu īkṣitṛtvena vivakṣitamiti nagauṇekṣaṇaprāpayapāṭhaśaṅkāpi| api ca mumukṣoḥ svetaketoḥ tatvamasī ti sadātmakatvānusandhānamupadiśya "tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvannavimokṣye athasaṃpatsya" iti tatphalatayā śarīrapātānantaraṃ mokṣaupadiśyate| naca pradhānaniṣṭhasya mokṣa iti sāṃkhyānāmapyabhimataṃ mokṣaupadiśyate| naca pradhānaniṣṭhasya mokṣa iti sāṃkhyānāmapyabhimataṃ, teṣāmapi mumukṣūṇāṃ heyameva hi tadabhimataṃ, atoheyatvāvacanādapi na satpradhānaṃ| sarvavijñānaprijñāviradhādapi nasatpradhānaṃ, nahi pradhānajñānena tatkāryatayā tadabhinnasya mahadādikāryavargasya jñātatvasambhave'pi cetanavargasya jñātatvamupapadyate| apica suptapuruṣasya satā somya tadā sampannobhavatīti satsampattimupadiśya svamapītobhavatī ti tasyāḥ svāpyayarūpatvaṃ saṅkīrtitamidaṃ sadbrahmeti
pakṣe ghaṭate, puruṣaḥ suṣupteḥ prāk jñānavikāsādirūpasthūlākāraviśiṣṭajīvaśarīrakaḥ kāryarūpaḥ paramātmā suṣuptau tamākāraṃ parityajya jñānasaṅkocarūpasūkṣmākāraviśiṣṭajīvaśarīrake svasmin kāraṇarūpe līno bhavatīti| apipūrvakasya iṇdhātorlayārthatvāt "sarvasya prabhavāpyayau hi bhūtanā"mityādiśrutyaṃtareṣu bhavāpyayau hi bhūtānā miyādigītāvacaneṣu ca tasya tādarthyadarśanāt, sacchabdasya ca atra kāraṇavastuparasya sūkṣmacitacidvastuśarīraka paramātmaparatāyālakṣaṇasūtre darśitatvāt| satpradhānamiti pakṣetu svāpyasaṅgīrtanaṃ naghaṭate, pradhāne svaśabdasya svīyaparatayopapattāvapi jīvaṃ taccharīrakaparamātmanāñca prati akāraṇe tasmin tayorlayāsambhavāt| kiñca " ātmāvā idamekaevāgraāsīti " | ātmana ākāśassaṃbhūta " ityādiśrutyantaraṇāṃ paramātmanaeva sargādyakāle avasthānaṃ ākāśādisraṣṭutvañca pratipādayantīnāṃ yā gatiḥ pravṛttiḥ tatsāmānyaṃ sadvidyāyāmapsti sadevasomyedamagraāsī diti| tato'pi nasatpradhānaṃ, śvetāśvataropaniṣadi sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ puruṣaevedaṃ sarvamiti puruṣasūktamantrābhyāṃ tatpratipādyaṃ bhagavaṃtaṃ prastutya sakāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhiponacāsya kaścijjanicā nacādhipa iti tasya sākṣādeva jagatkāraṇatvapratipādanena sadvidyāyāstadaikārthyāvaśyambhāvāditi sat bhagavān puruṣottamaḥ paraṃbrahmaiveti||
saṃṅgrahakārikāḥ||
sadvidyāyāṃ kāraṇaṃ satpadoktam
sāṃkhyairuktaṃ liṅgagamyaṃ pradhānaṃ|

yadisato muravyamīkṣaṇaṃ vācyaṃ, tarhi cetanatvānmuravyekṣaṇārho jīvaeva jagatkāraṇamāstviti pūrvapakṣotthānādanantarasaṅgatiḥ, taittirīyāḥ ānandavallayāṃ"brahmavidāpnotipara, mityupakmya kintabhma kīddaśantaddhedanaṃ kīddaśaṃtaddhedanaphalamityākāṃkṣāyāṃ satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma| yoveda vihitaṃ guhāyāṃ paramevyoman| so'kṣnute sarvānkāmān saha brahmaṇā vipakṣcitā| iti mantreṇa brahmaṇassvarūpaṃ taddhedanaprakāraṃ tatprāpya phalasvarūpañcopavaṇarya|tasmādvā eta smādātmana ākāśassambhūta" ityādinā tasmādeva brahmaṇaākāśādisakala jagadutpattimuktavā tasya sukhapratipattyarthaṃ sthūlārundhatīnyāyena annaprāṇamano buddhiṣu krameṇa tahuddhimavatārya tasmādvā etasmādvijñānamayādanyo'ntara ātmā''nandamaya|iti ānandamayamupakrabhya tasminneva prastutajagatkāraṇabrahma buddhissamāpitā, tadanantaraṃ tasmāddhā etasmādānaṃdamayādanyo'ntara ātmo, ti ātmāntaropadeśābhāvāt|sa
kimānandamayo jīvaḥ, uta paramātme"ti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - jīva evāyaṃ, eṣa eva śārīra ātme, ti śārīrasvaśravaṇāt, tasya priyameva śiraḥ| modo dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ| prabhoda uttaraḥ pakṣaḥ ānanda ātmā| brahmapucchaṃ pratiṣṭha"ti priyamodapramodānandaśābditeṣṭavastu darśanatallābhatadupayogajanyasukhatadanantarānu - vartamānatṛptisukhasambandhaliṃgāt, yantasampādanīyabhogyasya jīvasyeva nityāvāptasamastakāṃmasya īkṣvarasya iṣṭavastulābhajanyasukhaviśeṣāṇāmāśaṅkā'nāspadatvāt, tasmāddhā etasmādātmanaākāśassaṃbhūta" ityatra tasmādityanena parāmṛṣṭasya prakṛtasya brahmaṇo jīvarūpatvapratipattyarthaṃ "etasmā, diti nirdeśena idānīmanubhūyamānatāyāḥ "ātmāna" iti nirdeśena lekavedaprasiddhātmaśabdavācyatāyākṣca sphuṭīkaraṇāt, tadanantaraṃ laukikājñāmagahaṃbuddhiṣu sthulārundhatīnyāyena taṭughghavatāraṇāñca, annaprāṇādiṣuṃ laikikānāṃ brahmabughghabhāvena teṣu brahmāviṣayasthulārūndhatīnyāyānavatāraṇāt| na ca jīvasya sargāghakāle svaśarīrasṛṣṭeḥ prāk śarīrābhāvena tatsādhyasṛṣṭi saṅkalpānupapatteḥ svaśarīrasṛṣṭacyogāt tanmūlasṛjyāntarasṛṣṭacasambhavaśśaṅkanīyaḥ, tadānīmapyanādīliṅgaśarīrasattvena tataeva sṛṣṭisaṅkalpasya tanmūlasṛjyavanargasya copapatteḥ, abhāvaṃ vādarirāha hyeva, mityadhikaraṇe muktānāṃ śarīrābhāvadaśāyāṃ saṅkalpajapitrādistraṣṭatvasya tataeva upapādayiṇyamāṇatvāt| nanu ānandamayoktayanantaraṃ brahmapucchaṃ pratiṣṭhe" ti tato'nyadeva brahma śrūyata iti canne|priyameva śira" ityādinā jīvānandāvayavānāṃ muktayanantaraṃ tadīyākhaṇḍānandasya saṃsāradaśāyāmāvṛtasya tatra brahmaśabdenābhidhānāt, tasyaiva ca akhaṇḍānandasya muktidaśāyāmābhivyaktiḥ tadvedanaphalatvena brahmaṇā vipakṣcite, ti brahmaśabdena pradarśitā, tatra hi bhogya sāhitye tṛtīyā, na bhoktṛkartṛkabhogasya ānandamayopāsanā phalakoṭau nirdeśāyogāt| na ca pucchabrahyaṇojīvānyatvābhāve asanneva sabhavati asti brahmeti ceddheda santamenantato vidu riti brahmasattvāsātvavedanoktirnayujyate, jīvasattvasya suprasiddhatvena tasyāsattvaśahkānarhatvāditi vācyam| annamayādikṣlokānāṃ pucchaladviṣayatvena ānandamayaparyāyakṣalokasyāpi pucchavadānandamayaviṣayatāyā eva yuktatvāt, ānandamayasya priyamodādimattayā prasiddhāvapi brahmaśabdoktākhaṇḍānandavattayā prasidhdyabhāvena sattvāsattvavedanayostadviṣayatopapatteḥ| tasmādānandavallyāṃ jīvaeva jagatkāraṇatvena pratipādyate, natu tadatiriktaṃ
brahma, evaṃca tadanusāreṇa kāraṇavākyāntarāṇyapi jīva eva paryavasyanti| ataeva sadvidyāyāṃ sacchabdena upakrāntaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ vastu tattvamasī ti jīvarūpatayopasaṃhṛtamiti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - "yatovāconivartante aprāpya manasā saha| ānandaṃ brahmaṇovidvānnabibhetikutaścane " ti niratiśayadaśāśiraskatvena abhyasyamāno mahānando jīve'lpasukhabhāgini asambhāvyamānaḥ taditaramīśvaramāśrayamāsādyaiva nirvṛṇoti| evaṃ bhūtamahānandabhāgānandamaya īśvara eva, na jīvaḥ, jīvasyāpyevaṃbhūta ānandobhyupeyata ityuktamiticet, satyamuktaṃ duruktaṃ tu tat| "yuvāsyātsādhu
yuvādhyāyakaḥ āśiṣṭho draḍhiṣṭhobalaḥ " tasyeyaṃ pṛthivi sarvā vittasya pūrṇā syāt| sa eko mānuṣaānanda "ityuktalakṣaṇamānuṣānandaprabhṛtiprājāpatyānandāntasakalaprāṇivargānandajātādadhikatamatayā " te ye śataṃ prajāpaterānandāḥ| ekobrahmaṇaānanda " iti kīrtitasya ānaṃdamayānandasya pūrvavākyeṣu tadapekṣayā atikṣudrānandabhāktvena kīrtite jīvavarge saṃbhāvanānarhatvāt| kiñca " tasmādvā etasmādvijñānamayādanyo'ntaraātmānaṃdamaya " iti vijñānamayaśabditājjīvādanyatvenopakrāṃtasya ānaṃdamayasya kathaṃ jīvatvāśaṅkā| naca vijñānamayaśabdabuddhiparaśśaṅkanīyaḥ, " yoyaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇeṣu hṛdyantarjyoti "rityādiśrutyantareṣu jīva eva prayogāt vijñānamayaparyāye " vijñānaṃ devāssarve brahmajyeṣṭhamupāsata " iti kevalavijñānaśabdo'pi śrūyata iti cet - tasyāpi "yo vijñāne tiṣṭha "nnityādiśrutyantareṣviva jīvaparatvāt, vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute karmāṇi tanute'pice ti tasya yajñādiṣu kartṛtvaśravaṇāt, buddhesteṣu karaṇatvena kartṛtvābhāvāt| api ca jīvamānaṃdamayaṃ vadatā tatra mayaḍarthaḥ kaiti vaktavyaṃ, na vikāraḥ, "mayaḍvaitayorbhāśāyāmabhakṣyācchādanayo" riti sūtreṇa bhāṣāyāmeva vikārāvayavārthayormayaḍvidhānāt, kathantarhi " yasya parṇamayījuhūrbhavatī "tyādivaidikaprayogaḥ, "vdyacaśchandasī"ti vidhānāṃtarāt| idaṃ hi chandasimayaṭo'prāptasya ca prāptyarthaṃ vidhānaṃ, kathaṃ tarhi vaidiko vijñānamayaśabdaḥ, tasmādvikārārthatvāsaṃbhavāt ānaṃdamayo'pi mayaḍprācuryārthaeva vācyaḥ| prācuryārthatvañca prāgudāhṛtānaṃdamīmāṃsāvākyaśeṣopadarśitaprakāreṇa mānuśādiprājāpatyāṃtasakalajīvavargasukhāpekṣayā ādhikyarūpamiti
na kasyāpi jīvasya ānandamayatvaśaṅkāvakāśaḥ| etena ānaṃdaprācuryārthaka ānaṃdamayaśabdo jīve'pyupapadyate, sāṃsārikaduḥkhalavabhāji muktyabhivyaṃgyamahānandavati jīve samānādhikaraṇaduḥkhālpatvāpekṣā ''nandādhikyarūpaprācuryārthasaṃbhavāt| prācuryārthaśabdasya uttaratve samānādhikaraṇavijātīyālpatvāpekṣameva hi prācuryamarthaḥ, natu "pracurabrāhmaṇogrāma " ityatreva vyadhikaraṇasajātīyālpatvāpekṣaṃ eva prācurye kīrtyamāne vidhāntareṇa prācuryagrahaṇāyogat| avyavasthitapaurvāparyasya pracuraśabdarūpasya nāmapadasya uttaratve tathā vyutpattāvapi "tatprakṛtavacane mayaḍi"ti sūtravihitasya niyatottarabhāvasya mayaṭpratyayasya uttaratve tathā vyutpattikalpakābhāvācca| tasmādānandamayobrahmaiva| ata eva tasyopakrame jīvopāsyatvanirdeśaḥ, yoveda nihitaṃ guhāyā" miti jīvāsaṃbhāvitavividhadraṣṭṛtvarūpasārvajñyanirdeśaḥ, " saha brahmaṇā vipaścite " ti vākyaśeṣe ca " kohyevānyāt kaḥ prāṇyāt - yadeṣaākāśaānaṃdonasyāt - e - ṣahyevāndayātī" ti jīvānaṃndayitṛbhāvyapadeśaḥ, sokāmayata bahusyāṃ prajāyeye ti hiraṇyagarbhādijīvāsaṃbhāvitapradhānānapekṣasvatantrasṛṣṭikāmanāvyapadeśaḥ, rasovaisaḥ rasaṃ hyevāyaṃ labdhvā''nandī bhavatīti jīvāsvādanī yatvavyapadeśaśca|etairapyupakramopasaṃhāragatahetubhirānandamaya na jīvaḥ, kintu brahmaiveti pradhānasyeva jīvasyāpi na sadvidyānaṃdavallyādikāraṇavākyapratipādyatvamiti siddham||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||

jagatkāraṇaṃ vastu īkṣaṇaśravaṇāt "tatvamasī" tyupasaṃhārācca mābhūtpradhānaṃ mābhūccajīvasāmānyaṃ tatobhedakanirdeśāt, tathāpyupacitapuṇyaviśeṣāṇāṃ brahmendracandrasūryādīnāṃ viśvāmitrādīnāmṛṣīṇāñca madhdye yaḥ kaścijjīvaviśeṣaḥ kalpabhedena syāt, tatrānandavallyuktajīvasāmānyavyāvartakasakalamahimopapatteriti pratyavasthānātsaṅgatiḥ| tatra sthālīpulākanyāyena kiñciddevatāviṣayaṃ vākyamudāhriyate - chāndogye - " atha yaeṣo'ntarāditye hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣodṛśyate hiraṇyaśmaśrurhiraṇyakeśaāpraṇakhatsarvaeva suvarṇaḥ tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevamakṣiṇī tasyoditināma saeṣasarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhyauditaḥ udeti ha vai sa sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhyoyaevaṃ veda tasya ṛkca sāmaca goṣṇā| vityādinā ādityamaṇḍalasyāṃtarupāsyaḥ puruṣo'bhihitaḥ, "atha ya eṣo'ntarakṣiṇi puruṣodṛśyate saiva ṛk tatsāma tadukthaṃ tadyajustadbrahma tasyaitasya tadeva rūpaṃ yadamuṣyarūpaṃ yāvamuṣya geṣṇau tau geṣṇau yannāma tannāmetyādinā akṣyantarupāsyaḥ puruṣo'bhihitaḥ| asmin vidyādvaye upāsyaḥ puruṣaḥ ṣuṇyaviśeṣeṇa ādityamaṇaḍalādhiṣṭātṛdevatāpadaṃ prāpratojīvaviśeṣaiti saeva ānandavallyuktasakalamahimaśālī samastakāraṇavākyaviṣayassyāt, uta tato'nyaḥ paramātmeti saeva kāraṇavākyānāṃ viṣayaiti saṃśayaḥ| ānaṃdavallyāṃ "bhīṣāsmādvātaḥ pavaṃtebhīṣodeti sūryaḥ " iti sūryabhayahetutvena tatobhinnaānandamayaḥ pradarśitaḥ, iha kathaṃ saevānandamayaiti pūrvapakṣasyāvakāśaḥ|ucyate - sthālīpulākanyāyena iha ādityajīvaudāharaṇamityuktaṃ|
brahmendrādayo'pi vastutaihodāharaṇānīti kalpabhedena yatteṣāṃ jagatkāraṇatvaṃ pūrvapakṣyābhimataṃ, tadāsādyasūryabhayahetutvasamāmnānañcaritārthaṃ syāt| indrādibhayahetutvasamāmnānasyāpi upapattirevamevānusaṃdheyā| adhikaraṇadhyutpādyanyāyasya brahmendrādisādhāraṇye'pi siddhāṃte maṇḍalābhimānijīvātiriktaṃ brahma jagatkāraṇaṃ sidhdyet, puṇḍarīkākṣatvaliṅgena bhagavān puruṣottama evetyapi, ekayatnena sidhdyatvityabhisandhāya sūtrakṛtā antaśśabdena antarādityavidyā'ntarakṣividyādvayamihodāharaṇamiti sūtritaṃ|pūrvapakṣastu - maṇḍalābhimānī jīva ekāyaṃ, natu tato'nyatsiddhāntyabhimataṃ brahma, śarīraśravaṇāt, karmanimittaṃhiśarīraṃ, kathaṃ karmarahitatvena siddhāṃtābhyupagatasya brahmaṇastadupapadyate| naca saeṣasarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhyaudita iti sarvapāparahitatvena śrutasya kathaṃ karmanimittaṃ śarīraṃ syāditi śaṃkanīyaṃ, trividhaṃ hi śarīraṃ, kevalapuṇyārabdhaṃ sukhaikāyatanaṃ devānāṃ, kevalapāpārabdhaṃ duḥkhaikāyatanaṃ sthāvarādīnāṃ, puṇyapāpobhayārabdhaṃ sukaduḥkhobhayāyatanaṃ manuṣyādīnāṃ, evaṃ ca na havai devāt pāpaṃ gacchatīti śruteḥ devānāṃ apāpatve'pi puṇyamātrārabdhaśarīre na kācitanupapattiḥ| nanu siddhāṃtyabhimatasya brahmaṇo.pyantaryāmibhāvena jīvakarmanimittaśarīreṣu hṛdayadeśe avasthānalakṣaṇaṃ śārīratvamupapadyata iti cet, upapadyatānānāma, tadyathākathañcit, iha na tathābhūtaṃ śārīratvamucyate kiṃtu atiramaṇīyatayā śarīriṇaḥ spṛhaṇīyaṃ tasya divyabhogāyatanaṃ tenā''keśamānakhāgramanupraviṣṭaṃ yaccharīraṃ tena śarīreṇa śarīravatvamucyate| atra "hiraṇmayaḥ puruṣa "ityādi evamakṣiṇī ityantaṃ tasyātirāmaṇīyakaṃ varṇayadudāhṛtaśrutivacanameva sāśri, tasmādatyantopācitapuṇyaviśeṣalabhyamidaṃ śarīraṃ jīvasyaiva saṃbhavatītyevaṃrūpaśarīraśravaṇādayaṃ jīvaeva, na tadatirikte brahmaṇī pramāṇamastīti saevānaṃdamayaḥ| ataeva ānandavallyāṃ śrūyate "sayaścāyaṃ puruṣe yaścāsāvāditye saeka " iti | siddhāṃtastuneha śrūyamāṇaṃ śarīraṃ karmanimittaṃ, śarīriṇassarvapāpodayaśravaṇāt sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhya uditatvaṃ| uditatvaṃ hi nīḍādudgatasya śakuntasya sarvadhā nīḍasaṃbandharāhityavat sarvapāpmasaṃbaṃdharāhityarūpaṃ gauṇaṃ, mukhyasya pāpmāpādānakodgamanasyāsaṃbhavāt| naca sarvapāpātyantābhāvavataḥ karmanimittaṃ śarīraṃ saṃbhavati, naca pāpābhāve.pi puṇyapāpobhayasādhāraṇatvāt| "nainaṃ setumahorātre tarataḥ na jarā na mṛtyurna śoko na sukṛtaṃ na duṣkṛtaṃ sarve pāpmāno ato nivartata " iti brahmaprakaraṇe pāpmaśabdasya sukṛtasādhāraṇyaśravaṇāt, mumukṣuṃ prati duṣkṛtavat sukṛtasyāpi ete vai nirayāstāta sthānasya paramātmana iti bhagavatparāśaroktarītyā aniṣṭaphalatvena tasyāpi pāpmaśabdena grahaṇaucityāt, alaukikāniṣṭaphalasādhanatvasyaiva pāpmaśabdupravṛttinimittatvena mumukṣuṃ prati tasyāpi pāpmaśabdārthatvānivāraṇācca| athāpi syāt - vedāṃteṣu na pāpmaśabdasya jarāmṛtyuśokasukṛtaduṣkṛtasādhāraṇyaniyamo'sti, na jarā na mṛtyurna śoko na sukṛtaṃ na
duṣkṛtaṃ sarve pāpmāno'tonivartata " ityevaṃ saṅkīrtamavatyeva daharavidyāprakaraṇe " ya eṣa ātmāpahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoka" iti pāpmābhāvamuktvāpi pṛthak jarāmṛtyuśokābhāva pratipādanāditi, tathā'pi "sa eṣa sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhya udita" ityatra pāpmaśabdassukṛtasādhāraṇa eva vācyaḥ, " udeti ha vai sarvebhyaḥ pāpmabhyo ya evaṃ vede " ti sarvapāpodayasya tatkratunyāyānusārividyāphalatvaśravaṇāt, sakṛtanivṛtterapi vidyāphalatvasya caturthādhyāye vakṣyamāṇatvāt. tasmādiha śrūyamāṇaṃ śarīraṃ na karmanimittamiti, akarmanimittātiramaṇīyadivyamaṅgalavigrahavattvena jīve kvacidapi asambhāvitena ayaṃ maṇḍale śrūyamāṇaḥ puruṣo jīvādanyadbrahmaiva, tacca brahma puṇḍarīkākṣatvaliṅgena bhagavān puruṣottama eva| yadyapi yāskena vākyakāreṇa ca " tasya yathā kapyāsa puṇḍarīkameva makṣiṇī " ityasya yathā kapyāsaṃ sūryamaṇḍalaṃ hṛdayapuṇḍarīkaṃ ca tasyopāsanāsthānaṃ, evamakṣiṇī api tasyopāpasanāsthānamiti puṇḍarīkākṣatvaliṅgāsparśyapi arthāntaramuktama, tathāpi vaibhavārthaṃ tatkliṣṭārthāntaravarṇanaṃ| " aeṣaetasminmaṇḍale puruṣaḥ yaścāyandakṣiṇekṣa "nniti bṛhadāraṇyake dakṣiṇasyaivākṣṇastadupāsanāsthānatvaprasiddheḥ puṇḍarīkākṣatvāliṃgasparśyevārthastasyāpyabhimataḥ, tatra tasminnarthe ca kapyāsamityasya tena tredhārtho varṇitaḥ| kaṃ udakaṃ| svaraṃdhraiḥ pibatīti kapiḥ - nālaḥ| tatrāsta iti kapyāsam| abhagrakramikīṭādyaṣṭanālamityekorthaḥ| kapāiti somapāśabdavat prakriyāyāṃ kapīti saptamyantaṃ bhinnaṃ vā padam| ke jale pyāsta iti jalādanuddhṛtamityanyorthaḥ| āsadhātoḥ apipūrvatve " vaṣṭivāgururallopamavāpyorūpasargayo " rityakāralope ca sati pyāstaiti rūpam| kaṃ udakaṃ raśmibhiḥ pibatīti kapissūryaḥ, tasyāsaṃ vikāsyaṃ, ravikaravikasitamityaparorthaiti|
amuṃ trividhamapyarthaṃ gṛhītvā vedārthasaṃgrahe bhāṣyakārairasyavākyasyārthovarṇitaḥ, "gaṃbhīrāṃbhassamūdbhūtasumṛṣṭanālaravikasitapuṇḍarīkadalāmalāyatekṣaṇa " iti| nanu evaṃbhūtovigrahaḥ kathaṃ akarmanimittaityupagantavyaṃ, śarīrāṇāṃ karmajanyatvaniyamasya loke dṛṣṭatvāditi cet, ucyateihaiva pāpātyantābhāvavattvenoktasya puṇyapāpobhayārabdhaṃ śarīrantāvadvaktumaśakyaṃ, puṇyamātrārabdhamucyate cedapi lokadṛṣṭasya puṇyapāpobhayārabdhaniyamasya " naha vai saśarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayorapahatirasti| aśarīraṃ vāva santaṃ na priyāpriye spṛśata " iti śrutyupabṛṃhitasya ullaṅghanamanivāryaṃ| tathāsati " na bhūtasaṅgasaṃsthāno deho'sya paramātmanaḥ|" na tasya prākṛtā mūrtiḥ jagatāmupakārāya na sā karma nimittaje tyādyupabrahmaṇānusāreṇa bhagavato divyamaṅgalavigraho 'prākṛtaḥ akarmanimitta ityeva nyāyavidbhirabhyupagantuṃ yuktam| lokadṛṣṭaniyamādupabrahmaṇasya balavatvāt| nanu uktanyāyaḥ antarādityavākyamātre vaktuṃ śakyaḥ, natu brahmendrādimahāmahimapratipādaka vākyepvapi, nahi hiraṇyagarbhapurandarādilokeṣvapi ādityamaṇḍala iva bhagavaddivyamaṅgalavigrahasannidhānaṃ prasiddhaśrutiṣvasti| ucyate - brahmādiṣvanyatama eva ekasmin kalpe puṇyaviśeṣeṇa evaṃbhūtamaiśvaryaṃ prāpto jagatsṛṣṭyādyapi karotīti bhāṣyavākyaṃ brahmendrādimahimapratipādakaviṣayavākyāntareṣvapi jīvapūrvapakṣatadatiriktabrahmagocarasiddhāntau pravartanīyāvityāśayena pravṛttaṃ na bhavati, kiṃtu yathā'sminviṣayavākye ādityadevatāpadaṃ prāpto jīvaḥ pratipādya iti pūrvapakṣaḥ, evamasminneva viṣayavākye śakraḥ pratipādyaḥ, hiraṇyagarbhaḥ pratipādya ityevaṃ rūpeṇa pūrvapakṣāntaramapi pravartanīyamityāśayena, tadvā kathaṃ saṅgacchate, itthaṃ - " yadadya kaccavṛtrahannudagā abhisūrya| sarvaṃ tadindra te vaśa " iti mantravarṇāt ādityamaṇḍalādhiṣṭhātā saka iti pratīyate| " asadevedamagra āsīttatpadāsīttatsamabhavattadāṇḍaṃ nivartata tatsaṃvatsarasya mātrāmaśayata tannirabhidyata te āṇḍakapāle rajataṃ ca suvarṇaṃ cābhavatāṃ| tadyadrajataṃ seyaṃ pṛthivi yatsuvarṇaṃ sā dyauryajjarāyu te parvatāḥ yadulbaṃ sa megho nīhāro yādhamanayastā nadyo yadvāsteyamudakaṃ sa samudraḥ| atha yattadajāyata so'sāvādiyastaṃ jāyamānaṃ ghoṣā ullūvo'nūdatiṣṭhan sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni sarve ca kāmāstasmāttasyodayanaṃ prati pratyāyanaṃ prati ghoṣā ulūlavo'nuttiṣṭhanti sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni sarve ca kāmā " iti chāndogyavākyasandarbhādāditya jīvo hiraṇyagarbha ityapi pratīyata iti| api ca yecāmuṣmātparāñco lokāsteṣāṃ ceṣṭe devakāmānāṃ ca yaicaitadasmādarvāñco lokāsteṣāṃ ceṣṭe manuṣyakāmānāñce ti sakalalokeśvaratvasakaladevamuṣyābhīvsitatvaphalapradānaśaktatvaśravaṇādapi nāyamakṣyādityāntarvartipuruśojīvaḥ, yadyapi devalokakāmeśvaratvamanuṣyalokakāmeśvaratvañca akṣyādityasthānabhedena vibhajya śrutaṃ tathāpi sthānadvayameka eva puruṣo'dhitiṣṭhati tatprāpnuvat dvividhalokakāmeśvaratvaṃ sarvalokakāmeśvaratvarūpatayā paryavasyati evaṃ ṛksāmageṣṇatvaśravaṇādapi nāyaṃ jīvaḥ, geṣṇaśabdogānavācī, gānaṃ dvividhaṃ, śabdakarmakamekaṃ yathā padyaṃ gāyatīti arthakarmakamekaṃ yathā rājānaṃ gāyatīti, ihārthakarmakagānarūpatvamṛksāmayorvivakṣitaṃ, taccātra sāmarūpagītikriyābhivyaṃgyaṃ
ṛksādhyaguṇiniṣṭhaguṇābhidhāmanarūpastutyupāyatvumṛksāmasādhāraṇaṃ, evañca ādityapuruṣasya stutau ṛksākṣātsādhanaṃ, sāma ṛgabhivyaktidvārā, sāmnāstuvīteti sāmnopi stutasādhanatvaśravaṇāt ityubhayorapi kramikastutusādhanatayā gānatvamityādityapuruṣasya ṛksāmasādhyastutu viṣayāgnīndrasomādirūpatayā sarvātmyasiddhiḥ| yadyapi prastāvādisāmabhaktyavayavarūpe sāgānāṃ parvetyeva prasiddhe sāmnāmapacchede geṣṇākyā teṣāṃ prasiddhā, auÐtthakyākhāye sāmaparvalakṣaṇagrathepi tathaiva vyavahāraḥ, tathāpi kramikastotrasādhanatvasādṛśyāt ṛksāmayorgeṣṇadvayatopacāraḥ, tasmādṛksāmageṣṇatvapratītasarvātmyāsaṃbhavāt, ya āditye tiṣṭhannityādyantaryāmibrāhmaṇe bhadavyapadeśācca, nādityapuruṣojīvaḥ, kiṃtu tadatiriktaṃ brahmetyabhyupagantavyamiti||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
antarmārttāṇḍavarttī tanumadhivasati śmaśrukeśādiyuktāṃ
tādṛkkarmānubandhātkhaluṃ bhavati tanustena tāvatsa jīvaḥ|
evaṃ cānandavallī paripaṭhitajagatkāraṇaṃ jīva eva
syāttasyāmeva tena sphuṭataramabhidā tasya yatpratyapādi||
na khalu sukṛtapāpānyuttaretko'pi jīvo
vidhutasakalapāpo yadyapi syādamartyaḥ||
upaniṣadupagītaḥ pāpmaśabdo'bhidhatte
sukṛtamapi nimittā bhedataḥ pāpatulyam||
tatsarvapāpmodayaliṅgadarśanāddevontarādityagataḥ paraḥ pumān|
aprākṛtaṃ rūpamamuṣya viśrutaṃ vyūhāvatāra pramukhāśca tādṛśāḥ||
antarādhikaraṇam||
evaṃ sadvidyānandavallīpratipādyaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ vastu jīva iti talliṅgavalambana pūrvapakṣāvāntarapeṭikā vṛttā, atha tajjagatkāraṇaṃ vastu ākāśādikameva, na tato'nyajjagatkāraṇaṃ brahmanāmāstīti śrutyavalambanapūrvapakṣamavāntarapeṭikāntaraṃ prastūyate||
ākāśastalliṅgāt||23||
pūrvamādityapuruṣasya vaiśeṣikaliṅgāt vedāntavedyajagatkāraṇatvamuktam| tadayuktam| liṅgataḥ prabalayā śrutyā bhūtākāśasyaiva jagatkāraṇatvādityākṣepeṇotthānadavāntarasaṅgatiḥ| chāṃdogye - " asya lokasya kāgati" riti pṛṣṭavantaṃ dālamyaṃ prati ākāśa iti hovāce ti jaivaleruttaramupakramya śrūyate "sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtānyākāśādevasamutpadyante, ākāśaṃ pratyastaṃ yanti ākāśo hyevaibhyo jyāyānākāśaḥ parāyaṇa"miti| kimayamākāśobhūtākāśaḥ, uta tadariktaḥ paramātmeti bhūtākāśasya śrūyamāṇasarvabhūtotpattisthitilayakāraṇatvayogyatyavāyogyatvābhyāṃ saṃśayaḥ| tatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| bhūtākāśaevāyaṃ, ākāśaśrutestatrarūḍhatvāt, ato " yatovā imāni bhūtāni jāyanta " ityādilakṣaṇalakṣitajagatkāraṇaṃ brahma bhūtākāśaeva, sarvabhūtotpattisthitilayakāraṇatvasya tatroktalakṣaṇasyaiva atraṃ " sarvāṇi ha vā imānī bhūtānī "tyādinā bhūtākāśaparyavasānavarṇanāt, tasya kvacidviśeṣe paryavasānasākāṃkṣatvācca| nanu bhṛguvalyāmeva " ānandādhdyeva khalvimāni bhūtāni jāyanta" ityādinā''nandarūpe viśeṣe paryavasānamuktam| maivaṃ - tatrahi "ko hyevānyātkaḥ prāṇyāt| yadeṣa ākāśaānandonasyā " dityānandaśabda ākāśaśabdasamānādhikaraṇaśśrutaḥ, tataśca viruddhārrathayostayorubhayorapi mukhyatayā sāmānādhikaraṇyāyogāt| ki mānandaśabdo gauṇaḥ uta ākāśaśabdaityatra vinigamanāśravaṇāt| tatra jagatkāraṇatvasya lakṣaṇasya kvaviśeṣe paryavasānamiti nāsti nirṇayaityāśaṅkya ākāśaśrutyā bhūtākāśaeva jagatkāraṇamiti niścite tatra ānandaśabdo gauṇaitīhaiva nirṇayaḥ| nanu acetanasya bhūtākāśasya jagatkāraṇatvaṃ kathamihāśaṅkyate, sadvidyānandavallīśrutacetanaliṅgabalādānandamayaśabdoktaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ vastu cetanaviśeṣaeveti hi prāk sthitaṃ, satyam| sadānandaśabdau cetane'pi yojayituṃ śakyau, liṅgānusāreṇa tathā yojitau, iha tu bhūtākāśe rūḍhākāśaśruti liṅgānusāreṇa yojayituṃ na yuktā| īkṣitṛtvādiliṅgameva "kūlaṃ pipatiṣatī"tyādinyāyena bhūtākāśe yojyamityākṣepaḥ| nanu " ātmana
ākāśassambhūta" ityutpattimatvena śrutasya bhūtākāśasya kathaṃ sarvabhūtasthitilayakāraṇatvarūpalakṣaṇasambandhopapattiḥ, itthaṃ - tatra " ātmana" iti svasmādeva śabdatanmātrarūpa sūkṣmāvasthāt sthūlāvasthobhūtākāśassambhūtaiti tadarthaḥ| ātmaśabdasya śarīrapratisaṃbaṃdhini cetanaiva "mṛdātmakoghaṭaityādiṣu svarūpe'pi prayogāt, ātmā jīve dhṛtau dehe svabhāve paramātmanī "tyādi kośeṣu tasya nānārthatvānuśāsanāt| tasmāt bhūtākāśasyaiva jagatkāraṇatvarūpa brahmalakṣaṇaparyavasānanirṇaye yat sadvidyānandavallī bhṛguvallīṣu jagatkāraṇavastuparaṃ brahmapadaṃ śrutam, tatsarvabhūtakāśaparameva| ataeva "ākāśohavai nāma nāmarūpayornirbahitā te yadantarā tadrabrahme " ti vākyāntare tasmin brahmapadaṃśrūyataiti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - ayamākāśobhūtākāśādanyaḥ paramātmaiva, bhūtākāśāsaṃbhāvitasarvabhūtakāraṇatvaśravaṇāt, taddhisarvapraṇivargakāraṇatvaṃ, "yatovā imāni bhūtāni jāyate yena jātāni jīvaṃtī"ti lakṣaṇavākyārthaikarūpyāt| na ca bhūtākāśasya acetanavargakāraṇatvasaṃbhave'pi cetanakāraṇatvamupapadyate|| na ca pārāyaṇatvaṃ cetanānāṃ paramaprāpyatvamacetanasya apuruṣārthasya heyasya bhūtākāśasya ghaṭate| na vā
sarvatojyāyastvaṃ, taddhikalyāṇaguṇaissarvebhyoniratiśayotkṛṣṭatvaṃ, na ca jagatkāraṇaviśeṣākāṃkṣāyāmākāśaśabdena viśeṣasamarpaṇāt idaṃ sarva mūtākāśaeva yathākathañcitvaktavyamiti vācyaṃ| "sarvāṇi ha ve "ti vākyena hyatra kāraṇatvaṃ na vidhīyate, havaiśabdena prasidyarthakena vākyāṃtaraprasiddhārthānuvādatvadyotanāt, ataḥ purovādākāṃkṣāyāṃ "sanmūlāssomyemāssarvāḥ prajā" ityādivākyameva purovādattvenāvatiṣṭhaiti tatrekṣitṛcvādibhiścetanatvena siddhaṃ sacchabdābhidheyaṃ kāraṇaṃ vasviha ākāśaśabdena parāmṛśyataiti vaktavyatvāt, "ātmana ākāśassaṃbhūta " ityatra ātmaśabdasya śarīrapratisaṃbaṃdhini cetane prasiddhiprācuryeṇa "tasyaiṣaeva śarīraātme"ti śārīratvaśravaṇena ca cetanaparatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt, svasmādeva ākāśautpannaityarthānupapattyā bhūtākāśasya sarvabhūtakāraṇatvānvayāyogyatvācca, bhūtākāśoyaṃ, kiṃtu tadatiriktaṃ brahmaiveti tasminneva ākāśaśabdasya āsamantātkāśata ityādirūpeṇa yogavṛttirāstheyā, purovādānusāreṇa anuvāde rūḍhibhaṅgasya adoṣatvāt, yogyārthaśrutimūlaliṅgāt ayogyārthapadarūḍhibhaṅgasya gaṅgāghoṣādau dṛṣṭatvācceti||
saṅgrahakārikāḥ||
avyaktaṃ jīvo vā mābhūjjagato'sya kāraṇamathāpi|
ākāśassyādeva ṅāndogye sa hi tathā''mnātaḥ||
ātmana ākāśa iti svātmana evetyananyajanitoktā|
prathamaśrutyanusārātatrānandaśrutirgauṇī|
maivaṃ chāndogyutiranuvādaḥ khalu ha vai śabdāt|
satu satkāraṇameva pratipannaṃ sevate purovadāt||
iti ākāśādhikaraṇam||8||
ata eva prāṇaḥ||24||
chāndogye kasyacidrājaḥ kṛtau samāgatasya uṣasteḥ prastāvabhaktidevatāpraśne tāmajānānena prastotrā "katamā sā devate" ti pṛṣṭasya uṣasteḥ "prāṇa iti hovāce"ti uttaramupakramya śrūyate "sarvāṇi ha vā imāni bhūtāni prāṇamevābhisaṃviśaṃti prāṇamabhyujjihate saiṣā devatā prastāvamanvāyatte " ti atideśādhikaraṇasyāsya pūrvādhikaraṇavat pūrvottarapakṣau| prāṇaśruteḥ prāṇavāyureva sarvabhūtalayotpattikāraṇamiti prāpte| ha vai śabdena tadanuvādyatvadyotanāt purovādānusāreṇa yogyatānusāreṇa ca prāṇavāyvatiriktaḥ paramātmaiva sarvabhūtalayotpattikāraṇabhūtaḥ prāṇaiti| atideśasya tu sarvasya bhūtajātasya prāṇādhīna sthitipravṛttyādidarśanāt prasiddhaeva vāyuḥ sarvabhūtalayotpattikāraṇatāmarhati, sākṣācca bṛhadāraṇyake "yadā vai puruṣassvapiti prāṇantarhi vāgapyeti prāṇañcakṣuḥ prāṇaṃ śrotraṃ prāṇaṃ manaḥ sayadā prabuddhyate prāṇādevā'tha punarjāyata" iti svāpaprabodhayoḥ prāṇavāyussarvabhūtalayotpattisthānamiti śravaṇāccetyadhikā''śaṅkānirāsaḥ prayojanam| tannirāsastu cetanavargaṃ prati prāṇavāyoḥ kāraṇatvā'yogāt, ānaṃdavallyāṃ cetanaviśeṣotpannādākāśādutpannatvena śrutasya tasya sarvācetanakāraṇatvāyogācca, na tasya sarvabhūtalayotpattisthānatvanirdeśārhatā| ata eva bṛhadāraṇyakoktāprāṇo'pi paramātmaiveti||
saṅgrahakārikā||
evaṃ tasyāṃ prāṇavākyādanūkteḥ prāṇepitasyānnaivatatkāraṇatvaṃ|
prāṇādhīnaṃ dṛśyatāṃ dehamātraṃ naivaṃ bhūtaṃ jīvargādidṛṣṭam||
iti prāṇādhikaraṇam||
evaṃ kevalaśrutyavalambanapūrvapakṣāvāntarapeṭikāvṛttā - atha kāraṇatvākṣepakaliṅgānugṛhītāśrutyavalambanamavāntarapeṭikāntaraṃ prastūyate||
jyotiścaraṇāśridhānāt|| 25||
chandośridhānānneti cenna tathā cetorpaṇanigamāttathā hi darśanam||26||
bhūtādipādavyapadeśopapatteścaivam||27||
upadeśabhedānneti cennobhayasminnapyavirodhāt ||28||
chandogāssamāmananti| "atha yadataḥ paro divo jyotirdīpyate| viśvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣu sarvataḥ pṛṣṭhavanuttameṣūttameṣu lokeṣvidaṃ vāvatadyadidamasminnantaḥpuruṣe jyoti"riti| atra ākāśaprāṇavākyayoḥ sa vāyvākāsasarvabhūtakāraṇatvaśravaṇavat iha sajyotiṣkasarvakāryakāraṇatvāśravaṇena virodhābhāvāt, virodhabhāve śrutyavalaṃbanāyogāt, prasiddhamādityādyeva jyotirastviti ākṣepeṇotthānādavāntarasaṅgatiḥ| nanu yadīhaprasiddhamādityādyeva jyotiriti pūrvapakṣaḥ kriyate, kastena siddhāntasyopamardaśśaṅkito bhavati| na hi jyotiriha jagatkāraṇaṃ śrūyate, yenādityādyeva jagatkāraṇaṃ na tato'nyajjagatkāraṇaṃ brahmāstīti siddhāntopamardaśśaṅkitassyāt| athocyeta - niratiśayadīptiyoga iha śrūyamāṇaḥ kāraṇatvākṣepakaṃ liṅgaṃ, "na tatra sūryo bhāti" "tasya bhāsā sarvamidaṃ vibhātī"ti prakaraṇe "tathākṣarātsaṃbhavatīhaviśva"miti niratiśayadīptiyuktavastunaḥ kāraṇatvaśravaṇāditi| yadi sūryacandrādyabhibhāvakaṃ prākṛtatejorūpaṃ muṇḍake śrutaṃ, jagatkāraṇaṃ vastviha niratiśayadīptiyuktajyotiḥ jyotiśśabdārtthaḥ pūrvapakṣiṇā gṛhyate, kathantarhi ādityādikamiha jyotiriti pūrvapakṣaḥ kriyate, nahyādityādireva ādityādyabhibhāvakajyotirityetaddhaṭate| ucyate - " na tatra sūryo bhātī "ti mantreṇa sūryādyabhibhāvakatvavannocyate, kiṃtu sūryādyabhāsyatvaṃ, " na tadbhāsayate sūryo na śaśāṃko na pāvaka " iti gītāvacanenaiva sūryādyabhāsyatvañca sūryāderapi saṃbhavatīti pūrvapakṣyāśayaḥ| iha jyotiḥ kimādityādi uta tadatiriktaṃ brahmeti prasiddhibhūtacaraṇatvābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| ādityādyeva jyotiḥ, prasiddhyilaṅghanāyogāt, "idaṃ vā va tadyaditamasminnantaḥ puruṣe jyoti "riti jāḍharaikyopadeśācca| prākṛtajyoyiṣvevahi tatsājātyābhiprāyeṇa tadaikyopadeśoghaṭate| naca "viśvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣu sarvataḥ pṛṣṭheṣvanuttameṣu lokeṣvi"ti śrutasarvottarātyuttamalokaprasṛmaradīptimatvamādityāderna ghaṭata iti vācyaṃ| viśvataḥ pṛthivīlokāt sarvatontarikṣalokācca uparitaneṣvatyuttameṣu dyulokeṣvādityasyāpi raśmiprasṛtisadbhāvāt, tasmānniratiśayadīptirūpaliṅgākṣiptaṃ jagatkāraṇatvamādityādijyotiṣa eveti na tatonyajjagatkāraṇaṃ brahma nāmāstītyevaṃ prāptesiddhāntaḥ - jyotiriha prasiddhajyotiratiriktaṃ brahmaiva, pūrvatra " pādo'sya sarvābhūtāni tripādasyāmṛtaṃdivī" ti sarvabhūtapādatvābhidhānāt tasya ca prasiddhajyotiṣyasaṃbhavāt, pūrvatra yasya sarvabhūtapādatvamabhihitaṃ tadeveha jyotiśśabdena parāmṛśyataiti dyusaṃbandhapratyabhijñānenāvagamāt| jāṭharaikyopadeśastu phalāya tadātmakatvānusandhāvidhānārthaḥ| "tadetadṛṣṭañca śrutañcetyupāsīta cakṣuṣyaśśruto bhavatī "tyagre tathā vidhānadarśanāditi na tena tasya prākṛtajyotiṣṭvasiddhiḥ| nanu pūrvatra "gāyatrī vā idaṃ sarva" mityupakramya "saiṣā catuṣpade "ti tasyāścatuṣpadatvamabhidhāya " tadetaddacābhyanūktaṃ tāvānasya mahimā tatojyāyāṃśca pūruṣaḥ pādo'sya sarvābhūtāni tripādasyāmatandivī"tyuktārthe pramāṇatvena asya mantrasyodāhṛtatvāt gāyatrīviṣayo'yaṃ mantraḥ, ato naitadatena sarvabhūtapādatvābhidhānena tathābhūtaparamātmasiddhiriti cet| maivaṃ - na gāyatrīśabdena chandomātramihābhidhīyate, chandasassarvabhūtapādatvāsaṃbhavāt, apitu brahmaṇi gāyatrīsādṛśyānusandhīnamihaphalāyopadiśyate, astica " pādosya sarvābhūtāni tripādasyāmṛtaṃdivī"ti catuṣpādatvenāmnātasya brahmaṇaścatuṣpadayā gāyatryā sādṛśśaṃ, asti hi catuṣpadāpi gāyatrī| "indraśśacīpatiḥ| valena pīḍitaḥ| duścyavano vṛṣā| samitsu sā sahi" riti tathā hyanyatrāpi gāyatrīsādṛśyāt chando'bhidhāyi śabdaḥ arthāntare prayujyamāno dṛśyate| yathā saṃvargavidyāyāṃ "te vā ete pañcānye pañcānye daśa santa " ityārabhya "saiṣā virāḍi" tyuktaṃ, atra hi vāyūssūryaścandro'gnirāpa ityete pañca, prāṇo vākcakṣuśśrotraṃ mana ityete pañca, ityeteṣu daśasu saṃkhyāsāmyāt virāḍ chandorūpatvamuktaṃ, daśākṣaraṃ hi virāḍchandaḥ| kiñca bhūtapṛthivīśarīrahṛdayāni nirdiśya "saiṣā catuṣpade " ti bhūtādicatuṣṭayapādatvavyapadeśādapi gāyatriśabdoktaḥ paramātmaiva| chaṃdastathātvānupapatteḥ| nanu astu yatkiñcitsarvabhūtapādaṃ
vastupūrvatrābhihitaṃ, tathāpi na tasyeha pratyabhijñānaṃ, "divi" "diva" iti nirdeśabhedāditi cenna| nirdeśabhede'pi arthavirodhābhāvāt, vṛkṣāgrasthite śyene vṛkṣātparaśśyenaityapi vyapadeśadarśanāt, tasmāt "tāvānasya mahime" ti maṃtrapratyabhijñāpitapuruṣasūktapratipādyaṃ
"ādityavarṇe tamasastu pāra" iti niratiśayadīptiyuktadivyamaṅgalavigrahavatvena tatra pratipannaṃ yatparamapuruṣākhyaṃ paraṃ brahma tadevātra niratiśayadīptiyuktaṃ jyotiriti nirdiśyate| nanu, puraṣasūktenaiva sakalacetanācetanaprapañcavilakṣaṇaḥ tasya sarvasyāpi kāraṇabhūtaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ paraṃbrahmeti siddhyatīti kimarthoyamayogavyavacchadārthaḥ pādaḥ| ucyate bahuṣu śrutyantareṣu prāṇakāśajyotirindrādiśabdaiḥ kāraṇavastunirdeśe jāgrāta puruṣasūktamātreṇa tatpratipādyaṃ paramapuruṣo jagatkāraṇaṃ brahmetyetat na dṛḍhīkaraṇārtha evāyamayogavyavacchedārtho vicāraḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
paraṃ jyotissāmniśrutamudaravahnyātmakatayā
paraṃ jyotiṣṭvaṃ ca prathitamiha na kāraṇagatam|
ato bhānurliṅgādbhavatu jagatāṃ kāraṇamiti
pravāsyā śaṅkeyaṃ puruṣacaraṇoktyā viśadayā||

prāṇastathānugamāt ||29||
na vakturātmopadeśāditi cedadhyātmasambandhabhūmāhyasmin||30||
śāstraddaṣṭyātūpadeśo vāmadevavat||31||
jīvamukhyaprāṇaliṅgānneti cennopāsātraividhyādāśrita
tvādiha tadyogāt||32||
kauṣītakināmupaniṣadi "pratardano havai daivodāsirindrasya priyaṃ dhāmopajagāme " tyupakramya "varaṃ vṛṇīṣve "tyuktavati indre "tvameva varaṃ vṛṇīṣva yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyasa" iti manuṣyāya mahyaṃ hitatamaṃ varaṃ tvameva jānāsi, atastādṛśaṃ varaṃ tvameva vicitya mahyandehīti pratardanena prārthitasyendrasya vacanaṃ śrūyate - "sahovāca prāṇo'smi prajñātmā taṃ māmāyuramṛtamupāsve "ti| tatra saṃśayaḥ - kimiha hitatamopāsanakarmabhūtaindraprāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭaḥ prasiddhendrajīvaḥ, uta tadatiriktaḥ paramātmeti| tatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ - "prāṇākāśavākyayostattadvākyaeva prāṇākāśaśrutivirodhisarvabhūtakāraṇatvaśravaṇavat jyotirvākye prāk jyotiḥśrutivirodhisarvabhūtapādatvaśravaṇavacca neha svavākye tataḥ pūrvaṃ vā indraśrutivirodhiśravaṇamasti, pratyuta indravākyopakramaeva iṃdrasya hitatamaśabdoktāmṛtatvaprāptyupāyopāsanākarmatvaśravaṇamasti, tathābhūtopāsanākarmatvañca jagatkāraṇatvavyāptaṃ liṃgamiti " tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvannavimokṣye atha saṃpatsya " iti sadvidyāvākyāvagataṃ, tasmādindrākhyo jīvaviśeṣaeva jagatkāraṇaṃ, na tadatiriktaḥ, paramātmā'stī"ti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - ayamindraprāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭo na jīvamātraṃ, apitu jīvādarthāntarabhūtaṃ brahma "sa eṣa prāṇaeva prajñā''tmā''naṃdo'jaro'mṛta " iti indraprāṇaśabdābhyāṃ prastutasya sakalajīvāsaṃbhāvitadharmanirdeśāt| nanu upakrame "māmeva vijānīhī"ti indrassāvadhāraṇaṃ svātmānameva upāsyamupadideśa, tadupapattyarthe "tvāṣṭramahanaṃ arunmukhānyatīn sālāvṛkebhyaḥ prāyacchaṃ bahvīssandhāatikramya divi prāhādīnatṛṇahaṃ antarikṣe paulomān pṛthivyāṃ kālakañjān tasya me na lomaca mīyata" iti tvāṣṭravadhādibhikṛtyaiḥ svasya lomā'pi nahisyata iti mahāmahimayattvena svātmānaṃ tuṣṭāva| tasmādupakrame jīvaviśeṣa upāsya iti nirṇaye sati tadanusāreṇa aupasaṃhīrikaṃ ānandājarāmṛtatvaśravaṇaṃ stāvakatayā netavyamiticet maivaṃ| sakalajīvāsaṃbhāvitādhyātmasaṃbandhabhūyastvaṃ hi asmin prakaraṇe dṛśyate| tathāhi - prathamaṃ " tvameva caraṇaṃ vṛṇīṣva yaṃ tvaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manyasa " iti pṛṣṭavantaṃ prati "māmeva vijānīhi etadevāhaṃ manuṣyāya hitatamaṃ manye yanmāṃ vijīnīyā " diti paramātmāsādhāraṇamokṣasādhanopasānakarmatvaṃ indraprāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭasyocyate, tadupapādakañca puṣkarapalāśavākyapratipannaṃ sarvapāpāśleṣaphalamupavarṇyate "sayomāṃ vijānīyāt nāsya kena karmaṇā loko mīyate na mātṛvadhena na pitṛvadhena na steyena na bhrūṇahtyaye "ti| athāpi syāt indrasyaiva evaṃbhūtaphalapradānasāmarthyamastviti hi pūrvapakṣaḥ kriyate - "indrorājā jagatoya īśe indrorājā
jagataścarṣaṇīnāṃ| indroyātovasitasya rājā na tvāvāmanyo divyo napārthivo najātonajaniṣyata " ityādiṣu mahāmahimatvañca
tasya śrūyate, kā tasyāmṛtatvasādhanopāsanākarmatvānupapattiriti| tathā'pi "tadyathā rathasyāreṣu nemirarpitā nābhāvarāvarpitāevamevaitābhūtamātrāḥ prajñāmātrāsvarpitāḥ prajñāmātrāḥ prāṇe'rpitā " iti bhūtamātrāśabdoditānāmacetanānāṃ prajñāmātrāśabdoditeṣu cetaneṣu pratiṣṭhitatvamabhidhāya yatteṣāmapi prāṇe pratiṣṭhitatvamabhidhīyate tat " prāṇo'smī" tyādeḥ prasiddhendrātiriktaparamātmaviṣayatāmantareṇa nopapadyate| nahi devatā'dhikaraṇanyāyena muktyarthīndraḥ kañcidupāsanena aprasādya svayaṃ mukiṃta labheta, ataḥ prasiddhendrātiriktaparamātmopāsanameva indreṇa "māmupāsve"tyupadiṣṭaṃ, tena svakriyamāṇamuktyarthopāsanāvāntaraphalabhūtaḥ sarvapāpāśleṣaḥ "triśīrṣāṇa "mityādinopanyasya ityeva śliṣṭataraṃ| evañca "sana sādhunā karmaṇā bhūyānnoevāsādhunā kanīyān eṣahyeva sādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamebhyo lokebhyo unninīṣati eva uevāsādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamadho ninīṣate eṣalokādhipatireṣa lokapāla eṣa lokeśa" iti maddhyagatanyapi sakalajīvāsaṃbhāvitasādhvasādhukarmaprayukta bhūyastvakanīyastvarīhityādivacanāni saṅgacchate| tasmānmāmupāsveti upāsanakarmatayā upadiṣṭaḥ indrajīvātiriktaḥ paramātmaiva| kathantarhīdreṇa māmupāsvetyupadiṣṭaṃ "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā apahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇa" ityādiśāstradṛṣṭyā svāntaryāmiṇa upāsyatvābhiprāyeṇa, yathā'hamādipadānāṃ tattaccharīrakaparamātmaparyatatvaṃ jānatā vāmadevena svāṃtaryāmiṇaḥ svātmyābhiprāyeṇa ahaṃ manusūryādisarvātmabhāva upadiṣṭaḥ| tathāhi śrūyate - "taddhaitatpaśyan ṛṣirvāmadevaḥ pratipede ahaṃ manurabhavaṃ sūryaśce"tyādi| nanu kathamiha adhyātmasaṃbandhabhūyastvaṃ, "navācaṃ vijijñāsīta vaktāraṃ vidyāt, triśīrṣāṇaṃ tvāṣṭramahanaṃ, yāvaddhyasmin śarīre prāṇo vasati tāvadāyu" rityādijīvamukhyaprāṇaliṅgānāmatra sattvāditi cet ucyate - paramātmana eva trividhamihopāsanaṃ vivakṣitaṃ| tatra "ānando'jaro'mṛta" iti svarūpeṇopāsanaṃ, jīvaliṃgavākyaiḥ cidviśiṣṭayopāsanaṃ, mukhyaprāṇaliṃgairacidviśiṣṭatayopāsanañceti| anyatrāpi trividhāmupāsanaṃ dṛśyate, yathā ānandavallyāṃ - "satyaṃ jñānamanaṃtaṃ brahme"tyādau svarūpeṇopāsanaṃ "tatsṛṣṭvā tadevānuprāviśat tadanupraviśya saccatyaccābhavat niruktañcāniruktañca nilayanañcānilayanañca vijñānañcāvijñānañce" tyādiṣu cetanaṃ viśiṣṭatvena acetanaviśiṣṭatvena copāsanaṃ, evamihāpi paramātmanasrividhopāsanasamāśrayaṇamupapadyata iti sarvamanavadyam|
saṃgrahakārikā|
syādiṃdrastvihakāraṇaṃ hitatamopāsaikakarmatvato
naitatsādhvajarāmṛtādyanugamādagre taduktyātmakāt|
indrasya svaśarīrakeśvaradṛśā māmityupāsāspade
nirdeśo ghaṭate tato'sya cidacidbhinnaṃ paraṃ kāraṇam||
iti indraprāṇādhikaraṇam||
prathama pāde samastacidacidvalakṣaṇaśrīpuruṣottamākhyavedāntavedyajagatkāraṇāstitvaṃ prasādhitaṃ, tathā'pi kānicidvedāntavākyāni cidacidantarbhūtavastuviśeṣapratipādakāni dṛśyaṃta ityāśaṅkānirāsaḥ kriyate| kiñca janmādisūtrai brahma kāraṇameva brahmaiva kāraṇamiti ayogānyayogavyavacchedau lakṣaṇasya asambhāvātivyāptiparihārāya vivakṣitau, tatra ayogavyavacchedaḥ prathamapādena siddhaḥ anyayogavyavacchedārthasripādyārambhaiti ca vibhāgaḥ| tatra spaṣṭatarajīvaliṅgakāni vākyāni prathame pāde vicāritāni, aspaṣṭajīvaliṃgakāni dvitīye vicāryante, spaṣṭajīvaliṅgakāni tṛtīye, pradhānādipratipādanacchāyānusārīṇi caturtha iti prāyiko'yaṃ pādārthavibhāgaḥ||
