chandogāssamāmanaṃti - "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalāniti śāṃtaupāsīta atha khalu kratumayaḥ puruṣo yathākraturasmin loke puruṣo bhavati tathetaḥ pretya bhavati sakratuṃ kurvīta manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīro bhārūpaḥ satyasaṅkalpa " ityādi| atra "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalāniti śāṃtaupāsīte"tyanena sarvātmakaṃ brahma śāṃtassannupāsīteti vidhīyate, "sakratuṃ kurvīte"ti tasyaivopāsanasya manomayatvādyagrimaguṇavidhānārtho'nuvādaḥ| nanu utpattiśiṣṭasārvātmyaguṇāvaruddhe kathaṃ manomayatvādiguṇāṃtaravidhānaṃ ghaṭate| athocyate - "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalāni"tyaṃtaṃ bhinnaṃ vākyaṃ, sarvaśabdayogena "sarvaṃ khalvi"tyasya "śāntaupāsīte"tyetadekavākyatvanivāraṇāditi| naitadyuktaṃ| tathā'pi "śāntaupāsīte"tyatra anyārthasannihitasya brahmaṇa upāsyatveneva tathā bhūtasya sārvātmyasyāpi upāsyaguṇatvena anvayānivāraṇāt, śatapathe agnirahasyabrāhmaṇe "satyaṃ brahmetyupāsīte"ti śāṇḍilyavidyāvidhau upāsyabrahmaṇaḥ satyatvaviśeṣaṇaśravaṇena atrāpi tadaikārthyena tādātmyaguṇavidhānaucityācca||
ubhayoraikārthyañca "saccatyaccābhava"diti śrutyanusāreṇa satyaśabdasya parokṣāparokṣātmakasakalavastvātmakatvaparatayā sūpapādaṃ| evañca kathaṃ sārvātmyaguṇāvaruddhe manomayatvādiguṇāntaravidhānamiti cet ucyate - utpattiśiṣṭe guṇaḥ svavirodhinamevotpannaśiṣṭaṃ na sahate, na tu guṇāṃtaramātraṃ| na hi somadravyamutpattiśiṣṭamiti dravyāṃtaramiva devatādiguṇāṃtaramapi pratikṣipati| nanu tathā'pi upāsyabrahmaguṇatayā manomayatvādividhānamityetanna yujyate, "sakratuṃ kurvīta manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīra " ityādisāmānādhikaraṇyena manomayatvādīnāmupāsakaviśeṣaṇatvapratīteriticet, ucyate - agnirahasye samānaprakaraṇe manomayatvādikamupāsyaviśeṣaṇamāmnāyate| tatrahi - "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalāniti śāṃtaupāsīte" tisthāne satyatvaviśiṣṭabrahmopāsanāṃ vidhāya "atha khalu kratumayo'yaṃ puruṣaḥ sayāvakraturayaṃ asmāllokātpraiti evaṃkraturihāmuṃ lokaṃ pretyabhisaṃbhavatī"ti tatkratunyāyapradarśanapūrvakaṃ "saātmānamupāsīta manomayaṃ prāṇaśarīraṃ bhārūpamākāśātmanaṃ kāmarūpiṇaṃ manojavasaṃ satyasaṅkalpaṃ satyadhṛtiṃ sarvagandhaṃ sarvarasaṃ sarvāanudiśaḥ prabhūtaṃ sarvamidamabhyāttamavākamantadara" miti manomayatvādīnāmupāsyaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ spaṣṭamāmnātaṃ| nanu tadanusāreṇāpyatra prathamāṃtānāṃ manomayādipadānāṃ "kratuṃ kurvīte "tyatra kathamupāsyaguṇasamarpakatayā anvaya upapādanīyaḥ, prathamāṃtānāṃ ṣaṣṭhyantatayā vipariṇāmenopapādanīyaḥ| yadvā "kratuṃ kurvīte"tyanūditasarvātmabrahmopāsanāṃ prati prakārasamarpako "manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīra" ityādiḥ etamitaḥ pretyabhisaṃbhavitā'smī"tyantaḥ, tadanaṃtaramitikaraṇabalāt| anena prakāreṇa kratuṃ kurvītetyanvayapratīteḥ, tathā'nvaye satyeva "eṣamaātmā'ntarhṛdaya" iti vākyasāmañcasyācca| tasyopāsanāprakārābhinayaparatvaṃ vināsvataṃtraśrutivākyatve "ma" ityasyānvayasāmañjasyābhāvāt, tathāsatyevaca "eṣamaātmā'ntahṛdaya" iti vākyānāṃ triḥ pāṭhasya "sarvakarmā sarvakāma" ityādīnāṃ dviḥ pāṭhasyacopapatteḥ| tathāsati hi "prāyaṇīyaṃ prathamamahaḥ caturviṃśaṃ dvitīyaṃ catvāro'bhiplavāṣṣaḍahāḥ ekaḥ pṛṣṭhyaṣṣaḍahaḥ samāsaḥ sadvitīyaḥ satṛtīyaḥ sacaturthaḥ sapaṃcamaḥ trayo'bhiplavāṣṣaḍahāekaḥ pṛṣṭhyaṣṣaḍaha" ityādau gavāmayanasatragatāhaḥ kalṛptivākye abhiplavaṣaḍahādiśabdānāṃ asakṛtpāṭhasya tatratatrasthāne ṣaḍahādyanuṣṭhānābhyāsārthatveneva tatratatrasthāne hṛdayāṃtarvartitvānusandhānābhyāsārthatvena asakṛtpāṭhaḥ sāphalyamaśnute, teṣāṃ vākyānāmupāsye tattaddharmasadbhāvamātrabodhakaśrutivākyatve tadvaiphalyaṃ syāt| evaṃca vihitopāsanānuvādena manomayatvādyanekaguṇavidhāne vākyabheda ityapi śaṅkā nirastā bhavati| teṣāmitikaraṇoktena prakārabhāvenaikena rūpeṇa vidheyatvāt, vedheyatā'vacchedakarūpaikye vidheyapadārthabhūtarūpāṃtarabhedena vākyabhedāprasaṅgāt| ataeva "gauścāśvaścāśvataraśca gardabhaścājāścāvayavaśca vrīhiyavāśca tilāśca māṣāśca tasya dvādaśaśataṃ, dakṣiṇeḥ "ti jyotiṣṭomadakṣiṇāvidhivākye gavādīnāṃ rūpabhede satyapi teṣāṃ dakṣiṇātvenaikena rūpeṇa vidheyatvānnavākyabhedaiti gavātmakaikadakṣiṇāvidhānāṅgīkāreṇa vākyabhedaḥ parihṛtaḥ| yadvā śrutāvitiśabdaḥ kramarūpaṃ manomayatvādīnāṃ prakāramācaṣṭemanomayatvādyanusandhānakrameṇa kratuṃ kurvīteti| tathā ca vitatirūpa ekaḥ krama evātra vidheya iti na
vākyabhedaḥ, manomayatvādīnāmupāsyaguṇānāṃ vidhānaṃ tu tatkramavidhānādākṣepato labhyate, yathā "viśvajitsarvapṛṣṭo'tirātra" ityatra pṛṣṭhagatasarvatāvidhānāt pṛṣṭhasāmnāṃ rathantarādīnāmityalaṃ prapañcena| evaṃ "upāsīte"tyante vākye sarvātmakabrahmopāsanāvidhiḥ, kratuvākye manomayatvādyupāsyaguṇavidhiriti sthite saṃśayaḥ| kimiha brahmaśabdena upāsyatayā nirdiṣṭo jīvaḥ, uta paraṃbrahmeti, tatra jīva iti pūrvapakṣaḥ| tasyaiva sarvapadasāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśopapatteḥ, sarvapadanirdiṣṭaṃhi brahmādistaṃbaparyataṃ jagat,
brahmādibhāvaśca jīvasyaiva anādikarmapravāhavipākānusāriṇā janmabhedenopapadyate, parasya tu brahmaṇaḥ samastamaṅgalaguṇākarasya nirastasamastaheyasambandhasya atiheyasarvabhāvo nopapadyate| naca paricchinne jīve brahmaśabdāyogaḥ, idaṃ brahmāgacchatīti śrutyantare tatrāpi tatprayogāt, tadvyāvartanārtha paramātmani parabrahmeti tatra tatra saviśeṣanirdeśadarśanācca| jīvasya ca nirmuktasaṃsāropādheḥ muktidaśāyāṃ dharmajñānavikāsena bṛhattvañcāstīti tadīyakarmanimittatvājjagajjanmasthitilayānāṃ "tajjalāni"ti hetunirdeśo'pyupapadyate| tasmājjīva eva atra upāsyatayā nirdiṣṭaṃ brahmeti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - "sarve khalvida"miti nirdiṣṭe sarvasmin jagati tadatmatayā vidhīyamānaṃ brahma parameva brahma| na jīvaḥ| jagajjanmādihetukatādāmyasya prasiddhatvenopadeśāt| brahmaṇo jātatvāt brahmaṇi layavattvāt brahmādhīnajīvanatvācca hetoḥ brahmātmakaṃ khalu sarvamidaṃ jagadityukte hi yasmājjagajjanmasthitilayā vedānteṣu prasiddhā tadatra brahmeti pratīyate| taccā parameva brahmā, "yatovā imāni bhūtāni jāyanta" ityādiśruteḥ| tataśca brahmādhīnajanmajīvanalayasya sthūlacidacidvasturūpasya sarvasya jagataḥ tadapṛthaksidhyā taccharīratvena tadātmabhūtatvalakṣaṇaṃ tattādātmyaṃ tasya sakalaheyapratyanīkakalyāṇaguṇākaratvena na virudhyate, prakārabhūtaśarīragatadoṣāṇāṃ prakāriṇyātmanyaprasaṅgāt| jīvasya tu sarvatādātmyanirdeśo nopapadyate| tasya pratiśarīraṃ bhinnasya anyonyatādātmyāsambhavāt| tasmādiha brahmaśabdena nirdiṣṭaṃ parameva brahmā| nanvevaṃ sati kathaṃ "manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīraṃ"ityanayorūpāsyaguṇatayā brahāmaṇyanvayaḥ, manaḥprāṇayorupakaraṇopakaraṇibhāvena jīvasambandhasyaiva suprasiddhatvāditicet ucyatesāṃsārikamanaḥprāṇasambandho'tra nārthatvena grahītuṃ yogya, tasya heyasya muktidaśāyāṃ tyaktavyasya anupadoktatatkratunyāyavirodhena upāsyaguṇtavāyogāt| tasmānmuktadaśāvikasitadharmajñānarūpaviśuddhamanogrāhyatvaṃ manomayatvaṃ, sarvaprāṇiprāṇadhārakatvaṃ prāṇaśarīratvaṃ, niratiśayadīptiyuktadivyamaṅgalavigrahavattvaṃ bhārūpatvamityādirbhāṣyoktaeva teṣāmartho grāhyaḥ| evaṃ ca evaṃrūpā manomayatvādayo jīva eva nopapadyate| tathā "etamitaḥ pretya abhisambhavitā'smī"ti prakṛtasya brahmaṇaḥ upāsakaprāpyatayā nirdeśaḥ, "eṣama ātmā'ntarhṛdaya" iti ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣṭādupāsakāt prathamāntena tasya vyatirekanirdeśaśca, "īśvarassarvabhūtānāṃ hṛddeśe'rjuna tiṣṭhatī"tyādismṛtiśca, hṛdayadeśāsthitamupāsyaṃ brahmajīva iti pakṣe nopapadyate| nanu brahmeti pakṣe'pi "eṣama ātmā'ntarhṛdaye'ṇīyān vrīhervā yavādve"ti alpaukastvāṇīyastvavyapadeṣo nopapadyate| maivaṃ, dhyānārthamaṇīyastvamupadiśyate, na punaraṇīyastvamevāsya svarūpamiti| vyomavaccaiṣa vyapadeśo draṣṭavyaḥ| ataeva "jyāyān pṛthivyā jyāyānantarikṣājjyāyān divo jyāyānebhyo lokebhya" iti tasya svābhāvikaṃ mahattvamapi śrūyate| evaṃ ca yasyopādhyanuktyā svābhāvikaṃ mahattvamavagam, tasyopādhyuktipūrvakamucyamānaṃ paricchinnatvamaivopādhikamiti evamaucityāt| iha alpatvopādheḥ hṛdayāyatanatvasyaiva jyāyastvopādheraśravaṇāt upāsanārthamaupādhikamaṇīyastvaṃ svābhāvikaṃ jyāyastvañca vivakṣitamiti na kaścidvirodhaḥ| evaṃ tarhi jīvasyeva parasyāpi śarīrāntarvarticetanatvāviśeṣāt tatprayuktasukhaduḥkhabhogaḥ prāpnuyāditi cenna| hetuviśeṣāt, nahi śarīrāntarvarticetanatvamātraṃ sukhaduḥkhabhogahetuḥ api tu karmapāravaśyamapi, tattvapahatapāpmanaḥ paramātmano na śakyaśaṅkaṃ, tasmādihopāsyaṃ brahma parameva brahmeti siddham||
saṅgrahakārikāḥ||
sarvaṃ khalvityatra sārvātmyamuktaṃ heyaṃ yasya brahma tajjīva eva|
paryāyeṇānādiṃsārabhājastasyahyetadyujyate no parasya||
maivaṃ khaluprayogāddhi prasiddhavadanūdyate|
sārvātmyaṃ kāraṇatvotthaṃ tacca brahmaṇi viśrutam||
ye'nye manomayatvādyā vapākyaśeṣaśrutā guṇāḥ|
brahmaṇyeva ca te sarve yujyante na tu bhoktari||
iti sarvatraprasiddhyadhikaraṇam||1||
attā carācaragrahaṇāt||9||
prakaraṇācca||10||
guhāṃpraviṣṭāvātmanau hi taddarśanāt||11||
viśeṣaṇācca||12||
kaṭhavallīṣu śrūyate - "yasya brahma ca kṣatrañcobhe bhavata odanaḥ| mṛtyuryasyopasecanaṃ ka itthāveda yatra sa " iti| asya yasmin prakāre sthitaḥ yatprakāraviśiṣṭaḥ| taṃ prakāraṃ ko vedetyarthaḥ| atra odanopasecanasambandhāt yasyeti ṣaṣṭhīnirdiṣṭaḥ tayorattā pratīyate, sa kiṃ jīvaḥ, uta paramātmeti attṛtvasya karmaphalabhoktṛtvajagadupasaṃhartṛtvānyatararūpatvānirddhāraṇāt saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - odanopasecanasambandhāt attā bhoktaiva| naca mṛtyorūpasaṃhārakāriṇaḥ upasecanatvarūpaṇāt attā saṃhartā pratīyata iti vācyam| jīvasya nityatvena mṛtyuvaśyatvābhāvāt mṛtyurjīvasya vidheyaprāya ityāśayena mṛtyūpasecanavādopapatteriti| siddhāntastu - mṛtyūpasecanavādopapatteriti| siddhāntastu - mṛtyūpasecanavādo mṛtyorabādhakatvābhiprāyo na vaktuṃ yuktaḥ, upasecanaśabdasya gauṇatve'pi svayamadyamānatve satyanyādanahetutvena viśeṣākāreṇa gauṇatve sambhavati abādhatvarūpeṇa gauṇatvāyogāt| nahi "siṃho devadatta " ityatra śauryādiviśeṣākāreṇeva dravyatvādisāmānyākāreṇa saṃhaśabdasya gauṇatvamiṣyate| ata eva pūrvataṃtre'pi "udgātṛcamasamekasya śrutisaṃyogā"dityadhikaraṇe "praituhotuścamasaḥ prabrahmaṇaḥ prodgātṝṇāṃ prayajamānasye"ti adhvaryupraiṣagatasya udgātṛpadasya bahuvacanānusāreṇa ajahallakṣaṇayā bahuṣu vṛttau vaktavyāyāṃ udgātṛgaṇamātravṛttirviśeṣākārastannimittaṃ, natu ṣoḍaśāÐtvaksādhāraṇaḥ sāmānyākāra iti nirūpitam| evaṃ ca yathaudano yena dadhipayaḥprabhṛtinā tadbhojanāyopasicyate tasyāpyadanīyatā, evamodanasthānīyaṃ brahmakṣatraśabditaṃ yena mṛtyunopasicyamānamadanīyaṃ pratīyate, tasyāpi mṛtyostena mahādanīyatāpratīteḥ, mṛtyunā saha jagadadanasya pralaya eva bhāvāt , pralaye ca sarvasya carācarasya saṃhārāt, samastaṃ carācarabṛndaṃ brahmakṣatraśabdalakṣitamavasīyate| naca samastasya carācarasya mukhyamadanaṃ sambhavatīti odanaśabdasya vināśyatve lakṣaṇā'vasīyate, yadyapi vināśyatvaṃ bhojyatvabhogyatvāpekṣayā sāmānyākāraḥ. tathā'pi sarvasya carācarasya brahmakṣatramātrasya vā kañcitpratyodanavat bhojyatvābhāvāt srībhṛtyādivat bhogyatvābhāvācca samabhivyāhṛdapadāntarasāmarthyaprāptaṃ vināśyatvarūpasādhāraṇākāranimittatvaṃ nyāyaprāptatvādurarīkaraṇīyam| ataeva "imāṃ spṛṣṭvodgāyet imāṃ hi viśvabhūtānyupajīvantī"tyatra viśvabhūtopajīvyatvasambhivyāhāraprāptaṃ bhūmiparatvaṃ imāmityasyeṣyate, natu sannihitauduṃbarīparatvaṃ, evaṃ siddhe sakalacarācarasaṃhartṛtve paramātmaliṃge tenāttā paramātmaiva| "mahāntaṃ vibhumātmānaṃ matvā dhīro na śocatī "tyādinā prakṛtasya paramātmano "yatrasa" iti tatpadena paramarśādapyevamavasīyate| atha syāt "ṛtuṃ pibantau sukṛtasya loke guhāṃ praviṣṭau parame parārthye| chāyā''tapau brahmavido vadaṃti pañcāgnayo ye ca triṇāciketā" ityanantaramantre ṛtupānaśabditakarmaphalabhoktṛtvābhidhānāt, tasya ca jīvaliṅgatayā pūrvādhikaraṇe samarthitatvāt, tayorekastāvat ṛtupānakartā jīvaḥ, tasya dvitīyo'pi buddhiprāṇayoranyataraḥ, karmaphalabhogopakaraṇatvena ṛtapānasambandhitvāt, ataḥ pūrvaprastuto'ttāpi tadekaprakaraṇakvājjīvaevāvasīyata iti cet ucyate - 
na prāṇajīvau budvijīvau vā "guhāṃ praviṣṭau" "ṛtaṃpibantāvi"ti cocyante, apitu jīvaparamātmānau hi tathocyete; tasmin prakaraṇe jīvaparayoreva gahāpraveśavyapadeśadarśanāt, "taṃ durdarśa gūḍhamanupraviśṭaṃ gūhāhitaṃ gahvareṣṭaṃ pūrāṇaṃ| adyātmayogādhigamena devaṃ matvā dhīro harṣaśokau jahātī"ti parasya guhāpraveśavyapadeśo dṛśyate, "yā prāṇena saṃbhavatyaditirdevatāmayī| guhāṃ praviśya tiṣṭaṃtī yā bhūtebhirvyajāyate"ti jīvasya| atra karmaphalānyattīti yaugikenāditiśabdena jīva ucyate, natu devamātā pṛthivī vā, tayorasmadādihṛdayaguhāpraveśābhāvāt|
"prāṇenasaṃbhavati" saha vartate| devatāmayī| devatāśabdādiṃdriyavācinaḥ svārthe mayaṭpratyaya iti indriyādhīnabhogā| hṛdayaguhāṃpraviśyatiṣṭhaṃtīti - pṛtīvyādibhūtaiḥsahitā devādirūpeṇa vividhā jāyata iti| evañca "ṛtaṃpibaṃtā" viti vyapadeśaḥ chatrinyāyena ajahallakṣaṇayā yojyaḥ|yadvā - svataṃtraprayojakobhayasādhāraṇakartṛtvasāmānyaparaḥ śatṛpratyaya iti jīvaparayorubhayorapi mukhyatayaiva yojyaḥ, budviprāṇayorūpakaraṇāyoḥ kartrarthe pratyayo hi "cakṣūrūpagrāhaka" mitivat kartṛtvopacārāditi samarthanīyaṃ syāt| na ca "sukṛtasya loka " ityasya karmaphalabhogārthamupāttasya paramātmadvitīyapakṣe'nupapattiḥ, bhoktiriva bhojayiturapi bhogasthāne sannidhānasya vaktavyatvāt| na ca jīvaparayoḥ prakāśarūpatvāt chāyātapatvanirdeśānupapattiḥ, ajñatvasarvajñatvavailakṣaṇyena tadupapatteḥ, "jñājñau dvāvajāvīśanīśā"vityāduśruteḥ, hṛdayaguhāṃ praviṣṭau buddhijīvāviti yuktam, "ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ rathamevaca| budiṃdha tu sārathiṃ viddhi manaḥ pragrahameva ce "tyatraivaprakaraṇe jīvasya rathitvena buddheḥ sārathitvena ca śarīrākārarathārūḍhatvavarṇanāt| jīvaparāviti tu na yuktaṃ, "so'dhvanaḥ pāramāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ pada "miti tayoḥ prāptṛprāpyabhāvanirdeśāt prāpyasya ratha eva prāptatve kasya prāptaye rathārohaḥ kalpyata iti cet| ucyate - guhāṃ praviṣṭau jīvaparāviti yuktaṃ, "taṃ durdarśa gūḍhamanupraviṣṭaṃ guhāhitaṃ gahvareṣṭaṃ pūrāṇaṃ| adhyātmayogādhaigamena devaṃ matvā dhīro harṣaśokau
jahāti" tyādipūrvāparamaṃtreṣu tayoreva upāsyopāsakabhāvena prāpyaprāptṛbhāvenaca tayoreva viśeṣyamāṇatvāt| rathirathasārathyādirūpaṇantu upāsanopayogiśarīrādivaśīkaraṇasivdyartha, "yastu vijñānavānbhavati yuktena manasā saha| tasyendriyāṇi vaśyāni sadaśvāiva sārathe" rityādimaṃtrātarāmnānāt| na tu paramapadaprāptyarthe, tāvatparyantaṃ prākṛtaśarīroṃdriyaviṣayādigamanābhāvāt| sarvametadānumānidhikaraṇe spaṣṭīkariṣyate| tasmāt guhāṃ praviṣṭau jīvaparaunatu budvijīvau, attāca paramātmaiveti sidvam||
saṃgrahakārike||
audanasambandhitvādattā bhokteti jīvaeva syāt|
nāsti brahmaprakaraṇamṛtamantro'gre hi budvijīvaparaḥ||
mṛtyupasecananirūpaṇasannidhānāt kṛtstrañcarāmihādyatayā pratītam|
tenādanaṃ haraṇamityucitastadattā nāthassaeva ṛtamaṃtragatassa jīvaḥ||

anavasthiterasaṃbhavācca netaraḥ||18||
chandogāḥ samāmananti - "ya eṣo'kṣiṇa puruṣo dṛśyata eṣa ātmetihovāca etadamṛtamabhayametadbrahmeti tadyadyapyasmin sarpirvodakaṃ vā siñcati vartmanīhyeva gacchati etaṃ saṃyadvāma ityācakṣate etaṃ hi sarvāṇi vāmānyābhisaṃyaṃti sarvāṇyenaṃ vāmānyābhisaṃyaṃti yaevaṃveda eṣa uevaṃ vāmanīreṣa hi sarvāṇi vāmāni nayati eṣa u eva bhāmanīreṣa hi sarveṣu lokeṣu bhātī"ti| atra akṣyantaraḥ puruṣaḥ pratibiṃbaḥ syāt, "ya eṣa "iti prasiddhavannirdeśāt "dṛśyata" ityāparokṣyābhidhānācca| jīvātmāvā, tasyāpi cakṣuṣi viśeṣasannidhānāt unmīlitaṃ hi cakṣurudvīkṣya jīvasya śarīre sthitinniścinvanti| "netrasthaṃ jāgrata"miti ca māṇḍūkyaśruteḥ| cakṣuḥ pratiṣṭho devatāviśeṣo vā syāt, "raśmibhireṣo'sminpratiṣṭhita" śrautaprasiddheḥ| na tu paramātmā| "tandurdarśa"miti durdarśatvenoktasya tasya "ya eṣa dṛśyata" iti nirdeśāyogāt| iti pūrvapakṣaḥ| nanu kathamayaṃ pūrvapakṣo ghaṭate, viṣayavākya eva amṛtatvābhayatvayorātmabrahmaśrutyoḥ saṃyadvāmatvavāmanītvabhāmanītvānāñcā talliṅgānāmāmnāt, "tadyadyapyasmi"nniti sthāninirlepatvaprayuktasthānanirlepatvāmnānāt, "tadyathā puṣkarapalāśaāpo na śliṣyante evamevaṃvidaṃ pāpaṃ karma na
śliṣyata" iti vākyena "atha yaducaivāsmin śavyaṃ kurvanti yaducana arciṣamevābhisambhavantī"ti vākyena ca brahmavidyāphalatvena prasiddhayoḥ sakalapāpaśleṣabrahmaprāptiphalayoḥ pratipādanāt, "prāṇo brahma kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahme"tyupakrāntabrahmaprakaraṇānuvṛtteśca| evaṃ paramātmapakṣa ivaṃ pratibiṃbādipakṣeṣu śrutiliṅgādīnāmanyatamasyāpyabhāvāt| akṣisthatvantu "yaccakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan, ya eṣo'kṣṇi puruṣo dṛśyata" ityādiśrutyantareṣu paramātmanyapi śrutaṃ tatsādhāraṇamiti cet ucyate - akṣyādhāratvaṃ paramātmavyāvṛttaṃ pratibiṃbādipakṣotthāpakaṃ bhavatyeva| tasya hi svamahimapratiṣṭhasya mukhyaṃ tāvadakṣyādhāratvaṃ na sambhavati, rūpaviśeṣāvacchedopādhikaṃ aupacārikaṃ sambhavadapi tadā'ṅgīkāryam, yadyakṣisthasya paramātmatvagamakaṃ śrutiliṃgādiṣu kiñcidiha jāgṛyāt| natvetadasti| ātmabrahmaśrutyoḥ saṃyadvāmatvādiguṇaśravaṇasya ca iti śabdāśiraskatvena "manobrahmetī" tyādivat atasmit tadrūpapratyayapatvāvagamāt tatsamabhivyāhṛtāmṛtatvādiśravaṇasya ca tanmātraparatvāt udāhṛtaphalānāṃ abrahmopāsanayā'pi vaivāhikavaravadhūdarśanena abhyudayavat vacanabalādupapatteḥ| brahmaprakaraṇānuvṛttistvasiddhā, "prāṇo brahme"tyādeḥ "mano brahme"tyādisārūpyeṇa brahmadṛṣṭividhiparatvāt, tasya brahmopāsanāvidhipatve'pi tadagnīnāṃ vākyaṃ idamācāryavākyamiti vaktṛbhedāt, agnividyābhirvyavadhānācca| evaṃ hyupākhyāyate - upakosalaḥ satyakāmamācāryamupasannaḥ tasya dvādaśavarṣāmyagnīn paricarannāsta, tasmai brahmopadeśamakṛtvaiva ācāryeṃ proṣite tadā'pi ācāryāgniparicaryāyamavahitaṃ tamavekṣyagārhapatyādayasrayo'gnayaḥ sambhūya karuṇayā tasmai "prāṇobrahme"tyādinā brahmavidyāṃ "yaeṣaāditye puruṣodṛśyate
sohamasmī"tyādinā pratyekaṃ svasvaviṣayavidyāṃca upadiśya "eṣā somya te'smadvidyā cātmavidyāce"tyabhidhāya "ācāryāddhaiva vidyā viditā sādhiṣṭhaṃ prāpa" diti śrutyanusāreṇa svopadiṣṭā brahmavidyā tadācāryakartṛgatyupadeśena sādhiṣṭhatvaṃ prāpnotviti ālocya "ācāryastu te gatiṃ vakte "tyavocanniti| atogatimātrasya vaktavyatvena
pariśeṣitatvāt, tasya brahmavidyāśeṣatve'pi "yaeṣo'kṣṇī" tyādestaccheṣatvābhāvāt tasya pratibiṃbādiviṣayatāyāṃ na kācidanupapattiḥ| yattu sthānimāhātmyaprayuktasarpirūdakādyaśleṣavarṇanaṃ sthāninaḥ paramātmatve liṃgamititannaḥ, vastutaḥ salepe'kṣṇisthānimāhātmyāyattasarpirudakāśleṣavarṇanasya stutimātratvāt| tasmātpratibiṃbādīnāmanyatamaevākṣipuruṣa iti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - yatrocyamānasyārthasyāptavacanasaṃbandhaḥ pratipādyaḥ tatra itiśabdonārthavivakṣāṃ vārayati| yathā "iti hasmopādhyāyaḥ kathayatī"ti tasmādihāmṛtatvādīnāmācāryavacanasaṃbaṃdhasya pratipādyatvāt itiśabdo nārthavivakṣāvārakaḥ | svābhāvikaṃcāmatatvādikaṃ jīvasya na saṃbhavati pratibiṃbasya tu kathamapi nasaṃbhavatīti ātmabrahmaśrutyamṛtatvābhayatvasaṃyadvāmatvavāmanītvābhamanītvalaṃgairbahubhirakṣitapuruṣasya paramātmatvaniścaye sati akṣyādhāratvamekamaupacārikaṃ netavyaṃ, upakramasthaikapramāṇāpekṣayā uparitanānekapramāṇānāṃ balavattvāt| kiṃca "ya eṣa 'kṣṇī puruṣo dṛśyata" iti vyapadeśāt upāsanākole'kṣṇi vartamānatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti gamyate, tattāvatpratibiṃbe na saṃbhavati, upāsanākāle'kṣyabhimukhabiṃbasannidhāpanakalpane gauravaṃ, nāsāgranyastadṛṣṭike tasmin kāle biṃbasannidhāpanakalpane'pi na pratibiṃbodayaḥ saṃbhavati, jīvasyāpi jāgare niyamena akṣiṇasthitirnasaṃbhavati, aṇuparimāṇasya tasya sarvasminnapi jāgare netramātravartitve śabdādijñānānupapatteḥ| atassarvodriyakadabhūteḥ hṛdisthitasya tasya dharmabhūtajñānaprasareṇa netrapadopalakṣitasarvendriyādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ "netrastha"miti śrutyocyata ityevābhyupagantavyaṃ| cakṣuradhiṣṭhātadevatāviśeṣasyāpi "raśmibhireṣā'smin pratiṣṭhita" iti śruteḥ tatparigrahe'pi akṣyādhāratvasyaaupacārikatvānapāyāt, taṃ parigṛhya brahmaśrutyādyupekṣaṇamayuktaṃ| kiṃca "kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahme"ti prakṛtasya sukhaviśiṣṭasya paramātmana upāsanāsthānasya guṇāṃtarāṇāṃca vidhānārthaṃ "ya eṣo'kṣṇi puruṣaḥ" ityabhidhānādaṣyakṣipuruṣaḥ paramātmā| nanu agnividyāvyavadhānāt "kaṃbrahme"ti prakṛtaḥ paramātmā neha sannidhatte| ucyate - pravāsātpunarāgata ācāryaḥ prasannamukhamupakosalaṃ vīkṣya "brahmavidaiva vai somyate mukhaṃ bhāti konu tvānuśaśāse" ti pṛṣṭvā sahāgnibhirbhīte tasmin agnayo'smā upadidiśuriti niścitya "kintu somya kila te'vocanni"ti pṛṣṭvā etāvadavocanniti tenokte tatra vaktavyaviśeṣo'stītyavadhārya "ahaṃ tu te tadvakṣyāmi yathā puṣkarapalāśa" ityādinā svavaktavyaṃ pratijñāya "bravītu me bhagavā"niti tena prārthitaḥ tataḥ saṃyadvāmatvādiguṇakamakṣipuruṣaṃ yadupadideśa tasya prakṛtasya sukhaviśiṣṭabrahmānyaviṣayatvaṃ kathaṃ kalpayitumīśmahe| nanu "eṣā somyate asmadvidyācātmavidyāce"ti nicaśeṣamupadiṣṭatayā upasaṃtāyāṃ paramātmavidyāyāṃ
vaktavyaśeṣasadbhāvamālocya ācāryaḥ svayamupadideśeti kalpane'pi agnīnāmanṛtavāditvaṃ anabhijñatvaṃ vā prasajyata iti cenna| "ācāryastu te gatiṃ vakte"tyagnināṃ vacane gatigrahaṇasya yadācāryeṇa agre vaktavyaṃ tasya sarvasyāpaypalakṣaṇārthatvāt, anyathā agrimāryavacanānāmānvayayogāt| nanu "kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma" tyatra na brahmavidyāvidānaṃ kiṃ tu prasidvākāśalaukikasukhayoḥ brahmadṛśṭividhānamātraṃ "kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahma"ti pratyekaṃ brahmapadayogiticet| ucyate - bhavabhayabhītastāvadupakosalaḥ satyakāmamupasampannaḥ| "vyādhibhiḥ pratipūrṇo'smi" tyācāryajāyāṃ pratyukosalasyasāṃsārikeṣu viṣayeṣu vyādhitvamārepya niṃdāvacanāt, adhyayanārthamupasanneṣu sarvaṣvanyeṣu brahmacāriṣu adhyāpya samāvartiteṣvapi amumasamāvartya agniparicaryāyāṃ niyujya svayaṃ satyakāmaḥ provāsetyasmācca liṃgāt| evaṃ mumukṣayā upampannaṃprati kathaṃ tadanupayoginaṃ dṛṣṭividhimagnaya upadiśeṣuḥ, tasmāt yathā "prāṇobrahme"tyatra prāṇaśarīratayā brahmaṇi sukhākāśaśabdau, uta tayoranyonyavyavacchedakatayā niratiśayānandabrahmasvarūpasamarpakatvābhiprāyeṇa vetyantaranirdidhārayiṣayā praśnaḥ| amumabhiprāyaṃ jānanto'gnayaḥ "yadvāva kaṃ tadeva khaṃ yadeva khaṃ tadeva kaṃ" mityucuriti teṣāṃ dvitīyapakṣaevābhimata ityāśayaḥ| ata evaṃ "kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahme" ti brahmapadadvayamapyupapadyate, ākāśavat brahmānavacchinnaṃ ca bhavati, tadeva brahma sukharūpaṃca bhavatīti pratyāyituṃ| evaṃ ca "yadvāva kaṃ tadeva khaṃ yadeva tadeva ka"mityubhayathā viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvoktiḥ, anavacchinnasukharūpaṃ brahma sukhātmakānavacchinnavastusvarūpaṃ ca brahmeti ubhayathā upāsanāsidyarthamiti sarvamanavadyam| śrutopaniṣatkasya adhigataparamapuruṣayāthātmyasya śrutyanta rapra sidvā yā arcirādigatiḥ tāmapunarāvṛttilakṣaṇaparamapuruṣaprāptikarīṃ upakosalāyākṣipuruṣaṃ śrutavate "arciṣamabhisambhavantī"tyādinā "eṣa devapatho brahmapathaḥ etena pratipadyamānā imaṃ mānavamāvartannāvartanta" ityantena yadācārya upadideśa, tato'pyakṣipuruṣaḥ paramātmā, na
tu pratibiṃbādiḥ| nahi tasya niyamenopāsanākāleṣu vā'kṣiṇyavasthānamasti, na vā tasminnirupādhikamamutatvādikaṃ saṃbhavatīti||
saṃgrahakārikā|
yaeṣapuruṣo'kṣṇidṛśyata iti prasidvigakādupakramagatāt|
śarīrabhṛdino'thavā pratikṛtiḥ saityanucitaṃ bahupratihate||
iti antarādhikaraṇam||3||
antaryāmyadhidaivādhilokādiṣu tadvarmavyapadeśāt|18|
nacasmārtamataddharmābhilāpācchārīraśca||19||
ubhaye'pi hi bhedenainamadhīyate||20||
"sthanādivyapadeśācce"ti sūtre "yaścakṣuṣitiṣṭha" nnityādinoktaṃ cakṣuṣi sthitiniyamanādikaṃ paramātmadharmaiti siddhaṃ kṛtvā akṣipuruṣasya paramātmatvaṃ sādhitaṃ, tadevedānīṃ samarthyata iti saṅgatiḥ| vājasaneyinaḥ samāmanaṃti - "yaḥ pṛthivyāṃtiṣṭhan pṛthivyāantaroyaṃ pṛthivī na veda yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīmaṃtaro yamayati eṣataātmāṃtaryāmyamṛta " iti| evaṃ abvagnyantarikṣavāyvādityadikcandratārakādiṣvapi tiṣṭhanta tadaṃtarabhūtaṃ tadavedyaṃ taccharīrakantattadyamayantamāmanaṃti| māddhyandināstu "yassarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu vedaṣu sarveṣu yajñeṣvi"ti paryāyāṃtarāṇyapyāmanaṃti| tatra kimayamaṃtaryāmī jīvaḥ paroveti saṃśaye jīvaiti tāvatprāptaṃ, vākyaśeṣe "draṣṭā śrotāmantā vijñāte"ti cakṣurādikaraṇāyattajñānatāśruteḥ| naca darśanaśravaṇamananavijñānaśabdāḥ rūpādisākṣātkāravācinaḥ, na cākṣuṣādijñānavācinaḥ, na cākṣuṣādijñānavācinaḥ, rūpādisākṣātkārāstu parasyāpi santi, "paśyatyacakṣussa śrṛṇotyakarṇa " ityādiśrutoriti vācyam| tathāsati "nānyo'to'sti draṣṭā nānyo'to'sti śrotā nānyo'to'sti mantā nānyo'to'sti vijñāte"ti vākyaśrutadraṣṭantarādiniṣedhāyogāt, darśanādiśabdānāṃ cākṣuṣādijñānaparatve hi jīvādanayastadvān nāstīti niṣedho yujyate| rūpādisākṣātkāravācitve tasya parasminnapi sattvādanyatra tanniṣedho na yujyate| na ca jīva iti pūrvapakṣe'pi "ya imañca lokaṃ parañca lokaṃ sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni yo'ntaroyamayatī"tyekavacanaṃ, "yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭha"nnityādipṛthivyādisthitatvavacanaṃ, "yaṃ pṛthivī na vede"ti pṛthivyādyavedyatvavacanaṃ, "yaḥ pṛthivīmantaroyamayatī"tyādipṛthivyādi niyantṛtvavacanañca, na yujyataiti vācyam|
ekavacanasya jīvasamudāyābhiprāyeṇa, sthitivacanasya svamahimapratiṣṭhitatvoktarītyā, tadantaratvatanniyantṛtvavacanayorātmeśvaratvoktarītyā, tadavedyatvokteḥ niṣkṛṣṭasvarūpajñānābhāvābhiprāyeṇa ca, yojayituṃ śakyatvāt| tasmājjīvaevāyamantaryāmīti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - "ya imañca lokaṃ parañca lokaṃ sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni yo'ntaroyamayati tamantaryāmiṇaṃ brūhī"ti sarvāntaryāmī kaścideko'ntaryāmyasti tamantaryāmiṃ brūhityuddālakapraśnasya hi "yaḥ pṛthivyāntiṣṭha"nnityādi yājñavalkyasyottaraṃ, atraikavacanaṃ jātyabhiprāyādinā yojayituṃ na yuktaṃ, prathamapravṛttaikaviṣayapraśnānusāreṇa uttaravacanānāṃ sarvadelokādiniyaṃtrekaviṣayatvasya vaktavyatvāt| tasmāt subālopaniṣadi "yasya pṛthivīśarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīmantare sañcaran yaṃ pṛthivī na veda yasyāpaśśarīra" mityādivākyasandarbhapratinirdeśe "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā'pahatapāpmā divyo devaeko nārāyaṇa " iti vākye śrutaḥ parabrahmarūpaḥ puruṣottamaevātrāntaryāmi vivakṣitaiti yuktam| tasyaiva ca "eṣata ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛta" iti nirdiśyamānaṃ jīvādvyatiriktatvaṃ svābhāvikāmṛtatvañca saṅgacchate| yattu "nānyato'to'sti draṣṭe"tyadikaraṇāyattajñānavato'nyasya niṣedhaeva saṅgacchate, natu rūpādijñānavato'nyasya niśedha iti jīvapūrvapakṣopādbapakamuktaṃ, tatredamuttaraṃ, "nānyo'to'sti draṣṭe"tyādi na draṣṭrāntarādiniṣedhamātraparaṃ, kiṃtu tatsadṛśasarvabhūtaniyantṛrūpadraṣṭrantarādiniṣedhaparaṃ, "nañivayuktamanyasadṛśādhikaraṇe tathāhyarthagati"riti śābdikanyāyāt, tameva hi nyāyamavalambya iṣunāmake kratau katipayavaiśeṣikadharmāmnānānantaramāmnāte "samānamitiracchyenene"ti vākye itaraśabdaḥ syenanāmakakratuvaiśeṣikadharmānāmatideśārthaḥ, na tu śyenasādhāraṇānāñcodakaprāptānāṃ prākṛtadharmāṇāmanuvādaka iti nirṇītaṃ pūrvatantre saptamādhyāye| etena "na dṛṣṭerdraṣṭāraṃ paśyeḥ na śrutaḥ śrotāraṃ śrṛṇuyāḥ na matermantāraṃ manvīthāḥ na vijñātervijñātāraṃ vijānīyāḥ ātmā vā're draṣṭavyaśśrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavya" iti maitrīyabrāhmaṇe śrotetyādipadānāṃ śravaṇamanananididhyāsanasākṣātkārakartṛparatāyā dṛṣṭatvāt ihāpi tatparatvaṃ vaktimucitamiti śravaṇamananādikartrantaraniṣedho
jīvapakṣaeva ghaṭate na paramātmapakṣa iti śaṅkā'pi nirastā| nañivayuktanyāyāvalambanena tatsadṛśaniyantrantaraniṣedhasya "paśyatyacakṣu"rityādiśrutyantaraprasiddhārthagrahamānukūlasyaiva tadarthatayā grāhyatvasamarthanāt| yadyapi cākṣuṣādijñānavattvena sadṛṣasyānyasyāpi niṣedho jīvapakṣe'pi ghaṭate, tathā'pi pradhānavajjīvo'pi nāntaryāmī bhavitumarhati, sarvaniyantṛtvanirūpādhikāmṛtvādyasambhavāt| apicaitaṃ jīvaṃ kāṇvāmādhyandināśca ubhaye'pi antaryāmiṇo bhedenādhīyate| "yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan vijñānādantara" ityādi hi kāṇvā adhīyate| tatraiva sthāne "ya ātmani tiṣṭha"nnityādi mādhyandināḥ| tasmādantaryāmī paramātmaiva na jīvaḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
draṣṭṛśrotrādiśabdābhihitakaraṇajajñānabhedāśrayatvā
dantaryāmī śarīrī svaniyamanavacobhāktamatretyasādhu|
ekasyaiva hyadhītākhilayamayitṛtā brahmaṇo'nyatra kasmin
saṃbhāvyā tatra rūpādyavagativiṣayādarśanādyā ghaṭante||
iti antaryāmyadhikaraṇam||4||
adṛśyatvādiguṇako dharmokteḥ||22||
viśeṣaṇabhedavyapadeśābhyāñca netaro||23||
rūpopanyāsācca||24||
ātharvaṇikāssamāmananti| "atha parā yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate yattadadreśyamagrāhyamagotramavarṇamacakṣuraśśrotrantadapāṇipādaṃ nityaṃ vibhuṃ sarvagataṃ susūkṣmaṃ tadavyayaṃ yadbhūtayoniṃ paripaśyaṃti dhīrā " iti tadanantarañcāmanaṃti| "divyohyamūrtaḥ puruṣassabādyābhyaṃtarohyajaḥ| aprāṇohyamanāśśubhrohyakṣarātparataḥ para " iti | tatra kimadṛśyatvādiguṇakamakṣaraṃ akṣarātparataḥ paraśca prakṛtipuruṣau, utobhayamapi paramātmeti| prakṛtipuruṣāviti tāvatprātaṃ, nahyasminnakṣare'ntaryāmiṇi "adṛṣṭodraṣṭe"tyādau
draṣṭṛtvādiriva cetanadharmaḥ kaścitsamāmnāyate| "akṣarātparataḥ para" ityatra ca sarvasmādvikārajātāt paraṃ yatprakṛtaṃ bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ tasmātparaḥ kṣetrajñassamaṣṭipuruṣaucyate| nanu iha cetanadharmāśravaṇe'pyadṛśyatvādikaṃ sādhāraṇamiti kuto'kṣarasya pradhānatvanirṇayaḥ| ucyate - yathākhalu "abālo'yamataruṇo'ya"mityādiśabdāḥ puruṣaviśeṣe prayujyamānāḥ pratyāsannāvasthāṃtarāśraye tasminneva vartate, evaṃ dṛśyatvagrāhyatvarūpajñānaṃdriyakarmeṃdriyaviṣayatvaniṣedhāḥ dharmiviśeṣākāṃkṣāyāṃ dṛśyatvagrāhyatvavadacetanajātaeva sūkṣmāvasthe vartate iti yuktaṃ, tasyaivāvasthāṃtarāpannasya dharmitve saṃbhavati dharmyetarakalpanāyogāt| nanu tarhi "agotramavarṇamacakṣuraśrotramapāṇipāda"miti jīve prasiddhānāṃ gotrādīnāṃ niṣedhajātasya dharmyākāṃkṣāyāṃ tasyaiva śarīrānniṣkṛṣṭasya sūkṣmāvasthasya dharmitvopapatyā bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ jīvaeva syāt, "adreśyamagrāhya"miti prakṛtiliṃganiṣedhayorūpakramagatatvepi gotraniṣedhādīnāṃ jīvaliṃgānāṃ bahutvena tataḥ prābalyāt
"kṣaraṃ pradhānamamṛtākṣaraṃ hara " iti jīvepyakṣaraśabdadarśanāt, na kṣarati svarūpeṇa na vikriyataiti tasminnapyakṣaraśabdopapatteḥ, adṛṣṭadvārā jagatkāraṇe tasmin bhūtayonitvavpadeśopapatteśceticet| ucyate - bhūtayonyakṣarasamāmnānantaraṃ tasya bhūtayonitvavivaraṇārthaṃ pravṛtte "yathorṇanābhissṛjate gṛhṇate ca yathā pṛthivyāmoṣadhayassaṃbhavanti| yathā sataḥ puruṣātkeśalomāni tathākṣarātsaṃbhavatīha viśva " miti mantre pariṇābhyupādānabhūtorṇanābhyādidṛṣṭāṃtopādānena akṣarasya bhūtayonitvaṃ pariṇābhyupādānatvarūpamavagamitaṃ, tadanantarañca "yathā sudīptātpāvakādvisphuliṃgāssahasraśaḥ prabhavante sarūpāḥ| tathākṣarādvividhāssomyabhāvāḥ prajāyaṃte
tatracaivāpiyaṃtī "ti bhūtayonyakṣarasya sarūpakāryaprabhavatvokyāpi tasya bhūtayontavaṃ pariṇābhyupādānatvarūpamavagamittaṃ, tasmādupakramopasaṃhārābhyāmavagamite bhūtayonyakṣarasya prakṛtitve "yadagotra" mityādijīvaliṃgantat tatraiva kathañcinnetavyaṃ| nanu uktarītyā bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ prathānamityastu, "akṣarātparataḥ parassamaṣṭipuruṣaititvayuktaṃ| upakramādivirodhāt| tathāhi paramopakrame "sabrahmavidyāṃ sarvavidyāpratiṣṭhāmatharvāya jyeṣṭhaputrāya prāhe " ti
brahmavidyāsamākhyā āmnāyate| tataḥ "kasminnubhagovijñāte sarvamidaṃ vijñātaṃ bhavatī" ti ekavijñānena sarvavijñānamucyate akṣaravākye'pi paravidyāsamākhyā dṛśyate| api ca sadvidyāvākyānāṃ pradhānaparatvanirāsā'rthaṃ
īkṣatyadhikaraṇadarśitāyuktayo'trāpi pravartataeva, astikhalvīkṣāpūrvakasraṣṭṛtvaśravaṇaṃ atrāpi "tapasā cīyate brme"ti, yatastapaśśabdo'tra "tapaālocana"iti dhātorniṣpannaḥ sraṣṭavyālocanarūpajñānavācī, "yasya jñānamayaṃ tapa" ityagre vivaraṇāt, ātmaśabdaścāsti "divye brahmapure hyeṣa vyomanyātmā pratiṣṭhita" iti tanniṣṭhasya mokṣopadeśo'pyasti "yadāpaśyaḥ paśyate rukmavarṇaṃ kartāramīśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahmayoniṃ| tadā vidvān puṇyapāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaitī"ti| heyatvañca tasya nocyate, pratijñāvirodho'pyastītyuktaṃ, sākṣācca brahmaṇaḥ kartṛtvamupādānatvaṃ ca śrūyate - "kartāramīśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahmayoni"miti| etāni pramāṇāni akṣarātparaḥ puruṣo brahmeti pūrvapakṣaścet tattadviṣayatayā upapadyante| so'pi samaṣṭipuruṣaiti pūrvapakṣe keṣāṃcidupapattāvapi kā bhavedanyeṣāṃ gatiriti cet, ucyate - "yassarvajñassarvavit yasya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ| tasmādetadbrahma nāmarūpamannañca jāyata" iti yassāmānyato viśeṣataśca samastavettā sarvavedānteṣu prasiddhaḥ paramātmā'si "tasmādetadbrahma, sa brahmavidyā"mityupakrame brahmaśabdenoktaṃ sakalakṣetrajñasamaṣṭarūpaṃ hiraṇyagarbhākhyaṃ brahma tajjñāyata ityucyate, tadvidyāyāḥ paravidyātvaṃ mokṣaphalakatvādupapadyate, aparabrahmavidyāyāḥ mokṣaphalakatvañca vacanabalādyujyate, samaṣṭipuruṣajñānena tadvyaṣṭayo'nyepi kṣetrajñāḥ vijñātābhavaṃtīti sarvavijñānavacanamapi ghaṭata iti pūrvapakṣī manyate| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ tāvadbrahmaiva, bhūtayonitvivaraṇārthe " yathorṇanābhi"riti mantre "tathā'kṣarātsambhavatīha viśva" miti viśvaśabdasya cetanācetanātmakasakalaprapañcaparatvāt, cetanavarge ca brahmakāryatvavat prakṛtikāryatvasyābhāvāt| na ca ūrṇanābhyādyupādānopanyāsānusāreṇa viśvaśabdaḥ tatpariṇāmatvayogyācetanamātraparaḥ khyāditi vācyam| pratijñāvākyānusāreṇa tadapekṣitopādānatvāṃśamātre dṛṣṭāntopanyāsa ityupapatteḥ. pratijñātārthānapekṣitadṛṣṭāntagatasāmyāntarasya anupayuktatvāt| tathā "tapasā cīyate brahme"ti tadanantaramantro'pi tata iti pañcamyā prakṛtākṣaragatasya bhūtayonitvasya annādiśabdoktaśabdatanmātrādikāraṇatvapratipādanadvārā vivaraṇapara iti| tatratyabrahmaśrutyā'pi akṣaraṃ brahmetyavasīyate| tathā "yassarvajña" iti tadanantaramantre
sarvajñatvādibrahmaliṃgapratipādake yacchabdasya prakṛtaparatvādapi tathā'vasīyate| na ca akṣare pulliṃgānupapattiḥ, bhūtayonyabhiprāyeṇa tadupapatteḥ, yoniśabdasya dviliṃgatvāt| na ca "yathā sudīptā" diti maṃtroktaṃ kāryakāraṇasārūpyaṃ akṣarasya prakṛtitvaṃ gamayatīti vācyam| tatra "bhāvā" ityanena cetanavargasyaiva grahaṇena tatkāraṇatvasya brahmaṇyeva sattvāt| atra hi "sudīptā"diti viśeṣaṇena prakāśamānatvaṃ sārūpyaṃ vivakṣitaṃ gamyate, tattu cetanavargasyaivāsti| kiñca idaṃ prakaraṇaṃ pradhānātpuruṣācca bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ vyāvartayati sarvavijñānoktyādibhiḥ| na ca "sarvamidaṃ vijñāta" miti cetanācetanātmakasakalaprapañcavijñānatvoktiḥ samaṣṭipuruṣajñānena vyaṣṭipuruṣajñānena vijñātatvaviṣayatayā kleśena yojanāmarhati| prakṛtasyākṣarasya pradhānātsamaṣṭipuruṣācca bhedo vyapadiśyate - "divyohyamūrtaḥ puruṣaḥ sabāhyābhyantarohyajaḥ| aprāṇohyamanāśśubhraḥ akṣarātparataḥ para" iti| akṣarādavyākṛtāt paro yaḥ samaṣṭipuruṣaḥ tasmādapi paro'yaṃ bhūtayonyakṣararūpaḥ puruṣaityarthaḥ| nanu svākāryebhyaḥ paraṃ yatprakṛtamakṣaraṃ tasmātpara ityarthaḥ kiṃ na syāt, sa eva ca yuktaḥ, "akṣarāt parata" iti pañcamyoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyalābhādicet, ucyate - "sabāhyābhyāntara" ityatra sa iti bhinnaṃ padaṃ puruṣaviśeṣaṇaṃ, yathā sudīptāditi mantraprakṛtabhūtayonyakṣaraparāmarśi sat akṣarātparasya puruṣasya tadabhedabodhanārthaṃ, natu "sabāhyāntarābhyantara" iti ekaṃ padaṃ, vaiyarthyāt| "sabāhyāntarābhyantara" iti sarvagatvokyaiva sakalavastusaṃsargasiddheḥ| evaṃbhūtayonyakṣarasya akṣarātparasya puruṣasya ca abhedena brahmarūpatvasiddhau paṃcamyantākṣarapadavācyamavyākṛtameva paryavasyati| "akṣarātparataḥ para" ityataḥ prācīne maṃtre "yenākṣaraṃ puruṣaṃ veda satyaṃ provāca tāṃ tatvato brahmavidyā"mityakṣarapuruṣapadasāmānādhikaraṇyādapyayamartho niścīyate| api ca "agnirmūrddhā cakṣuṣī candrasūryaudiśaśśrotre vāgvivṛtāśca vedāḥ| vāyuḥ prāṇo hṛdayaṃ viśvamasya padbhyāṃ pṛthivīhyeṣa sarvabhūtāntarātme"ti maṃtre prakṛtaṃ bhūtayonyakṣararūpaṃ puruṣaṃ "asye"ti sarvanāmnā parāmṛśya trailokyaśarīratvopanyāsādapi na pradhānamakṣaraṃ, nāpi samaṣṭipuruṣo'kṣarātparataḥ paraḥ, yadyapi purāṇādiṣu hiraṇyagarbhasyāpi trailokyātmakaṃ vairājaṃ śarīraṃ prasiddhamasti, tathāpi yasmin cāracarātmakaṃ samastaṃ jagat hṛgayavatpratiṣṭhitaṃ tathābhūtaṃ tattasya na sambhavati| yatsambhavati tadapi paramātmātmakameva, "sṛṣṭyantaraṃ kariṣyāmi tvāmāvisya prajāpata " iti vacanāt| na ca "agnirmūrddhe"kati maṃtroktaṃ sarvabhūtāntarātmatvaṃ hiraṇyagarbhe sambhavati| tasmādakṣaraṃ akṣarātparataḥ paraśca paramātmaiva||
saṃgrahakārike||
bhajati khaviṣayāṇāṃ lāghavātsūkṣmarūpaṃ
jaḍajagadanuvṛttaṃ dṛṣyatāderniṣedhaḥ|
tadabhihitamatharvaṇyakṣaraṃ syātpradhānaṃ
tadupari ca niruktastatparaḥ paṃcaviṃśaḥ||
sarvajñatvādidharmokteḥ paraevādyamakṣaram|
anyadavyākṛtantasmādyatparastatparopi saḥ||
iti adṛśyatvādiguṇakādhikaraṇam||5||
vaiśvānarassādhāraṇaśabdaviśeṣāt||25||
smaryamāṇamanumānaṃ syāditi||26||
śabdādibhyo'ntaḥ pratiṣṭhānācca neti cenna tathādṛṣṭyupadeśādasaṃśravātpuruṣamapi cainamadhīyate||27||
ata eva na devatā bhūtañca||28||
sākṣādapyavirodhaṃ jaiminiḥ||29||
abhivyakterityāśmaradhyaḥ||30||
anusmṛterbādariḥ||31||
saṃpatteriti jaiministathāhi darśayati||32||
āmanaṃti cainamasmin||33||
trailokyātmakarūpopanyāsādakṣaravadvaiśvānaropi paramātmeti nirṇīyata iti saṅgatiḥ| chandogāḥ "ātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ saṃpratyadhyeṣi tameva no brūhi"ti ṛṣibhiḥ pṛṣṭasya kekayarājasyottareṣvāmananti - "yastvetamevaṃ prādeśamātramabhivimānamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāsta" iti | tatra kimayaṃ vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmeti śakyanirṇayaḥ utāśakyanirṇayaiti vaiśvānaraśabdasya sādhāraṇyāt| saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| vaiśvānaraśabdaścaturṣvartheṣu dṛśyate| jāṭharāgnau tāvat "ayamagnivaiśvānaro yenedamannaṃ pacyate yadidamadyate tasyaiṣaghoṣo bhavati yadetatkarmāvapidhāya śrṛṇotī"ti, mahābhūtatṛtīye ca "viśvasmāagniṃ bhuvanāya devāḥ vaiśvānaraṃ ketumahnāmakṛṇva"nniti, devatāyāṃca "vaiśvānarasya sumatau syāma rājā hi kaṃ bhavanānāmabhiśrī"riti, paramātmanica "saeṣavaiśvānaro viśvarūpaḥ prāṇognirudayata" iti| nanu ayamādityaviṣayovaiśvānaraśabdaḥ, na tu paramātmaviṣayaḥ, tadanantaraṃ "tadetadṛcābhyukta"mityupakramya hi "sahasraraśmiśśatadhāvartamānaḥ prāṇaḥ prajānāmudayatyeṣasūrya" iti sauramantrasya vaiśvānaraviṣayatvenodāharaṇāt, tatra "saeṣa" iti sarvanāmnoḥ "ādityohaprāṇa" iti prakṛtādityaparatvācceticet ucyate - 
na kevalamādityaparastatra vaiśvānaraśabdaḥ, kiṃtu ādityāntarvartiparamātmaparyantaḥ, "athottareṇa tapasā śraddhayā vidyayā''tmānnamanviṣyādityamabhijāyante etaddhai prāṇānāmāyatanamamṛtamabhayaṃ parāyaṇametasmānna punarāvartanta " ityagre śravaṇāt| nanu "ko na ātmā kiṃ brahme"tyupakramagatabrahmaśrutyā vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmeti śakyanirṇayaḥ| naca vājasaneyiśākhāyāṃ "vaiśvānaraṃ ha vai bhagavān samprati veda tanno brūhi"ti vaiśvānara śabdena upakramāt upakramo na vinigamaka iti vācyam| tasya sādhāraṇasya vaiśvānaraśabdasya chāndogyopakramagatabrahmaśrutyanusāreṇa paramātmani paryavasānopapatteriticet maivam| "ko na ātmā kiṃ brahme"ti vimṛṣṭavatāṃ prācīnaśālādināmagre dyuprabhṛtiṣu vaiśvānarātmadṛṣṭirvarṇiteti tadanusāreṇa tadīyavacanagatayorātmabrahmaśrutyorātmabrahmadṛṣṭiviṣayatvākāreṇa anyatra gauṇatayaiva prayogāt| evaṃ hyupākhyāyate - aupamanyavādayaḥ paṃca maharṣayaḥ "ko na ātmā kiṃ brahme" ti mīmāṃsitvā niścayamalabhamānaḥ vaiśvānaropāsanāparamuddālakaṃ gatvā tenāpi tasyāmakṛtsnavedinā aśvapatiḥ kekayo vaiśvānaramupāste tamabhyāgacchāmetyuktāḥ uddālakaṣaṣṭhāstamabhijagmuḥ, sa ca teṣāṃ jñānaṃ parīkṣya tato vaktavyaśeṣaṃ vakṣyāmītyabhisandhāya aupamanyava kaṃ tvamātmānamupāsta" ityādinā tān ṣaḍapi ṛṣīn krameṇa pṛṣṭau taiḥ "divameva bhagavorāja"nnityādibhiryathākramaṃ dyusūryānilākāśajalāvaniṣu vaiśvānarabuddyā svairūpāsyamānatayokteṣu divādayo vaiśvānaraṃ prati mūrddhacakṣuḥ prāṇamadhyasthānapādāvayavatvenopāsyāḥ, na tu vaiśvānaratveneti vyastopāsanāḥ pratiṣidhya samastopāsanāviṣayasya vaiśvānarasya prāgupakṣiptaṃ dyumūrddhatvādiviśiṣṭaṃ rūpamekīkṛtyopadideśa "mūrddhaiva sutejā" ityādineti| tasmādvaiśvānaraḥ paramātmeti na śakyanirṇaya iti | evaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - paramātmeti śakyanirṇayaeva, "ko na ātmā kiṃ brahme
"ti jīvātmanāmātmabhūtaṃ paramātmānaṃ jijñāsamānaistadabhijñaiti rājānamupasannaiḥ prācīnaśālādibhiḥ pṛṣṭena rājñopadiṣṭatvāt| na ca teṣāmātmabrahmaśabdayorgauṇatvābhimānaśśaṅkanīyaḥ, yataḥ "ko na ātmā kiṃ brahme " ti vimarśaḥ asmābhiranyonyavirodhena ātmabrahmabuddhadyopāsyamāneṣu dyuprabhṛtiṣu vastutaḥ kintathābhūtaṃ brahma kimeteṣāmanyatamamanyadveti vastutatvaviṣayaḥ, na tu teṣu kiṃtathābhūtabrahmadṛṣṭimāropyopāsyamityāropitarūpaviśiṣṭaviṣayaḥ, "annabrahme manobrahme "tyādivat anekatra pratīke brahmadṛṣṭyāporasaṃbhavāt virodhābhāvenāsmadupāsyānāṃ dyuprabhṛtīnāṃ madhye kiṃtathābhūtabrahmadṛṣṭyopāsyamiti nirddhāraṇānapekṣanāt| ataeva "divameva bhagavorāja" nnityādivākyaiḥ svasvabhramaevoddhāṭitaḥ, sambhavati hyadyāpi candrasūryādiṣu keṣāṃcit brahmatvabhramavat dyuprabhṛtiṣu teṣāṃ tadbhramaḥ, atastatra gauṇatvaśaṅkā'nāspadābhyāmātmabrahmaśrutibhyāṃ vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmeti śakyaeva nirṇetuṃ| kiṃ ca "sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣvātmasvannamattī"tyanena sarvatra vartamānamanavadhikātiśayānandarūpatayā bhogyaṃ paraṃbrahmānubhavatītyucyate, na tu karmavaśyairātmabhiryadannaṃ bhujyate tadattīti, tasya mumukṣūn prati heyatvāt| paramānandānubhavaphalañca paramātmapakṣaeva ghaṭate| "tadyathaiṣīkātūlamagnau protaṃ pradūyetaivaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyata" ityucyamānaṃ sarvapāpapradāhaphalañca tatpakṣaeva ghaṭate| dyuprabhṛtipṛthivyantatrailokyātmakaṃ śarīramihopadisyamānaṃ "agnirmūrddhā cakṣuṣī candrasūryā " viti śrutau "dyāṃ mūrddhānāṃ yasya viprā vadaṃti khaṃ vai nābhiścandrasūryau ca netre| diśaśśrotre viddhi pādau kṣitiñca so'cintyātmā sarvabhūtapreṇeta"tyādismṛtiṣu ca paramātmanaḥ prasiddhaṃ rūpaṃ pratyabhijñāpayadvaiśvānaraṃ
paramātmānamavagamayati| syādetat, uktahetubhiḥ vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmeti naśakyanirṇayaḥ, śabdādibhyontaḥpratiṣṭhānācca jāṭharaityapi pratīteḥ| agniśabdastāvat vājasaneyake "saeṣognivaiśvānara" iti vaiśvānarasamānādhikaraṇaśśrūyate| chāṃdogye ca "hṛdayaṃ gārhapatyomano'nvāhāryapacanaāsyamāhavanīya" iti vaiśvānarasyāgnitrayasthānakalpanaṃ kriyate| "tadyadbhaktaṃ prathamamāgacchettaddhomīyaṃ sayāṃ prathamāmāhutiṃ juhuyāttāṃ juhuyāt prāṇāya svāhe " tyādinā vaiśvānarasya prāṇāhutyādhāratvamāmnāyate| tasya ca puruṣe'ntaḥ pratiṣṭhitatvaṃ vājasaneyibhirāmnāyate - "sayohaitamevamagniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ puruṣavidhaṃ puruṣe'ntaḥpratiṣṭhitvaṃ vede"ti| evamuktahetubhiḥ vaiśvānarojāṭharaityapi pratīyata iti cet, naiṣadoṣaḥ| trailokyaśarīrasya vaiśvānarasya paramātmanojāṭharāgniśarīratayā tadviśiṣṭasyopāsanopadeśāt| agniśabdādibhiḥ na kevalo jāṭharaḥ pratipādyate, kintu tadviśiṣṭaḥ paramātmā, kevalasya jāṭharasya trailokyaśarīratvāsaṃbhavāt, "ahaṃ vaiśvānaro bhūtvā prāṇināṃ dehamāśritaḥ| prāṇāpānasamāyuktaḥ pacāmyannaṃ caturvidha "miti gītāsu bhagavato jāṭharāgniśarīratayā aśitapītānnādipaktṛtvapratipādanācca| etebhyaeva hetubhyaḥ agndevatāyāḥ tṛtīyamahābhūtasya ca vaiśvānaratvaśaṅkā'pi pratyuktā| jaiministvācāryomanyate - yathā vaiśvānaraśabdassādhāraṇopi paramātmāsādhāraṇadharmadarśanāt viśveṣāṃ narāṇāṃ netā vaiśvānaraḥ sa eva svārthikāṇpratyayena vaiśvānara ityevaṃ paramātmābhidhāyako niścīyate, evaṃmagnśabdo'granayanādiyogena paramātmābhidhāyaka iti niścīyate, tasya yogasya jvalanādapi sarvāntarātmani paramātmanyeva supratiṣṭhitatvāditi| nanu aparicchinnasya paramātmanaḥ "yastvetamevaṃ prādeśamātra"miti śrūyamāṇaṃ dyuprabhṛtipṛthivyantapradeśasambandhinyā mātrayā paricchinnatvaṃ kathamupapadyate, upāsakabuddhyārohārthatayā tathā prādeśamātratvapratipādanaṃ, na tu paricchinnatvābhiprāyeṇa| tatraiva "abhivimāna"miti abhito vigatamānatvokteḥ| tathā'pi prākṛtadyumūrddhādimattvaṃ aprākṛtarūpasya paramātmano na sambhavatīti cenna| upāsanārthaṃ tathā puruṣavidhatvakalpanopapatteḥ| paramātmā vaiśvānaraścet uraḥprabhṛtīnāṃ "uraeva vedirlomāni barhirhadayaṃ gārhapatya" ityādinā kathaṃ vedyāditvopadeśaḥ, jāṭharaparigrahe hi sa upapadyata iti cet, ucyate - tasya vaiśvānarasya samārādhanabhūtāyā upāsakairaharahaḥ kriyamāṇāyāḥ prāṇāhuteragnihotratvasampādanārtho 'yamuraḥprabhṛtīnāṃ vedyāditvopadeśaḥ| ata eva prāṇāhuteragnihotratvasampatiṃta paramātmopāsanocitaphalañca darśayati śrutiḥ - "sa ya idamavidvānagnihotraṃ juhoti yathā'ṅgārānapohya bhasmani juhuyāttādṛktatsyāt atha ya etadevaṃ vidvānagnihotraṃ juhoti tasya sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣvatmasu hutaṃ bhavati tadyathaiṣikātūlamagnau protaṃ pradūyeta evaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyanta" iti| nanu paramātmano dyuprabhṛtīśarīratvamayuktaṃ, upāsakasya mūrddhādyavayavānāmeva dyuprabhṛtitvenopāsanāvidhānāt, "tasya hevā etasye" tyādi hyupāsakasya dyumūrddhatvādipratipādakaṃ, "ura eva vedi"rityādisamabhivyāhārāditi cet satyaṃ, upāsakadyumūrddhatvādyanusandhānaṃ na paramātmadyumūrddhatvādikamuktaṃ, tadīyameva dyumūrddhatvādikamupāsakasyāpyuktaṃ, "tasya havā etasyātmano vaiśvānarasye "ti tadīyatvena parāmṛṣṭameva hi dyumūrddhatvādikamupāsake varṇitam| ataḥ parasya mūrddhādinā ahaṃ dyumūrddhā parasya cakṣuṣā sūryeṇa ahaṃ sūryacakṣuka ityādirūpeṇānusandhānaparasya prāṇāgnihotreṇārādhaṃnaṃ kāryam| tasmādihopakrānto vaiśvānaraḥ paramātmeti siddham||
saṃgrahakārike||
vaiśvānaro na paramātmatayā niyantuṃ śakyaścaturviṣayasaṃśayahetusattvāt|
sādhāraṇaṃ hi jaṭharāgnitṛtīyabhūtataddaivatasya ca padaṃ paramātmanaśca||
maivaṃ mumukṣupraśnācca brahmaśabdaśruteśca saḥ|
tādṛkphalopadeśācca paraṃ brahmāvadhāryate||
iti vaiśvānarādhikaraṇam||
||iti dvitīya pādaḥ||
||atha tṛtīya pādaḥ||
dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ svaśabdāt||1||
muktopasṛpyavyapadeśācca||2||
nānumānamatacchabdātprāṇabhṛcca||3||
bhedavyapadeśācca||4||
prakaraṇāt||5||
sthityadanābhyāñca||6||
ātharvaṇikāḥ samāmanaṃti - "yasmin dyauḥ pṛthīvī cāṃtarikṣamotaṃmanassaprāṇaiśca sarvaiḥ tamevaikaṃjānathātmānamanyāvāco vimuṃcatha amṛtasyaiṣaseturiti| atra dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ jīvaḥ paroveti saṃśaye jīvaiti tāvatprāptaṃ, "arāiva rathanābhau saṃhatāyatra nāḍyaḥ saeṣo'ntaścarate bahudhā jāyamana iti uttaramatre nāḍyādhārattavaśarīrāṃtassaṃcārajanmavattvānāṃ jīvaliṃgānāmāmnānāt, tatra ādimantre "otaṃ manassaha prāṇaiśca sarvai"riti manaḥ prāṇāśrayatvarūpajīvaliṃgāmnānāt, "eṣo'ṇurātmā cetasā veditavyo yasmin prāṇaḥ paṃcadhā saṃviveṣa| prāṇaiścittaṃ sarvabhotaṃ prajānāṃ yasmin viśuddhe vibhavatyeva ātme"tyanyatra jīve manaḥ prāṇayorotatvaśravaṇāt| dyupṛthivyādyāyatanatvantu jīvasya adṛṣṭadvārā saṅgamayitavyamityevaṃ prāpte'bhidhīyate - dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ paramātmā, tadasādhāraṇāmṛtasetutvaśravaṇāt, amṛtasetutvaṃ hyamṛtatvaprāpakatvaṃ, seturhi nadyādiṣu kūlasya pratilambhakaḥ| evaṃ saṃsārārṇavapārabhūtasyāmṛtasya pratilambhaka ityarthaḥ| ātmaśabdaścātra svaśabdaḥ, sahyāpnotermanpratyaye bāhulake dakāre ca sati niṣpannaḥ| niyaṃtṛtvena sarvatra vyāptimati paramātmanyeva mukhyavṛttiḥ| nāḍyādhāratvaṃ parasyāpi sambhavati "santataṃ sibhistu lambatyākośasannibha"mityupakramya "tasyāśśikhāyāmadhye paramātmā vyavasthita" ityāmnānāt| bahudhā jāyamāntavamapi tasyāsti, "ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate, ajo'pi sannavyayātmā bhūtānāmūśvaro'pi san| prakṛtiṃ svāmadhiṣṭhāya sambhavāmyātmamāyaye" tyādiśrutismṛtidarśanāt| kiṃca "yadā paśyaḥ paśyate sukmavarṇaṃ kartāramūśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahyayoniṃ| tadā vidvān puṇyapāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti, yathā nadyassyandamāpāssamudre'staṃ gacchanti nāmarūpe vihāya| tathā vidvānmārūpādvimuktaḥ parātparaṃ puruṣamupaitidivya"miti muktaprāpyatvaśravaṇādapi dyupṛthivyādyāyatanaṃ paramātmā| api ca yathā'smin prakaraṇe pradhānapratipādakaśabdābhāvāt na pradhānaṃ dyupṛthivyādyāyatanaṃ, evaṃjīvo'pi, tatpratipādakaśabdasyāpyabhāvāt, nanu idaṃ dṛṣṭāṃtadārṣṭāntikadvayamapyuktaṃ| tathāhi - pradhānasya tāvat "yasmin dyau"rityādireva tacchabdaḥ, akṣarabrāhmaṇe "yadūrdhvaṃ gārgi divo yadavākpṛthivyā " ityādinā pradhānasya dyupṛthivyādisakalalokādhāratvapratipādanāt "yasmin dyau" rityasya gacchabdayogena tadanuvādatvapratīteḥ| naca tasmin "amṛtasyaiṣasetu" riti vyapadeśānupapattiḥ| amṛtaśdohyatra brahmaparaḥ, natvamṛtatvarūpamokṣaparaḥ| "yasmin dyau" riti mantrātpūrva "tadetatsatyamamṛta"miti "ānandarūpamamṛtaṃ yadvibhātī"ti ca brahmaṇi amṛtaśabdāmnānena tasyaiva atrāmṛtaśabdena pratyabhijñānāt| ataḥ pradhānaṃ "amṛtasyaiṣasetu"riti vyapadeṣṭuṃ śakyam| brahmādhīnatvena brahmasaṃbandhitvāt| "setu" rityapi vyapadeṣṭuṃ śakyam| setu śabdohi "ṣiñbandhan " iti dhātoḥ vyutpannobandhahetumācaṣṭe| naca bandhahetumātraṃ jalabandhahetumātraṃ vā tadarthaḥ, ghaṭodaranissarajjalabandhahetumadhūcchiṣṭādau tadaprayogāt, kiṃtu pārāvāramadhyavartitvaviśeṣitaṃ jalabandhanahetutvaṃ tadarthaḥ, tasmādiha lakṣakeṇa setuśabdena pāravattvaṃ lakṣayituṃ yuktaṃ, sahajatvāt śabdārthaikadeśatvācca| pāravattvantu pradhānasyaiva asti, na brahmaṇaḥ, "mahadbhūtamanaṃtamapāra"miti śruteḥ| ātmaśabdo'pi dehaparatayā tasminnupapadyate, "ātmā jīve dhṛtau deha " ityādismaraṇāt| "anyavāco vimuṃcathe"tyetadapi pradhānameva dehaṃ jānīdhvaṃ sthūladehaviṣayāstu dehatvavāco vimuṃcatha
dehaviviktātmajñānārthaṃ hi dehomumukṣubhirjñātavyaḥ, satu na sthūladehaḥ, āgamāpāyinaḥ tato vivekasya mujñānatvāt| kiṃtu antaḥkaraṇādirūpāpannāvyākṛtātmā sūkṣmadeha iti, tasmāt pradhānaṃ dyubhvādyādhāra iti ghaṭate| jīvaiti pakṣo'pi ghaṭate, jīvopakaraṇānāṃ teṣāṃ jīvādṛṣṭāyattotpattisthitikatvena tadāśritatvavyapadeśopapatteḥ| yathāvā asanābhau samarṣitāevamasmin prāṇe sarvaṃ samarpitamiti bhṛmavidyāyāṃ, "evametābhūtamātrāḥ prajñāmātrasvarpitā " iti pratardanavidyāyāṃca, acidvargasya jīvāśritatvapratipādanācca| "eṣo'ṇurātmā cetasā veditavyo yasmin prāṇaḥ paṃcadhā saṃviveśe"ti "prāṇaiścittaṃ sarvamota prajānāṃ yasminviśuddhe vibhavatyeṣaātme"ti muṇḍakaeva agre manasaḥ prāṇānāṃca jīvāśritatvapratipādanāt| ātmaśabdaśca tasminnupapadyate
"praṇavodhanuśśarohyātmā brahma tallakṣyamucyate| apramattena veddhavyaṃ śaravattanmayo bhave"diti prācīnamantre tatraiva ātmaśabdasya prayuktatvāt| tamevaikaṃ jānathātmana"mityetadapi tasminnupapadyate|
"manomayaḥ prāṇaśarīranetā pratiṣṭhito'nne hṛdayaṃ sannidhāya| tadvijñānena paripaśyanti dhīrā ānandarūpamamṛtaṃ yadvibhātī"ti jīvavijñānasya brahmadarśanahetutvoktyā tasyāprādhānyena apekṣitatvāt| "anyā vāco vimuñcathe"tyetadapi tasminnupapadyate| "dhanurgṛhītatvaupaniṣadaṃ mahāsraṃ śaraṃhyupāsāniśitaṃ sandadhīta| āyamya tadbhāvagatena cetasā lakṣyantadevākṣaraṃ somya viddhī"ti vyavahitapūrvamaṃtre dehasthaulyādiviśiṣṭatayā bhāsamānasya jīvasya upāsanayā dehādivivekadārḍhyāpādanena tanūkaraṇokteḥ, jīvopāsanāyāñca tadanyaviṣayavāgvimokāvaśyambhāvāt| "amṛtasyaiṣa setu"riti vyapadeśastu pradhāne varṇitayaiva rītyā jivepyupapadyate| tasmāt pradhānajīvapakṣayoḥ na kācidanupapattiriti cet ucyate - "yasmin dyau"rityāderakṣarabrāhmaṇoktākṣararūpādhārāntarasāpekṣadyupṛthivyādyādhārapradhānānuvādakatvantāvadayuktam| sanmūlāssomyemāssarvāḥ prajā" ityādi chāndogya śrutyāmnātanirapekṣasarvādhārabrahmānuvādakatve sambhavati sāpekṣādhārānuvādakatvakalpanāyogāt| evaṃ ca dyubhvādisarvādhāratvoktinirvāhārthaṃ apekṣitatvena ātmaśabdasya vyāpakatvarūpārthaeva grāhyaḥ, samabhivyāhṛtapadāntaraikavākyatvayogyaeva lākṣaṇikārthogrāhyaiti atradhikaraṇe vyavasthāpitatvāt| evaṃ brahmaikāntikadyupṛthivyādyādhāratvātmaśrutyanusāreṇa "niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam| amṛtasya paraṃ setuṃ dagdhendhamivānala"miti śvetāśvatare brahmaṇi amṛtasetutvavyapadeśadarśanāt, tadanusāreṇa "amṛtasyaiṣasetu" rityasya amṛtasyasvasya svayameva prāpaka ityevārthau grāhyaḥ, tathā'pi uttaramantragatā śarīrāntassañcāroktirvyāpake brahmaṇi na yujyata iti cet na| subālopaniṣadi - "yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaḥ pṛthivīmantare sañcara"nnityādivākyasandarbhe sarvaniyantṛtayā sarvatra vyāptessaṃcāratvena vyapadeśadarśanāt, ihāpi tasyāstathātvena vyapadeśopapatteḥ| api ca asmin prakaraṇe "samāne vṛkṣe puruṣo nimagno'nīśayā śocati muhyamānaḥ| juṣṭaṃ yadā paśyatyanyamīśamasya mahimānamiti vītaśoka" iti maṃtraḥ śrūyate| asyārthaḥ - samāne ekasmin vṛkṣe vṛkṣavadūścanārhasvabhāve śarīre nimagnaḥ pāṃsupuñjanimagnajalakaṇa iva tadaikyamāpannaḥ puruṣo jīvaḥ anīśayā prakṛtyā muhyamānaśśocati| ayaṃ yadā svasvamādanyaṃ juṣṭaṃ prīyamāṇaṃ prasīdantaṃ sarvasyeṣaṃ asyaceśvarasya nikhilajaganniyamanarūpaṃ mahimānaṃ ca paśyati tadā vitaśoko bhavatīti| asmin maṃtre yadyapi brahmavajjīvo'pyupāttaḥ, tathā'pi draṣṭṛtvasamānādhikaraṇaṃ muhyamānatvaṃ dṛśyatva samānādhikaraṇamīśatvaṃ ca vyapadiśantīyaṃ śrutiḥ "tamevaikaṃ jānathe"ti jñayatayā nirdiṣṭaṃ dyubhvādyādhāramīśameva pratyabhijñāpayati| kiṃ ca prakaraṇamidaṃ paramātmana iti prāgakṣarādhikaraṇe darśitam| ato'pyayaṃ paramātmā| na ca akṣaraprakaraṇādeva akṣarādhikaraṇasiddhāt dyupṛthivyādyādhārasya paramātmatvasiddhau kimanenādhikaraṇeneti śaṅkanīyam| prakaraṇasya nāḍīsambandhabahudhājāyamānatvamanaḥprāṇādhāratvādibhiḥ jīvaikātibhiḥ vicchedaśaṅkāyāṃ tatparihārārthatvāt| api ca asminprakaraṇe "dvāsuparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte| tayoranyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvatti anasnannanyo dīpyamānaḥ paramātmā ca pratipādyate, tathā'pi dyubhvādyādhāraḥ paramātmaiveti yuktaṃ, dīpyamānatvoktyā tatraiva prākaraṇikasarvajñatvādyanvayasvārasyāt| anena sūtreṇa sarvavijñānapratijñopakramāt " brahma veda brahmaiva bhavatī" tyupasaṃhārācca jīvabrahmābhede prakaraṇasya tātparyamiti śaṅkānirākaraṇamapi kṛtam| "abhi , anaśna" nnityupapattisahitabhedavyapadeśena tayorbheda eva siddhyoditi||
saṅgrahakārike||
dyubhvādyāyatanaṃ jīvo manaḥ prāṇānvayaśruteḥ|
samanantaramantre ca nāḍīyogajaniśruteḥ||
parameśvara eva syāt sarvavastuvidhārakaḥ|
amṛtaprāpayitṛtāpramukhapratipādanāt||
iti dyubhvādyadhikaraṇam||
bhūmā samprasādādadhyupadeśāt||7||
dharmopapatteśca||8||
muṇḍa keparamātmaprakaraṇāvicchedenaṃ paramātmatvaṃ sādhitam, tarhi bhūmavidyāyāḥ jīvaprakaraṇāvicchedena jīvaparatvamiti utthāmātsaṅgatiḥ| chandogāssamānaṃti| "yatra nānyatpaśyati nānyacchṛṇoti nānyadvijānāti sabhūmā
yatrānyatpaśyatyanyacchṛṇotyanyadvijānāti tadalpa" miti| bhūmaśabdaḥ "pṛthvādibhya imanijve "ti pṛthvādigaṇapaṭhitāt bahuśabdāt bhāvārthe imanicpratyaye "bahorlopo bhūca bahori"ti bahoḥ parasya imanicaḥ ikāralope bahuśabdasya bhūityādeśe ca sati niṣpanno bahutvavācī| yadyapi bahutvaṃ vaipulyaṃ saṃkhyāviśeṣaśca| tathā'pyatra vaipulyamarthaḥ, "yatrānyatpaśyati tadalpa"mityalpapratiyogitvaśravaṇāt, pūrvatra "ākāśovāva tejaso bhūyā" nityādau bahutvasaṃkhyāhīne'pyakāśādike vaipulyavati bahuśabdaniṣpannabhūyaśśabdadarśanācca| bhūmaśabdo'yamaplaśabdanirdiṣṭadharmipratiyogiviṣayatvādeva dharmiparaḥ, na dharmamātraparaḥ| tataśca bhūmeti vipula ityarthaḥ| vaipulyaviśeṣyaścātmā| "tarati śokamātmavi" diti prakṛtya bhūmānamupadiśya "ātmaivedaṃ sarva "mityupasaṃhārāt| tatra kimayaṃ bhūmaviśiṣṭaḥ pratyagātmā paramātmā veti saṃśaye pratyagātmeti tāvatprāptam| ātmajijñāsayopaseduṣe nāradāya nāmādiprāṇaparyanteṣu upāsyatayopadiṣṭeṣu "asti bhagavo nāmno bhūyaḥ, asti bhagavo vāco bhūya "ityādipraśnatatprativacanavacca "asti bhagavaḥ prāṇādbhūya " iti praśnasya "adovāva prāṇādbhūya" iti prativacanasyacābhāvena ātmopadeśasya prāṇaparyantatāpratīteḥ prāṇaśabdasya prāṇasahacarite pratyagātmani vṛttyupapatteḥ, "prāṇoha pitā prāṇo mātā prāṇo bhrātā prāṇassvase" tyādicatanavācipitrādiśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyācca pitrādirūpajīvavācitvāvagamāt, natvayamātmopadeśaḥ paramātmaviṣaya iti yuktam| "sa yadi pitaraṃ vā mātaraṃ vā bhrātaraṃ vā svasāraṃ vā ācārye vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā kiñcidbhṛśamiva pratyāha dhiktvāstvityevainamāhuḥ pitṛhāvai tvamasi mātṛhāvai tvamasī" tyādinā saprāṇeṣu pitrādiṣu adhikṣipyamāṇeṣu hiṃsopakrośavacanāt| "atha yadyapyenānutkrāntaprāṇān śūlena samāsaṃ vyatiṣandahennaivainaṃ brūyuḥ pitṛhāsīti na mātṛhāsī" tyādinā teṣu utkrāntaprāṇeṣu śūlena samyakprakṣepapūrvakaṃ vyatyasya samantādṛhyamāneṣvapi hiṃsāvyavahārābhāvavarṇanācca| nahi parasya hiṃsyatvamasti, na vā tasyotkrāntirasti| na ca prāṇaśabdoktajīvapratipādanānantaraṃ "eṣa tu vā ativadati yassatyenātivadati" ti tuśabdena tatprakaraṇavicchedadyotanāt tataḥprabhṛti satyaśabdoktaparamātmaprakaraṇamiti tasmin prakaraṇe pratipādyamāno bhūmā paramātmaiva syāditi śaṅkanīyam| "savā eṣa evaṃ paśyannevaṃ manvāna eṃ vijānannativādī bhavatī"ti prāṇavādino yadativāditvaṃ svopāsyasyānyatautkarṣavāditvarūpamuktaṃ tasya "eṣatuvā" iti vākye pratyabhijñānena prakaraṇavicchedābhāvāt| "eṣa tu vā agnihotrī yassatyaṃ vadatī"tyuktau prasiddhāgnihotriṇa iva prakṛtaprāṇātivādinassatyavadanamaṅgaṃ vidhīyate, satyeva tu satyavacane agnihotritvavat prāṇātivāditvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ bhavatītyetatadarthatayā tuśabdasāmañjasyāt| tasmāt pratyagātmaivātra pratipādyaḥ| ata eva "ahamevādhastādahamupariṣṭā"
dityārabhya "ahamevedaṃ sarva " mityupasaṃhāre pratyagātmana eva vaibhavamupadiśyate, tasya ca bhūmaśabdena "ahamevādhastā " dityādinācoktaṃ vaipulyaṃ muktau dharmajñānavikāsādupapadyata" iti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
"eṣatu vā ativadati yassatyenātivadatī" tyatra tuśabdasatyaśabdābhyāṃ prāṇātivādino'nyaḥ satyādivādī pratīyate| "apaśavovā anya goaśvebhyaḥ paśavo goaśvā"nityatra paśvantarebhyogo aśvavat prakṛtaprāṇātivādinaḥ satyādivādī praśasta ityapi pratīyate| tataśca tasmādetasya prāśastyaṃ tadativadananimittāt prāṇāt etadativadananimittasya satyasyādhikyenaiveti paryavasyatītyanena vākyena prakṛtājjīvāt adhikatvena bhūmopādiśyate| nacātra "eṣatu vā agnihotrī"ti vākyānyāyāvatāraḥ, tatrahi dravyadevatā'ntarābhāvena agnihotrāṃtarapratīteḥ tuśabdasvārasyabhaṅgaḥ, iha prakṛtātivadananimittaprāṇavyatiriktasatyaśabdokta paramātmarūpātivadananimittāntarapratīteḥ kimiti tatsvārasyabhaṅgaḥ kāryaḥ| satyaśabdohi paramātmavācakaḥ, chāṃdogyaeva "tasya brahmaṇonāma satyamitī" ti śravaṇāt| "satyane" ti tṛtīyāvibhaktiḥ karaṇatvavācinī tasyātivadananimittatvamāha| atastuśabdasyanāmākaraṇavibhaktisvarasyabhaṅgena prakṛtaprāṇādivādiviṣayatvakakalpanāyā nāvakāśaḥ| ataśca prakṛtāt prāṇaśabdoktāt jīvādadhikatvenopadeśāt
satyaśabdoktobhūmā paramātmā| naca "mahataḥ paramavyakta"miti śrutau jīvādadhikatvenopadiṣṭe śarīre vyabhicārānnāyaṃ heturiti vācyam| jīvādhikātmatvenopadeśe tātparyāt| evaṃ niratiśayānandatvaśravaṇādapi bhūmā paramātmā| nanu satyaśabdoktobhūmaiva prakaraṇī paramātmā, prāṇaśabdoktojīvastu tadadhastanaparvabhūta iti siddhānte "asti bhagavonāmnobhūyaḥ vāgvāvanāmnobhūyasī" tyādi praśrnaprativacanābhyāṃ| na hi yadarthamanyatprakrāṃtaṃ tadanuktiryuktiticet, ucyate - pūrvaparyāyeṣvanuktasyātivāditvasya prāṇaparyāye pratipādanāt upakrāṃtātmopadeśaḥ prāṇaeva samāpta iti matvā nārade bhūya - pṛcchati sanatkumāraḥ prāṇavijñānamapyukrāṃtātmaviṣayaṃ nabhavatīti na tena samyagativāditvaṃ kiṃtu satyena paramātmaneti satyavijñānaṃ svayameva upacikṣepa, tataḥ "sohaṃ bhagavassatyenātivadānī"ti pṛṣṭavate tasmai vijñānamatiśraddhādisādhanaraparaṃparayā tameva satyaśabdoktaṃ paramātmānaṃ bhūmānamupadideśa ityevamanyathopapannau praśrnaprativacanābhāvau| nanu "yatranānya" dityādiviṣayavākyasya yasmin adhikaraṇe anyannapaśyatītyādyarthapratīteḥ brahmaṇi darśanaśravaṇādigocarasya sarvasya mithyātvena nirviśeṣaviṣayantadbhūmavākyaṃ pratibhāti, kathantadanantakalyāṇuguṇākarasya bhagavatapratipādakaṃ varṇyate, kathañca "napaśyomṛtyuṃ paśyati narogaṃ nota duḥkhatām| sarvaṃ hi paśyaḥ paśyati sarvamāpnoti sarvaśa" iti mantreṇa bhūmadarśinaḥ sarvadarśitvaṃ varṇayatā'pi duḥkhādarśanaṃ pratipādyate| ucyate - tatra "yasmi" nnityatra anubhūyamānaityadhyāhāraḥ kāryaḥ| na hi brahmasadbhāvamātrāttasminnanyasyādarśanādikaṃ bhavati, asmadādīnāmapi ddaśyadarśanādyabhāvāpatteḥ| "yatre" tyasya ca nijasakalaguṇavibhūtiviśiṣṭaityarthaḥ| bhūmaśabdasya brahmaśabdavat trividhaparicchedarāhityārthakatvāt tadvaiśiṣṭyena ca darśanaṃ ca satyeva ca sarvasya tadantargatatvāt nijasakalaguṇavibhūtiviśiṣṭāt tasmādanyasya tataḥ pṛthagbhūtasyādarśanādivacanamupapadyate| yaditu vibhūtiraiśvaryaṃ niyamanarūpaṃ, tadā tanniyāmyasya pratītisāpekṣamiti niyāmyarūpapratiyogitayā sarvasyāpi darśanādikamopekṣitaṃ| astvevaṃ, tathāpi kathaṃ duḥkhādarśanamupapādānīyaṃ, bhūmavibhūtyantargatatayā sakala saṃsārigataduḥkhānāmapi darśanasyānivāraṇāt| tataśca samanvayādhikaraṇoktarītyā sukhasākṣātkāramātrasya puruṣārthatvavat duḥkhasākṣātkāramātrasya heyatvaṃ vācyamiti bhūmānaṃ paśyato muktasya saṃsāradaśāpekṣayā'pyadhikaduḥkhānubhavatvaṃ syāt| na ca brahmavibhūtitayā anubhūyamānaṃ sarvaṃ sukharūpameva syāditivācyaṃ| brahmaṇo'nyatayā saṃsāribhiranubhūyamānaṃ duḥkharūpaṃ parimitasukharūpañca bhavat brahmavibhūtitvenānubhūyamānaṃ sukharūpaṃ bhavatītyasya viruddhatvāt, ekasyasukhaduḥkhobhayarūpatānupapatteriticet, ucyate - karmavaśyairbrahmānyatvenānubhūyamānaṃ jagat teṣāṃ tattatkarmānurūpaṃ duḥkharūpaṃ parimitasukharūpañca bhavati, karmarūpāvidyāvimuktasya tadeva jagat vibhūtiguṇaviśiṣṭabrahmānubhavāntargataṃ sukhameva bhavati, yathā pittopahatena pīyamānaṃ payaḥ pittatāratamyena duḥkharūpamalpasukhañca bhavati, tadeva payaḥ pittānupahatena pīyamānaṃ sukhātmakameva bhavati, yadā hi yadanubhūyamānaṃ puruṣānukūlaṃ bhavati tadā tatsukhaṃ, yadātadananukūlaṃ tadā tadeva duḥkhaṃ| ataeva himātapādīnāṃ nidāghahematādyavasthābhedena sukhaduḥkhobhayarūpatvamiti na kācidanupapattiḥ| yattu "ahamevādhastā"dityādi "saevedaṃ sarva " mityantasya svarūpeṇopāsanārtha, "ātmaivādhastādityādi "ātmaivedaṃ sarva " mityantaṃ vidvadantaryāmitvenopāsanārthaṃ, "tasyaha vā etasyaivaṃpaśyata evaṃ
manvānasyaivaṃ vijānata ātmataḥ prāṇaātmataāśā ātmatassamara " ityādinā prakṛtādātmanaḥ prāṇādyutpattipratipādane prakṛtasyātmano vidvāṃsaṃ pratyātmatvaṃ viśiṣyoktaṃ| evaṃ tredhā tasya sarvātmyopadeśaḥ trividhopāsanāsiddhyarthaṃ iti na kasyāpi vaiyarthyamiti vivektavyam||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
bhūmā prāṇastasmāt ūrdhvannāmna iva vāgādeḥ|
praśnaprativacanābhyāṃ vinaiva bhūmno'vatāraṇataḥ||
prāṇaśca jīvaeva sphuṭamātmopakramopasaṃhārāt|
mātṛtvādiśravaṇāt hiṃsyatvokterahaṃtvavacanācca||
satyaṃ prāṇo jīvaḥ sa na bhūmā kiṃ tu puruṣa eva paraḥ|
eṣa tu vā ativadatītyatra tuśabdena pūrvavinivṛtteḥ||
niratiśayānandatvaśravaṇādapi yatra nānyadityādau|
ahamevādhastādityahaṃ graho darśitastasmin||
iti bhūmādhikaraṇam||2||
akṣaramambarāntadhṛteḥ||9||
sā ca praśāsanāt||10||
anyabhāvavyāvṛtteśca||11||
yathā jīvādūrdhvapadeśāt bhūmā na jīvaḥ, evaṃ pradhānādūrdhvamupadeśāt akṣaraṃ na pradhānamiti samarthyataiti saṅgatiḥ| vājasaneyinassamāmanaṃti - "etadvai tadakṣaraṃ gārgi brāhmaṇā abhivadanti asthūlamanaṇvahrasvamadīrghamalohitamasnehamacchāya " mityādi| atrākṣaraṃ pradhānaṃ paramātmāveti saṃśaye pradhānamiti tāvatprāptam| "akṣarātparataḥ para " ityādiṣu akṣaraśabdasya pradhāne prayogāt| tathā "tadakṣaramadhigamyata " ityādiṣu paramātmani tasya prayogasattve'pi pramāṇāntaraprasiddhaśrutiprasiddhayoḥ pradhānaparamātmanormadhye pramāṇāṃtaraprasiddhasya prathānasya jhaṭiti buddhyāroheṇa prathamapratītatvāt anumānasya pratyakṣamātrāpekṣāpekṣā śrutervyutpattigrahādāvubhayāpekṣeti ānumānisyākṣarasya śrutipratipannāt paramātmanaśśīghrapratītikasya śrutiliṃgādiṣu pūrvapūrvasya uttarottarasmādiva tataḥ prabalatvāt asthūtvādīnāṃ pradhāne'pi saṃbhavena bhūtayonyakṣaraiva paramātmaikāṃtikaliṅgābhāvāt| evaṃprāptesiddhāṃtaḥ - akṣaraṃparamātmā, nāvyākṛtaṃ pradhānam| "etasmin khalvakṣare gārgyākāśaotaśca protaśca"tyavyākṛtādhāratayā'kṣarasyopadeśāt| syādetat, ākāśaśābdoyaṃ bhūtākāśaparo'stu, prasivdyatilaṅghanāyogāt| yadyucyeta akṣarabrāhmaṇātpūrvaṃ gārgibrāhmaṇe gārgiyājñavalkyayoḥ praśnottaraparaṃparāyāṃ "yadidaṃ sarvamapsvotañca protañca kasminnu khalvāpaotāśca protāśceti vāyaugārgīti kasminnu khalu vāyurotaśca protaśceti antarikṣalokeṣu gārgi"tyārabhya, "brahmalokeṣu gārgi" tyantena sandarbheṇa pṛthivīsalilavāyvantarikṣalokagandharvalokādityalokacandralokanakṣatralokadevalokeṃdralokaprajāpatilogabrahmalokānāṃ pūrvapūrvasya uttarottasmin otatvaprotatvavarṇanānantaraṃ "kasminnu khalu brahmalokā otāśca protāśca"ti punaḥ pṛṣṭe yājñavalkyena "gārgi mātiprākṣīrmā te mūrdhā vyapaptadanatipraśanyo vai daivatāmatipṛcchasi mātiprākṣī" riti sakrodhamuktā bhītā gārgyupararāma| anena brahmalokāṃtasakalalokādhāratayā vaktvyā mahatī kāciddevateti sūcitaṃ| tataśca bahuṣubrāhmaṇeṣu yājñavalkyavijigīṣayā tena saha jalpakathāyāṃ paryāyeṇa pravartamāneṣvanupraviṣṭā gārgī tadānīṃ bhītyoparatā'pi svoparamānantaramuddālake prasiddhabrahmavidi sūtramantaryāmiṇañca pṛṣṭavati yājñavalkyena jite sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇānāmeva parījayojāyate, samābhūditi manyamānā yājñavalkyaṃ prakṣyāmīti brāhmaṇānanujñāpya "ahaṃ vai yājñavalkya yathā kāśyovā vaidehovā ugraputraujjayaṃ dhanuradhijyaṃ kṛtvā dvau bāṇavantau śarau sapatnātivyāyinau" haste kṛtvopottiṣṭhedevamevāhaṃ tvā dvābhyāṃ praśnābhyāṃ upodasthāṃ tau me brūhī"ti dvitīyapraśnanivāraṇaparijihīrṣayā praśnadvayānujñāṃ yugapadeśa yājñavalkyaṃ pratipṛṣṭvā pṛcchati tadanujñātā "yadūrdhvaṃ yājñavalkya divo yadavākpṛthivyā yadantarā dyāvāpṛthivī ime yadbhūtañca bhavacca bhaviṣyaccetyāccatyācakṣate kasmin tadotañca protañca"ti pṛṣṭvā "yadūrdhvaṃ gārgi diva" ityādinā "ākāśaevatadotañca protaṃce"tyantenottarite "kasminnu khalvākāśaotaśca protaśce"ti dvitīyapraśnasya uttaratayā "etadvaitadakṣara"mityādi "etasmin khalvakṣare gārgyākāśaotaśca prodaśce"tyantaṃ kṛtsnamakṣarabrāhmaṇaṃ pravṛttaṃ| tatra gārgibrāhmaṇāṃte yājñavalkyena yaḥ praśnassakrodhaṃ nivāritaḥ, saeva khalu gārgyā sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ parājayobhaviṣyatīti bhītayā tasya prāgvaktvyatayā upakṣiptā devatā seha ākāśaśabdenoditeti sūcitaṃ| evañca kathamayamākāśaśabdo bhūtākāśaparassyāditi tarhi pradhānaparopi nasyāt, tasyāpi devatātvābhāvāt| tasmāt prathānaparatvavādinā itthaṃ samādheyaṃ| pūrvaṃ brahmalokāṃtasalalokādhārapraśne yadavyavahitottaraṃ tatparadevātaviṣayamityāśayena na tannivāraṇaṃ, kiṃtu dhṛṣṭaiṣā tasyāpyādhāraṃ prakṣyati, sapraśnaḥ paradevatāviṣayobhaviṣyatīti dūraddaṣṭyā tannivāraṇaṃ kṛtamiti, tasyākāśaśabdasya bhūtākāśaviṣayatvepyevameva samādhātuṃ śakyamiti cet, ucyate - "yadūrdhvaṃ gārgidiva" ityādinā "ākāśaśabdasya bhūtākāśaparatve ghaṭate, tasyāpi vikārāṃtargatatvāt, tasmādihākāśapadamavyākṛtaparamiti tasyāpyādhāratayopadiśyamānamakṣaraṃ paramātmaiva| nāvyaktam| yattu śrutiprasiddhāt pramāṇātaraprasiddhaṃ prathamaṃ pratīyata iti tanna| akṣaraśabdasya aśnoti vyāpnotīti avayavaśaktyā
svārthapratipādane pramāṇāntarānapekṣaṇāt vyutpattidaśāyāmarthasvarūpaṃ yena pramāṇenāvagataṃ tasya pratipādanadaśāyāmanupayogāt| uktaṃ hi vārtike - "arthabodhenapekṣatvānmānāṃtaramanāddataṃ| vyutpattyupāyabhūtaṃ yat tacca tanmātraeva tvi" ti| kiṃca gośābdādisambandhagrahaviṣayasāsnāvavdyaktyādiṣviva pradhāne pratyakṣe na pravartate, anumānamapi sāṃkhyādyabhimataṃ tatra na pravartata iti sāṃkhyādhikaraṇe vakṣyate, tataśca brahmavat pradhānamapi śrutyekagamyamiti na prasidhyaprasiddhikṛto'pi tayorviśeṣaḥ, tasmāt
sarvavikārādhāratvaliṃgena ākāśaśabdoditamavyākṛtamityavagamāt, akṣaraśabdodita mavyākṛtamityatra hetvasiddheśca, ākāśaśabdoditasya avyākṛtasyādhāratayocyamānamakṣaraṃ paramātmaiva| "tadā etadakṣaraṃ gārgyaddaṣṭaṃ draṣṭraśrutaṃ śrotṛ amataṃ mantṛ avijñātaṃ vijñātṛ" iti draṣṭṭacvādyupadeśādapyakṣaraṃ paramātmā, natvavyākṛtaṃ| nanu tarhyakṣaraṃ jīvāstu, tatra darśanādyaviṣayatvasya darśanādimattvasya copapatteḥ| "yathā vā arānābhau samarpitā evamasmin prāṇe sarvaṃ samarpita"miti śravaṇena tasyasakalacidvargādhāratvopapatteśceticet maiva - "etasya vā akṣarasya praśāsane gārgi dyāvāpṛthivyau vidhṛte tiṣṭhata" ityādinā praśāsanādhīnadhārakatvaśravaṇāt| yadyapi paryāyeṇa devamanuṣyādijanmavati jīvepi dyāvāpṛthivyādiśāsitṛtvaṃ tattadadikāropayuktajanmaprāptyākathaṃcitsamarthayituṃ śakyaṃ, tathāpi nirapekṣaśāsitṛtvarūpaṃ prakṛṣṭaśāsitṛtvaṃ tatpūrvakajacandrasūryādidhārakatvañcāsminnasambhavatyeva| kiṃca sarvairaddaṣṭasya satassarvadraṣṭṭatvādikaṃ "nānyadato'sti draṣṭṭa nānyadato'sti śrotṛ" ityādinākriyamāṇaṃ draṣṭrantaraniṣedhādikañca jīve nopapadyate, kiṃtu antaryāmyadhikaraṇoktarītyā paramātmanyevopapadyate| tasmādakṣaraṃ paramātmaiva||
saṃgrahākārikā||
akṣaramanumitigamyā prakṛtirnāgamanirūpitaṃ brahma|
anumānamakṣaramātrāpekṣaṃ sahi taddvayāpekṣaḥ||
maivamavyākṛtadhṛteḥ śrautaṃ brahma tadakṣaraṃ|
śruterapekṣā vyutpattau na tyarthapratipādane||
draṣṭṭatvaśrotṛtvapramukhā nāvyākṛtaṃ hi paśyaṃti|
na ca jīvo'kṣarametat praśāsitṛtvādivaighaṭyāt||
iti akṣarādhikaraṇam||3||
īkṣatikarmavyapadeśātsaḥ||12||
praśnopaniṣadi - "sayoha vai tadbhagavanmanuṣyeṣu prāyaṇāṃtarmokāmabhidhyāyīta katamaṃ vāva sa tena lokraṃ jayatī" ti pippalādaṃ prati satyakāmapraśne "etadvai satyakāma parañcāparañca brahma yadoṅkārastasmādvidvānetenaivāyatanena ekataramanvetī"ti tatprativacanamupakramya "sa yadyekamātramabhidhyāyīta sa tenaiva saṃveditastūrṇameva jagatyāmabhisamabhisampadyate tamṛco manuṣyalokamupanayante sa tatra tapasā brahmacaryeṇa śraddhayā sampanno mahimānamanubhavatyatha yo dvimātreṇa manasi sampadyate so'ntarikṣaṃ yajurbhirunnīyate somalokaṃ sa somaloke vibhūtimanubhūya punarāvartata " ityekamātramoṅkāramupāsīnasya tatprathamamātrātmakaṛkprāpaṇīyamanuṣyalokaprāptiphalaṃ dvimātraṃ tamupāsīnasya taddvitīyamātrātmakayajuḥ prāpaṇīyāṃtarikṣalokaprāptiphalañcābhidhāya idamāmnāyate - " yaḥ punaretaṃ trimātreṇa omityanenaivākṣareṇa paraṃpuruṣamabhidhyāyīta sa tejasi sūrye saṃpanno yathā pādodarastvacā vinirmucyata evaṃ haiva sa pāpmanā vinirmuktaḥ sa sāmabhirunnīyate brahmalokaṃ sa etasmājjīvaghanātparātparaṃ puriśayaṃ puruṣamīkṣata" iti| atra dhyātavyaḥ paramapuruṣaevekṣaṇīyaḥ| phalaphalinorīkṣaṇadhyānayorekaviṣayatvaniyamāt, "paraṃ puruṣa "mityatra paratvāvadhitvena vivakṣitasyāpi kebhyaścit paratvasambhavena "parātparaṃ puriśayaṃ puruṣa "miti nirdeśasya tato bhedāvahatvābhāvādita sthite dhyātavya īkṣaṇīyaśca puruṣaḥ kiṃ jīvasamaṣṭirūpaścaturmukhaḥ, uta sarveśvara iti saṃśaye caturmukha iti tāvatprāptam| brahmalokaśabdoditacaturmukhalokagatenekṣyamāṇatvāt| na ca brahmalokaśabdo bhagavallokapara iti vācyam| manuṣyalokāntarikṣalokaprāyapāṭhena tasyāpi prākṛtacaturmukhalokaparatvāt| na ca jīvaghaśabdo jīvasamaṣṭirūpacaturmukhavācīti tasmātparaḥ puruṣassarveśvara eva syāditi vācyam| tasya
vyaṣṭijīvasaṃghātavācitvopapatteḥ| "ghanāḥ kaṭhinasaṃghātameghakāṭhinyamudgarā" iti saṃghāte'pi ghanaśabdasmaraṇāt| na ca tathā'pi vyaṣṭijīvasaṃghātāt parassamaṣṭijīva iti tasmātparaḥ puruṣassarveśvara eva syāditi vācyam| dehendriyādibhyaḥ parādvyaṣṭijīvasaṃghātāt caturmukhasya paratvopapatteḥ| parajīvaghanaśabdayossāmānādhikaraṇyasambhave vaiyadhikaraṇyakalpanāyogāt| na ca "ṛgbhiretaṃ yajurbhirantarikṣaṃ sāmabhiryattatkavayo vedayante tamoṅkāreṇānveti vidvān yattacchāṃtamajaramamṛtamabhayaṃ parañce" ti tadanantaraślokoktamamṛtatvādikaṃ sarveśvara evānvetīti tadanusāreṇa paraḥ puruṣassarveśvarassyāt dhyātavyāsyaiva prāpyatvāditi
vācyam| upakramānusāreṇa aupasaṃhīrikāmṛtatvādeścaturmukhaevāpekṣikāmṛtatvādirūpatayā anvayopapatteriti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
īkṣatikarma paramātmā| uttaramantragatāt amṛtatvādivyapadeśāt,etasmājjīvaghanātparāpara"miti vyapadeśācca jīvatvāviśeṣeṇa hiraṇyagarbhasyāpi jīvaghanakoṭiniviṣṭatvāt| brahmalokaśabdaśca bhagavallokaparaḥ| sarvapāpavinirmuktaprāpyatvāt| "yattatkavayo vedayanta"ityatra "tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyaṃti sūraya"ityādiśrutyantarasiddhakaviśabdoktasūriddaśyatvaliṅgāt "etadvai satyakāme"tyādinā upakrāntaparopāsanāprakaraṇācca liṃgaprakaraṇaiḥ prāyapāṭhasya bādhena tasya akiñcitkaratvāt| nanu upakrame paropāsanāvat aparopāsanasyāpi śravaṇāt tadapi nirupaṇāyamiticet satyam| pūrvameva tannirūpitam| pūrvaṃ hi tadupāsanaṃ aihikāmuṣmikatvena dvidhā vibhajya ekamātrapraṇavena tadupāsanasya manuṣyalokāvāptiṃ, dvimātrapraṇavena tadupāsanasya svargāvāptiñca upanyasya hi trimātrapraṇavena paramapuruṣopāsanaṃ prakrāntam| tasmāt dhyānekṣaṇaviṣayabhūtaḥ paraḥ puruṣaḥ sarveśvara iti siddham||
saṃgrahakārike||
ādiṣṭassatyakāmaṃ pratihi puriśayaḥ pūruṣohyeṣa vedhāḥ
puṃsā lokaṃ tadīyaṃ samadhigatavatā vīkṣyamāṇatvaliṅgāt|
ukto'tra brahmaloko na bhavati hi parabrahmalokastadā hi
syāduktaikadvimātrapraṇavavidi phalaprāyapāṭhasya bādhaḥ||
viṣṇureṣa vijarāmṛtatokteḥ sarvajīvaparatāvyapadeśāt|
brahmalokamiti tatpadamuktaṃ liṃgato bhavatu sannidhibādhaḥ||
iti īkṣatikarmādhikaraṇam||4||
daharuttarebhyaḥ||13||
gatiśabdābhyāṃ tathā hi dṛṣṭaṃ liṅgañca||14||
dhṛteśca mahimno'syāsminnupalabdheḥ||15||
prasiddheśca||16||
iraparāmarśātsa iti cennāsambhavāt||17||
uttarāccedāvirbhūtasvarūpastu||18||
anyārthaśca parāmarśaḥ||19||
alpaśruteriticettaduktam||20||
anukṛtestasya ca||21||
api smaryate||22||
pūrvādhikaraṇe "parātparaṃ puruśayaṃ puruṣamīkṣata" ityuktasya puriśayasya paramātmatvaṃ samarthitam| ihāpi tathaiva puriśayasya paramātmatvaṃ samarthyata iti saṅgatiḥ| chāndogye - "atha yadidamasmin brahmapure daharaṃ puṃḍarīkaṃ veśmadaharosminnantarākāśastasmin yadantastadanveṣṭavyaṃ tadvāva vijijñāsitavyaḥ" miti brahmapuraśabdoktaśarīrāntarvartihṛdayapuṇḍarīkamadhyavartitvena śrutau daharākāśo bhūtākāśaḥ|"tasminyadanta"riti daharākāśe'ntaranveṣṭavyatayā śrutaḥ paramātmā| athavā daharākāśaḥ paramātmā| "tasmin yadanta"riti śrutaḥ tadīyo'pahatāmatvādiguṇanivaha iti saṃśaye bhūtākāśa iti tāvatprāptaṃ, atra rūḍheḥ| "tasmin yadanta" riti śrutastu
paramātmā, na tu tadīyaguṇanivahaḥ| "tadanveṣṭavya"mityasya "tasminyadanta" rityetanmātrānvayitvena guṇamātrasyopāsyatvāpattyā guṇino'nupāsyatvaprasaṅgāt | evañca vākyaśeṣe śrūyamāṇāḥ sarve paramātmadharmāḥ "tasmin yadanta"riti nirdiṣṭe paramātmanyanvayaṃ labhanta iti na teṣāmapyanupapattirita| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - daharākāśaḥ paramātmā| na bhūtākāśaḥ| vākyaśeṣagatebhyo hetubhyaḥ| vākyaśeṣe hi "yāvanvā ayamākāśaḥ tāvāneṣo'ntarhṛdaya ākāśa" iti daharākāśasya bhūtākāśāt bhedāpekṣaḥ| tenopamānopameyabhāvo nibadhyate| tadanantaraṃ "asmindyāvāpṛthivī antareva samāhite ubhāvagniśca vāyuśca sūryācandramasāvubhau
vidyunnakṣatrāṇī"ti prakṛtaṃ daharākāśaṃ asminniti nirdiśya tasya sarvajagadādhāratvamucyate| tato "yaccāsyehāsti yacca nāsti sarvaṃ tadasmin samāhita" mityasyopāsakasya loke yadbhogyajātaṃ vartate yacca manorathamātraspadamiha nāsti sarvaṃ tadbhogyajātamasmin daharākāśe samāhitamiti tasya niratiśayabhogyatvena tasya niratiśayānandatvaṃ pradarśyate| "tadetatsatyaṃ brahmapura" miti tameva daharākāśametacchabdena parāmṛśya tasya satyabhūtabrahmākhyapurarūpatvamucyate| "brahmapura" mityatra niṣādasthapatinyāyena sāmānādhikaraṇyaucityāt| tataḥ "asminkāmāssamāhitā" iti tameva daharākāśaṃ "asminniti" nirdiśya kāmyabhūtāṃśca guṇān "kāmā" iti nirdiśya teṣāṃ daharākāśāntarvartitvamuktvā tadeva daharākāśasya kāmyabhūtakalyāṇaguṇaviśiṣṭatvaṃ tasyātmatvañca "eṣa ātmā'pahapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko valijighatso'pipāsassatyakāmassatyasaṅkalpa" iti varṇyate| tataḥ "atha ya ihātmānamanuvidya vrajantyetāṃśca satyānkāmāṃsteṣāṃ sarveṣu lokaṣu kāmacāro bhavatī"tyātmaśabdena parāmṛṣṭaṃ tadantarvartinaśca kāmyānapahatapāpmatvādiguṇāṃśca anusandadhatāṃ phalaṃ pradarśyate| tathā "tadyathā'pi hiraṇyanidhiṃ nihitamakṣetrajñā uparyupari saṃcaranto navindeyurevamevemāssarvāḥ prajā aharahargacchantya etaṃ brahmalokaṃ na vindantyanṛtena hi pratyūḍhā" iti "eta"miti prakṛtaṃ daharākāśaṃ nirdiśya tatra aharahassarveṣāṃ gamanaṃ gantavyasya tasya brahmalokaśabdena nirdeśaśca dṛśyate| paramātmani khalu sarveṣāmaharahassuṣuptikāle gamanamanyatrābhidhīyamānaṃ dṛṣṭam, "evameva khalu somyemāssarvāḥ prajāssatisampadya naviduḥ satisampatsyāmaha" iti| brahmalokaśabdaśca tasmindṛṣṭa "eṣa brahmalokassamrāḍiti hovāce" ti | tathā "atha ya ātmā sa seturvidhṛtireṣāṃ lokānāmasambhedāye"ti, prakṛtadaharākāśaṃ "ya ātme" ti nirdiśya tasya jagadvidhārakatvaṃ pratipādyate| saca paramātmano mahimā| "eṣa sarveśvara eṣa bhūtādhipatireṣa bhūtapāla eṣa seturvidharaṇa eṣāṃ lokānāmasambhedāye"ti śrutyantarāt| etebhyo vākyaśeṣagatebhyo hetubhyo daharākāśaḥ paramātmā| ākāśaśabdasya hiparamātmanyapi vaidikī prasiddhirapyasti "ākāśa iti hovāce"tyādiśrutyantare| nanu yadi "tasmiyadanta"rityetat paramātmavartiguṇanivahaparaṃ syāt, na tu prādhānyena daharākāśasyāpi| tasyāpi prādhānyena upāsyatvamupapādanīyam| "atha ya ihātmānamanuvidya vrajatyetāṃśca satyā kāmā"niti vākyaśeṣānusārāditi cet| satyaṃ itthaṃ tadupapādanaṃ| "tadanveśaṣṭavya"mityatra tatpadena prathamānirdiṣṭamantarvartimātraṃ na parāmṛśyatesa, kiṃ tu saptamīnirdiṣṭo daharākāśo'pi| "tyadādīni sarvairnityaṃ, napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyā"miti sūtrairnapuṃsakaikaśeṣasya ekavacanasyacopapatterityuktaṃ bhāṣyādiṣu| atredaṃ vicāryate - taditi napuṃsaka śeṣatvaṃ "tyadādīni sarvairnitya" miti parasūtreṇaiva hi vaktavyam| tatra naikavadbhāvānuvṛttirasti| "ekavaccāsyānyatarasyā"mityatra
asyetadyasya napuṃsakasūtrakṛtaikaśeṣasyaivāyamekavadbhāva ityetadarthatvāt| na ca vācyaṃ tyadādiṣu napuṃsakānapuṃsakayornapuṃsakaśeṣasidhyarthaṃ "napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyā"miti sūtrasyāpyanuvṛttyā bhāvyamiti tyadādiṣu sa ca sā ca tāviti srīpuṃsakayoḥ puṃśeṣasidhyarthaṃ sa ca tacca te sā ca tacca te iti napuṃsakānapuṃsakayornapuṃsakaśeṣasidhyarthaṃ ca "tyadāditaśśeṣe puṃnapuṃsakato liṃgavacanā"nīti vārtikakṛtāyatnāntarasya kṛtatvāt tathaikavadbhāvasidhyarthaṃ bhāṣyavārtikādiṣu yatnāntaradarśanāt| kathaṃ tarhi taṃtrarūpe "dadhi madhu ghṛtaṃ payo dhānā udakaṃ taṃḍulāstatsaṃsṛṣṭaṃ prājāpatya"miti citreṣṭividhivākye dadhyādipratyekadravyako yāgagaṇaḥ na tu saṃsṛṣṭaikadravyako yāga iti siddhāntasiddhaye tatpadaṃ saṃsṛṣṭaśabdoktaikadravyaparaṃ na bhavati, kiṃ tu dadhyādyanekadravyaparamityuktvā dadhyādiṣu taditi napuṃsakaikaśeṣābhyāṃ nirvyūḍhuṃ abhyuccāyārthaṃ tathā nirvāhāntaraṃ kṛtam| sarvanāmnāṃ prakrāntaparatva eva svārasyena prakraṃsyamānaparatvasyānanyagatikatvāt dravyaviśeṣasyaivākāṃkṣāpūrakatvena saṃsṛṣṭamātrasya prajāpatisambandhānarhatvācca nyāyatastatpadasya dadhyādyanekadravyaparatvāvaśyaṃ bhāvesati
tadityekavacanopāttamekatvaṃ "guṇetvanyāyyakalpane"ti nyāyena pāśabahutvavadanivakṣitamiti mukhyahetuktayanantaraṃ athavā "napuṃsakanamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyā"miti smaraṇāt napuṃsakānapuṃsakānekābhiprāyamupi yuktameva taditi napuṃsakaikavacanaṃ evaṃ prājāpatyamapi draṣṭavyamityuktatvāditi| atredaṃ samādhānaṃ "anveṣṭavya"mityatra napuṃsakānapuṃsakaikaśeṣe kṛte sati tatraikasminnartha ivaikavacanarupaṃ kāryaṃ bhavat tadviśeṣaṇe tadarthānupapattini sarvanāmnyāpi bhavati, yathā "asmadodvayośce"ti sūtreṇāsmadarthasya ekatve dvitve ca bahuvacanaṃ bhavat asmadarthavartini kriyāpade'pi bhavati vayaṃ brūmaiti ihāpi "śuklaśca śuklā ca śuklañca śuklaṃ vartata" iti kriyāpade'pi ekavacanaṃ dṛśyate tadvat sarvanāmnyapi bhavitumarhati| ata eva mahābhāṣye - "śuklaśca kamvalaḥ śuklā ca bṛhatikā śuklañca vasraṃ taditaṃ śuklaṃ tānīmāni śuknālī"ti dvividhe napuṃsakasūtrodāharaṇe kṛtaikavadbhāvameva taditi padaṃ prathamodāharaṇaviśeṣaṇaṃ kṛtam| eṃ citreṣṭivākye'pi prājāpatyaśabde napuṃsakasūtrapravṛttau,
tadantarvartini taditi pade'pi napuṃsakaikavacanaṃ bhavatīti tantraratnābhiprāyaḥ| evamihāpi "anveṣṭavya" mityatra napuṃsakasūtreṇaikavadbhāve kṛte tadantavartini taditi pade'pi napuṃkaliṃgamekavacanaṃ ca yuktameva| tasmāddaharākāśaḥ svāntarvartyapahatapāpmatvādiguṇanivahena sahopāsyaḥ paramātmaiva| na bhūtākāśa iti siddham| nanu mābhūdayaṃ bhūtākāśaḥ, jīvastu syāt, daharavidyāprakaraṇe - "atha ya eṣa saṃprasādo'smāccharīrātsamutthāya paraṃjyotirupasampadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyata" iti tasya parāmarśāditi cenna| tatra apahatapāpmatvādyasambhavāt| nanu daharavākyāduttarasmin prājāpativākye jīvasyāpyapahatapāpmatvādikamāmnātam| tatra hi "ya ātmā'pahatapāptmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko vijighatso'pipāsassatyakāmassatyasaṅkalpasso'nveṣṭavyassavijijñāsitavyassasarvāṃśca lokānāpnoti sarvāṃśca kāmān yastamātmānamanuvidya vijānātī" ti prājāpativacanamaitihyarūpeṇopaśrutya anveṣṭavyātmajijñāsayā prājāpatimupaseduṣe maghavate prajāpatinā "ya eṣo'kṣṇi puruṣo dṛśyate eṣa ātme"ti "ya eṣa svapne mahīyamānaśca ratyeṣa ātme"ti ca ājāgarādyavasthātrayavān jīva eva jijñāsitāpahatapāpmatvādi guṇakātmetyupadiṣṭaḥ| satyam| saṃsāradaśāyāmavidyātirohitāpahatapāpmatvādiguṇako jīvaḥ paścādvimuktakarmarūpāvidyābandhaḥ śarīrāt samutthitaḥ paraṃjotirupasampannassannapahatapāpmatvādiguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ prajāpativākye'bhidhīyate| "eṣa saṃprasādo'smāccharīrātsamutthāya paraṃjyotirupasampadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyata" iti jīvasya paraṃjyotiḥ prāptyā tasyāpahatapāpmatvādiguṇakasvarūpābhiniṣpattimuktvā prāpyasya paraṃjyotiṣaḥ "sa uttamaḥ puruṣa" iti puruṣottamatvaṃ vidhāya "sa tatra paryeti jakṣatkrījan ramamāṇassrībhirvā yānairvā jñātibhirve"tyādinā taṃ prāpnuvato jīvasya tallokānubhāvyamahāphalakīrtanāt| daharavākye tu nityāvirbhūtāpahatapāpmatvādiguṇa eva daharākāśaḥ pratīyate| tathābhūtāpahatapāpmatvādigrahaṇasambhave kāraṇādhīnāvirbhāvasya tasya grahaṇāyogāt| tasmāddaharākāśe jīvatvāśaṅkā na kāryā| yadyevaṃ daharavidyāprakaraṇe - "asmāccharīrātsamutthāye"tyādinā jīvaparāmarśaḥ kimarthaḥ, daharākāśasya jagatvidharaṇādivat jīvassvarūpāvirbhāvāpādānarūpamahimaviśeṣapratipādanārthaḥ prajāpativākyasiddhasyārthasyāyamanuvādaḥ| nanu yadi daharo na jīvaḥ, kathaṃ tarhyalpaparimāṇaśravaṇamiti cet, atra vaktavyamuttaram "sarvatra prasiddhopadeśā" dityadhikaraṇe "nicāyyatvādevaṃ vyomavacce" ti pūrvamevoktam| nanu saṃsāradaśātirohitajaivāpahatapāpmatvādivyatirekeṇa nityāvibhūtamanyadapahatapāpmatvādikamastītyatraiva na pramāṇam| daharavidyāprakaraṇe apahatapāpmatvādiśravaṇasya prajāpativākyasiddhasaṃpratipannajaivāpahatapāpmatvādiviṣayatvopapatteḥ| ato nityāvirbhūtairapahatapāpmatvādibhiḥ daharo na jīva ityetannayuktam| kiṃtu sūkṣmatvaliṃgāt daharo jīva ityeva yaktamiticeducyate - paramātmānukārarūpā tatsāmyāpattirhi muktiḥ| "yadā paśyaḥ paśyate rūkmavarṇa"mityādi śruteḥ, "idaṃ jñānamupāśritya mama sādharmyamāgatāḥ| sarge'pi nopajāyante pralaye na vyadhaṃtice"ti smṛteśca| evaṃ ca jīvasya muktāvapahatapāpmatvādiguṇāvirbhāvaḥ paramātmano nityāvirbhūtāpahatapāpmatvādiguṇakatvamantareṇa na sambhavati| anyathā jīvasya muktau tatsāmyāpattayayogāt| ato jīvasya muktidaśāvirbhāvavat jaivāpahatapāpmatvādapratipādakaprajāpativākyasāmarthyādeva parasya nityāvirbhūtamapahatapāpmatvādikamastīti siddheḥ tadviṣayatvaṃ daharavidyāprakaraṇaśrutasya "ya ātmā'pahatapāpme"tyāderyuktamiti tataḥ paramātmaiva daharākāśaḥ na jīva iti siddham||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
yukto daharākāśoḥ bhūtākāśaḥ prasiddhatvāt|
apahatapāpmatvādyā guṇāstadantaragatānvayinaḥ||
jīvovā'so śravaṇādamuṃ prakṛtyaiṣa saṃpradāya iti|
saṃti ca tatroktaguṇāḥ prājāpatyena vākyena||
maivaṃ paramātmā'sau svāminyabhihitaguṇebhya ityucitam|
tasmādyaditi guṇoktarguṇaviṣaya iti tadvṛttam||
nityāvirbhūtāste guṇā na santyeva jīvasya|
saṃti tu parasya liṃgānmuktestatparamasāmyarūpatvāt||
iti daharādhikaraṇam||5||
śabdādeva pramitaḥ||23||
tddadyapekṣayā tu manuṣyādhikāratvāt||24||
