pūrvatrāṇīyastvarūpaṃ alpaparimāṇaṃ parasminnupapāditam| idānīmaṃguṣṭhamātrarūpamapyalpaparimāṇaṃ tasminnupapādyata saṃgatiḥ| kaṭhavallīṣu śrūyate - "aṃguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo madhya ātmani tiṣṭhati| īśāno bhūtabhavyasya na tato vijugupsata" ityādiḥ| kimayamaṃguṣṭhapramito jīvaḥ paroveti saṃśaye jīva iti prāptam| "sa viṣvarūpasriguṇasrivartmā prāṇādhipassañcarati svakarmabhiḥ| aṃguṣṭhamātro ravituvalyarūpasaṅkalpāhaṅkārasamanvito ya" iti tasyāṃguṣṭhamātratvaśrayaṇāt| na tu paraḥ, tasya kvacidapi tathātvaśravaṇābhāvāt| evañcopakramabhūtāṃguṣṭhamātratvānusāreṇa bhūtabhavyeśānatvaśravaṇaṃ jīva eva dehendriyādiniyantṛtvādiviṣayatayā yojanīyamiti| nanu śvetāśvatare - "aṃguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo'ntarātmā sadā janānāṃ hṛdaye sanniviṣṭaḥ| hṛdā manīṣā manasābhiklṛpto ya etadviduramṛtāste bhavantī"ti, taittirīyake - "aṃguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo'guṣṭhañca samāśritaḥ| īśassarvasya jagataḥ prabhuḥ prīṇāti viśvabhu"giti parasyāpguṣṭhamātratvaṃ śrūyate| satyam| parasyāṃguṣṭhamātratvaśravaṇaṃ kvacidapi nāstīti kṛtvāciṃtayedamadhikaraṇam| yadvā tadapi maṃtradvayaṃ "aṃguṣṭhamātro ravitulyarūpa" iti mantrāntarānusāreṇa jīvaparamiti pūrvapakṣī manyate| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
aṃguṣṭhapramitaḥ paramātmā| bhūtabhaviṣyadātmakasarvajagadīśitṛtvaśravaṇāt| tasya dehendriyādiniyantṛtvaviṣayatayā saṅkocāyogāt| nanvaguṣṭhamātratvaṃ prathamaśrutamiti tadanusāreṇa dehendriyādiniyantṛtvaviṣayatayā caramaśrutaṃ tat yojanīyam| taṃ svāccharīrāt pravṛhenmuñjādiveṣīkāṃ dhairyeṇa| taṃ vidyācchukramamṛta" miti maṃtre hṛdayaniviṣṭatvaśravaṇāt tadupādhikamaṃguṣṭhamātratvaṃ paramātmanassambhavatīti vācyam| śāṇḍilyavidyāyāṃ - "eṣama ātmā'ntarhṛdaye'ṇīya"niti hṛdayopādhikaṃ parasyāṇīyastvameva śrutam| daharavidyāyāmapi taittirīyake - "tasya madhaye vahniśikhā aṇīyordhvā vyasthitaḥ| tasyāśśikhāyamadhye paramātmā vyavasthita" ityaṇīyovahniśikhāmadhyavartitvoktyā paramātno'pyaṇīyastve tātparyamavagatam| tasmāduṃguṣṭhamātratvaṃ hṛdayopādhikamityetat na sambhavatīticet sambhavatyeva| daharaśāṇḍilyavidyayohṛdayakamalordhvavartisuṣiravartitayopāsyaḥ paramātmeti tatra tadavacchedopādhikamaṇīyastvaṃ, iha kṛtsnahṛdayavartyupāsyo vivakṣita iti tadavacchedopādhikamaṃguṣṭhamātratvamiti vyavasthopapatteḥ| na ca kharaturagabhujagādīnāṃ aṃguṣṭhābhāvāt aṃguṣṭhamātratvaṃ hṛdayopādhikamityetannavyāpakamiti vācyam| manuṣyāṇāmupāsanādhikārāt teṣāñca hṛdayasya tattadaṃguṣṭhapramitatvāt| ārāgramātrasya jīvasya hṛdayopādhinā'pyaṃguṣṭhamātratvaṃ na sambhavatīti| atredaṃ vaktavyam| aṃguṣṭhamātratvaṃ jīvaliṃgameva| aṃguṣṭhamātraśabdena hi aṃguṣṭhādhikaparimāṇavyavacchedaḥ kriyate| na tvaṃguṣṭhaparimāṇamapi vidhīyate| ekasya śabdasya ubhayatra tātparyāyogāt| ata eva agnīṣomīyapaśau adhrigupraiṣe chāgasya pārśvadvayagatānāmasthnāṃ vaṃktiśabdavācyānāmiyattāprakāśakasya "ṣaḍviṃśatirasya vaṃkraya" iti maṃtrasya aśvamedhapaśau aśve codakaprāptasya
tatprakaraṇāmnātena "catusiṃraśadvājino devabaṃdhorvakrīraśvasya svadhitisametī"ti vaiśeṣikamaṃtreṇa apavādaprasaktau tadapavādārthena "ṣaḍviṃśatireṣāṃ vaṃkraya"iti ṣaḍviṃśatipadayukta eva saḥ prākṛtomaṃtraḥ prayoktavyaḥ| na tu "aśvastūparo gomṛgaste prājāpatyā" iti vihitāśvatūparagomṛgavaṃkrīṇāṃ samasyavacana ūhaḥ kartavya iti pūrvapakṣe prāpte||
"ṣaḍviṃśatirityeva brūyā"diti vākyasyaivakāreṇa ṣaḍviṃśatimātravyatiriktaṃ yadanyat catusiṃraśaditi matrāntaraṃ prāptam, tannakartavyamityatra tātparyāvagamāt ṣaḍviṃśatipadayoge'pi tātparyakalpane vākyabhedāpatteḥ nyāyaprāptasya aśvatūparagamṛgavaṃkrīṇāṃ samasvacanarūpasya ūhasya na bādha iti vyavasthāpitaṃ pūrvataṃtre navamādhyāye| ata eva ca bhāṣyakārairihādhikaraṇe "yāvanvā ayamākāśa" iti daharākāśasya paramātmano bhūtākāśasamaparimāṇatvoktirayuktā, "jyāyān diva" ityādivākyāntare tasya tadadhikaraparimāṇaśravaṇādityāśaṃkya "yāvanvā" ityādivākyaṃ hṛdayāvacchedaprayuktālpaparimāṇatvanivṛttimātraparam na tu bhūtākāśasamaparimāṇatvaparamapītyuktam| evamaṃguṣṭhamātratvasya aṃguṣṭhādhikaparimāṇavyavacchedarūpatāyāmārāgramātratvasyāpyārāgrādhikaparimāṇavyavacchedarūpatvaprāpterārāgraparimāṇatvamapi jīvasya na syāditi cet| nāstyevavastutatastasyārāgraparimāṇatvamapi| ata eva "ārāgramātro'pyavaro'pi dṛṣṭa" ityetadanantaraṃ" vālāgraśatabhāgasya śatadhā kalpitasya ca| bhāgo jīvassa vijñeyassacānantyāya kalpata" iti maṃtreṇa ārāgraparimāṇatvādatyalpamatisūkṣmaṃ parimāṇāntaraṃ pratipāditam| tasmādaṃguṣṭhādhikaparimāṇavyavacchedasya paramātmanyanupapatteḥ
jīve tadupapatteśca jīva evāṃguṣṭhamātra iti niścite tadanusāreṇa caramaśrutaṃ bhūtabhavyeśitṛtvaṃ tasminneva saṅkocanīyamiti pūrvapakṣo varṇanīyaḥ| "aṃguṣṭhamātra " ityetanmātraśabdasya avadhāraṇārthaṃ parigṛhya aṃguṣṭhaparimāṇādhikaparimāṇavyavacchedaparatvaṃ na kalpanīyam, kiṃ tu śabdasya "pramāṇe dvayasacdaghnacmātra ca" iti pramāṇārthavihitamātrac pratyayarūpatvaṃ parigṛhya aṃguṣṭhasamaparimāṇatvaparatvakalpanaṃ yuktam| bhāvarūpaladhvarthaparigṛhye sambhavati abhāvadvayaghaṭitagurvarthakalpanāyogāt| evaṃ ca aṃguṣṭhamātratvaṃ paramātmanyeva sambhavati| adhikaparimāṇasya alpaparimāṇāvacchedopādhikālpatvasya vyomādiṣu dṛṣṭatvāt| vālāgrāyutāṃśaparimāṇasya jīvasya tu hṛdayāvacchedaprayuktamapyaṃguṣṭhamātratvaṃ na sambhavati| alpaparimāṇasya adhikaparimāṇavakavastumadhyavartitvaprayuktādhikaparimāṇatvavyapadeśasya kvacidapyadarśanāt| jīvavākyeṣu aṃguṣṭhamātratvaśravaṇantu aṃguṣṭhādhikaparimāṇavyavacchedaviṣayatvenopapādanīyam| ekasyāpi śabdasya tatra tatra ucitārthabhedasambhavāt| yadvā - teṣu hṛdayaśatatamanāḍyavacchedopādhikayordharmajñānagatayoraṃguṣṭhamātratvārāgramātratvayostasmin sadvārakānvayaḥ| nacehāṃguṣṭhavākye sadvārakānvayaḥ kalpyaḥ| paramātmani sākṣāttadanvayasambhavāditi siddhāṃto varṇanīyaḥ| yadyevaṃ prathamaśrutena aṃguṣṭhamātratvaliṃgenaiva paramātmeti nirṇayasambhave kimiti tannirṇayārthaṃ īśānaśrutissūtrakāreṇāśritā| tasyāssarveśvare rūḍhatayā tato jhaṭiti nirṇayamabhipretya| ata eva sūtre "śabdādeve"tyevakāraḥ| āstāṃ yaugikārthavicārāvagamyālliṃgānnirṇayaḥ rūḍhatayā'bhidhānaśrutirūpādīśānaśabdādeva tāvannirṇayassaṃbhavatītihi tadābhiprāyaḥ| tasmādaṃguṣṭhapramitaḥ paramātmeti siddhaṃ||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
aṃguṣṭhamātraḥ puruśśarīrī śrutyantare tasya tathā prasiddheḥ|
īsānatātvastu jaghanyapāṭhāt tadbhogyabhogasthitihetvapekṣā||
īśānaśabdasvarasopanītassarvasya netā puruṣottamo'sau|
aṃguṣṭhamātratvamaṇau vibhau ca svārasyahīnaṃ hṛdayapekṣayā'stu||
asmādbhayena jagataḥ pravṛttiruktā svakāryeṣu|
asya ca niravadhi dṛṣṭaṃ sarvānugrāhakaṃ jyotiḥ||
yadidaṃ kiṃceti manau na tatra sūryaiti maṃtre ca|
ābhyāmapi liṃgābhyāṃ paramātmāṃ'guṣṭhamātro'sau|| iti||
"hṛdyapekṣayātvi" tyetāvatīha sthitamadhikaraṇaṃ kampanāt jyotirdarśanāditi sūtrābhyāmuttaratra
samāpayiṣyate| madhye prāsaṃgikamadhikaraṇatrayaṃ pūrvataṃtragartedrapītādyadhikaraṇavat garbhādhikaraṇatayā prāsaṃgikaṃ pravṛttam| saṃgrahaślokeṣu tu buddhisaukaryārthaṃ uparitanasūtradvayārtho'pi atraiva saṃgṛhītaḥ||
taduparyapi bādarāyaṇassambhavāt||25||
virodhaḥ karmaṇīti cennānekapratipatterdarśanāt||26||
śabda iti cennātaḥ prabhavātpratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ||27||
ata eva ca nityatvaṃ||28||
samānanāmarūpatvāccāvṛttāvapyavirodho darśanātsmṛteśca||
manuṣyādhikāraprasaṅgāt devādīnāṃ brahmavidyāyāmadhikārosti na veti vicāryate| tatra pūrvapakṣaḥ - nāstyadhikāraśśarīrarahitānāṃ teṣāṃ brahmavidyānuṣṭhāne sāmarthyābhāvāt| na ca teṣāṃ śarīravattve pramāṇamasti| naca "vajrahastaḥ purandaraḥ, indrovṛtrāya vajramudayaccha" dityādimantrārthavādaḥ tatra pramāṇaṃ| anuṣṭheyārthasmṛtistutyarthānāṃ teṣāntatra tātparyābhāvāt| naca smṛtidvāratayā tatsiddhiḥ| satācāsatācastutidarśanena stutyarthaṃ dvārabhūtārthasattānapekṣaṇāt| na ca stutyarthaṃ pratīyamānatāmātreṇa tatsiddhiḥ| tātparyābhāve tadasiddheḥ, tātparyādhīinatvācchabdaprāmāṇyasya | naca tatrāpi tātparyaṃ, kalpakābhāvāt| prāmāṇyanirvāhaprayojanaparyavasānāya hi tatkalpanaṃ| karmakāṇḍavacasāṃ vidhiniṣedhādhīnapravṛttinivṛttibhyāṃ brahmakāṇḍavacasāṃ svataḥ puruṣārthabrahmapratipādanena ca prayojanaparyavasānaṃ labdhameva| na ca devatoddesyakadravyatyāgātmakayāgavidhivākyānyeva devatāpekṣaṇīti vācyaṃ| śarīrarahitasyāpi uddeśasambhavena tatastāsāṃ śarīrāpekṣā'siddheḥ| na ca kṣaṇadhvaṃsināṃ karmaṇāṃ kālāṃtarabhāviphalasādhanatvanirvāhāya dvārāpekṣāyāṃ "tṛpta evaivamiṃdraprajayā paśubhistarpayatī" tyādyarthavādataḥ pratītasya devatāprasādasya dvāratvena parigrāhyatayā tāsāṃ vigrahādipañcakasiddhiḥ| apūrvasya dvārasya sadbhāvena dvārāntarānapekṣaṇāt |naca
arthavādapratīte devatāprasāde sthite nāpūrvaṃ kalpyamiti vācyam| devatārahiteṣu dānādiṣu apūrvakalpanā'vaśyaṃbhāvāt yāgahomeṣvapi sampratipannācetanadevatākeṣu cetanadevatākeṣvapi keṣucit tatkalpanā'vaśyambhāvācca| na hyaśvamedhe medhyasyāśvasya guṇakriyāṃgānyuddiśya "kṛṣṇāya svāhā śvetāya svāhā yanmehasi tasmai svāhā yacchakṛtkaroṣi tasmai svāhā dadbhyassvāhā hanūbhyāṃ svāhe" tyādimantraiḥ kiryamāṇeṣu homeṣu cetanādevatāsti| na ca tiryagjanmagatān pitṝnuddiśya kriyamāṇeṣu śrāddheṣu tiryagbhūtāḥ pitaraḥ phalapradānasamarthāḥ| na ca indrādayopi kalpāvasāneṣu nivṛttādhikāraḥ kalpāvasānānuṣṭhitabhāvikalpabhogyaphalayāgādiphalapradānasamarthāḥ| etena "yajadevapūjāyā"miti smaraṇena yāgasya devatārādhanarūpatvāt tadārādhanānāṃ rājārādhanavat ārādhanaprasannārādhanīyadeyaphalārthatvāvaśyambhāvāt yāgavidhisāmarthyena devatāvigrahasiddhirityapi śaṅkā nirastā| rājñaśśiśavaḥ tatpādukādayaśca rājabhṛtyairucitopacāreṇa pūjyante| jyotiṣṭome somalatā payonnanivedanena pūjyate| pitṛmātṛgurubhartratithividyāvṛddhādayaśca śuśrūṣādibhiḥ pūjyante| na ca teṣāṃ phalapradātṛtvamasti| rājakumārādīnāmupaṃcāro rājādīnāmeva pūjā, teṣāñca phaladātṛtvamastīti yadyucyeta, tadā devatānāmijyā varṇāśramādirūpācārapravartakasya sarveśvarasyaiva pūjā, tasya phaladātṛtvamastīti tataeva yāgasya devapūjātvasiddheḥ na tata indrādidevānāṃ vigrahasiddhiḥ| etena "indro ha vai devānāmabhipravabrājavirocano'surāṇā"mityādi brahmakāṇḍagatopākhyānaiḥ indrādīnāṃ vigrahasiddhiśaṅkā'pi nirastā| karmakāṇḍagatopākhyānānāmapi pāriplavādhikaraṇanyāyat tattadvidyāvidhiśeṣatvena karmakāṇḍagatairiva brahmakāṇḍagatairapi arthavādaiḥ stutidvārābhūtārthāsiddheḥ| tasmāddevādīnāṃ vigraharahitānāṃ sāmarthyābhāvena nāsti brahmavidyāsvadhikāra ityevaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ||
brahmopāsanaṃ manuṣyāṇāmupari ye devādayaḥ teṣvapi saṃbhavatyeva| na ca vigrahābhāvena teṣāṃ sāmarthyābhāvaḥ, sarvāsvapyupaniṣatsu sṛṣṭiprakaraṇeṣu "nārāyaṇadbrahmā jāyate, nārāyaṇādrudrojāyate, indro varuṇassome rudra" ityādivākyaiḥ devādīnāṃ sṛṣṭipratipādanāt, sarvāsu śrutiṣu indrādipadānāṃ karmaviśeṣaphalatvapratipādanācca| tathā hi sāmagānāṃ sāmavidhibrāhmaṇe "mayivarca" ityanena kalpena catvāri varṣāṇi prayuñjānaḥ trayāṇāṃ lokānāmādhipatyaṃ gacchatī"ti śrūyate| trailokyādhipatyamiṃdratvameva| tathā chandogānāmupaniṣadi - "vasūnāmevaiko bhūtvā'gninaiva
mukhenaitadevāmṛtaṃ ddaṣṭvā tṛpyatī"tyādivākyaiḥ vasurudrādityamarutsādhdyākhyagaṇadevatāpadaprāptiḥ madhuvidyāphalatvena pratipādyate| tathā devatāvaśīkaraṇasya karmaviśeṣaphalatvaśravaṇādapi tāsāṃ vigrahasiddhiḥ| tathā hi sāmavidhibrāhmaṇe śrūyate - "yaḥ kāmayetāvartayeyāmityekarātraṃ kṣurasaṃyuktastiṣṭhet sutāsāṃmadhumattamā" iti vargaḥ| "eteṣāmekamanekaṃ vā sarvāṇi vā prayuñjānaḥ ekarātreṇa kuṭuṃbinamāvartayati, dvirātreṇa rājopajīvinaṃ, trirātreṇa rājānaṃ, catūrātreṇa grāmaṃ, pañcarātreṇa nagaraṃ, ṣaḍrātreṇa janapadaṃ, saptarātreṇāsurarakṣāṃsi, aṣṭarātreṇa pitṛpiśācān, navarātreṇa yakṣān, daśarātreṇa gandharvāpsarasaḥ, ardhamāsena vaiśravaṇaṃ, māseneṃdraṃ, caturbhiḥ prajāpatiṃ, saṃvatsareṇa yatkiñcijjagat sarvaṃ hāstha guṇībhavatī"ti| evaṃ devatānāṃ vigrahasāmānyasiddhau "sahasrākṣo gotrabhidvajravāhurasmāsudevo draviṇaṃ dadhātu, catvāri śṛṅāgā trayo asya pādā dveśīrṣe saptahastāso asye"tyādimantraiḥ indrāgnyādīnāṃ sahasrākṣasaptahastatvādiviśiṣṭavigrahaviśeṣasiddhiḥ| na ca teṣu vigrahaviśeṣapratipādanānapekṣā śaṅkanīyā| "yāṃ devatāṃ vaṣaṭkariṣyan syāttāṃmanasādhyānāsambhavāt, tathā arthavādairapi vajrahastatvādiviśiṣṭavigrahasiddhiḥ| ata eva śābarabhāṣye prajāpaticapotkhananādivākyānāmapi lākṣaṇikārthaparigraheṇa dvārabhūtasyāpyarthasya sadrūpatā darśitā| kiṃca "indro vṛtrāye"tyādivādānāṃ indravṛtrādiśabdebhyopi vibhaktyutpattyarthamarthavattvantāvadapekṣitam| tadabhāve prātipadikatvābhāvena vibhaktyanutpatteḥ| apekṣitorthastadarthavādaissamarpito vajrahastatvādirūpa eva paryavasyati| apekṣitārthaviśeṣasamarpaṇe vidyamāne taṃ parityajya apekṣāpūraṇārthaṃ arthāṃtaragrahaṇāyogāt| nanu "nakirindra tvaduttaraḥ na jyāyo asti vṛtrahan nakyevaṃ yathātvaṃ, ātvā viśaṃtvindavaḥsamudramiva sindhavaḥ, natvāmindrātiricyate, indraṃ viśvā avīvṛdhan samudravyacasaṅgiraḥ" ityādayo mantrāḥ na kevalamindraṃ prakāśayanti| kintu sarvottaratvasarvasomagrahapātratvasarvāgjālapratipādyatvādibhirasadarthaisstuvantyapi| ato na stutidvārā arthasattāniyama iti cet, yatra virodhaḥ tatra "śrṛṇotagrāvāṇa" ityādiṣviva asadarthāvalambanatvasyāpi darśanāt| yatra na virodhaḥ tatra autsargikasvataḥ prāmāṇyavaśādapi arthasattā'bhyupagantavyā| kimuta tadapekṣāyāṃ| tasmādvidhimantrārthavādairdevādīnāṃ vigrahasiddheḥ sāmarthyasadbhāvāt mokṣaphalāyāṃ brahmavidyāyāmastyadhikāraḥ| nanu mantrārthavādādivaśāt devānāṃ vigrahavattvābhyupagame "agnimagna āvaha, indrāgaccha hariva āgaccha, adhvaryo drāvayatvaṃ somamindra pipāsati, uponūnaṃ yuyuje bṛṣaṇā harī, ā ca jagāma vṛtrahā, kasyavā'hadevā yajñamāgacchati kasya vā na bahūnāṃ yajamānānā"mityādibahutaramaṃtrārthavādadarśanāt devānāṃ
yajñadeśāgamanamapi aṅgīkartavyam| kathamekasyendrasya yugapadanekayajñadeśāgamanaṃ ghaṭate| bhūtavaśinānteṣāṃ yogināmiva yugapat bahuśarīraparigrahasambhavāditi brūmaḥ| "agnestrayo jyāyāṃso bhrātara āsan te devebhyo havyaṃ vahantaḥ prāmīyanta, avāḍhḍhavyāni surabhīṇi kṛtve"tyādi śrutiṣu lokāntarasthadevasamīpaprāpaṇārthaṃ havyavadṛnaśravaṇaṃ tu yeṣu darvīhomādiṣu devatāhvānaṃ tadāgamanaṃ vā na śrutaṃ tadviṣayaṃ| yadvā yajñadeśamāgatā devāḥ havirgṛhītvā "yadvai devā havijuṣante alpamapyekamāhutimapi tadgirimātraṃ vardhata" iti yajñadeśāgamanahavyavahanaśrutyorna virodhaḥ| mābhūdindrādīnāṃ vigrahavatve karmaṇi virodhaḥ| śabde tu syāt, vigrahāṇāmanitvāvaśyaṃbhāvena tadviśiṣṭavācināmindrādiśabdānāṃ tadutpatteḥ pūrvaṃ paścācca arthaśūnyatvasya anivāryatvāt| yadyucyeta ḍitthādisāṅketikaśabdānāmiva kādācitkenāpyarthena arthavattvamupapadyata iti tadā'pi indrādipūrvāparakālayoḥ - "indrāgacche"tyādīnāmarthaśūnyatvamanivāryamiti cenna| indrādīnāmiṃdrādiśabdaprabhavatayendrādiśabdānāṃ ghaṭādiśabdavat pravāhanādivyaktiparamparāgajātivācitvāt| yathā hi śilpī śilpaśāsrāvagatamiṃdrādyākāraṃ smṛtvā tadākārāṃ pratimāṃ nirmāti, tathā prajāpatirvedāvagataṃ pūrvapūrvendrādyākāraṃ smṛtvā tattadākāraviśiṣṭāniṃdrādīn sṛjatīti hi "vedena rūpe vyākarot satāsatī prajāpatissabhūriti vyāharat sa bhūmimasṛjata sa bhuva iti vyāharat so'ntarikṣamasṛjate"tyādiśrutiṣu śrūyate| tathā smaryate ca "nāmarūpaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ kṛtyādīnāṃ prapañcanam| vedaśabdebhya evādau devādīnāṃ cakāra sa" iti| nanu mābhūdarthadvārako vaidikaśabde virodhaḥ| śabdadvārakastu syāt| maṃtrārthavādādīnāṃ hi pratīyamānārthe prāmāṇyamupapāditam| tathā ca "maṃtrakṛto vṛṇīte" viśvāmitrasya sūktaṃ bhavati, saṃhitākārapadakārasūtrakārabrāhmaṇakārāṇā" mityādiprāmāṇyādvedasya pauruṣeyatvanivāryamiticet nāyamapi doṣaḥ| "viśvāmitrasya sūktaṃ bhavatī"tyādivedaśabdebhyo viśvāmitrādīnāṃ tattatsūktaviśeṣadarśanādirūpakṛtyaviśeṣān nāmarūpāṇicānusandhāya pūrvakalpānuṣṭhitapuṇyavaśāt viśvāmitrādīn sūktādīnanadhītyaiva draṣṭuṃ samarthān prajāpatiḥ
sṛṣṭvā viśvāmitrādipadeṣu niveśayati| anadhītya tattatsuktādidarśanasāmarthyameva ca mantrakṛttvādikaṃ| tato na vedānityatvāpattiḥ| atha syāta caturmukhasya svamānena parārdhadvayajīvinaḥ caturyugasahasrarūpaikaikadivasātmakasya avāntarakalpasyāvasāne tāvatkālapramāṇarātrirūpe naimittikapralaye bhūrbhuvassvariti trailokyamātrasya nāśa iti vedasattvāt bhavatuvedaśabdapūrvikā tadanaṃtarasṛṣṭiḥ| tadīyavarṣaśatāvasāne tāvatpramāṇoḥ yaḥ prākṛtaprayakālaḥ tadā vyākṛtaprayantasarvanāśe vedasyāpi nāśāvaśyambhāvāt kathantadanantaramahākalpādau vedaśabdapūrvikā sṛṣṭiḥ, kathañca prākṛtapralaye naśyato vedasya nityatvamiticet ucyate - tadā vedānāṃ nāśo na doṣaḥ, "chandāṃsi jajñiretasmādi"ti śrutiṣu mahākalpādau teṣāmapi sṛṣṭiśravaṇāt| samānānupūrvikatvarūpe tu nityatve nāsti virodhaḥ| mahākalpāvṛttāvapi sṛjjyānāṃ samānanāmarūpatvāt| naca tadasiddhiḥ| " yo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvaṃ yo vai vedāṃśca prahiṇoti tasmā" iti mantre mahākalpādau brahmasṛṣṭyanantaraṃ tasmai vedapradānaśravaṇasya brahmā śilpinyāyena pūrvakalpendrādināmarūpāṇi mantrārthavādebhyo'vagatya tatsamānanāmarūpānindrādīn sṛjatvityetadarthatvāt, "tasya suptasya devasya nābhau padmamajāyata| tasmin padme mahābhāvaga vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ| brahmotpannassatenoktaḥ prajāssṛjamahāmata" ityādismaraṇācca| tasmāddevādīnāṃ vigrahavatāmarthitvasāmarthyayogādastibrahmavidyāyāmadhikāra iti siddhaṃ||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
devādīnāmadhikṛtirvidyāyāṃ naiva vidyate|
teṣāṃ vigrahaśūnyānāṃ nakhalpavasti samarthatā||
na ca mantrārthavādebhyastatsiddhiprārthanocitā|
ānyaparyādvirodhācca durvārātkagarmaśabdayoḥ||
maivammantrārthavādānāṃ dvārārthe'pyasti mānatā|
na karmaṇi virodho'sti naikavigrahasambhavāt||
na śabde jātiśabdatvāt samtvānnāmarūpayoḥ||
iti devatādhikaraṇam||7||
madhvādiṣvasambhavādanadhikāraṃ jaiminiḥ||30||
jyotiṣi bhāvācca||31||
bhāvaṃ tu bādarāyaṇo'sti hi||32||
pūrvādhikaraṇe sāmānyena devānāṃ tadabhīpsitaparopāsanādhikāro'stītyuktam| atha upāsanāviśeṣeṣu devatāviśeṣāṇāmadhikāro'sti na veti cintyata iti saṅgatiḥ| asti chāndogye madhuvidyāḥ| tasyāṃ vasvādaya upāsyāḥ prāpyāśca śrūyante| tatrahyādityavyapāśrayāṇi lohitādipañcarūpātmakāni pañcāmṛtāni vasurudrādityamarutsādhyagaṇabhogyatvenopāsyāni, tadupāsanānāñca vasvādipadaprāptiḥ phalamiti varṇitaṃ, "tadyatprathamamamṛtaṃ tadvasava tṛpyanti sayaetadevamamṛtaṃ veda vasūnāmevaiko bhūtvā agninaiva mukhena etadevāmṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā tṛpyantī"tyādinā| tasyāṃvasvādīnāṃ nāstyadhikāraḥ| svasya svopāsyatvāsambhavāt, svapadaprāpteḥ phalatvasamvācceti pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ| nanu nāyaṃ pūrvapakṣo yuk iha kalpe vasvādīnāṃ satāṃ kalpāntare'pi vasutvādikamviti, ihi janmani brāhmaṇasya sato janmāntare'pi brāhmaṇyamastvitīcchāvadicchopapatteḥ, bhūmavidyāmaddhyagatāyāṃ prāṇavidyāyāṃ svasya svopāsyatvasampratipatteśceti cet, evaṃ pūrvapakṣī manyate| madhuvidyāyā na vasvādibhāvaḥ phalam| kintu vasvādisthānaprāptipūrvakaṃ tattadbhogasāmyaṃ| yathā "asmadbhrātṝṇāmmadhye tvameka" ityuktau bhrātṛbhirbhogasāmyaṃ| anyathā madhuvidyāniṣṭhānāṃ prāyaṇānantaraṃ vasvādibhāvaprāptau "aṣṭauvasava" ityādiśrutisiddhasaṃkhyātirekaprasaṅgāt| dṛṣṭañca "vaiṣṇavaṃ vāmanamālabheta spardhamāna" iti vidhiśeṣasya "viṣṇureva bhūtvemān lokānabhijayatī"tyarthavādasya viṣṇuvajjetṛtvamātraparatvaṃ| tasmādvasvādīnāmaprāptaphalatvoktiryuktaiva| tathā svopāsanāsambhavoktirapi yuktā| tadupāsanayā tadbhogasāmyaprāpteśtatprītidvāratvasyautsargikatvena vasvādiṣu prīṇayitṛtvaprīṇanīyatvarūpakartṛkarmabhāvavirodhāt| na hi svayameva svātmānaṃ prīṇayitvā prītātsvasmāt phalaṃlabhata ityasti sambhavaḥ| prāṇavidyāyāṃtu bhagavatprītyaiva phalalābhaḥ| na hi tatra svopāsanasya svasamabhogaprāptiphalaśravaṇamastīti| kiṃca "tandevā jyotiṣāṃ jyotirāyurhopāsate'mṛta" miti śrutiḥ na devādīnāṃ brahmavidyādhakāraprāptyarthā| manuṣyāṇāmiva teṣāmapyarthitvādisattve - nādhikārasya svataḥ prāptatvāt| kintu madhuvidyāyāmadhikāra iti pūrvapakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - 
vasvādīnāmapi tasyāmastyevādhikāraḥ| teṣāmapi svāntaryāmitayā brahmopāsanasambhavāt "ya etāmevaṃ brahmopaniṣadaṃ vede" tyupasaṃhāre brahmavidyātvaśravaṇāt teṣāmapi bhāvivasvāditvaprāptipūrvakabrahmaprepsāsambhavācca| etena pūrvapakṣyāpāditaḥ prīṇayitṛprīṇanīyaikyāpattimūlassarvo'pi doṣo nirastaḥ| vasvādibhissvāntaryāmitayopāsyasya sarveśvasyaiva prīṇanīyatvāt| tasmādvasvādīnāgapi svaprīṇanīyopāsyatatphalaprepsayossambhavāt asti madhuvidyādhikāraḥ| evaṃ vāyvādīnāṃ saṃvargavidyādyadhikāro'pi draṣṭavyaḥ| nanvagnyādibhya upari sthitānāmindrādīnāmarcirādigatismaraṇaṃ na sambhavati| tacca vidyāṅgamiti "tadokograjvalana" miti sūtre sūtrakāra eva "taccheṣagatyanusmṛtiyogā" diti sūtrāṃśena sphuṭikariṣyati| apto'ṅgānuṣṭhānāśaktānāṃ devānāṃ kathaṃ vidyādhikāraḥ| yadi gatismaraṇamadṛṣṭārthaṃ tadā'rcirādidevatā api manusyāṇāṃ mucyamānānāṃ arcirādikā gatiriti smariṣyati| yadi tvevaṃbhūtena mārgeṇa mayā vidyayā paramapadaṃ prāpyamiti smaraṇamaharahaḥ kartavyā| yāmupāsanāyāṃ ṛtvikkartṛkahaviśśeṣabhakṣaṇaniyamasteṣāṃkariṣyamāṇakarmasantatāvivotsāhajananārthaṃ, tadāgnyādayaḥ svasvānapekṣitāṃśa vihāyāpekṣitaṃ svapadānmuparitthitaṃ gatyaṃśaṃ smariṣyanti| yathā ṛtvijo'śvamedhādiṣu atibahupaśuprabhavahaviśśeṣamadhye apekṣitāṃśamātraṃ bhakṣayantīti na saṅkaṭaṃ kiṃcit| nanu devānāṃ brahmavidyādhikāracinteyaṃ kkopayujyate| na tāvadasmadādīnāṃ pravṛttau| nāpi devānāṃ| teṣāṃ svakīye vigrahavattve tatprayuktasāmarthyādimattve ca svayamevāvagatisadbhāvāt| ucyate - devatā na saṃti cet madhuvidyādīnāṃ vasvādipadaprāptiḥ phalaṃ na bhavet| varuṇagrahanirmokādivat bādhitasya phalatvena śrutasyāpi vidheyakriyāphalatvāyogāt anyadeva tatphalaṃ kalpyaṃ syāt| antarādityavidyādiṣvādityadevatāntaryāmitvena brahmopāsanaṃ na sidhyat| gatyanusmṛtāvacetanānyarcirādīnyeva mārgaparvatvena cintanīyāni syuḥ| " bhāktaṃ vānātmavittvā"dityādisūtroktanyāyena yāgādiphalaṃ bhuñjānānānnirantaramājānadevakarmakaratāvagatyā vairāgyaṃ na sidhyat| manuṣyāṇāmetādṛśābhimatāsiddhiḥ phalaṃ devatāsadbhāvaprasādhanasya| maṃtrārthavādāḥ prāptivirodhayorasatoḥ pratīyamānārthe prāmāṇyaṃ na jahātīti devatāvigrahaprasādhanopayoginyāyasamarthanasyāpi "plavāhyete adṛḍhā yajñarūpā aṣṭādaśoktamavaraṃ yeṣu karma| etacchreyo ye'bhinandanti ,mūḍhā jarāmṛtyū te punarevāpiyaṃti|| jāyasva mriyasvetyetat tṛtīyaṃ sthānaṃ, tenāsau loko na saṃpūryate tasmājjuguṣsete"tyādimantrārthavādaprāmāṇyāvadhāraṇena vivekavairāgyādisiddhiḥ phalamityādyūhyam||
saṃgrahakārike||
tathā'pi madhuvidyāyāṃ vasvādīnānnavidyate|
adhikāro virodhācca prāptatvāttatphalasya ca||
maivaṃ sā brahmavidyātadadhigatiphalā varṇitā vākyaśeṣe
tasmādvasvādiśabde pravikasitaparabrahmaparyantaśaktiḥ|
svāvasthabrahmacintāvidhiṣu na valate karmakatraikyaśaṅkā
mukteḥ prāk dvārabhūtaṃ phalamapi ghaṭate bhāvi vasvādiprāptiḥ||
iti madhvadhikaraṇam||8||
śugasya tadanādaraśravaṇāttadādravaṇāt sūcyate hi||33||
kṣatriyatvagateśca||34||
uttaratra caitrarathena liṃgāt||35||
saṃskāraparāmarśāttadabhāvāṣtilāpācca||36||
tadaṣtāvanirdhāraṇe ca pravṛtteḥ||37||
śravaṇādhyayanārthapratiṣedhāt||38||
smṛteṣca||39||
vasvādīnāmarthitvasāmarthyasadbhāvāt brahmāvidhyāyāmadhikāro'stītyuktaṃ|tarhi śūdrāṇāmapi tatsatbhāvāt adhikāra ssyā diti śaṅkayā saṅgatiḥ|śūdrāṇāmadhikaro'sti na veti tadupasaṃhārasāmarthyasatvāsattvābhyāṃ,saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ|astyadhikārasteṣāmapi|agnividhyābhāvādhdi karmānadhikāraḥ pūrvatantrāpaśūdrādhikaraṇe sādhitaḥ|na copāsaneṣu manovṛttimātrarūpeṣu tadāpekṣā'sti|ataḥ srīṇāmiva teṣāmapyadhikāro'styeva|na ca teṣāṃ vidhyāsahakārikarmāsambhavaḥ|
svavarṇāśramadharmasambhavāt |na ca teṣāṃ brahmasvarūpatadurāsānaprakārajñānāsambhavaḥ,iti hāsapurāṇasattvāt |ata eva hi vidurādayo brahmaniṣṭhāḥ|astica chandogyopaniṣadi teṣāṃ vidhyādhikāraliṅgam|tatrāhi saṃvargavidhyāyāṃ vidhyādhikāriṇaḥ śūdretyāmantrāṇaṃ ddaśyate |"ājahāremāśśūdrānenaiva mukhena māmālāpayiṣyatā" ityevantatropākhyāyate|
jānaśrutirnāma rājyaparipālakaḥ śradhdayābahupradaḥ sarvāsu dikṣu grāmanagaramārmāraṣyatīrthādiṣu pānthānāmannodakaśayanācachādanādibhogyajātasaṃpūrṇānāvasathān māpayāṃcakre |tasya dhārmikapravarasya karmābhiḥ kṣīṇaduritasya brahmmavividiṣāmutpādāyituṃ kecana maharṣayo haṃsarūpamāsthāya niśāyāṃ hamrye śayāvasya tasyopari paṅktimābadhyājammuḥ| teṣāmagresaraṃ haṃsaṃ "bhogo bhallākṣa bhallākṣe"ti viparītalakṣaṇayā mandalocaneti sambhrameṇa dvivāraṃ sambodha kaściddhaṃsa uvāca| jānaśraterannadānādiprabhāvajanitaṃ jyotiḥ dyulokaparyantaṃ vyāptaṃ vartate| tatra tvaṃ sagnaḥ pradagdho mābhūriti, ekamuktavantantagragāmī haṃsaḥ pratyuvāca| etaṃ varākaṃ jānaśrutiṃ śakaṭasahitaṃ raikvamiva kīddaśamāhātmyaśālinamāttheti| etadākarṇya tena pṛṣṭhagāminā haṃsena ko'sau raikva iti punaḥ pṛṣṭassaeva pratyuvāca| sarveṣāmapi sarvaṃ satkarmavijñānajātaṃ yadajīyasatkarmavijñānantarbhūtaṃ saraikva iti| tadetaddhaṃsavākyaṃ jānaśrutiḥ svaniṃdāraikvapraśaṃsāgarbhamākarṇya kathamapi niśāmativāhya talpaṃ tyacanneva kṣattāramāhūya śakaṭacihnasahitaṃ raikvamuktvā tadanveṣaṇāya preṣayitvā kvaciddeśe śakaṭasyādhastāt pāmānaṃ kaṇḍūyamānaṃ raikvaṃ ddaṣṭvā pratyāgatya tasminniveditavati svayaṃ raikvamupasaṃpadya gavāṃ ṣaṭśatāni niṣkamaśvatarīyuktaṃ rathañcopahṛtya tvadupāsyāṃ devatāmupadiśeti prārthayāmāsa| tatastamuvāca raikvaḥ "ahahāretvā śūdra tavaiva saha gobhirastvi"ti| aheti nipātaḥ| hārasahitaḥ itvā rathaḥ gobhissaha tavaivāstviti tadarthaḥ| punarjānaśrutau raikvasyaitāvatā na santoṣa iti matvā gavāṃ sahasraṃ pūrvānītaṃ hārādikaṃ raikvasya vivāhārthaṃ svakanyāñcopahṛtya vidyopadeśārthaṃ prārthayamāne raikvasyedaṃ vākyaṃ "ājahāremāśśūdre"tyādi| imāḥ dakṣiṇāḥ āhṛtavānasi| anenaiva mukhena vidyāgrahaṇopāyena māmālāpayiṣyasi| vidyopadeṣṭāraṃ kariṣyasītyarthaḥ| yadyapi "ahahāretavāśūdre"ti pūrvavākye'pi "śūdre" tyāmantraṇamasti| tathā'pi śūdravat brahmavidyāgauravamajānānassvalpāṃdakṣiṇāmāharasīti kopena kṣatriya eva "śūdre"tyāmantrita ityanyathāsiddhirvaktuṃ śakyā| na tu dvitīyavākya ityabhipretya idaṃ santoṣavākyamudāhṛtaṃ bhāṣye| evaṃ śūdrasyāpi vidyādhikāro'stīti prāpte rāddhāntaḥ - 
śūdrāṇāntāt na brahmasvarūpatadupāsanaprakāraparijñānopāyo'sti| na ca purāṇādikaṃ tadupāyaḥ, "itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ vedaṃ samupabṛhmaye"diti vedatātparyanirṇayārthatvena tasya viniyuktam| na ca "śrāvayeccaturovarṇān kṛtvā brāhmaṇamagrata" iti śūdrāṇāṃ purāṇādiśravaṇānumatervaiphalyaṃ, pāpakṣayārthatvopapatteḥ| viduradharmavyādhādīnāntu brahmaniṣṭhā na purāṇādinā, kiṃtu janmāntaravāsanānuvṛttyā| "śūdre"tyāmantraṇamapi na śūdrāṇāṃ vidyādhikāre liṅgaṃ| iha śūdraśabdasya rūḍhyā jadātivācitvābhāvāt "śūcerddaśce"tyuṇādisūtreṇa "śuca śoka" iti dhātoḥ "sphāyitañcivañciśakikṣipikṣudisṛpī"tyādisūtrānuvṛttarapratyayasya "amitamyordīrghaśce"ti tadanantarasūtrānuvṛttadīrghasya dhātucakārasthāne dakārādeśasya ca vidyāyakena niṣpannasya tasya yogena śocitṛtvārthakatvāt| naceha rūḍhiprābalyaṃ śaṅkanīyaṃ|haṃsavākyānādaraśravaṇena tadaiva raikkopasarpaṇena kāryeṇa ca sūcitasya śokasya vuddhisthatayā buddhisthayogasya rūḍhitaḥ prabasatvāt |kiṃca jānaśruteḥkṣatriyatvāvagamādapi taddhiṣayaśūdra śabdo yaugikaḥ|upākyānopakramehi "jānaśratirha pautrāyaṇaḥ śraddhā deyo bahudāyi bahuvākya āsasahasarta āvasathānmāpayāṃcakre sarvata eva mennamatsyantī"ti jānaśruteḥ dānapatitvaṃ sarvebhyaḥpakkānnadayitvañcoktam|madhye ca "saha sañjihāna eva kṣattarāmuvāce"ti kṣattṛpreṣaṇaṃ|"iyaṃ jāyā ayaṃ grāmo yasminnāssa"iti raikkāya kanāyāpradānaṃ
raikkastadā yatra grāmesthitaḥ tasya grāmasya dānaṃcoktam|ante ca "te haite raikkaparṇānāmamahāvṛṣeṣu yatrāsma uvāse"
ti tasmai mahāvṛṣākhyadeśagatabahugrāmapradatvamuktam|etairligaiḥjānaśruteḥ kṣatriyatvamavagatam|yadyapi anyāyavṛttāśśūdrā api rājyaṃ paripālayantaḥ "vaiśyāt brāhmaṇakanyāyāṃ kṣattā nāma prajāyate"|"jīvikāvṛttiretasya rājāṃtaḥpurarakṣaṇa"mityuktalakṣaṇatrasaṃgrahādikaṃ kurvati| tathā'pi vedanibaddhaṃ tat na tādṛśaṃ vaktuṃ śakyam| bādhakābhāve vaidikaliṃgāvagamyasya dharmyasyaiva grāhyatvāt| anyathā śūdretyāmantraṇenāvagataśūdrasya vidyāgrahaṇaṃ adharmyaṃ vedena nibaddhamiti vaktuṃ śakyatayā śūdrasya vidyādhikāre uktavaidikaliṃgāsidhyā tadbādhakānāmanupanyasanīyatāpatteḥ| tasmājjānaśrutiḥ kṣatriyo'vagamyata iti yuktameva| kiṃca asyāmeva saṃvargavidyāyāmuttaratra caitrarathasya kṣatriyatvāt tasyāpi kṣatriyatvamavagamyate| uttaratra hi "atha ha śaunakañca kāpeyamabhipratāriṇañca kākṣaseniṃ pariviṣyamāṇau brahmacārī bibhikṣa" ityādau śaunakasya brāhmaṇasya dvitīyo'bhipratāriṃ kṣatriya iti tatprāyapāṭhāt raikkasya brāhmaṇasya dvitāyo jānaśrutirapi kṣatriya ityavagamyate| syādetat| jānaśruteḥ kṣatriyatvanirdhāraṇamiha prāyapāṭhasatvādhīnaṃ, tat abhipratāriṇaḥ kṣatriyatvanirdhāraṇādhīnaṃ, tadapi tasya caitrarathatvanirdhāraṇādhinaṃ, tacca tasya kāpeyayājjayatvanirdhāraṇādhīnaṃ, tatkuto nirdhāryate| nahi tadiha vā anyatra vā śrutamasti| na ca kāpeyasahapāṭhāt tatsambandhaḥ kvacitsidhyan tadyājyatvarūpassidhyatīti vācyam| bhojanārthaṃ sahopaviṣṭāvityeva hi sahapāṭhaḥ| na ca tāvanmātreṇa kaścana kulasambandhassidhyati| atithigṛhasthayorvyabhicārāt, prācīnaśālīyasaṃjñādiṣu vyabhicārācca| teṣāmekavidyatvaṃ sambandhaścet, ihāpi tadeva sambandhassyāt| ubhayorapi asaṃvargāvidyopāsakatvāt| nanu chandogabrāhmaṇe "atha yasya jyotirukthyaḥ pūrvamaharbhavatyāyuruttara" iti vihitasya dvirātrasya "ekākinamevainamannādyasyādyakṣaṃ karotī" tyenaṃ dvirātrayājinamekameva bhrātṛvargādimadhye aiśvaryasyādhyakṣaṃ karotītyevaṃ phalārthavādamuktvā tadupapādakamarthavādāntaramāmnātam| "etena vai citrarathaṃ kāpeyā ayājayan temekākimannādyasyādhyakṣamakurvan tasmāccaitrarathīnāmaikaḥ kṣatrapatirjāyata" iti citraratatadvaṃśyānāṃ kāpeyayājyatvaṃ śrutamiti cet, kiṃtataḥ| na khalvabhipratārī citrarathaḥ, nāmabhedena tato bhedāvagamāt| nāpi citrarathavaṃśyaḥ, tathā śravaṇābhāvāt| bhāṣye tu "ete navai citrarathaṃ kāpeyā ayājayanni"ti śrutipāṭho likhitaḥ| tatrāpi abhipratāriṇaḥ kāpeyayājyatvāsiddhiśaṅkā tulyā| tasya caitrarathatvāśravaṇādeva| tasmādabhipratāriṇaḥ kāpeyayājyatvaṃ tataścitrarathavaṃśyatvañca avagantumaśakyamiti cet ucyate - kāpeyastāvat śrutyantarabalāt citrarathayājakatvena pratyabhijñāyata iti tāvannirvivādaṃ| tatsahopaviṣṭo'bhipratārī kāpeyayājyaḥ citrarathavaṃśyaśceti buddhirautsargikī| bādhakābhāvāt| bhavatyeva yathāhviditapūrvasya hastino nikaṭe dṛśyamāne puruṣe rājño hastipako'yamiti pratyabhijñāyamāne sati ayamasya hastipakasya śikṣaṇīyo hastī rājakīyaśceti buddhiḥ| nanu sidhyatu kāpeyayājyatvamabhipratāriṇaḥ, tataścitrarathavaṃśyatvaṃ na sidhyati, anyasyāpi tadyājyatvasambhavāditi cet| na trividhāḥ khalu yājakāḥ pitṛpitāmahādiparamparayā kulakramāgātāḥ yājakāḥ pitṛbhūtāḥ tadasambhave kasyacidādhānaprabhṛtiṣu sarveṣu karmasu yājakā manuṣyabhūtāḥ, tadasambhave tatra karmaṇi daivādāgatya āÐtvajyaṃ kurvanto devabhūtāḥ| atassamānānvayānāṃ yājyayājakabhāvasya mukhyatvena autsargikatvāt, pūrvarājacaritreṣu bahuśo yājyayājakakulavyavasthādarśanācca kāpeyānāṃ pitṛbhūtayājakatvāvadhāraṇāt kāpeyayājakayogena citrarathavaṃśyatvasiddhiḥ| tasmāt saṃvargavidyānvayinordvayoḥ brāhmaṇasya dvitīyaḥ
kṣatriya ityuttaradarśanāt tatprāyapāṭhādapi raikvasya dvitīyo jānaśrutiḥ kṣatriyaḥ| nanu raikvo brāhmaṇa iti kuto'vagataṃ, yena śaunakadvitīyavat tadvitīyaḥ kṣatriyassyāt| tadanveṣaṇārthaṃ preṣite kṣattari tamanviṣyapratyāgate sati "yatrāre brāhmaṇasyānveṣaṇā tadenamacche"ti yat taponavādau brāhmaṇānāmāvāsasthale brāhmaṇasyānveṣaṇaṃ kartumucitaṃ, tatra gatvainaṃ prāpnuhītyarthakena tasya brāhmaṇatvasya sphuṭīkaraṇāt| tasmādiha āmantryamāṇasya kṣatriyatvāvagamāt na śūdretyāmantraṇena tasya vidyādikāraḥ| vidyāperadeśeṣu upadeśyasya upanayanasaṃskāraparāmarśāt śūdrasya tadabhāvābhilāpācca na śūdrasya vidyādhikāraḥ| chāndogye hyevamupākhyāyate śaisave pramītapatṛkassatyakāmo "vidyārthamācāryamupesya, brahmacarya cariṣyāmi kiṃ gotro'ha"miti mātaraṃ pṛṣṭvā tayā tvajjanakaparicaraṇaṃ kṛtvaiva sthitā'smināhaṃ gotraṃ vedetyuktaḥ gautamamāsādya "brahmacaryaṃ bhavati vatsyāmyupeyāṃ bhagavanta" mityuktvā "kiṃgotrasva " miti pṛṣṭo "nāhaṃ gotravaṃ veda mātā'pi nāvedīt
mātā me jabālā satyakāmo'ha" mityuvāca| atha gautamassatyavacanena "nāyamabrāhmaṇa" iti nirdhārya "samidhaṃ somyāharopatvānepya" ityuktavāniti| nanu aparāmṛṣṭamupanayanaṃ vidyāṅgamadhyayanāṅgaṃ vā ubhayadhāpi nopapadyate| vidyāṃgopanayane gotraparijñānasya anapekṣitatvena gotrapraśnānupapatteḥ| adhyayanāṃgopanayana eva gotrabhedena prakārabhedasattvena gotrapraśnopayogāt adhyayanāgopanayane "samidhaṃ somyāhare" ti viniyogānupapatteḥ| vidyāṃgopanayana eva "tadvijñānārthaṃ sa gurumevābhigacchetsamitpāṇiḥ śrotriyaṃ brahmaniṣṭha"miti tamitpāṇitvasya vihitatvāt, agre satyakāmāya ṣoḍaśakalabrahmavidyopadeśasyaiva darśanāt, vedādhyāpanasyādarśanācca| ucyate - upakramagatogotrapraśnānusārādadhyayanāṃgamidamupanayanaṃ| adhyayanantu "upanīye"tyanenaiva gṛhītaṃ| tasya adhyayanaparyaṃtopanayanaparatvāt| pūrvatantre hi "tasmādagnicidvarṣati na dhāve"dityatra agnici"dityasya yadarthogneścayanasaṃskāraḥ, tatparyantāgnicayanaparate tyagnicayanāṃgakakrataubhūta eva "varṣati na dhāve"dityādīnā āgnicidvatāni, na tvagnicayanasaṃskāramātre bhūta iti nirṇītaṃ kramalakṣaṇe|vidyāpradeśe adhyayanāṃgopa"nayanaparāmarśo vidyārthamapi tadapekṣāsti,atastadrahitasya na vidyādhikāra"iti jñānapanārthaḥ|samidāharaṇaniyogastu tasmin dayayā tasmai vidyāmapyupadekṣyāmītyabhiprāyo varṇanīyaḥ|astu śūdrasyāpyupanayanamiti cet na "na śūdrepātakaṃ kiñcinnaca saṃskāramarhati"tyetadanantaraṃ "dharmepsavastu dharmajñāssatāṃ vṛttimanuvratāḥ"|mantravarjannaduṣyanti praśsāṃ prāpnuvanti ce "ti
dhārmikāṇāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ mantravarjasaṃskārābhyanujñānaṃ kṛtamiti cet satyaṃ|tajjātakarmādimātraviṣayaṃ|na tu upanayanaviṣayamapi|"śūdraścaturtho varṇa ekajāti"riti gautamasmṛtau|āhatya tasya dvitīyajanmarūpopanayanābhāvasraṇāt ihāpi śūdratvābhāvanirdhāraṇe satyeva adhyayanāṃgopanayanapravṛtyupanyāsācca|kiṃca śūdrasya vedavākyaśravanatadadhyayanatadarthavicārajñānānuṣṭhānāni,"padyu vā etatśmaśānaṃ yacchūdraḥtasmācchūdrasamīpe nādhyetavya"miti
śūdrasamīpe adhyayanapratiṣedhena gautamasmṛtau "athahāsyavedamupaśruṇvatasrapujatubhyāṃ śrotrapratipūraṇamudāharaṇe jihvācchedo dhāraṇe śarīrabheda"iti śūdrasya vedaśravaṇādiṣu daṇḍavidhānena cārthātpratiṣidhyante|yasya hi samīpe'pi nādhyetavyaṃ vedavākyaṃ,yasya ca tacchravaṇe'pi mahān daṇḍaḥ,sa tatkathaṃ śruṇuyāt|aśruṇvataḥ kuto'dhyayanamanuṣṭhānaṃ vā,tasmānnaśūdrasya vidyādhikāra iti siddham||

kampanāt||40||
evaṃ prāsaṃgike'dhikaraṇatraye vṛtte yadaṃguṣṭhādhikaraṇānte sthitamuttaratra samāpayiṣyataityuktaṃ, tatsūtradvayena samāpayati - "aṃguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo madhya ātmanī"ti " aṃguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo'ntarātme"tyanayormantrayormadhye "yadidaṃ kiṃ ca jagatsarvaṃ prāṇa ejati nissṛtam| mahadbhayaṃ vajramudyataṃ ya etadviduramṛtāste bhavantī"ti mantraḥ paṭhyate| asyārthaḥ| asminnaṃguṣṭhamātre puruṣe prāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭe sthite tato nissṛtamidaṃ sarvaṃ jagadudyatavajratulyādetasmādyanmahatobhayaṃ tasmāddhetoḥ ejati - kampate|
tacchāsanātivṛttau pratyavāyo bhavaṣyatīti bhayāt svasvakāryeṣu pravartata ityarthaḥ| "bhayādasyāgnistapati bhayattapati sūryaḥ| bhayādindraśca vāyuśca mṛtyurdhāvati pañcama" ityanantaramaṃtre āgnyādīnāmaṃguṣṭhamātranimittabhayahetukasvasvavyāpārapravṛttyupanyāsādihāpimaṃtre "mahadbhayaṃ vajramudyata" miti prathamānāṃ pañcamyarthatāṃ kampata ityasya svasvavyāpārapravṛtyarthatāñcolocyaivaṃ vyākhyātam| evaṃrūpāt kampanādapi aṃguṣṭhapramitaḥ paramātmā||
vyotirdarśanāt||41||
tayoreva aṃguṣṭhamātraviṣayayormantrayormadhye " na tatra sūryo bhāti na caṃ dratārakaṃ nemā vidyuto bhāti
kuto'yamagniḥ| tameva bhāntamanubhāti sarvaṃ tasya bhāsā sarvamidaṃ vibhātī"ti paramātmāsādhāraṇaṃ sarvatejasāṃ chādakaṃ teṣāṃ kāraṇabhūtaṃ anugrāhakañca aṃguṣṭhamātrapuruṣasya jyotirdṛśyate| atra hi "tameva bhāntamanubhāti sarva" miti tadbhānānubhānarūpabhānapaurvāparyoktyā sarvatejaḥ - kāraṇatvamuktam| pūrvārdhena sarvatejaśchādakatvamuktam| caturthapādena sarvatejo'nugrāhakatvamuktaṃ| idaṃ paramātmāsādhāraṇam| "taṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ, yasyādityo bhāmupayujya bhātī" tyādiśrutyantaradarśanāt ātharvaṇasyaiva " na tatra sūrya" iti mantrasya pāṭhācca| evambhūtajyotirdarśanādapi aṃguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣaḥ paramātmā||
iti pramitādhikaraṇam||10||
ākāśo'rthāntaratvādivyapadeśāt||42||
suṣuptyutkrāntyorbhedena||43||
patyādiśabdebhyaḥ||44||
pūrvaṃ daharākāśasya parabrahmatvasamarthanāya alpaśrutiparihāre kṛte tatprasaṅgādaṃguṣṭhamātrasya paramātmatvaṃ sādhitam| tatprasaṅgāmataṃ tu adhikaraṇatrayam| atha pūrvaprakṛcadaharavidyāyā buddhisthatvena tadanantaraśrutasyākāśavākyasya paramātmaparatvaṃ nirūpyata iti saṅgatiḥ| chāndogye daharavidyānantaraṃ śrūyate - "ākāśo havai nāmarūpayornirvahitā te yadantara tadbrahma tadamṛtaṃ sa ātme"ti| tatra ākāśaḥ "aśva iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpaṃ candra iva rāhormukhātpramucya| dhūtvā śarīramakṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahmalokamabhisambhavānī"ti pūrvamantraprakṛto muktātmā, pūrvapakṣaḥ - sa muktātmā| " te yadantare"ti nāmarūpamadhyasthitatvoktyā nāmarūpāspṛṣṭatvavarṇanāt| "nāmarūpayornirvahate"ti tu muktātmanu pūrvāvasthābhiprāyeṇa upapadyate| sa hi baddhāvasthāyāṃ nāmarūpayornitarāṃ voḍhā, devamanuṣyādijanmaparyāyeṇa bahuvidhanāmarūpavoḍhā sthita ityevaṃ prāpta ucyate - 
ayamākāśaḥ paramātmā| na muktātmā| muktātmabhedopapādakanāmarūpanirvoḍhṛtvaśravaṇāt| nirvoḍhṛtvaṃ hi rūḍhyā kartṛtvam| na tu yogena nitarāṃ voḍhṛtvam| rūḍheḥ prabalatvāt| nāmarūpakartṛtvaṃ tu jīvasya na bandhamuktyavasthayossambhavati| jagadvyāpāraniṣedhāt| tattu paramātmana eva dharmaḥ| "nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi, tasmādetadbrahma nāma rūpamannañca jāyata" ityādi śruteḥ| tasmādyadyapi pūrvamaṃtre abhisambhavitṛtvena muktātmā prakṛtaḥ| tathā'pi abhisambhāvyatvena paramātmā'pi prakṛta iti tadviṣaya evāyamākāśaśabdaḥ| ākāśaśabdena prakṛtasya daharākāśasya pratyabhijñānādapi tadviṣayo'yaṃ| naca prajāpativākyena vyavadhānāt na tatpratyabhijñānamastīti śaṅkanīyam| tasyāpyupāsakasvarūpayāthātmyakathanadvārā daharavākyaśeṣatvāt| daharavākye daharākāśaupāsyatayopakrānta iha prāpyatayopasaṃhriyata iti viśeṣaḥ| atha syāt jāvātmano bhinnaṃ ātmāntarameva nāsti| bṛhadāraṇyake - "yo'yaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇe" ṣviti prakṛtasya jīvasya "eṣa brahmalokassammrāḍi" ti nirdiṣṭaparamātmaikyopadeśāt, "neha nānāsti kiṃ cane" ti bhedaniṣedhācca| eṃ ca nāmarūpakartṛtvamapi muktātmani ghaṭate| tasyaiva paramātmatvāt| ato'yamākāśo na muktātmā, kiṃ tu tadbhinnaḥ paramātmeti nirṇayo na yukta iti cet, maivaṃ| bṛhadāraṇyake prāṇeṣviti jīvaṃ prakṛtya "prājñenātmanā'nvarūḍhautsarjanyātī"tyutkrāntau ca, bhedena vyapadeśāt "eṣa sarveśvara eṣa bhūtādhipatireṣa bhūtapāla" ityādivākyaśrutapariṣvajjakaviṣayapatyādiśabdebhyaśca jīvabhinnasya paramātmanaḥ pratyākhyānāsambhavāt| aikyopadeśabhedaniṣedhau tu prakāryaikyanibandhanāviti samanantarapāde vakṣyate| tasmādihākāśaḥ paramātmā na muktātmeti yuktameva||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
muktātmā prakṛtassyāt ākāśo nāmarūpanirvoḍhā|
