mahadvacca||7||
iha pāde sāṃkhyatantrapratipādanacchāyāpannānāṃ vākyānāṃ sāṃkhyābhimatārthanirāsena brahmaṇi samanvayaḥ pratipādyata iti pādārthastatsaṅgatiścetyubhayamapi prathamapāda eva darśitaṃ| kāṭhake śrūyate - "indriyebhyaḥ parā hyarthā arthebhyaśca parammanaḥ| manasastu parā buddhi rbuddherātmā mahān paraḥ|| mahataḥ paramavyaktamavyaktātpuruṣaḥ paraḥ|| puruṣānnaparaṃ kiṃcit sā kāṣṭhā sā parāgati"riti| tatra kiṃ sāṃkhyatantrasiddhaṃ pradhānamavyaktaśabdanocyate, uta śarīramiti śākhyatatvaprakriyāpratyabhijñānaśarīrīpariśeṣābhyāṃ saṃśaye, pūrvapakṣaḥ sāṃkhyābhimataṃ pradhānamidamavyaktam| "mahataḥ paramavyaktamavyktātpuruṣaḥ paraḥ" iti tatprakriyāpratyabhijñānāt, "puruṣānna paraṃ kiṃci"diti pañcaviṃśapuruṣātiriktatatvaniṣedhācca| na tu śarīramavyaktaṃ| vyakte tasminnavyaktaśabdasya śaktyabhāvāt| lakṣaṇātaḥ pradhāne rūḍherbalavattvāt| sāṃkhyakalpitā sā rūḍhiḥ naiyāyikakalpitapakṣasapakṣādirūḍhivadanyatraśabdārthanirṇaye anupayoginīti cenna| sāṃkhyatantrasiddhatatvānuvādoyamiti tatprakriyāpratyabhijñānāt siddhau tadīyarūḍhereva grāhyatvāt, "na pakṣe na sapakṣe vā vipakṣe kevalaṃ sthitaḥ| citraṃnisiṃraśadhūmaste pratīpāgnyanumāpaka" iti kāvyaśloke naiyāyikakalpatirūḍherapyupayogadarśanāt| nanu "puruśānna paraṃ kiṃci"dityatra puruṣaḥ paṃcaviṃśa iti kathamavagamyate, yenāyaṃ paṃcaviṃśātiriktaniṣedhassyāditi cet, itthaṃ| jñānaṃkarmeṃdriyāṇi daśoṃdriyaśabdagṛhītāni, bhūtatanmātrāḥ daśārthaśabdoditāḥ, mano'ntaḥ karaṇaṃ buddhirahaṅkāraḥ mahānātmā mahattattvamavyaktaṃ prathānamiti sāṃkhyābhimatāni caturviṃśatistatvāni prāṅnirdiṣṭāni| ataḥ puruṣaḥ paṃcaviṃśaḥ| yadyapi sāṃkhyānāmmahattatva eva buddhiśabdaḥ, ātmaśabdaśca cetane, tathāpi pratyabhijñāpakabhūyastvānusāreṇa kārye kāraṇopacārādahaṅkāre buddhiśabdaḥ, mahattatve vyāpakatvayogenātmaśabdaḥ| "yacchedvāṅmanasī prājñastadyacchejjñāna ātmanī" tyuparitanamantre jñānepyātmaśabdadarśanāt| nanu pūrvatra "ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ rathameva tu| buddhintu sārathiṃ viddhi manaḥ pragrahamevaca|| indriyāṇi hayānāhurviṣayāṃsteṣugocarā" nityātmaśarīrādiṣu rathirathādirūpeṇa rūpiteṣu indriyādipatvavākye śarīravyatiriktānīṃdriyādiśabdairgṛhītāni, śarīramekaṃ svaśabdena nagṛhītaṃ, avyaktaśabdaścādhikostīti pariśeṣādavyaktaśabdo lakṣaṇayāpi śarīrārthako vaktuṃ yukta iti cet na| rathirathādirūpeṇa nirūpitānyeva indriyādiparatvavākyepyanyūnātirekeṇa
grāhyāṇīti niyame kāraṇābhāvāt| tathā niyame hi na
tāvadrūpakavākyasyākāṃkṣākāraṇaṃ| tasya vākyasyendriyādibhirasaṃyatairjīvaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ saṃsārameti saṃyataistumuktimiti pradarśanārthe rathirathādirūpeṇāṃgabhāvamāsādya nirākāṃkṣatvāt| nāpīndriyādiparatvavākyasyākāṃkṣā tatra kāraṇaṃ| tasyāpi puruṣapatvapratipādane'ṅgabhāvamāsādya nirākāṃkṣatvāt| ataḥ pariśeṣādavyaktaśabdaśśarīralakṣaka ityetadayogāt sāṃkhyatantraprakriyāpratyabhijñānusāreṇa tattantrasiddhapradhānārthaka ityeva yuktamiti| evaṃ prāpte rāddhāntaḥ - 
pūrvatra rathirathādirūpeṇa rūpitānāṃ madhye yaccharīraṃ ratharūpakeṇa vinyastaṃ tadātmanārūpitaṃ tadavyaktaśabdena pariśeṣāt gṛhyata iti yuktam| tatrahyātmā śarīraṃ buddhirmana indriyāṇitadviṣayā iti ṣaḍarthā ratharathisārathitadgṛhyamāṇaturagarajjuturagatanmārgātmanā yadrūpitāḥ tanmuktayarthopāsanopakaraṇatvādvaśīkāryatvāyeti tāvannirvivādaṃ| "yastvavijñānavānbhavati ayuktena manasā saha| tasyendriyāṇyavaśyāni duṣṭāśvā iva sāratheḥ| yastu vijñānavānbhavati yuktena manasā saha| tasyeṃndriyāṇi vaśyāni sadaśvā iva sārathe"riti tatraiva śravaṇāt| tatra ca vaśīkāryatāyāṃ prādhānyāprādhānyavivecanamapekṣitaṃ yat pradhānaṃ tadvaśīkaraṇe yatnātiśayasiddhye| indriyādiparatvavākyamapi vaśīkāryatāyāṃ prādhānyāprādhānyarūpaṃ parāparabhāvaṃ vinā viṣayāntarannalabhate| na hi "daśamanvantarāṇīha tiṣṭhantīndriyacintakāḥ| bhautikāstu śataṃ pūrṇaṃ sahasrantvābhimānikāḥ|| bauddhā daśasahasrāṇi tiṣṭhanti vigatajvarāḥ| pūrṇaṃ śatasahasrantu tiṣṭhantyavyaktacintakāḥ|| puruṣannirguṇaṃ prāpya kālasaṃkhyā na vidyata" iti sāṃkhyakalpitatattadupāsanāphalabhūyastvālpatvarūpaḥ parāparabhāva iti vaktuṃ śakyam| tatra manasaḥ indriyebhyaḥ pṛthakparigaṇanābhāvena bhūtatanmātrāpekṣayā nikarṣāpattyā tadviṣayatve "arthebhyaścaparammana" iti manaindriyasya arthaśabdoktabhūtaparatvoktyanupapatteḥ| nāpi hetuhetumadbhāvarūpa iti vaktuṃ śakyam| sāṃkhyamate indriyāṇāmāhaṅkārikatvena " indriyebhyaḥ parāhyarthā" iti bhūtatanmātrāṇāmiṃdriyaparatvoktyanupapatteḥ, teṣāṃ bhautikatvābhyupagame "arthebhyaśca paraṃ mana " iti manaṃiṃdriyasya bhūtaparatvoktayanupapatteḥ| tasmāt iṃdriyebhyaḥ tadviṣayāḥ vaśīkāryatve parāḥ| vaśyeṃdriyasyāpi viṣayasannidhāviṃdriyavijṛmbhaṇāt| tebhyo'pi manaḥ paraṃ| manasi viṣayadhyānapravṛtte viṣayāsannidhāne rāgavijṛmbhaṇāt| tasmādapi buddhiḥ parā| adhyavasāyābhāve manaso'kiṃcitkaratvāt| tato'pyātmā paraḥ| sarvasyā api viṣayavṛtteḥ ātmecchāyattatvāt| tata evātmā aṇurapi mahāniti viśeṣyate| tasmādapi śarīraṃ paraṃ| tadāyattatvāt sarvāsāmapi puruṣapravṛttīnāṃ| tasmādapi parassarvāntaryāmī sarvaprerakaḥ paripūrṇaḥ puruṣottama iti indriyādayo vaśīkāryatāyāmuttarottaraṃ parā ucyanta iti tadartho vācyaḥ| sarvāntaryāmiṇo bhagavato vaśīkaraṇaṃ taccharaṇāgatireva| "tameva śaraṇaṃ gacche" tyādivacanāt| tadidamiṃdriyādiparamapuruṣāntānāṃ vaśīkāryatāyāmuttarottaraparatvamagre śrutireva darśayati - "yacchedvāṅmanasī prājñastadyacchejjñāna ātmani| jñānamātmani mahati niyacchettadyacchecchāntaātmanī"ti| asya hi mantrasyāyamarthaḥ| vāgupalakṣitaṃ sarvaṃ jñānakarmendriyajātaṃ manasi niyacchet| bāhyendriyavyāpāramutsṛjya manomātreṇāvatiṣṭhet| viṣayavikalpābhimukhaṃ tanmanaḥ ātmani sthitāyāṃ buddhau viṣayadoṣādhyavasāyarūpāyāṃ niyacchet| tāṃ budiṃdha mahati pratyagātmani niyacchet| tadupādeyatāviṣayāṃ tatpravaṇāṃ kuryāt| tañca pratyagātmānaṃ nirūpaplave paramātmani niyacchet| taccheṣatāpratipatiṃta kuryāditi| tasmātsūkṣmavyākṛtamavyaktaśabdārhamiti tadavasthāṃtararūpaṃ śarūramupacārādavyaktamityuktam| yadi sūkṣmamavyākṛtamabhyupagamyate tarhi tadeva sāṃkhyamatamāpannamiti kathaṃ tannirasanam| svatantramevāvyākṛtaṃ kāryāṇi sādhayatīti sāṃkhyamatannirasanīyam| paramapuruṣādhinatvāt tena preryamāmamevāvyākṛtammahadādikāryajātaṃ sādhayatīti tvasmākamabhyupagama iti viśeṣaḥ| kiñca - yadi sāṃkhyābhimatamavyaktamihaśrutiravakṣyat, tadātasyatatkāryasya vyaktajātasyaca jñeyatvamapyavakṣyat| "vyaktāvyaktajñavijñānānmokṣa" iti hi sāṃkhyānāmuddhoṣaḥ| nanu "aśabdamasparśamarūpamavyayantathā'rasannityamagandhavacca yat| anādyanantammahataḥ paraṃ dhruvaṃ nicāyya tanmṛtyumukhāt pramucyata " ityanantarameva sā śrutiravyākṛtaṃ vadati| vyaktadharmabhūtaśabdādiniṣedhānāmavyaktaparyavasānasya nyāyyatvāt mahataḥ paratvokteśca yattaditi sāmānyato'pi nirdeśasyāvyākṛtaparatvaucityāditicenmaivam| "vijñānasārathiryastu manaḥpragravānnaraḥ| so'dhvanaḥ pāramāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ pada"miti paramapuruṣaprakaraṇāt| sa eva hyanyatra nicāyyatvana pratipādyate| ata eva "puruṣānnaparaṃ ki ci"diti pañcaviṃśapuruṣātiriktatatvaniṣedha iti śaṅkā
nirastā| prakaraṇena tatra puruṣaśabdasya paramapuruṣaparatvāt| "aśabdamasparśa" mityādi tu "yattadadreśyamagrāhya" mityādivat tasminnapyupapadyate| "mahataḥ para"miti "buddherātmā mahānpara" iti prastutāt jīvāt paratvamupaktam| apicāsminprakaraṇe prāpyasya paramapuruṣasya prāptyupāyasyopāsanasya prāpturupāsakasyaceti trayāṇāmeva jñeyatvenopanyāsaḥ praśnaśca dṛśyate| tatra "taṃ durdarśaṃ
gūḍhamanupraviṣṭaṃ guhāhitaṃ gahvareṣṭhaṃ purāṇam| adhyātmayogādhigamena devaṃ matvā dhīro harṣaśokau jahātī"ti| ataṃ "devaṃ matvā dhīra" iti krameṇa upāsyopāsanopāsakānāmupanyāsaḥ, "yeyaṃ prete vicikitsā manuṣye'stītyeke nāyamastīti caike| etadvidyāmanuśiṣṭastvayā'haṃ varāṇameṣa varastṛtīya" iti mokṣarūpapraśnadvārā upāyopeyopetṝṇāṃ trayāṇāmapi praśnaḥ| pretaḥ prakarṣeṇetaḥ apunarāvṛtiṃta mukiṃta prāptaḥ| ātmocchattirmuktiriti śūnyavādināṃ vimatiḥ "nāyamastīti caika " ityupanyastā| natvatra pretaśabdo mṛtavacanaḥ| mṛtāstitvasya vaivasvataṃ lokaṃ prāpya taṃ pṛcchatā " na sāmparāyaḥ pratibhāti bālaṃ pramādyaṃtaṃ vittamohena mūḍham| ayaṃ loko nāsti para iti mānī punaḥpunaryaśamāpadyate ma" ityādivaivasvatavacanaṃ śrutavatā ca naciketasā nirjñātatvena mṛte dehātirikte nāstitvavimatyupanyāsāyogāt| tasmātrayāṇāmevātra jñeyatvenopanyāsaḥ praśnaśceti neha tāṃtrikasyāvyaktasyānuvādaprasaktirasti| api ca "buddherātmā mahānpara" ityatra mahattatvannagrāhyaṃ, cetanarūḍhātmaśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt| "tadyacchejjñāna ātmanī"tyatra saptamyau tu vaiyadhikaraṇyena prāgvyākhyātau| ato'pi jīvātmaparatvenocyamānamavyaktaṃ na sāṃkhyatantrasiddhaṃ pradhānamiti yuktam| mahattatvaparatvānabhidhānāt||
saṃgrahakārike||
mahataḥ paramavyaktaṃ paṭhitaṃ yaddhi kāṭhake|
pradhānaṃ kapiloktaṃ tattattantrapratyabhijñayā||
maivaṃ tatsyāccharīraṃ rathirathaturagādyātmanā rūpitānā
mātmādīnāṃ mumukṣo rniyatamiha vaśīkāryatājñāpanārtham|
etasyāṃ tāratamyasphuṭasamadhigamāyendriyebhyaḥ parāi
tyārabhyāmnātamantre nyasanamabhilaṣatyasvaśabdaṃ śarīram||
iti ānumānikādhikaraṇam||
camasavadaviśeṣāt||8||
jyotirupakramātu tathāhyadhīyata eke||9||
kalpanopadeśācca madhvādivadavirodha||10||
pūrvatra rūpakavākye śarīraśravaṇādavyaktaṃ śarīraṃ na taṃtrasiddhā prakṛtirityuktam| tarhi tadaśravaṇādaṃjā taṃtrasiddhā prakṛtissyāditi pratyavasthānātsaṅgatiḥ| śvetāśvaropaniṣadaḥ paṭhyate - "ajāmekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇāṃ bahvīḥ prajāssṛjamānāṃ sarūpāḥ| ajohyeko juṣamāṇo'nuśete jahātyenāṃ bhuktabhogāmajo'nya" iti| atra kimajāśabdena tantrasiddhā svatantrā prakṛtirucyate, uta siddhāntābhimatā brahmādhīnā prakṛtiriti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| tantrasiddhaivocyate| "ajā"miti janmarāhityaśravaṇāt, "sṛjamānā"miti svātaṃtryaśravaṇācca| na tu brahmātmikā| tasyāssiddhānte brahmakāraṇakatvābhyupagamena janmarāhityābhāvāt, sṛṣṭau brahmapreryatvābhyupagamena svātantryābhāvācceti| siddhāntastu - 
brahmakāraṇikāyā api janmarāhityamupapādayiṣyate| tathā ajāśabdayaugikārthasya janmarāhityasya sādhāraṇatvāt na tena tatrasiddheti viśeṣaḥ pratīyate| yathā "arvāgbilaścamasaūrdhvabudhnastasmin yaśonihitaṃ viśvarūpam| tasyāsata ṛṣayastaptatīra" ityasmin maṃtre yaugikena camasaśabdena camyate bhakṣyate aneneti vyutpattimatā bhakṣaṇasādhanamātraṃ pratīyate| na kaścitviśeṣaḥ, yaugikaśabdānāmārthaprakaraṇavākyaśeṣādibhirvinā'rthaniścayāsambhavāt| tatra "yathedaṃ tacchira eṣahyarvāgbilaścamasa ūrdhvabudhna" iti vākyaśeṣaḥ śiro'tra camasa iti niścāyako'sti| tasminnihitaṃ yaśaḥ prāṇavāyuḥ tasya
tīrasthāssapta ṛṣayaśśrotracakṣurghrāṇayugalarasanānīti niścāyako vākyaśeṣo'sti| naivamihājā taṃtrasiddhā prakṛtirityatra kiñcinniścāyakamasti| sṛṣṭisvātaṃtryaṃ tu svavyāpārakartṛtvarūpaṃ parapreryatve'ryupapadyate| sārathinā preryasyāpi rathasya "ratho gacchatī" ti svātaṃtryavyavahārāt| tasmādiha tantrasiddhā ajā na grāhyā| kiṃ tu brahāmātmikaiva grāhyā| brahmakāraṇakatvāmnānāt| taittirītyā hi "aṇoraṇīyānmahato mahīyā" niti maṃtreṇa brahmopakramya "sapta prāṇāḥ prabhavaṃti tasmā"dityādinā "yasmājjātā na parā naiva kiṃ ca nāse"tyaṃtena prāṇasamudraparvatādikasya sarvasya prapañcasya brahmaṇassakāśātprabhavamadhīyānāsteṣāṃ maṃtrāṇāṃ madhya eva "ajāmekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇāṃ bahvīṃ prajāṃ janayantīṃ sarūpām| ajohyeko juṣamāṇo'niśete jahātyenāṃ bhuktabhogāmajo'nya" iti paṭhanti| ato'tra brahmakāryavargamadhye pāṭhādajā'pi
brahmakāraṇikā vivakṣitetyavasīyate| nanu ayamajāmantrastadbhogatyāgarūpabandhamuktajīvṛttāṃtaviṣayaḥ, kathamiha "ajā"miti dvitīyāntena "sapta prāṇāḥ prabhavaṃtī tyanuvṛttakriyānvayaḥ| ato nāsyāḥ prāṇādivat brahmakāraṇakatvaṃ pratīyaiticet ucyate - yathā "tābhyāmetamagnīṣomīyamekādaśakapālaṃ pūrṇamāse prāyaccha" ditivākyamiṃdravṛttāṃtamātraviṣayatayā pratīyamānamapi darśapūrṇamāsaprakaraṇānurodhādiṃdreṇa agnīṣomābhyāṃ dattatayocyamānasya puroḍāśadravyakasya yāgasya vidhāyakaṃ, tathā vṛttāntamātraviṣayatayā pratīyamāno'pi ayaṃ maṃtrassakalakāryajanayitryāḥ prakṛteḥ parameśvarasṛjyatvapratipādaka iti pūrvāparaparāmarśādaṅgīkartavyam| nanu kathaṃ prakṛteḥ parameśvarakāraṇakatvaṃ janmarāhityaḍhca| sthūlasūkṣmabhedānnaitadvirūddham| yathā madhuvidyāyāmādityasya vvādidevatābhogyarasāśrayatvodayāstamayādimattvaṃ, "atha tataūrdhvaṃ udetya naivodetā nāstametaikalaeva madhye sthāte" tyuktam sarvaviśeṣaprahāṇenaikatayā'vasthānañca kāryakāraṇavasthābhedarūpasthūlasūkṣmāvasthābhedena na viruddham| "ekala" iti lakāropajanaśchāndasaḥ| ekaevetyarthaḥ| tasmāt brahmādhīnanaiveyamajā taṃtrasiddheriti siddham||
saṃgrahakārike||
svātaṃtryajanirāhitye sṛjamānāmajāmiti|
yasyāśśrute sā prakṛtissāṃkyoktā na parātmikā||
maivaṃ brahmātnikāyāmapi yadajanitā kāraṇāvasthayā syāt
svavyāpārāśrayatvaṃ rathavadiha parapreryatāyāmapi syāt'
tasmānnāyaṃ viśeṣo'styapitu parasṛjiprakriyāsteviśeṣa
statsṛāyāntatassyādativiśadamajāmantratātparyamasyāḥ||
iti camasādhikaraṇam||2||
na saṃkhyopasaṃgrahādapi nānābhāvādatirekācca||11||
prāṇādayo vākyaśeṣāt||12||
jyotiṣaikeṣāmasatyanne||13||
ajā śabdasya svatantraprakṛtipatve viśeṣakasyābhāvāt vākyāntarānurodhena brahmātmakatvaṃ sādhitam| tadatranaprasarati| brahmasatve saṃkhyādhikyāt, tena vinaiva saṃkhyāpūraṇaena abrahmātmikā prakṛtiriti niścayasaṃbhavāditi śaṅkayā saṅgatiḥ| vājasaneyinassamāmanaṃti| "yasminpañca pañajanā ākāśaśca pratiṣṭhitaḥ| tameva manyaātmānaṃ vidvān brahmāmṛto'mṛta"miti| ayammantraḥ sāṃkhyatantrasiddhatatvaparaḥ uta vākyaśeṣāmnātaprāṇādipara iti paṃcaviṃśatisaṃkhyātatvādhikyapratītibhyāṃsaṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| sāṃkhyatatvaparaḥ| paṃcaviṃśatisaṃkhyātatvādhikyapratīteḥ| tatpratiśca paṃcaśabdaviśeṣitātpañcaśabdāt| paṃcajanāiti hi na manuṣyādivācīśabdaḥ| prathamapaṃcaśabdasamabhivyāhāreṇa dvitīyapaṃcaśabdasyāpi saṃkhyāparatvāvasāyāt| tataśca pañcānāṃ janānāṃ samūhāiti samāhāraviṣayoyaṃ samāsaḥ| tathā niścaye ca paṃcapūlyaitivadīkārābhāvo liṅgavyatyayaśca chāndasassamarthanīyaḥ| taddhitārthottarapadayorabhāvena samāsasya samāhāviṣayatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt| te samūhāḥ katītyākāṃkṣāyāṃ punaḥ pañcasaṃkhyayā viśeṣyante "pañca pañca pūlya" itivat| nanu samāsamabhaayupagamya chāndasatvena rūmasamarthanaṃ kimarthaṃ, asamāse'pi pañcaśabdadvayena
pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyālābhopapatteḥ| evaṃ saṃvardhanena saṃkhyāviśeṣalābho'pyanyatra saṃpratipanna eva| yathā "pañcapañcāśatasrivṛtassaṃvatsarā" ityatra pañcaguṇitapaṃcāśatsaṃkhyayā paṃcāśaduttaradviśatasaṃkhyālābhaḥ| yathāvā "vayaso vayasassapta daśasaptadaśāni dadātī"tyatra saptadaśaguṇitasaptadaśasaṃkhyayā ekonanavatyuttaradviśatasaṃkhyālābhaḥ| yathāvā "paṃcabhirdhātā vidadhāvidaṃ yattāsāṃ svasṝrajanayat paṃca paṃca tāsāmuyaṃti prayaveṇa paṃca nānārūpāṇi kratavo vasānāḥ tiṃraśatsvasāra upayaṃti niṣkṛta" mityatra paṃcaguṇitapaṃcasaṃkhyayā paṃcaviṃśatisaṃkhyālābhā iti cet maivaṃ| paṃcajanaśabdasya samāsapadatvena tannāyāyanavatārāt| samāsapadatvaṃca śatapathabrāhmaṇapaṭhitasyāsya mantrasya antānudāttasvareṇa niścīyate| asyāntānudāttasvarasya śatapathabrāhmaṇasvaravidhāyakabhāṣikākhyagranthavihitabrāhmaṇapaṭhitamantratvanimittodāttādeśarūpatvāt| atastasya "sthāninā samāsasye" ti vyākaraṇavihitena antodāttasvareṇa "paṃcānāṃtvā paṃcajanānā"miti mantrāntare samāsadarśanena ca atrāpi samāsena bhāvyamiti| tasmātsamāhārasamāso'yaṃ, chāndasatvena rūpasiddhirityeva yuktam| na ca tatvādhikyaprasaṅgaḥ| caturviṃśatestatvānāṃ ātmani pratiṣṭhitatvavacanaṃ tadadṛṣṭāyattatvāt bhāktaṃ, ātmanastadvacanaṃ svamahimapratiṣṭhitatvavacanavat bhāktamityupapatteḥ|
sāṃkhyatatvāparigrahe śrutapaṃcaviṃśatisaṃkhyānvayānirvāhāditi| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ||
paṃcaviṃśatisaṃkhyopasaṃgrahādapi na sāṃkhyatatvapratītiḥ| tebhyaḥ pṛthagbhāvāt| tadvailakṣaṇyāt| "yasmin paṃca paṃcajanā" iti teṣāṃ yacchabdanirdiṣṭabrahmātmakatāpratīteḥ, ātmākāśābhyāṃ saṃkhyātirekācca| naca paṃcaviṃśatisaṃkhyānvayānurodhena pūrvapakṣāvasānoktā kliṣṭagatiḥ kalpanīyā| kaṭhavallīṣu "indriyebhyaḥ parāhyarthā" ityādinā puruṣāvaratvena varṇitānāṃ jñānendriyakarmendriyabhūtatanmātrā manobuddhibhoktṛsthūlasūkṣmaśarīrāṇāṃ "taccākāśaṃ sanātana"mityādiṣvākāśaśabdena prasiddhasya aprākṛtabhagavallokasya ca grahaṇasambhavāt kaṭhavallīṣu avyaktaśabdasya sthūlasūkṣmaśarīradvayaparatvopapatteḥ. evamakleśena antaraṅgaśākhāntaradṛṣṭasaṃkhyopasaṃgrahasaṃbhave kliṣṭagatyā bahiraṅgāprāmāṇikasāṃkhyatatvopasaṃgrahāyogtā| vastuto'tra paṃcaviṃśatisaṃkhyāpratītireva nāsti| samāhārasamāse srīpratyayābhāvapulliṃgayoḥ chāndasatvena samarthanīyatvāt, paṃcajanaśabdasya saṃjñāśabdatvamabhyupagamya "diksaṃkhye saṃjñāyā" miti sūtravihitasaṃjñaāsamāsavarṇanopapatteḥ| nanu tarhi paṃcajanasaṃjñakāḥ kecidiha varṇanīyā iti cet satyam| santyeva vākyaśeṣaśrutāḥ prāṇādayaḥ "prāṇasya prāṇamuta cakṣuṣaścakṣuśśrotrasyaśrotramannasyānnaṃ manaso ye manovidu"riti vākyaśeṣe hi ṣaṣṭhyantaiḥ prāṇādiśabdaiḥ prasiddhāḥ prāṇādayaḥ śrūyante| nanu śatapathabrāhmaṇe mādhyandinānāmevānnapāṭhaḥ| na kāṇvānām| atasteṣāṃ prāṇādayaḥ paṃcapaṃcajanā nasyuriti cet ucyate - "yasminpaṃcapaṃcajanā" ityasmāt pūrvasminmaṃtre "taṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ jyotirāyurhopāsate'mṛta" miti jyotiṣāṃ jyotiṣṭvena brahmaṇyabhidhīyamāne brahmādhīnasvakāryāṇāṃ keṣāṃcijjotiṣāṃ pratipādakena ṣaṣṭhyantajyotiśśabdena prakāśakāni jñānendriyāṇai gṛhyanta iti indriyaviśeṣāṇāṃ samarpakeṇa "prāṇasya prāṇa" mityādivākyaśeṣeṇāvadhāryate| tatra "prāṇasye"ti śabdena vāyusambandhinassparśendriyasya grahaṇam| mukhyaprāṇasya jyotiśśabdena ṣaṣṭhyantena pradarśanāyogāt| "cakṣuṣa" iti "śrotrasye" ti cakṣuśśrotrendriyayorgrahaṇam| "annasye"tyannaśabdoditapṛthivīsambhandhighrāṇendriyagrahaṇam| tenaiva adyate'nenānnamiti vyutpattyā taṃtreṇa rasanendriyasyāpi grahaṇam| "manasa" iti mana indriyasya| evaṃ gṛhītānāṃ sarveṣāmindriyāṇaāṃ grahaṇasidhyarthaṃ kaṇvaśākhāyāmapyupasaṃhartavyam| ṣaṣṭhyantajyotiḥpadoditendriyopakramasya vākyaśeṣagateṃdriyaviśeṣopanyāsasya ca tatrāpi tulyatvāt tadevaṃ "yasmin paṃca paṃcajanā ākāśaśca pratiṣṭhita " iti paṃcajanaśabdanirdiṣṭāni indriyāṇi ākāśaśabdopalakṣitāni bhūtāni ca brahmaṇi pratiṣṭhitānīti sarvatatvānāṃ brahmāśrayatvapratipādanāt natantrasiddhabrahmānadhīnapaṃcaviṃśatitatvānuvādaśaṅkāvakāśaiti sthitam||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
yasminpañceti manau pacoktāḥ paṃcajanasamāhārāḥ|
tatvāni paṃcaviṃśatirete kapilena kathitāni||
maivaṃ brahmāśrayatābhedānnahidhīstaduktatvānāṃ|
adhikaraṇā''kāśābhyāṃ taduktasaṃkhyātirekācca||
paṃcajanāiti saṃjñāpadena paṃceṃndriyāṇi kathyante|
dṛṣṭāni yāni śeṣe brahmādhīnasvakāryāṇi||
saṃgrāhyaṃ kāṇvairapyannapadaṃ ghrāṇarasanaparaṃ|
jyotiḥ padoditendriyasamupakramavākyaśeṣagatitaulyāt||
iti saṃkhyopasaṃgrahādhikaraṇam||3||
kāraṇatvenacākāśādiṣu yathāvyapadiṣṭokteḥ||14||
samākarṣāt||15||
mābhūvan paṃcapaṃcajanāssāṃkhyatatvāni, tathāpi brahmakāraṇamiti siddhāṃtyabhimataṃ na sidhyati| kāraṇavākyānāṃ parasparavirodhena kvacidapi pratiṣṭhitatvābhāvāt, tāni vihāya smṛtyanusāreṇa pradhānasyaiva kāraṇatayā vaktavyatvāditi pratyavasthānātsaṅgatiḥ| kāraṇavākyaiḥ brahma kāraṇamiti niścetuṃ śakyaṃ, uta parasparavirodhena taiḥ kimapi niścetuṃ naśakyamiti, tānyanapekṣya kapilādismṛtibhiḥ pradhānaṃ kāraṇamiti niśceyamiti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - chāṃdogye "sadevasomyedamagraāsīdekamevādvitīya"miti satpūrvikāsṛṣṭirāmnātā| tatraiva "taddhaika āhurasadevedamagraāsīt ekamevādvitīya"mityasratkāraṇavādamupakṣipya "kutastukhalu somyaivaṃsyāt kathamasatassajjāyete"ti asatkāraṇavādaṃ nirākṛtya
"sadevasomyedamagra āsī"diti satpūrvikāsṛṣṭiḥ sthirīkṛtā| taittirīyaketu chāndogye nīrākṛtamasatkāraṇavādaṃ parigṛhya asatpūrvikā sṛṣṭirāmnātā| "asadvā idamagraāsīt tato vai sadajāyata" iti| bṛhadāraṇyake'pi tathaivāmnāyate - "sadevedamagraāsīt tatsadāsīt tatsamabhava"diti| tatraiva avyākṛtapūrvikā'pi sṛṣṭirāmnāyate - "taddhedaṃ tarhyavyākṛtamāsīt tannāmarūpābhyāṃ vyākriyate"ti| avyākṛtaṃ hyavyaktaṃ pradhānameva| evaṃ kāraṇavākyānāṃ parasparavirodhena brahma vā svatantraṃ prādhānaṃvaikaṃ kāraṇamiti nirṇayālābhesati āptatamakapilādipraṇītasmṛtibhiḥ pradhānaṃ kāraṇamiti niśceyaṃ, tatraiva sarvāṇi kāraṇavākyāni yojanīyāni, īkṣaṇādayo'pi gauṇāyojanīyāiti| evaṃprāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
brahmaṇaeva jagadutpadyata iti niścetuṃ śakyate| janmādisūtre sarvajñatvasatyasaṅkalpatvādiviśiṣṭaṃ brahmayathāvyapadiṣṭaṃtathābhūtasyatasyaikasyaiva "tasmādvāetasmādātmanaākāśassaṃbhūtaḥ, tattejo'sṛjate"tyādiśrutibhirākāśādiṣu kāraṇatvena pratipādanāt| nanvastu tathāpratipādanaṃ, tathā'pyudāhṛtakāraṇavākyajātavigānaṃ kathaṃ samādheyaṃ| ucyate - ānandavallyāṃ "so'kāmayata bahusyāṃ prajāyeye" ti padena pūrvaprakṛtamānaṃdamayaṃ parāmṛśya tasya jagatsṛṣṭisaṅkalpamuktvā "idaṃ sarvamasṛjata yadidaṃ kiṃce"tyādinā tasya sarvasraṣṭṛtvasarvānupraveśasarvātmabhāvānabhidhāya "tadapyeṣa ślokobhavatī"tyukte sarvasminnarthe sākṣitvena "asadvā idamagra āsī"diti śloka udāhṛtaḥ| tasmādānandamayasamākarṣāt asacchabdastasminnevānabhivyaktanāmarūpatābhiprāyo vaktavyaḥ|"asadevedamagra āsī" dityatrāpiyameva nirvāhaḥ| asacchabdoktasya "tatsadāsī" diti satpadena samākarṣāt, atyantāsataśca "nāsatodṛṣṭatvā" diti nyāyena sadrūpatāpattyayogāt| "taddhedaṃ tarhyavyākatamāsī" dityatrāpyavyākṛtapadaṃ avyākṛtaśarīrakaparamapuruṣaparaṃ| "saeṣa iha praviṣṭa ānakhāgrebhyaḥ paśyaṃcakṣuśśrṛṇvan śrotraṃ manvānomana" ityanantaravākye "saeṣa" ityanena prakṛtamavākṛtaṃ samākṛṣya tasya antaḥpraviṣṭasarvāṃtaryāmipuruṣatvābhidhānāt | "tannāmarūpābhyāṃ vyākriyate"ti karmakartarilakāraḥ| tadavyākṛtaśarīramavibhaktanāmarūpaṃ brahma svayameva svātmānaṃ vibhaktanāmarūpamakarodityarthaḥ| "tadātmānaṃ svayamakurute" ti śrutyantaraikārthyāt| evaṃ kāraṇavākyānāṃ brahmaparatve sati īkṣaṇabrahmātmaśrutayo'pyanugṛhītā bhavatī| tasmādbrahmaikakāraṇakaṃ jagaditi siddham||
saṃgrahakarike||
kāraṇavākyavigānādāptatamoktaṃ pradhānameva bhavet|
kāraṇamaśeṣajagataḥ kāraṇavākyāni tatra neyāni||
maivaṃ sarvatrāpi brahmasamākarṣasatvena |
teṣāṃ nāsti vigānaṃ tasmādbrahmaiva kāraṇaṃ jagataḥ||
iti kāraṇatvādhikaraṇam||4||
jagadvācitvāt||16||
jīvamukhyaprāṇaliṅgāditi cettadvyākhyātam||17||
anyārthaṃ tu jaiminiḥ praśnavyākhyānābhyāmapicaivameke||
atreyaṃ viṣayaśuddhiḥ - ajātaśatrurnāma kāśīrājaḥ svasamīpamāgatya "brahme te bruvāṇī"tyuktavantaṃ bālākiṃ gārgyaṃ "sahasraṃ dadma etasyāṃ vāci janako janaka iti vai janā dhāvanti"ti praśasya bālākinā "ya evaiṣa āditye puruṣastamevāhamupāsa" ityādibhirvākyairbrahmatvenopadiṣṭānādityacandravidyutstanayitnvādigatān puruṣānabrahmatvena tattatpuruṣoktyanantarameva pratyākhyāya tatastūṣṇīṃbhūte tasmin kimetāvadeva parijñānamiti pṛṣṭvā tenaitāvadevetyabhimate "mṛṣā vai kila māsamavādiṣṭhāḥ brahma te bravāṇī"tyuktyā tamapodya "yo vai bālāka eteṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ kartā yasya vaitatkarma savai veditavya" iti svayamupadideśa| atra yo veditavya upadiṣṭaḥ puruṣaḥ sa kiṃ sāṃkhyatantrasiddhaḥ puruṣaḥ utapuruṣottama iti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ|tantrasiddhaḥ puruṣa evāyam| "yasya vai tatkarme"ti puṇyapāpasambandhaśravaṇāt| naca tasya "eteṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ karte"ti śrutādityādipuruṣakartṛtvāsambhavaḥ| ālokadānādinā tadbhogopayogināṃ teṣāṃ tadadṛṣṭajanyatvena adṛṣṭadvārā tasya tatkartṛkvāk| "tadyatā śreṣṭhī svairbhuṅkte yathā vā svāśśreṣṭhinaṃ bhuñjanti evamaiveṣa prājña ātmā etairātmabhirbhuṅkte evamevaita ātmānaḥ etamātmānaṃ bhuñjanti"ti vākyaśeṣe yathā
pradhānapuruṣaḥ svakīyairbhṛtyādibhirbhogasādhanānyupaharadbhirbhogānbhuṅkte yathā ca te bhogasādhanānyupaharantaḥ pradhānapuruṣaṃ pālayanti tathā veditavyatvenopadiṣṭa ātmā prakṛtairādityādipuruṣairbhuṅkte te ca taṃ pālayantītyuktatvācca| nanu tathā'pi puṇyapāpavān puruṣo veditavyaḥ parigrāhya ityatra kinniyāmakam| karmaśabdasya puṇyapāparūḍhitavat kriyata iti vyutpatyā kāryamātrayogasyāpi satvena paramapuruṣapakṣe'pi "yasya vaitatkarme"tyetadupapannam| rūḍhiprābalyāvalambane calane'pi rūḍhessatvenānyataraparigrahe vinigamanāvirahāt rūḍhyoḥ parasparaṃ kalahāyamānayorlabdhonmeṣo yoga eveha prabala iti vaktuṃ śakyatvāditi cenna| ādityādipuruṣakartṛtvamapūrvadvārakaṃ vivakṣitamityetadarthasamarpaṇārthatayā karmaśabdasyāpūrvarūḍherihopayuktatvena rūḍhyantarādyogācca tasyāḥ prabalatvāt calanarūḍheḥ paramapuruṣapakṣe vibhau tasminnananvayena sāṃkhyapuruṣapakṣe'pi vibhutvena tairabhyupagate tasminnananvayena jīvāṇutvapakṣe'pi canalokteḥ prayojanābhāvena rūḍhyantaronmeṣaśaṅkānavakāśācca| tasmādvaktavyatayopakrāntaṃ brahma tantrasiddhaḥ puruṣa eveti na tadvyatirikteśvarasiddhiḥ| evañca yadyapi pūrvādhikaraṇeṣu sakalavedāntaprasiddhabrahmādhiṣṭhānena pradhānasya jagatpariṇāmitvamuktaṃ, tathā'pi tathābhūtaṃ brahma sāṃkhyapuruṣa evetīha brahmanirūpaṇopakramasāṃkhyapuruṣanirūpaṇābhyāmavagamāt tasminneva sarveṣāṃ vedāntānāṃ paryavasānamabhyupeyamiti sarvapūrvādhikaraṇākṣepeṇāyaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ| tata evāsya pūrvādhikaraṇasaṅgatiśca| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
"yasya vaitatkarme"tyatra etacchabdastāvat pratyakṣādisannidhāpitasakalajagadvācī| prakaraṇopapadādisaṅkocakābhāvāt| atastatsamānādhikaraṇaḥ karmaśabdo'pi kāryatvayogena sakalajagadvācītyabhyupeyaṃ| sakalajagadvācyetacchabdasāmānādhikaraṇyena durbalasyāpi yogasyonmeṣāt| pūrvavākyoktamādityādipuruṣakartṛtvaṃ prati dvāratayā nirvāhakatvena apūrvaṃ buddhisthamiticet na| jagadekadeśabhūtādityādikīrtanena tadekadeśi jagat buddhisthamityapi vaktuṃ śakyatvāt| ubhayorbuddhisthatvasaṃbhave ca etatpadasāmānādhikaraṇyena jagadvācitvasyaiva yuktatvāt| apūrvavācitvamādityādipuruṣakartṛtvanirvāhakatayā prakṛtopayogīticet na| ekaḥ kathametāvatāmādityādipuruṣāṇaāṃ kartetyākāṃkṣāyāṃ nahyanteṣāmeva kartā kiṃtu sarvasyāsyajagataḥ| tathāca yasyaitatsarvameva jagatkāryaṃ tasyādityādipuruṣakartṛtve kā vicikitseti kaimutikanyāyena kāryasāmānyayogasyāpyādityādipuruṣakartṛtvanirvāhakatayā prakṛtopayogasāmyāt, tatsāmyeca
etatpadasāmānādhikaraṇyabalāt kāryatvayogonmeṣeṇa jagadvācitvasyaivābhyupeyatvāt, "eteṣāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ karte"ti prakṛtaṃ kartāraṃ "yasya" ti parāmṛśya karmaśabdena tatsaṃbandhinirdeśarūpeṇa kartṛkarmasambhivyāhāreṇāpi karmaśabde kartṛvyāpāraviṣayatvātmakakāryatvayogonmeṣāt sarvakāryasādhāraṇena kāryasāmānyogenaiva apūrve'pi vṛttisambhavena tatra tasya asādhāraṇyena rūḍhikalpanāyogācca| pūrvataṃtre hi barhirājyādhikaraṇe barhiśśabdasya lavanādisaṃskṛteṣu darbheṣvājyaśabdasyotpavanādisaṃskṛte ghṛte ca sārvabhaume śiṣṭaprayogo satyapi kvācitkaṃ tayordarbhaghṛtajātimātre prayogamavalambya tenaiva saṃskṛprayoganirvāhāt tayossaṃskārarūpaṃ pravṛttinimittāntaraṃ na kalpyamityuktam| "ekadeśe'pi yo ddaṣṭaśśabdo jātinibandhanaḥ| tadatyāgānnatasyāsti nimittāntarakalpane"ti| ataḥ "klṛpto yogaḥ kalpyāṃ rūḍhiṃ bādhata" iti nyāyāt nāpūrvarūḍikalpanāvakāśaḥ| tasmāt "yasya vaitatkarme" ti sakalajagatkartṛtvopadeśo'yam| evaṃ ca pratyakṣādisannidhāpitasakalajagadupasthāpakaitacchabdaḥ ādityapuruṣādyekadeśamātrakartṛtvamātravyavacchedako vāśabdaśca sārthako bhavati| brahmanirūpaṇopakramaḥ "cakṣosmīryo ajāyate" tyādiśrutipratipannaparabrahmadharmādityapuruṣādikartṛtvopanyāsaśca sarvalokaprasidvajīvātirikiktabālākyaviditapuruṣopadeśaśca anukagīlito bhavati| "sarvān pāpmano'pahatya sarveṣāñca bhūtānāṃ śraiṣṭhayaṃ svarājyaṃ paryeti yaevaṃ vede"tyaupasaṃhārikabrahmavedanaphalavarṇanamapyupapāditaṃ bhavati| nanu "etairātmabhirbhuṅkte" iti bhoktṛtvaṃ, "athāsmin prāṇaevaikadhā bhavatī"ti saptamyantanirdiṣṭaveditavyapuruṣāśritamukhyaprāṇasaṃbandhaśceti jīvaliṃgadvayaṃ veditavyapuruṣasya jīvatvagamakaṃ vākyaśeṣe śrūyata iti cet, tasya nirvāhaḥ pratardanavidyāyāmevābhihitaḥ| upakramopasaṃhārabalāt brahmaparatvena niścite vākye tanmadhyagatānyanyaliṃgāni tadanurodhena neyānīti| kathantannayanamiti cet| itthaṃ jīvātiriktabrahmasvarūpapratibodhanārthaṃ jīvasaṅkīrtanamiti tadidaṃ praśnavyāpākhyānābhyāmavagamyate| praśnastāvat rājñā gārgyaṃ prati kutaḥ, "kvaiṣa etatbalāke puruṣo'śayiṣṭa kkaiṣatadā'bhūt kutaetadāgā"diti| evaṃ rājñā pṛṣṭaṃ suṣuptajīvādhikaraṇamavijānāt gārgye "hitānāma hṛdayasya nāḍya" ityādinā "tāsu tadā bhavati yadā suptaḥ svapnaṃ nakathañcana paśyatī"tyantena jīvasya suṣuptyadhikaraṇaṃ hṛdayāvacchinnaṃ brahma prāptaṃ mārgatayā'dhikantavyāsu nāḍīṣu sarpaṇamuktvā "athāsmin prāṇaevaikadhābhavatī" ti rājñaiva kṛtaṃ svapṛṣṭārthavivaraṇam| tasya vyākhyānamidaṃ suṣuptau jīvasya brahmaṇi layapratipādakaṃ| "satā somya tadā sampanno
bhavatī" ti chandogaśrutyaikārthyāt, "ya traiṣaetatsuptobhūt, yaeṣa vijñānamayaḥ puruṣaḥ kvaiṣatadā'bhūt kutaetadāgā"diti "yatraiṣaetatsuptobhūt yaeṣavijñānamayaḥ puruṣaḥ tadaiṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ vijñānena vijñānamādāya yaeṣo'ntarhṛdayaākāśastasminśeta"iti vājasaneyiśākhāmnātabālākyajātaśatrusaṃvādagatapraśnottaraikārthyācca| tasmādveditavyatayopadiṣṭaḥ puruṣassuṣuptajīvādhikaraṇatvena tatobhinna uktaiti sabhagavān paramapuruṣaeveti siddham||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
brahmeti sarvāgamasīmalālitaḥ sāṃkyoditaḥ pūruṣaeva nādhikaḥ|
brahmaprasaṅgo yadajātaśatruṇā karmānvite tatra samāptimāpitaḥ||
maivamiha karmaśabdaḥ kāryaparo naiva puṇyapāpaparaḥ|
nikhilajagatsvarasaitatpadasamabhivyāhṛtigrāsāt|
bālākyaviditapuruṣopadeśavākyapraveśācca|
cakṣossūrya itīdṛkprāpakavacanānurodhācca||
iti jagadvācitvādhikaraṇam||5||
vākyānvayāt||19||
pratijñāsiddherliṃgamāśmarathyaḥ||20||
utkramiṣyata evaṃbhāvādityauḍulomiḥ||21||
avasthiteriti kāśākṛtsnaḥ||22||
bṛhadāraṇyake maitreyībrāhmaṇe yājñavalkyaḥ svayaṃpravivrajiṣayā maitreyīkātyāyanyoḥ svabhāryayorvittasaṃvibhāgaṃ cikīrṣan vittena kimamṛtatvaṃ prāptuṃ śakyamiti maitreyyā pṛṣṭo "yathaivopakaraṇavatāṃ jīvitaṃ tathaivatejīvitaṃsyāt amṛtatvasya tu nāśāsti vittena " tyuktvā "yenāhannāmṛtāsyāṃ kimahaṃ tena kuryāṃ yadeva bhagavānveda tadeva mebrūhī" ti tayā punaramṛtatvasādhanaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ "na vā are patyuḥ kāmāya patiḥ priyobhavati ātmanastu kāmāya patiḥ priyobhavatī" tyādibhiḥ "na vā are sarvasya kāmāya sarvaṃ piryaṃ bhavati ātmanastu kāmāya sarvaṃ priyaṃ bhavatī"tyantaiḥ vākyaiḥ patijāyāputravittādikaṃ sravaṃ yadbhogārthatayaiva priyaṃ bhavati taṃ patyādisarvabhoktāramātmānamupakramya "ātmāvā are dṛṣṭavyaśśrotvyomanvyonididhyāsitavya " ityamṛtatvasādhanamātmadarśanaṃ tatsādhanāni śravaṇādīni copadiśya "ātmanikhalvare dṛṣṭe śrute mate vijñāte idaṃsarvaṃvidita " mityātmavijñānena sarvavijñaānamuktvā "sayathā''rdraidhāgnarabhyāhitasya pṛthakdhūmāviniścarantyevaṃvā are'sya mahatobhūtasya niśvāsitametadyadṛgveto yadurvedassāmavedo'tharvāṅrasaitihāsaḥ purāṇaṃ vidyāupaniṣadaślokāssūtrāṇyanuvyākhyānāni vyākhyānānīṣṭaṃ hutamāśitaṃ pāyitamayañca lokaḥ paraśca lokassarvāṇi ca bhūtanyasyaivaitānisarvāṇiniśvasitānī"ti tasya nāmarūpātmakasakalaprapañcakāraṇatvaṃ pradarśya "prajñānaghanaevaitebhyo bhūtebhyassamutthāya tānyevānuviśyati napretyasaṃjñā'stī"ti tasyotpattivināśaviśeṣavijñaānābhāvānuktvā "etāvadarekhalvamṛtva"miti tasyātmanovedanamamṛtatvasādhanamupasañḍahāreti śrūyate| atra patijāyādivākyajātamutpattivināśavākyañca paramātmaviṣayamiti pratīyate| sarvāṇi caitāni vākyāni ekaviṣayatvena pravṛttāni prakṛtaparamarśibhirātmaśabdasarvanāmaśabādairuttarottaravākyapravṛtteḥ| caturthādhyāye maitreyībrāhmaṇe mahadbhūtamanantamapāra"miti paramātmāsādhāraṇaviśeṣaṇāni "prajñānaghanaeve"tyādivākyoktotpattivināśādijīvaliṃgasāmānādhikaraṇyena nirdiṣṭāni| evaṃ jīveśvaraliṃgayuktavākyānāmekaviṣayatvapratītāvapi nābedastayośśrutitātparyaviṣa ityabhyupagantuṃ yuktam|
"nāhaṃ sarvajña " ityādyanubhavena jīve sarvajñatvādiviśiṣṭeśvarabhāvabādhāt| ubhayorbhedena atra pratipādanakalpanaṃtvekaviṣayatvapratītiviruddham| tasmāt jīveśvarayoranyataraevātra pratipādyaḥ| apratipādyaliṃgāni ca pratipādye yojyānīti sthite jīvadharmāṇāmīśvare yojanena siddhānto bhaviṣyati| īśvaraliṃgānāṃ jīve yojanena sāṃkhyatantrasiddhaḥ puruṣa evātra pratipādya iti tāvatpūrvapakṣaḥ| tathāhi - patijāyādisambandhastāvadavicalaṃ jīvaliṃgam| "navā are patyuḥ kāmāye"tyatra vaiśabdena prasiddhidyotakena| prasiddhaṃ hyetat tavāpi, maitreyi yat patyādayo jāyādīnāṃ patyādiprayojanāya priyā na bhavaṃti kiṃ tu svaprayojanāyaivetyevamarthapratīteḥ| ataḥ patyādikaṃ parityajyāmṛtatvasādhakaṃ svātmadarśanameva śravaṇādibhissampādanīyamiti
"ātmā vā'are draṣṭavya" ityādi nocyata iti saṅgacchate| nanu svātmabhogāya patyādīnāṃ priyatvamuktvā tataḥ kevalamātmasvarūpamanveṣṭavyamiti vacanaṃ na saṃghaṭate| priyamevahyanveṣṭavyaṃ natu priyaviyuktasvarūpamiti cet, ucyate - sarveṣāmapi svātmā svataḥ priyaḥ, patijāyādayastu na patitvādinā priyāḥ, teṣāṃ sveṣṭasaṃpādakatvadaśāyāmiva svāniṣṭakaraṇatvadaśāyāṃ prītyadarśanāt| ataḥ svātmanaḥ patyādīnāṃ cānubhavānusāreṇa vyavasthitāvyavasthitapuruṣārthatvarūpaṃ tatvamālocyāvyavasthitagauṇapuruṣārtharūpaṃ patyādikaṃ parityajya mukhyamevāśrayediti prakṛtiviyuktātmadarśanatadarthaśravaṇamananadhyānavidhānaṃ saṅgacchata eva| "daśamanvantarāṇīha tiṣṭhantīṃdriyacintakā" ityādinā tantrasiddhapuruṣadhyānasya sāṃkhyairmuktaphalakatvapratipādanā| sarvavijñānavacanamapi tatra saṅgacchate| muktyarthānusandheyaprakṛtiviyuktajīvasvarūpāṇāṃ viśeṣakāntarābhāvenaikāratayā svātmani tathā vijñātesati "evamākārassarve jīvā" iti jñātuṃ śakyatvāt| sarvopādānatva vacanaṃ tu jīvādhiṣṭhitapradhānaviṣayatayā saṅgacchate| tatrahyādreṃdhanaviśiṣṭasyāgneḥ dhūmopādānatvaṃ dṛṣṭāntīkṛtaṃ| na cāgnirdhūmopādanāṃ, kiṃtvādreṃdhanameva| tadutkarṣeṇa dhūmotkarṣadarśanāt| eṃ dārṣāntikabhāve'pyādreṃdhanakalpajaḍaprakṛtiviśiṣṭasyāgnikalpasya cetanasyopādānatvavarṇanaṃ dṛṣṭāntānusārāt "dadhnā juhotī"ti vidhānamiva viśeṣaṇae saṃkrāmati| evamiha brahmadharmāṇāṃ jīve yojayituṃ śakyatvāt, jīvadharmāṇāṃ patijāyādisambandhotpattivināśāviśeṣavijñānābhāvānāṃ brahmaṇi yojayituṃ śakyatvāt| bṛhadāraṇyakacaturthaṣaṣṭhādhyāyāmnātayormaitreyībrāhmaṇayostantarasiddhe puruṣe paryavasānena tatsāmānyāt sarvāṇyapi
kāraṇavākyāni puruṣādhiṣṭhitapradhānakāraṇatvaparāṇi| ataḥ pradhānameva jagatkāraṇaṃ natvīśvaro nāma kaścidastīti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
amṛtatvasādhanavedanenopakramastenopasaṃhāmadhyecāmṛtatvasādhanātmadarśanaśravaṇamananadhyānakīrtanañca prakaraṇasya paramātmaviṣayatva evopapadyate, natantrasiddhapuruṣaviṣayatve| "nānyaḥ panthā" iti śruteḥ śrutiviruddhasya "puruṣaṃ nirguṇaṃ prāpye"tyāditāntrikavacanasya heyatvāt| kathaṃ brahmaṇi patijāyādivākyaghaṭanā| itthaṃ - patijāyāputravittādayo matprayojanāyāhamasyapriyassyāmiti svasaṅkalpāt priyā na bhavanti, kintu paramātmanassvārādhakapriyapratilaṃbhanaviṣayakāmanānirvṛttaya iti| paramātmā hi karmabhirārādhitastattatkarmānuguṇaṃ pratiniyatadeśakālasvarūpaparimāṇamārādhakānanāntattadvastugataṃ priyatvamāpādayati| "eṣa hyevānandayātī"ti śruteḥ| na tu kimapi vastu svataḥ priyamapriyaṃ vā bhavati| yathoktaṃ bhagavatā paraśareṇa - "tadeva prītaye bhūtvā punarduḥkhāya jāyate| tadeva kopāya punaḥ prasādāya ca jāyate| tasmādduḥkhātmakannāsti na ca kiñcit sukhātmaka"miti| nanvitthaṃ patijāyādivākyaṃ paramātmaviṣayatāṃ prāpnotu nāma| "tānyevānuvinaśyatī"tyādi jīvaparāmarśaḥ kathantadviṣayatāṃ prāpnuyāditi cet, itthaṃ jīvastāvat īśvarādutpadyate| "yathā sudīptātpāvakādvipphuliṅgā"ityādiśravaṇāt| atastayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhūtayorasti ghaṭamṛtpiṇḍayoriva mukhyamevaikyaṃ, tadiha jñāpanīyam "ātmanikhalvara"ityādipūrvavākyoktasarvavijñānapratijñāsidhyarthamiti tatjñāpanārthaṃ jīvaśabdairīśvarābhidhānamityāśmarathya ācāryo manyate| saṃsāradaśāyāṃ jīveśvarayorbhede'pi mokṣadaśāyāṃ "yathā nadyassyaṃdamānāssamudra"iti mantroktanadīsamudranyāyena abhedaprāpteḥ jīvaśabdairīśvarābhidhānamityoḍulomirācāryo manyate| īśvarasya jīve'ntaryāmitayā'vasthitestanniyāmyatvena taccharīrasya jīvasya śabdāśśarīriparyantāiti jīvaśabdairīśvarābhidhānamiti kāśakṛtsnaācāryo manyate| itameva mataṃ sūtrakārasyābhimatam| jīvotpatteḥ "nātmā'śrute"rityatra, muktāvāgantukarūpābhiniṣpatteḥ "saṃpadyāvirbhāva"ityatra nirākariṣyamāṇatvāt, sarvaśabdānāṃ śarīriparamātmaparyantatvasya "carācaravyapāśrayastu syā" diti sūtreṇa prasādhayiṣyamāṇatvācca| tasmādādimadhyāvasānaparāmarśāt maitreyībrāhmaṇaṃ paramātmaparam| natu tantrasiddhapuruṣaparamiti sarveṣāṃ kāraṇavākyānāṃ siddhaṃ paramātmaparatvam||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
maitreyībrāhmaṇoktaḥ patisutavanitādyanvayāt pañcaviṃśastasya jñānādvimuktiḥ prakṛtivirahitasyeti taṃtre prasiddhiḥ|
tannyāyātsarvavedāvadhiparipaṭhitāścetanaikāntaśabdāḥ
tatraivākhyāṃ labhaṃtāṃ bhavatu ca tadadhiṣṭhātṛkaṃ tatpradhānam||
patisutavanitādikīrtane'pi spṛśati na saṃsṛtibhājamātmaśabdaḥ|
iha sudṛḍhamupakramādidṛṣṭyā paraviṣaye sati tasya kaḥ prasaṅgaḥ||
tasmādihātmā bhaṇitaḥ pumānparastadiṣṭamārādhakasaukhyalambhanam|
tatsiddhaye syustanayādayaḥ priyāḥsvarūpato neti tadarthaiṣyatāṃ||
jīvaparāmarśo'yaṃ śarīriparamātmaparyantaḥ|
yatrotpattivināśajñānābhāvāhi na gaditāstasya||
iti vākyānvayādhikaraṇam||6||
prakṛtiśca pratijñādṛṣṭhāntānuparodhāt||23||
abhidhyopadeśācca||24||
sākṣāccobhayāmnāt||25||
ātmakṛteḥ||26||
pariṇāmat||27||
yoniśca hi gīyate||28||
evaṃ nirīśvarasāṃkhye niraste satīśvaraḥ kevalaṃ nimittabhūtaḥ pradhānameva tu pariṇāmijagadupādānamiti vadatasseśvarasāṃkhyasya mataṃ nirākriyata iti saṅgatiḥ| kiṃ brahma jagatonimittamātraṃ utopādānamapīti tasyopādānatve śrutinyāyavirodhatadānukūlyābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - īśvaronimitimātraṃ, pradhānameva upādānaṃ| "māyāntu prakṛtiṃ vidyānmāyinantu maheśvaraṃ, mayādhyakṣeṇa prakṛtissūyate sacarācara"mityādiśrutismṛtidarśanāt, loke kulālamṛtpiṇḍādinimittopādānabhedadarśanācca paramātmanaupādānatvāsambhavācca| kāryakāreṇa pariṇāmitvaṃ hyupādānatvaṃ, natu pariṇāmyādhāratvamapi| mṛdi ghaṭākāreṇa pariṇamamānāyāṃ tadādhārasya bhūtalaksyāpyupādānatvaprasaṅgāt| na ca "bahusyāṃ prajāyeye"ti brahmaṇassṛkṣyamāṇatejaḥprabhṛtirūpeṇa svasya bahubhavanasaṅkalpaśravaṇādupādānatvamapyeṣṭavyamiti vācyam| tadanupādānatvepi tato bhede'pi svakīyatvaprayuktābhedopacāreṇatadupapatteḥ| "prajāyemahirūdraprajābhiḥ, agne yanme tanuvā ūnantanmaāpṛṇetyāha yanme prajāyai paśūnāmūnantanmaāpūrayetivāvaitadāhe"tyādimantrārthavādā hi prajāpaśurūpeṇa svasyaiva jananaṃ svasyaiva prajāpaśvātmakatvañcāvedayanti| naca pratijñādṛṣṭāntānusāreṇopādānatvamapyeṣṭavyamiti vācyam| brahmajñāne satyapi viyadādiṣu mṛtipiṇḍādijñāne satyapi ghaṭaśarāvādiṣu ca saṃśayadarśanena pratijñādṛṣṭāntayoraupacārikatayā netavyatvāt| "tadātmānaṃ svayamakurute"tyādeḥ "yadāyadā hi dharmasya glānirbhavati bhārata| abhyutthānamadharsya tadā''tmānaṃ sṛjāmyaha" mityādivannayanasambhavāditi| evaṃprāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
brahma jagata upādānamapi, na nimittamātraṃ| upādānatve satyevahi sarvavijñānapratijñāmṛtpiṇḍaghaṭaśarāvādidṛṣṭāntau noparudhyeyātām| na ca tayoraupacārikatvaṃ kalpyaṃ, mukhyatvasamarthanopapatteḥ| tathāhi - "sadeva somye damagraāsīt ekamevādvitīya" mityatra satpadaṃ sūkṣmacidacidviśiṣṭa vastuparam|anyathā brahmātiktasya jīvavargasya avyaktasya ca satvena avadhāraṇavirodhāt, avadhāraṇasya tatpṛthagbhūtavastvantaravyavacchedakatvenopāpādanīyatvāt| tajjñāne ca tatkārye sarvamapi viṣayobhavati| kāryadravyasya kāraṇānanyatvāt| kāryagatasthūlāvasthā paraṃ viṣayo nabhavati| atastenākāreṇa saṃśaya upapadyate| anyadihāpekṣitaṃ pratijñāddaṣṭāntavākyayorviśvāraṇīyamārambhaṇādhikaraṇe vicārayiṣyate| na ca kulālamṛtpiṇḍanyāyena nimittopādānabhedanyamaśśaṅkanīyaḥ| tatra hi nimittopādānābhedagrāhakapramāṇābāvāt tadbhedaḥ, iha tatpramāṇe jāgrati kathamabhedo na syāt| na ca bhūtalavatpariṇāmyupādānādhārasyopādānatvoktirnirālaṃbaneti vācyaṃ| apṛthaksidvakārvāśrayatvarūpatvādupādānatvasya tasya brahmaṇyapimukhyatvāt| naca kāryāśrayatve sākṣāttvanniveśanīyaṃ, yena sākṣāttadāśrayabhūtā prakṛtirevopādānaṃ syāt| sākṣāttvaviśeṣaṇe gauravāt| kiṃca pratijñāddaṣṭāntābhyāṃ tāvadbrahmaṇa upādānatvamavasīyate| ""bahusyāṃ prajāyeye"" tyabhidhyopadeśādapitadavasīyate| na catasya mukhyatvasambhave pūrvapakṣodāhṛtamantrārthavādavatkliṣṭagatiḥ kalpanīyā| tathā "brahmavanaṃ brahmasavṛkṣa āsīt yatodyāvāpṛthivī niṣṭatakṣuḥ| manīṣiṇo manasā vibravīmivaḥ brahmādhyatiṣṭhadbhuvanāni dhāraya"nniti sākṣācca tasya nimittopādānobhayarūpatvamāmnāyate| atra hi "vṛkṣa" iti takṣaṇanirvartyadyāvāpṛthivyupādānatvamuktam| "brahmādhyatiṣṭha" diti nimittatvaṃ| tathā "tadātmānaṃ svayamakurute" ti sṛṣṭau
tasyaiva kartṛkarmabhāvaśravaṇādapi tasya nimittopādānobhayarūpatvamavagamyate| "tadātmānaṃ sṛjāmyaha"mitivadupapadya ityuktamiticet na| tatrāpi nimittopādānabhāvasidhyaviśeṣāt| iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ| tatra avatāraśarīrarūpeṇa svātmānaḥ svema sṛṣṭirucyate, iha prapañcarūpeṇeti| eṃ bahuvidhaśrutisiddhamapi tasyopādānatvaṃ "niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śānta" mityādinirvikāratvaviṣayabahuśrutyantarāvirodhena kalpanīyam| tathā tatkalpanañca niruktopādānalakṣaṇe sākṣādāśrayatvāvivakṣyaiva nirvahatītyato'pi sākṣātvannavivakṣaṇīyam| na ca vyavahitakāraṇavadvyavahitāśrayo nāsti, sa kevalamāśrayasyaivāśraya iti śaṅkanīyam| paryaṅkāstīrṇavasre śayānaṃ prati tadvahanākṣamasya vasramātrasya āśrayatvāyogyatayā vyavahitasya paryaṅgasya vasradvārā tadāśrayatvāt prāsādasthaparyaṅkaśayite puruṣe "prasāde śeta" iti vyavahāradarśanācca| etena svābhinnakāryāvasthāśrayatvamupādānalakṣaṇam, tathā ca brahmaprapañcayorapṛthaksiddhisatve'pyabhedābhāvāt na brahmaṇaḥ prapañcopādānatvamityapi śaṅkā nirastā|
śrutopādānatvanirvāhārthamapṛthaksiddhigarbhasyaiva lakṣaṇasya vācyatvāt śrutopapattyanusāreṇa aśrutakalpanaucityāt| tasmāduktalakṣaṇapariṇāmitvābhyupagame śrutopādānatnirvāhāt śrutinirvikāratvāvyākopācca niruktapariṇāmitvarūpaṃ niravadyamevopādānatvam, yadakṣaravākyagatabhūtayonyādiśabdairapi joghuṣyate| yoniśabdohyupādānaparyāyaḥ| akṣaraprakaraṇe "kartāramīśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahyayoni" miti kartṛtvasyāpi śravaṇāt tato'pi nimittopādānobhayarūptavasiddhiḥ| tasmāt brahma jagata upādānaṃ nimittañceti siddham||
saṃgrahakārike||
astīśvarastathā'pi tu nopādanāmayamapiṇāmitvāt|
tadadhiṣṭhitaṃ pradhānaṃ śrutismṛtibhyāmupādānam||
nirdoṣo nirvikārassvata iti cidaciddeha eṣa svadeha
dvārādoṣairvikārairapi ca na sahate saṅgatiṃ viśvanetā|
tenopādānalakṣmacyutamiha na bhavedbhāvyavasthāśrayatvaṃ
paryaṅkāstīrṇavasrasthitijuṣa iva yaddehagatyāśrayo'sau||
iti prakṛtyadhikaraṇam||7||
etena sarve vyākhyātā vyākhyātāḥ||29||
atītapañcatiṃraśadadhikaraṇanyāyādideśarūpatvāt saṅgatiḥ| atītādhikaraṇeṣvanudāhṛtāḥ kāraṇavākyaveśeṣāḥ kimuktalakṣaṇabrahmaparā uta neti teṣāmuktanyāyaviṣayatvasambhavāsamabhavābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| anudāhṛtāḥ kāraṇavākyaviśeṣāssarve paramapuruṣaikāntikā na bhavanti| "hiraṇyagarbhassamavartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patireka āsīt, yadā tamastannadivā na rātrirnasannacāsacchiva eva kevala" ityādikāraṇavākyāntareṣu brahmaśivādiśabdoktakāraṇāntarapratīteriti| siddhāntastu - 
atītādhikaraṇoktanyāyaireva te'pi paramapuruṣaviṣayāssiddhyantīti| tatsiddhiprakārastu śrutaprakāśikādiṣu prapañcito draṣṭavyaḥ| ativistarāpatterneha salikhyate| kāraṇabhūte vastuni śivādiśabdā api śrutā iticet pūrvanyāyaiste'pi brahmaṇi nārāyaṇe yojyāḥ||
iti sarvavyākhyānādhikaraṇam||8||
iti śrīmadbharadvājakulajaladhikaustubhaśrīmadadvaitāvidyācāryaśrīviśvajidyājiśrīraṅgarājādhvarivarasūnorappayadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturmatasāre
nayamayūkhamālikākhye dvitīyaparicchede prathamasyādhyāyasya
caturthapādaḥ
prathamo'dyāyassamāptaḥ||
||śrīḥ||
dvitīyo'ddhyāyaḥ
||smṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṃgaiticennānyasmṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṃgāt||
||itareṣāñcānupalabdheḥ||
prathame'dhyāye śrīmannārāyaṇākhyaṃ paraṃbrahma sakalajagatkāraṇamiti tatra sarveṣāṃ vedāntānāṃ nyāyatassamanvayavyutpādanena prasādhitaṃ| tatra saṃbhāvitasarvavidhavirodhasamādhānena tadeva sthirīkartuṃ dvitīyo'dhyāya ārabhyate| tatra prathamādhikaraṇe sāṃkhyasmṛtivirodhena vedāṃtānāmanyaparatvāśaṅkā nirasyate| nanu kathaṃ smṛtivirodhe śruteranyaparatvaśaṅkā, śrutismṛtivirodhe smṛterevāprāmāṇyasya "virodhetvanapekṣyaṃ syādasati hyanumāna" miti pūrvatantrādhikaraṇe sthitatvāt| tatra hi "auduṃbarīṃ spṛṣṭvodrāye"diti pratyakṣaśrutivirodhena "auduṃbarī sarvā veṣṭayitavye"ti smṛteranādaraṇīyatvaṃ, asatyeva pratyakṣaśrutivirodhe smṛtermūla śrutyanumāpakatvena prāmāṇyañcoktam| satyaṃ| pratyakṣaśruteḥ sphuṭārthatve tathaiva| jagatkāraṇaviṣayāvedāntāstu sparśanaśrutivannasphuṭārthāḥ, kiṃtvatigahanā nānāprakārāḥ "vedārthoyassvayaṃ jñātastatrājñānaṃ bhavedapi| ṛṣibhirniścite tasmin kā śaṅkā syānmahātmabhi" rityuktarītyopabṛhmaṇaniścayārthāiti kapiladimaharṣipraṇītasāṃkhyasmṛtirūpopabṛhmaṇānusāreṇa teṣāmanyaparatvaśaṅkā bhavatyeveti tannirāsārthamidamadhikaraṇam| atra prathamādhyāyaprasādhitaṃ brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvamayuktaṃ yuktaṃveti sāṃkhyamanvādismṛtivipratipatteḥ kiṃ sāṃkhyasmṛtyopabṛṃhaṇīyāssanto vedāntāstadanusāreṇa pradhānakāraṇaparāḥ, uta manvādismṛtyupabṛṃhaṇīyāssantaste brahmakāraṇaparāityanirdhāraṇāt saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ sāṃkhyasmṛtyopabṛṃhaṇīyāḥ| tasyā niravakāśatvāt| manvādismṛtīnāṃ varṇāśramādidharmeṣu agnihotrādikarmasu ca sāvakāśatvāt| na ca manuvyāsādi bahusmṛtivirodhāt sāṃkhyasmṛterbhrāntimūlatvaṃ kalpyatāmiti vācyam| "ṛṣiṃ prasūtaṃ kapilaṃ yastamagre jñānairbibharti jayamānañca paśye"diti śvetāśvataropaniṣadi sāṃkhyakartuḥ kapilasya jñānaparipūrṇatvaśravaṇena tatkalpanā'yogāt| "yadvai kiñca manuravadattadbheṣaja" miti manūktīnāmapi kārtsnyena prāmāṇyaṃ śrutamiticenna| bheṣajaśabdasya prāmāṇyārthatvābhāvena tadasiddheḥ| yajurvedehi "yo barahmavarcasakāmassyāt tasmā etaṃ somāraudraṃcaruṃ nirvape"diti brahmavarcasakāmasyeṣṭiṃ vidhāya "yāvadeva brahmavarcasaṃ tatsarvaṃ karotī"ti phalārthavādānantaraṃ "atbrahmavarcasaṃkriyata ityāhurīśvaro duścarmābhavito"rityevaṃrūpeṣṭhyanuṣṭhānena "atibrahmavarcasaṃ kriyate tena yajamānaśvitrībhavituṃ prabhavatī"tyevaṃ phalameva punardoṣaparyavasāyinaṃ saṅkīrtya tatparihārārthaṃ "mānavī ṛcau dhāyye kuryā"diti manunā dṛṣṭe "agniṃ vaḥ pūrvyaṃgirā devamīḍe vasūnā"mityādike ṛcau sāmidhenīmadhye dhāyyārūpe vedhāya kathaṃ mānavībhyāmṛgbhyāṃ śvitraparihāraityākāṃkṣāyāṃ "yadvai kiñca manuravadattadbheṣajaṃ bheṣajamevāsmai karotī"tyarthavādena tatsaṅghaṭakaṃ, na tu manudṛṣaṛgarthānāṃ "agnirvasūnāmmadhye pūrvya" ityādīnāṃ manusmṛtivacanārthānāñca prāmāṇikatvapratipādanam| manūktyarthānāṃ śvitre vyādhyantare vā bheṣajarūpatvaṃ bādhitamiti cet, tarhi asadarthāvalambanārthavādatvamastu| nahyarthavādasya sadarthāvalambanatvasidhyarthaṃ prakṛtārthāsaṅdhaṭakārthāntaraparigraho yuktaḥ| tasmāt yadvai kiñce tyādikamanuvākyānāṃ sarveṣāṃ svasvaviṣayatvena pratīte sarvatrārthe prāmāmyapratipādanaparamityetadasiddheḥ manusmṛtivacanānāṃ brahmakāraṇavādāṃśamapahāya varṇāśramadharmeṣveva avakāśakalpanaṃ yuktamiti vedāntānāṃ niravakāśayā sāṃkhyasmṛtyaivopabṛṃhaṇīyatvāt te kapilasūtrahariharavārtikādipratipāditena prakāreṇa pradhānakāraṇatvaparā netavyāḥ| teṣu ye puruṣasyaiva kāraṇatvaṃ sphuṭaṃ vadanti tetvamātyajayaṃ rājñīva pradhānakṛtyaṃ puruṣe vyapadiśaṃtīti netavyāiti manvādismṛtibhirevopabṛṃhaṇīyāḥ| tāsāmāptatamapraṇītānāṃ bahūnāmanavakāśatvāt| na ca karmabhistāsāṃ sāvakāśatvaṃ, paramapuruṣārādhanabhūtakarmapratipādakānāmapi tadārādhanīyatayā taccheṣiṇi paramapuruṣe jagatkāraṇe brahmaṇyeva tātparyāt| ata eva manunā varṇāśramādidharmān ṛṣibhiḥ pṛṣṭena tadārādhanīyaparamapuruṣopadeśapūrvakameva teṣāmupadeśaḥ kṛtaḥ| "āsīdidaṃ tamobhūtamaprajñātamalakṣaṇam| apratarkyamanirdeśyaṃ prasuptamiva sarvataḥ|| tatassvayambhūrbhagavānavyakto vyañjayannidam| mahābhūtādivṛttaujāḥ prādurāsīttamonudaḥ|| so'bhidhyāya śarīrātsvāt sisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ| apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamapāsṛjat|| tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham| tasmin jajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ|| āpo nārā iti proktā āpo vai narasūnavaḥ| tā yadasyāyanaṃ proktaṃ tena nārāyaṇassmṛtaḥ"
ityādinā purāṇeṣu paraḥ pumān| viṣṇurārādhyate panthā nānyastattoṣakāraka" ityādivacanaistadārādhanatayā taccheṣatvenaiva karmapratipādanamiti spaṣṭameva| tathā yājñavalkyenāpi "kṣetrajñasyeśvarajñānādviśuddhiḥ paramā matā" itīśvaraṃ prastutya "mukhapādorupajjāssyustasya varṇā yathākrama"miti puruṣasūktaprakriyayā tasya jagatkāraṇatvaṃ pratipādayatā manuvadeva
svavarṇitavarṇāśramadharmāṇāmārādhanīye puruṣasūktapratipādye śriyaḥpatau nārāyaṇa eva tātparyamāviṣkṛtam| tathā āpastambenāpi "pūḥ prāṇinassarva eva guhāśayasyāhanyamānasyavikalmaṣasye"ti evaṃ "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā'pahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇa" iti subālopaniṣatpratipādyarūpaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prastutya "tasmātkāyāḥ prabhavati sarve samūlaṃ śāśvatikassanitya"iti tasya sakalamūlakāraṇatvaṃ vadatā saeva svavarṇitavarṇāśramādidharmāṇāmārādhanīya iti tatra tātparyamāviṣkṛtam| evamanyeṣāmapi smatarṝṇāṃ vacanānyudāharaṇīyāni| naca anavakāśatvataulye kathaṃ manvādismṛtibhirevopabrahmaṇīyā iti nirṇaya iti vācyam| manvādīnāmāptatamatvāt| manostāvadāptatamatvaṃ taduktīnāṃ bheṣajatvaśrutyā'vasitam| tathāhi - bheṣajatvamiha rogaduritobhayaśāntyathasā dhāraṇadoṣaparihārārthatvamātraṃ vivakṣitaṃ, natu viśipya rogaśāntyārthatvameva| "yadvaikiṃce"ti vākyasyātyaṃtāsārthatvāpatteḥ| nahi manusmṛtivacanānāṃ madhye kimapi vacanaṃ vaidyakavadrogaśāṃtyarthabheṣajapratipādakamasti| dupitaśāṃtyarthapratipādakaṃtvastyeva varṇāśramadharmaviṣayacanajātam| nityanaimittikānāṃ varṇāśramadharmāṇāṃ duritakṣayahetutvāt| nanu tathā'pi sarvāṇi manusmṛtivacanāni duritakṣayopayogīni na bhavaṃti, manusmṛtau rājanītivyavahārapadanirṇayaviṣayatvena ddaṣṭārthānāmapi sattvāditi vīpsārthakayatkiñcicchabdāsāṃgatyamastītivācyaṃ|
teṣāmapi duritakṣayārthakarmānuṣṭhānopakārakatvena paramparayā tādarthyasattvāt, tadabhāve'pi kārtsnyaparayatkiñcicchabdasya varṇāśramadharmaviṣayavacanajātaparatvena saṅkocopapatteśca asaṃkucitasarvaparatvāsaṃbhave saṃkucitasarvaparatvaucityāt| ataeva "sarvaeva hāriyojanasya lipsanta" iti jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇa śrutivākye sarvaśabdasya asaṃkucitasarvaparatvāsambhavāt kenacidavacchedena sāmastyaparatve vācye prākaraṇikajyautiṣṭomikāÐtvajyāvacchinnasāmastyaparatvaṃ yuktamiti ṛtvijāmeva hāriyojanabhakṣaṇavidhāyakamidaṃ vākyamiti nirṇītaṃ pūrvatantre| evañca bheṣajatvoktyā labdhaprāmāṇyānāṃ manūktīnāṃ paramatātparyaviṣayamārādhanīyaṃ vihāya tadārādhanamātre tātparyaparyavasānāyogasya uktatvāt| tatraiva dharmaśāstrarūpāṇāṃ sarvāsāṃ manūktīnāṃ paramatātparyaviṣayamārādhanīyaṃ vihāya tadārādhanamātre tātparyaparyavasānāyogasya uktatvāt| tatraiva dharmaśāstrarūpāṇāṃ sarvāsāṃ mūktīnāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ labhyata iti tatra tasyāptatamatvam| tathā "sahovāca vyāsaḥ pārāśarya" iti śrutyā svapratipādye'rthe vyāsoktatvena gauravapradarśanāt tathā vyāsopadiṣṭānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ labhyata iti tasyāptatamatvam| evaṃ yājñavalkyādīnāmapi "janakoha vai deho bahudakṣiṇena yajñeneja" iti bṛhadāraṇyakādhyāyādivacanabhaṅgībhirāptatamatvaṃ, kapilasyatvāptatvamātram| "ṛṣiṃ prasūta" mityādestadṛṣitvopayogikatipayavedavedārthagocarajñānavattvamātraviṣayatvenāpi cāritārthyāt| tathā sāṃkhyaśāstrapravaktāraḥ kapilāsuripañcaśikhādayastricaturāeva smṛtītihāsapurāṇavaktāro manvādayastvatibahavaiti suprasiddham| smṛtītihāsapurāṇānica kapilādipraṇītakatipayasūtrarūpāt sāṃkhyaśāstrāt atibahulānītivyaktameva| tasmādāptatamamanvādibahupraṇītabahuśāstravirodhāt sāṃkhyaśāstrasya niravakāśatvamakiṃcitkaramiti nivakāśābhirmanvādismṛtibhirevopabṛhmaṇīyā vedāṃtāḥ| tataśca teṣāṃ samanvayāt brahmaṇojagatkāraṇatvamavicalamiti||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
vedāntaughasya sāṃkhyasmṛtiratigahanasyārthatatvāvadhṛtyai
vaktuṃ yuktopabṛhmaṇyanitaraviṣayā sāhyalabdhāvakāśā|
natvanyā kāpi dharmāvagatiparatayā viśrutā sāvakāśā
tasmādvedāntavedyaṃ na bhavati jagataḥ karaṇaṃ brahmatatte||
maivaṃ manuvyāsamukhasmṛtīnāmapi pratīto'navakāśabhāvaḥ|
ārādhanīye khalu karmabhissvairnārāyaṇe bhāvamuśanti tāsām||
tasmādbhajantyāptatamaiḥ praṇītāstāeva teṣāmupabṛhmaṇatvam|
yā tadviruddhārtharaiva dṛṣṭā sāṃkhyasmṛtirbhrāntinibandhanā sā||
iti smṛtyadhikaraṇam||
||etena yogaḥ pratyuktaḥ||3||
atrāpi viṣayasaṃśayapūrvapakṣasiddhāntahetavaḥ pūrvavadbraṣṭavyāḥ| hiraṇyagarbhasya bhramāsambhavāt tatkṛtasya
śāstrasyeśvarābhyupagamena śvetāśvataropaniṣadādyuktayogānuṣṭhānavarṇanena ca kapilādismṛtivailakṣaṇyena ca vedāntāntaraṅkatvāt tenopabṛṃhaṇīyā vedāntā iti taduktarītyā īśvarādhiṣṭhitasya pradhānasya kāraṇatvonābhyupagantavyatvāt brahma kāraṇamityetadayuktaṃ ityasyātideśādhikaraṇasyādhikā śaṅkā| hiraṇyagarbhasyāpi kṣetrajñatvena tatkṛtasya śāstrasya bhramamūlatvasaṃbhavāt, abrahmātmakapradhānakāraṇatvābhyupagamāt, īśvarasya nimittamātratvāṅgīkārāt, vedāṃtābhimatadhyeyavaiṣamyāt, tannirūpyavilakṣaṇadhyānavidhānācca viruddhena tena vedāntā nopabṛṃhaṇīyā iti tatparihāraḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
yogastarhyupabṛṃhaṇatvamayatāṃ sahyabjabhūnirmitastatrāṅgīkṛta īśvaraśśrutimito yogaśca tadrocaraḥ|
maivaṃ śrutyuditeśvarāttaduditādyogācca tadgocarādvailakṣaṇyajuṣau hi tau bhavavataḥ kasyeha nāsti bhramaḥ||
iti yogapratyuktyādhikaṇam||
na vilakṣaṇatvādasya tathātvañca śabdāt||
abhimānivyapadeśastu viśeṣānugatibhyām||
dṛśyate tu||
asaditi cenna pratiṣenugatibhyām||
apītau tadvatprasaṅgādasamañjasam||
na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvāt||
svapakṣadoṣācca||
tarkāpratiṣṭhānādapi||
anyathā'numeyamiti cedevamapyanirmokṣaprasaṅgaḥ||
manvādismṛtibhirovopabṛṃhaṇaṃ kāryamityuktamayuktam| tāsāṃ tarkaviruddhatvāt| atastarkānugṛhītayā sāṃkhyasmṛtyaivopabṛṃhaṇaṃ kāryamiti pratyavasthānātsaṅgatiḥ| brahmakāraṇatvaṃ yuktamayuktaṃveti cetanāt brahmaṇo'cetanatayā tadvilakṣaṇasya jagata utpattisambhavāsambhavābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ cetanādbrahmaṇo'cetanasya jagata utpattirna sambhavati| vilakṣaṇatvāt| ghaṭakuṇḍalamṛtsuvarṇādīnāṃ mṛttvasuvarṇatvādikāraṇagatāsādhāraṇarūpeṇa sālakṣakhaṇye satyeva hi kāryakāraṇabhāvo dṛṣṭaḥ| cetanasya ca cetanatvamasādhāraṇaṃ na tatkāryeṣūpalabhyate| na ca kadā'pyunupalabhyamānaṃ caitanyaṃ suṣuptajīveṣviva ghaṭādiṣvanudbhūtaṃ śruta brahmakāraṇatā'nyathā'nupapattyā kalpanīyam| sati caitanye kadācidudbhavāvaśyambhāvena tathā kalpanā'yogāt| anyathā śrutayṛpādityabhāvānyathānupapattyā yūpe'pyanudbhūtādityatvakalpanāpatteḥ, "vijñānañcāvijñānañce"tyādicetanācetanavibhājakaśrutivirodhāpatteśca vilakṣaṇāvilakṣaṇayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvābhyupa gatau vailakṣaṇye sati bhedāvaśyaṃbhāvena kāryakāraṇaikyasiddhāṃtavirodhāpatteśceti| siddhāṃtastu - 
krimimākṣikādiṣu satyapi vailakṣaṇye kāryakāraṇabhāvo dṛśyate| tatrācetanāṃśa eva mākṣikaprakṛtika iti sālakṣaṇyamastīti cet na| yathā kathañcitsālakṣaṇyasya kāryakāraṇabhāvaprayojakatve sarvasya sarvasmādutpattiḥ
prasajyata iti kāraṇagatāsādhāraṇarūpeṇa sālakṣaṇyaṃ tavābhimatam| na ca tadihopapādayituṃ śakyam| krimau mākṣikatvasyābhāvāt| evamupapadyate vilakṣaṇayorapi jagadbrahmaṇoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ| na ca tathā sati bhedābhyupagamāpattiḥ| sālakṣaṇyapratiṣedhe'pi kuṇḍalahiraṇyayorivābhedopapatteḥ| na ca jagadbrahmaṇorabhede jagatsṛṣṭyādiṣu satsu heyā jagadavasthāḥ jagatīva brahmaṇyapi syurityekasminneva brahmaṇi heyaguṇāḥ tadviruddhāḥ śrutyantarasiddhāpahatapāpmatvādayaścetyasāmañjasyāpattiḥ| sūkṣmacidacidvastuśarīrakaṃ brahma kāraṇaṃ, sthūlacidacidvastuśarīrakaṃ brahma kāryamityevaṃrūpe siddhāntābhimate kāryakāraṇābhede tadaprasakteḥ śarīragatadharmāṇāṃ śarīriṇyaprasaṅge bālyayauvanatthāvirādīnāmātmanyasambandhasya dṛṣṭāntasya sadbhāvāditi| evaṃ ca yadyapi sthūlasūkṣma śarīrarūpaviśeṣaṇāṃśe kāryakāraṇayoracetanatvena viśeṣyāṃśe tayoścetanatvena ca sālakṣaṇyamastīti vaktuṃ śakyam, tathā'pi kāraṇagataviśeṣyāṃśasya kāryagata viśeṣaṇāṃśe'pi kāraṇatvaṃ vaktavyamityabhyupetya vailakṣaṇyam| tatra dṛṣṭānta
upanyastaḥ| bālyāvasthasya yauvanādyavasthāntarāpattāvapi hi kāraṇagataviśeṣyāṃśasya kāryagataviśeṣaṇāṃśe'pi kāraṇatvamasti, nirātmakaśarīrasya yauvanādiprāptyabhāvāt, tathehāpi satyapi vailakṣaṇye kāryakāraṇabhāva upapadyata iti darśayitum||
saṃgrahakārike||
acetanaṃ cetanataḥ kathaṃ syādvilakṣaṇādviśvamiti sthavīyaḥ|
viśiṣṭayoḥ kāraṇakāryabhāve na codyametaddhi padaṃ labheta||
tathā'pi tarkapratitarkaucyate na sarvasāmyaṃ kvacidapyudīkṣyate|
yathā kathañcitprakṛte'pi vartate viśeṣato na krimimākṣikādiṣu||
iti vilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇam||
etena śiṣṭāparigrahā api vyākhyātāḥ||
pradhānakāraṇavādisāṃkhyāmātratarkeṇa samanvayabhaṅgābhāve'pi paramāṇukāraṇavādikaṇabhakṣākṣacaraṇakṣapaṇakādibahutvāntrikābhimatatarkeṇa tadbhaṅgassyādityadhikāśaṅkānirāsāya pūrvanyāyātideśārthamidamadhikaraṇam| paramāṇukāraṇavādināṃ tarkeṇa samanvayabhaṅgo bhavati na veti teṣā parasparavirodhasadbhāve'pi tadbhaṅgasāmarthyasambhavatadasambhavābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| sambhavati tadbhaṅge sāmarthya, yathā śunāṃ parasparavirodhasattve'pi ekaṃ varāhaṃ mārayitumaikamatyena tadbhakṣaṇalipsayā pravṛttānāṃ sarveṣāṃ śunāṃ tadalābhe'pi varāhasya māraṇaṃ bhavati| evaṃ paramāṇavassatyāḥ śūnyāḥ vijñānātmakāḥ kṣaṇikāścaturvidhāḥ ekavidhā ityevamavāntarakalahasadbhāve'pyalpaparimāṇānāmeva taṃtvādīnāṃ mahāparimāṇapaṭādyārambhakatvadarśanāt tadviparītaparimāṇayorbrahmajagatorupādānopādeyabhāvo na sambhavatīti pratiṣṭhitena sakalaparamāṇuvādyabhimatena tarkeṇa tadbhaṅga upapadyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - 
yathā nānādigviprakīrṇamapi bahulantamastomaṃ eka evādityaḥ pratikṣipati, evamudāhṛtaṃ anyadapyetādṛśaṃ tarkajālaṃ "yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛṇmaya" mityādyo mṛtyādyo mṛtpiṇḍārabdhaghaṭaśarāvādipratyudāharaṇena pratikṣipan veda eva jāgartīti nātraprayatitavyamastīti||
saṃgrahakārike||
aṇukāraṇatā bahavādimatā pratitiṣṭhati tarka iheti mudhā|
tadavāntarabhedakathākalahakṣapitaśśrutisīmni na sa prabhavet||
alpānmahatsyānmahato nālpamityeṣa vādināṃ|
śvavarāhanayaḥ kṣiptaḥ śrutyā dṛṣṭāntavarṇanāt||
iti śiṣṭāparigrahādhikaraṇam||
||bhokrāpatteravibhāgaścetsyāllokavat||
pūrvādhikaraṇasyātideśādhikaraṇatvāt tadvihāya tanmūlādhikaraṇena saṅgatamidamadhikaraṇam| tatra hi "na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvā"diti sūtreṇa śarīragatabālatvayuvatvādīnāṃ śarīriṇyaprasaṅgaṃ dṛṣṭāntīkṛtya cidaciccharīrakaṃ brahmeti tadgatadoṣāṇāṃ brahmaṇyaprasaktiruktā| sā'nupapannā| jīvavaccharīritve tatkṛta sūkhaduḥkhabhogaprasakterityākṣipyate| prathame'dhyāye "sambhogaprāptiriti cenna vaiśeṣyā"diti sūtreṇa parasyāsmadādiśarīrānupraveśaprayuktā sukhaduḥkhabhogaprasaktirnirākṛtā| yathā paragṛhapraviṣṭasyāgṛhasvāmino gṛhasākalyavaikalyaprayuktaharṣaviṣādabhāktvaṃ nāsti evaṃ parasyāpīti| iha punaḥ śarīrasvāmitva prayuktā sā nirākriyate| "na ha vai saśarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayorapahatirastī"ti śravaṇāt śarīrasvāmitve tatkṛtasukhaduḥkhabhogo na nivārayituṃ śakya iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - 
yathā'parādhinamaparādhānuguṇaṃ kleśayituṃ tadarthaṃ kārāgṛhaṃ nirmāya tatkleśanārthaṃ svayamapi tatra praviṣṭasya svatantrasya tatkṛtakleśabhāktvaṃ na bhavati, evaṃ parasyāpi| "nahavai saśarīrasye"tyādiśrutirapi svakarmopārjitaśarīraviṣayā| na tu śarīrasvāmimātraviṣayā| antaryāmibrāhmaṇo"yasya pṛthivī śarīra"mityādinā parasya pṛthivyādiśarīrasvāmitvamuktvā
tattadabhimānidevatāvat tatkṛtasukhaduḥkhabhākatvaśaṅkānirāsāyaiva pratiparyāyaṃ "eṣata ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛta" ityamṛtatvavacanāditi||
saṃgrahakārike||
yadi varṇyeta cidaciccharīraṃ brahma kāraṇam|
tadā śarīriṇo'sya syāt bhoktṛtvaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ||
maivaṃ karmakṛtaṃ tanna śarīrakṛtaṃ tato'sya tanna bhavet|
rājānugrahanigrahavaśavartitvaṃ yathā rājñaḥ||
iti bhoktrāpattyadhikaraṇam||
tadananyatvamārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ||
bhāvecopalabdheḥ||
satvāccāparasya||
asadvyapadeśānneti cenna dharmāntareṇa vākyaśeṣādyukteśśabdāntarācca||
paṭavacca||
yathā ca prāṇādiḥ||
vilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇe kāraṇāt brahmaṇaḥ kāryasya jagato vailakṣaṇye'pyananyatvaṃ "asaditi cenna pratiṣedhamātratvā" diti guṇasūtroktaṃ kuṇḍalahiraṇyanyāyena sāṃkhyaṃpratyupapāditam| idānīṃ vailakṣaṇye satyananyatvamayuktam| vailakṣaṇyasya bhedavyāptatvāt| ata eva kuṇḍalahiraṇyayorapyananyatvamasiddham| sarvatra kāryakāraṇayorbuddhiśabdāntarādibhirbhedāvaśyambhāvāditi vadato vaiśeṣikasya matamāsthāyānanyatvamākṣipya samādhīyata iti saṅgatiḥ| tatraivaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ - ananyatve brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvaṃ na saṃbhavati| loke kāryakāraṇabhūtānāṃ ghaṭamṛtpiṇḍādīnāṃ buddhiśabdāntarādibhiḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvasya bhedavyāptinirdhāraṇāt| nahi mṛtpiṃḍe piṃḍaśabdo vā ghaṭe vā piṃḍākārā buddhirudeti| na ca ghaṭaśabdo mṛtpiṃḍe piṃḍaśabdo vā ghaṭe prayujyate| na ca piṃḍākāro ghaṭe pṛthubudhnodarākāro mṛtpiṃḍevā'sti| na ca mṛtpiṃḍenodakāharaṇaṃ ghaṭena vā kuḍyanirmāṇaṃ kriyate| na ca taṃtuṣviva paṭe bahutvaṃ paṭaiva taṃtuṣvekatvaṃ vā'sti| na ca ghaṭasyeva mṛtpiṃḍasya daṇḍacakrādi kāraṇam, mṛtpiṃḍasyeva ghaṭasya jalasaṃyojanapūrvakamardanaṃ vā kāraṇaṃ bhavati| na cottarakāle mṛtpiṃḍaḥ pūrvakāle ghaṭovā'sti| na ca pūrvakāle ghaṭādidarśanamuttarakāle mṛtpiṃḍadarśanaṃ vā'sti| tasmādbuddhiśabdādibhedāt kāryakāraṇayorbheda eṣṭavyaḥ| ekasminneva śarīre bālyayauvanādyavasthābhedeneva ekasminneva mṛddravye piṃḍa ghaṭāvasthābhedena buddhiśabdāntarādaya upapadyanta iti cet na | avasthānāmanityatāyāmasatkāryavādāpatteḥ, anityānāmavasthānāṃ prāksatā kāraṇenānanyatvāsambhavāt
kāryakāraṇānanyatvasiddhāṃtabhaṅgāpatteśca| tāsāmapi nityātāyāṃ buddhiśabdāntarādivyavasthityasambhavāt daṇḍacakrādikāraṇakalāpavaiyarthyaprasaṅgācca| nanu mṛdaḥ piṇḍatādaśāyāmanabhivyaktā ghaṭāvasthā, abhivyaktaiva sā buddhiśabdāntarādibhājanaṃ, abhivyaktyarthaśca daṇḍacakrādi kāraṇakalāpaḥ| na ca tathā sati mṛtpiṇḍagataghaṭāvasthābhivyaktyarthena daṇḍacakrādivyāpāreṇa tadgatakarakakapālamaṇikamallakādyavasthānāmapyapavarakagataghaṭābhivyaktyarthamāropitena pradīpana tadgatakarakādīnāmivābhivyaktiprasaṅgaḥ| nailyotpalatvayoḥ pradīpaprabhayotpalatvasyaivābhivyaktiḥ| kūpajalasya kūpāntarasthitisamaye jalatvaśauklyayorjalatvasyaivābhivyaktirityādivyavasthādarśanena tadvadihāpi yathā darśanamabhivyaṅgyābhivyañjakabhāvavaicitryopapatteriti cet - maivam|
abhivyaktāvapi nityatvānityatvavikalpe pūrvoktadoṣānātiprasakteḥ|kiṃca keyamabhivyaktiḥ, jñānaṃ cet, tadā daṇḍacakrādimelanasya ghaṭajñānakāraṇatvāpattyā tasminnasati kvacidapi ghaṭajḍhānodayo na syāt| āvaraṇābhibhavaścet tena kimāvaraṇamabhibhūyate? pūrvaṃ mṛdisthitā piṃḍāvastheti cet - na| tathā sati ghaṭadhvaṃsakamudgarapātānaṃtaramapi ghaṭāvasthāyāḥ pratītiprasaṅgaḥ| tadāvaraṇabhūtāyāḥ piṇḍāvasthāyāstirohitatvāt| nanu tadānīṃ mudgarapātābhivyaktā kapālāvasthā tadāvaraṇaṃ, svakāraṇābhivyaktāyāḥ kasyāścit kāryāvasthāyāḥ tadgatasakalakāryāvasthāṃtarāvaraṇatvābhyupagamāt| na ca tathā sati kapāleṣu kadācit ghaṭakāraṇamelane ghaṭāvasthābhivyaktiprasaṅgaḥ| iṣṭāpatteḥ| kapālāni cūrṇīkṛtya jalamṛdantarasaṃyojanamardanavyāpārairmārdavaṃ
prāpayya daṇḍacakrādimelane ghaṭābhivyaktidarśanāt| na ca dagdhapaṭakāraṇānāṃ punaḥ paṭabhāvo na dṛśyata iti śaṅkanīyam| tadbhasmanāṃ kṣititalapatitānāṃ tatkṣitiprarūḍhakārpāsataruphalapariṇatau krameṇa teṣāṃ paṭabhāvābhivyaktidarśanāditicet maivaṃ| evaṃ ghaṭādyavasthānāṃ piṇḍādyavasthāsvapi sadbhāvasya tadānīṃ tadapratītyarthaṃ pratīyamānapiṃḍādyavasthānāṃ tadāvaraṇatvasya ca kalpane kalpakābhāvāt yathānubhavamavasthānāmāgantukatvena vyavasthityupapatteḥ| anyathā kadācit kasyāścitevāvasthāyā anubhavaḥ, nāvasthāntarāṇāmityanubhavānanubhavavyavasthāyā apyanupapatteḥ| na hyabhivyaktiranubhavasvayaṃvaralabhyaḥ, yena daṃḍacakrādisamavadhānātpūrvaṃ piṃḍāvasthāyā evānubhavaḥ, tadanantaraṃ ghaṭāvasthāyāeva| tato mudgarapātānantaraṃ kapālāvasthāyā evetyādivyavasthā sidhyet| na ca jalasaṃyojanamṛṇmṛdukaraṇādikaṃ daṇḍacakrādikaṃ mudgarapātanādikañca piṃḍaghaṭakapālāvasthānāmanubhaveṣu vā tattaditarāvasthānāmananubhaveṣu vā karaṇānītyuktyā tathā vyavasthānirvāhaḥ| udāhṛtatattatkāraṇasamavadhāneṣvatīteṣvapi piṃḍādyavasthānāmanubhavasya tattatpratidvaṃdvivyavasthānāmananubhavasyacānuvṛttisattvāt| tasmādyathānubhavaṃ pūrvaṃ piṃḍāvasthaiva, madhye ghaṭāvasthaiva, tataḥkapālāvasthaiveti vyavasthaivāṅgīkartuṃ yuktā| tathā satyavasthānāmāgantukatvenāsatkāryavādaḥ prasajyata iti tatparihārārthamidaṃkalpyata iti cet, kimabhivyaktīnāmāgantukatve sa na prasajyate, abhivyaktīnāmapi nityatve tu kathamapi tvaduktavyavasthāsiddhiḥ| evamavasthānāṃ tattadabhivyaktīnāṃ vā''gantukatvevāsatkāryavādasya apratikṣepyatvasthitau dravyamātre satkāryavādābhyupagamasya niṣprayojanatvāt| kulālena ghaṭaḥ kṛtaḥ, kalādena kuṇḍalaṃ kṛtamiti kartṛkāryayoriva mṛtpiṇḍena ghaṭaḥ kṛtaḥ suvarṇena kuṇḍalaṃ kṛtamiti samavāyikāraṇatatsamavetakāryayorapyabādhita bhedavyavahāradarśanāt kāryakāraṇayoravasthābhedavaddravyabheda upagantuṃ yuktaḥ| "mṛddhaṭassuvarṇaṃ kuṇḍala" mityabhedavyavahāreṇa bhedavyavahāro bādhita iti cet na| mṛttvasuvarṇatvayoravayavāvayavivṛttijātitvena tathā vyavahāropapattyā tena bhedavyavahārabādhanāyogāt| ata eva yatrāvayavavṛttistantutvādijātirnāvayavipaṭādivṛttiḥ, na tatra tantayaḥ paṭa ityādivyavahāraḥ| tasmāt sahāvasthābhiḥ kāryakāraṇadravyayorbheda eva yukta iti loke kāryakāraṇabhedaniyamadarśanāt jagadbrahmaṇorananyatve brahmaṇojagatkāraṇatvāsambhavāt jagatkāraṇe brahmaṇi samanvaya ityetannaghaṭata iti evaṃprāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
tadananyatvaṃ paramakāraṇādbrahmaṇo'nanyatvameva| jagataḥ tadupapādayadbhya ārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ| tathā ca chāndogye - "śvetaketurhāruṇeya āsa taṃ ha pitovāce"ti prastutya "śvetaketo vasa brahmacaryaṃ na va somyāsmatkulīno'nanūcya brahmabandhuriva bhavatī"ti pitrā niyuktasya tasya gurukulavāsaṃ pratinivṛttiñca "sahadvādaśavarṣa upetya caturviṃśativarṣaḥ punareyāye"tyuktvā tasya vedābhyāsamūlamavalepaṃ
paśyatātamavalepamapanīyatasyābrahmajijñāsāmutpipādayiṣatā pitrā sa itthaṃ pṛṣṭa ityuktam| "stabdhosyuta tamādeśamaprākṣyo yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatyamataṃ matamavijñātaṃ vijñāta"miti| yena śrutena matena vijñātena cetyarthaḥ| "ātmani khalvare dṛṣṭe śrute matevijñāte idaṃ sarvaṃ viditaṃ bhavatī"ti śrutyantarāt| evaṃ kṛtsnasya jagato brahmaikakāraṇakatvaṃ kāraṇāt kāryasyānanyatvaṃ ca hṛdi nidhāya brahmavijñānena sarvavijñāne pratijñāte sati tadabhiprāyamajānatā putreṇa kathamanyavijñānenānyadvijñātaṃ bhavatīti manyamānena "kathannubhagavassa ādeśa" iti pṛṣṭe pitā svapratijḍhātamarthamupapādayituṃ kāraṇāt kāryasyānanyatve laukikṛṣṭāntamuvāceti varṇitam - "yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṃḍena sarvaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vijñātaṃ syā"diti | yathā ekamṛtpiṇḍārabdhaghaṭaśarāvāda yastadanatiriktadravyatvāt tajjñānena jñātā bhavantītyarthaḥ| atra vaiśeṣikaprakriyayā kāraṇātkāryasya buddhiśabdāntarādibhirdravyāntaratvamāśaṃkya lokapratītyaiva kāryakāraṇānanyatvamupapādayituṃ vākyaśeṣaḥ "vācāraṃbhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttiketyeva satya"miti| asyārthaḥ - ārabhyate ālabhyate spṛśyata ityārambhaṇam| "yatparyapaśyatsarirasya madhya" itivat ralayorabhedādrephaḥ| ālambha iha sparśaḥ| "ālambhassparśahiṃsayo"riti hi kośakārāḥ| "kṛtyalyuṭo bahula"miti karmaṇi lyuṭ| vācā - "ghaṭenodakamāhare"tyādivākpūrvakodakāharaṇādivyavahāreṇa hetunetyarthaḥ| vākpadenājahallakṣaṇayā vyavahārasyāpi saṃgrahaḥ| vāceti hetau tṛtīyā, adhyayanena hetunā vasatītivat prayojanasya hetutvavikṣayā prayojanārthā| vākpūrvakavyavahārasiddhyarthaṃ mṛddravyeṇaiva pṛthubudhnodaratvādivikārassaṃsthānaviśeṣaḥ tataprayuktañca ghaṭa iti nāmadheyaṃ spṛśyate| "napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyā"miti vikāranāmadheyobhayavartina ārambhaṇaśabdasya napuṃsakaśeṣataikavacanañca| udakāharaṇādivyavahāraviśeṣasidhyarthaṃ mṛddravyamevāvasthāṃtaranāmadheyāṃrabhāgbhavati| ato ghaṭādyapi mṛttiketyeva satyaṃ prāmāṇikaṃ na tu dravyāṃtaratveneti| "ārambhaṇaśabdādibhya" ityādiśabdena "sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyaṃ, tadaikṣatabahusyāṃprajāyeyetī"tyādayaḥ kāryakāraṇayorjagadbrahmaṇorananayatvapratipādakāśśabdāgṛhyaṃte| evañca yadyapi bālasthavirarītyā
buddhiśabdāntarādayo'vasthābhedeneva ghaṭarucakādirītyā dravyabhedenāpyupapadyante| dravyabheda eva cātyantasāmañjasyam| tathā'pi kāryakāraṇānanyatve mṛdghaṭādikaṃ dṛṣṭāṃ tayaṃtyā śrutyā'vasthābheda eva darśita iti avasthābhedakṛtā eva buddhiśabdāntarādaya ityurarīkaraṇīyam| na cāvasthānāmāgantukatāyāmasatkāryavādāpattirdoṣaḥ| sata eva dravyasyotpattirhi satkāryavādaḥ, na tvavasthānāmapyanāgantukatvaṃ| na ca prāgapi sato dravyasyotpattyanupapattiḥ| na hyabhūtvā bhavanumutpattirityabhyupeyate| kiṃ tvavasthāntarotpattirutpattiḥ, pūrvāvasthāprahāṇaṃ nāśa iti| evaṃ ca ekamṛtpiṇḍārabdheṣu ghaṭaśarāvādiṣvekatvāvasthāprahāṇenānekatvāvasthāpattiḥ| sāṃdrataṃtvārabdhe paṭe anekatvāvasthāprahāṇenaikatvāvasthāpattiḥ| ato viralataṃtvārabdhe paṭe paṭagataikatvāvasthāpattāvapi na tantusatastantutvasyevā'nekatvasyāpi na grahāṇam| kintu tantutvāvacchedenānekatvānuvṛttiḥ, paṭatvāvacchedenaikatvāpattirityādayopi viśeṣā yathādarśanamanusandheyāḥ| nanu "mṛttiketyevasatya"mitya śrutyā ghaṭaśarāvādikaṃ mṛttiketyeva prāmāṇikam, na tu dravyāṃtaratvenetyevamarthayā kāryakāraṇadravyānanyatve pramāṇāṃtaramapyādarśitamiti vyākhyātam, tat kiṃ pramāṇāntaramiti cet - pratyabhijñāpratyakṣamiti brūmaḥ| mṛtpiṇḍāvasthāsadbhāvepi hi ghaṭaśarāvādidravyasadbhāvaḥ pratyabhijñāpratyakṣeṇopalabhyate| idaṃ ghaṭaśarāvādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvāhne mṛtpiṇḍa evāsīditi pūrvāhnasthita eka eva mṛtkūṭaḥ etāvadghaṭaśarāvādirūpa āsīditi ca pratyabhijñā dṛśyate| nanvidaṃ ghaṭaśarāvādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvāhne nāsīdityapi loke vyavahāro dṛśyate| śrutāvapi "asadevedamagra āsī"diti prāgjagadasattvaṃ vyapadiśyate| kathaṃ pratyakṣaśrutibhyāṃ lokavedayoḥ kāryakāraṇānanyatvavyavasthāsiddhiriti ceta ucyate - pṛthubudhnodarādisthūlatvavirodhisūkṣmatvaṃ yaddharmāntaraṃ tato'yamasadvyapadeśaḥ| na tu prāksarvathaivābhāvāt| kutaḥ śrutau vākyaśeṣāt "idaṃ vā agrenaiva kiṃca nāsī"dityatra hi "tadasadeva sanmano'kuruta syā"mitītyasato manaskārākhyamānasasaṅkalpapratipādako vākyaśeṣaśśrūyate| loke'pi tata evāsadvyapadeśa iti yuktito'vagamyate| na hi sarvatra tattadvirodhidharmavyatirekeṇābhāvonāma kaścidasti| kalpanīyābhāvavat tadvirodhanaḥ klṛptabhāvarūpadharmāṃtarasyaivābhāvavyavahārālaṃbanatopapattāvatiriktābhāvakalpane gauravāt| lokavedayorasadvyapadeśapratidvaṃdvinaśśabdāntarācca "sadeva somyedamagra āsīt" "idaṃ ghaṭaśarāvādikaṃ pūrvāṇhe
mṛdevāsī"dityevaṃrūpāttathā'vagamyate| asadvyapadeśāt sadvyapadeśasya kāryakāraṇānanyatvaparyavasāyino yuktisāhityena balavattavāt| yuktirhi virodhibhāvadharmavyatirekeṇābhāvanāmakaṃ vastvantaraṃ na kalpanīyamiti prāgupanyastā| yathā tantava eva vyatiṣaktatantuvyatirekeṇa paṭanāmakamavayavidravyāntamasti| tasya rūpādirāhitye cākṣuṣatavādyabhāvaprasaṃgāt| rūpāntarādimattve'vayavarūpavyatirekeṇa avayavini rūpāntarasya gurutvadvaiguṇyasya copalabdhiprasaṃgāt| evaṃ vyatiṣaktantūnāmevapaṭabhāvavat ekasyaiva vāyośśarīre vṛttiviśeṣeṇa prāṇāpānādibhāvavacca brahmaṇa eva vicitracarācararūpajagadbhāvopapatterna vastubhedaḥ kalpanīya ityanayāpi yuktyā sadvyapadeśebhyobalavattvamavadhāraṇīyam| evañca "suvarṇena kuṇḍalaṃ kṛta" mityādivyavahāropyavasthābhedakṛtenaiva kāryakāraṇabhāvena upapādanīyaḥ| anyathā kuṇḍalāya suvarṇe tolayitvārpite suvarṇakāreṇa kuṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā dviguṇagurutvena tolayitvā pratyarpaṇīyatvaprasaṃgāt| tasmānmṛtpiṇḍaghaṭaśārāvādīnāmanunyatvāt taddṛṣṭāṃ tena brahmajagatorapyupādānopādeyabhūtayorananyatvamiti||
saṃgrahakārikā||
kāryāṇāṃ buddhiśabdākṛtiphalagaṇanāhetukālādibhedādbhinnatvaṃkāraṇebhyassthiramiti na daśābhedatassyātsa bhedaḥ|
ekasyaiva hyanekā samudayati daśā hetubhiḥ kālabhedāt

iti ārambhaṇādhikaraṇam||
itaravyapadeśāddhitākaraṇādidoṣaprasaktiḥ||21||
adhikantu bhedanirdeśāt||22||
aśmādivacca tadanupapattiḥ||23||
brahmaṇassvānanyajagatsraṣṭṛtve tasya hitākaraṇādayo doṣāssyurityākṣepikī saṃgatiḥ parvapakṣaśca - na ca prasādhitena mṛtpiṃḍaghaṭaśarāvādidṛṣṭāṃtena brahmaṇassūkṣmacidacitprapañcaviśiṣṭarūpeṇa kāraṇatvaṃ, sthūlacidacitprapañcarūpeṇa kāryatvamiti
brahmaṇa eva kāryakāraṇabhāvenānanyatvamucyate, na tu brahmajagatoriti vācyam| brahmaṇassarvakāraṇasya svaviśeṣaṇe cidacidātmake jagadrūpakārye'pi kāraṇatvasyāvaśyavaktavyatvāt| tataśca brahmaṇastenāpyananyatvaṃ vācyamiti sarvajñaṃ sarvaśaktikaṃ brahma svābhinnatvena jīvaṃ paśyadapi tasya hitarūpaṃ jagannasṛjati, ahitarūpameva jagatsṛjatīti svasyaiva hitākaraṇādidoṣaḥ prasajyate| atassvasmādananyadeva jagadbrahma sṛjatītyetannayuktamiti| rāddhāntastu - 
jīvādadhikaṃ brahma tato bhinnameva| "satyakāmassatyasaṅkalpaḥ, jñājñau dvāvajāvīśanīśau, sakāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipaḥ, asmānmāyī sṛjate viśvameta tasmiṃścānyo māyayā sanniruddhaḥ" ityādi bhedanirdeśāt| ananyatvaśrutayastu apṛthagbhāvaviṣayāḥ| pṛthagbhūtaprakāro hi brahmaṇaścidacidātmakaprapañcaḥ| "sadeve" tyādiśrutau agre "idaṃ sarvaṃsadevāsī"diti sāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśena jagatassūkṣmatādaśāyāṃ sacchabdanirdiṣṭabrahmaviśeṣaṇatvasya "tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeye"ti brahmaṇe bahubhavanasaṅkalpādyuktyā| sthūlatādaśāyāṃ tadviśeṣaṇatvasya ca pratipādanena jagataḥ kadāpi brahmapṛthagbhāvo nāstītyavagamāt mṛdghaṭaśarāvādirapyapṛthagbhāvarūpa evānanyatve dṛṣṭāntaḥ| svābhinnāt svasya kadāpi pṛthagbhāvāsaṃbhavāt| na cānanyatvaśrutayāḥ "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tattvamasī"tyādyāvā mukhyābhedaviṣayāḥ, bhedaśrutayastu tattadviśeṣaṇabhedādaupacārikabhedaviṣayā ityastviti vācyam| tathāsīta prasaktahitākaraṇādidoṣasyaiva parihārālābhādaśmakāṣṭhaloṣṭādivadasyantaheyaguṇānāṃ jīvānāṃ nirdoṣakalyāṇaguṇaikatānabrahmābhedāsaṃbhavācca| tasmāt "avasthiteritikāśakṛtsna" ityādi sūtroktaprakāremānanyatvaśrutaya evāpṛthagbhūtaprakāraprakāribhāvaviṣayā ityeva yuktamiti||
saṃgrahakārikā||
evaṃ jīvā api niyamatastatvamasyādivākyā
dbrahmābhinnā iti tadahitāṃ naiva kurvīta sṛṣṭim|
maivaṃ vākyaṃ na tadabhidayā bhedanirdeśadṛṣṭestatsthatvenatvitihikathitaḥ kāśakṛtsnīyapakṣaḥ||
iti itaravyapadeśādhikaraṇam||
upasaṃhāradarśanānneticennakṣīravaddhi||24||
devādivadapi loke||25||
satyasaṅkalpatvādibhedanirdeśādananyatvaśrutayassaṃtvapṛthagbhāvamātraviṣayāḥ| naivaṃ satyasaṅkalappatve'pi sarvaśaktimattve'pi sahakārisāhitasya brahmaṇo jagadupādānatvatatsraṣṭṛtvaśrutayassamarthayituṃśakyante| śaktasyāpi sahakārirahitasyākāryakaratvādityutthānādavāṃtarasaṅgatiḥ| pūrvapakṣaśca - "sadeva somyedamagra āsī"dityavadhāraṇena prāktsṛṣṭessarvasahakāriśūnyaṃ khalu brahma śrūyate| tatkathaṃ jagatkāraṇabhāvamaśnuvīta| na ca sarvaśaktikatvena kaścidviśeṣaḥ| ghaṭādyutpādanaśaktaeva hi kulālādissahakāriṇaḥ apekṣyaiva ghaṭādikamutpādayati| evaṃ brahmāpi sarvakāryotpādanaśaktañcet, tattatkāryocitāni sahakārīṇyapekṣyaiva tāni tāni kāryāṇi janayet| na hi kulālo ghaṭotpādanaśaktaiti sahakāryanapekṣaḥ tamutpādayati| aśaktassahakāriṣu satsvapi na tamutpādayatītyeva śaktasyāśaktādviśeṣaḥ| tasmāt sahakārirahitaṃ brahma na kāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhatīti pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ| rāddhāntastu - 
brahmaṇassahakārirāhitye'pi kṣīrasya dadhibhāvāpattivat devarṣiprabhṛtīnāṃ svasvabhogasādhanasraṣṭṛtvavat jagadupādānatvaṃ tatsraṣṭṛtvavañca upapadyate| na ca kṣīrasya dadhibhāvāpattau pākātaṃcane sahakāriṇī staḥ| na ca brahmaṇo jagadupādānatve kiñcitsahakāryasti| devādīnāñca yogaprabhāvāsāditaiśvaryatayā bhūtavaśināṃ svasaṅkalpākṛṣṭairbhūtaissraṣṭṛtvamupapadyate| na tatheśvarasya nityasiddhasya bhūtavaśitāpādako yogaprabhāvo'stīticet - maivaṃ| vināpi pākātaṃcanābhyāṃ cireṇa dadhibhāvo bhavatyeva| acireṇa dadhibhāvāpattyarthaṃ rasaviśeṣārthañca tayorapekṣā| yogābhyāsādaiśvaryāvāptiśca śvetāśvataropaniṣadādimantrārthavādebhyo'vagantavyā| brahmaṇo'pi saṅkalpamātreṇa sraṣṭṛtve mantrārthavādāssaṃtīti na kaścidviśeṣaḥ|
kiṃca sarvasyāpi saṅkalpamātreṇa svaśarīravyāpārayitṛtvaṃ dṛṣṭam| kṛtsnañjagat brahmaṇaśśarīramiti tasya jagadvyāpāre na kācidanupapattiḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
agre sadekaṃ sahakāriśūnyaṃ kathaṃ hyupādānatayā jagatsyāt|
saṅkalpamātreṇa kathaṃ ca kuryāt kṣīrādidevādivadityavehi||
iti upasaṃhāradarśanādhikaraṇam||
kṛtsnaprasaktirniravayavatvaśabdakopovā||26||
śrutestu śabdamūlatvāt||27||
ātmani caivaṃ vicitrāśca hi||28||
svapakṣadoṣācca||29||
sarvopetā ca taddarśanāt||30||
vikaraṇatvānneti cettaduktam||31||
yadi kṣīraddaṣṭāṃtena brahmaṇojagadbhāvāpattiḥ, tarhi kārtsnyena kṣīrasya dadhibhāvavat brahmaṇo'pi kārtsnyena jagadbhāva āpadyeteti ākṣepikī saṅgatiḥ| pūrvapakṣastu - kṣīraṃ dadhyākāreṇeva brahma kārtsnyena jagadākāreṇa
pariṇamate, uta agnisaṃyuktaikabhāgojatupiṃḍomudrikādyupayuktaikadeśeneva tattatkāryopayuktaikadeśena pariṇamate| nādyaḥ| kṛtsnasya jagadbhāvāpattyā brahmāvaśeṣābhāvaprasaṅgāt| na ca sūkṣmacidacitprapañcaviśiṣṭaṃ brahma kāraṇaṃ, sthūlacidacitprapañcaviśiṣṭaṃ brahma kāryāmiti pakṣe nabrahmānavaśeṣadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ, kāryarūpatayā brahmāvasthānāditi vācyam| idānīṃ kāraṇarūpeṇāpi brahmaṇassattvasya vaktavyatvāt| anyathā kāraṇasya brahmaṇassārvadikasattābodhakānāṃ tatsākṣātkāropāyavidyāvidhāyakānāṃca vākyānāmānarthakyaprasaṅgāt| sarvopādānasya brahmaṇaścidacidrūpaviśeṣaṇāṃśe'pi kāraṇatvasya vaktavyatayā tatra kṛtsnasya brahmaṇa ekakāryātmanā pariṇāme kāryāntarātmanā pariṇāmaḥ kāraṇabrahmāvaśeṣaśca na syāditi dūṣaṇasya vajralepāyamānatvācca| tasmādādyaḥ pakṣo na yuktaḥ| nāpi dvitīyaḥ| niravayavasyaiṃkadeśena pariṇāmāyogāt sāvayavatvamiṣyatāmiti cenna| "niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāṃta"mityādiniravayavatva śrutivyākopaprasaṅgāt| tasmādbrahma jagadupādānamityetannayuktamiti|| siddhāṃtastu - 
naivaṃ śaṅkāvakāśo'sti| niravayavameva brahma jagadākāreṇa pariṇamate| svayañca yathāpūrvaṃ paripūrṇamavatiṣṭhata iti śrutyaiva mukhataḥ pratipādanāt| na ca śrutipratipanno'rthaḥ kutarkeṇopanayamarhati| "naiṣā tarkeṇa matirāpaneye"ti śrutiḥ| nanu śrutirapi grāvaplavanādivat pramāṇāṃtaraviruddhaṃ pratipādayituṃ naśaktotīti cet, na| brahmaṇaśśabdaikabodhyatvena tadvailakṣaṇyāt| pratyakṣādiyogyehyarthe tadviruddhaṃ śrutirapi na bodhayati| śrutyekagamyastvartho yathā śrutyeva sidhyati| na tu tadviṣayatvāyogyena pramāṇāṃtareṇa bādhituṃ śakyam| na hi rūpasādhanākṣamasya śrotrasya tadbādhakatvamasti| kiṃca jīvātmanyaciddharmavilakṣaṇāḥ kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādayo'bhyupeyante| evaṃ paramātmani cidaciddharmavilakṣaṇā dharmāḥ kuto na syuḥ| loke'gnijalādayo'pi auṣṇyaśaityādibhirvicitrā dṛśyante| na khalu te parasparadṛṣṭāṃtena parasparavilakṣaṇadharmaśūnyāssvīkriyante| na ca "jātirvyaktau nāṃśena vartate niravayavatvāt| nāpi parisamāpya vartate| vyaktyantare tadabhāvaprasaṅgā"diti kṛtsnaikadeśavṛttivikalpena jātervyaktau vṛttirnirākriyate| svapakṣe ca sāṃkhyasya tulyamidaṃ doṣāpādanam, niravayavaṃ pradhānaṃ kathamiva mahadādivicitrakāryārambhakamiti| satvarajastamāṃsi tasyāvayavāssantīti cenna| pradhānātiriktānāṃ teṣāṃ tadavayavānamabhyupagame tattvānāṃ caturviṃśatisaṃkhyāvirodhāt, teṣāmapi kṛtsnaikadeśavikalpena mahadahaṅkārādyārambhakatvāyogācca| paramāṇvādikāraṇavādināmapi kṛtsnaikadeśasaṃyogādivikalpaidrvyaṇukādyāraṃbhāyogācca| tasmāt kalpyamapi pradhānaṃ kṛtsnaikadeśavṛttivikalpāgocaraṃ manyamānasya śrutisiddhe brahmaṇi kā tadgocaratvapratyāśā|kiñca śrutivaśādiva sarvaśaktyupetatvādapi brahmaṇo niravayavasyāpi vicitrajagadākāreṇa pariṇāmo ghaṭate| sarvaśaktyupetaṃ hi brahma| "para'sya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyata" ityādidarśanāt| alpaśaktimatāmapi prapañcāntargata cidacitāmatarkaṇīyā vividhā vaicitrī dṛśyate| sarvaśaktimatassā kathaṃ na bhavet| nanu śaktimatāmapi karaṇakalebarāpekṣa eva śaktyunmeṣo dṛṣṭaḥ| atastadanapekṣaṃ sadapi
sarvaśaktimattvamadhyayanānapekṣaṃ dhīśaktimattvāmivāprayojakamiti cet - atrottaramuktameva| śabdaikapramāṇakaṃ vastu yatāśabdameva grāhyam| na tu lokadṛṣṭakāraṇaviraheṇa pratyākhyātuṃ śakyam| tathaiva ca śruttiḥ - "apāṇipādo javano gṛhītā paśyatyacakṣussaśrṛṇotyakarṇaḥ| na tasya kāryaṃ karaṇaṃca vidyate na tatsamaścābhyadhikaśca dṛśyate| parāsyaśaktirvivadhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyāce"ti| ataśśrutyā śrutidarśitayuktyupodbalitayā niravayavamapi brahma jagadākāreṇa pariṇamate, tathaiva paripūrṇamatiṣṭhate ceti yuktameva||
saṃgrahakārike||
kṛtsnaṃ yadi pariṇamate brahma tadā nāvaśiṣyeta|
nāṃśena niraṃśatvādviśiṣṭapakṣe'pi tulyamidam||
etādṛśāḥ pradhāne paramāṇvādau ca doṣāssyuḥ|
na brahmaṇi śrutiśatapratipanne vividhaśaktiyute||
iti kṛtsnaprasaktyādhikaraṇam||
na prayojanavattvāt||32||
lokavattu līlākaivalyam||33||
vaiṣamyanairghṛṇye na sāpekṣatvāttathāhi darśayati||34||
nakarmāvibhāgāditi cennānāditvādupapadyate cāpyupalabhyateca||35||
sarvadharmopapatteśca||36||
yuktyupodbalitaśrutyā kṛtsna prasaktyādidoṣanirākaraṇena brahmaṇa upādānatvasamarthane'pi sraṣṭṛtvasamarthanaṃ na saṃbhavati| prekṣāvatpravṛtteḥ prayojanavattvaniyamādityutthānātsaṃgatiḥ| pūrvapakṣastu - brahmaṇo jagatsṛṣṭissvārthā parārthā vā| nādyaḥ| avāptasamastakāmasyāpekṣitaprayojanābhāvācca| na ca prekṣāvatāmapi niṣprayojanā kaṃtukakrīḍādipratītirdṛṣṭeti śaṃkyam| tato'pi krīḍāparāṇāṃ tātkālikānandasattvāt| na ca brahmaṇo jagatsṛṣṭāvapi tādṛśaṃ prayojanaṃ kalpyam| avāptasamastakāmatvaśrutivirodhāt| nanvavāptasamastakāmānāmapi rājñāṃ kantukakrīḍādipṛvṛttirdṛśyata iti cet - na| avāptabhogāntaraprayuktasakalakāmānāmapyanavāptakantukakrīḍānandārthameva tatra pravṛtteḥ| na caivaṃ brahmaṇi vaktuṃ śakyam| kiṃca svaprayojanārthaṃ jīvān saṃsāraduḥkhe nimajjayataśca brahmaṇo vaiṣamyanairghṛṇye syātāṃ| tallīlācāsamañjākhyasagaratanayalīlākalpā syāt| etena parārthāsṛṣṭiriti dvitīyapakṣo'pi nirastaḥ| sṛṣṭeḥ pārārthye hi sarvān sukhina eva sṛjet| pakṣapātarūpeṇa vaiṣamyeṇa kāṃścitsukhinaḥ kāṃściduḥkhinaśca na sṛjet| duḥkhinassṛjataśca nairghṛṇyaṃ prasajyate| na ca karmakṣapaṇena rakṣitumeva pāpino duḥkhaṃ prāpayati brahma, yathā vraṇino rakṣitumeva vraṇadāhacchedaduḥkhaṃ vaidyaḥ prāpayatīti vācyam| vraṇadāhādikamakṛtvā vraṇaśāṃti kartumaśaktasya vaidyasya dāhādiduḥkhaprāpakatve'pi sarvaśaktikasya brahmaṇoduḥkhamaprāpayyāpi rakṣituṃ śaktasya tatprāpakatvāyogāt| na ca tattatkarmāṇyapekṣya viṣamasṛṣṭikartuḥ pakṣapātitvanairghṛṇyadoṣau na prasajyeteiti vācyam| "sadeva somyedamagra āsī"diti brahmamātrāvaśeṣatvena śrutāyāmādyasṛṣṭau karmābhāvena tadānīṃtaniviṣamasṛṣṭyā vaiṣamyanairghaṇyaprasaṅgā nivāraṇāt| tasmādbrahmaṇo jagatsṛṣṭṛtvaṃ na yuktamiti|| siddhāntastu - 
satyaṃ| prayojanārthaiva prekṣāvatpravṛttiḥ| ihāpyastyeva sṛṣṭyā brahmaṇo līlārūpaṃ prayojanaṃ rājña iva bālasyeva cākṣadyūtādinā, "krīḍā hareridaṃ sarvaṃ kṣaramityupadhāryatāṃ| krīḍato bālaksyeva ceṣṭāstasya niśāmaye"tyādi bhagavatparāśaravacanāt| na caivaṃ sati brahmaṇo'vāptasamastakāmatvavirodhaḥ| yadyadepsati tattadā'vāptuṃ sāmarthyaṃ hyavāptasamastakāmatvaṃ sārvabhaumādisādhāraṇaṃ, na tu sarvadā sarvakāmāvāptimatvam| śītoṣṇādikālabhedena suranaratiryagā ca viṣamasṛṣṭhyā vaiṣamyanairghṛṇyadoṣāpattiḥ, "puṇyo vai puṇyena karmaṇā bhavati pāpaḥ pāpene"ti śruteḥ| karmaviśeṣamūlaṃ jagadvaiṣamyamīśvarastadutpattau nimitamātraṃ sādhāraṇakāraṇam| yathā sasyotpattau parjanyassādhāraṇakāraṇam|
