tattaccharīrakātparamātmano jagadutpadyate| īkṣaṇaliṃgāt| na ca śrutibādhaḥ| carācaraprapañcavācināṃ sarveṣāṃ śabdānāṃ tattaccharīrake paramātmani mukhyatvāt| loke "brāhmaṇo'dhīte, brāhmaṇo vidvā" nityādiprayogeṣu, vede "brāhmaṇamupanayīta, brāhmaṇo yajete"tyādiprayogeṣu ca śarīravāciśabdānāṃ śarīriparyantatvasya vyutpattisiddhatvāt| sarvasya carācarasya paramātmaśarīratāyā antaryāmibrāhmaṇena, "anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇī"ti tejaādyanupraveśapūrvakanāmarūpavyākaraṇaśrutyācāvagamāt| na ca sarvaśarīratvaśravaṇamaupacārikaṃ, tejaādisṛṣṭyanantaraṃ nāmarūpavayākaraṇārthamevānupraveśaśravaṇāt "tatsṛṣṭvā tadevānuprāviśadi"ti śrutyantarācceti vācyaṃ| sarvagatasyeśvarasya kvacidasataḥ paścātpraveśāsaṃbhavena praveśaśrutīnāṃ niyaṃtṛtayā sārvadikasattapritāpādanaparatvāt| tasmāttejaḥ pṛbhṛtiśabdānāṃ paramātmani mukhyatvāt "tatteja aikṣate" tyādibhiḥ paramātmana eva sarvasmin prapaṃce sākṣātsraṣṭṛtvamavasīyate| "etasmājjāyata"ityādyātharvaṇamaṃtreṇāpi tathā'vasīyate| ayaṃ hi maṃtro'vyaktamahadahaṃkārādikāryeṣūttarottaraḥ pūrvapūrva smādutpadyata iti putra pautranyāyānusāriṇaḥ kramādvaiparītyena sarvaṃ paramātmana evotpadyata iti sarvasya paramātmānantaryarūpo yaḥ kramaḥ tatraiva svārasikaḥ| sa ca tattaccharīrakāt brahmaṇassākṣājjagatsṛṣṭerevopapadyate "etasmājjāyata" ityatra sākṣāttvasya mukhyatvenautsargikatvāt| nanvayaṃ maṃtraḥ prāṇasya bhūtānāṃ ca madhye cakṣurādīni manaścotpadyanta ityevaṃrūpakramatātparyakaḥ| khādipṛthivyantānāṃ kramāmnānaliṃgāt| na tu sarvasya prāṇādeḥ paramātmana utpattipara iti cet - na| indriyamanaḥkhādīnāmapi "etasmājjāyata" iti sākṣāt paramātmasṛṣṭatayopāttaprāṇāviśeṣāt| na hi prāṇa eva sākṣājjāyate anye pautrādinyāyena paraṃparayā brahmaṇo jāyanta iti viśeṣaśravaṇamasti| natvayaṃ maṃtraḥ kramaparaḥ| kramastu khādiṣu śrutyaṃtarasiddhonūdyate| anyatra śrutyaṃtaraviruddho na gṛhyate| subālopaniṣadi spaṣṭaṃ kraṃmāmnānāt| tasmāt sarvaṃ jagat
sākṣāt paramātmana evotpadyata iti siddhaṃ||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
tejojalapṛthivīnāṃ mārutatejojalebhya utpattiḥ|
śrutyanurodhāt brahma tu mūlaṃ kūṭasthapuruṣaiva||
"tattejaaikṣate"tyādyāmnāyoktādabhidhyānāt|
sākṣādeva nidānaṃ teṣāṃ tattaccharīrakaṃ brahma||
evaṃ sa "tyetasmājjāyata" ityādivacanamupapannaṃ|
taddhyaviśeṣādeṣāṃ brahmānaṃtaryameva bodhayati||
tejaḥ pramukhāśśabdā loke'nyatra prasiddhimaṃtopi|
vyākaraṇaśrutyādergamyaṃte brahmaparyaṃtāḥ||
iti tejodhikaraṇam||
nātmā śruternityatvācca tābhyaḥ||18||
viyadādivajjīvopyutpadyate na veti ciṃtyate| utpadyata iti pūrva pakṣaḥ| "yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante, yena jātāni jīvanti, prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjate"tyādiśravaṇāt ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñānāt prāksṛṣṭerekatvāvadhāraṇācca| na ca "tatvamasī"ti jīvasya brahmābhedaśravaṇādutpattyanupapattiḥ| "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahme" tyādiśravaṇena viyadādīnāmapyanutpattiprasaṃgāt| naca "na jāyate mriyate vā vipaści"dityanena jīvotpattiniṣedhaśśaṃkanīyaḥ| vāyvākāśāmṛtatvavacanena tayorapyutapattiniṣedhāpatteḥ tasya dehotpattivināśānubaṃdhyutpattivināśābāvaparatvācca| "jīvāpetaṃ vāva kiledaṃ mriyate najīvo mriyata"iti dehamaraṇānubandhimaraṇaniṣedhaka śrutyantarānusāreṇāsyāpi tathaivābhiprāyāvagamācca| na ca kṛtahānākṛtābhyāgamaprasaṃgo doṣaḥ| sarvatra dravyaṃ nityaṃ tasya pūrvāvasthāprahāṇaṃ vināśaḥ avasthāṃtarāpattirutpattiriti siddhāṃtyabhimate pariṇāmavāde dravyātmanā nitye jīve tadaprasaṃgāt| naiyāyikamate ghaṭādīnāmiva jīvasyābhūtvā bhavanarūpotpattyabhyupagamo hi prasajyeta| tasmājjīvo viyadādivadutpadyata iti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
jīvo viyadādivannotpadyate| "na jāyate mriyate vāvipaścit, jñājñau dvāvajāvīśanīśā"vityādiṣu tadanutpattiśravaṇāt "ajo nityaśśāśvatoyaṃ purāṇo na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre, nityonityānāñcetanaścetanānām" ityādiśrutibhyaḥ tasya nityatvenotpattyabhāvāvagamācca| na ca "na jāyate mriyata" iti hi śrutiḥ dehotpattimaraṇānubaṃdhyutpattimaraṇābhāvamātrapareti vācyaṃ| tatraiva "na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre"iti dehahananānubandhihananā bhāvoktyaivārthasiddhasya tasya pṛthagavaktavyatvāt| prāgaviyatoviyadrūpeṇeva prāgajīvasya jīvarūpeṇa pariṇāmābhyupagame jīvakartṛkatvaniyatayoḥ puṇyapāpayorajīvāśritatvāsaṃbhavena kṛtahānākṛtābhyāgamaprasaṃgāparihārācca| sarvavijñānapratijñānamekatvāvadhāraṇañca sṛṣṭeḥ prāgjīvasya sūkṣmatāpattyā sṛṣṭau sthūlakāryatāpattyācopapadyate| kathaṃ tarhi vidādibhyo viśeṣaḥ| itthaṃ - viyadādīnāmasti svarūpānyathābhāva iti tena rūpeṇa kāryatvaṃ| jīvasya tvavikriyasya na svarūpānyathābhāvosti| kintu jñānākhyaguṇasaṃkocavikāsalakṣaṇa dharmānyathābhāvamātraṃ| tāvanmātreṇa tasya kāryatvamiti| idamuttarādhikaraṇe spaṣṭībhaviṣyati||
saṃgrahakārikā||
śruterjīvo'pi janimān maivaṃ tadajanuśruteḥ|
dharmajñānānyathābhāvaviṣayā tajjaniśrutiḥ||
iti ātmādhikaraṇam||3||

pṛthagupadeśāt||28||
tadguṇasāratvāttadvyapadeśaḥ prājñavat||29||
yāvadātmabhāvitvācca na doṣastaddarśanāt||30||
puṃstvādivattvasya sato'bhivyaktiyogāt||31||
nityo'palabdhyanupalabdhiprasaṃgonyataraniyamovānyathā||32||
jīvotpattivicāraprasaṃgāt tasya jñātṛtvādidharmaścityate jñānarūpatva jñātṛtvādiviṣayaśrutivirodhasamādhānena jagatkāraṇabrahmaviṣayaśrutīnāṃ nirvicikitsaprāmāṇyasidhyarthaṃ| api ca jīvasya dharmānyathābhāvarūpo vikāraḥ, na svarūpānyatābhāvarūpaityetadayuktaṃ| svarūpātirekeṇa jñānākhyadharmābhāvāt| jñānameva hi tasya svabhāvaḥ ityutthānādākṣepikī saṃgatirapyanusaṃdheyā| kiṃ jñānasvarūpo jīvaḥ utajaḍa evāgaṃtukajñānaḥ, athavā nityameva jñānaguṇādhāratayā svatojñāteti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - "vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute karmāṇi tanute'pica| yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan vijñānādaṃtaraḥ, jñānasvarūpamatyaṃtanirmalaṃ paramārthata" ityādiśrutismṛtibhyo jñānasvarūpaevetyekaḥ pūrvapakṣaḥ| aparastu jīvasya jñānasvarūpatve tasya sarvagatasya sarvadā sarvaviṣayopalabdhiprasaṃgena cakṣurādivaiyarthyaprasaṃgena ca jñānavikalasuṣuptimūrchādyabhāvaprasaṃgena ca tasya jñānarūpatvaṃ nayuktaṃ| ataeva siddhāṃtyabhimataṃ nityajñānādhāratayā jñātṛtvamapyayuktaṃ| kintu naiyāyikādimatarītyā cakṣurādikaraṇādhīnajñānavattvamevābhyupeyamiti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
"atha yovededaṃ jighrāṇīti sa ātmā, manasaitān kāmān paśyan ramate, napaśyo mṛtyuṃ paśyati, jānātyevāyaṃ puruṣaḥ, eṣahi draṣṭā śrotā ghrātā rasayite"tyādiśrutibhyaḥ jñātaivāyamātmā| nacaivaṃ sati sarvadā draṣṭā śrotā ghrātā ramayite"tyādiśrutibhyaḥ jñātaivāyamātmā| nacaivaṃ sati sarvadā sarvaviṣayopalabdhiprasaṃgaḥ| tasyāṇutvāt| aṇutvaṃca utkrāṃtigatyāgatiśravaṇāt| "tena pradyotenaiṣa ātmā viṣkrāmati, yaivaikecāsmāllokātprayaṃti candramasameva te sarve gacchanti, tasmāllokātpunaraityasmai lokāya karmaṇa"iti hi tasyotkrāntigatyāgatayaśśrūyante| nacaitāsāṃ vibhutve śakyamupapādanaṃ| nanu "yoyaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇeṣvi"ti prāṇasambandhinaṃ jīvaṃ prastutya "savāeṣa mahānajaātme"ti mahatvāmnānānnāṇuriti cenna| tatra jīvāditarasya brahmaṇodhikārāt| upakrame jīvaprastāvepi madhye "yasyānuvittaḥ pratibuddhaātme"ti parapratipādanāt, tadanubandhitvācca mahatvāmnānasya| kiñca "eṣoṇurātmā cetasā veditavyāḥ yasmin prāṇaḥ paṃcadhā saṃviveśe"ti jīvasya sākṣādaṇutvaṃ śrūyate| "vālāgraśatabhāgasya śatadhā kalpitasya ca| bhāgo jīvassavijñeya" iti "ārāgraśatabhāgasya" iti ca tasyālpaparamāṇatvaṃ śrūyate| evaṃ sati śarīraikavartino jīvasya kathaṃ sarvāṃgīṇasukhādyanubhava iti cet - caṃdanavaditi brūmaḥ| yathā haricandanabiṃdurdehaikadeśa vartyapi sakaladehavyāpinamāhlādaṃ janayati, tathaikadeśasthitopi jīvassarvāṃgīṇasukhamanubhavati| "yoyaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇeṣu hṛdyaṃtarjyotiḥ, hṛdihyayamātme"tyādiśrutibhiḥ tasyāpi hṛdayadeśe sthityabhyupagamāt| vastutastu - jīvo hṛdi sthitopi svaguṇena jñānena sakaladehaṃ vyāpnoti, yathāmaṇirdyumaṇirvā ekadeśavartibhiḥ svālekairanekadeśaṃ| naca jñānasvarūpaevātmā najñānaguṇakaiti śaṃkanīyaṃ| yathā gandhasya pṛthivīguṇatvenopalabhyamānasya tato vyatirekaḥ tathā
jānāmīti ātmaguṇatvenānubhūyamānasya jñānasya jīvādvyatirekāt| "jānātyevāyaṃpuruṣa" iti śrutyāpi tasya jñātṛguṇatvapradarśanāt, "na hi vijñāturviparilopovidyata" iti śrutau jñātṛjñānayoḥ pṛthagupadeśācca| kathaṃ tarhi "vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanuta" ityādiṣu tasya jñānarūpatva vyavahāraḥ| jñānaguṇasāratvāt| jñānaṃ hi tasya sārabhūto guṇaḥ| tataśca yathā brahmaṇaḥ prājñasya jñānamānandaśca sārabhūto guṇa iti "satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma, ānando brahmeti vyajānā"diti jñānāndavyapadeśaḥ, eva mihāpi jñānavyapadeśaḥ| nanu "satyaṃ jñāna"mityatra antodāttasvarabalāt jñānavatvamarthaḥ pratibhāti| kevalaṃ bhāvārthalyuḍantatāyāmaṃtodāttasvarānupapatteḥ| tathā hi - lyuḍādeśasya anaityasya "ādyudāttaśce"ti pratyayasvareṇa prathamākāre udāttelitīti litsvareṇa prakṛtyākārecodātte "anudāttaṃ padamekavarja"miti śeṣanighātasvareṇa pratyayasthayorakārayoranudāttayoḥ "ekādeśa udāttenodātta"ityekādeśasvareṇa prakṛtyākārapratyayādyakārayoḥ ekādeśecodātte sati pratyayāntyākārasyānudāttasya
vibhaktyakārasya ca "anudāttau suptiṣā"viti sup svareṇa anudāttasya "amipūrva" ityekādeśo bhavan "sthāno'ntaratama" iti paribhāṣayānudāttassyāt| tasya cānudāttasya "udāttādanudāttasya ca svarita" iti svaritādeśo bhavet| ata eva tat jñānamiti padaṃ kevalalyuḍantatve svaritāntaṃ syāt| yathā kāṭhake "saṃjñānaṃ vijñānaṃ prajñāna"miti| tasmādantodāttanirvāhārthamidaṃ jñānapadaṃ lyuḍantaraṃ matvarthīyāc pratyayāntaṃ vācyaṃ| arśāderākṛtigaṇatvāt| tathāsatyaṃtodāttasvaraupapadyate| arśāṃdyacpratyaye "yasyeti ce"ti prakṛtāvaṃtyasya akārasya lope "cita" iti citsvareṇa matvarthīṃyapratyayasyodāttatve ca sati tasya vibhaktyakārasya caikādeśasyāpi "ekādeśaudāttenodātta" ityudāttasyaiva bhāvāt| evamiha jñānapadaprakriyāśrayaṇe jñānavatvamevātmanassidhyet| na jñānarūpatvaṃ| tasmātjñānarūpatvamanena na pratīyataiti ceducyate - auṇādikanapratyayāṃtatvāśrayaṇe tadapi pratibhāti| jñādhātorāhatya na pratyayavidhānābhāve'pi "uṇādayo bahula"miti bahulagrahaṇena yato vihitāstato'nyatrāpi te bhavantītyetadarthena tatsiddheḥ| auṇādikapratyayāṃtatve bhāvārthatvamapyupapadyate| artyādisūtravihitamanpratyayasvareṇodāttatve sati tasya vibhaktyakāreṇa bhavataekādeśasyāpyudāttasyaiva bhāvāt| evamubhayatheha jñānapadaprakriyāśrayaṇasaṃbhavepi prakriyālāghavādauṇādikanapratyayāṃtatayā bhāvārthatvameva yuktamityabhipretya bhāṣyakārairjñānasvarūpatvepramāṇatayedamudāhṛtaṃ| jñānasya yāvadātmabhāvitvādapi tena tasya vyapadeśaḥ| khaṃḍādīnāṃ yāvatsvarūpabhāvigotvādidharmavācigavādiśabdena vyapadeśadarśanāt| jīvasya svaprakāśatvādapi jñānavyapadeśaupapadyate| svaprakāśatvamapi svāpekṣameva natu parāpekṣamapi| svāpekṣamapi ahamitipratyaktvaikatvaviśiṣṭatāmātreṇa| svaparaprakāśatvaṃ tu dharmajñānasyaiva| soyaṃ prabhāprabhāvannyāyodharmadharmijñānayoḥ| yadāhuḥ - "svasyaiva bhāsakodīpaḥ svātmano'nyasya ca prabhā| evaṃbhedosti sāmyepi jñānayordharmadharmiṇo"riti| nanu suṣuptyādiṣu jñānābhāvāt kathaṃ jñānasya gotvādivat svarūpānubandhidharmatvamiti cet| puṃstvādivadabhivyaktyeti brūmaḥ - yathā saptadhātumaye śarīre sadāsannapi puṃstvarūpasaptamadhātuḥ na bālye yauvanaivābhivyajyate, evaṃ sadāsanneva jñānaguṇaḥ suṣuptau jāgaraiva nābhivyajyate| tadānīmapi svayameva prakāśamānatārhatvarūpasya svayaṃprakāśatvasya na hāniḥ| ahamarthasya tu suṣuptāvapi prakāśamānatā'styeva| "sukhamahamasvāpsa"miti parāmarśāt| yadāhuḥ - "pratyaksvayaṃprabhasukhe nitye puṃsi vyavasthite| asvāpsaṃ sukhamityādau kālādyaṃśe'numāsthitiḥ|| agāyaṃ madhuraṃ madamayāmityevamādiṣu| mādhuryādivadatrāpi sukhaṃ bhāti tadātana"miti| tasmājjñānaguṇassaṃkocavikāsaśālī, taddharmī jīvastvaṇurityasmatpakṣe na kaściddoṣaḥ| kintu "jñānasvarūpojīvaḥ saca sarvagata" iti pakṣaeva nityopalabdhyanupalabdhiprasaṃgonyataraniyamovā syāt| tathāhi - sarvagatajñānarūpaṃ jīvasvarūpaṃ kimupalabdherevahetuḥ, uta anupalabdhereva, athāvāubhayorapi| ādye dehāvacchinnapradeśa iva anyatrāpi tadupalabdhissyāt| dvitīye dehāvacchinnapradeśepi tadupalabdhirnasyāt| tṛtīye sadopalabdhyanupalabdhī parasparaviruddhe syātāṃ| sarvagatojīvaścakṣurādikaraṇāyattagantukajñānaiti naiyāyikādipakṣepyayaṃdoṣassamānaḥ| ayamarthaḥ "aṃśonānāvyapadeśā"dityadhikaraṇe sphūṭībhaviṣyati|
saṃgrahakārike||
jīvaṃ vijñānarūpaṃ katicidabhidadhuśśrūyate'sau tatheti
jñānairāgantukaistaṃ katicadatha punarjñātṛtābhājamūcuḥ||
nityajñātṛtvamanye tadiha samudito madhyamastarkapakṣaḥ
jānīyāddūradeśe sthitamapi hi vibhurjīvaeṣo'nyathā te||
jānātyevāyamityādyupaniṣadudito jñātṛrūpassanityaṃ
sarvajñastāvatāsyāditi na yadaṇurutkrāṃtigatyādimānaiḥ|
dehavyāptistu dīpadyutisadṛśaguṇajñānatastasya yuktā
jñānatvoktissasāroguṇaiti ghaṭate svaprakāśassaceti||
iti jñādhikaraṇam||
kartā śāstrārthavattvāt||33||
upādānādvihāropadeśācca||34||
vyapadeśācca kriyāyāṃ na cennirdeśaviparyayaḥ||35||
upalabdhivadaniyamaḥ||36||
śaktiviparyayāt||37||
samādhyabhāvācca||38||
yathāca takṣobhayathā||39||
jñātṛtvanirūpaṇānantaraṃ kartṛtvaṃ nirūpyeta jñānādhīnatvāt kṛteriti kāryakāraṇabhāvassaṅgatiḥ| jñānasvarūpassarvagato jīva ityatra upalabdhiniyamādivyavasthityasiddhivat prakṛteḥ kartṛtve tasyāssarvasādhāraṇatvāt kartṛniyamavyavasthityasiddhivat prakṛteḥ kartṛtve tasyāssarvasādhāraṇatvāt kartṛniyamavyavasthityasiddhirucyata iti dṛṣṭāntasaṅgatiśca| kiṃ prakṛtirevakartrīti tadīyakartṛtvādhyāsena jīvasya kartṛtvavyahāraḥ uta svabhāvataeva jīvaḥ karteti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - akartaivātmā| kaṭhavallīṣu "hantācenmanyate hantuṃ hataścenmanyate hatam| ubhau tau na vijānīto nāyaṃ hanti na hanyata" iti śravaṇāt| hantuṃuhantāram| "jantu" mityādāviva auṇādikastun pratyayaḥ| "ya enaṃ vetti hantāraṃ yaścainaṃ manyate hatam| ubhau tau na vijānīto nāyaṃ hanti na hanyata" iti gītāvacanaikārthyāt| hananakartṛtvapratiṣedhaśca kartṛtvamātrapratiṣedhopalakṣaṇam| ata eva gītāsu bhagavadvacanaṃ - "prakṛteḥ kriyamāṇāni guṇaiḥ karmāṇi sarvaśaḥ| ahaṅkāravimūḍhātmā kartā'hamiti manyate|| kāryakāraṇakartṛtve hetuḥ prakṛtirucyate| puruṣassukhaduḥkhānāṃ bhoktṛtve heturucyata" iti| ato jīvaḥ prakṛtigataṃ vā kartṛvaṃ tattajjīvāsādhāraṇaprakṛtipariṇāmabuddhigataṃ vā kartṛtvamātmanyadhyasyan karteti vyapadiśyate| śāstramapi tathābhūtameva kartṛtvamapekṣya pravartate sahopādānādivyavahāreṇeti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
buddheḥ prakṛtervā kartṛtve "yajeta svargakāmaḥ, mumukṣurbrahmopāsīte"tyevamādiśāstraṃ svargamokṣādiphalakāmaṃ bhoktārameva niyuṅkte| "śāstra phalaṃ prayoktarī"ti nyāyāt| kiṃ ca śāstreṇa kartā bodhajananadvārā niyujyate| na ca acetanayoḥ buddhiprakṛtyoḥ bodhanīyatvamasti, ato bhoktṛtvāt bodhanīyatvācca ātmaiva vidhiniṣedhaśāstraniyojya iti yuktam| na ca kūṭastha ātmā'haṅkarteti parakīyaṃ kartṛtvamātmanyadhyasitumapi prabhavati| tasmādadhyāso'pyātmaprabhāsthānīyadharmajñānapariṇāmatayā taddharmatāṃ netavya iti tathaiva kṛtirapi taddharmatāṃ nīyatām| kimarthaṃ bādhānupalambhe'pi "ahaṃ karte"ti pratibhāsasya bhrāntirūpatvaṃ, śāstrasya bhrāṃtikṛtakartṛtvālamvanatvañca, kalpyate| "hantā cenmanyate hantu"miti maṃtrastu "na jāyate mriyate vā vipaści"diti mantre "na hanyate hanyamāne'pi deha" iti dehātiriktasya ātmano hananāyogyatvena prakṛtatvāttadviṣayatayopapadyate| kartṛtvapratiṣedhakāni| "tatraivaṃ sati kartāramātmānaṃ kevalaṃ tu yaḥ| "paśyatyakṛtabuddhitvānna sapaśyati durmati"riti tadīyavacanāṃtareṇaikārthyāt| kiñca "sayathā mahārājo jānapadān gṛhītvā sve janapade yathākāmaṃ parivartetaivamevaiṣa etān prāṇān gṛhītvā sve śarīre yathākāmaṃ parivartata" ityupādānavihāropadeśadarśanāt, "vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute karmāṇi tanute'pi ce"ti laukikavedikakriyāsu kartṛtvavyapadeśācca, ātmā kartaiva| naca vijñāna śabdo'ntaḥkaraṇaparaḥ| antaḥ karaṇanirdeśe
śrutiṣu tṛtīyāyāeva darśanāt| ihāpi tathā nirdeśaviparyayaprasaṅgāt| "tadeṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ vijñānena vijñānamādāye"tyādivākyāntareṣu antaḥ karaṇaparasya vijñānaśabdasya tṛtīyānirdeśo hi dṛśyate| kiṃ ca prakṛteḥ kartṛtve tasyāssarvapuruṣasādhāraṇatvāt pūrvādhikaraṇoktopalabdhyādiniyamāsambhavarītyā karmaniyamasyāpyasambhavāt| tathā ca sarvāṇi karmāṇi sarveṣāṃ bhogāya syuḥ| ātmanāṃ sāṃkhyamate vibhutvena sarvatra sarveṣāṃ sannidhānāviśeṣāt| na ca antaḥ karaṇasya kartṛtvapakṣe nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, antaḥ karaṇānāṃ pratiśarīraṃ bhinnatvāditi vācyam| teṣāṃ tadāyatanaśarīrāṇāñca sarvātmasannidhāvutpadyamānānāṃ pratyātmavyavasthiterupapādayitumaśakyatvāt| buddheḥ kartṛtvapakṣe ca karturanyasya bhoktṛtvānupapatteḥ bhoktṛtvamapi tasyā eva syāditi ātmano bhoktṛtvaśaktirnasyāt| tathā ca "puruṣo'sti bhoktṛbhāvā"diti teṣāmeva abhyupagamavirodhaḥ| buddheḥ kartṛtve ca "prakṛteranyo'smī"tyevaṃrūpamokṣasādhanasamādhikartṛtvamapi tasyā eva vācyam| na ca prakṛtiḥ svarūpeṇa buddhipariṇatirūpeṇa vā prakṛteranyaḥ kūṭastho'smīti samādhātuṃ prabhavati| kiṃca ātmanaḥ kartṛtvābhyupagame satyāmicchāyāṃ karoti, anyadā na karoti| yathā takṣā vāsyādikaraṇasampanno'pi satyāmevecchāyāṃ karoti nānyadeti niyama upapadyate| buddhestu acetanāyāḥ kartṛtve tasyā bhogecchādiniyamakāraṇābhāvāt sarvadā kartṛtvaṃ syāt| tasmādātmaiva svabhāvataḥ karteti yuktam||
sa na kartā guṇānāṃ hi kartṛtvaṃ vibhunā smṛtam|
maivaṃ śāstrārthavatvādyaiḥ kartā sa guṇayogataḥ||
iti kartradhikaraṇam||
parāttu tacchaØteḥ||33||
kṛtaprayatnāpekṣastu vihitapratiṣiddhāvaiyarthyādibhyaḥ||34||
kartṛtve cintite tadviśeṣaścintyata iti saṅgatiḥ| jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ syāttaṃ, uta paramātmāyattamiti vidhiniṣedhaśāstrāntaryāmiśāstrābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - svāyattameva jīvasya kartṛtvam| anyathā vidhiniṣedhaśāstrayoratyantavaiyarthyaṃ syāt| na ca "antaḥ praviṣṭaśśāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā, eṣahyeva sādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamebhyo lokebhya unninīṣate eṣa u evāsādhukarma kārayati taṃ yamadho ninīṣata" ityādyantaryāmiśāstrāt paramātmāyattaṃ kartṛtvamiti śaṅkanīyam| tathāsati puruṣapravṛttinivṛttyaidaṃparyeṇa pravṛttānāṃ vidhiniṣedhaśāstrāṇāṃ atyantavaiyarthyāpatteḥ, antaryāmiśāstrasyāpyasāmañjasyaprasaṅgācca| na hi kaścit svayameva kiṃcidaniṣṭaṃ karma kārayitvā tatphalaṃ nigrahamapi karotīti yuktamiti| evaṃprāpte siddhāntaḥ|
paramātmāyattamasya kartṛtvam| antaryāmiśāstrabṛndābādhasambhave tadbādhanāyogāt| sambhavati ca tadabādhena vidhiniṣedhaśāstrāvaiyarthyopapādanam| ādyapṛvṛttau bhagavānudāsīnaḥ dvitīyādipravṛttiṣvanumantā svabhaktānāṃ svadveṣiṇañca sveṣṭāniṣṭakarmasu kārayiteti bhagavato'vasthātrayasatvāt| tathā hi - ādau jīvasya karaṇakalebarādi datvā kimayaṃ kariṣyatīti paśyannudāsīno bhagavānāste| sa yatkaroti puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ vā tatphalarūpaṃ dvitīyādipravṛttimanumanyate| pravṛttapravartanamanumatiḥ| yattu mumukṣūṇāṃ prapadanopāsanādikaṃ sādhukarma, yacca tāmasānāṃ bhagavadveṣiṇāṃ bhagavannindādirūpamasādhukarma, tadubhayaṃ svayaṃ kārayati tadviṣayaṃ "eṣahyebe"tyādiśāstradvayam| na sarvaviṣayam| teṣāṃ satatayuktānāṃ bhajatāṃ prītipūrvakaṃ| dadāmi buddhiyogantaṃ yena māmupayānti ta"iti tānahaṃ dviṣataḥ krūrān saṃsāreṣu narādhamān| kṣipāmyajasramaśubhānāsurīṣveva yoniṣu" itica bhagavadvacanātū tatonyatravidhiniṣedhaśāstrāvakāśaḥ tannātyantavaiyarthyamiti kecidācāryāḥ tadvaiyarthyaṃ samādadhuḥ|
anyetvāhuḥ - naivamantaryāmiśāstrasya viṣayasaṅkocaḥ kalpanīyaḥ| bhagavato jīvakarmaviśeṣeṣu audāsīnyānumantṛtvakalpane pramāṇābhāvāt udāhṛtagītāvacanayoḥ karmāntarakārayitṛtvābhāvaparyantakalpane kalpakābhāvāt| na ca vidhiniṣedhaśāstrāvaiyarthyārthaṃ tathākalpanam| sarveṣu vihitaniṣiddhakarmasu īśvarasya kālādṛṣṭendriyādivat kāraṇatve'pi jīvasya kartṛtvākṣateḥ tadicchādīnāmiveśvarasyāpi kāraṇatvopapatteḥ| na ca
tathāsaṃti jīvasya vihitaniṣiddhakarmasu "svatantraḥ karte"ti lakṣitaṃ kartṛtvaṃ nasyāt, balādīśvarasya kārayitṛtvena nadīrayākṛṣyamāṇapuruṣasyeva svātaṃtryahāniriti vācyam| prayoyojakena rājñā niyuktānāṃ prayojyakatarṝṇāmamātyādīnāmapi svakarmasu svecchayā pravartamānānāṃ svātaṃtryadarśanāt| tasmādantaryāmiśāstrāsaṅkocena vidhipratiṣedhāvaiyarthyopapādanāsambhavāt na tatsaṅkocaḥ kalpya iti| matadvayasyāpyupapādanaprapañcastattadgrantheṣu draṣṭavyaḥ||
saṅgrahakārikāḥ||
kartṛtā paravatī śarīriṇāmīśvareṇa yadi nāma varṇyate|
vyarthatā vidhiniṣedhaśāstrayośśakyate śamayituṃ na kenacit||
asti tāvadaraviṃdalocanaśśāsitā yamayiteti ca śrutiḥ|
sā yathā vidhiniṣedhalaṅghanaṃ nādadhīta nayatastathocyate||
datvādau karaṇakalebarādi paśyatyudyogaṃ prathamamudāsitaiva devaḥ|
paścāttatphalamanumanyate dvitīyaṃ keṣāṃcidbhavati kṛtau prayojako'pi||
antaryāmiśruteritthamanyavaiyarthyaśaṅkayā|
saṅkocamūcire kecit mumukṣudveṣikarmasu||
icchādyadhīnatāvatsyāt īśvarādhīnatā'pinaḥ|
tāvatā śāstravaiyarthyaṃ netyasaṅkocanaṃ pare||
iti parāyattādhikaraṇam||
aṃśonānāvyapadeśādanyathācāpi dāśakitayādivatvamadhīyataeke||35||
maṃtravarṇāt||36||
apica smaryate||37||
prakāśādivattu naivaṃ paraḥ||38||
smaranti ca||39||
anujñāparihārau dehasambandhājjyotirādivat||40||
asantateśvāvyatikaraḥ||41||
ābhāsaeva ca||42||
adṛṣṭāniyamāt||43||
abhisaṃdhyādiṣvapi caivam||44||
pradeśabhedāditi cennāntarbhāvāt||45||
jīveśvarayoḥ pravartyapravartakabhāve rājabhṛtyayoriva atyantabhedassyāditi jīvasya paramātmāṃśatvakathanamapyaupacārikaṃ syāditi śaṃkayā saṃgatiḥ| kiṃ jīvaḥ parasmādatyaṃtabhinnaḥ, utājñānāvasthāpannaṃ parameva brahma, yadvā brahmaivopādhyavacchinnaṃ, athavā viśiṣṭasya brahmaṇoviśeṣaṇarūpoṃśaiti śrutivipratipattessaṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - atyaṃtabhinnojīvaḥ| "jñājñaudvā"vityādibhedanirdeśāt| ajñaṃbrahmaivavā jīvaḥ| "tatvamasi, ayamātmābrahme"tyadiśravaṇāt| brahmaiva satvopādhyavacchinnaṃ vā jīvaḥ| tatvamasyādiśravaṇādeva| na tu brahmaṇoṃśojīvaiti yuktaṃ| aṃśatvaṃ hi tantvādivannāvayavatvarūpaṃ| niravayavasya brahmaṇo'vayavārabdhatvābhāvāt| ataeva ṭaṅkacchinnapāṣāṇaśakalavadapi nāṃśatvam| brahmaṇaḥ khaṇḍanānarhatvācceti|| siddhāntastu - 
brahmaṇoṃ'śa eva jīvaḥ| nānāvyapadeśāt| abhedavyapadeśācca| sraṣṭṝtvasṛjyatvaniyantṛtvaniyāmyatva sarvajñatvakiṃcidjñatvasvādhīnatvaparādhīnatvapatitvaśeṣatvādibhirbhedavyapadeśo hi bhūyān dṛśyate| tathaiva abhedavyapadeśo'pi tatvamasyādirdṛśyate| api ca ātharvaṇikāḥ brahmasūkte brahmaṇodāśakitavādirūpatvamapyadhīyate|
"brahmadāśā brahmadāsā brahme me kitavā uta| strīpuṃsau brahmaṇo jātau striyo brahmā'thavā pumā"niti| tasmādviśiṣṭabrahmaṇo viśeṣaṇarūpoṃśo jīvaityeva abhyupagantavyam| evaṃ satyeva bhedābhedaśrutīnāṃ baṃdhamokṣavyavasthādīnāñca upapattiḥ| satyamithyopādhyavacchinnabrahmajīvavādayostu bandhamokṣavyavasthādyanupapattiḥ, sarvaśrutisāmañjasyānupapattiśca anyatra prapañcitā| ato viśiṣṭaikadeśarūpoṃśo jīvaityeva yuktam| "pādo'sya viśvābhūtāni, tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divī"ti maṃtravarṇāt, "mamaivāṃśo jīvaloke jīvabhūtassanātana" iti gītāvacanācca evamevāvagamyate| evañca yathā prakāśo maṇyādīnāṃ prakāśavatāṃ viśeṣaṇāṃśaḥ, yathā vā dehināṃ devamanuṣyādidehaḥ, tathaiva apṛthaksiddhaprakāratayā śarīratayā ca īśvarasya jīvo viśeṣaṇa rūpoṃśaḥ| na tu saeva saiti| viśeṣaṇabhedāt bhedaśrutayaḥ viśeṣṭarūpaikyādabhedaśrutayaścopapadyante| yathābhūto jīvaśśeṣatvādilakṣaṇaḥ naivaṃbhūtaḥ paraśśeṣatvādilakṣaṇa iti prabhāprabhāvannyāyena śarīraśarīribhāvena ca jīveśvarayorvyavasthitaśeṣaśeṣibhāvādayaśca upapadyante| smaranti ca parāśarādayaḥ jīveśvarayoḥ prabhāprabhāvannyāyaṃ śarīraśarīribhāvañca, "ekadeśasthitasyāgnerjyotsnāvistāriṇī yathā| parasya brahmaṇaśśaktistathedamakhilañjagat|| yatkiñcitsṛjyate yena satvajātena vai dvija| tasya sṛjyasya saṃbhūtau tatsarvaṃvai harestānu" riti| nanu sarveṣāmīśvarāṃśatvāviśeṣe kathaṃ dvijānāṃ vedādhyayanādyanujñā, śūdrāṇāṃ tatparihāra iti cet, dehaviśeṣasambandhāditi brūmaḥ| yathā'gnyudakādīnāṃ śrotriyāgārasthitidaśāyāṃ karmārhatvaṃ, teṣāmeva śmaśānadeśaṃ prāptānāṃ karmānarhatvaṃ, evamātmanāṃ vividhadehasthitidaśāyāmanujñāparihāradvayamupapadyate| sarveṣāmātmanāṃ brahmāṃśatvenaikarūpye'pi teṣāmanyonyabhedāt aṇutvena pratiśarīraṃ bhinnatvācca bhogavyatikaro'pi na bhaviṣyati| kintu brahmātmaikyavāda eva jīvaparayorjīvānāñca saprasajyate| na ca avidyākṛtopādhibhedāt tadaprasaṅgaḥ| akhaṇḍaikarasaprakāśamātrasvarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpatirodhāyakopādhibhedopapādakahetūnāmābhāsatvāt| na khalu dārukhaṇḍe nipatan kuṭhāro dārukhaṇḍamiva brahmaṇi samanvayannupādhirbrahma bhinatti| upādhayo bhinnā iti cet| kiṃ tataḥ? bhogānāśrayāṇāṃ teṣāṃ bhedena bhogāśrayaikyādāpatatsāṅkaryaṃ kathaṃ parihriyeta| satyamithyopādhyupahita brahmajīvavādayorupādhihetubhūtānādyadṛṣṭavaśāt vyavasthā bhaviṣyatīti cet, na|
upādhibhirivādṛṣṭairapi akhaṇḍe brahmaṇi chedanādyasaṃbhavāt| etena adṛṣṭahetusaṅkalpānuṣṭhānaśarīrādipratiniyamāt vyavasthetyāśaṅkā'pi nirastā| saṅkalpādīnāmapi sarvātmasananidhāvatpadyamānānāṃ pratyātmaniyamahetvabhāvāt| nanu ātmapradeśanavyavasthayā bhogavyavasthā'stviti cet na|brahmaṇaḥ pradeśāśśarīrāntarbhūtāeva bhogāśrayāvācyāḥ| tadantarbhūtapradesāstu na vyavasthitāḥ| prāyaḥ sarveṣāmapi pradeśānāṃ sañcaratsu sarveṣu śarīreṣvantarbhāvāt| tasmātsarvagatātmapakṣa iva satyamitopādhyupahitātmaikya pakṣe'pi na bhogavyavasthopapadyata iti ātmanāmaṇutvavat bhedo'pyabhyupagantavya iti siddham||
saṃgrahakārikā||
jīvasyātyantabhede gatirupacaraṇaṃ syādabhedaśrutīnāṃ
gāḍhābhede tu bhedaśrutitatisahitassaṅkaro durnivāraḥ|
tasmādaṃśaḥparasyetyucitamiha sakhalvekavastvekadeśo
viṃkhyāto'yaṃ viśiṣṭe bhagavatica bhavatyekadeśaḥ prakāraḥ||
iti aṃśādhikaraṇam||7||

gauṇyasaṃbhavāttatprākchaØteśca||2||
tatpūrvakatvādvācaḥ||3||
jīvotpattitadanubadhijñātṛtvādinirūpaṇānaṃtaraṃ jīvopakaraṇānāmiṃdriyāmāmutpattiprakāro viśodhyata iti saṃgatiḥ| kimiṃdriyāṇāṃ viyadādivat svarūpānyathābhāvarūpotpattiḥ, uta jīvavat dharmānyathābhāvamātrarūpeti śrutivipratipattessaṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - yadyapi viyadadhikaraṇe "brahmātiriktaṃ sarvamutpattima" diti nirṇītaṃ, tathāpīṃdriyāṇāṃ viyadādivat na svarūpānyathābhāvarūpotpattiḥ| "asadvā itamagraāsīt tadāhuḥ kiṃ tadāsīditi ṛṣayo vāva te agre sadāsīt ke te ṛṣaya iti prāṇāvāva ṛṣaya itī" ti, sargādyakāle'pi sthitiśravaṇāt prāṇaśabdasya bahuvacanāṃtasya iṃdriyeṣveva, "prāṇamanūtkrāmaṃtaṃ sarve prāṇā anūtkrāmaṃtī"ti prayogadarśanāt "tasyāsataṛṣayassaptatīra" iti ṛṣiśabdasyāpi teṣu dṛṣṭaprayogatvācca| yadyapi prāṇaśabdo brahmaṇyapi dṛṣṭaprayogaḥ, ṛṣiśabdaśca jñānavattvādupapannaḥ, tathā'pi bahuvacanāṃtayoranayostasminnanvayo na ghaṭate| na "aditiḥ pāśā" niti bahavacanavat iha bahuvacanaṃ avayavabahutvaviṣayatayā yojayituṃ śakyam| brahmaṇo niravayavatvāditi| siddhāntastu - 
brahmaṇa ekasyaiva sargādyakāle'vasthiteḥ sāvadhāraṇāsu bahuṣu śrutiṣu śravaṇāt, prāṇaśabdasya tasminnirūḍhisatvāt, ṛṣiśabdasya jñānasādhanendriyāpekṣayā jñānavati tatra svārasyācca, brahmaivātra prāṇāḥ| bahuvacanaṃ tu "guṇetvanyāyyakalpane" ti nyāyāt pāśabahutvavannetavyam| avayavābhāve'pi viśeṣaṇabahutvasya tadālambanasya sadbhāvāt| naca ekatvāvadhāraṇe'pi jīvavadiṃdriyāṇāmapi satvamastviti vācyam| kṛtahānākṛtābhyāgamaparihārāya adṛṣṭāśrayatayā jīvasyeva teṣāṃ tadā satvasyāvaktavyatvāt, udāhṛtaśruteḥ brahmaṇyeva svarasyasya darśitatvācca| sargādyakāle jīvakṛtyā dṛṣṭāvasthitivat vāgādīndriyakṛtyasya kasyāpyabhāvāt, bhūtasṛṣṭhyanaṃtarameva nāmarūpavyākaraṇaśravaṇena tataḥ prāgvāgādīṃdriyakṛtyānāṃ nāmoccāraṇādīnāmabhāvāvagamāt, "etasmājjāyata" iti maṃtre teṣāṃ viyadādisamānasvarūpānyathābhāvarūpajananaśravaṇācca||
saṃgrahakārikā||
agre satvātprāṇāḥ nityā iticenna sṛjyatvāt|
prāṇapadantadbrahmaṇi bahuvacanaṃ pāśavanneyaṃ||
iti prāṇādhikaraṇam||
saptagaterviśeṣitatvācca||4||
hastādayastu sthite'to naivaṃ||5||
viyadādivadutpattimattvena siddhānāmiṃdriyāṇāṃ saṃkhyāviśeṣaścityata iti saṃṅgatiḥ| "kimiṃdriyāṇi sapta uta ekādaśe" ti śrutivipratipattessaṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| saptaiva| iha paratra ca jīvena saha saptānāmeva hi gatiḥ śrūyate|
"sapta prāṇāḥ prabhavanti tasmāt saptārciṣassamidhassaptajihvāḥ| sapta ime lokā yeṣu caraṃti prāṇā guhāśayā nihitāssapta sapte"| vīpsā puruṣabhedābhiprāyā| viśeṣitāśca te gatimantaḥ prāṇāḥ svarūpanirdeśena - "yadā pañcāvatiṣṭhante jñānāni manasā saha| buddhiśca na viceṣṭeta tāmāhuḥparamāṃ gati"miti|atra hi jñānasādhanāni cakṣurādīni pañca manobuddhiśceti saptaivedaṃtayoṃdriyāṇi nirdiṣṭāni| paramāṃgatimiti paramagatirūpamokṣasādhanaṃ yogamāhurityarthaḥ| "yāni tvaṣṭau grahāḥ aṣṭāvatigrahāḥ sapta vai śīrṣaṇyāḥ prāṇāḥ dvāvavāñcau, nava vai puruṣe prāṇānābhirdaśamī, daśa vai paśoḥ prāṇā ātmaikadaśa" ityādyaṣṭādyadhikasaṃkhyāśravaṇaṃ tadanyeṣāmapi kiṃcitkiṃcijjīvopakārakatvamātreṇa iṃdriyatvopacārāt nābhivat| na hi nābhiraṃdhramiṃdriyam| iṃdriyāṇi tu sahagatimaṃti saptaiva| nyūnādhikasaṃkhyāśravaṇae ca nyūnasaṃkhyāgrahaṇaṃ nyāyyam| lāghavāt| adhikasaṃkhyāgrahaṇe nyūnasaṃkyānāṃ tatrāntarbhāvāt sarvābādho bhavatītyadhikasaṃkhyāgrahaṇameva nyāyyamiti cenna| tathāsati
vāgādipcagāhaṅkāracittābhyāṃ saha kvaciccaturdaśeṃdriyāṇi śrūyanta iti caturdaśasaṃkhyāgrahaṇāpattyā ekādaśeti niyamābhāvaprasaṅgāditi| siddhāntastu - 
hastādīnāmapi śarīrasthite jīve kāryabhedena tadbhogopakaraṇatvāt, jñāneṃdriyāṇi paṃca, karmeṃdriyāṇi paṃca, manaścetyekādaśeṃdriyāṇītyevaṃ maṃtavyam| ato "daśeme puruṣe prāṇā ātmaikādaśaekādaśamataśce"tyādiśrutismṛtisiddhendriyasaṃkhyā vyavasthitaiva| nānāvidhasaṃkhyāśravaṇe kāryānusārema saṃkhyāniyamanameva hi yuktaṃ| nānāvidhāvadānasādhanaśravaṇe sāmarthyānusāreṇāvadeyaniyamanamiva| tadadhikasaṃkhyāvādāstu manovṛttibhedābhiprāyāḥ| mahadanugṛhīte manaḥ adhyavasāyaṃ, ahaṅkārānugṛhītaṃ manaḥ abhimānaṃ, pūrvasaṃskārānugṛhītaṃ manaḥ cintāṃcakāroti| mana evādhyavasāyādirūpavṛttibhedopahitaṃ buddhyahaṅkāracittaśabdairabhidhīyata iti| nyūnasaṃkhyāśravaṇāni tu tatra tatra vivakṣitagamanādikāryaviśeṣābhiprāyāṇi| yogadaśāyāṃ karmendriyebhyo'pi jñānendriyāṇāṃ yatnena vaśīkartavyatvarūpamasti prādhānyaṃ tadabhiprāyā "yadā paṃcāvatiṣṭhaṃta" iti smṛtiḥ| "saptavai śīrṣaṇyāḥ prāṇā" iti saptānāṃ śīrṣaṇyatvarūpamasti prādhānyam, tadabhiprāyā "saptaprāṇā" iti śrutiḥ gatiśrutiśca| ekādaśānāmapi gatiśrutirastyeva| "daśeme puruṣe prāṇāḥ ātmaikādaśaḥ te yadā'smānmartyāccharīrādutkrāmantī" tyādikā| ato ekādaśendriyāṇīti siddham|| saṅgrahakārikā||
saptaprāṇā yuktāḥ saptānāmeva gatikathanāt|
maivaṃ saha hastādyaiḥ ekādaśakāryabhedātsyuḥ||
iti saptagatyadhikaraṇam||
aṇavaśca||6||
tānīndriyāṇi kiṃ vibhūni paricchinnāniveti saṃśaye "ta ete sarva eva samāḥ sarve'nantā" iti ānantyaśravaṇāt asyendriyasya idaṃ parimāṇamiti parimāṇaviśeṣānuktisahitāt vibhureveti prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
utkarāntigatyā gatiśravaṇāt, ānantyaśravaṇasya "sa yohaitānanantānupāste anantaṃ salokaṃ jayati" iti vākyaśeṣeṇa upāsanārthatvāvagamācca paricchinnānyeva| dūragatavastugrahaṇaṃ tu śīghragatimatvādinopapadyate||
saṃgrahakārikā||
te vibhavo'naṃtatvaśravaṇāt maivaṃ gatāgatotkramaṇaiḥ|
sūkṣmāste teṣāṃ punarupāsanāphalamanantatāśravaṇam||
śreṣṭhaśca||7||
indriyāṇāmutpattivicārānantaraṃ chandogānāṃ prāmasaṃvāde indriyaissaha vivādena labdhaśraiṣṭyasya mukhyaprāṇasya indriyaissaha vivādoktyaiva tatsamānacchāyatāvagamāt indriyotpattivicārānantaraṃ mukhyaprāṇotpattirvicāryata iti saṃgatiḥ| yadyapi "etasmājjāyate prāṇa" iti maṃtrata eva indriyāṇāmiva prāmasyāpi viyadādivadutpattissidhyati, tathāpi prāṇasya bhūtavāyoḥ indriyavṛttibhyaścānyatvaṃ śrutivirodhasamādhānena nirninīṣuḥ tasya prākū prakṛtotpattivicāreṇa prāsaṃgikatvasaṃpādanāya kiṃcidadhikaśaṃkānirākaraṇāya ca prāṇasyotpattiriha ciṃtyate| adhikaśaṃkātvitthaṃ - sargādyakāle jīvakāryamadṛṣṭāvasthānamiva vāgādīndriyakāryaṃ nāmadheyoccāraṇādikaṃ nāsti| ataḥ prāṇarṣi śabdau brahmaṇi dṛṣṭaprayogau brahmaparāvityuktaṃ| prāṇasyatu "nāsadāsīnnosadāsīttadānīṃ tama āsīttamasā gūḍhamagre praketa" mityupakrānte nāsadāsīye sūkte "ānīdavātaṃ svadhayātadeka"miti ananākhyāsādhāraṇavyāpārasadbhāvaśśrutaḥ| na ca ānīcchabdaḥ kvacidapi brahmaṇi dṛṣṭaprayomaḥ| na ca "abāta"miti viśeṣaṇāt ananākhyaprāṇavyāpāro nabhavatyānīcchabdaḥ| kintu brahmasattāpara iti vācyam| saṃvargavidyādiṣu vāyuprāṇayoḥ pṛthagupadeśena vāyvabhāve'pi prāṇasadbhāvopapatteriti|| tannivṛttistvitthaṃ - vāyostatvāṃtarābhāvena vāyuviśeṣātmako bhavatyeva prāṇaḥ| tasmāt "avāta"miti vāyusāmānyaniṣedhāt ānīcchabdasya prāṇavyāpāratvāyogena brahmaparatvameva vaktuṃ yuktaṃ| tathāca svena brahmaṇa dhīyate upabhujyata iti vyutpattyā svadhāśabdena lakṣmīrucyata iti lakṣmīviśiṣṭasya bhagavataḥ
parabrahmaṇassargādyasamaye sthitiruktā bhavatīti "śreṣṭhaśce"ti sūtraṃ "pauṣṇaṃ peṣaṇaṃ vikṛtau pratīyete"ti pūrvataṃtrasūtravat uttaracintārthatvāt uttarādhikaraṇaśeṣaeva| nādhikaraṇāntaramiti kecidāhuḥ|| iti śreṣṭhāṇutvādhikaraṇam||
na vāyukriye pṛthagupadeśāt||8||
cakṣurādivattu tatsahaśiṣṭhyādibhyaḥ||9||
akaraṇatvācca na doṣastathā hi darśayati||10||
paṃcavṛttirmanovadvyapadiśyate||11||
kimayaṃ mukhyaprāṇaḥ vāyumātraṃ. uta ucchvāsaniśvāsarūpā tatkriyā, athavā avasthāviśeṣāpanno vāyuriti saṃśaye "yaḥ prāṇassa vāyu"riti śravaṇāt vāyumātraṃ ucchvāsaniśvāsarūpā tatkriyāvā| tatra prāṇaśabdaprayogāditi pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - 
"etasmājjāyata" iti maṃtre pṛthagupadeśānnavāyumātram| vāyumātre prāṇaśabdāprayogācca| ata eva tadīyā niśvāsādikriyā'pi na bhavati| agnyādikriyāyā iva vāyukriyāyā api "etasmājjāyata" iti dravyotpattimaṃtre pṛthagutpattyupadeśānarhatvāt tasyābhāvārthapratyayāntaprāṇaśabdārhatve'pi prāṇitīti kartrarthapratyayāntasya prāṇiti anena jīvatīti karaṇārthapratyayāntasya vā tasyāṃ prayogābhāvāditi| nanu yadi vāyureva kiṃcidavasthāntaraṃ prāptaḥ prāṇaḥ, tarhi kimagniriva prāṇopi vāyutvaṃ parityajet, netyucyate| nāyamagnivadbhūtāntaraṃ| kintu vāyutvāparityāgenaiva jīvasya cakṣurādivadupakaraṇaviśeṣaḥ| tairviśiṣṭatvāt| prāṇasaṃvāde hi "atha ha prāṇā ahaṃśreyase vyūdire ahaṃ śreyānasmī"ti cakṣurādibhissahakalahābhidhānena tatsamānacchāyatvāvasāyāt, tadanantaraṃ "tvaṃnaśreṣṭho'sīti cakṣurādivacanena prāṇasya mukhyatvanirdhāraṇācca| samānajātīyānāmeva hi sahaśiṣṭastulyopakārakatva saṃśayena parasparotkarṣakalahaḥ| kasyacidupakārādiśayadarśanena mukhyatvanirdhāraṇaṃ ca bhavati| jīvopakaraṇatvenaprasiddhaissamānajātīyatvañca prāṇasyāpi tadupakaraṇatve satyeva bhavati| nanu cakṣurādibhiriva prāṇena jīvasyopakāraviśeṣo na dṛśyataiticennāyaṃ doṣaḥ| śrutirevāhi śarīrendriyadhāraṇarūpāṃ kriyāṃ tadupakāratvena darśayati| yathā prāṇasaṃvāde cakṣurādīn prati prāṇasya vacanaṃ "māmohamāpadyathāhamevaitatpaṃcadhātmānaṃ pravibhajya etadbāṇamavaṣṭabhya vidhārayāmī"ti| nanu paṃcadhā bhede vyānādayaḥ kiṃ prāṇāttatvāṃtarāṇi netyucyate| "kāmassaṃkalpovicikitsā śraddhā'śraddhādhṛtiradhṛtirhrīrdhīrbhīrityetatsarvaṃ manaeve"ti vacanātkāmasaṃkalpādayo manasa iva prāṇavāyorakasyaiva prāṇāpānādayovṛttibhedāḥ| etenendriyāṇāṃ sādhāraṇī vṛttiḥ prāṇaiti sāṃkhyapakṣe nirastaḥ| tehi kalpayanti "paṃjarasthānaṃ sarveṣāṃ śukānāṃ paṃjaracālanamiva vā sarvāsāṃ
khalakapotikānāmuparipatitavāgurotkṣepaṇamivavā śarīrasthānāṃ sarveṣāmindriyāṇāṃ sādhāraṇavayāpāraḥ prāṇa"iti| naitadyuktaṃ| indriyakāryaviṣayopalabdhivyatirekeṇocchvāsaniśvāsakriyāyāḥ, svātmānaṃ paṃcadhā vibhajya dehadhāraṇakriyāyāśca mukhyaprāṇasvataṃtrakāryasyadarśanāt||
saṃgrahakārike||
mukhyaprāṇopyakṣavargoktarītyā brahmotpannovāyumātraṃ tathokteḥ|
dehāṃtarvā tatkriyā śvāsarūpā maivaṃ dravyaissākamutpattivādāt||
vāyorevatvavasthāṃtaramayamiti saṃgacchate vāyutoktiḥ
pūrvāvasthānuvṛtterbhavati dahanavannāsya bhūtāṃtaratvaṃ|
nityaṃ saṃsārabhājāmupakaraṇamidaṃ śiṣyate cakṣurādhaiḥ
khyātātyaṃtopakārastanukaraṇamayaṃ dhārayanpañcavṛttiḥ||
iti vāyukriyādhikaraṇam||
aṇuśca||12||
prāṇasvarūpanirdhāraṇāntaraṃ tatparimāṇaṃ nirūpyataiti saṃgatiḥ| prāṇovibhuḥ paricchinnoveti śrutivipratipattessaṃśaye vibhuriti pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ| "samaebhistribhirlokaissamonena sarveṇa, prāṇe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ, sarvaṃ hīdaṃ prāṇenāvṛta"miti śravaṇāt| nanvindriyāṇāmānantyaśravaṇe'pi ukramaṇādiliṃgena paricchinnatvaṃ prasādhitaṃ, tenaiva nyāyena prāṇasyāpi paricchinnatvaṃ sidhyet| kimanenādhikaraṇena| ucyate - upāstividhānāṃtargatamānantyaṃ, idaṃtu natatheti pūrvapakṣīmanyate| yadvā "sarvaṃhīdaṃprāṇenāvṛta"mitītyupapattisahitaṃ sarvasāmyaśravaṇamutkramaṇādiliṃgāt prabalaṃ| atastadaupayikotkramaṇādirūpaṃ vācyamiti pūrvapakṣyabhiprāyaḥ| upāsanāvidhiśeṣatvaṃ sarvasāmyaliṃgepi tulyaṃ "evametatsāmyaṃ vede"tyupāsanāvidhisannidhānāt| "prāṇe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ sarvāhīdaṃ prāṇenāvṛta"mityetadapi sarvasya śarīrasya prāṇe pratiṣṭhitatvaṃ prāṇenāvṛtatvaṃ ca brūte| na tu sarvasya jagataḥ prāṇāvṛtatvarūpaṃ| prāṇasya vyāpakatvaṃ hiśabdaprayogena prasiddhārthavādatvāvagamāt śarīrasyeva ca prāṇapratiṣṭhitatvaprāṇāvṛtatvayoḥ lokataśśrutyaṃtarataśca prasiddheriti siddhāntaḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
samaebhistribhirlokairityāmnānādayaṃ vibhuḥ|
aṇurutkramaṇādibhyaḥ sarvasāmyaśrutisstutiḥ||
iti prāṇāṇutvādhikaraṇam||
jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānantu tadāmananātprāṇavatā śabdāt||
tasya ca nityatvam||14||
prāṇānāmutpattimattāparimāṇādivicārānantaraṃ agnyādidevatādhiṣṭhitatvaprakāraviśeṣaścityata iti saṃgatiḥ| kimagnyādidevatānāṃ prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ "abhimānivyapadeśastu viśeṣānugatibhyā"miti prākprasaṃgāduktaṃ| tat svasvāyattaṃ uta īśvarāyattamiti saṃdehe pūrvapakṣaḥ - yadyapi "parātta tacchaØte"rityadhikaraṇe jīvānāṃ kartṛtvamīśvarāyattamityuktaṃ, tathāpi agnyādityādīnāmīśvaradattaiśvaryāṇāṃ īśvaravat varadāyitvaphalapradatvādisatvena svātaṃtryasatvāttattadāyattamevādhiṣṭhātṛtvamastu parāyattatāśruteḥ manuṣyādiviṣayatvena saṃkocopapatteriti| siddhāntastu - 
jyotirādīnāmagnyādityādīnāṃ yaccakṣurādyadhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ tatparameśvarasyaiva saṃkalpāt| kutaḥ? tasya sarvāntaryāmitvaśruteḥ| ato yathā jīvasya "evamevaiṣa etān prāṇān gṛhītvā sve śarīre yathākāmaṃ paricartata" iti svātaṃtryaśravaṇe'pi tasya indriyādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ parāyattaṃ, evamabhimānidevatānāmapīti yuktaṃ| nanu parameśvaradattaiśvaryema devatānāṃ manuṣyādibhyo viśeṣostīti cenna| sarveṣāṃ saṃsāriṇāmīśvaraniyamyatvasya nityattvāt "sa īśo'sya jagatonityaeve"tyaviśeṣaśravaṇāt| ataḥ
prāgdevatāpadaprāpteḥ parameśvaraniyamyānāṃ tāsāṃ tadanantaramapi amātyānāṃ rājaniyamyatvavat īśvaraniyamyatvamavarjanīyame||
saṃgrahakārike||
agnyādidevatānāmārādhyānāṃ phalapradānāṃca|
prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ svāyattaṃ bhavatu ko doṣaḥ||
maivaṃ tadīśvarādīnāṃ tāsāṃ prāṇavatā saha|
nityānubandhinīhyeṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ tanniyamyatā||
iti jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānādhikaraṇam||
ta indriyāṇi tadvyapadeśādanyatraśreṣṭāt||15||
bhedaśrutervailakṣaṇyācca||16||
indriyāṇāṃ prāṇasya cotpattyādinirūpaṇāntaraṃ kiṃ sarve prāṇaśabdanirdriṣṭā indriyāṇi uta mukhyaprāṇavyatiriktā iti vicāryate| nanu kimasya vicārasya prayojanaṃ| mukhyaprāṇānugatatadvyāvṛttalakṣaṇavicārasya vyavahāra viśeṣa eva prayojanamiti cet maivaṃ| na khalu vaiśeṣikaśāstra iva vyavahāramātraprayojano lakṣaṇavicārostyasmin śāstre ucyate| śrutivipratipattinirāsārtha evāyamapi vicāraḥ| tathāhi - bahuvacanāṃtaḥ prāṇaśabdaḥ indriyaparyāya iti nirvivādaṃ| tasya mukhyavyāvṛttaḥ prayogo dṛśyate - "prāṇamanūtkrāmantaṃ sarve prāṇā anūtkrāmantī"ti| tena śreṣṭhādanye cakṣurādaya eveṃdriyāṇīti pratīyate| "athaha yaevāyaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇastamudgīthamupāsāṃcakrira"iti śreṣṭhe mukhyaprāṇaśabdaprayogāt sopīndriyāṃtarbhūta iti pratīyate| yena pravṛttinimittena cakṣurādiṣu prāṇaśabdapravṛttiḥ tenaiva śreṣṭhe tatpravṛttau hi śreṣṭhaḥprāṇānāṃ madhye mukhyaprāṇo bhavati yathā brāhmaṇānāṃ madhya eva kaścinmukhyabrahmaṇo bhavati| evaṃ śrutivipratipattau satyāṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ - prāṇopīndriyameva| prāṇaśabdavācyeṣu tasya mukhyatvapratipādanāt| na ca "etasmājjāyata" iti maṃtre prāṇasya pṛthagutpattivacanāndindriyebhyo bhedaśśaṃkanīyaḥ| tathā sati manasopi tebhyo bhedāpatteḥ| prāṇasyāpīndriyatve satyeva prāyassajātīyeṣveva loke dṛṣṭaḥ parasparotkarṣakalahaḥ cakṣurādīnāṃ prāṇasya ca śrūyamāṇassaṃgacchate| na cāṃtaraṃgeṇendriyatvena sājātye saṃbhavati bahiraṃgeṇa jīvopakaraṇatvena sājātyāt sa upapādanīyaḥ| evaṃ ca "indriyāṇi daśaikañce"tyādivacanaṃ mukhyaprāṇātiriktendriyādiviṣayaṃ| yathā "saptaprāṇā"iti vacanaṃ śīrṣaṇyaprāṇamātraviṣayamiti naikādaśasaṃkhyoktivirodhaḥ| tasmāt "sa yohaitānanaṃtānupāsta" iti vihitamindriyopāsanaṃ mukhyaprāṇasahitameva kāryamiti| siddhāṃtastu - 
śreṣṭhe mukhyaprāṇaśabdaḥ prāṇaśabdoditānāmindriyāṇāṃ madhye mukhyendriyarūpa ityevamartho na bhavati, kintu mukhebhavaḥ prāṇa ityetadarthakaḥ| bṛhadāraṇyake samānaprakaraṇe "athahemamāsanyaṃprāṇamūcu"riti mukhyaśabdasthāne āsanyaśabdaprayogāt, tasyacāsyebhava ityetadarthakatvāt| tatrahyāsyaśabdasya "paddannomās"ityādisūtreṇāsannādeśaḥ| yadi tu mukhyaḥ prāṇa ityasya prāṇānāṃ madhyemukhya ityarthaḥ, tathāpi prāṇasyendriyatvaṃ na labhyate| ayaṃ prāṇaḥ prāṇaśabdasya mukhyobhidheyaḥ, anye prāṇā indriyāṇi tasya lakṣaṇīyā na mukhyā ityevamarthakatvopapatteḥ, "ta etasyaiva sarve rūpamabhavaṃ stasmādeta etenākhyāyaṃta" iti mukhyaprāṇavācakenaiva satā cakṣurādīnāṃ lakṣaṇīyatvokteḥ| sarvametat "anyathātvaṃśabdāditice"diti guṇopasaṃhārapādādhikaraṇe spaṣṭībhaviṣyati| tasmānmukhyaprāṇavyatiriktāścakṣurādaya evendriyāṇi| "etasmājjāyate prāṇa"iti maṃtre pṛthaṅnirdeśādapi tathāvasīyate| manasa indriyebhyaḥ pṛthaṅnirdeśe'pi "manaṣṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇī"ti vacanāṃtarānusāreṇa manasa indriyatvamabhyupeyate| na tatheha prāṇasyendriyatve vacanāṃtaramasti| kiṃca prāṇasyendriyāṇāṃca vailakṣaṇyamapyasti| suṣuptau hi cakṣurādiṣu sarveṣūparateṣvapi prāṇasya vṛttirupalabhyate| "prāṇāgnaya evaitasmin pure jāgrati bhūrbhūrityeva śvasitī"ti śrutyāpyanūdyate| kāryavailakṣaṇyaṃ cāsti - jñānāni karmāṇi ca indriyāṇāṃ kāryāṇi, prāṇasya tu dehadhāraṇaṃ| tasmānmukhyaprāṇavyatiriktāścakṣurādayaeva indriyāṇi| teṣāṃcaikādaśānāmanugataṃ lakṣaṇaṃ sātvikāhaṃkāravikāratvam||
saṃgrahakārikā||
prāṇo'pīndriyamehaḥ sarveṣāṃ praṇaśabdāvācyatvāt|
maivaṃ pṛthagupadeśā danyeṣāṃ prāṇatā tu lakṣaṇayā||
iti indriyādhikaraṇam||
saṃjñāmūrtiklṛptistu trivṛtkurvata upadeśāt||17||
māṃsādibhaumaṃ yathāśabdamitarayośca||18||
vaiśeṣyāttu tadvādastadvādaḥ||19||
khādiṣu pūrvapūrvaśarīrakasya brahmaṇa uttarottarasṛṣṭikartṛtvapratipādanena sarvatrāpi sṛṣṭau brahmaṇassākṣātakartṛtvaṃ "bhedaśrute"riti sūtreṇa darśitaṃ| tannopapadyate - nāmarūpavyākaraṇe kevalaṃ hiraṇyagarbhasya kartṛtāyāḥ śrutismṛti
prasiddhatvādityākṣepeṇotthānāt saṃgatiḥ| khādisṛṣṭāviva nāmarūpavyākaraṇe'pi tasya sākṣātkartṛtvamupapadyata iti dṛṣṭāntasaṅgatirvā| "anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇī"ti śrutiḥ, "nāmarūpañca bhūtānāṃ kṛtyānāṃca prapaṃcanam| vedaśabdebhyaevādau devādīnāṃ cakāra sa" icyādi smṛtiśca hiraṇyagarbhasya nāmarūpavyākartṛtvaṃ pratipādayati, uta taccharīrakasya paramātmana iti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - hiraṇyagarbhasyaiva pratipādayati| "jīvena vyākaravāṇī"ti śravaṇāt| nanvevaṃ sati "yathā cāreṇa parasainyaṃ kalayānīti narapatiramanyate"ti laukikavākye cārakartṛkaṃ parasainyasaṅkalanaṃ rājñi gauṇaṃ vyapadiśyate| evamihāpi jīvakartṛkaṃ nāmarūpavyākaraṇaṃ brahmaṇi gauṇaṃ vyapadiṣṭamiti syāt, naiṣa doṣaḥ| rājacārayoriva jīveśvarayoraṃśāṃśinossvarūpabhedābhāvenātrāpi "jīvenātmane"ti jīvasyeśvarakartṛtvasvātmatvaparāmarśena ca jīvakartṛkasya saṅkalanasya mukhyatayaiveśvarakartṛtvasvātmatvaparāmarśena ca jīvakartṛkasya saṅkalanasya mukhyatayaiveśvarakartṛkatvopapatteḥ| naca sahayogalakṣaṇeyaṃ tṛtīyāstu, tathāca īśvarasyāpi tatra kartṛtvaṃ ghaṭata iti vācyam| upapadavibhaktitaḥ kārakavibhakteḥ padāntarayoganirapekṣatvena balīyastvāt| na ca karaṇārthatayā kārakavibhaktitvanirvāhaḥ| īśvarakartṛke vyākaraṇe jīvasya sādhakatamatvābhāvāt| kiṃ ca karaṇārthatve'pi vyākaraṇaṃ jīvakartṛkameva paryavasyati| neśvarakartṛkam| cetana kartāraṃ prati cenāṃtarasya karaṇatayopakārakatvaṃ hi tatprayojyakartṛtayaiva bhavati| asminpakṣe rājñaścārakartṛke saṅkalana iva īśvarasya jīvakartṛke vyākaraṇe kartṛtvamamukhyaṃ vivakṣitamiti netavyam| evaṃ karaṇārthatvakalpane sati tṛtīyānupapattiśaṅkā'pi nirastā bhavati| kartrarthatve hi tacchaṅkā syāt| vyākaraṇakartuḥ tiṅā'bhihitatvāt| na ca jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ praveśamātre paryavasyati| vyākaraṇaṃtvīśvarasyaivetikalpanena kartari tṛtīyānirvāhaḥ| "samānakartṛkayoḥ pūrvakāla"iti ktvāpratyayasya kartraikyārthatvasmaraṇena bhinnakartṛkayostadasambhavāt| tasyāpi kartrarthatvasmaraṇena tadabhihite'pi kartari tṛtīyānupapatteśca| atassaṃkalanasya cāra iva vyākaraṇasya jīva eva mukhyakartā| tathā satyeva "nāmarūpaṃ ca bhūtānā"miti smṛtirapi saṃgacchate| na hi hiraṇyagarbhajīvāṃtaryāmī parameśvaro devādināmarūpasṛṣṭaye pūrvasargavṛttatannāmarūpasmṛtyarthaṃvedaśabdānapakṣata iti| evaṃprāptesiddhāṃtaḥ
saṃjñāmūrtiklṛptiḥ - devādīnāṃ nāmarūpavyākaraṇaṃ trivṛtkurvataḥ parameśvarasyaiva sṛṣṭiḥ| "seyaṃ devataikṣatahaṃtāhamimāstisro devatāḥ anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāṃ karavāṇī"ti trivṛtkaraṇanāmarūpavyākaraṇayossamānakartṛkatvaśravaṇāt| trivṛtkaraṇaṃ hi parameśvarasyaiva karma| na tu "tasminnaṃḍe'bhavadbrahmā sarvalokapitāmaha" iti aṃḍāṃtassaṃbhūtatvena smṛtasya hiraṇyagarbhasya| trivṛtkṛtaistejovannairaṃḍasyotpādanīyatvāt| tasmāt trivṛtkaraṇakarteśvaraḥ nāmarūpavyākaraṇasyāpi kartā| hiraṇyagarbhasyāpi tatkartṛtvasmṛtiḥ ghaṭāderiva tasya dvikartṛkatvādupapadyate| kathamidānīṃ "jīvenātmane"ti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ| jīvaśabdasya jīvaśarīrakaparamātmaparatvādityavehi| kathamākhyātābhihite kartari tatkartṛkākhyātopāttakriyāpekṣayā ktvāpratyayaprakṛtyupāttakriyāyāḥ pūrvakālatvaṃ tayoḥ kriyayoḥ karturaikyaṃ cetyetāvanmātrameva hi ktvārthaḥ| lāghavāt| natvanyalabhyā kartṛśaktirapi| tathā'pi pradhānakriyānvayasyābhyarhitatvāt vyākaraṇānvaya eva jīvaśarīrakasya paramātmano vaktavyassyāditi cet - satyaṃ| pradhānakriyānvayāsaṃbhave upasarjanakriyānvayopi yukta eva| yathā "iṣṭakāḥ kṛtvā bhuṅkta" ityatra tatra iṣṭakānāṃ bhojanakriyākarmatvena anvayānarhatvādasaṃbhavaḥ| iha tṛtīyāṃtanirdiṣṭasya kartuḥ kartrarthakākhyātānvayānarhatvādityeva viśeṣaḥ| yadi ktvāpratyayasya kartraikyapūrvakālatvamātramarthamaṃgīkṛtya kartṛśaktyabhidhāyakatvaṃ pratyākhyāyeta tadeha jīvenetivat "iṣṭakāḥ kṛtvā'haṃbhokṣya" ityatrāpi mayeti
anabhihitādhikāravihitayā tṛtīyayaiva bhāvyaṃ syāt| atastasya kartṛśaktyabhidhāyakatvamaṃgīkṛtyaiva tatra prathamā samarthanīyā| tataśca atrāpi ktvāpratyayena karturabhidhānāt jīveneti tṛtīyā na syāt prathamayaiva bhāvyaṃ syāditi kartṛśaktyanabhidhānamāśritya tṛtīyāsamarthanāyāmanupapattiḥ, tadā chāṃdasatveneyaṃ vyākaraṇakartari jīvaśarīrake paramātmani tṛtīyopapādanīyā| na tu jīvasyeśvarābhinnatvaṃ vyākaraṇasya jīvakartṛkatvaṃcāṃgīkṛtya tṛtīyeyamupapādanīyā| jīveśvarayorbhedasya samarthitatvāt| atastrivṛtkaraṇasamānakartṛkaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ trivṛtkaraṇakarturīśvarasyaiva kṛtiriti siddhaṃ| yadyapi hiraṇyagarbhasṛṣṭyanaṃtaranāmarūpavyākaraṇānantaraṃ
trivṝtkaraṇaṃ śrutau paṭhitaṃ, tathāpi pāṭhakramādarthakramasya balīyastvāt trivṛtkaraṇaṃ prāgevetyasyārthasya prapaṃcanaṃ bhāṣye draṣṭavyaṃ||
saṃgrahakārike||
sarasijasaṃbhava eva vyākaraṇanāmarūpayoḥ kartā|
jīveneti śravaṇāt tatsṛjyaprakaraṇe ca tatsmaraṇāt||
maivaṃ yastrivṛtaṃ cakāra kathitaḥ kartā sa hi vyākṛtau
taddhātā na tathā trivṛtkṛtadharādyutpāditāṇḍodbhavaḥ|
tena vyākaraṇatrivṛtkaraṇayoḥ kartā sa eveśvaro
jīveneti tu taccharīrakatayā tatraiva śaktaṃ padam||
iti saṃjñāmūrtyadhikaraṇam||
iti śrīmadbharadvājakulajaladhikaustubhaśrīmadadvaitavidyācāryaśrīviśvajidyājiśrīraṅgarājādhvarivarasūnorappayadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturmatasāre
nayamayūkhamālikākhye dvitīyaparicchede dvitīyasyādhyāyasya
ca tu rthaḥ pā daḥ||
||dvitīyo'dhyāyassamāptaḥ||
śrīmate hayavadanaparabrahmaṇe namaḥ||
||śrīḥ||
dvitīyo'ddhyāyaḥ
||smṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṃgaiticennānyasmṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṃgāt||
||itareṣāñcānupalabdheḥ||
prathame'dhyāye śrīmannārāyaṇākhyaṃ paraṃbrahma sakalajagatkāraṇamiti tatra sarveṣāṃ vedāntānāṃ nyāyatassamanvayavyutpādanena prasādhitaṃ| tatra saṃbhāvitasarvavidhavirodhasamādhānena tadeva sthirīkartuṃ dvitīyo'dhyāya ārabhyate| tatra prathamādhikaraṇe sāṃkhyasmṛtivirodhena vedāṃtānāmanyaparatvāśaṅkā nirasyate| nanu kathaṃ smṛtivirodhe śruteranyaparatvaśaṅkā, śrutismṛtivirodhe smṛterevāprāmāṇyasya "virodhetvanapekṣyaṃ syādasati hyanumāna" miti pūrvatantrādhikaraṇe sthitatvāt| tatra hi "auduṃbarīṃ spṛṣṭvodrāye"diti pratyakṣaśrutivirodhena "auduṃbarī sarvā veṣṭayitavye"ti smṛteranādaraṇīyatvaṃ, asatyeva pratyakṣaśrutivirodhe smṛtermūla śrutyanumāpakatvena prāmāṇyañcoktam| satyaṃ| pratyakṣaśruteḥ sphuṭārthatve tathaiva| jagatkāraṇaviṣayāvedāntāstu sparśanaśrutivannasphuṭārthāḥ, kiṃtvatigahanā nānāprakārāḥ "vedārthoyassvayaṃ jñātastatrājñānaṃ bhavedapi| ṛṣibhirniścite tasmin kā śaṅkā syānmahātmabhi" rityuktarītyopabṛhmaṇaniścayārthāiti kapiladimaharṣipraṇītasāṃkhyasmṛtirūpopabṛhmaṇānusāreṇa teṣāmanyaparatvaśaṅkā bhavatyeveti tannirāsārthamidamadhikaraṇam| atra prathamādhyāyaprasādhitaṃ brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvamayuktaṃ yuktaṃveti sāṃkhyamanvādismṛtivipratipatteḥ kiṃ sāṃkhyasmṛtyopabṛṃhaṇīyāssanto vedāntāstadanusāreṇa pradhānakāraṇaparāḥ, uta manvādismṛtyupabṛṃhaṇīyāssantaste brahmakāraṇaparāityanirdhāraṇāt saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ sāṃkhyasmṛtyopabṛṃhaṇīyāḥ| tasyā niravakāśatvāt| manvādismṛtīnāṃ varṇāśramādidharmeṣu agnihotrādikarmasu ca sāvakāśatvāt| na ca manuvyāsādi bahusmṛtivirodhāt sāṃkhyasmṛterbhrāntimūlatvaṃ kalpyatāmiti vācyam| "ṛṣiṃ prasūtaṃ kapilaṃ yastamagre jñānairbibharti jayamānañca paśye"diti śvetāśvataropaniṣadi sāṃkhyakartuḥ kapilasya jñānaparipūrṇatvaśravaṇena tatkalpanā'yogāt| "yadvai kiñca manuravadattadbheṣaja" miti manūktīnāmapi kārtsnyena prāmāṇyaṃ śrutamiticenna| bheṣajaśabdasya prāmāṇyārthatvābhāvena tadasiddheḥ| yajurvedehi "yo barahmavarcasakāmassyāt tasmā etaṃ somāraudraṃcaruṃ nirvape"diti brahmavarcasakāmasyeṣṭiṃ vidhāya "yāvadeva brahmavarcasaṃ tatsarvaṃ karotī"ti phalārthavādānantaraṃ "atbrahmavarcasaṃkriyata ityāhurīśvaro duścarmābhavito"rityevaṃrūpeṣṭhyanuṣṭhānena "atibrahmavarcasaṃ kriyate tena yajamānaśvitrībhavituṃ prabhavatī"tyevaṃ phalameva punardoṣaparyavasāyinaṃ saṅkīrtya tatparihārārthaṃ "mānavī ṛcau dhāyye kuryā"diti manunā dṛṣṭe "agniṃ vaḥ pūrvyaṃgirā devamīḍe vasūnā"mityādike ṛcau sāmidhenīmadhye dhāyyārūpe vedhāya kathaṃ mānavībhyāmṛgbhyāṃ śvitraparihāraityākāṃkṣāyāṃ "yadvai kiñca manuravadattadbheṣajaṃ bheṣajamevāsmai karotī"tyarthavādena tatsaṅghaṭakaṃ, na tu manudṛṣaṛgarthānāṃ "agnirvasūnāmmadhye pūrvya" ityādīnāṃ manusmṛtivacanārthānāñca prāmāṇikatvapratipādanam| manūktyarthānāṃ śvitre vyādhyantare vā bheṣajarūpatvaṃ bādhitamiti cet, tarhi asadarthāvalambanārthavādatvamastu| nahyarthavādasya sadarthāvalambanatvasidhyarthaṃ prakṛtārthāsaṅdhaṭakārthāntaraparigraho yuktaḥ| tasmāt yadvai kiñce tyādikamanuvākyānāṃ sarveṣāṃ svasvaviṣayatvena pratīte sarvatrārthe prāmāmyapratipādanaparamityetadasiddheḥ manusmṛtivacanānāṃ brahmakāraṇavādāṃśamapahāya varṇāśramadharmeṣveva avakāśakalpanaṃ yuktamiti vedāntānāṃ niravakāśayā sāṃkhyasmṛtyaivopabṛṃhaṇīyatvāt te kapilasūtrahariharavārtikādipratipāditena prakāreṇa pradhānakāraṇatvaparā netavyāḥ| teṣu ye puruṣasyaiva kāraṇatvaṃ sphuṭaṃ vadanti tetvamātyajayaṃ rājñīva pradhānakṛtyaṃ puruṣe vyapadiśaṃtīti netavyāiti manvādismṛtibhirevopabṛṃhaṇīyāḥ| tāsāmāptatamapraṇītānāṃ bahūnāmanavakāśatvāt| na ca karmabhistāsāṃ sāvakāśatvaṃ, paramapuruṣārādhanabhūtakarmapratipādakānāmapi tadārādhanīyatayā taccheṣiṇi paramapuruṣe jagatkāraṇe brahmaṇyeva tātparyāt| ata eva manunā varṇāśramādidharmān ṛṣibhiḥ pṛṣṭena tadārādhanīyaparamapuruṣopadeśapūrvakameva teṣāmupadeśaḥ kṛtaḥ| "āsīdidaṃ tamobhūtamaprajñātamalakṣaṇam| apratarkyamanirdeśyaṃ prasuptamiva sarvataḥ|| tatassvayambhūrbhagavānavyakto vyañjayannidam| mahābhūtādivṛttaujāḥ prādurāsīttamonudaḥ|| so'bhidhyāya śarīrātsvāt sisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ| apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamapāsṛjat|| tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham| tasmin jajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ|| āpo nārā iti proktā āpo vai narasūnavaḥ| tā yadasyāyanaṃ proktaṃ tena nārāyaṇassmṛtaḥ"
ityādinā purāṇeṣu paraḥ pumān| viṣṇurārādhyate panthā nānyastattoṣakāraka" ityādivacanaistadārādhanatayā taccheṣatvenaiva karmapratipādanamiti spaṣṭameva| tathā yājñavalkyenāpi "kṣetrajñasyeśvarajñānādviśuddhiḥ paramā matā" itīśvaraṃ prastutya "mukhapādorupajjāssyustasya varṇā yathākrama"miti puruṣasūktaprakriyayā tasya jagatkāraṇatvaṃ pratipādayatā manuvadeva
svavarṇitavarṇāśramadharmāṇāmārādhanīye puruṣasūktapratipādye śriyaḥpatau nārāyaṇa eva tātparyamāviṣkṛtam| tathā āpastambenāpi "pūḥ prāṇinassarva eva guhāśayasyāhanyamānasyavikalmaṣasye"ti evaṃ "eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātmā'pahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇa" iti subālopaniṣatpratipādyarūpaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prastutya "tasmātkāyāḥ prabhavati sarve samūlaṃ śāśvatikassanitya"iti tasya sakalamūlakāraṇatvaṃ vadatā saeva svavarṇitavarṇāśramādidharmāṇāmārādhanīya iti tatra tātparyamāviṣkṛtam| evamanyeṣāmapi smatarṝṇāṃ vacanānyudāharaṇīyāni| naca anavakāśatvataulye kathaṃ manvādismṛtibhirevopabrahmaṇīyā iti nirṇaya iti vācyam| manvādīnāmāptatamatvāt| manostāvadāptatamatvaṃ taduktīnāṃ bheṣajatvaśrutyā'vasitam| tathāhi - bheṣajatvamiha rogaduritobhayaśāntyathasā dhāraṇadoṣaparihārārthatvamātraṃ vivakṣitaṃ, natu viśipya rogaśāntyārthatvameva| "yadvaikiṃce"ti vākyasyātyaṃtāsārthatvāpatteḥ| nahi manusmṛtivacanānāṃ madhye kimapi vacanaṃ vaidyakavadrogaśāṃtyarthabheṣajapratipādakamasti| dupitaśāṃtyarthapratipādakaṃtvastyeva varṇāśramadharmaviṣayacanajātam| nityanaimittikānāṃ varṇāśramadharmāṇāṃ duritakṣayahetutvāt| nanu tathā'pi sarvāṇi manusmṛtivacanāni duritakṣayopayogīni na bhavaṃti, manusmṛtau rājanītivyavahārapadanirṇayaviṣayatvena ddaṣṭārthānāmapi sattvāditi vīpsārthakayatkiñcicchabdāsāṃgatyamastītivācyaṃ|
teṣāmapi duritakṣayārthakarmānuṣṭhānopakārakatvena paramparayā tādarthyasattvāt, tadabhāve'pi kārtsnyaparayatkiñcicchabdasya varṇāśramadharmaviṣayavacanajātaparatvena saṅkocopapatteśca asaṃkucitasarvaparatvāsaṃbhave saṃkucitasarvaparatvaucityāt| ataeva "sarvaeva hāriyojanasya lipsanta" iti jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇa śrutivākye sarvaśabdasya asaṃkucitasarvaparatvāsambhavāt kenacidavacchedena sāmastyaparatve vācye prākaraṇikajyautiṣṭomikāÐtvajyāvacchinnasāmastyaparatvaṃ yuktamiti ṛtvijāmeva hāriyojanabhakṣaṇavidhāyakamidaṃ vākyamiti nirṇītaṃ pūrvatantre| evañca bheṣajatvoktyā labdhaprāmāṇyānāṃ manūktīnāṃ paramatātparyaviṣayamārādhanīyaṃ vihāya tadārādhanamātre tātparyaparyavasānāyogasya uktatvāt| tatraiva dharmaśāstrarūpāṇāṃ sarvāsāṃ manūktīnāṃ paramatātparyaviṣayamārādhanīyaṃ vihāya tadārādhanamātre tātparyaparyavasānāyogasya uktatvāt| tatraiva dharmaśāstrarūpāṇāṃ sarvāsāṃ mūktīnāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ labhyata iti tatra tasyāptatamatvam| tathā "sahovāca vyāsaḥ pārāśarya" iti śrutyā svapratipādye'rthe vyāsoktatvena gauravapradarśanāt tathā vyāsopadiṣṭānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ labhyata iti tasyāptatamatvam| evaṃ yājñavalkyādīnāmapi "janakoha vai deho bahudakṣiṇena yajñeneja" iti bṛhadāraṇyakādhyāyādivacanabhaṅgībhirāptatamatvaṃ, kapilasyatvāptatvamātram| "ṛṣiṃ prasūta" mityādestadṛṣitvopayogikatipayavedavedārthagocarajñānavattvamātraviṣayatvenāpi cāritārthyāt| tathā sāṃkhyaśāstrapravaktāraḥ kapilāsuripañcaśikhādayastricaturāeva smṛtītihāsapurāṇavaktāro manvādayastvatibahavaiti suprasiddham| smṛtītihāsapurāṇānica kapilādipraṇītakatipayasūtrarūpāt sāṃkhyaśāstrāt atibahulānītivyaktameva| tasmādāptatamamanvādibahupraṇītabahuśāstravirodhāt sāṃkhyaśāstrasya niravakāśatvamakiṃcitkaramiti nivakāśābhirmanvādismṛtibhirevopabṛhmaṇīyā vedāṃtāḥ| tataśca teṣāṃ samanvayāt brahmaṇojagatkāraṇatvamavicalamiti||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
vedāntaughasya sāṃkhyasmṛtiratigahanasyārthatatvāvadhṛtyai
vaktuṃ yuktopabṛhmaṇyanitaraviṣayā sāhyalabdhāvakāśā|
natvanyā kāpi dharmāvagatiparatayā viśrutā sāvakāśā
tasmādvedāntavedyaṃ na bhavati jagataḥ karaṇaṃ brahmatatte||
maivaṃ manuvyāsamukhasmṛtīnāmapi pratīto'navakāśabhāvaḥ|
ārādhanīye khalu karmabhissvairnārāyaṇe bhāvamuśanti tāsām||
tasmādbhajantyāptatamaiḥ praṇītāstāeva teṣāmupabṛhmaṇatvam|
yā tadviruddhārtharaiva dṛṣṭā sāṃkhyasmṛtirbhrāntinibandhanā sā||
iti smṛtyadhikaraṇam||
||etena yogaḥ pratyuktaḥ||3||
atrāpi viṣayasaṃśayapūrvapakṣasiddhāntahetavaḥ pūrvavadbraṣṭavyāḥ| hiraṇyagarbhasya bhramāsambhavāt tatkṛtasya
śāstrasyeśvarābhyupagamena śvetāśvataropaniṣadādyuktayogānuṣṭhānavarṇanena ca kapilādismṛtivailakṣaṇyena ca vedāntāntaraṅkatvāt tenopabṛṃhaṇīyā vedāntā iti taduktarītyā īśvarādhiṣṭhitasya pradhānasya kāraṇatvonābhyupagantavyatvāt brahma kāraṇamityetadayuktaṃ ityasyātideśādhikaraṇasyādhikā śaṅkā| hiraṇyagarbhasyāpi kṣetrajñatvena tatkṛtasya śāstrasya bhramamūlatvasaṃbhavāt, abrahmātmakapradhānakāraṇatvābhyupagamāt, īśvarasya nimittamātratvāṅgīkārāt, vedāṃtābhimatadhyeyavaiṣamyāt, tannirūpyavilakṣaṇadhyānavidhānācca viruddhena tena vedāntā nopabṛṃhaṇīyā iti tatparihāraḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
yogastarhyupabṛṃhaṇatvamayatāṃ sahyabjabhūnirmitastatrāṅgīkṛta īśvaraśśrutimito yogaśca tadrocaraḥ|
maivaṃ śrutyuditeśvarāttaduditādyogācca tadgocarādvailakṣaṇyajuṣau hi tau bhavavataḥ kasyeha nāsti bhramaḥ||
iti yogapratyuktyādhikaṇam||
na vilakṣaṇatvādasya tathātvañca śabdāt||
abhimānivyapadeśastu viśeṣānugatibhyām||
dṛśyate tu||
asaditi cenna pratiṣenugatibhyām||
apītau tadvatprasaṅgādasamañjasam||
na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvāt||
svapakṣadoṣācca||
tarkāpratiṣṭhānādapi||
anyathā'numeyamiti cedevamapyanirmokṣaprasaṅgaḥ||
manvādismṛtibhirovopabṛṃhaṇaṃ kāryamityuktamayuktam| tāsāṃ tarkaviruddhatvāt| atastarkānugṛhītayā sāṃkhyasmṛtyaivopabṛṃhaṇaṃ kāryamiti pratyavasthānātsaṅgatiḥ| brahmakāraṇatvaṃ yuktamayuktaṃveti cetanāt brahmaṇo'cetanatayā tadvilakṣaṇasya jagata utpattisambhavāsambhavābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ cetanādbrahmaṇo'cetanasya jagata utpattirna sambhavati| vilakṣaṇatvāt| ghaṭakuṇḍalamṛtsuvarṇādīnāṃ mṛttvasuvarṇatvādikāraṇagatāsādhāraṇarūpeṇa sālakṣakhaṇye satyeva hi kāryakāraṇabhāvo dṛṣṭaḥ| cetanasya ca cetanatvamasādhāraṇaṃ na tatkāryeṣūpalabhyate| na ca kadā'pyunupalabhyamānaṃ caitanyaṃ suṣuptajīveṣviva ghaṭādiṣvanudbhūtaṃ śruta brahmakāraṇatā'nyathā'nupapattyā kalpanīyam| sati caitanye kadācidudbhavāvaśyambhāvena tathā kalpanā'yogāt| anyathā śrutayṛpādityabhāvānyathānupapattyā yūpe'pyanudbhūtādityatvakalpanāpatteḥ, "vijñānañcāvijñānañce"tyādicetanācetanavibhājakaśrutivirodhāpatteśca vilakṣaṇāvilakṣaṇayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvābhyupa gatau vailakṣaṇye sati bhedāvaśyaṃbhāvena kāryakāraṇaikyasiddhāṃtavirodhāpatteśceti| siddhāṃtastu - 
krimimākṣikādiṣu satyapi vailakṣaṇye kāryakāraṇabhāvo dṛśyate| tatrācetanāṃśa eva mākṣikaprakṛtika iti sālakṣaṇyamastīti cet na| yathā kathañcitsālakṣaṇyasya kāryakāraṇabhāvaprayojakatve sarvasya sarvasmādutpattiḥ
prasajyata iti kāraṇagatāsādhāraṇarūpeṇa sālakṣaṇyaṃ tavābhimatam| na ca tadihopapādayituṃ śakyam| krimau mākṣikatvasyābhāvāt| evamupapadyate vilakṣaṇayorapi jagadbrahmaṇoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ| na ca tathā sati bhedābhyupagamāpattiḥ| sālakṣaṇyapratiṣedhe'pi kuṇḍalahiraṇyayorivābhedopapatteḥ| na ca jagadbrahmaṇorabhede jagatsṛṣṭyādiṣu satsu heyā jagadavasthāḥ jagatīva brahmaṇyapi syurityekasminneva brahmaṇi heyaguṇāḥ tadviruddhāḥ śrutyantarasiddhāpahatapāpmatvādayaścetyasāmañjasyāpattiḥ| sūkṣmacidacidvastuśarīrakaṃ brahma kāraṇaṃ, sthūlacidacidvastuśarīrakaṃ brahma kāryamityevaṃrūpe siddhāntābhimate kāryakāraṇābhede tadaprasakteḥ śarīragatadharmāṇāṃ śarīriṇyaprasaṅge bālyayauvanatthāvirādīnāmātmanyasambandhasya dṛṣṭāntasya sadbhāvāditi| evaṃ ca yadyapi sthūlasūkṣma śarīrarūpaviśeṣaṇāṃśe kāryakāraṇayoracetanatvena viśeṣyāṃśe tayoścetanatvena ca sālakṣaṇyamastīti vaktuṃ śakyam, tathā'pi kāraṇagataviśeṣyāṃśasya kāryagata viśeṣaṇāṃśe'pi kāraṇatvaṃ vaktavyamityabhyupetya vailakṣaṇyam| tatra dṛṣṭānta
upanyastaḥ| bālyāvasthasya yauvanādyavasthāntarāpattāvapi hi kāraṇagataviśeṣyāṃśasya kāryagataviśeṣaṇāṃśe'pi kāraṇatvamasti, nirātmakaśarīrasya yauvanādiprāptyabhāvāt, tathehāpi satyapi vailakṣaṇye kāryakāraṇabhāva upapadyata iti darśayitum||
saṃgrahakārike||
acetanaṃ cetanataḥ kathaṃ syādvilakṣaṇādviśvamiti sthavīyaḥ|
viśiṣṭayoḥ kāraṇakāryabhāve na codyametaddhi padaṃ labheta||
tathā'pi tarkapratitarkaucyate na sarvasāmyaṃ kvacidapyudīkṣyate|
yathā kathañcitprakṛte'pi vartate viśeṣato na krimimākṣikādiṣu||
iti vilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇam||
etena śiṣṭāparigrahā api vyākhyātāḥ||
pradhānakāraṇavādisāṃkhyāmātratarkeṇa samanvayabhaṅgābhāve'pi paramāṇukāraṇavādikaṇabhakṣākṣacaraṇakṣapaṇakādibahutvāntrikābhimatatarkeṇa tadbhaṅgassyādityadhikāśaṅkānirāsāya pūrvanyāyātideśārthamidamadhikaraṇam| paramāṇukāraṇavādināṃ tarkeṇa samanvayabhaṅgo bhavati na veti teṣā parasparavirodhasadbhāve'pi tadbhaṅgasāmarthyasambhavatadasambhavābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| sambhavati tadbhaṅge sāmarthya, yathā śunāṃ parasparavirodhasattve'pi ekaṃ varāhaṃ mārayitumaikamatyena tadbhakṣaṇalipsayā pravṛttānāṃ sarveṣāṃ śunāṃ tadalābhe'pi varāhasya māraṇaṃ bhavati| evaṃ paramāṇavassatyāḥ śūnyāḥ vijñānātmakāḥ kṣaṇikāścaturvidhāḥ ekavidhā ityevamavāntarakalahasadbhāve'pyalpaparimāṇānāmeva taṃtvādīnāṃ mahāparimāṇapaṭādyārambhakatvadarśanāt tadviparītaparimāṇayorbrahmajagatorupādānopādeyabhāvo na sambhavatīti pratiṣṭhitena sakalaparamāṇuvādyabhimatena tarkeṇa tadbhaṅga upapadyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - 
yathā nānādigviprakīrṇamapi bahulantamastomaṃ eka evādityaḥ pratikṣipati, evamudāhṛtaṃ anyadapyetādṛśaṃ tarkajālaṃ "yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛṇmaya" mityādyo mṛtyādyo mṛtpiṇḍārabdhaghaṭaśarāvādipratyudāharaṇena pratikṣipan veda eva jāgartīti nātraprayatitavyamastīti||
saṃgrahakārike||
aṇukāraṇatā bahavādimatā pratitiṣṭhati tarka iheti mudhā|
tadavāntarabhedakathākalahakṣapitaśśrutisīmni na sa prabhavet||
alpānmahatsyānmahato nālpamityeṣa vādināṃ|
śvavarāhanayaḥ kṣiptaḥ śrutyā dṛṣṭāntavarṇanāt||
iti śiṣṭāparigrahādhikaraṇam||
||bhokrāpatteravibhāgaścetsyāllokavat||
pūrvādhikaraṇasyātideśādhikaraṇatvāt tadvihāya tanmūlādhikaraṇena saṅgatamidamadhikaraṇam| tatra hi "na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvā"diti sūtreṇa śarīragatabālatvayuvatvādīnāṃ śarīriṇyaprasaṅgaṃ dṛṣṭāntīkṛtya cidaciccharīrakaṃ brahmeti tadgatadoṣāṇāṃ brahmaṇyaprasaktiruktā| sā'nupapannā| jīvavaccharīritve tatkṛta sūkhaduḥkhabhogaprasakterityākṣipyate| prathame'dhyāye "sambhogaprāptiriti cenna vaiśeṣyā"diti sūtreṇa parasyāsmadādiśarīrānupraveśaprayuktā sukhaduḥkhabhogaprasaktirnirākṛtā| yathā paragṛhapraviṣṭasyāgṛhasvāmino gṛhasākalyavaikalyaprayuktaharṣaviṣādabhāktvaṃ nāsti evaṃ parasyāpīti| iha punaḥ śarīrasvāmitva prayuktā sā nirākriyate| "na ha vai saśarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayorapahatirastī"ti śravaṇāt śarīrasvāmitve tatkṛtasukhaduḥkhabhogo na nivārayituṃ śakya iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - 
yathā'parādhinamaparādhānuguṇaṃ kleśayituṃ tadarthaṃ kārāgṛhaṃ nirmāya tatkleśanārthaṃ svayamapi tatra praviṣṭasya svatantrasya tatkṛtakleśabhāktvaṃ na bhavati, evaṃ parasyāpi| "nahavai saśarīrasye"tyādiśrutirapi svakarmopārjitaśarīraviṣayā| na tu śarīrasvāmimātraviṣayā| antaryāmibrāhmaṇo "yasya pṛthivī śarīra"mityādinā parasya pṛthivyādiśarīrasvāmitvamuktvā
tattadabhimānidevatāvat tatkṛtasukhaduḥkhabhākatvaśaṅkānirāsāyaiva pratiparyāyaṃ "eṣata ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛta" ityamṛtatvavacanāditi||
saṃgrahakārike||
yadi varṇyeta cidaciccharīraṃ brahma kāraṇam|
tadā śarīriṇo'sya syāt bhoktṛtvaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ||
maivaṃ karmakṛtaṃ tanna śarīrakṛtaṃ tato'sya tanna bhavet|
rājānugrahanigrahavaśavartitvaṃ yathā rājñaḥ||
iti bhoktrāpattyadhikaraṇam||
tadananyatvamārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ||
bhāvecopalabdheḥ||
satvāccāparasya||
asadvyapadeśānneti cenna dharmāntareṇa vākyaśeṣādyukteśśabdāntarācca||
paṭavacca||
yathā ca prāṇādiḥ||
vilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇe kāraṇāt brahmaṇaḥ kāryasya jagato vailakṣaṇye'pyananyatvaṃ "asaditi cenna pratiṣedhamātratvā" diti guṇasūtroktaṃ kuṇḍalahiraṇyanyāyena sāṃkhyaṃpratyupapāditam| idānīṃ vailakṣaṇye satyananyatvamayuktam| vailakṣaṇyasya bhedavyāptatvāt| ata eva kuṇḍalahiraṇyayorapyananyatvamasiddham| sarvatra kāryakāraṇayorbuddhiśabdāntarādibhirbhedāvaśyambhāvāditi vadato vaiśeṣikasya matamāsthāyānanyatvamākṣipya samādhīyata iti saṅgatiḥ| tatraivaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ - ananyatve brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatvaṃ na saṃbhavati| loke kāryakāraṇabhūtānāṃ ghaṭamṛtpiṇḍādīnāṃ buddhiśabdāntarādibhiḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvasya bhedavyāptinirdhāraṇāt| nahi mṛtpiṃḍe piṃḍaśabdo vā ghaṭe vā piṃḍākārā buddhirudeti| na ca ghaṭaśabdo mṛtpiṃḍe piṃḍaśabdo vā ghaṭe prayujyate| na ca piṃḍākāro ghaṭe pṛthubudhnodarākāro mṛtpiṃḍevā'sti| na ca mṛtpiṃḍenodakāharaṇaṃ ghaṭena vā kuḍyanirmāṇaṃ kriyate| na ca taṃtuṣviva paṭe bahutvaṃ paṭaiva taṃtuṣvekatvaṃ vā'sti| na ca ghaṭasyeva mṛtpiṃḍasya daṇḍacakrādi kāraṇam, mṛtpiṃḍasyeva ghaṭasya jalasaṃyojanapūrvakamardanaṃ vā kāraṇaṃ bhavati| na cottarakāle mṛtpiṃḍaḥ pūrvakāle ghaṭovā'sti| na ca pūrvakāle ghaṭādidarśanamuttarakāle mṛtpiṃḍadarśanaṃ vā'sti| tasmādbuddhiśabdādibhedāt kāryakāraṇayorbheda eṣṭavyaḥ| ekasminneva śarīre bālyayauvanādyavasthābhedeneva ekasminneva mṛddravye piṃḍa ghaṭāvasthābhedena buddhiśabdāntarādaya upapadyanta iti cet na | avasthānāmanityatāyāmasatkāryavādāpatteḥ, anityānāmavasthānāṃ prāksatā kāraṇenānanyatvāsambhavāt
kāryakāraṇānanyatvasiddhāṃtabhaṅgāpatteśca| tāsāmapi nityātāyāṃ buddhiśabdāntarādivyavasthityasambhavāt daṇḍacakrādikāraṇakalāpavaiyarthyaprasaṅgācca| nanu mṛdaḥ piṇḍatādaśāyāmanabhivyaktā ghaṭāvasthā, abhivyaktaiva sā buddhiśabdāntarādibhājanaṃ, abhivyaktyarthaśca daṇḍacakrādi kāraṇakalāpaḥ| na ca tathā sati mṛtpiṇḍagataghaṭāvasthābhivyaktyarthena daṇḍacakrādivyāpāreṇa tadgatakarakakapālamaṇikamallakādyavasthānāmapyapavarakagataghaṭābhivyaktyarthamāropitena pradīpana tadgatakarakādīnāmivābhivyaktiprasaṅgaḥ| nailyotpalatvayoḥ pradīpaprabhayotpalatvasyaivābhivyaktiḥ| kūpajalasya kūpāntarasthitisamaye jalatvaśauklyayorjalatvasyaivābhivyaktirityādivyavasthādarśanena tadvadihāpi yathā darśanamabhivyaṅgyābhivyañjakabhāvavaicitryopapatteriti cet - maivam|
abhivyaktāvapi nityatvānityatvavikalpe pūrvoktadoṣānātiprasakteḥ|kiṃca keyamabhivyaktiḥ, jñānaṃ cet, tadā daṇḍacakrādimelanasya ghaṭajñānakāraṇatvāpattyā tasminnasati kvacidapi ghaṭajḍhānodayo na syāt| āvaraṇābhibhavaścet tena kimāvaraṇamabhibhūyate? pūrvaṃ mṛdisthitā piṃḍāvastheti cet - na| tathā sati ghaṭadhvaṃsakamudgarapātānaṃtaramapi ghaṭāvasthāyāḥ pratītiprasaṅgaḥ| tadāvaraṇabhūtāyāḥ piṇḍāvasthāyāstirohitatvāt| nanu tadānīṃ mudgarapātābhivyaktā kapālāvasthā tadāvaraṇaṃ, svakāraṇābhivyaktāyāḥ kasyāścit kāryāvasthāyāḥ tadgatasakalakāryāvasthāṃtarāvaraṇatvābhyupagamāt| na ca tathā sati kapāleṣu kadācit ghaṭakāraṇamelane ghaṭāvasthābhivyaktiprasaṅgaḥ| iṣṭāpatteḥ| kapālāni cūrṇīkṛtya jalamṛdantarasaṃyojanamardanavyāpārairmārdavaṃ
prāpayya daṇḍacakrādimelane ghaṭābhivyaktidarśanāt| na ca dagdhapaṭakāraṇānāṃ punaḥ paṭabhāvo na dṛśyata iti śaṅkanīyam| tadbhasmanāṃ kṣititalapatitānāṃ tatkṣitiprarūḍhakārpāsataruphalapariṇatau krameṇa teṣāṃ paṭabhāvābhivyaktidarśanāditicet maivaṃ| evaṃ ghaṭādyavasthānāṃ piṇḍādyavasthāsvapi sadbhāvasya tadānīṃ tadapratītyarthaṃ pratīyamānapiṃḍādyavasthānāṃ tadāvaraṇatvasya ca kalpane kalpakābhāvāt yathānubhavamavasthānāmāgantukatvena vyavasthityupapatteḥ| anyathā kadācit kasyāścitevāvasthāyā anubhavaḥ, nāvasthāntarāṇāmityanubhavānanubhavavyavasthāyā apyanupapatteḥ| na hyabhivyaktiranubhavasvayaṃvaralabhyaḥ, yena daṃḍacakrādisamavadhānātpūrvaṃ piṃḍāvasthāyā evānubhavaḥ, tadanantaraṃ ghaṭāvasthāyāeva| tato mudgarapātānantaraṃ kapālāvasthāyā evetyādivyavasthā sidhyet| na ca jalasaṃyojanamṛṇmṛdukaraṇādikaṃ daṇḍacakrādikaṃ mudgarapātanādikañca piṃḍaghaṭakapālāvasthānāmanubhaveṣu vā tattaditarāvasthānāmananubhaveṣu vā karaṇānītyuktyā tathā vyavasthānirvāhaḥ| udāhṛtatattatkāraṇasamavadhāneṣvatīteṣvapi piṃḍādyavasthānāmanubhavasya tattatpratidvaṃdvivyavasthānāmananubhavasyacānuvṛttisattvāt| tasmādyathānubhavaṃ pūrvaṃ piṃḍāvasthaiva, madhye ghaṭāvasthaiva, tataḥkapālāvasthaiveti vyavasthaivāṅgīkartuṃ yuktā| tathā satyavasthānāmāgantukatvenāsatkāryavādaḥ prasajyata iti tatparihārārthamidaṃkalpyata iti cet, kimabhivyaktīnāmāgantukatve sa na prasajyate, abhivyaktīnāmapi nityatve tu kathamapi tvaduktavyavasthāsiddhiḥ| evamavasthānāṃ tattadabhivyaktīnāṃ vā''gantukatvevāsatkāryavādasya apratikṣepyatvasthitau dravyamātre satkāryavādābhyupagamasya niṣprayojanatvāt| kulālena ghaṭaḥ kṛtaḥ, kalādena kuṇḍalaṃ kṛtamiti kartṛkāryayoriva mṛtpiṇḍena ghaṭaḥ kṛtaḥ suvarṇena kuṇḍalaṃ kṛtamiti samavāyikāraṇatatsamavetakāryayorapyabādhita bhedavyavahāradarśanāt kāryakāraṇayoravasthābhedavaddravyabheda upagantuṃ yuktaḥ| "mṛddhaṭassuvarṇaṃ kuṇḍala" mityabhedavyavahāreṇa bhedavyavahāro bādhita iti cet na| mṛttvasuvarṇatvayoravayavāvayavivṛttijātitvena tathā vyavahāropapattyā tena bhedavyavahārabādhanāyogāt| ata eva yatrāvayavavṛttistantutvādijātirnāvayavipaṭādivṛttiḥ, na tatra tantayaḥ paṭa ityādivyavahāraḥ| tasmāt sahāvasthābhiḥ kāryakāraṇadravyayorbheda eva yukta iti loke kāryakāraṇabhedaniyamadarśanāt jagadbrahmaṇorananyatve brahmaṇojagatkāraṇatvāsambhavāt jagatkāraṇe brahmaṇi samanvaya ityetannaghaṭata iti evaṃprāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
tadananyatvaṃ paramakāraṇādbrahmaṇo'nanyatvameva| jagataḥ tadupapādayadbhya ārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ| tathā ca chāndogye - "śvetaketurhāruṇeya āsa taṃ ha pitovāce"ti prastutya "śvetaketo vasa brahmacaryaṃ na va somyāsmatkulīno'nanūcya brahmabandhuriva bhavatī"ti pitrā niyuktasya tasya gurukulavāsaṃ pratinivṛttiñca "sahadvādaśavarṣa upetya caturviṃśativarṣaḥ punareyāye"tyuktvā tasya vedābhyāsamūlamavalepaṃ
paśyatātamavalepamapanīyatasyābrahmajijñāsāmutpipādayiṣatā pitrā sa itthaṃ pṛṣṭa ityuktam| "stabdhosyuta tamādeśamaprākṣyo yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatyamataṃ matamavijñātaṃ vijñāta"miti| yena śrutena matena vijñātena cetyarthaḥ| "ātmani khalvare dṛṣṭe śrute matevijñāte idaṃ sarvaṃ viditaṃ bhavatī"ti śrutyantarāt| evaṃ kṛtsnasya jagato brahmaikakāraṇakatvaṃ kāraṇāt kāryasyānanyatvaṃ ca hṛdi nidhāya brahmavijñānena sarvavijñāne pratijñāte sati tadabhiprāyamajānatā putreṇa kathamanyavijñānenānyadvijñātaṃ bhavatīti manyamānena "kathannubhagavassa ādeśa" iti pṛṣṭe pitā svapratijḍhātamarthamupapādayituṃ kāraṇāt kāryasyānanyatve laukikṛṣṭāntamuvāceti varṇitam - "yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṃḍena sarvaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vijñātaṃ syā"diti | yathā ekamṛtpiṇḍārabdhaghaṭaśarāvāda yastadanatiriktadravyatvāt tajjñānena jñātā bhavantītyarthaḥ| atra vaiśeṣikaprakriyayā kāraṇātkāryasya buddhiśabdāntarādibhirdravyāntaratvamāśaṃkya lokapratītyaiva kāryakāraṇānanyatvamupapādayituṃ vākyaśeṣaḥ "vācāraṃbhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttiketyeva satya"miti| asyārthaḥ - ārabhyate ālabhyate spṛśyata ityārambhaṇam| "yatparyapaśyatsarirasya madhya" itivat ralayorabhedādrephaḥ| ālambha iha sparśaḥ| "ālambhassparśahiṃsayo"riti hi kośakārāḥ| "kṛtyalyuṭo bahula"miti karmaṇi lyuṭ| vācā - "ghaṭenodakamāhare"tyādivākpūrvakodakāharaṇādivyavahāreṇa hetunetyarthaḥ| vākpadenājahallakṣaṇayā vyavahārasyāpi saṃgrahaḥ| vāceti hetau tṛtīyā, adhyayanena hetunā vasatītivat prayojanasya hetutvavikṣayā prayojanārthā| vākpūrvakavyavahārasiddhyarthaṃ mṛddravyeṇaiva pṛthubudhnodaratvādivikārassaṃsthānaviśeṣaḥ tataprayuktañca ghaṭa iti nāmadheyaṃ spṛśyate| "napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyā"miti vikāranāmadheyobhayavartina ārambhaṇaśabdasya napuṃsakaśeṣataikavacanañca| udakāharaṇādivyavahāraviśeṣasidhyarthaṃ mṛddravyamevāvasthāṃtaranāmadheyāṃrabhāgbhavati| ato ghaṭādyapi mṛttiketyeva satyaṃ prāmāṇikaṃ na tu dravyāṃtaratveneti| "ārambhaṇaśabdādibhya" ityādiśabdena "sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyaṃ, tadaikṣatabahusyāṃprajāyeyetī"tyādayaḥ kāryakāraṇayorjagadbrahmaṇorananayatvapratipādakāśśabdāgṛhyaṃte| evañca yadyapi bālasthavirarītyā
buddhiśabdāntarādayo'vasthābhedeneva ghaṭarucakādirītyā dravyabhedenāpyupapadyante| dravyabheda eva cātyantasāmañjasyam| tathā'pi kāryakāraṇānanyatve mṛdghaṭādikaṃ dṛṣṭāṃ tayaṃtyā śrutyā'vasthābheda eva darśita iti avasthābhedakṛtā eva buddhiśabdāntarādaya ityurarīkaraṇīyam| na cāvasthānāmāgantukatāyāmasatkāryavādāpattirdoṣaḥ| sata eva dravyasyotpattirhi satkāryavādaḥ, na tvavasthānāmapyanāgantukatvaṃ| na ca prāgapi sato dravyasyotpattyanupapattiḥ| na hyabhūtvā bhavanumutpattirityabhyupeyate| kiṃ tvavasthāntarotpattirutpattiḥ, pūrvāvasthāprahāṇaṃ nāśa iti| evaṃ ca ekamṛtpiṇḍārabdheṣu ghaṭaśarāvādiṣvekatvāvasthāprahāṇenānekatvāvasthāpattiḥ| sāṃdrataṃtvārabdhe paṭe anekatvāvasthāprahāṇenaikatvāvasthāpattiḥ| ato viralataṃtvārabdhe paṭe paṭagataikatvāvasthāpattāvapi na tantusatastantutvasyevā'nekatvasyāpi na grahāṇam| kintu tantutvāvacchedenānekatvānuvṛttiḥ, paṭatvāvacchedenaikatvāpattirityādayopi viśeṣā yathādarśanamanusandheyāḥ| nanu "mṛttiketyevasatya"mitya śrutyā ghaṭaśarāvādikaṃ mṛttiketyeva prāmāṇikam, na tu dravyāṃtaratvenetyevamarthayā kāryakāraṇadravyānanyatve pramāṇāṃtaramapyādarśitamiti vyākhyātam, tat kiṃ pramāṇāntaramiti cet - pratyabhijñāpratyakṣamiti brūmaḥ| mṛtpiṇḍāvasthāsadbhāvepi hi ghaṭaśarāvādidravyasadbhāvaḥ pratyabhijñāpratyakṣeṇopalabhyate| idaṃ ghaṭaśarāvādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvāhne mṛtpiṇḍa evāsīditi pūrvāhnasthita eka eva mṛtkūṭaḥ etāvadghaṭaśarāvādirūpa āsīditi ca pratyabhijñā dṛśyate| nanvidaṃ ghaṭaśarāvādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvāhne nāsīdityapi loke vyavahāro dṛśyate| śrutāvapi "asadevedamagra āsī"diti prāgjagadasattvaṃ vyapadiśyate| kathaṃ pratyakṣaśrutibhyāṃ lokavedayoḥ kāryakāraṇānanyatvavyavasthāsiddhiriti ceta ucyate - pṛthubudhnodarādisthūlatvavirodhisūkṣmatvaṃ yaddharmāntaraṃ tato'yamasadvyapadeśaḥ| na tu prāksarvathaivābhāvāt| kutaḥ śrutau vākyaśeṣāt "idaṃ vā agrenaiva kiṃca nāsī"dityatra hi "tadasadeva sanmano'kuruta syā"mitītyasato manaskārākhyamānasasaṅkalpapratipādako vākyaśeṣaśśrūyate| loke'pi tata evāsadvyapadeśa iti yuktito'vagamyate| na hi sarvatra tattadvirodhidharmavyatirekeṇābhāvonāma kaścidasti| kalpanīyābhāvavat tadvirodhanaḥ klṛptabhāvarūpadharmāṃtarasyaivābhāvavyavahārālaṃbanatopapattāvatiriktābhāvakalpane gauravāt| lokavedayorasadvyapadeśapratidvaṃdvinaśśabdāntarācca "sadeva somyedamagra āsīt" "idaṃ ghaṭaśarāvādikaṃ pūrvāṇhe
mṛdevāsī"dityevaṃrūpāttathā'vagamyate| asadvyapadeśāt sadvyapadeśasya kāryakāraṇānanyatvaparyavasāyino yuktisāhityena balavattavāt| yuktirhi virodhibhāvadharmavyatirekeṇābhāvanāmakaṃ vastvantaraṃ na kalpanīyamiti prāgupanyastā| yathā tantava eva vyatiṣaktatantuvyatirekeṇa paṭanāmakamavayavidravyāntamasti| tasya rūpādirāhitye cākṣuṣatavādyabhāvaprasaṃgāt| rūpāntarādimattve'vayavarūpavyatirekeṇa avayavini rūpāntarasya gurutvadvaiguṇyasya copalabdhiprasaṃgāt| evaṃ vyatiṣaktantūnāmevapaṭabhāvavat ekasyaiva vāyośśarīre vṛttiviśeṣeṇa prāṇāpānādibhāvavacca brahmaṇa eva vicitracarācararūpajagadbhāvopapatterna vastubhedaḥ kalpanīya ityanayāpi yuktyā sadvyapadeśebhyobalavattvamavadhāraṇīyam| evañca "suvarṇena kuṇḍalaṃ kṛta" mityādivyavahāropyavasthābhedakṛtenaiva kāryakāraṇabhāvena upapādanīyaḥ| anyathā kuṇḍalāya suvarṇe tolayitvārpite suvarṇakāreṇa kuṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā dviguṇagurutvena tolayitvā pratyarpaṇīyatvaprasaṃgāt| tasmānmṛtpiṇḍaghaṭaśārāvādīnāmanunyatvāt taddṛṣṭāṃ tena brahmajagatorapyupādānopādeyabhūtayorananyatvamiti||
saṃgrahakārikā||
kāryāṇāṃ buddhiśabdākṛtiphalagaṇanāhetukālādibhedādbhinnatvaṃkāraṇebhyassthiramiti na daśābhedatassyātsa bhedaḥ|
ekasyaiva hyanekā samudayati daśā hetubhiḥ kālabhedāt

iti ārambhaṇādhikaraṇam||
itaravyapadeśāddhitākaraṇādidoṣaprasaktiḥ||21||
adhikantu bhedanirdeśāt||22||
aśmādivacca tadanupapattiḥ||23||
brahmaṇassvānanyajagatsraṣṭṛtve tasya hitākaraṇādayo doṣāssyurityākṣepikī saṃgatiḥ parvapakṣaśca - na ca prasādhitena mṛtpiṃḍaghaṭaśarāvādidṛṣṭāṃtena brahmaṇassūkṣmacidacitprapañcaviśiṣṭarūpeṇa kāraṇatvaṃ, sthūlacidacitprapañcarūpeṇa kāryatvamiti
brahmaṇa eva kāryakāraṇabhāvenānanyatvamucyate, na tu brahmajagatoriti vācyam| brahmaṇassarvakāraṇasya svaviśeṣaṇe cidacidātmake jagadrūpakārye'pi kāraṇatvasyāvaśyavaktavyatvāt| tataśca brahmaṇastenāpyananyatvaṃ vācyamiti sarvajñaṃ sarvaśaktikaṃ brahma svābhinnatvena jīvaṃ paśyadapi tasya hitarūpaṃ jagannasṛjati, ahitarūpameva jagatsṛjatīti svasyaiva hitākaraṇādidoṣaḥ prasajyate| atassvasmādananyadeva jagadbrahma sṛjatītyetannayuktamiti| rāddhāntastu - 
jīvādadhikaṃ brahma tato bhinnameva| "satyakāmassatyasaṅkalpaḥ, jñājñau dvāvajāvīśanīśau, sakāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipaḥ, asmānmāyī sṛjate viśvameta tasmiṃścānyo māyayā sanniruddhaḥ" ityādi bhedanirdeśāt| ananyatvaśrutayastu apṛthagbhāvaviṣayāḥ| pṛthagbhūtaprakāro hi brahmaṇaścidacidātmakaprapañcaḥ| "sadeve" tyādiśrutau agre "idaṃ sarvaṃsadevāsī"diti sāmānādhikaraṇyanirdeśena jagatassūkṣmatādaśāyāṃ sacchabdanirdiṣṭabrahmaviśeṣaṇatvasya "tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeye"ti brahmaṇe bahubhavanasaṅkalpādyuktyā| sthūlatādaśāyāṃ tadviśeṣaṇatvasya ca pratipādanena jagataḥ kadāpi brahmapṛthagbhāvo nāstītyavagamāt mṛdghaṭaśarāvādirapyapṛthagbhāvarūpa evānanyatve dṛṣṭāntaḥ| svābhinnāt svasya kadāpi pṛthagbhāvāsaṃbhavāt| na cānanyatvaśrutayāḥ "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tattvamasī"tyādyāvā mukhyābhedaviṣayāḥ, bhedaśrutayastu tattadviśeṣaṇabhedādaupacārikabhedaviṣayā ityastviti vācyam| tathāsīta prasaktahitākaraṇādidoṣasyaiva parihārālābhādaśmakāṣṭhaloṣṭādivadasyantaheyaguṇānāṃ jīvānāṃ nirdoṣakalyāṇaguṇaikatānabrahmābhedāsaṃbhavācca| tasmāt "avasthiteritikāśakṛtsna" ityādi sūtroktaprakāremānanyatvaśrutaya evāpṛthagbhūtaprakāraprakāribhāvaviṣayā ityeva yuktamiti||
saṃgrahakārikā||
evaṃ jīvā api niyamatastatvamasyādivākyā
dbrahmābhinnā iti tadahitāṃ naiva kurvīta sṛṣṭim|
maivaṃ vākyaṃ na tadabhidayā bhedanirdeśadṛṣṭestatsthatvenatvitihikathitaḥ kāśakṛtsnīyapakṣaḥ||
iti itaravyapadeśādhikaraṇam||
upasaṃhāradarśanānneticennakṣīravaddhi||24||
devādivadapi loke||25||
satyasaṅkalpatvādibhedanirdeśādananyatvaśrutayassaṃtvapṛthagbhāvamātraviṣayāḥ| naivaṃ satyasaṅkalappatve'pi sarvaśaktimattve'pi sahakārisāhitasya brahmaṇo jagadupādānatvatatsraṣṭṛtvaśrutayassamarthayituṃśakyante| śaktasyāpi sahakārirahitasyākāryakaratvādityutthānādavāṃtarasaṅgatiḥ| pūrvapakṣaśca - "sadeva somyedamagra āsī"dityavadhāraṇena prāktsṛṣṭessarvasahakāriśūnyaṃ khalu brahma śrūyate| tatkathaṃ jagatkāraṇabhāvamaśnuvīta| na ca sarvaśaktikatvena kaścidviśeṣaḥ| ghaṭādyutpādanaśaktaeva hi kulālādissahakāriṇaḥ apekṣyaiva ghaṭādikamutpādayati| evaṃ brahmāpi sarvakāryotpādanaśaktañcet, tattatkāryocitāni sahakārīṇyapekṣyaiva tāni tāni kāryāṇi janayet| na hi kulālo ghaṭotpādanaśaktaiti sahakāryanapekṣaḥ tamutpādayati| aśaktassahakāriṣu satsvapi na tamutpādayatītyeva śaktasyāśaktādviśeṣaḥ| tasmāt sahakārirahitaṃ brahma na kāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhatīti pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ| rāddhāntastu - 
brahmaṇassahakārirāhitye'pi kṣīrasya dadhibhāvāpattivat devarṣiprabhṛtīnāṃ svasvabhogasādhanasraṣṭṛtvavat jagadupādānatvaṃ tatsraṣṭṛtvavañca upapadyate| na ca kṣīrasya dadhibhāvāpattau pākātaṃcane sahakāriṇī staḥ| na ca brahmaṇo jagadupādānatve kiñcitsahakāryasti| devādīnāñca yogaprabhāvāsāditaiśvaryatayā bhūtavaśināṃ svasaṅkalpākṛṣṭairbhūtaissraṣṭṛtvamupapadyate| na tatheśvarasya nityasiddhasya bhūtavaśitāpādako yogaprabhāvo'stīticet - maivaṃ| vināpi pākātaṃcanābhyāṃ cireṇa dadhibhāvo bhavatyeva| acireṇa dadhibhāvāpattyarthaṃ rasaviśeṣārthañca tayorapekṣā| yogābhyāsādaiśvaryāvāptiśca śvetāśvataropaniṣadādimantrārthavādebhyo'vagantavyā| brahmaṇo'pi saṅkalpamātreṇa sraṣṭṛtve mantrārthavādāssaṃtīti na kaścidviśeṣaḥ|
kiṃca sarvasyāpi saṅkalpamātreṇa svaśarīravyāpārayitṛtvaṃ dṛṣṭam| kṛtsnañjagat brahmaṇaśśarīramiti tasya jagadvyāpāre na kācidanupapattiḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
agre sadekaṃ sahakāriśūnyaṃ kathaṃ hyupādānatayā jagatsyāt|
saṅkalpamātreṇa kathaṃ ca kuryāt kṣīrādidevādivadityavehi||
iti upasaṃhāradarśanādhikaraṇam||
kṛtsnaprasaktirniravayavatvaśabdakopovā||26||
śrutestu śabdamūlatvāt||27||
ātmani caivaṃ vicitrāśca hi||28||
svapakṣadoṣācca||29||
sarvopetā ca taddarśanāt||30||
vikaraṇatvānneti cettaduktam||31||
yadi kṣīraddaṣṭāṃtena brahmaṇojagadbhāvāpattiḥ, tarhi kārtsnyena kṣīrasya dadhibhāvavat brahmaṇo'pi kārtsnyena jagadbhāva āpadyeteti ākṣepikī saṅgatiḥ| pūrvapakṣastu - kṣīraṃ dadhyākāreṇeva brahma kārtsnyena jagadākāreṇa
pariṇamate, uta agnisaṃyuktaikabhāgojatupiṃḍomudrikādyupayuktaikadeśeneva tattatkāryopayuktaikadeśena pariṇamate| nādyaḥ| kṛtsnasya jagadbhāvāpattyā brahmāvaśeṣābhāvaprasaṅgāt| na ca sūkṣmacidacitprapañcaviśiṣṭaṃ brahma kāraṇaṃ, sthūlacidacitprapañcaviśiṣṭaṃ brahma kāryāmiti pakṣe nabrahmānavaśeṣadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ, kāryarūpatayā brahmāvasthānāditi vācyam| idānīṃ kāraṇarūpeṇāpi brahmaṇassattvasya vaktavyatvāt| anyathā kāraṇasya brahmaṇassārvadikasattābodhakānāṃ tatsākṣātkāropāyavidyāvidhāyakānāṃca vākyānāmānarthakyaprasaṅgāt| sarvopādānasya brahmaṇaścidacidrūpaviśeṣaṇāṃśe'pi kāraṇatvasya vaktavyatayā tatra kṛtsnasya brahmaṇa ekakāryātmanā pariṇāme kāryāntarātmanā pariṇāmaḥ kāraṇabrahmāvaśeṣaśca na syāditi dūṣaṇasya vajralepāyamānatvācca| tasmādādyaḥ pakṣo na yuktaḥ| nāpi dvitīyaḥ| niravayavasyaiṃkadeśena pariṇāmāyogāt sāvayavatvamiṣyatāmiti cenna| "niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāṃta"mityādiniravayavatva śrutivyākopaprasaṅgāt| tasmādbrahma jagadupādānamityetannayuktamiti|| siddhāṃtastu -
