naivaṃ śaṅkāvakāśo'sti| niravayavameva brahma jagadākāreṇa pariṇamate| svayañca yathāpūrvaṃ paripūrṇamavatiṣṭhata iti śrutyaiva mukhataḥ pratipādanāt| na ca śrutipratipanno'rthaḥ kutarkeṇopanayamarhati| "naiṣā tarkeṇa matirāpaneye"ti śrutiḥ| nanu śrutirapi grāvaplavanādivat pramāṇāṃtaraviruddhaṃ pratipādayituṃ naśaktotīti cet, na| brahmaṇaśśabdaikabodhyatvena tadvailakṣaṇyāt| pratyakṣādiyogyehyarthe tadviruddhaṃ śrutirapi na bodhayati| śrutyekagamyastvartho yathā śrutyeva sidhyati| na tu tadviṣayatvāyogyena pramāṇāṃtareṇa bādhituṃ śakyam| na hi rūpasādhanākṣamasya śrotrasya tadbādhakatvamasti| kiṃca jīvātmanyaciddharmavilakṣaṇāḥ kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādayo'bhyupeyante| evaṃ paramātmani cidaciddharmavilakṣaṇā dharmāḥ kuto na syuḥ| loke'gnijalādayo'pi auṣṇyaśaityādibhirvicitrā dṛśyante| na khalu te parasparadṛṣṭāṃtena parasparavilakṣaṇadharmaśūnyāssvīkriyante| na ca "jātirvyaktau nāṃśena vartate niravayavatvāt| nāpi parisamāpya vartate| vyaktyantare tadabhāvaprasaṅgā"diti kṛtsnaikadeśavṛttivikalpena jātervyaktau vṛttirnirākriyate| svapakṣe ca sāṃkhyasya tulyamidaṃ doṣāpādanam, niravayavaṃ pradhānaṃ kathamiva mahadādivicitrakāryārambhakamiti| satvarajastamāṃsi tasyāvayavāssantīti cenna| pradhānātiriktānāṃ teṣāṃ tadavayavānamabhyupagame tattvānāṃ caturviṃśatisaṃkhyāvirodhāt, teṣāmapi kṛtsnaikadeśavikalpena mahadahaṅkārādyārambhakatvāyogācca| paramāṇvādikāraṇavādināmapi kṛtsnaikadeśasaṃyogādivikalpaidrvyaṇukādyāraṃbhāyogācca| tasmāt kalpyamapi pradhānaṃ kṛtsnaikadeśavṛttivikalpāgocaraṃ manyamānasya śrutisiddhe brahmaṇi kā tadgocaratvapratyāśā|kiñca śrutivaśādiva sarvaśaktyupetatvādapi brahmaṇo niravayavasyāpi vicitrajagadākāreṇa pariṇāmo ghaṭate| sarvaśaktyupetaṃ hi brahma| "para'sya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyata" ityādidarśanāt| alpaśaktimatāmapi prapañcāntargata cidacitāmatarkaṇīyā vividhā vaicitrī dṛśyate| sarvaśaktimatassā kathaṃ na bhavet| nanu śaktimatāmapi karaṇakalebarāpekṣa eva śaktyunmeṣo dṛṣṭaḥ| atastadanapekṣaṃ sadapi
sarvaśaktimattvamadhyayanānapekṣaṃ dhīśaktimattvāmivāprayojakamiti cet - atrottaramuktameva| śabdaikapramāṇakaṃ vastu yatāśabdameva grāhyam| na tu lokadṛṣṭakāraṇaviraheṇa pratyākhyātuṃ śakyam| tathaiva ca śruttiḥ - "apāṇipādo javano gṛhītā paśyatyacakṣussaśrṛṇotyakarṇaḥ| na tasya kāryaṃ karaṇaṃca vidyate na tatsamaścābhyadhikaśca dṛśyate| parāsyaśaktirvivadhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyāce"ti| ataśśrutyā śrutidarśitayuktyupodbalitayā niravayavamapi brahma jagadākāreṇa pariṇamate, tathaiva paripūrṇamatiṣṭhate ceti yuktameva||
saṃgrahakārike||
kṛtsnaṃ yadi pariṇamate brahma tadā nāvaśiṣyeta|
nāṃśena niraṃśatvādviśiṣṭapakṣe'pi tulyamidam||
etādṛśāḥ pradhāne paramāṇvādau ca doṣāssyuḥ|
na brahmaṇi śrutiśatapratipanne vividhaśaktiyute||
iti kṛtsnaprasaktyādhikaraṇam||
na prayojanavattvāt||32||
lokavattu līlākaivalyam||33||
vaiṣamyanairghṛṇye na sāpekṣatvāttathāhi darśayati||34||
nakarmāvibhāgāditi cennānāditvādupapadyate cāpyupalabhyateca||35||
sarvadharmopapatteśca||36||
yuktyupodbalitaśrutyā kṛtsna prasaktyādidoṣanirākaraṇena brahmaṇa upādānatvasamarthane'pi sraṣṭṛtvasamarthanaṃ na saṃbhavati| prekṣāvatpravṛtteḥ prayojanavattvaniyamādityutthānātsaṃgatiḥ| pūrvapakṣastu - brahmaṇo jagatsṛṣṭissvārthā parārthā vā| nādyaḥ| avāptasamastakāmasyāpekṣitaprayojanābhāvācca| na ca prekṣāvatāmapi niṣprayojanā kaṃtukakrīḍādipratītirdṛṣṭeti śaṃkyam| tato'pi krīḍāparāṇāṃ tātkālikānandasattvāt| na ca brahmaṇo jagatsṛṣṭāvapi tādṛśaṃ prayojanaṃ kalpyam| avāptasamastakāmatvaśrutivirodhāt| nanvavāptasamastakāmānāmapi rājñāṃ kantukakrīḍādipṛvṛttirdṛśyata iti cet - na| avāptabhogāntaraprayuktasakalakāmānāmapyanavāptakantukakrīḍānandārthameva tatra pravṛtteḥ| na caivaṃ brahmaṇi vaktuṃ śakyam| kiṃca svaprayojanārthaṃ jīvān saṃsāraduḥkhe nimajjayataśca brahmaṇo vaiṣamyanairghṛṇye syātāṃ| tallīlācāsamañjākhyasagaratanayalīlākalpā syāt| etena parārthāsṛṣṭiriti dvitīyapakṣo'pi nirastaḥ| sṛṣṭeḥ pārārthye hi sarvān sukhina eva sṛjet| pakṣapātarūpeṇa vaiṣamyeṇa kāṃścitsukhinaḥ kāṃściduḥkhinaśca na sṛjet| duḥkhinassṛjataśca nairghṛṇyaṃ prasajyate| na ca karmakṣapaṇena rakṣitumeva pāpino duḥkhaṃ prāpayati brahma, yathā vraṇino rakṣitumeva vraṇadāhacchedaduḥkhaṃ vaidyaḥ prāpayatīti vācyam| vraṇadāhādikamakṛtvā vraṇaśāṃti kartumaśaktasya vaidyasya dāhādiduḥkhaprāpakatve'pi sarvaśaktikasya brahmaṇoduḥkhamaprāpayyāpi rakṣituṃ śaktasya tatprāpakatvāyogāt| na ca tattatkarmāṇyapekṣya viṣamasṛṣṭikartuḥ pakṣapātitvanairghṛṇyadoṣau na prasajyeteiti vācyam| "sadeva somyedamagra āsī"diti brahmamātrāvaśeṣatvena śrutāyāmādyasṛṣṭau karmābhāvena tadānīṃtaniviṣamasṛṣṭyā vaiṣamyanairghaṇyaprasaṅgā nivāraṇāt| tasmādbrahmaṇo jagatsṛṣṭṛtvaṃ na yuktamiti|| siddhāntastu - 
satyaṃ| prayojanārthaiva prekṣāvatpravṛttiḥ| ihāpyastyeva sṛṣṭyā brahmaṇo līlārūpaṃ prayojanaṃ rājña iva bālasyeva cākṣadyūtādinā, "krīḍā hareridaṃ sarvaṃ kṣaramityupadhāryatāṃ| krīḍato bālaksyeva ceṣṭāstasya niśāmaye"tyādi bhagavatparāśaravacanāt| na caivaṃ sati brahmaṇo'vāptasamastakāmatvavirodhaḥ| yadyadepsati tattadā'vāptuṃ sāmarthyaṃ hyavāptasamastakāmatvaṃ sārvabhaumādisādhāraṇaṃ, na tu sarvadā sarvakāmāvāptimatvam| śītoṣṇādikālabhedena suranaratiryagā ca viṣamasṛṣṭhyā vaiṣamyanairghṛṇyadoṣāpattiḥ, "puṇyo vai puṇyena karmaṇā bhavati pāpaḥ pāpene"ti śruteḥ| karmaviśeṣamūlaṃ jagadvaiṣamyamīśvarastadutpattau nimitamātraṃ sādhāraṇakāraṇam| yathā sasyotpattau parjanyassādhāraṇakāraṇam|
tadbhūyo'lpatvavrīhiyavatvādivaiṣamyaṃ kṣetrabījādiviśeṣakṛtamityabhyupagamāt| yathoktaṃ bhagavatā parāśareṇa - "nimittamātramevāsau sṛjyānāṃ sargakarmaṇi| pradhānakāraṇībhūtā yato vai sṛjyaśaktaya"iti| sṛjyaśaktayassṛjyakarmāṇi| evaṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ prajānāṃ vyavahāradarśane tadīyayuktāyuktavacanānusāreṇānugrahaviśeṣaṃ kurvato rājña iva na niravadyatvahāniḥ| pratyuta viṣamakarmaṇāmekarūpasṛṣṭāveva yuktāyuktavādināmekarūpānugrahe vyavahāradraṣṭuriva taddhānissyāt| na ca sṛṣṭeḥ prāgekatvāvadhāraṇāt tadā jīvānāmapyabhāvena tatkarmaṇāmabhāvaśśaṅkanīya iti vācyam| jīvānāṃ tatkarmapravāhāṇāñcānāditvenaikatvāvadhāraṇasya pṛthagvyapadeśārhavibhaktanāmarūpātmakasthūlarūpābhāvābhiprāyatvāt| ato jīvatatkarmaṇāmanāditvena satve'pyavibhāga upapadyate| anāditvaṃ jīvānāṃ tatsṛṣṭipravāhāṇāñca "na jāyate mriyate vā vipaścit, sūryācandramasau dhātā yathāpūrvamakalpaya" dityādiśrutiṣūpalabhyate| vicitrasṛṣṭipravāhānāditvenaiva tatkāraṇavicitrakarmapravāhānāditvamapyavasīyate| tasmājjagadupādānatvatatsraṣṭṛtvopayuktānāṃ sarveṣāṃ dharmāṇāṃ brahmaṇyupapatteḥ pradhānaparamāṣvādiṣveva uktavakṣyamāṇanyāyaistadunupapatteḥ brahma jagato mūlakāraṇāmityetadyuktameveti siddham||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
nāvāptavyaṃ kimapi phalamastyāptakāmasya sṛṣṭhyā
līlā'pyevaṃ jagati valate nāsamañjetarāṇām||
tatpārārthye kathamapi srajennaiva kasyāpi duḥkhaṃ
karmāpekṣā parihṛtirapi vyāpikā nādyasṛṣṭiḥ||
rājñaiva dyūtakṛtiḥ līleyaṃ brahmaṇo jagatsṛṣṭeḥ|
karmāpekṣāniyamānnaviṣamatā nāpi nairghṛṇyam||
taccāsti pralayeṣvapi saṃsārabhujāmanāditvāt|
śrutiṣu sadevetyavadhṛtiravibhāgārhāstadā'taiti||
kāraṇatānirvāho yaissyātsarve'pi te dharmāḥ|
upapadyante brahmaṇi tadvaikalyaṃ pradhānādau||
iti prayojanavattyādhikaraṇam||
iti śrīmadbharadvājakupalajaladhikaustubhaśrīmadadvaitavidyācāryaśrīviśvaji
dyājiśrīraṅgarājādhvarivarasūnorappayadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturmatasāre
nayamayūkhamālikākhye dvitīyaparicchede dvitīyasyādhyāyasya
prathamaḥ pādaḥ||
||śrīmate hayavadanaparabrahmaṇe namaḥ||
||atha dvitīyasyādhyāyasya dvitīya pādaḥ||
racanānupapatteśca nānumānaṃ pravṛtteśrva||1||
payo'mbuvaccettatrāpi||2||
vyatirekānavasthiteśrvānapekṣatvāt||3||
anyatrābhāvācca na tṛṇādivat||4||
puruṣāśmavaditi cettathā'pi||5||
aṃgitvānupapatteśca||6||
anyathā'numitau ca jñaśaktiviyogāt||7||
abhyu'pagame'pyarthābhāvāt||8||
vipratiṣedhāccāsamaṃjasam||9||
prathamādhyāyapratipādite brahmaṇo jagatkāraṇatve pradhānaparamāṇukāraṇavādibhissāṃkhya vaiśeṣikādibhiḥ
kevalatarkamavaṣṭabhyāpāditāni dūṣaṇānīti prathamapādena nirākṛtāni| tatrāpi praṇetṛgauraveṇa teṣāṃ tarkāṇāmupādeyatva śaṅkāsmṛtyadhikaraṇayogādhikaraṇābhyāṃnirākṛtā| tattattarkasvarūpaparyālocanayā teṣāmupādeyatvaśaṅkā navilakṣaṇatvādhikaraṇamārambha nirākṛtā| evamādyādhikaraṇayostṛtīyādīnāmaṣṭānāmadhikaraṇānāñcāvāntarārthabhede'pi brahmavādapratikṣepakatarkanirākaraṇārthatvenaikārthyaṃ, asminpāde śuṣkatarkālambanāssāṃkhyādipakṣāstathābhūtaireva tarkairdūṣyante| anyathā pradhānādikāraṇatvapratiṣṭhāpakānāṃ parapakṣāṇāṃ tadarkābhāsamūlatvaṃ jānatāṃ mandadhiyāṃ teṣu prāmāṇikatvaśaṅkayā brahmavādaśraddhā śithilībhavet| evaṃ svapakṣasthāpanārthaḥ prathamapādaḥ| parapakṣapratikṣepārtho'yaṃ pādaḥ| svapakṣasthāpanasyābhyarhitatvāt prathamaṃ kṛtamiti tadānantaryamasya| yadyapi tṛtīyacaturthapādayorapi kāryagocaraśrutivirodhaparihāreṇa svapakṣasthāpanaṃ kriyate, tathā'pi kāraṇaviṣayatvena prathamadvitīyapādayoraikārthyena avāntarasaṅgatiḥ| tatra vaidikānumatasatkāryavādādyabhyupagamena sāṃkhyamatasyopādeyatvasambhāvanāspadatvātirekāt prathamaṃ tannirākaraṇaṃ kriyate| sāṃkhyāḥ svatantratarkaiḥ svamataṃ prasādhya svamatānuguṇyena śrutivākyānāṃ nayanaṃ kurvanti| taiḥ kṛtanayanamīkṣatyādyādhikaraṇeṣu nirākṛtam| idānīṃ tadīyāssvatantratarkā nirākriyanta iti na paunaruktyam|
eṣā sāṃkhyānāṃ prakriyā "mūlaprakṛtiravikṛtirmahadādyāḥ prakṛtivikṛtayassapta| ṣoḍaśakaśca vikāro na prakṛtirna vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ"| tatra mūlaprakṛtiḥ satvarajastamoguṇarūpāṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ sāmyāvasthā| sācaikā svayamacetanā sakalacetanabhogāpavargārthā nityā sarvagatā ca svayaṃ prakṛtireva na kasyacidvikṛtiḥ| mahadādyāḥ prakṛtermahān mahato'haṅkāraḥ ahaṅkārāt pañca tanmātrāṇi śabdasparśarūparasagandhātmakānītyetāssapta svasvakāryāpekṣayā prakṛtayaḥ svasvakāraṇāpekṣayā vikṛtayaśca| ākāśādīni pañca mahābhūtāni śrotrādīni pañca jñānendriyāṇi vāgādīni pañca karmendriyāṇīti ṣoḍaśako guṇo vikṛtireva nakasyacittatvāntarasya prakṛtiḥ puruṣastvapariṇāmitvānnakasyacitprakṛtiḥ| nityatvānnakasyacidvikṛtiḥ| nirdharmakaścaitanyamātravapuḥ sarvagataḥ pratiśarīraṃ bhinnaśca| nirvikāratvānnakartā, nāpi bhoktā| evaṃbhūte'pi tatve mūḍhāḥ prakṛtipuruṣasannidhimātreṇa puruṣasya caitanyaṃ prakṛtāvadhyasyanti| tena caitanyayogāt kartṛśaktimattvācca jñātṛtvam| prakṛterevāntaḥ karaṇarūpāpannāyāḥ tasyāśca kartṛtvaṃ puruṣe'dhyasyanti| evamanyonyādhyāsādahaṃkartā ahaṃbhoktetyabhimānātmakobhogastatvajñānādapavargaityevaṃ pañcaviṃśatistatvānīti| tatra sakalamūlakāraṇatvābhimatatvajñānādapavargaityevaṃ pañcaviṃśatistatvānīti| tatra sakalamūlakāraṇatvābhimatasvatantrapradhānānumānanirasanena tanmataṃ sarvaṃ nirastaṃ bhavatīti tadiha nirasyate| idamiha teṣāmanumānaṃ - sarvaṃ kāryaṃ sukhaduḥkhamohātmakadravyaprakṛtikaṃ, tatsamanvitatvāt| sāmānyavyāptau ghaṭādivaditi| ādyānumāne sukhaduḥkhamohātmakāni dravyāṇi kāryāṇāṃ kāraṇatvena sidhyaṃti satvarajastamāṃsyeva paryavasyaṃti, teṣāntathātvāt| uktaṃtatvasaṃgrahe - "prītyaprītiviṣādātmakāḥ prakāśapravṛttiniyamārthā"iti| ayamarthaḥ - satvarajastamoguṇāḥ prītyaprītiviṣādātmakāḥ kramātsukhaduḥkhamohātmakāḥ kramādeva prakāśapravṛttiniyamaprayojanāśca| svacchaṃ satvaṃ prakāśayati, rajoguṇaḥ prakāśakārye satvaṃ pravartayati, laghossatvaguṇasya calanasvabhāvena rajasā sarvatra pravartane prasakte yathākāryaṃ gurustamoguṇo niyacchatīti| nacādyahetorasiddhiḥ| kāryāṇāṃ sukhaduḥkhamohānvitatvasya lokasiddhatvāt| tathāhi - ghaṭo yena prāpyate taṃ prati satvaguṇodbhavāt ghaṭassukhātmakobhavati| yadīyassonyenāpahriyate taṃ prati rajoguṇodbhavāt duḥkhātmakobhavati| yasya tasminnupekṣayā darśanameva nodeti taṃ prati tamoguṇodbhavāt mohātmakobhavati| "muhavaicittya"itidhātuḥ| naca guṇānāṃ puruṣabhedavyavasthayodbhave vinigamanāvirahaḥ| tattatpuruṣabhogarūpaprayojanavaśādudbhavāsiddheḥ| tadyathā - satvaṃ rajastamasī kañcitprati kadācidabhibhūya śāntāṃ svavṛtiṃta labhate, evaṃ rajassatvatamasī abhibhūya ghorāṃ svavṛtiṃta, tamaḥ satvarajasīābhibhūya mūḍhāṃ svavṛttimiti| naca dvitīyānumānasya paramāṇvādikāraṇakatvenārthāntaraṃ, lāghavādekakāraṇakatvasiddheḥ| naca tathāpi brahmaprakṛtikatvenārthāntaraṃ jaḍaṃ sukhaduḥkhamohātmakaṃ kāryajātaṃ pratyatathābhūtasya kāraṇatvena sidhyanavakāśāditi| evaṃrūpasya sāṃkhyānumānasya duṣaṇānumānamucyate| pradhānaṃ na jagadracanāyāṃ samartham| acetanatve sati cetanānadhiṣṭhitatvāt| yadevaṃ tadevam| yathā cetanānadhiṣṭhitaṃ mṛddārvādikam| na ca vyāptyasiddhiḥ, cetanānadhiṣṭhitasya kāryārambhakatvānupapatteḥ, cetanādhiṣṭhitasyaiva kāryārambhapravṛttessarvatra darśanācca| nanu siddhānte pakṣāsiddhiḥ| naca "pradhānakṣetrajñapatirguṇeśa" iti śrutisiddhaṃ pradhānaṃ siddhānte'pyastīti vācyam| tasyānumānagamyatvābhāvena karmavyutpanna sūtrānumānapadanirdeśyatvāyogāt| sādhyasya ca bādhaḥ, pradhānasya siddhānte'pi jagadracanāsāmarthyābhyupagamāt| hetoścāsiddhiḥ, tasya siddhānte cetanādhiṣṭhānasattvāditi cet - satyaṃ|
paraprakriyayā paro dūṣaṇīya iti parasiddhaṃ pakṣādikaṃ parigṛhya idamanumānamukatm| yadvā parānumānasya pratikūlatarkarūpaṃ dūṣaṇānumānamuktam| yadi pradhānaṃ acetanatvesati cetanānadhiṣṭhitaṃ syāt, jagadracanāyāmasamarthaṃ syāt| athavā hetorviśeṣyāṃśasya sādhye pratiyogiviśeṣaṇatayā'nvayaḥ, tataśca pradhānaṃ na cetanānadhiṣṭhitaṃ kāryajanakaṃ, acetanatvāt ghaṭagṛhādijanakamṛddārvādivacadityanumānaśarīram| nacānumānika pradhānānabhyupagamādāśrayasiddhiḥ| mahadādikaṃ saprakṛtikaṃ kāryatvādghavaṭadityanumānasya virodhābhāvena siddhānte'bhyupagantuṃ śakyatvāt, sādhye svātaṃtryāpraveśāt, tasyeśvarapārataṃtryamātre śrutyapekṣaṇāt| nanu acetanasya cetanādhiṣṭhitasyaiva pravṛttiriti na niyamaḥ, vṛtsavṛdhyarthaṃ dhenugatasya kṣīrasya svayameva prasravadarśanāt| sasyavṛdhyarthaṃ vāridamuktasya jalasya svayameva nissaraṇadarśanāt| evaṃ puruṣasya bhogāpavargārthaṃ cetanānadhiṣṭhitasyāpi pradhānasya pravṛttisambhavāditi cenna| kṣīrajalayorapyacetanatvāccetanādhiṣṭhānābhāve pravṛttyasambhavataulyena pakṣa iva pakṣataulye'pi vyabhicārābhāvāt| etena
dhenvādyupayuktatṛṇādeḥ kṣīrādibhāvāpattirapi vyākhyātā| tatrāpi cetanādhiṣṭhānāvaśyambhāvena pakṣataulyāt| anyathā cetanaviśeṣasaṅkalpasya niyāmakasyābhāve anaḍuhādyupayuktasyāpi tṛṇādeḥ kṣīrādibhāvaprasaṅgāt| na ca dhainavajāḍharāgnisaṃyoge kaścitpāke viśeṣaḥ| agnisaṃyogamātrasya pākahetutvāt, tasya anaḍuhādijaṭharasaṃyoge'pyaviśeṣāt| pācakasaṅkalpaviśeṣāt pākaviśeṣastu loke dṛṣṭaḥ| nanu aprayojakamidaṃ cetanādhiṣṭhānānumānaṃ, yathā pravṛttiśaktiyukto'ndhaḥ dṛkchaktiyuktapaṃgusannidhānātpravartate, yathā ca ayaskānmaṇisannidhānādayaḥ pravartate, tathā caitanyavapuṣaḥ puruṣasya sannidhānāt pradhānasya pravṛttyupapatteriti cet, evaṃ dṛṣṭāntopādāne'pi pradhānasya pravṛttyasambhavarūpadoṣastadavasthaḥ| asti he paṃgorgamanaśaktivikalasya aṃdhenohyamānasya svayaṃ mārgadarśanaṃ, voḍhāramandhaṃ prati tadupadeśa iti ca vyāpāraḥ| puruṣasya tu niṣkriyasya na tādṛśaḥ kaścidvyāpāraḥ| ayaḥ pravṛttāvapi cetanādhiṣṭhānāvaśyambhāvānnavyabhicāro'pi| tasmānnirdoṣaṃ dūṣaṇānumānam| sāṃkhyānumānameva ca pratikūlatarkaparāhatam| puruṣasannidhānamātreṇa pradhānasya pariṇāmitve sargavyatirekaḥ pralayaḥ kadā'pi na syāt, nityasya puruṣasannidhānasya sarvadā'pyaviśiṣṭatvāditi| tasmādadhiṣṭhāturīśvarasyaiva saṅkalpaviśeṣāssargapralayaniyāmakā ityubhyupeyam| na ca dharmādharmāveva tanniyāmakau syātāmiti vācyam| dharmādharmayoḥ puramapuruṣārādhanatadviparyayarūpatayaiva sakalaśrutiprasiddhayostadanabhyupagatāvātmalābhābhāvāt| yadi ca guṇānāṃ kadācitsāmyenāvasthānamabhyupetya tadā guṇānāmutkarṣānibandhanāṅāgāṅgibhāvābhāvāt tannimittā jagatprasūtirnīstīti pralayāvasthāyāṃ sāmyenāvasthitānāṃ guṇānāṃ kṣobhakapuruṣavyāpārabhāvena svayameva vaiṣamyāvasthāṃ prāptumakṣamāṇāmaṅgāṅgibhāvānupapatteḥ kadācidapi jagatsargo na syāt| yadi sukhaduḥkhamohātmakavaiṣamyāvasthaṃ jagat dṛṣṭvā yathākāryaṃ vaiṣamyāvasthāpannameva triguṇamanumīyate, tadā dharmigrāhakamānasiddhāyā vaiṣamyāvasthāyāssadāvṛttessadāsarga eva syāt| yadyucyeta triguṇaṃ na vaiṣamyāvasthaṃ nāpi sāmyāvasthamanumīyate, yena sadā sargaḥ pralayo vā syāt| kiṃ tu anādisṛṣṭipralayapravāhamūladvividhasaṃskārānuvṛttisadbhāvāttattatsaṃskārodbodhānasārataḥ sāmyavaiṣamyabhāgubhayasvabhāvamanumīyata iti| naitadapi yuktam| andhasya mārgopadeśakapuṃgusannidhānāt mārgajñātṛtvavat pradhānasya nirvyāpārapuruṣasannidhānāt cicchāyāpattyā jñātṛtvāsambhavasya uktatvāt, tadasambhavecācetanasya cetanādhiṣṭhānaṃ binā pravṛttyayogāt| kiṃ ca pradhānābhyupagame yadi prayojanaṃ kiṃcitsyāt tadā yatākathaṃcidabhyupagacchema| na kimapi prayojanamasti| puruṣasya bhogāpavargau hi prayojanamitīṣyate| bhogaśca sukhaduḥkhānubhavarūpaḥ pradhānapariṇāmarūpāyā buddherdharmaḥ| puruṣasya tu buddhicchāyāpattyā tadaikyamivāpannasya taddarśanaṃ bhogaḥ| tena tasya vevekāgrahāt ādhyāsikaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ, bhoktṛtvādhyāsasya tadvivekakhyātyā nivṛttirmokṣa iti sāṃkhyairiṣyate| na ca nirvikāracaitanyamātravapuṣaḥ puruṣasya savikārabudhyaikyābhimānaḥ tadīyabhogadarśanaṃ tadvivekānundhānaṃ vā sambhavati| nanu sāṃkhyaireva budhyādiṣvacetaneṣvasambhāvitābhyāṃ bhoktṛtvamokṣopāyaśravaṇādipravṛttibhyāṃ budhyādyatirikto bhoktā śravaṇādipravṛttimāṃśca puruṣassādhitaḥ "puruṣo'sti bhoktṛbhāvātkaivalyārthapravṛtte"śceti| prakṛteḥ puruṣabhogārthatvaṃ ca tatsvarūpadarśane sati tadaiva puruṣasyāpavargaścetyetadubhayamapi bahudhā prapañcitam| "nānāvidhairupāyairupakāriṇyanupakāriṇaḥ puṃsaḥ| guṇavatyaguṇasya sataḥ tasyārthamapārthakaṃ carati|| raṅgasya darśayitvā nivartate nartakī yathā nṛttāt| puruṣasya tathā''tmānaṃ prakāśya vinivartate prakṛtiḥ|| prakṛtessukumārataraṃ na kiñcidastīti me matirbhavati| yā dṛṣṭāstīti punarnadarśanamupaiti puruṣasye"ti|| tasmādastyeva tanmate puruṣasya prakṛtibhoktṛtvaṃ tadyāthārthyadarśanārthaśravaṇādikartṛtvaṃca| ataḥ kathaṃ tanmate prayojanāsaṃbhavaiti cet tarhi parasparaviruddhārthakathanādeva
tanmatamasamaṃjasaṃ| tatvasaṃgraha badhyate mucyate ca nānāśrayā prakṛti"rityādibhiḥ prakṛtereva baṃdhamokṣāviti prapaṃcitaṃ| kiñca jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ sidhyat lāghavādekameva prakṛtirūpaṃ sidhyatīti vadadbhireva guṇatrayasaṃghātaeva prakṛtiḥ natu tadvyatiriktā prakṛtirnāmāstītyabhyupagataṃ, tatopi tanmataṃ vipratiṣiddhaṃ| paṃcaviṃśatistatvānītyuktvaiva guṇāḥ parasparabhinnāityapyuktaṃ, tadapi vipratiṣiddhaṃ| guṇabhede hi saptaviṃśatistatvāni syuḥ| arthavipratiṣiddhaṃcedaṃ darśitaṃ| na hi paṭalābhādimatāṃ sukhaduḥkhamohābhavantīti viṣayāṇāmapi sukhaduḥkhamohānvayakalpanaṃ yuktaṃ| rajatabhujagādidarśināmicchādveṣādyudayena viṣayāṇāmapi tadvattvakalpanāpatteḥ|
viṣayāṇāṃ sukhaduḥkhādyanvayena ca tatkāraṇaṃ kutassukhaduḥkhamohātkaṃ sidhyati| na khalu śauklyagotvādikaṃ viṣayānvitaṃ dravyaṃ ghaṭānvitamṛnnyāyena kāraṇaṃ bhavatīti cenna| satvarajastamasāṃ dravyatvāsiddheḥ| pṛthivyādidravyagatalaghutvaprakāśādihetavastadguṇā eva hi satvādayaḥ| ataeva guṇā ityeva teṣāṃ prasiddhiḥ| tasmātparasparavipratiṣedhārthavipratiṣedhābhyāmapyasamañjasaṃ sāṃkhyamatam||
iti racanānupapattyadhikaraṇam||
mahaddīrghavadvā hrasvaparimaṃḍalābhyām||10||
ubhayathā'pi na karmātastadabhāvaḥ||11||
samavāyābhyupagamācca sāmyādanavasthiteḥ||12||
nityameva ca bhāvāt||13||
rūpādimattvācca viparyayo darśanāt||14||
ubhayathā ca doṣāt||15||
aparigrahāccātyantamanapekṣā||16||
ānumānikapradhānakāraṇavādavat ānumānikaparamāṇukāraṇavādo'pi na yukta iti samarthitaiti dṛṣṭāntasaṅgatiḥ| īśvarānaṅgīkārāt cetanādhiṣṭhānābhāvena pradhānakāraṇavāde racanānupapattivat na paramāṇukāraṇavāde sā'sti| vaiśeṣikairīśvarasyāṅgīkṛtatvāt| ataeva pralayānupapattidoṣo'pi nāsti| sṛṣṭiprāpakaprāṇikarmaviśeṣopahitatvena sisṛkṣārūpāyā īśvarecchāyāstadānīmabhāvasya vaktuṃ śakytvāditi pratyudāharaṇasaṃgatiśca|| iyamiha paramāṇuvādināṃ prakriyā| pralīne jagati prāṇikarmonmeṣaviśeṣopadhānādīśvarasya icchāyāṃ sisṛkṣārūpāyāṃ satyāṃ niścaleṣu niravayaveṣu paramāṇuṣu ādyaṃ karmotpadyate| tena karmaṇā paramāṇudvayaṃ saṃyujyate| saṃyuktena paramāṇudvayena dvyaṇukamārabhyate| tribhidrvyaṇukaistryaṇukamityādikrameṇa sarvaṃ jagadutpadyata iti| tatra paramāṇūnāṃ niravayavatvaṃ tāvadayuktam| yathāṃgulidvayaṃ pratyekaṃ daśadiksambandhidaśapradeśāvacchinnaṃ parasparaṃ saṃślepyamāṇamekayā mahādiśā vidigdvayena cāvacchinnaiḥ pratyekaṃ tribhistribhiḥ pradeśaiḥ saṃśliṣyata ityevaṃ ṣaḍbhiḥ pārśvaissaṃyujyate, evaṃ paṭārambhakāstantavassaṃśleṣyamāṇāpṣaḍbhiḥ pārśvaissaṃyujyamānāḥ pratyekamasaṃyuktasaptapārśvayuktāssantaḥ svādhikaparimāṇaṃ paṭamārabhanta iti dṛṣṭam| evaṃ paramāṇavo'pi ṣaḍbhiḥ pārśvaissaṃyujyamānā eva pārimaṃḍalyākhyasvaparimāṇādhikaparimāṇamaṇutvahrasvasya dvyaṇukasya dvyaṇukānmahato dīrghasya tryaṇukasya ca pṛthimānupapatteḥ| naca vācyaṃ mābhūtparamāḥ ṇutaḥ prathimā dvyaṇukasya, dvyaṇukāt tryaṇukasya tu prathimā kāraṇabahutvādbhaviṣyatīti cenna| tathāsati paramāṇoḥ pārimāṇḍalyaṃ parimāṇaṃ dvyaṇukasyāṇutvahrasvatve iti vibhāgābhāvaprasaṅgāt| paramāṇudvayasaṃyogasya vyāpyavṛttitve dvyaṇukasyāpi parimaṇḍalatvāpatteḥ, dvyaṇukotpādakaṃ paramāṇvossaṃyogamabhyupagacchataḥ tenaiva tayoḥ pradeśasiddheśca| na hi paramāṇū saṃyujyamānau dvāvapi samānadeśau saṃtau tayoḥ pradeśasiddheśca| na hi paramāṇū saṃyujyamānau dvāvapi samānadeśau saṃtau saṃyujyete| mūrtānāṃ samānadeśatāvirodhāt| ataḥ prākpratyagbhāvena, dakṣiṇottarabhāvena, urdhvādharabhāvena vā, bhinnadeśasthitau saṃyujyete ityeva vaktavyam| tathāsati yaddeśasthitaḥ paramāṇuḥ yena paramāṇvantareṇa saṃyujyate sataddeśānyadeśāvacchinnenāpi tena kiṃ saṃyoktumarhati, anarhatve vā kathaṃ tasmin pradeśabhedo na bhavedeti tvamevaṃ paribhāvaya| nanu paramāṇūnāṃ nivayavatve'pi tattaddigavacchedalabhyaḥ pradeśabhedo vaktuṃ śakya iti tata eva paramāṇudvayasaṃyogasya prādeśikatvaṃ dvyaṇuke parimāṇotkarṣaśca bhuviṣyatīti cenna - digviśeṣasaṃyogasyāpi paramāṇāvavyāpyavṛttitvopapādakāṃśābhāvena vyapyavṛttitvāpattyā tasya paramāṇvaṃtarasaṃyogāvyāptyavṛttitvopapādakatvāyogāt| na hi kapisaṃyogatadabhāvāvacchedakavṛkṣamūlāgravat vyavasthitadeśāvacchedakābhāve'pi yathākathaṃcidavacchedakabhedasadbhāvamātreṇa
paramāṇudvayasaṃyogasyāvyāpyavṛttitvaṃ ghaṭata iti vaktuṃ śakyaṃ| sarvādiksaṃyogānāmapi vyāpyavṛttitvāviśeṣe kiṃ diksaṃyogaḥ paramāṇvaṃtarasaṃyogāvacchedakaiti atraiva niyāmakābhāvena sarvadiksaṃyogānāmapi tadavacchedakatvaprāptau avacchekabhedābhāvāt, yathākathaṃcidavacchedakabhedalābhe'pi vyavasthitadeśatvābhāve tayoravyāpyavṛttitvānupapādakatvācca|
anyathā pṛthivyāssaṃkhyāvattve dravyatvamavacchedakaṃ gaṃdhavattve prathivītvamityādyavacchedakabhedasattvena saṃkhyādīnāmavyāpyavṛttitvavyavahāraprasaṃgāt| tasmādvyaṇukotpādakasaṃyoganirvāhārthaṃ paramāṇuṣvavayavarūpapradeśabhedo'vaśyamabhyupeyaḥ| tathā tryaṇukotpādakadvyaṇukatrayasaṃyoganirvāhārthamapi so'bhyupeyaḥ| sa hi kāyapustakasaṃyogohastapustakasaṃyogeneva dvyaṇukasya dvyaṇukāṃtarāraṃbhakaparamāṇunā saha saṃyoge nirvāhyaḥ| kāraṇākāraṇasaṃyogāt kāryākāryasaṃyoga iti tvayā'bhyupagatatvāt| tathā ca dvyaṇukārabhaṃkaparamāṇau tadāraṃbhakaparamāṇvaṃtarasaṃyoge prāksiddhe sthite pradeśabhedaṃ vinā kathaṃ tryaṇukotpattisamaye dvyaṇukāṃtarasaṃyogo'pi syāt, mūrtānāṃ samānadeśatāvirodhādeva| yaditu mūrtatvepi paramāṇuṣu samānadeśatāvirodhamaryādā neṣyate, tadā ṣaṇṇāṃ paramāṇūnāṃ piṃḍarūpaṃ tryaṇukaṃ paramāṇumātraṃ bhavet| tadāhuḥ - "ṣaṭkena yugapadyogāt paramāṇoṣṣaḍaṃśatā| ṣaṇṇāṃ samānadeśatve piṃḍassyādaṇumātraka"iti| prāgādyūrdhvādhāradeśavartibhiḥ ṣaḍhbhiḥ paramāṇubhiḥ saṃbadhyamānomadhyavasthaḥ paramāṇuṣṣaḍaṃśaḥ prāpnoti| bhinnadeśatvātsaṃbandhānāṃ| atha samānadeśāssarve saṃbaṃdhāḥ, tadā sarveṣāmeṣāṃ piṃḍaḥ paramāṇumātraḥ prāpnotītyarthaḥ| samānamidaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ dvyaṇukebhyastryaṇukotpattāvapi| tasmātdvyaṇukatryaṇukādijananatatparimāṇātiśayasidhyarthaṃ paramāṇu ṣaḍaṃ śatvamanicchadbhirapi paramāṇuvādibhiraṃgīkāryaḥ| nanu tadaṃgīkāre tattadaṃśānāmapi uktanyāyena sāṃśatāyāmanavasthā prasajyate| tathācānantāvayavārabdhatvāviśeṣāt merusarṣapayostulyaparimāṇatvaprasaṃgaḥ| atassāṃśatvamapi nāṃgīkārārhamiti cet, tarhi sāṃśatve tvanavasthā niraṃśatve dvyaṇukotpattyādyanupapattiriti pakṣadve'pi samāpataddoṣaṃ paramāṇukāraṇavādavyasanameva tyaktvāsukhībhava| kiṃ ca paramāṇūnāṃ sāṃśatve nāsti doṣaḥ merusiddhārthayoranantāvayavārabdhatve'pi ānantyaviśeṣātparimāṇaviśeṣopapatteḥ| na hi pādmakalpātpūrvaṃ vārāhakalpātparaṃ pātālādūrdhvaṃ ravimaṇḍalādadhaśca avacchedyadeśānantyayordravyatvapṛthivītvādiparāparajātyādhāra vyaktyānantyayo rmerumandarāntargataparamāṇvānantyayośca na tāratamyam| yadi tu paramāṇossāvayavatāyāmanityatvāpattyā svābhyupagatamūlakāraṇatvāsiddhiprasaṃgāt sāṃśatvaṃ neṣyate, tadā dvyaṇukādijananatatprathimānupapattiranivāryā| tasmādanavasthā| paramāṇvanityatvadvyaṇukādijananatatprathimānupapattyānyataradūṣaṇākrāntaḥ paramāṇukāraṇavāda eva heyaḥ| kiṃ ca pralaye niścaleṣu paramāṇuṣu sargādyakāladvyaṇukārambhakasaṃyogotpādakakarmāsambhavena tatsaṃyogāsambhavādapi saheyaḥ| nanu "agnerūrdhvajvalanaṃ vāyostiryakpavanaṃ aṇumanasoścādyaṃ karmetyetāni adṛṣṭakāritānī"tyabhyupeyate| ato'dṛṣṭavaśādutpannena karmaṇā saṃyogassambhavatīti cenna| tadadṛṣṭaṃ yadi prāṇiṣu samavetaṃ, yadi vā paramāṇuṣu, ubhayathā'pi tatkarma na bhaviṣyati| tasya tatkarmakāraṇatve pūrvakalpopārjitasya tasya pralaye'pi sattvena tadānīmapi tatastatkarmotpattyā jagatkalpanāprasaṃgāt| atha "vipākādṛṣṭaṃ karma kāraṇaṃ karmeśvararūpobhayavidhajagatkāraṇasāphalyaprati pādanārthasyeśvaraḥ kāraṇaṃ puruṣakarmasāphalyadarśanā"dityākṣapāda sūtrajātasya vyākhyānāvasare vārtikakṛtā "na brūmaḥ karmānapekṣa īśvaraḥ kāraṇamiti karma puruṣasya īśvaro'nugṛhṇāti ko'nugrahārthaḥ yadyathābhūtaṃ yasya ca yadā pākakālaḥ tattadā tathā ca viniyukta"miti pratipādanāt ato nāyaṃ doṣa iti cenna| harītakyādidravyasyeva ātmaguṇasyādṛṣṭasya vipākāsambhavāt| phalakālāgamo vipākaḥ satu sargādyakālāgama eveti cenna| anantaiḥ prāṇibhirayugapadanuṣṭhitavidhidhakarmajanitādṛṣṭānāṃ sargādyakālaeva phalakālaḥ, natu tataḥ prāgdviparārthātmake pralaye na kopi kṣaṇaiti kalpanasya aprāmāṇikatvāt| nanu sargakālarūpe'hani niraṃtaragatāgatākhannānāṃ prāmināṃ tatsamānaparimāṇaṃ kālaṃ śarvarīrūpaṃ kṛtvā tasmin tān mahāsuṣuptiṃ prāpayya viśiśramayiṣerīśvarasya icchayā pralayamadhye samāgataphalakālānyapi karmāṇi pratibaddhaśaktikāni bhavanti| ata eva brahmahaṃtustaddehapātānantaraṃ kalpaparyantaṃ yātanānubhavaḥ tataḥ paraṃ saptasu janmasu kṣayaregāditaccihnānuvṛttiriti coditaṃ phalaṃ pralayāvyavahitapūrvakāle kṛtabrahmahananasya puṃsaḥ taddehapātānantarameva na bhavatīti cenna| tvanmate nityāyām īśvarecchāyāḥ pralayepi satvāt| mamatu pralaye prāksiddhānāṃ prāṇyadṛṣṭānāmīśvarasyecchāṃtarāṇāṃca satvepi "bahusyāṃ prajāyeya, tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāṃ karavāṇi, nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇī"ti tadānīntanasṛṣṭisaṃkalparūpāṇāṃ bhāvinīnāmīśvarecchānābhāvāt sargābhāvaḥ| evaṃ tarhi yasmin kāle tathā saṅkalparūpeśveracchā bhavīti tvayeṣyate, tatkālopahitaiva nityeśvarecchā mama paramāṇukarmotpipādayiṣā bhaviṣyatīti cet ucyate - mayā kaścit
tādṛksaṃkalpakālaḥ svamanīṣikākalpito na parigṛhyate, kintu "sadevasomyedamagraāsī"dityādi
śrutitadupabṛhmaṇapurāṇetihāsādipramāṇaka dviparārdhātmakamahāśarvaryaṃtarakālaḥ parigṛhyate| na ca taveśvaraṃ tadīyasṛṣṭyādivyāpāraṃ ca yuktibhireva kalpayitumicchataḥ etasmin kāle bahubhavanasaṃkalpaḥ tatobhūtānāṃ krameṇa sṛṣṭiḥ tatastrivṛtkaraṇasaṃkalpastrivṛtkaraṇaṃ ca tatonāmarūpavyākaraṇasaṃkalpaḥ tadvyākaraṇaṃ cetyādikaṃ yuktipathatvamavatarati| na hi rāmacaritādikaṃ rāmāyaṇādipratipannaṃ yuktibhiḥ kalpayituṃ śakyaṃ| yadyaiśvare kālaviśeṣāvacchinnasṛṣṭyādivyāpāre śrutiśśaraṇīkaraṇīyā, tadeśvarepi saiva śaraṇīkartuṃ yukteti yathāśrutyanantakalyāṇaguṇākarastadātadoditvaraissaṅkalpaviśeṣairjagadvyāpāralīlāṃ kurvanneveśvaro'bhyupeyaḥ, natu śrutiṣu svānabhimatāṃśaṃ parityajya nityajñānecchāprayatnamātravān svābhimatasṛṣṭivyāpāramātrakartā'bhyupeyaḥ| dūṣitaścānumānikeśvaraḥ śāstrayonityādhikaraṇe| tenāpi tasya paramāṇukarmotpādanaprakriyayā sraṣṭṛtvaṃ tvadabhimataṃ nirastprāyaṃ| ato'prāmāṇiparamāṇukāraṇavādābhyupagamādasamaṃjasaṃ vaiśeṣikamataṃ| tathā samavāyābhyupagamādapyasamaṃjasaṃ| ayutasiddheṣvavayaviguṇakriyājātiṣu ayutasidhyupapādanārthaṃ hi samavāyobhyupagamyate, tadā samavāyasyāpi sambandhātaramapekṣaṇīyaṃ| ayutasiddhisāmyāt| evaṃ tasya tasyāpi sambandhātarasyāyutasidhyupapādakaṃ sambandhātaramapekṣaṇīyamityanavasthānāt| samavayasyābhāvādivat svarūpasambandhaiṣyata iti cet, avayavyādiṣvapi sa evāstu| nanu avayavyāgīnāmanantatvāt lāghavena sarvānugataekassamavāyaḥ kalpyataiti cenna| eksaya bahūnāṃ ca kalpyatve hi lāghavādekasya kalpanaṃ| iha bahūnāṃ ca kalpyatve hi lāghavādekasya kalpanaṃ| iha bahūnāṃ klṛptatvāt kimarthamekasyāpi kalpanaṃ| nanu bahūnāṃ klṛptatvepi teṣu sambandhatvaṃ kalpyaṃ tattu naikaṃ kalpayituṃ śakyaṃ| avayavyādisvarūpānugataikasaṃnbadhatvānirvacanāt| atonantasambadhatvakalpanāt ekasya samavāyasya tadratasyaikasya sambadhatvasya ca kalpanaṃ tattu naikaṃ kalpayituṃ śakyaṃ| avayavyādisvarūpānugataikasaṃbandhatvānirvacanāt| atonantasaṃbandhatvakalpanāt ekasya samavāyasya tadratasyaikasya sambadhatvasya ca kalpanaṃ laghviti cenna| avayavyādiṣu tattadabhāvādikaṃ prati sambandhatvasyāpi klṛptatvāt, teṣvavayavādinirūpitasambandhatvaklṛptirnāstīti tānyanaṃtāni kalpyānīticenna| nirūpakabhedena sambandhatvabhede samavāyepi tadbhedena tānyanantāni kalpyāni samavāyaśca kalpya iti samavāyapakṣa eva gauravāpatteḥ| avayavaguṇakriyājātīstadvataścāyutasiddhatvena kroḍīkṛtya ayutasiddhanirūpitasambandhatvamekameva samavāye kalpyata iti cenna| evaṃ tāḥ kroḍīkṛtya teṣvekasambandhatvakalpanopapattau samavāyakalpana eva gauravāt| kiṃca avayavāvayavyādiṣu samavāyābhyupagame'pi svarūpasambandhābhyupagamaāvaśyakaḥ| anyathā samavāyaviśeṣeṇa avayavāvayavyādiṣu ādhārādheyavyavasthityabhāvaprasaṃgāt| tasmādavayavādiṣvavayavyādīnirūpitamādhāratvarūpaṃ svarūpasaṃbandhatvamekaṃ avayavyādiṣu avayavādinirūpitamādheyatvarūpaṃ tadanyaditi svarūpasambandhadvayena viṣayībhūtena "iha taṃtuṣu paṭa" ityādipratyayonirvāhyaḥ| samavāyena tannirvahaṇe vāyau rūpasamavāyasatvena vāyau rūpamiti pratītyāpatteḥ| atassamavāyasthale'pi svarūpasambandhasyāvaśyakatve tenaivādhārādheyabhāvapratīteḥ nirvahaṇīyatve ca kiṃ samavāyena| etena yaduktaṃ kāṇādasūtre - "iheti yataḥ kāryakāraṇayossasamavāya" iti tannirastaṃ| ihapratyayasya sarvatrādhārādheyabhāvarūpasvarūpasambandhadvayaviṣayatvāvaśyaṃbhāvena tenaiva viśiṣṭapratyayacāritārthyāt| syādetat iha sūtre varṇitapratyayanirvāhakatvena samavāyasiddhirukteti satyaṃ - natu tannirvāhakatvaṃ viṣayatayā vivakṣitaṃ| tanmate samavāyasyāpratayakṣatvāt| kintu kāraṇatayā avayavakriyādiviśiṣṭabuddhīnāmanugataṃ kiñcitkāraṇaṃ yatsidhyati sasamavāyaiti| maivaṃ - avayavādiviśiṣṭabuddhīnāṃ svayamananugatatvenānugatakāraṇāpekṣābhāvāt viśiṣṭabuddhiṣvatiprasaṃgāt| sphuṭataravivekarahitasambandhidvayagocaraviśiṣṭapratyakṣatvenānatiprasaktenānugamaiti cet tarhi sphuṭataravivekarahitatvenānugatīkṛtāṇāmavayavāvayavyādīnāmeva tatra kāraṇatvamastu kṛtaṃ samavāyakalpanayā| atiprasaktaṃcedamuktaviśiṣṭabuddhitvaṃ abhāvādhikaraṇayorviśiṣṭabuddhau, pītaṃ suvarṇamiti tadupaṣṭaṃbhakayorviśiṣṭabuddhāvapi| nanu daṃḍādiviśiṣṭabudadhisādhāraṇyena pratyakṣaviśiṣṭabuddhimātre anugataṃ kāraṇaṃ kalpyamānaṃ nityatvādekatvācca sarvatra saṃbhavan samavāyaeveti sidhyati| viṣayabhāvaṃ vināpi sarvāsu tāsu tasya kāraṇatvasaṃbhavāditi cenna| saṃyogasyānugatakāraṇasyasaṃbhavāt| saṃtihi cākṣuṣādiviśiṣṭapratyakṣeṣu tattaddravyendriyasaṃyogāḥ kāraṇatvena klṛptāḥ|
śabdapratyakṣatvepyasti śrotramanassaṃyogaḥ| teṣāmeva saṃyogatvenāstu kāraṇatā| kiñca viśiṣṭajñānatvaṃ viśeṣaṇajñānakāryatāvacchedakaṃ pratyakṣatvaṃ indriyakāryatāvacchedakamiti avacchedakāntaropapannakāryatādhikaraṇavṛttitvāt nīlaghaṭatvavadarthavaśasaṃpannaṃ viśiṣṭapratyakṣatvaṃ na kāryatāvacchedakamiti natadavacchedenānugatakāraṇāpekṣāsti| etena tantvādināśe tanniṣṭhāvayavaguṇādikaṃ yannaśyati tatra tanniṣṭhāvayavyādināśatvasyānanugamāt, tatsambandhināśatvasya tatsaṃyuktatṛṇādināśe'tiprasaktatvāt tāvadanugatassambandhaḥ sidhyan samavāyaḥ paryavasyaditi cenna| samavāyāṃgīkāre'pi tantunāśe
tantusamavāyavatāmaṃśūnāṃ nāśaprasaṃgānivāraṇāt| na ca samavāyavatve satyādheyatvarūpasamavatatvamavaccheke niveśanīyaṃ, tathāpi taṃtvāśritatṛṇādināśaprasangatādavasthyāt, samavāyasyaikatvena tantusamavāyasya tṛṇepi sattvāt, ayutasiddhādheyatvasyaiva akliṣṭasya klṛptasya kāryatāvacchedake niveśayituṃ yuktatvācca| naca tathāsati tadayutasiddhādheyasya paṭadhvaṃsādernāśaprasaṃgaḥ| parapakṣepi tantusamavetajātyādināśaprasaṃgataulyāt| tantunāśe tatsamavetakāryanāśa iti niyamaścettarhi tannāśe tadayutasiddhādheyasya vināśinonāśaiti niyamostu| nacadhvaṃsanāśenāpi kiñcidaniṣṭhamāpadyate| prāgabhāvadhvaṃsasya ghaṭasyeva tadhvaṃsasyāpi dhvaṃsopapatteḥ| ghaṭatadhvaṃsayorubhayorapi prāgamāvapradhvaṃsatvāṃgīkāreṇa prāgabhāvānunmajjanasamarthane ghaṭatadhvaṃsaparaṃparāyā ghaṭadhvaṃsatvāṃgīkāreṇa ghaṭānunmajjanasamarthanopapatteḥ| tadevaṃ samavāyāsiddhau taddhaṭitasya samavāyikaraṇatvasyāsidhyā tryaṇukadvyaṇukasamavāyikāraṇatvena dvyaṇukaparamāṇvanumānaṃ na pravartataityatopyasamañjasaḥ paramāṇukāraṇavādaḥ| kiñca samavāyasya nityatvābhyupagamāt tadāśrayāṇāmapi nityatvamabhyupagantavyaṃ| anyathā sambandhināśe saṃyogavat samavāyasyāpi nāśaprasaṅgāt| atassamavāyāśrayatayā tryaṇukasyāpi nityatvaṃ tvayā vaktavyaṃ syādityatopi tatkāraṇatayā dvyaṇukādyanumānāsiddhiḥ| yadyucyeta sambandhināśanāśyatve saṃyogatvaṃ prayojakaṃ| lāghavāt| na saṃbandhatvamupādhitvāditi, tadetthaṃ lāghavamanusaratā jaganmūlakāraṇānumānepyaikyalāghavamanusaraṇīyamiti tena brahmaṇaḥ pradhānasya vā siddherna tena paramāṇusiddhiḥ| naca kāryadravyasyāvayavasaṃyogārabhyatvaniyamasya paṭādiṣu dṛṣṭatvānaikasmāddravyotpattissaṃbhavatīti lāghavamihākiṃcitkaramiti vācyaṃ| ekāvayavirūpeṇa kṣīreṇa dadhyārambhe niyamabhaṅgāt kṣīrāraṃbhakāvayavāḥ parasparaṃ saṃyujya punassaṃyogāntaraṃ labdhvā dadhyārambhata iti kalpanāyāṃ pramāṇābhāvāt kṣīranāśakāraṇānupalabdhivat dadhyutpattivacca tadupapattiḥ na payasaḥ pariṇāmo guṇāṃtaraprādurbhāvādityādyakṣapādasūtrāṇāṃ pramāṇamūlatvābhāvāt sthite'pi dravye pārthivaparamāṇūnāmiva guṇāṃtaraprādurbhāvopapatteḥ| tantusaṃyogapaṭayoḥ kuvindavyāpārādirūpasāmagyaikyena samākālotpattikatayā paṭasya tantusaṃyogārabhyatvāsampratipatteśca| na ca tantusaṃyoganāśe paṭanāśadarśanāt akāraṇanāśena akāryanāśāsambhavāt paṭatantusaṃyogayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvo vaktavya iti vācyam| nāśayorapi sāmagnyaikyena samakālatvopapatteḥ| kiṃca yadi jaganmūlakāraṇaṃ rūpādimatsyāt tadā tatra nityatvaniravayavatvayorviparyayassyāt| rūpādimatāmanityatvasāvayavatvadarśanāditi tarkānugṛhītena tryaṇukamūlakāraṇaṃ rūpādirahitaṃ nityatvānniravayavatvādityanumānena parāhatatvādapi na rūpādivatparamāṇurūpakāraṇasiddhiḥ| vipakṣe bādhakābhāvāt vyāptyasidhyā praśithilamūlastarkaiti cet tryaṇukaṃ samavāyikāraṇārabhyaṃ mahadārambhakatvāditi dvyaṇukaparamāṇusādhakānumānayoḥ ko vipakṣabādhako dṛṣṭaḥ| yena madanumānasya daurbalyaṃ tvadanumānayoḥ prābalyañca syāt| nanu tryaṇukasādhakānumāne'sti vipakṣabādhakatarkaḥ| tathā hi mahattvaparimāṇasya janyatve mahattvāvāntarajātirūpāpakarṣamātraṃ prayojakam| natūtkarṣāpakarṣarūpatadavāntarajātidvayaṃ, gauravāt| tathā ca tryaṇukamahattvasya apakṛṣṭamahattvarūpatayā janyatvena kāraṇāpekṣāyāṃ mahattve svāśrayasamavāyikāraṇagatasaṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogaviśeṣāṇāmeva kāraṇatvāt tryaṇukasya samavāyikāraṇatvamavaśyamabhyupeyamiti, evaṃ tarhyaktarītyaiva tarkamūlavyāptāvapyasti vipakṣabādhakatarkaḥ| rūpādīnāṃ janyatve rūpatvādikaṃ prayojakaṃ anugatatvāllaghutvācca| na tu kāryagatarūpatvādikam| pārthivaparamāṇurūpādyananugatatvāt gurutvācca| tathā ca paramāṇuṣu rūpāṅgīkāre agnisaṃyogājanyā rūpādayaḥ svāśrayakāraṇagatarūpādijanyā iti tvadrītyaiva jalaparamāṇugatarūpādīnāṃ pārthivaparamāṇuśyāmatvasya cotpattāvapekṣitānāṃ rūpādīnāmāśrayatvena paramāṇūnāmavayavāssidhyanto na nivārayituṃ śakyāḥ|
sāvayavattvecānityatvamapyanivāryamiti rūpādīnāṃ janyatve rūpatvādikaṃ prayojakamityetat tvayā'pyavaśyameṣṭavyam| anyathā tryaṇukakāraṇatvena anumīyamāneṣu dvyaṇukeṣu rūpādyasiddhiprasaṅgāt| teṣu rūpādimattvānumāne hi rūpādīnāṃ kāraṇaguṇapūrvakatvaniyamamūlaevahi tatra vipakṣabādhakatarka eṣṭavyaḥ| kiñca mahattvasya janyatve parimāmāpakarṣaḥ prayojako yuktaḥ| ataeva ākāśādigataparamamahatvavyāvṛttisiddheḥ| natu mahatvāpakarṣaḥ, viśeṣarūpatvāt dairghyānanugatatvācca| tathā ca yadi tryaṇukamṛlakāraṇe apakṛṣṭaparimāṇamabhyupagamyeta tadā tasya janyatvena kāraṇaparimāṇāpekṣayā mūlakāraṇasya sāvayavatvaṃ prasajyeteti tatparihārāya tasmin paramamahattvamevābhyupagantavyam| na ca kāryadravyasya svanyūnaparimāṇadravyārabhyatvaniyamāt dīrghaistaṃtubhirdṛḍhatarāvartitaistannyūnadairghyasya viśālaiḥ paṭairāvartitaistannyūnavaipulyasya ca sūtrasyārambhadarśanāt| tasmādbalavatpratyanamānaparāhvataṃ rūpādivajjaganmūlakāraṇānumānam| tathā yadyanityatvādiparihārāya paramāṇūnāṃ rūpādiśūnyatvamucyate, tadā kāryaguṇānāṃ kāraṇaguṇapūrvakvaniyamāsiddhiḥ| tatsiddhaye rūpādimatvāṃgīkāre ca
anityatvādyāpattiriti ubhayathā'pi duṣṭaḥ paramāṇukāraṇavāda iti asamañjasaṃ vaiśeṣikamatam| atassāṃkhyapakṣasya śrutinyāyaviruddhasyāpi satkāryavādādau kvacidaṃśe vaidikaparigraho'sti | vaiśeṣikapakṣasya tu kenāpyaṃśena aparigrahādatyantamanapaikṣaiva kāryā||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
alpānāṃ saṃyogādvipulasyotpattirīkṣitā loke|
tasmādaṇavaḥ kāraṇamanavayavāste'navasthiteśśāntyai||
teṣāṃ pradeśabhedaissaṃyoge kvānavasthiteśśāntiḥ|
nocettadayutayogo'pyastu prathimā kathaṃ kārye||
samavāyikāraṇañca trasareṇonaiva ṣatmanumācum|
bahubādhakasadbhāvāt samavāyasyaiva niṣpramāṇatvāt||
tanmūlakāraṇatvasya ca rūpitvādāvanityatāpatteḥ|
rūpādimatāṃ loke hyavayavavatvādinityatā dṛṣṭā||
iti mahaddīrghādhikaraṇam||
samudāyauśrayahetuke'pi tadaprāptiḥ||17||
itaretarapratyayatvādupapannamiticenna saṃghātabhāvānimittatvāt||18||
uttarotpādecapūrvanirodhāt||19||
asatipratijñoparodhoyaugapadyamanyathā||20||
pratisaṃkhyā'pratisaṃkhyānirodhāprāptiravicchedāt||21||
ubhayathācadoṣāt||22||
ākāśocāviśeṣāt||23||
anusmṛteśca||24||
nāsatodṛṣṭatvāt||25||
udāsīnānāmapi caivaṃ siddhiḥ||26||
mādhyamikayogācārasautrāntikavaibhāṣikasaṃjñāścaturvidhā bauddhāḥ| te sarve'pyādibuddhasya śiṣyāḥ krameṇa sarvaśūnyatvabāhyarthaśūnyatvabāhyārthānumeyatvabāhyārthapratyakṣatvavādinaḥ| tatra "budhyā vivicyamānānāṃ svabhāvo nāvadhāryate| ato nirabhilapyasti nissvabhāvāśca darśitāḥ|| yathā yathā vicāryante tathā tathe"tyādinyāyaiḥ guruṇā sarvaśūnyatvaṃ bodhiteṣu śiṣyeṣu ye guruktaṃ tathaivāṃgīkṛtya paryanuyogaṃ nākurvan te guruktasya śraddhayāṃgīkaraṇāduttamāḥ, tata upari paryanuyogākaraṇādadhamāścetyuttamādhamatvayogāt madhyamāṃ sthitimāsthitā
mādhyamikāḥ| ye tu bāhyārthānāṃ śūnyatvamaṃgīkṛtya kathaṃ vijñānasyāpi śūnyatvaṃ, jagadāndhyaprasaṃgāditi paryanuyogamakurvan, te gurūktāṃgīkaraṇasya ācārasaṃjñayā tanmate paribhāṣitasya tata upari paryanuyogasya yogasaṃjñayā tanmate paribhāṣitasya ca sadbhāvāt yogācārāḥ| guruṇā tān pratyāntaraṃ vijñānamātramasti, tadeva nīlādyākāraṃ na tu tadvyatirekeṇa bāhyaṃ nīlādikamasti sahopalambhaniyamena nīlādīnāṃ tadvijñānānāñcābhedasiddheḥ, bhede gavāśvattanniyamābhāvaprasaṅgāt, tadeva vijñānākārātmakaṃ nīlādikamanādivāsanāvaśāt bahirvadavabhāsata iti bodhite kathaṃ sarvathaiva bāhyārthasya śūnyatvaṃ, bāhyasya nīlāderavabhāsāt| ahamullekhena vijñānasyāntaratayā itamullekhena nīlāderbāhyatayā ca bhede'vabhāsamāne sahopalambhaniyamamātrasyāprayojakasyābhedasādhanākṣamatvāt, sahatvoktyaiva bhedānumateśceti yaiḥ paryanuyuktaṃ, tān prati satyamasti bāhyaṃ nīlādikaṃ na tu tatpratyakṣaṃ anutpannasyāvidyamānasya pratyakṣatvāyogāt, utpannasya kṣaṇikasya sthityabhāvāt|
kiṃ tu utyapadyamānaṃ nīlādivijñāne svākāraṃ samarpya naśyati| tatovijñānagatākāreṇātītannīlādikamanumīyataiti bodhite sati tairitthaṃ śiṣyapraśnānurodhena kiyatparyaṃta mupadeśasūtraṃ pravartiṣyataiti sūtrasyāṃte pṛṣṭe guruṇā'bhihitaṃ - 
bhavantassūtrasyāṃtaṃ pṛṣṭavantassautrāṃtikābhavantviti| ataste sautrāṃtikāḥ| tato vijñānenānumeyaṃ bāhyamitīyaṃ viruddhābhāṣā| tasyaiva vijñānasya tadviṣayapratyakṣatvāt pratyakṣasya kasyacidapyarthasyābhāve vyāptisaṃvedanasthānābhāvena anumānāpravṛtteśceti yaiḥ paryanuyuktaṃ te vaibhāṣikāḥ| tāt prati bāhyapratyakṣatvamapyupadiṣṭaṃ| evaṃ yathāśrutaṃ sva sva praśnottaravākyārthaṃ śraddadhānāste tathaiva matabhedānāsthiṣata| tatra vaibhāṣikādikrameṇa caturvidhānapi bauddhān nirasituṃ krameṇa catvāryadhikaraṇāni| tataḥ kṣapaṇakānnirasitumetamadhikaraṇam| vaidikakāpilakaṇabhuṅnirākaraṇānantaramavaidikāste nirasyanta iti ardhavaināśikanirāsānantaraṃ sarvavaināśikā nirasyanta iti pūrvādhikaraṇena peṭikāsaṅgatiḥ vaibhāṣikādyadhikaraṇānāṃ trayāṇāṃ keṣāṃcit kṣaṇikatvamabhyupagacchantastārkikāḥ ardhavaināśikāḥ| te hi jāṭharāgninā pacyamānānāṃ bhuktapītāhārāvayavānāṃ pratikṣaṇamupacayāpacayavaiṣamyāt avayaviśarīramapi pratikṣaṇamupacayāpacayavadanyadanyadbhavati| yadyapi śarīrasya pratikalamupacayāpacayadarśanaṃ nāsti, tathā'pi varṣadhārānipātaistaṭākajalasyeva ghaṭīyaṃtrokṣepaṇaiḥ kūpajalasyeva cānte taddarśanāt yauktikaṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ kiṃcitkiṃcidupacayāpacayajñānastyeva| āhārāvayavānāṃ śarīrāvayavatprāptyā tadapacayopacayābhyāṃ śarīrāpacayopacayāvaśyambhāvāt| yathā hi candratārakādīnāṃ muhūrtādikālavyavadhānena bahudeśāntaraprāptidarśanāt pratikṣiṇaṃ svalpadeśāṃtaraprāptijñānaṃ yoktikamastītyabhyupagacchanti| evaṃ pratikṣaṇamavaśyambhāvibhiḥ khananapūraṇādibhiḥ bhūgolakasya, nadījalasaṃsargaśīkarotpatanaissamudrasya ca upacayāpacayavattvena kṣaṇikatvamabhuyapagacchantassarvavaināśikāḥ| bauddhāstu sarvasyāpi prapañcasya kṣaṇikatvamabhuyupagacchantassarvavaināśikāḥ| bauddhāstu sarvasyāpi prapañcasya kṣaṇikatvamabhyupagacchantassarvavaināśikāḥ| bauddheṣvapi vaibhāṣika sautrāntikāḥ paramāṇuvādinaiti kāṇādapratyāsattyā tadubhayasādhāraṇaissautrāntikāsādhāraṇaiścadūṣaṇairubhayeṣāṃ prathamādhikaraṇe nirāsaḥ, dvitīyādhikaraṇe kṣaṇikavādasāmyāt yogācārāṇāṃ. tato bauddhatvasāmyāt mādhyamikānāṃ, tadanantaramavaidikatvasāmyāt kṣapaṇakānāmitivivekaḥ| yadyapi kṣapaṇakāapi paramāṇuvādinaḥ, tathāpi vaibhāṣikasautrāṃtikaiḥ kharasnehoṣṇateṇasvabhāvāścaturvidhāḥ paramāmavastairabhyupagamyante| paramāṇūnāṃ tārkikaiḥ sthiratvaikāntyavat kṣaṇikatvaikāṃtyaṃtairabhyupagamyate| kṣapaṇakāstu paramāṇvaikavidhyavādinaḥ sthairyādyanaikāntyavādinaśceti vaibhāṣikasautrāṃntikānāṃ tārkikapratyāsattyatiśayostīti saṃgativiśeṣodraṣṭavyaḥ| tatra paramāṇuvādinovaibhāṣikasautrāntikāevamāhuḥ - rūparasagandhasparśasvabhāvāḥ pārthivāḥ paramāṇavaḥ rūparasasparśasvabhāvāāpyāḥ, rūpasparśasvabhāvāstaijasāḥ, sparśasvabhāvāvāyavīyāḥ, bhūsūdharasamudrabaḍabānalamahāvāyvātmakabhūtarūpeṇa saṃhanyante| tebhyaśca pṛthivyādibhyaḥ śarīrendriyaghaṭādiviṣayarūpabhautikasaṃghātābhavanti| sarvatra saṃghātaḥ avayavasamudāyaeva| na tadvyatirekeṇa avayavidravyāntarotpattirasti tadutpattau tatra cākṣuṣatvādyarthaṃ pūthagrūpādimattvasya abhyupagantavyatvāt| avayavarūpādivyatiriktasya ca avayavini rūpāderanupalambhāt| nahi nānāvarṇaistantubhirārabdhe paṭe tantugatanānārūpa vyatirekeṇa paṭe rūpāntaraṃ, avayavabhedavyavasthitamadhurāmlarasavyatirekeṇa āmraphale rasāntaraṃ atipakvālpapakva
bhāgabhedavyavasthitapūtigandhasurabhindhavyatirekeṇa nāgavallīparṇe gandhāntaraṃ, bhāgabhedavyavasthitakaṭhinasukumārasparśavyatirekeṇakadalīdalesparśāntaraṃ, mūlamadhyāgrabhāgavyavasthitamahadalpālpatara parimāṇavyatirekeṇa vṛkṣe parimāṇāṃtarañca upalabhāmahe| nānāvarṇatantvārabdheṣu paṭeṣu citrākhyaṃ rūpāntaraṃ kaiścidabhyupagatamiti cet tadanyairdūṣitameva| "citraṃ cedekatā nasyāt ekaṃceccitratā kathaṃ| ekaṃca tacca citraṃcetyetaccitrataraṃ ha"diti| rasādiṣu tu avyāpyavṛttitvaṃ kenāpi nābhyupagatam| tasmādaṇuhetuko bhūtasamudāyaḥ bhūtahetukobhautikasamudāyaityevamaṇubhūtahetukasamudāyadvayarūpaeva bāhyārthaḥ| seyaṃ prakriyā'nupapannā| yato'ṇubhūtahetukasamudāyadvaye'bhyupagamyamāne jagadātmakasamudāyabhāvaḥ cetanasya tadviṣayavyavahāraśca na prāpnoti| kutaḥ| aṇunāṃ bhūtānāṃca melanārthavyāpāraṃ vinā hetutve sadā kāryotpattyāpatteḥ, melanahetuvyāpāraviśiṣṭānāmaṇūnāṃ bhūtānāṃca bhūtabhautikahetutvaṃ vaktavyaṃ| na ca tatkṣaṇikatve ghaṭate| yeṣāṃ vyāpārāveśaḥ, teṣāṃ melanakṣaṇe naṣṭatvāt| yeṣāṃ melanaṃ teṣāṃ vyāpārakṣaṇānutpannatvāt| yasyacetanasyeṃdriyasannikarṣaḥ tasya jñānotpattikāle, yasya jñānotpattiḥ tasya hānādivyavahārakāle ca naṣṭatvāt| keṣāṃcinmelanahetuvyāpāreṇānyeṣāṃ melanābhyupagame kasyacidindriyasannikarṣādinā'nyasya vedanadyabhyugame cātiprasaṃgāt| naca vyāpāravatāṃ melanavatāṃcāṇubhūtānāmekasaṃtānavartitvāt indriyasannikarṣavato vedanādimataścetanasya ekasaṃtānavartitvāccānatiprasaṃgaḥ| kṣaṇikasaṃtānavyatiriktasya sthirasya saṃtānasya kṣaṇikatvavādibhiranabhyupagamāt| nanvasthireṣvapi sthiratvabhrāntirūpāvidyā'sti, sāca rāgādihetuḥ, rāgādipāravaśyaṃcāvidyāheturiti avidyārāgādeḥ
pravāhastāvadanubhavasiddhaḥ| tasya śarīrendriyaviṣayādirūpasamudāyabhāvābhāve anupapatteḥ tadākṣiptasamudāyasiddhirapratikṣepyeti cenna| kāraṇābhāvena samudāyabhāvānupapattāvuktāyāṃ kāraṇoktyā hi tadupapattissamarthanīyā| nacāvidyārāgādiḥ paramāṇvādivyāpāranirapekṣassamudāyahetuḥ| śuktirajatabhrāṃtirūpāvidyārajatarāgādinā'pi śukteśśuktirūpasamudāyatāprahāṇena rajatarūpasamudāyatāpattiprasaṃgāt| anyasyāvidyayānyasya rāgādijanane'tiprasaṃgenāvidyārāgādipravāhānupapatteśca| syādetadanyasya karmaṇā anyatra saṃyogajanane'tiprasaṃgaitidoṣo na bauddhamate prasarati| na hi taiḥ kṣaṇikeṣu paramāṇvādiṣu karmasaṃyogāvabhyupagamyete| kintu deśāntarasthena deśāntarasthasyotpādaḥ sakarmapratyayasyālambanaṃ, viprakṛṣṭadeśena sannikṛṣṭadeśasya utpādaḥ sasaṃyogapratyayasya| tataśca yathā nyāyamate nānādikprabhavaśabdamūlakaśabdaparamparājananakrameṇa bahūnāṃ śabdānāṃ melane sati kolāhalapratyayaḥ, evaṃ bahūnāṃ paramāṇūnāṃ tathā puñjībhāvesati bhūbhūdharādipratyayaḥ| dṛṣṭaṃhyekaikasya keśasya dūre dṛśyatvābhāve'pi bahūnāṃ keśānāṃ kavaryādirūpeṇa puñjībhāvesati tatsamudāyasya dṛśyatvaṃ| na cādṛśyasamudāyasyā dṛśyatvaniyamaḥ| aprayojakatvāt| ekaikasya tantossyavanākarṣaṇaśaktyabhāve'pi tatsamudāyasya tadākarṣaṇaśaktivat ekaikasya paramāṇoradṛśyatve'pi tatsamudāyasya tadākarṣaṇaśaktivat ekaikasya paramāṇoradṛśyatave'pi tatsamudāyasya dṛśyatvopapatteḥ| tadāhuḥ - "pratyekaṃ dṛṣṭyayogyatve'pyaṇūnāṃ dṛśyatā bhavet| melanesati tantūnāṃ naukādyākṛṣṭiśaktiva"diti| tasmānna hetvasambhavena samudāyabhāvānupapattiriti cet evamapi hetuhetumadbhāvo nopapadyate| uttaraghaṭakṣaṇotpattidaśāyāṃ pūrvaghaṭakṣaṇasya vinaṣṭatvena tasya uttaraghaṭakṣaṇaṃ prati hetutvānupapatteḥ| bauddhamate vastutaḥ kālo nāsti| udyanneva svarasabhaṃguro ghaṭādiḥ kṣaṇakalpanāmātranimittaṃ bhavati| sa ca ghaṭādiḥ svodayavināśaparikalpitakṣaṇavattvāt kṣaṇika ityupacaryate, vapuṣmān śilāputraka itivaditi teṣāṃ prakriyā| atastanmatamaryādayā ghaṭaeva kṣaṇa iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena ghaṭaḥ kṣaṇa iti grantheṣu vyavahāraḥ| nanu naṣṭasya hetutvāsambhavaḥ, uktaṃ hi teṣāṃ tatvasaṃgrahe - "bhāvānāṃ kāraṇatvaṃ na naṣṭatayā avasthitatayā vā| apitu bhūtatayā| bhūtaśca sthito vā bhavatu, naṣṭo vā| kāraṇāntarapauṣkalye sati svakāryamavilambena karotī"ti| dṛṣṭaṃ hi bījakṛtadohalasaṃskārasya kālāntarabhāvipuṣpaphalādigatavarṇarasaviśeṣahetutvaṃ tasmānnaṣṭatvena hetutvānāpattiriti cet| maivaṃ| yadi kāryadaśāyāmasadapi yadā kadācitpūrvasattvamātreṇa hetussyāt tadā sarvadā sarvatra sarvasyāpyutpattissyāt| ghaṭotpattidaśāyāmaṃkurāderapi utpattissyāt| anādau kālapravāhe naṣṭasya bījādessatvāt| na ca kṣetratatkarṣaṇādisahakāryabhāvaśśaṅkanīyaḥ| tasyāpi naṣṭasya pūrvasattvāviśeṣāt| dohaladravyāṇāṃ tu yāvatkāryotpattisūkṣmarūpeṇānuvṛttirastyeva vṛkṣāyurvedādidohalaśāstraprasiddhā| syādetacciranaṣṭasya
nirvyāpārasya mābhūddhetutvaṃ, svarūpeṇa svavyāpārarūpeṇa vā pūrvakṣaṇavartinastu syāt| avaśyañca kāraṇasya pūrvavṛttitvaṃ vācyam| tenaiva kāraṇatvopapattau kiṃ kāryaṃkālavartitvena| loke ca vinaṣṭasya bījasya aṅkure, kṣīrasya dadhni, mṛtpiṃḍasya ghaṭe ca kāraṇatvaṃ dṛṣṭam| yadi tu kāraṇasya kāryakālavṛttitvamapekṣyeta, tadā bījādīnāṃ vināśoṃkurādikāraṇaṃ pūrvaghaṭakṣaṇavināśa uttaraghaṭakṣaṇakāraṇamityastviti cet| yadyevaṃ kāryakālāsataḥ kāraṇatvamiṣyate, "adhipatisahakāryā lambanasamanantarapratyayāḥ catvāraḥ cittacaittahetava"iti hi bauddhānāṃ pratijñā| tathā hi buddhasūtram - "caturvidhān hetūn pratītya cittacaittyā utpadyanta" iti| adhipatirindriyaṃ| taddhi jñānasya rūpādiṣu pañcasu ekaikamātraviṣayatvaniyāmakam| niyāmakaśca loke adhipatirucyate| sahakāri ālokādikaṃ| ālambanaṃ ghaṭādikaṃ| samanantarapratyayaḥ pūrvajñānam| etān caturvidhān hetūn prāpya cittaṃ jñānaṃ caityāḥ tadabhinnāssukhādayaśca jāyanta iti pratijñārthaḥ| iyaṃ hīyeta pratijñā| pūrvakṣaṇavṛttitvābhāve kāraṇatvāyogāt| uttarakṣaṇavṛttitvābhāve vartamānatvagrāhipratyakṣaviṣayatvāyogāt| anyathā kāraṇasya viṣayatāsidhyarthaṃ tasyāpi kāryotpattikṣaṇe sthityabhyupagamyeta, tadā saṃprayogajñānayorhetuhetumatoḥ yaugapadyaṃ syāt| etena pūrvakṣaṇa vināśasyottarakṣaṇakāraṇatvamapi nirastam| tathā satyadhipatyādikāraṇatvapratijñāvirodhāt| kiṃ ca parābhimato nirodhākhye vināśa eva na saṃbhavati| evaṃ hi te kalpayanti| sarvatra niranvayo niranuvṛttika eva sa nirodhaśabde, nocyate, sa dvividhaḥ sthūlassūkṣmaśca| mudgarapātānantaraṃ sarvairupalabhyamāno ghaṭanāśassthūlaḥ pratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ, pratisaṃkhyā viṣayatvapratikūlā tadasatvagrāhiṇī laukikānāṃ buddhiḥ, tadviparītaḥ apratisaṃkhyānirodhassūkṣmaḥ, laukikopalabdhyayogyoyuktyā sādhyamānaḥ pratikṣaṇavināśa iti dvividho'pyayaṃ niranvayavināśo na sambhavati| avicchedāt| sadaivāvicchinnatayā'nuvartamānasya dravyasya pūrvāvasthāprahāṇottarāvasthāpattibhyāmeva nāśotpattivyavahāraḥ| na tu dravyasya kvidapi niranvayavināśo'stītyārambhaṇādhikaraṇe samarthitatvāt| ato vināśasya kāraṇatvaṃ na sambhavati| "ākāśaṃ dvau nirodhau ca nityaṃ trayamasaṃskṛtam| buddhibodhyaṃ trayādanyat saṃskṛtaṃ kṣaṇikañca ta"diti
bauddhaiḥ "saṃskṛtaṃ kāryamevānityaṃ, asaṃskṛtamakāryamākāśāditrayaṃ tu nitya"miti nityatvenābhyupagatasya nirodhadvayasya kāraṇatvānupapatteśca, avicchedenānuvartamānasya kāraṇatve sadā sarvatra sarvakāryotpattiprasaṅgāt| tatsahakāritvenābhimatānāmapi nirodhadvayasya nityatvāt| kiṃ ca nirodhadvayaṃ ākāśaṃ ceti trayamavasthātmakaṃ tucchamiti bauddhā manyante| tatra tucchāt kathaṃ jagadutpattiḥ? tucchādutpattau tucchasya nirviśeṣatvena kāraṇaviśeṣakṛtakāryaviśeṣa vyavasthityasambhavāt ghaṭakṣaṇānantaraṃ sarvasya jagata utpattissyāt| tucchādutpadyamānañca kāryaṃ tucchameva syāt| tasya sadrūpatayā pratītirna syāt| suvarṇarucakādinyāyena kāraṇānusāritvāt kāryasya| apica - ākāśamapi tucchamiti bauddhairucyate| tatkathamupapadyate| pratīyate hi spaṣṭamākāśaṃ "atra śyenaḥ patati, atra gṛdhra"iti śyenādipatanadeśatvena| nanviyaṃ pratītiścandrasūryālokaviṣayatvenopapadyate| mahāndhakāre "khadyota iha patatī"ti pratītiḥ khadyotaprakāśaviṣayatvenopapadyate| "iha candra iha sūrya" iti pratītirapi tattadālokaviṣayatvenopapadyate| tadāśritasyāpyālokasya svarūpasambandhaviśeṣeṇa tadādhāratayā pratītirapyupapadyate| "vane tilaka"iti vṛkṣasamudāyarūpasya vanasya svarūpasambandhaviśeṣeṇa tilakavṛkṣādhāratvapratītidarśanāt| ākāśasyāpi hi śyenādhāratā svarūpasambandhaviśeṣeṇaivopapādanīyā| nibiḍamūrtadravyasya bhūtalāderiva tasya mukhyatayā gurudravyādhāratvāsambhavāt| kiṃ ca - "iha pakṣī, neha pakṣī"ti ādhāratvānādhāratvapratītidvayaṃ avayavabhedāvacchedena śakyamupapādayitumāloke| natvākāśe| tasya niravayavatvāt| na cākāśe'pi tattadadhaḥ kṣitibhāgabheda eva avaccedakabhedasasyāditi vācyaṃ| ūrdhvādharadeśayoḥ "iha pakṣī, neha pakṣī"ti pratītyostadabhāvāt| tasmācchyenapatanādipratīterālokaviṣayatvasambhavāt ākāśe sā na pramāṇamiti cet ucyate - astitāvat "prāgbhāge śyenaḥ patati pratyagbhāge gṛdhra" ityādipratītiḥ| tasyāṃ prāgdeśādirupādhāraḥ pratīyate| sa evākāśaḥ| tadvyatirekeṇa diśo'naṃgīkārāt| tasyaiva tattadupādhibhedena prāgādirūpatvopapatteḥ| na ca vācyaṃ tattadupādhisambandhena āloka eva prāgbhāgādirūpo'stviti| "prāgbhāge candrālokaḥ pratyagbhāge tama" ityādipratītiṣvapi ālokāderapyādhāratvena prāgādideśapratīteḥ| evaṃ tarhi prāgbhāge'vakāśostītyādivyavahāreṇa avakāśātmakādākāśādapyanyastadādhāro deśassyāditi cet na| tatrāvakāśaśabdasya nibiḍamūrtadravyāvasthānavirodhimūrtadravyāntaranirodhābhāvaparatvāt| ata eva
"prāgbhāge'vakāśo'sti na pratyagbhāge bhuñjānairnibiḍa" iti kvaciddeśe avakāśaniṣedho'pi dṛśyate| na hyākāśasya tatra niṣedhassambhavati| sarvagatatvāt| asarvagatatve cāvaraṇāpāye'pyavakāśābhāvaprasaṅgāt| nahi pūrvamasannākāśaḥ| evaṃ ca mūrtadravyābhāva evāvakāśaḥ tattadupādhyavacchinnaḥ prāgādivyahārālambanamastītyapi śaṅkā nirastā| tasya deśādheyatvena pratīteḥ, "prāgbhāge'vakāśo nāsti tatra nīrandhramāsate dvijā" iti āvaraṇābhāvarūpāvakāśapratiṣedhe'pi prāgādideśasattvavyavahārācca| nacaiva māvaraṇābhāvarūpāvakāśasyeva prāgbhāgādirūpadeśasyāpi deśādheyatvena vyavahāro'sti| yena tato'pi vyatirikto deśassidhyet| ayaṃ prāgdeśa ityādireva hi vyavahāraḥ| na tvasmin deśe prāgdeśe ityādirūpaḥ| "ihākāśa" ityapi laukikapratītestadvyatirikto deśassyāditi cenna| laukikānāmākāśapratīterupari paritaścāvāṅmukhendranīlamahākaṭāhavadavabhāsamāne meruśikhara ratnaprabhāviśeṣatvenāgamasiddhe nīladravya eva vyavasthitatvāt tasya digviśeṣādheyatvena pratītyavirodhāt| deśarūpasyākāśasya "idānīmiha ghaṭa" ityādi pratītiṣu kālavat padārthaviṣayatvena bhānaṃvinā kvacidapi laukikapratītau svātaṃtryeṇa bhānānaṅgīkārāt| tasmādākāśasya prāmāṇikatvāt "ākāśaṃ nirodhadvayamiti tucchatrayameva nityaṃ, sadrūpamanyatsarvaṃ kṣaṇika"mityetadayuktaṃ| nanu kṣaṇikatvaṃ prāmāṇikaṃ kathaṃ pratyākhyāyate| pratyakṣaṃ hi vartamānavastuviṣayaṃ, avartamānādatītāt pūrvakṣaṇavartino'nāgatāccottarakṣaṇavartinassvaviṣayaṃ vyāvṛttamavagamayati| yathā "nīlo ghaṭa" iti nailyaviśiṣṭapratyakṣaṃ aruṇādivyāvṛttaṃ ghaṭamavagamayatīticet ucyate - udāhṛtamabhijñājñānaṃ ghaṭasya vartamānakālasambandhamiva "so'yaṃ ghaṭa" iti pratyabhijñānaṃ tasyāvartamānakālasambanadhamapi viṣayīkaroti| atastadanusārādekasya vartamānāvartamānakāladvaya sambandhasiddhau kramikatvena kāladvayasyāvirodhopapatteśca vartamānakālasambandhaviṣayaṃ jñānamayogavyavaccedamavagamayet nānyayogavyavacchekaṃ svāvacchedena vā'nyayogavyavacchedaṃ| na tu sarvātmanā| ata eva citrapaṭe nailyajñānaṃ nānīlavyāvṛtiṃta gamayati| pradeśaviśeṣāvacchedena vā tāṃ gamayati| na hi dharmayoravirodho dharmibhedenaivopapādanīyaḥ| api tu sāmānādhikaraṇyapramityanudayenāpi| tadudaye tu svamanīṣikāmātrakalpito virodho na prāmāṇikānāṃ hṛdayaṃgamaḥ| nanu smṛtipratyakṣayoḥ klṛptakāraṇābhyāṃ "sa" iti "ayaṃ ghaṭa" iti ca smṛnyanubhavarūpaṃ jñānadvayameva bhavitu marhati| na pratyabhijñārūpamekajñānamiti cenna| tadidaṃ mayā nihitaṃ rajatamiti tadarthina idamarthe pravṛttidarśanena pratyabhijñānasyaikajñānatvasiddhau tatra kāryabalāt tatra saṃskāra pratyāsattisahitamindriyaṃ kāraṇamiti kalpanopapatteḥ| nanu tathā'pi pratyabhijñābhrāntirūpaiva
kramasaṃvardhamāneṣu tarugulmalatādiṣu santatasantanyamānābayavopacayāpacayavatsu girisamudrādiṣvatayantavilakṣaṇeṣu bālavārdhakyāpannaśarīreṣvapi dṛśyate| ataḥ kūṭasākṣiṇī pratyabhijñā na kvāpi pramāṇamiti cenna| udāhṛtapratyabhijñānasyāvasthābhede'pyavasthāvaddravyaikyaviṣayatvena prāmāṇyopapatteḥ| na hi pratyabhijñānasya avasthaikyaṃ viṣayaḥ yenāprāmāṇyaṃ bhavet| kiṃ tvavasthāvaddravyaikyameva| na ca āgamāpāyyavasthātirekeṇānekāvasthānuyāyidravyameva nāstīti vācyam| tasminnantata udāhṛtapratyabhijñānāmeva prāmāṇyasidhyā tatpratyākhyānāyogāt| jvālālūnapunarjātakeśanakhādipratyabhijñāmātrasya sādṛśyanibandhanabhrāntitvakalpanamapi na yuktam| vādhitajñānadṛṣṭāntena abādhitajñānānāmapyaprāmāṇyakalpane pramāṇena vastusiddhimātrasya lopaprasaṅgāt| etena yadbauddhā vadanti - "smṛtiḥ pūrvānubhūte'rthe darśanaṃ vārtamānike| tayoratyantabhede ca kenābhedaḥ pratīyatām|| rūpaśaktyādibhedena bālādvṛddho'tibhedavān| parasminpūrvarūpasya na leśo'pyupalabhyate|| parasyāpi na pūrvatra kathamekantu tadbhavet| avasthānāmayaṃ bhedo nāvasthānavato yadi|| avasthā eva dṛśyante na svapne'pi tato para"miti| nanu idaṃ sarvaṃ kṣaṇikatvanirākaraṇahetujālaṃ vaibhāṣikamata iva na sautrāntikame pravartate| tatra yatpratyakṣaviṣayasya tatkāraṇatvaṃ na syāditi pratyabhijñāvirodhāpādanaṃ tattāvannapravartate| sā hi pratijñā bāhyārthapratyakṣatvavādināmeva| yacca kṣaṇikatvakalpanasya pratyabhijñāpratyakṣeṇa bādhavacanaṃ na tadapi pravartate| bāhyārtheṣu pratyakṣapratyabhijñāyāmasiddheḥ| evañca "ghaṭādi kṣaṇikaṃ arthakriyākāritvāt śarīrādivaditi bauddhānāṃ kṣaṇikatvānumānamapi pratyabhijñānabādhāsidhyā nirupaplavaṃ| asti cātravipakṣabādhakatarkasssatvocchittiprasaṃgaḥ| arthakriyākāryatvameva hi sattvaṃ| na tatonyajjātyādirūpaṃ| pramāṇābhāvāt| taccanthāyi vapakṣe na saṃbhavati| tathā hi - sthāyī bhāvaḥ svīyāmarthakriyāṃ krameṇa kuryāt yugapadvā| ādye kālāṃtarabhāvinīṃ pratyapi idānīṃ samartho vā na vā|
prathametābadinānīmeva kuryāt| nahīdānīmasamarthaḥ kālāntare samartho bhavati| yugapaditi pakṣe tvidānīmeva kartavyasya sarvasya kṛtatvāt kālāntare samarthe bhavati| yugapaditi pakṣe tvidānīmeva kartavyasya sarvasya kṛtatvāt kālāntare tasyāsattvaṃ bhavet| nanu iha kṣaṇikatvānumāne asti prabalaḥ pratikūlatarkaḥ| kṣaṇikatve hi tvaduktavipakṣabādhakatarkopi na pravartate| tadupayogyāpādyāpādakabhūyassahacāradarśanopādhyabhāvāvadhāraṇa vyāptigrahaṇādīnāmaikādhikaraṇyaniyamāt| maivaṃ| evamādīnāmaikādhikaraṇyaniyame kṣaṇikatvavādināmasaṃpratipatteḥ| atiprasaṃgaśaṃkā tu kāryakāraṇabhāvasya niyāmakatvena nivāraṇīyā| caitramaitrādayohi pratyekaṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvāpannaniraṃtarasantanyāmānālayavijñānakṣaṇanicayātmakāḥ| tasmāt sautrāṃtikapakṣe na kopi doṣa ityadhikayā pratyavatiṣṭhamānānāṃ sautrāṃtikānāṃ matamitthaṃ - jñānagatairnīlādyākāraiḥ bāhyārthagatāste'numīyaṃte| indriyasannikṛṣṭena nīlādyarthena jāyamānaṃ hi jñānaṃ svayamapyarthavannīlādyākāraṃ bhavati| tajjñānaṃ svaprakāśatayā svātmānaṃ viṣayīkurvat svagataṃ nīlādyarthino bahiḥ pravṛttistu nāparokṣajñānāt| kintu tadanantarabhāvina ānumānikajñānāt| yathā vāyuniṣevaṇārthinaśśākhācalanaṃ dṛṣṭvā vakṣamūle pravṛttiḥ| na cākṣuṣaśākhācalanajñānāt| kintu talliṃgavāyusañcāraviṣayānumānikajñānāditi| tatredaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ - indriyasannikṛṣṭabhāvakṣaṇānantarakṣaṇe hi jñānaṃ jāyate| tatra tasya bhāvakṣaṇasya svadharmasaṃkramaṇanimittatvaṃ na yujyate| asataḥ kusumādeḥ sphaṭikādiṣu svadhramasaṃkramaṇanimittatvādarśanāt| tathā'pi nīlādisaṃkramaṇābhyupagame tadgataṃ purovartitvanedīyastvadavīyasatvajaḍatvādikamapi saṃkrāmati na vā| saṃkrāmaticet, āntaratvajñānatvādestadvirodhino'vabhāso na syāt| sphuṭike lauhityasaṃkrāntau śauklyasyeva| atha na saṃkrāmati darpaṇe mukhagataprāṅmukhatvanedīyastvādivat tadā liṃgābhāvāttadavabhāso na syāt| ataḥ kṣaṇikavāde bāhyārthapratyakṣasyeva tadanumānasyāsambhavāt atasteṣāṃ vyavahārasidhyarthaṃ sthāyitvamevāṅgīkaraṇīyam| kṣaṇikatvena bhāvānāṃ yānīṣṭaprāptyaniṣṭaparihārārthāni laukikāni vihitāni ca karmāṇi teṣāṃ yaḥ kartā sa na tatphalabhoktā| kiṃ tvanya evetyāpatterudāsīnānāmapi phalasiddhissyāt| kasyāpi karmasu pravṛttirna syāt| bhāvibhokraikyabhramāt pravṛttiriti cenna| kṣaṇikatvaniścayavataḥ pravṛttirnasyāt| bhāvibhokraikyabhramāt pravṛttiriti cenna| kṣaṇikatvaniścayavataḥ pravṛttyanupapatteḥ| nanu bhedagrahe'pi putrapautrādiṣaramparānubhāvyaphalecchayā karmasu pravṛttidarśanāttathehāpi pravṛttissyāt| tatra kāryakāraṇabhāvasya niyāmakatvaklṛpteriti cet| tarhi pitṛkṛtānāṃ sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ phalaṃ pitranubhavādiphalasmaraṇādikañca putrapautrādiparamparāyāṃ bhavet| kāryakāraṇabhāvasya svakīyavijñānasantāna iva tasyāmapi sattvāt| tasmāt kāryakāraṇabhāvasyāniyāmakatvāt phalādivyavasthāsiddhaye pratyabhijñāprāmāṇyanirvāhāya ca ātmano'nyeṣāṃ ca sthairyamevāṅgīkāryam| kṣaṇikatvānumānasya vipakṣabādhakatarkastu na pravartate| samarthasyāpi sahakārisamavadhānakramāt
kāryakramopapatterityādikṣaṇikatvanirākaraṇaprapañco'nyatra draṣṭavyaḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
ukte vaibhāṣikeṇa kṣaṇabhavasamudāyadvaye sannikarṣajñānopādānahānakṣiti jaladahanādyudbhavā no ghaṭante|
jñānākārānumeyaṃ tadasadapi tadā vakti sautrāntikākhyo
naṣṭārthākārasaṃkrāntatyaghaṭanavacasā bodhanīyassamandaḥ||
iti samudāyādhikaraṇam||
nābhāva upalabdheḥ||27||
vaidharmyācca na svapnādivat||28||
nabhāvo'nupalabdheḥ||29||
kṣaṇikatve nīlādyarthānāṃ pratyakṣatvamanumeyatvaṃ vā na ghaṭata iti vaiśeṣikasautrāṃtikamatayoruktaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ na yogācāramate pravartate| nīlādyarthanāmāntaravijñānarūpatvena svaprakāśaistaireva tatpratyakṣīkaraṇasaṃbhavāt taditi pratyavasthānāt saṅgatiḥ| idānāmiha yogācāramataṃ ghaṭapaṭādijñānānāṃ tattadarthāsādhāraṇyaṃ tattadarthasārūpyamantareṇa
na ghaṭata ityavaśyaṃ jñāne'rthasarūpaṃ rūpamāstheyam| tāvataiva sarvavyavahāropapatteḥ| jñānākāranīlādivyatirekeṇa bāhyārthakalpanā nīlādīnāṃ vijñānākāratve ca "nīlādikaṃ jñānābhinnaṃ bhāsamānatvāt jñānasahopalambhaniyatatvāt jñānava"dityanumānañca| na ca sahatvasya bhedagarbhatvāddvitīyaheturviruddha iti vācyam| jñānopalambhavyāpyopalambhaviṣayatvasya tadarthatvāt, tadavinābhāvarūpasya ca vyāpyatvasyābhede'pi sattvāt| nacābhede "idaṃ jānāmī"ti kriyākarmabhāvarūpabhedāvabhāsavirodhaḥ| tasya dvicandrajñānavadbhrāntitvāt| tadāhuḥ "sahopalambhaniyamādabhedo bhedataddhiyoḥ| bhedastu bhrāntivijñānaiḥ dṛśyaścandra ivādvaya" iti| bāhyasya nīlādeḥ kathamāntarajñānabhedāvabhāso bhrama iti cet, bāhyatvāvabhāso'pi bhrama eva| uparisthitānāṃ nakṣatrādīnāmudyatāṃ bhūmisthatvāvabhāsavat| etena "ahaṃ jānāmī" ti kartṛkarmabhāvena bhedāvabhāso'pi vyākhyātaḥ| tadapyāhuḥ - "avibhāgo'pi budhyātmā viparyāsitadarśanaiḥ| grāhyagrāhakasaṃvittibhedavāniva lakṣya" iti| nanu vastuta ekasya jñānasya "ahamidaṃ jānāmī"ti kartṛkarmakriyātmatayā bhedenaiva sphuraṇaṃ kathamiti cet, pūrvapūrvavāsanāvaśāt| ghaṭākārajñānotpattyanantaraṃ kapālākārajñānanotpattistu vāsanāvaicitryāditi| idamayuktaṃ| kartuḥ kriyākarmatayā bāhyatvena pratyakṣānāṃ nīlādyupalabdhau jāgratyāṃ teṣāṃ jñānābhedānumānānāmanutthānāt| na ca vācyaṃ "bāhyaṃ nīlādikamupayadbhirapi jñānānāṃ nīlādyākāratvamanubhavasiddhamaṃgīkāryaṃ| tenaiva sarvavyavahāropapattau kiṃ bāhyārthakalpanayā| na hi nīlādyākāradvayamupalabhyata" iti| yato nīlādyarthaviśeṣaviṣayecchādivyavahārānuguṇyameva jñānasya nīlādyākāraḥ| natu bāhyārthasyeva mukhyaṃ nīlādyākāratvamasti| nīlaśītoṣṇādisamūhālaṃbanānāṃ parasparaviruddhānekarūpatvāpatteḥ| svapnadṛṣṭāṃtena jñānānāṃ bāhyārthaśūnyatvakalpanamapi nayuktaṃ| bādhābādhābhyāṃ svapnajñānānāmapi tādātvikaviṣayatvācca| vāsanāvaicitryāt vicitrajñāvodayasamarthanamapyayuktaṃ, niranvaya vināśavāde vāsanādhānasyāpyasaṃbhavāt||
saṃgrahakārikā||
vijñānameva tripuṭaṃ yogācārasya jalpataḥ|
nihnutirvedyaveditroḥ upalabdhyaiva vāryate||
iti upalabdhyadhikaraṇam||
sarvathā'nupapatteśca||
vijñānamātrāstitvavādiyogācāramatanirākaraṇāntaraṃ tadapyapalapatāṃ mādhyamikānāṃ mataṃ nirākriyata iti saṃgatiḥ| te khalu evamāhuḥ - neṣṭantadapi dhīrāṇāṃ vijñānaṃ pāramārthikaṃ| ekānekasvabhāvena viyogādviyadabjavat|| vijñānānāmapi nīlapītaśītoṣṇādyekānekasvabhāvavatvavikalpadausthyānna paramārthasat atastucchatvameva tatva"miti| idamayuktaṃ| tucchatvaṃ pratijānānasya tattucchatvaṃ sattvarūpamiti vā asattvarūpamiti vā prakārāṃtaramiti vā abhiprāyaḥ| sarvathā'pi nasaṃbhavati| loke
sattvāsatvayoḥ nityānuvartamānadravyāvasthāviśeṣatvena ārambhaṇādhikaraṇe pratipādatatayā tucchatvaṃ sattvameveti vā, asattvameveti vā, prakārāntaramiti vā, upapādanāsambhavāt sarveṣvapi kāryeṣu sataeva kāraṇatvena kevalādasataḥ prakārāntarāpannādvā kāryotpattayabhāvāt| etena abhāvātmakatvameva tucchatvaṃ, catuṣkoṭivinirmuktatvaṃ vā tucchatvaṃ| "nasannāsannasadasat nacāpyanubhayātmakaṃ| catuṣkoṭivinirmuktaṃ tattvaṃ mādhyamikāvidu"ritivacanāditi nirastaṃ| bhāvātiriktābhāvānagīkāreṇa abhāvakāraṇatokteḥ satkāraṇavādaeva paryavasānāt| sadbuddhiśabdavat sattvaniṣedhādibuddhiśabdānāmapi deśakālaparicchinnavastuviṣayatvena tatastucchatvāsiddheḥ| idamidānīmasti idamevaṃrūpabhavatīti sattvabuddhiśabdayorapi deśakālavastuparicchinnasattvāviṣayattvenaiva loke prasiddhatayā kvacidapi nirūpādhikaniṣedhāsiddheḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
sarvaśūnyatvavādastu sarvathānopapadyate|
yasmātsadasadityuktī vastvavasthātadāśraye||
iti sarvathānupapattyadhikaraṇam||
naikasminnasaṃbhavāt||31||
evañcātmākārtsnyam||32||
na ca paryāyādapyavirodhovikārādibhyaḥ||33||
antyāvasthiteścobhayanityatvādaviśeṣaḥ||34||
nirastāssaugatāḥ| jainānirasyante| te kilamanyante - jīvadharmādharma pudgalakālākāśākhyaṣaḍdravyātmakaṃjagat nirīśvaraṃ| tatra jīvā baddhayogasiddhamuktāstrividhāḥ| dharmo gatimatāṃ gatiheturvyāpī dravyaviśeṣaḥ| adharmassthitiheturvyāpī dravyaviśeṣaḥ| pudgalastvaṇubhūdharasamudrādiḥ| "abhūdastibhaviṣyatī"ti vyavahāraheturaṇudravyaviśeṣaḥ kālaḥ| ākāśo'pyekaḥ| kiṃtvanantapradeśa iti| punaścāparamapi saṃgrahaṃ kurvanti| jīvājīvāsravabandhanirjarasaṃvaramokṣā iti| jīvā uktāḥ| ajīvo jīvabhogyaṃ sarvaṃvastujātaṃ| āsravatyanena jīvo ghaṭādijñānarūpeṇetyāsrava indriyasaṃghātaḥ| kecitvāsravati jīve gacchatisati sarvatrānugacchatīti karmabandha āsrava ityāhuḥ| tadāha jinadattaḥ "āsravaḥ karmaṇā bandho nirjarastadvimocana"miti| bandhaḥ ghātikarmacatuṣkāghātikarmacatuṣkabhedhenāṣṭavidhaḥ| tatrādyacatuṣkaṃ jīvasya jñānadarśanasukhavīryaguṇānāṃ mokṣadaśāyāmāvirbhaviṣyatāṃ pratighātakaṃ pravāhānādipāparūpam| dvitīyacatuṣkaṃ śarīra saṃsthānatadabhimānatatsthiti tataprayuktasukhādi nimittapuṇyamiśritatvāt puṇyarūpam| nirjaro niśśeṣaṃ jīryatyanena kāmakrodhādiriti vyutpattyā keśolluṃchanataptaśilārohaṇadirūpaṃ tapaḥ| saṃvara indriyanirodhaḥ| svābhāvikātmasvarūpāvirbhāvo mokṣaḥ| kecittu satatordhvagamanaṃ mokṣaḥ| bandhamuktasya hyūrdhvagamanaṃ svabhāvaḥ| yathā pañjaramuktaśukasya, yathā vā nirbhinnasya pariṇatairaṇḍabījasya, yathāvā dṛḍhapaṅkaliptasya jalanimajjanaprakṣīṇapaṅkalepasya śuṣkālābuphalasya| ihvanādikālapravṛttabandhavigamāt satatordhvagamanaṃ sarveṣāṃ lokānāmupari sthito niravadhiralokākāśassatatordhvagamana syāvakāśa ityāhuḥ| tathā paṭhanti - "gatvā gatvā nivartante candrasūryādayo grahāḥ| adyāpi na nivartante tvalokākāśamārgagā"iti|| aparetvāvirbhūtajñānādiguṇasyoparideśāvastam mokṣaityāhuḥ| sarvametadvastujātaṃ saptabhaṅgīnayena yojayanti| "syādasti, syānnāsti, syādasti ca nāsti ca, syādavaktavyaṃ, syādasti cā vaktavyañca, syānnāsticāvaktavyañca, syādasti ca nāsticāvaktavyañce"ti| atra satvāsatvasadasatva sadasadvilakṣaṇatvarūpānirvacanīyatva viṣayāścatvāraḥ prakārāḥ| punaścānirvacanīyatvena saha satvāsatvasadasatvasaṃyojanena trayaḥ prakārā ityevaṃ saptaprakārāvādibhirutprekṣituṃ śakyāḥ| te sarve'pi prakārā jainairīṣadīṣatsvāṅgīkṛteṣu sarveṣvapi padārtheṣu varṇyate| syācchabda īṣadartha iti kecit| anaikāntyārthaṃ ityanye| ubhayathā'pi tiṅantapratirūpakamavyayam| satve pradarśitaḥ saptabhaṅgīnayassyānnityaṃ syādanityamityādirūpeṇa nityatvabhinnatvādidharmeṣvapi samāna iti tadidaṃ jainamatamapi na yuktam| ekasminvinopādhibhedaṃ viruddhānekadharmasamāveśāsambhavāt| nanvekasminnapi deśakālādyupādhibhedena viruddhadharmasamāveśo'bhyupagamyate| nanvekasminnapi deśakālādyupādhibhedena viruddhadharmasamāveśo'bhyupagamyate| sa na tatropādhibhedassambhavatītyetāvatā'bhyupagamyate| sa na tatropādhibhedassambhavatītyetāvatā'bhyupagamyate| kintu pramāṇavattvena| ata eva piṃḍatvaghaṭatvayoḥ kālopādhibhedena samāveśamabhyupagacchatā'pi siddhāntinā jīvatveśvaratvādīnāṃ tathā samāveśe pramāṇannāstīti teṣāṃ yatkiṃcidupādhibhedena samāveśo nābhyupagamyate| evañca pramāṇādhīna eva dharmasamāveśaḥ| na tūpādhisambhavādhīna iti sthite satyatvādikaṃ
vyavasthāpayadbhistaistairvādibhirūpapāditāni satvāsatvasadasattvādiṣu pramāṇānīti teṣāṃ samāveśaḥ kuto na syāditi cenna| taistairvādibhirutprekṣitānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāmāṇyasaṃbhavāt parasparavirodhitvena keṣāṃcidābhāsatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt| anyathā prapaṃcasattvādvivādibhistadasatyatvādau doṣāṇāmapyumānatvena teṣāmapyāropyatvāpattyā'naikāntyavādinastattadvādyukta doṣabhāktvasyāpi prasaṃgāt| etena yaduktaṃ syādvādamaṃjaryāṃ - "anyonyapakṣapratipakṣabhāvādyathāpare matsariṇaḥ pravādāḥ| na yānaśeṣānaviśeṣamicchannapakṣapātī samayastathāna" iti, tannirastaṃ| apakṣapātena sakalasamayoktasattvādisaṃgrahe tattaduktāsatvadoṣāṇāmapi saṃgrahāpatteḥ| nanu tarhi lokāvirodhenaiva saptabhaṃgīnayaṃ samarthayāmahe| sarvasaṃpratipannena tāvadekasya kālabhedena satvāsatve| atastayossvasvakālavivakṣayā astīti nāstīti ca bhaṃgadvayaṃ kramavivakṣayā
sadasadidamiti tṛtīyabhaṃgaḥ yugapatsatvāsatvavivakṣayā'nirvacanīyamiti cutarthabhaṃgaḥ| na hi yugapadekasya satvamasatvaṃ ca nirvaktuṃśakyaṃ| yagapatsatvāsatvayoranirvacanīyatvena saha satvasyāsatvasya kramikobhayasya ca melane'vaśiṣṭaṃ bhaṃgatrayamiti| tadāhānantavīryaḥ - "tadvidhānāvivakṣāyāṃ syādastīti gatirbhavet| syānnāstīti prayogassyāt tanniṣedhe vivakṣite|| krameṇobhayavāṃchāyāṃ prayogassamudāyabhāk| yugapattadvivakṣāyāṃ syādavācyamaśaktitaḥ| ādyāvācyavivakṣāyāṃ paṃcamo bhaṃgaṃ iṣyate| antyāvācyavivakṣāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhabhaṃgassamudbhavī|| samuccayena yuktaśca saptamo bhaṃga ucyata" iti| tasmādavirodhena saptabhaṃgīnaya upapadyata iti cet, yadyevaṃ pāribhāṣikoyaṃ saptabhaṃgīnayaḥ tadāsvīkriyata eva| ghaṭādissvadeśe'sti anyadeśe nāsti, svakāle'sti - anyakagāle nāsti, svātmanāsti - anyātmanā nāstīti deśakālapratiyogibhedena satvāsatvasamāveśe laukikaparīkṣakāṇāṃ vipratipattyabhāvāt| na caitāvatā jainābhimatavastvanaikāṃtyasiddhiḥ| svakāle sadeva anyakāle asadevetyādiniyamakṣatyabhāvāt| nanu sarvadharmānaikāṃtyavādināmekatvamapi na vyavasthitaṃ| ataḥ "syādaneka"mityaneka tvaparyāyamādāya satvāsatvādisamāveśa upapadyata iti cenna| prathamāpekṣitasyaikatvānekatvasamāveśasyāpyavirodhenopapādayitumaśakyatvāt| saṃkhyaikāṃtyānaṅgīkāre jīṃvadharmādharmādīni ṣaḍdravyāṇīti svasiddhāntasthityupanyāsāyogācca| kiṃ bahunā| sarvānaikāntyavādināṃ "ayamasyāḥ putraḥ, ayamasyāḥ patiḥ, ayamasyāḥ pite"tyādivyavasthāyā apyasidhyā tasyāṃ putrādīnāṃ mātṛtvādyucitavyavahārā api na vyavatiṣṭheran| tasmāt sarvabahiṣkāryo'yamanakāntavādaḥ| kiṃ cātmā dehaparimāṇa iti jainairiṣyate| tadapyayuktam| mātaṅgaśarīraṃ vyāpya sthitasyātmano maśakaśarīrapraveśe vaikalyaprasaṅgāt| bhaśakaśarīrānmātaṃgaśarīraṃ praviśatastadvyāpanasamarthapariṇāmābhāvena tatra tasya sarvāṃgīṇasukhānubhavābhāvaprasaṃgācca| nanu saṃkocavikāsavānātmā tattaccharīrānusāreṇa saṃkocarūpaṃ vikāsarūpaṃ vā paryāyamavasthāṃtaramāpadyata ityabhyupeyate| ato na doṣa iti cenna| tathāsatyātmano vikāra tatprayuktānityatvādiprasaṃgāt| tasmānmokṣadaśāyāmātmano yadaṃtyaṃ parimāṇaṃ tasya tathaivāvasthānaṃ jainairapi vaktavyam| tadanantaraṃ dehāntarābhāvena taducitaparimāṇāprasakteḥ| ato mokṣadaśāvasthitātmasvarūpavat tatparimāṇasyāpi nityatvāviśeṣāt tadevātmanassvābhāvikaṃ parimāmamityaṃgīkartuṃ yuktaṃ| na tu saṃkocavikāsavattvam||
saṃgrahakārikā||
saptabhaṃgīnaye kāryā na pratyāśā digaṃbaraiḥ|
satvāsatvādiyugapat nanvekasminnasaṃbhavi||
iti ekasminnasaṃbhavādhikaraṇam||
patyurasāmañjasyāt||35||
adhiṣṭhānānupapatteśca||36||
karaṇavaccenna bhogādibhyaḥ||37||
antavatvamasarvajñatāvā||38||
satvāsatvādivirodhājjainānāṃ matamiva parasparavirodhādvedavirodhācca śaivānāṃ matamapyasamaṃjasamityupapādyata iti saṃgatiḥ| kāpālakālāmukhapāśupatasiddhāṃtaśaivākhyacaturvidhaśaivamadhye kāpālānāṃ kālāmukhānāṃca mataṃ bhāṣye pradarśitaṃ| pāśupatāsvitthamāhuḥ - kāryakāraṇayogavidhiduḥ khāṃtāḥ paṃca padārthāḥ paśaupāśavimokṣāya boddhavyāḥ| tatra kāryāṇi pṛthivyādīni pañcabhūtāni, rūpādayaḥ pañca guṇāśceti daśavidhāni| karaṇāni jñānendriyāṇi pañca, karmendriyāṇi pañca,
mobudhyahaṅkārarūpāṇi trīṇyantaḥ karaṇānīti trayodaśavidhāni| dhyānādirūpo yagaḥ| dharmārthavyāpāro vidhiḥ| sa vratadvārarūpeṇa dvividhaḥ| bhasmasnānabhasmaśayanopahārādivratam| bhasmasnānaṃ dvividham| triyavaṇakartavyaṃ jalasnānapratinidhirūpaṃ nityamekam| malamūtrotsarjanakṣutaniṣṭhīvanādiṣu kartavyaṃ jalaśuddhipratinidhirūpānusnānākhyaṃ naimittikamanyadbhasmasnānam| bhasmaśayanañca gṛhasthagṛheṣvannavadbhikṣitena bhasmanā kāryaṃ| upahāraṣṣaḍvidhaḥ - hasitagītanṛttahuḍukāranamaskārajapabhedena| tatra hasitamaṭṭahāsaḥ| gītaṃ nṛttañca yathāruci gāndharvaśāstrānādareṇa| huḍukāro jihvā tālusaṃyogena niṣpādyamāno vṛṣabhanādasadṛśo nādaḥ| yatra laukikā
nabhavanti tatra catvāryetāni gūḍhaṃ prayoktavyāni| japaḥ pañcabrahamamantrāṇāṃ namaskāraḥ prasiddhaḥ| dvārāṇi kāyanaspandanamandayānaśrṛṅgāraṇāpitatkaraṇāpitadbhāṣaṇa bhedena ṣaḍvidhāni| asuptasyeva supliṅgapradarśanaṃ kāyanam| vātavyādhyabhi bhūtasyeva śarīrāvayavānāṃ kampanaṃ spandanam| upahatapādendriyasyeva gamanaṃ mandayānam| rūpayauvanasampannāṃ kāminīmavalokyātmānaṃ kāmukamiva yairvilāsaiḥ pradarśayati, tadācaraṇaṃ śrṛṅgāraṇam| kāryakāryavivekavikalasyeva lokaninditakarmakaraṇamapitatkaraṇam| vyāhatāpārthakādiśabdoccārāṇamapi tadbhāṣaṇam| duḥkhānto dvividhaḥ| anātmakassātmakaśca| anātmakassarvaduḥkhānāmatyantocchedaḥ| sātmakastu dṛkkriyāśaktibalalakṣaṇamaiśvaryam| tatra dṛkchaktirekā'pi vaṣayabhedātpañcadhopacaryate| darśanaṃ śravaṇaṃ mananaṃ vijñānaṃ sarvajñatvaṃ ceti| sūkṣmavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭāśeṣacākṣuṣasparśanādiviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ darśanam| aśeṣaśabdaviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ śravaṇam| niravaśeṣaśāstraviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ mananam| granthato'rthataścāsandigdhaṃ jñānaṃ vijñānam| sarvajñatvaṃ prasiddham| kriyāśaktirekā'pi trividhopacaryate - manojavatvaṃ kāmirūpatvaṃ vikaraṇatharmitvañceti| niratiśayaśīghragāmitvaṃ manojavitvam| karmānapekṣya svecchayaivānantaśarīrendriyādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ kāmirūpitvam| upasaṃhṛtaśarīrendriyasyāpi niratiśayaiśvaryaśālitvaṃ vikaraṇadharmitvam| paśupatistu parameśvarassarvatrāpi kārye nimittam| paśūnāṃ karmānapekṣya svecchayaiva phalapradaḥ| niratiśayasvātaṃtryaśālitvāt| na caivaṃ karmavaiyarthyam| īśvarecchānugṛhītakarmaṇassāphalyāt, tadananugṛhītasya karmaṇaḥ parjanyānanugṛhītakṛṣikarmaṇa iva vaiphalye'pi doṣābhāvāt, tadanugrahasambhāvanayā karmasu pravṛttyupapatteḥ| sṛṣṭikramo'pi tādātvikeśvarecchādhīnaḥ| na tu niyamenākāśādiriti| evaṃvidhasya pāśupatamatasya kāpālakālāmukhamatābhyāṃ virodhasspaṣṭaḥ| bhasmasnānādeḥ kriyātassāmyepi śavabhasmavat kiṃcidbhasmābhikṣitaṃ bhasmeti grāhyabhasmasvarūpe'sti virodhaḥ| vedenacāsti virodhaḥ| na huḍukārādikaṃ vedeṣu tadupabṛhmaṇeṣu spaṣṭamasti| śrṛṅgāraṇaṃ bhasmasnānānusnānābhyāṃ pratyāmnānatayā jalasnanaṃ jalaśuddhinivartanaṃ prāṇikarmaniyatasṛjyakramānapekṣaṇañcātyaṃtaṃ vedamārgaviruddhaṃ| evaṃ kāpālādiśaivatrayaṃ parasparavirodhādvedavirodhāccāsamaṃjasamiti siddhaṃ| yadyapi siddhāṃtaśaivasya nātyaṃtaṃ vedavirodhosti| vedavirodhābhāve ca kāmikādivātulāṃtānāmaṣṭāviṃśatitaṃtrarūpāṇāmavāṃtarabhedānāṃ parasparavirodhastvāgamasiddhāṃta divyasiddhāṃtataṃtrasiddhāṃtataṃtrāṃtarasiddhāṃtarūpapāṃcarātrāvāṃtarataṃtrabhedaparasparavirodhavat tattattaṃtragīkṣitādhikāribhedena samādhātuṃ śakyaḥ| kāmikādiṣu śivāgamasya vedādutkarṣoktiśca pāṃcarātrasya tata utkarṣoktivat samādhātuṃ śakyā| tathāpi vedaprāmāmyamaṃgīkṛtyāpi vedaṃ pracchādya siddhāṃtaśaivataṃtreṣu yadanumānenaiveśvaraprasādhanaṃ kṛtaṃ tannūnaṃ yathāvedamīśvarābhyupagame prāyaḥ kāraṇavākyeṣu puruṣottamāsādhāraṇena nārāyaṇanāmnā kāraṇavastunirdeśāt sa eva jagatkāraṇaṃ parabrahma bhediti tatpracchādanārthamityavasīyate| atasteṣāmanumāneneśvaraprasādhanamātrasyāsāmañjasyamudbhāvayāmaḥ| loke kulālamṛtpiṇḍādibhedadarśanena hi śrutisiddhajagannimittopādānābhedamavigaṇayyānumānena kevalanimitteśvaraḥ prasādhyate| tarhi lokadarśanenaiva śarīritvamapyaṃgīkaraṇīyaṃ| śarīriṇa eva kulālādeḥ kartṛtvadarśanāt| na cādhiṣṭeyaṃ māyādikameva tasya śarīraṃ bhavet, svaśarīravyāpārenāt| na cādhiṣṭheyaṃ māyādikameva tasya śarīraṃ bhavet, svaśarīravyāpāre niyamyātiriktaśarīrānapekṣaṇasya dṛṣṭatvāditi vācyaṃ| na hi śarīramātramāpādayāmaḥ| kintu bhogāśrayarūpaṃ| bhogārthaṃ tathābhūtaṃ śarīramadhitiṣṭhato hi sukhārthaṃ duḥkhaparihārārthaṃ vā kvācitkatvaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ| tathā ca kartṛtvanirvāhārthaṃ bhogāśrayaśarīrābhyupagame tanmūlapuṇyapāpavatvaṃ, śarīrasyānityatvaniyamāt antavatvaṃ, jananamaraṇāvṛttimatāṃ kiñcijjñatvadṛṣṭerasarvajñatvaṃ ca, syāt| ato'numāneneśvarasiddhimicchatāṃ yathābhimatarūpa īśvaro nasidhyediti| yattu śāsrayonitvādhikaraṇe anumāneneśvarasiddhau īśvarānekatvādikamapi sidhyediti duṣaṇamuktaṃ, tadapyatratyaduṣaṇameva| atraiva sūtrakārasyānumānadūṣaṇakaṃṭhoktidarśanāt| tatra tu sūtrakṛtāṃ "taṃtvaupaniṣadami"tyādiśrutitātparyavicāremaiva parameśvarasya śāstrayonitvaṃ vyavasthāpitaṃ| ato na tena paunaruktyaṃ| "racanāṃnupapatte"śceti sāṃkhyādhikaraṇe mṛdghaṭādinyāyena sukhaduḥkhamohātmakasya jagatastatsarūpeṇa kāraṇena
bhavitavyamityanumimānaṃ sāṃkhyaṃ prati tarhi tadṛṣṭāṃtenaiva cenādhiṣṭhānenaiva bhāvyamityetāvanmātramāpāditaṃ| na tu svataṃtramīśvarānumānamanavadyamiti teneśvaraḥ prasādhitaḥ| atastatreśvarānumānamanumoditaṃ| iha dūṣitamiti na virodhaḥ|
saṃgrahakārikā||
patyurmataṃ nādaraṇīyameva paraspareṇa śrutito'pi bādhāt|
nimittamātreśvarakalpanāyā madhiṣṭhiterapyasamaṃjasatvāt||
iti paśupatyadhikaraṇam||
utpattyasaṃbhavāt||39||
na ca kartuḥ karaṇam||40||
vijñānādibhāve vā tadapratiṣedhaḥ||41||
vipratiṣedhācca||42||
pāśupatavat pāñcarātrasyāpi taṃtratvenāprāmāṇyamāśaṃkya nirasyata iti saṃgatiḥ| pāñcarātriṇo bhāgavatā manyante - bhagavānnārāyaṇaḥ paraṃbrahma savāsudevādicaturvyūhātmanā'vatiṣṭhate| tatra vāsudevaḥ paripūrṇaṣāḍguṇyaśālī| ṣaḍguṇāstu jñānabalaiśvaryaśaktitejāṃsi| tatra cetanācetanātmakasakalaprapañcagatāśeṣaviśeṣollokhitatayā sakalavastugocaraṃ tasya jñānaṃ jñānamucyate| jagatprakṛtibhāvaśśaktiḥ| jagatsṛjato'sya śramābhāvastilakālakavadaprayatnena svasṛṣṭasakalajagadbharaṇañca balam| apratihatecchatvamaiśvaryam| jagatprakṛtitve'pi payaso dadhibhāveneva vikāraviraho vīryam| jagatsṛṣṭau sahakāryanapekṣatvaṃ parābhibhavasāmarthyañca tejaḥ| tato jñānabalonmeṣeṇa saṅkarṣaṇarūpaḥ prādurbhavati| tato vīryaiśvaryonmeṣeṇa pradyumnarūpāḥ| tataśśaktitejassamunmeṣeṇāniruddharūpaḥ|| saṅkarṣaṇādayaśca jīvamano'haṅkārūrapāḥ| evaṃ caturvyūhaṃ bhagavantaṃ pratyahaṃ pāñcakālikenābhigamanādipañcakena vārṣaśatikādivratena samārādhya puruṣaḥ kleśairmucyate| tatrābhigamanaṃ prātarbhagavanmantrajapastutinamaskārādikam| tataḥ pūjārthapuṣpādispādanamupādānam| tataḥ pūjanamijyā| tato bhagavacchāstratadanuguṇapurāṇāgamaśravaṇādikaṃ svādhyāyaḥ| tatassāyaṃsandhyānantaraṃ bhagavati cittasamādhānaṃ yoga iti| tadidamayuktamiti pūrvapakṣaḥ| tathā hi - pāñcarātre jīvasyotpattirāmnāyate| "vāsudevātsaṅkarṣaṇo nāma jīvo jāyate| saṅkarṣaṇātpradyumnasaṃjñaṃ mano jāyate| tasmādaniruddhasaṃjño'haṅkāro jāyata" iti| na ca jīvasyānāderutpattissambhavati| na ca saṅkarṣaṇādīnāṃ jīvādyabhimānitvamanenocyata iti vācyam| tathā sati "saṅkarṣaṇo nāma jīva" ityādyanupapatteḥ| anena hi prasiddhānāmeva jīvamano'haṅkārāṇāṃ pāñcarātraparibhāṣayā saṅkarṣaṇapradyumnāniruddhanāmāni vidhīyante| tataśca saṅkarṣaṇādināmabhiranūdya tasmin śāstre yadvigrahāyudhādividhānaṃ tatkakārādyakṣarāṇāṃ varṇaviśeṣavidhānavat teṣāmeva dhyānārthamiti sidhyati| na tu mano'bhimānino devāḥ tattadvigrahāyudhādiviśiṣṭā iti| kiṃ ca jīvasyotpattiriva jīvāt kartuḥ mana ādikaraṇotpattiśca na sambhavati| "etasmājjāyate prāṇo manassarvendriyāṇi ce"ti parasmādeva brahmaṇo mana ādyutpattiśravaṇāt| tasmāt pāñcarātrasyāpi vedavirodhādaprāmāṇyamiti||
siddhāntastu - pāñcarātre vāsudevājjīvasya jīvādibhyo mana ādīnāmutpattiśca noktā| kintu jīvādyadhiṣṭhātṝṇāṃ saṅkarṣaṇādivyūhānāṃ prādurbhāva eva "ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyata" iti śrutisiddha uktaḥ|
"janīprādurbhāva"iti ca dhātuḥ| na tu prasiddhānāṃ jīvādīnāṃ saṅkarṣaṇādisaṃjñā uktāḥ| tatra jīvādiśabdānāṃ niyāmyavācināṃ tattanniyantṛvyūhaviśeṣaparatvāccharīravācināṃ śarīriparyantatvasyāpṛthaksiddhaprakāravācināṃ prakāriparyantatvasya ca dvitīdhyāye vyutpādayiṣyamāṇatvāt, pāñcarātra eva jīvaṃ prakṛtya "sahyanādiranantaśca pāramārthyena niścita" iti tasyotpattipratiṣedhācca| tasmādbhagavatā vāsudevena praṇītaṃ pāñcarātraṃ pramāṇamūrdhanyameveti||
saṃgrahakārikā||
jīvotpattyabhidhānānnagrāhyaṃ pāñcarātramiti cenna|
vyūhābhivyaktyuktestatra niṣedhācca jīvajaneḥ||
iti utpattyasambhavādhikaraṇam||
iti śrīmadbharadvājakulajaladhikaustubhaśrīmadadvaitavidyācāryaśrīviśvajidyājiśrīraṅgarājādhvarivarasūnorappayadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturmatasāre
nayamayukhamālikākhye dvitīyaparicchede dvitīyasyādhyāyasya
dvi tī yaḥ pā daḥ ||
||dvitīyasyādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ||
sāṃkhyāditaṃtrāṇāṃ nyāyābhāsamūlatvādvipratiṣedhāccāsāmaṃjasyamuktaṃ| idānīṃ svapakṣasyāpi viyadutpattyunutpattyādiviṣayuśrutivipratiṣedhadoṣostīti pratibandīṃ manasi viparivartamānāṃ nirasituṃ tadabhāvakhyāpanāya brahmakāryasya prapañcasya kāryatāprakāraḥ pādadvayena viśodhyate| nanvevaṃ sati viyadādi kāryatvaprasādhanasya paroktadoṣaparihārārthena prathamapādena saṃgattyā tadānaṃtaryaṃ syāt| kāraṇaviṣayapādadvayānantaraṃ kāryaviṣayapādadvayārambha iti cet tarhi kāryaviṣayasyāsya pādadvayasyādhyāyasaṃgatireva na syāt| prathamādhyāyanirūpite vedāṃtānāṃ jagatkaraṇabrahmasamanvaye paripanthino virodhasya samādhānaṃ hi dvitīyādhyāyārtha iti cet ucyate - kāraṇaviṣayaśrutīnāṃ brahmaṇi samanvayo viyadādyutpattiśrutīnāṃ svārthasamanvayamapekṣate| anyathā viyannityatve paranādīnāṃ viyadādijanyatve ca brahma kathaṃ viyadādikasya kāraṇaṃ bhavet| kathaṃca brahma sarvasya kāryasya kāraṇaṃ syāt| ato viyadādyutapatti śrutisamanvayaciṃtā, tadanubandhena jīvanityatvajñātṛtvāṇutvādiciṃtā| athavā "vipratiṣedhāccāsamaṃjasa"mityādisūtrais sāṃkhyādidarśanānāṃ parasparavirodhādaprāmāṇye samarthite vedāṃteṣvapi viyadāderjīvasyacotpattyanutpattiśrutīnāṃ jīvasya jñātṛtvajñānarūpatvādiśrutīnāṃ ca parasparavirodhenāprāmāṇye teṣāṃ kāraṇaviṣayepyaprāmāṇyaśaṃkayā nirvicikitsaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ nasidhyediti prathamādhyāyanirūpitakāraṇaviṣayaśrutisamanvayākṣepakatayā jñātṛtvādivicārasyāpi sākṣādeva saṃgatiranusaṃdheyā| iha prathamaṃ viyadutpattissamarthyate - 
naviyadaśruteḥ||1||
astitu||2||
gauṇyasaṃbhavācchabdācca||3||
syāccaikasya brahmaśabdavat||4||
pratijñāhāniravyatirekāt||5||
śabdebhyaḥ||6||
yāvadvikāraṃtu vibhāgo lokavat||7||
etena mātariśvā vyākhyātaḥ||8||
asaṃbhavastu sato'nupapatteḥ||9||
