viyadutpadyate naveti tadutpattipratipādanakṣamaśrutisaṃbhavāsaṃbhavābhyāṃsaṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - viyannotpadyate| tadutpattipratipādanakṣaṇaśrutyabhāvāt| chāṃdogyetāvat tadviṣayā śrutireva nāsti| taittarīyakātharvaṇādiṣu satyapi sā na tadutpattipratipādanakṣamā| niravayavatvena sarvagatatvena ca tasya nityatvāvagamāt| naca dvitīyahetorasiddhiḥ| kvacidapi tadabhāvasya budhyārohāsaṃbhavāt| atra viyannāstīti niṣedhasya vyāhatatvāt| yatra niṣidhyate tasyaiva
deśasya viyadrūpatvāt| atastaittirīyakaśrutau viyatiśrutā saṃbhūtaśrutiḥ "etasmājjāyate prāṇomanassarvendriyāṇi ca| khaṃvāyurjyotirāpaḥ pṛthivī viśvasya dhāriṇī"tyātharvaṇe viyatyanuṣaktā jāyateśrutiśca gauṇyeva vācyā| na ca
śrutānuṣaktayossambhūtaśabdayorjāyateśabdayoścārthabhede kaściddoṣaḥ| ātharvaṇaeva "tapasā cīyate brahma tato'nnamabhijāyate| tasmādetadbrahma nāma rūpamannaṃ ca jāyata" iti śrutayorapi brahmaśabdayormukhyāmukhyatvadarśanāt viyadutpattivādinā'pi "ākāśavatsarvagataśca nitya" iti śrutau sakṛcchaØtayornityasarvagataśabdayoḥ ekatra mukhyatvasya anyatrāmukhyatvasya ca aṅgīkartavyatvācceti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
yatra tejasaḥ prāthamyaṃ śrutaṃ, tatraiva chāndogye mṛtpiṇḍādidṛṣāntapūrvakaṃ ekavijñānena sarvavijñānaṃ yatpratijñātaṃ tasyāhāniḥ ākāśasyāpi brahmakāryatvena tadavyatirekādeva bhavati| tenākāśasya brahmakāryatvaṃ chāndogyaśruterapyanumatamavasīyate| "sadeva somyedamagraāsī"diti sṛṣṭeḥ prāgekatvāvadhāraṇaṃ "aitadātmyamidaṃ sarva"miti sarvasya brahmātmatvapratipādanamityādiśabdaiśca tathā'vasīyate| evaṃcaibhiśśabdairākāśasyāpi sādhāraṇyena brahmavikāratve'dhyavasite tasya viśiṣyāpi brahmaṇo vibhāgarūpotpattiruktaiva bhavati| yathā loke "sarva ete devadattasya putrā" iti sāmānyenābhidhāya keṣāṃcidviśiṣya tata utpattivacanena sarveṣāmapi tata utpattiruktaiva bhavati| evaṃ sati chāndogye tejassṛṣṭiprāthamyaśravaṇaṃ kathamiticet na khalu brahmaṇā tejaḥ prathamaṃ sṛṣṭamiti śrutam| yena śrutiprāptaṃ tatprāthamyaṃ syāt| kintu tejassṛṣṭiḥ prathamapaṭhiteti sthānapramāṇatastatprāthamyaṃ prāpnoti| naca sthāna pramāṇaṃ "ākāśassambhūta" iti sambhūtaśruterīṣṭe| ākāśasya nityatvasādhako niravayavatvaheturaprayojakaḥ| śrutyudāhṛtaikamṛtpiṃḍārabdhaghaṭaśarāvādinyāyena pariṇāmavāde niravayavasyāpyanityatvopapatteḥ| na ca iha viyat nāstīti niṣedhasya vyāhatyā sarvagatatvasiddhiḥ| tatra ihapratyayavedyasya nibiḍamūrtadravyābhāvarūpāvakāśaparatvopapatteḥ| iha avakāśe sthātavyamityādivyavahāreṣu tasyāpi ihapratyayavedyatvāt| evaṃ sati "vāyuścāntarikṣaṃ caitadamṛta"miti viyadamṛtatvaśruteḥ kā gatiriticet, vāyvamṛtatvaśruteryā gatiḥ saiva| spaṣṭotpattikasya vāyorapi hyamṛtatvaṃ śrūyate| kiṃ bahunā| brahmavyatiriktaṃ yadyāvadasti avyaktamahadahaṅkāratejaḥ prabhṛtikaṃ, tatsarvamapi viyadvāyuvadutpattimadeva| sarvavijñānapratijñādyanurodhāt||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
viyannityaṃ niraṃśatvādamṛtatvasya ca śruteḥ|
sṛṣṭau ca tejaḥ prāthamyādgauṇī tajjananaśrutiḥ||
asādhakaṃ niraṃśatvamamṛtatvamamartyavat|
śrutaṃ na tejaḥprāthamyaṃ bādhaḥ pāṭhena kalpane||
tasmājjanimadākāśaṃ marucca mahadādibhiḥ|
evaṃ hi sarvavijñānapratijñādyupapadyate||
iti viyadadhikaraṇam||
tejo'tastathāhyāha||10||
āpaḥ||11||
pṛthivī||12||
adhikārarūpaśabdāntarebhyaḥ||13||
tadabhidhyānādeva tu talliṅgātsaḥ||14||
viparyayeṇa tu kamo'taupapadyate ca||15||
antarāvijñānamanasī krameṇa talliṅgāditi cennāviśeṣāt||16||
carācaravyapāśrayastu syāttadvyapadeśobhāktastadbhāvabhāvitvāt||17||
brahmavyatiriktaṃ sarvaṃ brahmakāryamiti nirṇītam| ihedaṃ cintyate kiṃ tejaḥ prabhūtīnāṃ pūrvapūrvasmādvāyvāderutpattiḥ, teṣāṃ brahmakāryatvaṃ tu paramparayā, uta pūrvapūrvaśarīrakātparamātmanasteṣāmutpattiriti| tatra pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ| pūrvapūrvasmādevotpattiḥ| "vāyoragniḥ, agnerāpaḥ, adbhyaḥpṛthivī"ti śrutirāha| nanu "tadaikṣata bahusyāṃ prajāyeyeti tattejo'sṛjata tatteja aikṣata tadapo'sṛjata tā āpa aikṣanta tā annamasṛjante"ti īkṣāpūrvakaṃ tejaḥ prabhṛtīnāṃ sraṣṭṛtvaṃ śrūyate| nacekṣaṇamacetanānāṃ sambhavati| ata īkṣaṇaliṃgāt teja ādiśabdāḥ tattaccharīrakaparamātmaparā iti tejaśśarīrāt paramātmano'pāṃ accharīrakāt tataḥ pṛthivyāścotpattirvaktuṃ yuktā| na ca "tā annamasṛjante"ti annaśabdena kathaṃ pṛthivyabhidhīyata iti vācyam| mahābhūtasṛṣṭyadhikārāt| "yadagnerohitaṃ rūpaṃ tejasastadrūpaṃ yacchuklaṃ tadapāṃ yatkṛṣṇaṃ tadannasye"ti trivṛtkṛtamahābhūtarūpoktiprakaraṇe annasya rūpokteḥ, samānaprakaraṇe "adbhyaḥ pṛthivī"ti pṛthivīvācakaśabdāntaraśravaṇācca| atastejaādiśabdāstattaccharīrakaparamātmaparā vācyāḥ| antaryāmyadhikaraṇe sarveṣāṃ paramātmaśarīratvasya uktatvāditicet maivaṃ - tathāsati teja ādiśabdānāṃ tattaccharīrake paramātmani lakṣaṇayā śrutahāniprasaṅgāt| na cekṣaṇaligena śrutibādhanaṃ yuktam| kintu śrutyanusārāt īkṣaṇameva gauṇaṃ vaktuṃ yuktam| "etasmājjāyate prāṇo manassarvendriyāṇi ca| khaṃ vāyurjyotirāpaḥ pṛthivī viśvasya dhāriṇī"tyātharvaṇamaṃtro'pi putrapautraprapautrādirītyā sākṣātparamparāsādhāraṇyena brahmakāryatvaparatayopapadyate| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
tattaccharīrakātparamātmano jagadutpadyate| īkṣaṇaliṃgāt| na ca śrutibādhaḥ| carācaraprapañcavācināṃ sarveṣāṃ śabdānāṃ tattaccharīrake paramātmani mukhyatvāt| loke "brāhmaṇo'dhīte, brāhmaṇo vidvā" nityādiprayogeṣu, vede "brāhmaṇamupanayīta, brāhmaṇo yajete"tyādiprayogeṣu ca śarīravāciśabdānāṃ śarīriparyantatvasya vyutpattisiddhatvāt| sarvasya carācarasya paramātmaśarīratāyā antaryāmibrāhmaṇena, "anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇī"ti tejaādyanupraveśapūrvakanāmarūpavyākaraṇaśrutyācāvagamāt| na ca sarvaśarīratvaśravaṇamaupacārikaṃ, tejaādisṛṣṭyanantaraṃ nāmarūpavayākaraṇārthamevānupraveśaśravaṇāt "tatsṛṣṭvā tadevānuprāviśadi"ti śrutyantarācceti vācyaṃ| sarvagatasyeśvarasya kvacidasataḥ paścātpraveśāsaṃbhavena praveśaśrutīnāṃ niyaṃtṛtayā sārvadikasattapritāpādanaparatvāt| tasmāttejaḥ pṛbhṛtiśabdānāṃ paramātmani mukhyatvāt "tatteja aikṣate" tyādibhiḥ paramātmana eva sarvasmin prapaṃce sākṣātsraṣṭṛtvamavasīyate| "etasmājjāyata"ityādyātharvaṇamaṃtreṇāpi tathā'vasīyate| ayaṃ hi maṃtro'vyaktamahadahaṃkārādikāryeṣūttarottaraḥ pūrvapūrva smādutpadyata iti putra pautranyāyānusāriṇaḥ kramādvaiparītyena sarvaṃ paramātmana evotpadyata iti sarvasya paramātmānantaryarūpo yaḥ kramaḥ tatraiva svārasikaḥ| sa ca tattaccharīrakāt brahmaṇassākṣājjagatsṛṣṭerevopapadyate "etasmājjāyata" ityatra sākṣāttvasya mukhyatvenautsargikatvāt| nanvayaṃ maṃtraḥ prāṇasya bhūtānāṃ ca madhye cakṣurādīni manaścotpadyanta ityevaṃrūpakramatātparyakaḥ| khādipṛthivyantānāṃ kramāmnānaliṃgāt| na tu sarvasya prāṇādeḥ paramātmana utpattipara iti cet - na| indriyamanaḥkhādīnāmapi "etasmājjāyata" iti sākṣāt paramātmasṛṣṭatayopāttaprāṇāviśeṣāt| na hi prāṇa eva sākṣājjāyate anye pautrādinyāyena paraṃparayā brahmaṇo jāyanta iti viśeṣaśravaṇamasti| natvayaṃ maṃtraḥ kramaparaḥ| kramastu khādiṣu śrutyaṃtarasiddhonūdyate| anyatra śrutyaṃtaraviruddho na gṛhyate| subālopaniṣadi spaṣṭaṃ kraṃmāmnānāt| tasmāt sarvaṃ jagat
sākṣāt paramātmana evotpadyata iti siddhaṃ||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
tejojalapṛthivīnāṃ mārutatejojalebhya utpattiḥ|
śrutyanurodhāt brahma tu mūlaṃ kūṭasthapuruṣaiva||
"tattejaaikṣate"tyādyāmnāyoktādabhidhyānāt|
sākṣādeva nidānaṃ teṣāṃ tattaccharīrakaṃ brahma||
evaṃ sa "tyetasmājjāyata" ityādivacanamupapannaṃ|
taddhyaviśeṣādeṣāṃ brahmānaṃtaryameva bodhayati||
tejaḥ pramukhāśśabdā loke'nyatra prasiddhimaṃtopi|
vyākaraṇaśrutyādergamyaṃte brahmaparyaṃtāḥ||
iti tejodhikaraṇam||
nātmā śruternityatvācca tābhyaḥ||18||
viyadādivajjīvopyutpadyate na veti ciṃtyate| utpadyata iti pūrva pakṣaḥ| "yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante, yena jātāni jīvanti, prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjate"tyādiśravaṇāt ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñānāt prāksṛṣṭerekatvāvadhāraṇācca| na ca "tatvamasī"ti jīvasya brahmābhedaśravaṇādutpattyanupapattiḥ| "sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahme" tyādiśravaṇena viyadādīnāmapyanutpattiprasaṃgāt| naca "na jāyate mriyate vā vipaści"dityanena jīvotpattiniṣedhaśśaṃkanīyaḥ| vāyvākāśāmṛtatvavacanena tayorapyutapattiniṣedhāpatteḥ tasya dehotpattivināśānubaṃdhyutpattivināśābāvaparatvācca| "jīvāpetaṃ vāva kiledaṃ mriyate najīvo mriyata"iti dehamaraṇānubandhimaraṇaniṣedhaka śrutyantarānusāreṇāsyāpi tathaivābhiprāyāvagamācca| na ca kṛtahānākṛtābhyāgamaprasaṃgo doṣaḥ| sarvatra dravyaṃ nityaṃ tasya pūrvāvasthāprahāṇaṃ vināśaḥ avasthāṃtarāpattirutpattiriti siddhāṃtyabhimate pariṇāmavāde dravyātmanā nitye jīve tadaprasaṃgāt| naiyāyikamate ghaṭādīnāmiva jīvasyābhūtvā bhavanarūpotpattyabhyupagamo hi prasajyeta| tasmājjīvo viyadādivadutpadyata iti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
jīvo viyadādivannotpadyate| "na jāyate mriyate vāvipaścit, jñājñau dvāvajāvīśanīśā"vityādiṣu tadanutpattiśravaṇāt "ajo nityaśśāśvatoyaṃ purāṇo na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre, nityonityānāñcetanaścetanānām" ityādiśrutibhyaḥ tasya nityatvenotpattyabhāvāvagamācca| na ca "na jāyate mriyata" iti hi śrutiḥ dehotpattimaraṇānubaṃdhyutpattimaraṇābhāvamātrapareti vācyaṃ| tatraiva "na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre"iti dehahananānubandhihananā bhāvoktyaivārthasiddhasya tasya pṛthagavaktavyatvāt| prāgaviyatoviyadrūpeṇeva prāgajīvasya jīvarūpeṇa pariṇāmābhyupagame jīvakartṛkatvaniyatayoḥ puṇyapāpayorajīvāśritatvāsaṃbhavena kṛtahānākṛtābhyāgamaprasaṃgāparihārācca| sarvavijñānapratijñānamekatvāvadhāraṇañca sṛṣṭeḥ prāgjīvasya sūkṣmatāpattyā sṛṣṭau sthūlakāryatāpattyācopapadyate| kathaṃ tarhi vidādibhyo viśeṣaḥ| itthaṃ - viyadādīnāmasti svarūpānyathābhāva iti tena rūpeṇa kāryatvaṃ| jīvasya tvavikriyasya na svarūpānyathābhāvosti| kintu jñānākhyaguṇasaṃkocavikāsalakṣaṇa dharmānyathābhāvamātraṃ| tāvanmātreṇa tasya kāryatvamiti| idamuttarādhikaraṇe spaṣṭībhaviṣyati||
saṃgrahakārikā||
śruterjīvo'pi janimān maivaṃ tadajanuśruteḥ|
dharmajñānānyathābhāvaviṣayā tajjaniśrutiḥ||
iti ātmādhikaraṇam||3||

pṛthagupadeśāt||28||
tadguṇasāratvāttadvyapadeśaḥ prājñavat||29||
yāvadātmabhāvitvācca na doṣastaddarśanāt||30||
puṃstvādivattvasya sato'bhivyaktiyogāt||31||
nityo'palabdhyanupalabdhiprasaṃgonyataraniyamovānyathā||32||
jīvotpattivicāraprasaṃgāt tasya jñātṛtvādidharmaścityate jñānarūpatva jñātṛtvādiviṣayaśrutivirodhasamādhānena jagatkāraṇabrahmaviṣayaśrutīnāṃ nirvicikitsaprāmāṇyasidhyarthaṃ| api ca jīvasya dharmānyathābhāvarūpo vikāraḥ, na svarūpānyatābhāvarūpaityetadayuktaṃ| svarūpātirekeṇa jñānākhyadharmābhāvāt| jñānameva hi tasya svabhāvaḥ ityutthānādākṣepikī saṃgatirapyanusaṃdheyā| kiṃ jñānasvarūpo jīvaḥ utajaḍa evāgaṃtukajñānaḥ, athavā nityameva jñānaguṇādhāratayā svatojñāteti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - "vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute karmāṇi tanute'pica| yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan vijñānādaṃtaraḥ, jñānasvarūpamatyaṃtanirmalaṃ paramārthata" ityādiśrutismṛtibhyo jñānasvarūpaevetyekaḥ pūrvapakṣaḥ| aparastu jīvasya jñānasvarūpatve tasya sarvagatasya sarvadā sarvaviṣayopalabdhiprasaṃgena cakṣurādivaiyarthyaprasaṃgena ca jñānavikalasuṣuptimūrchādyabhāvaprasaṃgena ca tasya jñānarūpatvaṃ nayuktaṃ| ataeva siddhāṃtyabhimataṃ nityajñānādhāratayā jñātṛtvamapyayuktaṃ| kintu naiyāyikādimatarītyā cakṣurādikaraṇādhīnajñānavattvamevābhyupeyamiti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāṃtaḥ - 
"atha yovededaṃ jighrāṇīti sa ātmā, manasaitān kāmān paśyan ramate, napaśyo mṛtyuṃ paśyati, jānātyevāyaṃ puruṣaḥ, eṣahi draṣṭā śrotā ghrātā rasayite"tyādiśrutibhyaḥ jñātaivāyamātmā| nacaivaṃ sati sarvadā draṣṭā śrotā ghrātā ramayite"tyādiśrutibhyaḥ jñātaivāyamātmā| nacaivaṃ sati sarvadā sarvaviṣayopalabdhiprasaṃgaḥ| tasyāṇutvāt| aṇutvaṃca utkrāṃtigatyāgatiśravaṇāt| "tena pradyotenaiṣa ātmā viṣkrāmati, yaivaikecāsmāllokātprayaṃti candramasameva te sarve gacchanti, tasmāllokātpunaraityasmai lokāya karmaṇa"iti hi tasyotkrāntigatyāgatayaśśrūyante| nacaitāsāṃ vibhutve śakyamupapādanaṃ| nanu "yoyaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇeṣvi"ti prāṇasambandhinaṃ jīvaṃ prastutya "savāeṣa mahānajaātme"ti mahatvāmnānānnāṇuriti cenna| tatra jīvāditarasya brahmaṇodhikārāt| upakrame jīvaprastāvepi madhye "yasyānuvittaḥ pratibuddhaātme"ti parapratipādanāt, tadanubandhitvācca mahatvāmnānasya| kiñca "eṣoṇurātmā cetasā veditavyāḥ yasmin prāṇaḥ paṃcadhā saṃviveśe"ti jīvasya sākṣādaṇutvaṃ śrūyate| "vālāgraśatabhāgasya śatadhā kalpitasya ca| bhāgo jīvassavijñeya" iti "ārāgraśatabhāgasya" iti ca tasyālpaparamāṇatvaṃ śrūyate| evaṃ sati śarīraikavartino jīvasya kathaṃ sarvāṃgīṇasukhādyanubhava iti cet - caṃdanavaditi brūmaḥ| yathā haricandanabiṃdurdehaikadeśa vartyapi sakaladehavyāpinamāhlādaṃ janayati, tathaikadeśasthitopi jīvassarvāṃgīṇasukhamanubhavati| "yoyaṃ vijñānamayaḥ prāṇeṣu hṛdyaṃtarjyotiḥ, hṛdihyayamātme"tyādiśrutibhiḥ tasyāpi hṛdayadeśe sthityabhyupagamāt| vastutastu - jīvo hṛdi sthitopi svaguṇena jñānena sakaladehaṃ vyāpnoti, yathāmaṇirdyumaṇirvā ekadeśavartibhiḥ svālekairanekadeśaṃ| naca jñānasvarūpaevātmā najñānaguṇakaiti śaṃkanīyaṃ| yathā gandhasya pṛthivīguṇatvenopalabhyamānasya tato vyatirekaḥ tathā
jānāmīti ātmaguṇatvenānubhūyamānasya jñānasya jīvādvyatirekāt| "jānātyevāyaṃpuruṣa" iti śrutyāpi tasya jñātṛguṇatvapradarśanāt, "na hi vijñāturviparilopovidyata" iti śrutau jñātṛjñānayoḥ pṛthagupadeśācca| kathaṃ tarhi "vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanuta" ityādiṣu tasya jñānarūpatva vyavahāraḥ| jñānaguṇasāratvāt| jñānaṃ hi tasya sārabhūto guṇaḥ| tataśca yathā brahmaṇaḥ prājñasya jñānamānandaśca sārabhūto guṇa iti "satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma, ānando brahmeti vyajānā"diti jñānāndavyapadeśaḥ, eva mihāpi jñānavyapadeśaḥ| nanu "satyaṃ jñāna"mityatra antodāttasvarabalāt jñānavatvamarthaḥ pratibhāti| kevalaṃ bhāvārthalyuḍantatāyāmaṃtodāttasvarānupapatteḥ| tathā hi - lyuḍādeśasya anaityasya "ādyudāttaśce"ti pratyayasvareṇa prathamākāre udāttelitīti litsvareṇa prakṛtyākārecodātte "anudāttaṃ padamekavarja"miti śeṣanighātasvareṇa pratyayasthayorakārayoranudāttayoḥ "ekādeśa udāttenodātta"ityekādeśasvareṇa prakṛtyākārapratyayādyakārayoḥ ekādeśecodātte sati pratyayāntyākārasyānudāttasya
vibhaktyakārasya ca "anudāttau suptiṣā"viti sup svareṇa anudāttasya "amipūrva" ityekādeśo bhavan "sthāno'ntaratama" iti paribhāṣayānudāttassyāt| tasya cānudāttasya "udāttādanudāttasya ca svarita" iti svaritādeśo bhavet| ata eva tat jñānamiti padaṃ kevalalyuḍantatve svaritāntaṃ syāt| yathā kāṭhake "saṃjñānaṃ vijñānaṃ prajñāna"miti| tasmādantodāttanirvāhārthamidaṃ jñānapadaṃ lyuḍantaraṃ matvarthīyāc pratyayāntaṃ vācyaṃ| arśāderākṛtigaṇatvāt| tathāsatyaṃtodāttasvaraupapadyate| arśāṃdyacpratyaye "yasyeti ce"ti prakṛtāvaṃtyasya akārasya lope "cita" iti citsvareṇa matvarthīṃyapratyayasyodāttatve ca sati tasya vibhaktyakārasya caikādeśasyāpi "ekādeśaudāttenodātta" ityudāttasyaiva bhāvāt| evamiha jñānapadaprakriyāśrayaṇe jñānavatvamevātmanassidhyet| na jñānarūpatvaṃ| tasmātjñānarūpatvamanena na pratīyataiti ceducyate - auṇādikanapratyayāṃtatvāśrayaṇe tadapi pratibhāti| jñādhātorāhatya na pratyayavidhānābhāve'pi "uṇādayo bahula"miti bahulagrahaṇena yato vihitāstato'nyatrāpi te bhavantītyetadarthena tatsiddheḥ| auṇādikapratyayāṃtatve bhāvārthatvamapyupapadyate| artyādisūtravihitamanpratyayasvareṇodāttatve sati tasya vibhaktyakāreṇa bhavataekādeśasyāpyudāttasyaiva bhāvāt| evamubhayatheha jñānapadaprakriyāśrayaṇasaṃbhavepi prakriyālāghavādauṇādikanapratyayāṃtatayā bhāvārthatvameva yuktamityabhipretya bhāṣyakārairjñānasvarūpatvepramāṇatayedamudāhṛtaṃ| jñānasya yāvadātmabhāvitvādapi tena tasya vyapadeśaḥ| khaṃḍādīnāṃ yāvatsvarūpabhāvigotvādidharmavācigavādiśabdena vyapadeśadarśanāt| jīvasya svaprakāśatvādapi jñānavyapadeśaupapadyate| svaprakāśatvamapi svāpekṣameva natu parāpekṣamapi| svāpekṣamapi ahamitipratyaktvaikatvaviśiṣṭatāmātreṇa| svaparaprakāśatvaṃ tu dharmajñānasyaiva| soyaṃ prabhāprabhāvannyāyodharmadharmijñānayoḥ| yadāhuḥ - "svasyaiva bhāsakodīpaḥ svātmano'nyasya ca prabhā| evaṃbhedosti sāmyepi jñānayordharmadharmiṇo"riti| nanu suṣuptyādiṣu jñānābhāvāt kathaṃ jñānasya gotvādivat svarūpānubandhidharmatvamiti cet| puṃstvādivadabhivyaktyeti brūmaḥ - yathā saptadhātumaye śarīre sadāsannapi puṃstvarūpasaptamadhātuḥ na bālye yauvanaivābhivyajyate, evaṃ sadāsanneva jñānaguṇaḥ suṣuptau jāgaraiva nābhivyajyate| tadānīmapi svayameva prakāśamānatārhatvarūpasya svayaṃprakāśatvasya na hāniḥ| ahamarthasya tu suṣuptāvapi prakāśamānatā'styeva| "sukhamahamasvāpsa"miti parāmarśāt| yadāhuḥ - "pratyaksvayaṃprabhasukhe nitye puṃsi vyavasthite| asvāpsaṃ sukhamityādau kālādyaṃśe'numāsthitiḥ|| agāyaṃ madhuraṃ madamayāmityevamādiṣu| mādhuryādivadatrāpi sukhaṃ bhāti tadātana"miti| tasmājjñānaguṇassaṃkocavikāsaśālī, taddharmī jīvastvaṇurityasmatpakṣe na kaściddoṣaḥ| kintu "jñānasvarūpojīvaḥ saca sarvagata" iti pakṣaeva nityopalabdhyanupalabdhiprasaṃgonyataraniyamovā syāt| tathāhi - sarvagatajñānarūpaṃ jīvasvarūpaṃ kimupalabdherevahetuḥ, uta anupalabdhereva, athāvāubhayorapi| ādye dehāvacchinnapradeśa iva anyatrāpi tadupalabdhissyāt| dvitīye dehāvacchinnapradeśepi tadupalabdhirnasyāt| tṛtīye sadopalabdhyanupalabdhī parasparaviruddhe syātāṃ| sarvagatojīvaścakṣurādikaraṇāyattagantukajñānaiti naiyāyikādipakṣepyayaṃdoṣassamānaḥ| ayamarthaḥ "aṃśonānāvyapadeśā"dityadhikaraṇe sphūṭībhaviṣyati|
saṃgrahakārike||
jīvaṃ vijñānarūpaṃ katicidabhidadhuśśrūyate'sau tatheti
jñānairāgantukaistaṃ katicadatha punarjñātṛtābhājamūcuḥ||
nityajñātṛtvamanye tadiha samudito madhyamastarkapakṣaḥ
jānīyāddūradeśe sthitamapi hi vibhurjīvaeṣo'nyathā te||
jānātyevāyamityādyupaniṣadudito jñātṛrūpassanityaṃ
sarvajñastāvatāsyāditi na yadaṇurutkrāṃtigatyādimānaiḥ|
dehavyāptistu dīpadyutisadṛśaguṇajñānatastasya yuktā
jñānatvoktissasāroguṇaiti ghaṭate svaprakāśassaceti||
iti jñādhikaraṇam||
kartā śāstrārthavattvāt||33||
upādānādvihāropadeśācca||34||
vyapadeśācca kriyāyāṃ na cennirdeśaviparyayaḥ||35||
upalabdhivadaniyamaḥ||36||
śaktiviparyayāt||37||
samādhyabhāvācca||38||
yathāca takṣobhayathā||39||
jñātṛtvanirūpaṇānantaraṃ kartṛtvaṃ nirūpyeta jñānādhīnatvāt kṛteriti kāryakāraṇabhāvassaṅgatiḥ| jñānasvarūpassarvagato jīva ityatra upalabdhiniyamādivyavasthityasiddhivat prakṛteḥ kartṛtve tasyāssarvasādhāraṇatvāt kartṛniyamavyavasthityasiddhivat prakṛteḥ kartṛtve tasyāssarvasādhāraṇatvāt kartṛniyamavyavasthityasiddhirucyata iti dṛṣṭāntasaṅgatiśca| kiṃ prakṛtirevakartrīti tadīyakartṛtvādhyāsena jīvasya kartṛtvavyahāraḥ uta svabhāvataeva jīvaḥ karteti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - akartaivātmā| kaṭhavallīṣu "hantācenmanyate hantuṃ hataścenmanyate hatam| ubhau tau na vijānīto nāyaṃ hanti na hanyata" iti śravaṇāt| hantuṃuhantāram| "jantu" mityādāviva auṇādikastun pratyayaḥ| "ya enaṃ vetti hantāraṃ yaścainaṃ manyate hatam| ubhau tau na vijānīto nāyaṃ hanti na hanyata" iti gītāvacanaikārthyāt| hananakartṛtvapratiṣedhaśca kartṛtvamātrapratiṣedhopalakṣaṇam| ata eva gītāsu bhagavadvacanaṃ - "prakṛteḥ kriyamāṇāni guṇaiḥ karmāṇi sarvaśaḥ| ahaṅkāravimūḍhātmā kartā'hamiti manyate|| kāryakāraṇakartṛtve hetuḥ prakṛtirucyate| puruṣassukhaduḥkhānāṃ bhoktṛtve heturucyata" iti| ato jīvaḥ prakṛtigataṃ vā kartṛvaṃ tattajjīvāsādhāraṇaprakṛtipariṇāmabuddhigataṃ vā kartṛtvamātmanyadhyasyan karteti vyapadiśyate| śāstramapi tathābhūtameva kartṛtvamapekṣya pravartate sahopādānādivyavahāreṇeti| evaṃ prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
buddheḥ prakṛtervā kartṛtve "yajeta svargakāmaḥ, mumukṣurbrahmopāsīte"tyevamādiśāstraṃ svargamokṣādiphalakāmaṃ bhoktārameva niyuṅkte| "śāstra phalaṃ prayoktarī"ti nyāyāt| kiṃ ca śāstreṇa kartā bodhajananadvārā niyujyate| na ca acetanayoḥ buddhiprakṛtyoḥ bodhanīyatvamasti, ato bhoktṛtvāt bodhanīyatvācca ātmaiva vidhiniṣedhaśāstraniyojya iti yuktam| na ca kūṭastha ātmā'haṅkarteti parakīyaṃ kartṛtvamātmanyadhyasitumapi prabhavati| tasmādadhyāso'pyātmaprabhāsthānīyadharmajñānapariṇāmatayā taddharmatāṃ netavya iti tathaiva kṛtirapi taddharmatāṃ nīyatām| kimarthaṃ bādhānupalambhe'pi "ahaṃ karte"ti pratibhāsasya bhrāntirūpatvaṃ, śāstrasya bhrāṃtikṛtakartṛtvālamvanatvañca, kalpyate| "hantā cenmanyate hantu"miti maṃtrastu "na jāyate mriyate vā vipaści"diti mantre "na hanyate hanyamāne'pi deha" iti dehātiriktasya ātmano hananāyogyatvena prakṛtatvāttadviṣayatayopapadyate| kartṛtvapratiṣedhakāni| "tatraivaṃ sati kartāramātmānaṃ kevalaṃ tu yaḥ| "paśyatyakṛtabuddhitvānna sapaśyati durmati"riti tadīyavacanāṃtareṇaikārthyāt| kiñca "sayathā mahārājo jānapadān gṛhītvā sve janapade yathākāmaṃ parivartetaivamevaiṣa etān prāṇān gṛhītvā sve śarīre yathākāmaṃ parivartata" ityupādānavihāropadeśadarśanāt, "vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute karmāṇi tanute'pi ce"ti laukikavedikakriyāsu kartṛtvavyapadeśācca, ātmā kartaiva| naca vijñāna śabdo'ntaḥkaraṇaparaḥ| antaḥ karaṇanirdeśe
śrutiṣu tṛtīyāyāeva darśanāt| ihāpi tathā nirdeśaviparyayaprasaṅgāt| "tadeṣāṃ prāṇānāṃ vijñānena vijñānamādāye"tyādivākyāntareṣu antaḥ karaṇaparasya vijñānaśabdasya tṛtīyānirdeśo hi dṛśyate| kiṃ ca prakṛteḥ kartṛtve tasyāssarvapuruṣasādhāraṇatvāt pūrvādhikaraṇoktopalabdhyādiniyamāsambhavarītyā karmaniyamasyāpyasambhavāt| tathā ca sarvāṇi karmāṇi sarveṣāṃ bhogāya syuḥ| ātmanāṃ sāṃkhyamate vibhutvena sarvatra sarveṣāṃ sannidhānāviśeṣāt| na ca antaḥ karaṇasya kartṛtvapakṣe nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, antaḥ karaṇānāṃ pratiśarīraṃ bhinnatvāditi vācyam| teṣāṃ tadāyatanaśarīrāṇāñca sarvātmasannidhāvutpadyamānānāṃ pratyātmavyavasthiterupapādayitumaśakyatvāt| buddheḥ kartṛtvapakṣe ca karturanyasya bhoktṛtvānupapatteḥ bhoktṛtvamapi tasyā eva syāditi ātmano bhoktṛtvaśaktirnasyāt| tathā ca "puruṣo'sti bhoktṛbhāvā"diti teṣāmeva abhyupagamavirodhaḥ| buddheḥ kartṛtve ca "prakṛteranyo'smī"tyevaṃrūpamokṣasādhanasamādhikartṛtvamapi tasyā eva vācyam| na ca prakṛtiḥ svarūpeṇa buddhipariṇatirūpeṇa vā prakṛteranyaḥ kūṭastho'smīti samādhātuṃ prabhavati| kiṃca ātmanaḥ kartṛtvābhyupagame satyāmicchāyāṃ karoti, anyadā na karoti| yathā takṣā vāsyādikaraṇasampanno'pi satyāmevecchāyāṃ karoti nānyadeti niyama upapadyate| buddhestu acetanāyāḥ kartṛtve tasyā bhogecchādiniyamakāraṇābhāvāt sarvadā kartṛtvaṃ syāt| tasmādātmaiva svabhāvataḥ karteti yuktam||
sa na kartā guṇānāṃ hi kartṛtvaṃ vibhunā smṛtam|
maivaṃ śāstrārthavatvādyaiḥ kartā sa guṇayogataḥ||
iti kartradhikaraṇam||
parāttu tacchaØteḥ||33||
kṛtaprayatnāpekṣastu vihitapratiṣiddhāvaiyarthyādibhyaḥ||34||
kartṛtve cintite tadviśeṣaścintyata iti saṅgatiḥ| jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ syāttaṃ, uta paramātmāyattamiti vidhiniṣedhaśāstrāntaryāmiśāstrābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - svāyattameva jīvasya kartṛtvam| anyathā vidhiniṣedhaśāstrayoratyantavaiyarthyaṃ syāt| na ca "antaḥ praviṣṭaśśāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā, eṣahyeva sādhu karma kārayati taṃ yamebhyo lokebhya unninīṣate eṣa u evāsādhukarma kārayati taṃ yamadho ninīṣata" ityādyantaryāmiśāstrāt paramātmāyattaṃ kartṛtvamiti śaṅkanīyam| tathāsati puruṣapravṛttinivṛttyaidaṃparyeṇa pravṛttānāṃ vidhiniṣedhaśāstrāṇāṃ atyantavaiyarthyāpatteḥ, antaryāmiśāstrasyāpyasāmañjasyaprasaṅgācca| na hi kaścit svayameva kiṃcidaniṣṭaṃ karma kārayitvā tatphalaṃ nigrahamapi karotīti yuktamiti| evaṃprāpte siddhāntaḥ|
paramātmāyattamasya kartṛtvam| antaryāmiśāstrabṛndābādhasambhave tadbādhanāyogāt| sambhavati ca tadabādhena vidhiniṣedhaśāstrāvaiyarthyopapādanam| ādyapṛvṛttau bhagavānudāsīnaḥ dvitīyādipravṛttiṣvanumantā svabhaktānāṃ svadveṣiṇañca sveṣṭāniṣṭakarmasu kārayiteti bhagavato'vasthātrayasatvāt| tathā hi - ādau jīvasya karaṇakalebarādi datvā kimayaṃ kariṣyatīti paśyannudāsīno bhagavānāste| sa yatkaroti puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ vā tatphalarūpaṃ dvitīyādipravṛttimanumanyate| pravṛttapravartanamanumatiḥ| yattu mumukṣūṇāṃ prapadanopāsanādikaṃ sādhukarma, yacca tāmasānāṃ bhagavadveṣiṇāṃ bhagavannindādirūpamasādhukarma, tadubhayaṃ svayaṃ kārayati tadviṣayaṃ "eṣahyebe"tyādiśāstradvayam| na sarvaviṣayam| teṣāṃ satatayuktānāṃ bhajatāṃ prītipūrvakaṃ| dadāmi buddhiyogantaṃ yena māmupayānti ta"iti tānahaṃ dviṣataḥ krūrān saṃsāreṣu narādhamān| kṣipāmyajasramaśubhānāsurīṣveva yoniṣu" itica bhagavadvacanātū tatonyatravidhiniṣedhaśāstrāvakāśaḥ tannātyantavaiyarthyamiti kecidācāryāḥ tadvaiyarthyaṃ samādadhuḥ|
anyetvāhuḥ - naivamantaryāmiśāstrasya viṣayasaṅkocaḥ kalpanīyaḥ| bhagavato jīvakarmaviśeṣeṣu audāsīnyānumantṛtvakalpane pramāṇābhāvāt udāhṛtagītāvacanayoḥ karmāntarakārayitṛtvābhāvaparyantakalpane kalpakābhāvāt| na ca vidhiniṣedhaśāstrāvaiyarthyārthaṃ tathākalpanam| sarveṣu vihitaniṣiddhakarmasu īśvarasya kālādṛṣṭendriyādivat kāraṇatve'pi jīvasya kartṛtvākṣateḥ tadicchādīnāmiveśvarasyāpi kāraṇatvopapatteḥ| na ca
tathāsaṃti jīvasya vihitaniṣiddhakarmasu "svatantraḥ karte"ti lakṣitaṃ kartṛtvaṃ nasyāt, balādīśvarasya kārayitṛtvena nadīrayākṛṣyamāṇapuruṣasyeva svātaṃtryahāniriti vācyam| prayoyojakena rājñā niyuktānāṃ prayojyakatarṝṇāmamātyādīnāmapi svakarmasu svecchayā pravartamānānāṃ svātaṃtryadarśanāt| tasmādantaryāmiśāstrāsaṅkocena vidhipratiṣedhāvaiyarthyopapādanāsambhavāt na tatsaṅkocaḥ kalpya iti| matadvayasyāpyupapādanaprapañcastattadgrantheṣu draṣṭavyaḥ||
saṅgrahakārikāḥ||
kartṛtā paravatī śarīriṇāmīśvareṇa yadi nāma varṇyate|
vyarthatā vidhiniṣedhaśāstrayośśakyate śamayituṃ na kenacit||
asti tāvadaraviṃdalocanaśśāsitā yamayiteti ca śrutiḥ|
sā yathā vidhiniṣedhalaṅghanaṃ nādadhīta nayatastathocyate||
datvādau karaṇakalebarādi paśyatyudyogaṃ prathamamudāsitaiva devaḥ|
paścāttatphalamanumanyate dvitīyaṃ keṣāṃcidbhavati kṛtau prayojako'pi||
antaryāmiśruteritthamanyavaiyarthyaśaṅkayā|
saṅkocamūcire kecit mumukṣudveṣikarmasu||
icchādyadhīnatāvatsyāt īśvarādhīnatā'pinaḥ|
tāvatā śāstravaiyarthyaṃ netyasaṅkocanaṃ pare||
iti parāyattādhikaraṇam||
aṃśonānāvyapadeśādanyathācāpi dāśakitayādivatvamadhīyataeke||35||
maṃtravarṇāt||36||
apica smaryate||37||
prakāśādivattu naivaṃ paraḥ||38||
smaranti ca||39||
anujñāparihārau dehasambandhājjyotirādivat||40||
asantateśvāvyatikaraḥ||41||
ābhāsaeva ca||42||
adṛṣṭāniyamāt||43||
abhisaṃdhyādiṣvapi caivam||44||
pradeśabhedāditi cennāntarbhāvāt||45||
jīveśvarayoḥ pravartyapravartakabhāve rājabhṛtyayoriva atyantabhedassyāditi jīvasya paramātmāṃśatvakathanamapyaupacārikaṃ syāditi śaṃkayā saṃgatiḥ| kiṃ jīvaḥ parasmādatyaṃtabhinnaḥ, utājñānāvasthāpannaṃ parameva brahma, yadvā brahmaivopādhyavacchinnaṃ, athavā viśiṣṭasya brahmaṇoviśeṣaṇarūpoṃśaiti śrutivipratipattessaṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - atyaṃtabhinnojīvaḥ| "jñājñaudvā"vityādibhedanirdeśāt| ajñaṃbrahmaivavā jīvaḥ| "tatvamasi, ayamātmābrahme"tyadiśravaṇāt| brahmaiva satvopādhyavacchinnaṃ vā jīvaḥ| tatvamasyādiśravaṇādeva| na tu brahmaṇoṃśojīvaiti yuktaṃ| aṃśatvaṃ hi tantvādivannāvayavatvarūpaṃ| niravayavasya brahmaṇo'vayavārabdhatvābhāvāt| ataeva ṭaṅkacchinnapāṣāṇaśakalavadapi nāṃśatvam| brahmaṇaḥ khaṇḍanānarhatvācceti|| siddhāntastu - 
brahmaṇoṃ'śa eva jīvaḥ| nānāvyapadeśāt| abhedavyapadeśācca| sraṣṭṝtvasṛjyatvaniyantṛtvaniyāmyatva sarvajñatvakiṃcidjñatvasvādhīnatvaparādhīnatvapatitvaśeṣatvādibhirbhedavyapadeśo hi bhūyān dṛśyate| tathaiva abhedavyapadeśo'pi tatvamasyādirdṛśyate| api ca ātharvaṇikāḥ brahmasūkte brahmaṇodāśakitavādirūpatvamapyadhīyate|
"brahmadāśā brahmadāsā brahme me kitavā uta| strīpuṃsau brahmaṇo jātau striyo brahmā'thavā pumā"niti| tasmādviśiṣṭabrahmaṇo viśeṣaṇarūpoṃśo jīvaityeva abhyupagantavyam| evaṃ satyeva bhedābhedaśrutīnāṃ baṃdhamokṣavyavasthādīnāñca upapattiḥ| satyamithyopādhyavacchinnabrahmajīvavādayostu bandhamokṣavyavasthādyanupapattiḥ, sarvaśrutisāmañjasyānupapattiśca anyatra prapañcitā| ato viśiṣṭaikadeśarūpoṃśo jīvaityeva yuktam| "pādo'sya viśvābhūtāni, tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divī"ti maṃtravarṇāt, "mamaivāṃśo jīvaloke jīvabhūtassanātana" iti gītāvacanācca evamevāvagamyate| evañca yathā prakāśo maṇyādīnāṃ prakāśavatāṃ viśeṣaṇāṃśaḥ, yathā vā dehināṃ devamanuṣyādidehaḥ, tathaiva apṛthaksiddhaprakāratayā śarīratayā ca īśvarasya jīvo viśeṣaṇa rūpoṃśaḥ| na tu saeva saiti| viśeṣaṇabhedāt bhedaśrutayaḥ viśeṣṭarūpaikyādabhedaśrutayaścopapadyante| yathābhūto jīvaśśeṣatvādilakṣaṇaḥ naivaṃbhūtaḥ paraśśeṣatvādilakṣaṇa iti prabhāprabhāvannyāyena śarīraśarīribhāvena ca jīveśvarayorvyavasthitaśeṣaśeṣibhāvādayaśca upapadyante| smaranti ca parāśarādayaḥ jīveśvarayoḥ prabhāprabhāvannyāyaṃ śarīraśarīribhāvañca, "ekadeśasthitasyāgnerjyotsnāvistāriṇī yathā| parasya brahmaṇaśśaktistathedamakhilañjagat|| yatkiñcitsṛjyate yena satvajātena vai dvija| tasya sṛjyasya saṃbhūtau tatsarvaṃvai harestānu" riti| nanu sarveṣāmīśvarāṃśatvāviśeṣe kathaṃ dvijānāṃ vedādhyayanādyanujñā, śūdrāṇāṃ tatparihāra iti cet, dehaviśeṣasambandhāditi brūmaḥ| yathā'gnyudakādīnāṃ śrotriyāgārasthitidaśāyāṃ karmārhatvaṃ, teṣāmeva śmaśānadeśaṃ prāptānāṃ karmānarhatvaṃ, evamātmanāṃ vividhadehasthitidaśāyāmanujñāparihāradvayamupapadyate| sarveṣāmātmanāṃ brahmāṃśatvenaikarūpye'pi teṣāmanyonyabhedāt aṇutvena pratiśarīraṃ bhinnatvācca bhogavyatikaro'pi na bhaviṣyati| kintu brahmātmaikyavāda eva jīvaparayorjīvānāñca saprasajyate| na ca avidyākṛtopādhibhedāt tadaprasaṅgaḥ| akhaṇḍaikarasaprakāśamātrasvarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpatirodhāyakopādhibhedopapādakahetūnāmābhāsatvāt| na khalu dārukhaṇḍe nipatan kuṭhāro dārukhaṇḍamiva brahmaṇi samanvayannupādhirbrahma bhinatti| upādhayo bhinnā iti cet| kiṃ tataḥ? bhogānāśrayāṇāṃ teṣāṃ bhedena bhogāśrayaikyādāpatatsāṅkaryaṃ kathaṃ parihriyeta| satyamithyopādhyupahita brahmajīvavādayorupādhihetubhūtānādyadṛṣṭavaśāt vyavasthā bhaviṣyatīti cet, na|
upādhibhirivādṛṣṭairapi akhaṇḍe brahmaṇi chedanādyasaṃbhavāt| etena adṛṣṭahetusaṅkalpānuṣṭhānaśarīrādipratiniyamāt vyavasthetyāśaṅkā'pi nirastā| saṅkalpādīnāmapi sarvātmasananidhāvatpadyamānānāṃ pratyātmaniyamahetvabhāvāt| nanu ātmapradeśanavyavasthayā bhogavyavasthā'stviti cet na|brahmaṇaḥ pradeśāśśarīrāntarbhūtāeva bhogāśrayāvācyāḥ| tadantarbhūtapradesāstu na vyavasthitāḥ| prāyaḥ sarveṣāmapi pradeśānāṃ sañcaratsu sarveṣu śarīreṣvantarbhāvāt| tasmātsarvagatātmapakṣa iva satyamitopādhyupahitātmaikya pakṣe'pi na bhogavyavasthopapadyata iti ātmanāmaṇutvavat bhedo'pyabhyupagantavya iti siddham||
saṃgrahakārikā||
jīvasyātyantabhede gatirupacaraṇaṃ syādabhedaśrutīnāṃ
gāḍhābhede tu bhedaśrutitatisahitassaṅkaro durnivāraḥ|
tasmādaṃśaḥparasyetyucitamiha sakhalvekavastvekadeśo
viṃkhyāto'yaṃ viśiṣṭe bhagavatica bhavatyekadeśaḥ prakāraḥ||
iti aṃśādhikaraṇam||7||
