gauṇyasaṃbhavāttatprākchaØteśca||2||
tatpūrvakatvādvācaḥ||3||
jīvotpattitadanubadhijñātṛtvādinirūpaṇānaṃtaraṃ jīvopakaraṇānāmiṃdriyāmāmutpattiprakāro viśodhyata iti saṃgatiḥ| kimiṃdriyāṇāṃ viyadādivat svarūpānyathābhāvarūpotpattiḥ, uta jīvavat dharmānyathābhāvamātrarūpeti śrutivipratipattessaṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - yadyapi viyadadhikaraṇe "brahmātiriktaṃ sarvamutpattima" diti nirṇītaṃ, tathāpīṃdriyāṇāṃ viyadādivat na svarūpānyathābhāvarūpotpattiḥ| "asadvā itamagraāsīt tadāhuḥ kiṃ tadāsīditi ṛṣayo vāva te agre sadāsīt ke te ṛṣaya iti prāṇāvāva ṛṣaya itī" ti, sargādyakāle'pi sthitiśravaṇāt prāṇaśabdasya bahuvacanāṃtasya iṃdriyeṣveva, "prāṇamanūtkrāmaṃtaṃ sarve prāṇā anūtkrāmaṃtī"ti prayogadarśanāt "tasyāsataṛṣayassaptatīra" iti ṛṣiśabdasyāpi teṣu dṛṣṭaprayogatvācca| yadyapi prāṇaśabdo brahmaṇyapi dṛṣṭaprayogaḥ, ṛṣiśabdaśca jñānavattvādupapannaḥ, tathā'pi bahuvacanāṃtayoranayostasminnanvayo na ghaṭate| na "aditiḥ pāśā" niti bahavacanavat iha bahuvacanaṃ avayavabahutvaviṣayatayā yojayituṃ śakyam| brahmaṇo niravayavatvāditi| siddhāntastu - 
brahmaṇa ekasyaiva sargādyakāle'vasthiteḥ sāvadhāraṇāsu bahuṣu śrutiṣu śravaṇāt, prāṇaśabdasya tasminnirūḍhisatvāt, ṛṣiśabdasya jñānasādhanendriyāpekṣayā jñānavati tatra svārasyācca, brahmaivātra prāṇāḥ| bahuvacanaṃ tu "guṇetvanyāyyakalpane" ti nyāyāt pāśabahutvavannetavyam| avayavābhāve'pi viśeṣaṇabahutvasya tadālambanasya sadbhāvāt| naca ekatvāvadhāraṇe'pi jīvavadiṃdriyāṇāmapi satvamastviti vācyam| kṛtahānākṛtābhyāgamaparihārāya adṛṣṭāśrayatayā jīvasyeva teṣāṃ tadā satvasyāvaktavyatvāt, udāhṛtaśruteḥ brahmaṇyeva svarasyasya darśitatvācca| sargādyakāle jīvakṛtyā dṛṣṭāvasthitivat vāgādīndriyakṛtyasya kasyāpyabhāvāt, bhūtasṛṣṭhyanaṃtarameva nāmarūpavyākaraṇaśravaṇena tataḥ prāgvāgādīṃdriyakṛtyānāṃ nāmoccāraṇādīnāmabhāvāvagamāt, "etasmājjāyata" iti maṃtre teṣāṃ viyadādisamānasvarūpānyathābhāvarūpajananaśravaṇācca||
saṃgrahakārikā||
agre satvātprāṇāḥ nityā iticenna sṛjyatvāt|
prāṇapadantadbrahmaṇi bahuvacanaṃ pāśavanneyaṃ||
iti prāṇādhikaraṇam||
saptagaterviśeṣitatvācca||4||
hastādayastu sthite'to naivaṃ||5||
viyadādivadutpattimattvena siddhānāmiṃdriyāṇāṃ saṃkhyāviśeṣaścityata iti saṃṅgatiḥ| "kimiṃdriyāṇi sapta uta ekādaśe" ti śrutivipratipattessaṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| saptaiva| iha paratra ca jīvena saha saptānāmeva hi gatiḥ śrūyate|
"sapta prāṇāḥ prabhavanti tasmāt saptārciṣassamidhassaptajihvāḥ| sapta ime lokā yeṣu caraṃti prāṇā guhāśayā nihitāssapta sapte"| vīpsā puruṣabhedābhiprāyā| viśeṣitāśca te gatimantaḥ prāṇāḥ svarūpanirdeśena - "yadā pañcāvatiṣṭhante jñānāni manasā saha| buddhiśca na viceṣṭeta tāmāhuḥparamāṃ gati"miti|atra hi jñānasādhanāni cakṣurādīni pañca manobuddhiśceti saptaivedaṃtayoṃdriyāṇi nirdiṣṭāni| paramāṃgatimiti paramagatirūpamokṣasādhanaṃ yogamāhurityarthaḥ| "yāni tvaṣṭau grahāḥ aṣṭāvatigrahāḥ sapta vai śīrṣaṇyāḥ prāṇāḥ dvāvavāñcau, nava vai puruṣe prāṇānābhirdaśamī, daśa vai paśoḥ prāṇā ātmaikadaśa" ityādyaṣṭādyadhikasaṃkhyāśravaṇaṃ tadanyeṣāmapi kiṃcitkiṃcijjīvopakārakatvamātreṇa iṃdriyatvopacārāt nābhivat| na hi nābhiraṃdhramiṃdriyam| iṃdriyāṇi tu sahagatimaṃti saptaiva| nyūnādhikasaṃkhyāśravaṇae ca nyūnasaṃkhyāgrahaṇaṃ nyāyyam| lāghavāt| adhikasaṃkhyāgrahaṇe nyūnasaṃkyānāṃ tatrāntarbhāvāt sarvābādho bhavatītyadhikasaṃkhyāgrahaṇameva nyāyyamiti cenna| tathāsati
vāgādipcagāhaṅkāracittābhyāṃ saha kvaciccaturdaśeṃdriyāṇi śrūyanta iti caturdaśasaṃkhyāgrahaṇāpattyā ekādaśeti niyamābhāvaprasaṅgāditi| siddhāntastu - 
hastādīnāmapi śarīrasthite jīve kāryabhedena tadbhogopakaraṇatvāt, jñāneṃdriyāṇi paṃca, karmeṃdriyāṇi paṃca, manaścetyekādaśeṃdriyāṇītyevaṃ maṃtavyam| ato "daśeme puruṣe prāṇā ātmaikādaśaekādaśamataśce"tyādiśrutismṛtisiddhendriyasaṃkhyā vyavasthitaiva| nānāvidhasaṃkhyāśravaṇe kāryānusārema saṃkhyāniyamanameva hi yuktaṃ| nānāvidhāvadānasādhanaśravaṇe sāmarthyānusāreṇāvadeyaniyamanamiva| tadadhikasaṃkhyāvādāstu manovṛttibhedābhiprāyāḥ| mahadanugṛhīte manaḥ adhyavasāyaṃ, ahaṅkārānugṛhītaṃ manaḥ abhimānaṃ, pūrvasaṃskārānugṛhītaṃ manaḥ cintāṃcakāroti| mana evādhyavasāyādirūpavṛttibhedopahitaṃ buddhyahaṅkāracittaśabdairabhidhīyata iti| nyūnasaṃkhyāśravaṇāni tu tatra tatra vivakṣitagamanādikāryaviśeṣābhiprāyāṇi| yogadaśāyāṃ karmendriyebhyo'pi jñānendriyāṇāṃ yatnena vaśīkartavyatvarūpamasti prādhānyaṃ tadabhiprāyā "yadā paṃcāvatiṣṭhaṃta" iti smṛtiḥ| "saptavai śīrṣaṇyāḥ prāṇā" iti saptānāṃ śīrṣaṇyatvarūpamasti prādhānyam, tadabhiprāyā "saptaprāṇā" iti śrutiḥ gatiśrutiśca| ekādaśānāmapi gatiśrutirastyeva| "daśeme puruṣe prāṇāḥ ātmaikādaśaḥ te yadā'smānmartyāccharīrādutkrāmantī" tyādikā| ato ekādaśendriyāṇīti siddham|| saṅgrahakārikā||
saptaprāṇā yuktāḥ saptānāmeva gatikathanāt|
maivaṃ saha hastādyaiḥ ekādaśakāryabhedātsyuḥ||
iti saptagatyadhikaraṇam||
aṇavaśca||6||
tānīndriyāṇi kiṃ vibhūni paricchinnāniveti saṃśaye "ta ete sarva eva samāḥ sarve'nantā" iti ānantyaśravaṇāt asyendriyasya idaṃ parimāṇamiti parimāṇaviśeṣānuktisahitāt vibhureveti prāpte siddhāntaḥ - 
utkarāntigatyā gatiśravaṇāt, ānantyaśravaṇasya "sa yohaitānanantānupāste anantaṃ salokaṃ jayati" iti vākyaśeṣeṇa upāsanārthatvāvagamācca paricchinnānyeva| dūragatavastugrahaṇaṃ tu śīghragatimatvādinopapadyate||
saṃgrahakārikā||
te vibhavo'naṃtatvaśravaṇāt maivaṃ gatāgatotkramaṇaiḥ|
sūkṣmāste teṣāṃ punarupāsanāphalamanantatāśravaṇam||
śreṣṭhaśca||7||
indriyāṇāmutpattivicārānantaraṃ chandogānāṃ prāmasaṃvāde indriyaissaha vivādena labdhaśraiṣṭyasya mukhyaprāṇasya indriyaissaha vivādoktyaiva tatsamānacchāyatāvagamāt indriyotpattivicārānantaraṃ mukhyaprāṇotpattirvicāryata iti saṃgatiḥ| yadyapi "etasmājjāyate prāṇa" iti maṃtrata eva indriyāṇāmiva prāmasyāpi viyadādivadutpattissidhyati, tathāpi prāṇasya bhūtavāyoḥ indriyavṛttibhyaścānyatvaṃ śrutivirodhasamādhānena nirninīṣuḥ tasya prākū prakṛtotpattivicāreṇa prāsaṃgikatvasaṃpādanāya kiṃcidadhikaśaṃkānirākaraṇāya ca prāṇasyotpattiriha ciṃtyate| adhikaśaṃkātvitthaṃ - sargādyakāle jīvakāryamadṛṣṭāvasthānamiva vāgādīndriyakāryaṃ nāmadheyoccāraṇādikaṃ nāsti| ataḥ prāṇarṣi śabdau brahmaṇi dṛṣṭaprayogau brahmaparāvityuktaṃ| prāṇasyatu "nāsadāsīnnosadāsīttadānīṃ tama āsīttamasā gūḍhamagre praketa" mityupakrānte nāsadāsīye sūkte "ānīdavātaṃ svadhayātadeka"miti ananākhyāsādhāraṇavyāpārasadbhāvaśśrutaḥ| na ca ānīcchabdaḥ kvacidapi brahmaṇi dṛṣṭaprayomaḥ| na ca "abāta"miti viśeṣaṇāt ananākhyaprāṇavyāpāro nabhavatyānīcchabdaḥ| kintu brahmasattāpara iti vācyam| saṃvargavidyādiṣu vāyuprāṇayoḥ pṛthagupadeśena vāyvabhāve'pi prāṇasadbhāvopapatteriti|| tannivṛttistvitthaṃ - vāyostatvāṃtarābhāvena vāyuviśeṣātmako bhavatyeva prāṇaḥ| tasmāt "avāta"miti vāyusāmānyaniṣedhāt ānīcchabdasya prāṇavyāpāratvāyogena brahmaparatvameva vaktuṃ yuktaṃ| tathāca svena brahmaṇa dhīyate upabhujyata iti vyutpattyā svadhāśabdena lakṣmīrucyata iti lakṣmīviśiṣṭasya bhagavataḥ
parabrahmaṇassargādyasamaye sthitiruktā bhavatīti "śreṣṭhaśce"ti sūtraṃ "pauṣṇaṃ peṣaṇaṃ vikṛtau pratīyete"ti pūrvataṃtrasūtravat uttaracintārthatvāt uttarādhikaraṇaśeṣaeva| nādhikaraṇāntaramiti kecidāhuḥ|| iti śreṣṭhāṇutvādhikaraṇam||
na vāyukriye pṛthagupadeśāt||8||
cakṣurādivattu tatsahaśiṣṭhyādibhyaḥ||9||
akaraṇatvācca na doṣastathā hi darśayati||10||
paṃcavṛttirmanovadvyapadiśyate||11||
kimayaṃ mukhyaprāṇaḥ vāyumātraṃ. uta ucchvāsaniśvāsarūpā tatkriyā, athavā avasthāviśeṣāpanno vāyuriti saṃśaye "yaḥ prāṇassa vāyu"riti śravaṇāt vāyumātraṃ ucchvāsaniśvāsarūpā tatkriyāvā| tatra prāṇaśabdaprayogāditi pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ| siddhāntastu - 
"etasmājjāyata" iti maṃtre pṛthagupadeśānnavāyumātram| vāyumātre prāṇaśabdāprayogācca| ata eva tadīyā niśvāsādikriyā'pi na bhavati| agnyādikriyāyā iva vāyukriyāyā api "etasmājjāyata" iti dravyotpattimaṃtre pṛthagutpattyupadeśānarhatvāt tasyābhāvārthapratyayāntaprāṇaśabdārhatve'pi prāṇitīti kartrarthapratyayāntasya prāṇiti anena jīvatīti karaṇārthapratyayāntasya vā tasyāṃ prayogābhāvāditi| nanu yadi vāyureva kiṃcidavasthāntaraṃ prāptaḥ prāṇaḥ, tarhi kimagniriva prāṇopi vāyutvaṃ parityajet, netyucyate| nāyamagnivadbhūtāntaraṃ| kintu vāyutvāparityāgenaiva jīvasya cakṣurādivadupakaraṇaviśeṣaḥ| tairviśiṣṭatvāt| prāṇasaṃvāde hi "atha ha prāṇā ahaṃśreyase vyūdire ahaṃ śreyānasmī"ti cakṣurādibhissahakalahābhidhānena tatsamānacchāyatvāvasāyāt, tadanantaraṃ "tvaṃnaśreṣṭho'sīti cakṣurādivacanena prāṇasya mukhyatvanirdhāraṇācca| samānajātīyānāmeva hi sahaśiṣṭastulyopakārakatva saṃśayena parasparotkarṣakalahaḥ| kasyacidupakārādiśayadarśanena mukhyatvanirdhāraṇaṃ ca bhavati| jīvopakaraṇatvenaprasiddhaissamānajātīyatvañca prāṇasyāpi tadupakaraṇatve satyeva bhavati| nanu cakṣurādibhiriva prāṇena jīvasyopakāraviśeṣo na dṛśyataiticennāyaṃ doṣaḥ| śrutirevāhi śarīrendriyadhāraṇarūpāṃ kriyāṃ tadupakāratvena darśayati| yathā prāṇasaṃvāde cakṣurādīn prati prāṇasya vacanaṃ "māmohamāpadyathāhamevaitatpaṃcadhātmānaṃ pravibhajya etadbāṇamavaṣṭabhya vidhārayāmī"ti| nanu paṃcadhā bhede vyānādayaḥ kiṃ prāṇāttatvāṃtarāṇi netyucyate| "kāmassaṃkalpovicikitsā śraddhā'śraddhādhṛtiradhṛtirhrīrdhīrbhīrityetatsarvaṃ manaeve"ti vacanātkāmasaṃkalpādayo manasa iva prāṇavāyorakasyaiva prāṇāpānādayovṛttibhedāḥ| etenendriyāṇāṃ sādhāraṇī vṛttiḥ prāṇaiti sāṃkhyapakṣe nirastaḥ| tehi kalpayanti "paṃjarasthānaṃ sarveṣāṃ śukānāṃ paṃjaracālanamiva vā sarvāsāṃ
khalakapotikānāmuparipatitavāgurotkṣepaṇamivavā śarīrasthānāṃ sarveṣāmindriyāṇāṃ sādhāraṇavayāpāraḥ prāṇa"iti| naitadyuktaṃ| indriyakāryaviṣayopalabdhivyatirekeṇocchvāsaniśvāsakriyāyāḥ, svātmānaṃ paṃcadhā vibhajya dehadhāraṇakriyāyāśca mukhyaprāṇasvataṃtrakāryasyadarśanāt||
saṃgrahakārike||
mukhyaprāṇopyakṣavargoktarītyā brahmotpannovāyumātraṃ tathokteḥ|
dehāṃtarvā tatkriyā śvāsarūpā maivaṃ dravyaissākamutpattivādāt||
vāyorevatvavasthāṃtaramayamiti saṃgacchate vāyutoktiḥ
pūrvāvasthānuvṛtterbhavati dahanavannāsya bhūtāṃtaratvaṃ|
nityaṃ saṃsārabhājāmupakaraṇamidaṃ śiṣyate cakṣurādhaiḥ
khyātātyaṃtopakārastanukaraṇamayaṃ dhārayanpañcavṛttiḥ||
iti vāyukriyādhikaraṇam||
aṇuśca||12||
prāṇasvarūpanirdhāraṇāntaraṃ tatparimāṇaṃ nirūpyataiti saṃgatiḥ| prāṇovibhuḥ paricchinnoveti śrutivipratipattessaṃśaye vibhuriti pūrvaḥpakṣaḥ| "samaebhistribhirlokaissamonena sarveṇa, prāṇe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ, sarvaṃ hīdaṃ prāṇenāvṛta"miti śravaṇāt| nanvindriyāṇāmānantyaśravaṇe'pi ukramaṇādiliṃgena paricchinnatvaṃ prasādhitaṃ, tenaiva nyāyena prāṇasyāpi paricchinnatvaṃ sidhyet| kimanenādhikaraṇena| ucyate - upāstividhānāṃtargatamānantyaṃ, idaṃtu natatheti pūrvapakṣīmanyate| yadvā "sarvaṃhīdaṃprāṇenāvṛta"mitītyupapattisahitaṃ sarvasāmyaśravaṇamutkramaṇādiliṃgāt prabalaṃ| atastadaupayikotkramaṇādirūpaṃ vācyamiti pūrvapakṣyabhiprāyaḥ| upāsanāvidhiśeṣatvaṃ sarvasāmyaliṃgepi tulyaṃ "evametatsāmyaṃ vede"tyupāsanāvidhisannidhānāt| "prāṇe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ sarvāhīdaṃ prāṇenāvṛta"mityetadapi sarvasya śarīrasya prāṇe pratiṣṭhitatvaṃ prāṇenāvṛtatvaṃ ca brūte| na tu sarvasya jagataḥ prāṇāvṛtatvarūpaṃ| prāṇasya vyāpakatvaṃ hiśabdaprayogena prasiddhārthavādatvāvagamāt śarīrasyeva ca prāṇapratiṣṭhitatvaprāṇāvṛtatvayoḥ lokataśśrutyaṃtarataśca prasiddheriti siddhāntaḥ||
saṃgrahakārikā||
samaebhistribhirlokairityāmnānādayaṃ vibhuḥ|
aṇurutkramaṇādibhyaḥ sarvasāmyaśrutisstutiḥ||
iti prāṇāṇutvādhikaraṇam||
jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānantu tadāmananātprāṇavatā śabdāt||
tasya ca nityatvam||14||
prāṇānāmutpattimattāparimāṇādivicārānantaraṃ agnyādidevatādhiṣṭhitatvaprakāraviśeṣaścityata iti saṃgatiḥ| kimagnyādidevatānāṃ prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ "abhimānivyapadeśastu viśeṣānugatibhyā"miti prākprasaṃgāduktaṃ| tat svasvāyattaṃ uta īśvarāyattamiti saṃdehe pūrvapakṣaḥ - yadyapi "parātta tacchaØte"rityadhikaraṇe jīvānāṃ kartṛtvamīśvarāyattamityuktaṃ, tathāpi agnyādityādīnāmīśvaradattaiśvaryāṇāṃ īśvaravat varadāyitvaphalapradatvādisatvena svātaṃtryasatvāttattadāyattamevādhiṣṭhātṛtvamastu parāyattatāśruteḥ manuṣyādiviṣayatvena saṃkocopapatteriti| siddhāntastu - 
jyotirādīnāmagnyādityādīnāṃ yaccakṣurādyadhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ tatparameśvarasyaiva saṃkalpāt| kutaḥ? tasya sarvāntaryāmitvaśruteḥ| ato yathā jīvasya "evamevaiṣa etān prāṇān gṛhītvā sve śarīre yathākāmaṃ paricartata" iti svātaṃtryaśravaṇe'pi tasya indriyādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ parāyattaṃ, evamabhimānidevatānāmapīti yuktaṃ| nanu parameśvaradattaiśvaryema devatānāṃ manuṣyādibhyo viśeṣostīti cenna| sarveṣāṃ saṃsāriṇāmīśvaraniyamyatvasya nityattvāt "sa īśo'sya jagatonityaeve"tyaviśeṣaśravaṇāt| ataḥ
prāgdevatāpadaprāpteḥ parameśvaraniyamyānāṃ tāsāṃ tadanantaramapi amātyānāṃ rājaniyamyatvavat īśvaraniyamyatvamavarjanīyame||
saṃgrahakārike||
agnyādidevatānāmārādhyānāṃ phalapradānāṃca|
prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ svāyattaṃ bhavatu ko doṣaḥ||
maivaṃ tadīśvarādīnāṃ tāsāṃ prāṇavatā saha|
nityānubandhinīhyeṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ tanniyamyatā||
iti jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānādhikaraṇam||
ta indriyāṇi tadvyapadeśādanyatraśreṣṭāt||15||
bhedaśrutervailakṣaṇyācca||16||
indriyāṇāṃ prāṇasya cotpattyādinirūpaṇāntaraṃ kiṃ sarve prāṇaśabdanirdriṣṭā indriyāṇi uta mukhyaprāṇavyatiriktā iti vicāryate| nanu kimasya vicārasya prayojanaṃ| mukhyaprāṇānugatatadvyāvṛttalakṣaṇavicārasya vyavahāra viśeṣa eva prayojanamiti cet maivaṃ| na khalu vaiśeṣikaśāstra iva vyavahāramātraprayojano lakṣaṇavicārostyasmin śāstre ucyate| śrutivipratipattinirāsārtha evāyamapi vicāraḥ| tathāhi - bahuvacanāṃtaḥ prāṇaśabdaḥ indriyaparyāya iti nirvivādaṃ| tasya mukhyavyāvṛttaḥ prayogo dṛśyate - "prāṇamanūtkrāmantaṃ sarve prāṇā anūtkrāmantī"ti| tena śreṣṭhādanye cakṣurādaya eveṃdriyāṇīti pratīyate| "athaha yaevāyaṃ mukhyaḥ prāṇastamudgīthamupāsāṃcakrira"iti śreṣṭhe mukhyaprāṇaśabdaprayogāt sopīndriyāṃtarbhūta iti pratīyate| yena pravṛttinimittena cakṣurādiṣu prāṇaśabdapravṛttiḥ tenaiva śreṣṭhe tatpravṛttau hi śreṣṭhaḥprāṇānāṃ madhye mukhyaprāṇo bhavati yathā brāhmaṇānāṃ madhya eva kaścinmukhyabrahmaṇo bhavati| evaṃ śrutivipratipattau satyāṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ - prāṇopīndriyameva| prāṇaśabdavācyeṣu tasya mukhyatvapratipādanāt| na ca "etasmājjāyata" iti maṃtre prāṇasya pṛthagutpattivacanāndindriyebhyo bhedaśśaṃkanīyaḥ| tathā sati manasopi tebhyo bhedāpatteḥ| prāṇasyāpīndriyatve satyeva prāyassajātīyeṣveva loke dṛṣṭaḥ parasparotkarṣakalahaḥ cakṣurādīnāṃ prāṇasya ca śrūyamāṇassaṃgacchate| na cāṃtaraṃgeṇendriyatvena sājātye saṃbhavati bahiraṃgeṇa jīvopakaraṇatvena sājātyāt sa upapādanīyaḥ| evaṃ ca "indriyāṇi daśaikañce"tyādivacanaṃ mukhyaprāṇātiriktendriyādiviṣayaṃ| yathā "saptaprāṇā"iti vacanaṃ śīrṣaṇyaprāṇamātraviṣayamiti naikādaśasaṃkhyoktivirodhaḥ| tasmāt "sa yohaitānanaṃtānupāsta" iti vihitamindriyopāsanaṃ mukhyaprāṇasahitameva kāryamiti| siddhāṃtastu - 
śreṣṭhe mukhyaprāṇaśabdaḥ prāṇaśabdoditānāmindriyāṇāṃ madhye mukhyendriyarūpa ityevamartho na bhavati, kintu mukhebhavaḥ prāṇa ityetadarthakaḥ| bṛhadāraṇyake samānaprakaraṇe "athahemamāsanyaṃprāṇamūcu"riti mukhyaśabdasthāne āsanyaśabdaprayogāt, tasyacāsyebhava ityetadarthakatvāt| tatrahyāsyaśabdasya "paddannomās"ityādisūtreṇāsannādeśaḥ| yadi tu mukhyaḥ prāṇa ityasya prāṇānāṃ madhyemukhya ityarthaḥ, tathāpi prāṇasyendriyatvaṃ na labhyate| ayaṃ prāṇaḥ prāṇaśabdasya mukhyobhidheyaḥ, anye prāṇā indriyāṇi tasya lakṣaṇīyā na mukhyā ityevamarthakatvopapatteḥ, "ta etasyaiva sarve rūpamabhavaṃ stasmādeta etenākhyāyaṃta" iti mukhyaprāṇavācakenaiva satā cakṣurādīnāṃ lakṣaṇīyatvokteḥ| sarvametat "anyathātvaṃśabdāditice"diti guṇopasaṃhārapādādhikaraṇe spaṣṭībhaviṣyati| tasmānmukhyaprāṇavyatiriktāścakṣurādaya evendriyāṇi| "etasmājjāyate prāṇa"iti maṃtre pṛthaṅnirdeśādapi tathāvasīyate| manasa indriyebhyaḥ pṛthaṅnirdeśe'pi "manaṣṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇī"ti vacanāṃtarānusāreṇa manasa indriyatvamabhyupeyate| na tatheha prāṇasyendriyatve vacanāṃtaramasti| kiṃca prāṇasyendriyāṇāṃca vailakṣaṇyamapyasti| suṣuptau hi cakṣurādiṣu sarveṣūparateṣvapi prāṇasya vṛttirupalabhyate| "prāṇāgnaya evaitasmin pure jāgrati bhūrbhūrityeva śvasitī"ti śrutyāpyanūdyate| kāryavailakṣaṇyaṃ cāsti - jñānāni karmāṇi ca indriyāṇāṃ kāryāṇi, prāṇasya tu dehadhāraṇaṃ| tasmānmukhyaprāṇavyatiriktāścakṣurādayaeva indriyāṇi| teṣāṃcaikādaśānāmanugataṃ lakṣaṇaṃ sātvikāhaṃkāravikāratvam||
saṃgrahakārikā||
prāṇo'pīndriyamehaḥ sarveṣāṃ praṇaśabdāvācyatvāt|
maivaṃ pṛthagupadeśā danyeṣāṃ prāṇatā tu lakṣaṇayā||
iti indriyādhikaraṇam||
saṃjñāmūrtiklṛptistu trivṛtkurvata upadeśāt||17||
māṃsādibhaumaṃ yathāśabdamitarayośca||18||
vaiśeṣyāttu tadvādastadvādaḥ||19||
khādiṣu pūrvapūrvaśarīrakasya brahmaṇa uttarottarasṛṣṭikartṛtvapratipādanena sarvatrāpi sṛṣṭau brahmaṇassākṣātakartṛtvaṃ "bhedaśrute"riti sūtreṇa darśitaṃ| tannopapadyate - nāmarūpavyākaraṇe kevalaṃ hiraṇyagarbhasya kartṛtāyāḥ śrutismṛti
prasiddhatvādityākṣepeṇotthānāt saṃgatiḥ| khādisṛṣṭāviva nāmarūpavyākaraṇe'pi tasya sākṣātkartṛtvamupapadyata iti dṛṣṭāntasaṅgatirvā| "anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇī"ti śrutiḥ, "nāmarūpañca bhūtānāṃ kṛtyānāṃca prapaṃcanam| vedaśabdebhyaevādau devādīnāṃ cakāra sa" icyādi smṛtiśca hiraṇyagarbhasya nāmarūpavyākartṛtvaṃ pratipādayati, uta taccharīrakasya paramātmana iti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ - hiraṇyagarbhasyaiva pratipādayati| "jīvena vyākaravāṇī"ti śravaṇāt| nanvevaṃ sati "yathā cāreṇa parasainyaṃ kalayānīti narapatiramanyate"ti laukikavākye cārakartṛkaṃ parasainyasaṅkalanaṃ rājñi gauṇaṃ vyapadiśyate| evamihāpi jīvakartṛkaṃ nāmarūpavyākaraṇaṃ brahmaṇi gauṇaṃ vyapadiṣṭamiti syāt, naiṣa doṣaḥ| rājacārayoriva jīveśvarayoraṃśāṃśinossvarūpabhedābhāvenātrāpi "jīvenātmane"ti jīvasyeśvarakartṛtvasvātmatvaparāmarśena ca jīvakartṛkasya saṅkalanasya mukhyatayaiveśvarakartṛtvasvātmatvaparāmarśena ca jīvakartṛkasya saṅkalanasya mukhyatayaiveśvarakartṛkatvopapatteḥ| naca sahayogalakṣaṇeyaṃ tṛtīyāstu, tathāca īśvarasyāpi tatra kartṛtvaṃ ghaṭata iti vācyam| upapadavibhaktitaḥ kārakavibhakteḥ padāntarayoganirapekṣatvena balīyastvāt| na ca karaṇārthatayā kārakavibhaktitvanirvāhaḥ| īśvarakartṛke vyākaraṇe jīvasya sādhakatamatvābhāvāt| kiṃ ca karaṇārthatve'pi vyākaraṇaṃ jīvakartṛkameva paryavasyati| neśvarakartṛkam| cetana kartāraṃ prati cenāṃtarasya karaṇatayopakārakatvaṃ hi tatprayojyakartṛtayaiva bhavati| asminpakṣe rājñaścārakartṛke saṅkalana iva īśvarasya jīvakartṛke vyākaraṇe kartṛtvamamukhyaṃ vivakṣitamiti netavyam| evaṃ karaṇārthatvakalpane sati tṛtīyānupapattiśaṅkā'pi nirastā bhavati| kartrarthatve hi tacchaṅkā syāt| vyākaraṇakartuḥ tiṅā'bhihitatvāt| na ca jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ praveśamātre paryavasyati| vyākaraṇaṃtvīśvarasyaivetikalpanena kartari tṛtīyānirvāhaḥ| "samānakartṛkayoḥ pūrvakāla"iti ktvāpratyayasya kartraikyārthatvasmaraṇena bhinnakartṛkayostadasambhavāt| tasyāpi kartrarthatvasmaraṇena tadabhihite'pi kartari tṛtīyānupapatteśca| atassaṃkalanasya cāra iva vyākaraṇasya jīva eva mukhyakartā| tathā satyeva "nāmarūpaṃ ca bhūtānā"miti smṛtirapi saṃgacchate| na hi hiraṇyagarbhajīvāṃtaryāmī parameśvaro devādināmarūpasṛṣṭaye pūrvasargavṛttatannāmarūpasmṛtyarthaṃvedaśabdānapakṣata iti| evaṃprāptesiddhāṃtaḥ
saṃjñāmūrtiklṛptiḥ - devādīnāṃ nāmarūpavyākaraṇaṃ trivṛtkurvataḥ parameśvarasyaiva sṛṣṭiḥ| "seyaṃ devataikṣatahaṃtāhamimāstisro devatāḥ anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāṃ karavāṇī"ti trivṛtkaraṇanāmarūpavyākaraṇayossamānakartṛkatvaśravaṇāt| trivṛtkaraṇaṃ hi parameśvarasyaiva karma| na tu "tasminnaṃḍe'bhavadbrahmā sarvalokapitāmaha" iti aṃḍāṃtassaṃbhūtatvena smṛtasya hiraṇyagarbhasya| trivṛtkṛtaistejovannairaṃḍasyotpādanīyatvāt| tasmāt trivṛtkaraṇakarteśvaraḥ nāmarūpavyākaraṇasyāpi kartā| hiraṇyagarbhasyāpi tatkartṛtvasmṛtiḥ ghaṭāderiva tasya dvikartṛkatvādupapadyate| kathamidānīṃ "jīvenātmane"ti sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ| jīvaśabdasya jīvaśarīrakaparamātmaparatvādityavehi| kathamākhyātābhihite kartari tatkartṛkākhyātopāttakriyāpekṣayā ktvāpratyayaprakṛtyupāttakriyāyāḥ pūrvakālatvaṃ tayoḥ kriyayoḥ karturaikyaṃ cetyetāvanmātrameva hi ktvārthaḥ| lāghavāt| natvanyalabhyā kartṛśaktirapi| tathā'pi pradhānakriyānvayasyābhyarhitatvāt vyākaraṇānvaya eva jīvaśarīrakasya paramātmano vaktavyassyāditi cet - satyaṃ| pradhānakriyānvayāsaṃbhave upasarjanakriyānvayopi yukta eva| yathā "iṣṭakāḥ kṛtvā bhuṅkta" ityatra tatra iṣṭakānāṃ bhojanakriyākarmatvena anvayānarhatvādasaṃbhavaḥ| iha tṛtīyāṃtanirdiṣṭasya kartuḥ kartrarthakākhyātānvayānarhatvādityeva viśeṣaḥ| yadi ktvāpratyayasya kartraikyapūrvakālatvamātramarthamaṃgīkṛtya kartṛśaktyabhidhāyakatvaṃ pratyākhyāyeta tadeha jīvenetivat "iṣṭakāḥ kṛtvā'haṃbhokṣya" ityatrāpi mayeti
anabhihitādhikāravihitayā tṛtīyayaiva bhāvyaṃ syāt| atastasya kartṛśaktyabhidhāyakatvamaṃgīkṛtyaiva tatra prathamā samarthanīyā| tataśca atrāpi ktvāpratyayena karturabhidhānāt jīveneti tṛtīyā na syāt prathamayaiva bhāvyaṃ syāditi kartṛśaktyanabhidhānamāśritya tṛtīyāsamarthanāyāmanupapattiḥ, tadā chāṃdasatveneyaṃ vyākaraṇakartari jīvaśarīrake paramātmani tṛtīyopapādanīyā| na tu jīvasyeśvarābhinnatvaṃ vyākaraṇasya jīvakartṛkatvaṃcāṃgīkṛtya tṛtīyeyamupapādanīyā| jīveśvarayorbhedasya samarthitatvāt| atastrivṛtkaraṇasamānakartṛkaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ trivṛtkaraṇakarturīśvarasyaiva kṛtiriti siddhaṃ| yadyapi hiraṇyagarbhasṛṣṭyanaṃtaranāmarūpavyākaraṇānantaraṃ
trivṝtkaraṇaṃ śrutau paṭhitaṃ, tathāpi pāṭhakramādarthakramasya balīyastvāt trivṛtkaraṇaṃ prāgevetyasyārthasya prapaṃcanaṃ bhāṣye draṣṭavyaṃ||
saṃgrahakārike||
sarasijasaṃbhava eva vyākaraṇanāmarūpayoḥ kartā|
jīveneti śravaṇāt tatsṛjyaprakaraṇe ca tatsmaraṇāt||
maivaṃ yastrivṛtaṃ cakāra kathitaḥ kartā sa hi vyākṛtau
taddhātā na tathā trivṛtkṛtadharādyutpāditāṇḍodbhavaḥ|
tena vyākaraṇatrivṛtkaraṇayoḥ kartā sa eveśvaro
jīveneti tu taccharīrakatayā tatraiva śaktaṃ padam||
iti saṃjñāmūrtyadhikaraṇam||
iti śrīmadbharadvājakulajaladhikaustubhaśrīmadadvaitavidyācāryaśrīviśvajidyājiśrīraṅgarājādhvarivarasūnorappayadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturmatasāre
nayamayūkhamālikākhye dvitīyaparicchede dvitīyasyādhyāyasya
ca tu rthaḥ pā daḥ||
||dvitīyo'dhyāyassamāptaḥ||
śrīmate hayavadanaparabrahmaṇe namaḥ||
||śrīḥ||
||śrīmate hayavadaparabrahmaṇe namaḥ||
||caturtho'dhyāyaḥ||
||caturthasyādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ||
sā dhananurūpaṇānantaraṃ phalamasminnadhyāye nirūpyate| dehe sthitasya viduṣaḥ pāpāśleṣavināśo mūrdhanyanāḍyā dehānnirgamorgatasyārcirādigatirbrahma prāptiśceti caturvidhaṃ phalam| tadidaṃ caturvidhaṃ phalaṃ kramātpādacatuṣṭayena vicāryate| ādau tāvādvidyāyāṃ vicāraṇīyāḥ kecana viśeṣāḥ vicāryente| sādhanabhūtāyā vidyāyā bhaktidaśāpannāyāḥ phalarūpatvamastīti darśayituṃ tṛtīyavicāraṇīyayānāmapūha vicāraḥ - 
āvṛttirasakṛdupadeśāt||1||
liṅgācca||2||
" brahmavidāpnoti para " mityādau brahmaprāptisādhanatvena śrutā vidyā kiṃ sakṛdevānuṣṭheyā utāsakṛdāvartanīyeti saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| sakṛdanuṣṭheyā| āvṛttau pramāṇābhāvāt| avaghātādivat dṛṣṭopāyatvābhāvāt| yāgādikarmavadvedanamapi hi " phalamataupapatteḥ " rityatra pratipāditam| nanu " manobrahmetyupāsīte " tyupāsinā'nukramya " yaevavede " tyupasaṃhārāt saṃvargāvidyāyāṃ " yastadvede " ti vidinopakarmya " yāṃ devatāmupāssa " ityupālinopasaṃhārāccaja vedānteṣu vididhātvarthavidhānyasyāvṛttigarbhopāsanāparyavasānāvagamādāvṛttiḥ sidhyeticet, astu tricatura vṛttyā'pyāvṛttiśāstrārthasiddhirna tanmātreṇāsakṛdāvṛttissiddhāntyabhimatā siddhyīti| rāddhāntastu| " draṣṭavyaśśrotavyomantavyo nididhyāsitavya " iti " tattastu taṃ paśyati niṣkalaṃ dhyāyamāna " iti ca viśadatarāvabhāsatayā vidyāyā darśanasamānākārapattyapekṣāpratipādanāt tādarthyena " tadrūpapratyayaikātmā santatiścānyanispṛhā| taddhyānaṃ prathamaiṣṣaḍbhiraṅgairniṣapadyate tade " ti smṛtyanusāremacāsakṛdāvartanīyā vidyeti|
saṃgrahakārike||
mumukṣubhissakṛtkarāya vihitaṃ brhamavedanam|
jyotiṣṭomāditulyaṃ hi dṛṣṭārthaṃ nāvahantivat||
āvartanīyamasakṛttadupāstitayā mitam|
upakramolapasaṃhāraśākhāntaragataiḥ padaiḥ||
iti āvṛttyadhikaraṇam||1||
ātmetitupagacchanti grāhayanti ca||3||
vidyāyāṃ viśadatarāvabhāsarūpaphalārthāpattiprasūtamāvṛttiguṇakatvaṃ nirūpitam| tadanantaraṃ muktāvapi bhagavatatpārataṃtryarūpaphalārthāpattiprasūtaṃ bhagavataḥ svātmatvenanānusandheyatvaṃ nurūpapyata iti saṅgatiḥ| vidyāsvīśvaraḥ svātmatvena nānusandheyāḥ| kiṃ tu pṛthaktvenanaiva| " pṛthagātmānāṃ preritārañca matvājuṣṭastatastenāmṛtatvematī " ti śruteḥ| atnaryāmibrāhmaṇoktaṃ bhagavataḥ svātmatvamasticet astu tanna niṣidhyate| muktyarthamanusandhānaṃ pṛthaktvenaiva kartavyamūdāhṛtaśruteriti| rāddhāntastu - tatkratunyāyena muktāvapi bhagavatpārataṃtryasiddhyartha svātmatvenānusandhānaṃ kāryam| tathaiva pūrve'pyupāsitāro'nusandedhate " tvaṃvā ahamasi bhagavo devate'haṃ vai tvamasī " ti| tathaiva ca śāstrāṇyantaryāmibrhamāṇādīnyupadiśanti| na ca pṛthaktvānusandhānavavirodhaḥ| svaśarīrāt svātmanaiva tato'pi paramātmāno'dhikatvena tathā'nusandhānopapatteḥ||
saṃgrahakārike|
anyatvenopāsiturapāsanaṃ bhagavataḥ kāryam|
natvahamiti bhinnatvāt pṛthagātmāmanamiti ca śravaṇāt||
bhinnamapi tāvadātmā brahmaśarīrasya jīvasya|
tasmānnaro'hamitivat brahmāhamitīdamupapannam||
iti ātmatvopāsanādhikaraṇam||2|
na pratīke na hi saḥ||4||
brahmadṛṣṭirutkarṣāt||5||
ahaṃ graheṣvīśvarasyātmatvenopāsanamuktam| pratīkopāsaneṣpapi tacchaṅkayā saṅgatiḥ|
" manobrahme " tyādiṣu brahmaṇa ātmatvenānusandhānaṃ kāryaṃ naveti tasyasvataṃtropāsyatvadṛṣṭiviśeṣaṇatvābhyāṃ saṃśayaḥ| yadi brahmaṇi manodṛṣṭirvidyeyā tadā brahmaṇassvatantropāsyatvāt pūrvādhikaraṇanyāyena tasyātmatvenānusandhānaṃ kāryam| yadi manasi brahmadṛṣṭirvidyeyā tadopāsyasyābrahmatvāt na tathopāsanaṃ kāryamiti phalaphalibhāvaḥ| tatra brahmopāsyaṃ| manodṛṣṭi viśaṣaṇam| acetanasya manasaḥ phalapradānāśaktasyopāsyatvāyogāditi pūrvapakṣaḥ| manasi brahmadṛṣṭirvidheyā brahmaṇa utkṛṣṭatvāt| apakṛṣṭehyutkṛṣṭabuddhirabhyudayāya bhavati| yathā rājabhṛtye rājabuddhiḥ| utkṛṣṭetvapakṛṣṭabuddhiranathāyaiva syāt| tasmānmanasa upāsyatvannātmatvenānusaṃdhānaṃ kāryam|manaso'pyabhimānidevatārūpeṇa cetanatvāditi||
saṅgrahakārike|
evamātmatvadhīḥ kāryā pratīkopāsaneṣvapi|
maivaṃ sa hi bhavakyātmā na khalupāsakaṃ prati||
sa hi brahmadṛśyopāsyo natvatra vipīratatā|
utkṛṣṭabrahmadṛṣṭirhi niṣkṛṣṭe phaladīyinī||
iti pratīkādhikaṇam||3||
ādityādimatayaśvāṃga upapatteḥ||6||
utkṛṣṭadṛṣṭirnikṛṣṭe kartavyetyuktam|tadodgīthādidṛṣṭirādityādau kartavyeti śaṅkayā saṅgatiḥ| " ya evāsau tapati tamudgīthamupāsāñcakrira " ityādiṣu kimādityādāvudgīthādidṛṣṭirvidheyā utodgīthādāvādityādidṛṣṭirityanyatarotkarṣānavadhāraṇāt saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| ādityādāvudgīthādidṛṣṭirvidheyā phalavatkarmāṅgatayā phalavattvāt| ādityāderdevatārūpeṇa karmāṅgatve satyapi svatassādhyasyodgīthādeḥ phalasannikarṣāt manobrahmetyatrevādityādeḥ prathamanirdiṣṭasyopāsyatvapratīteśceti||
siddhāntastu - 
udgīthādiṣvādityādidṛṣṭirvidheyā| karmaṇāmādityādidevatārādhanadvāreṇaiva phalasādhanatayā''dityādināmutkṛṣṭatvopapatteḥ| ārādhanānāmārādhyadevatāpradhānatayā sādhyānurāgeṇa tasyāpi sādhyatopapattestatprayuktotkarṣasyāpyanādaraṇīyatvāt| yadyādityādeḥ "aryamṇe caruṃ nirvapetsvargakāma " ityādi vidhyantaravihiteṣu karmasu phalapradatve'pyasminnupāsanāprakaraṇe devatātvābhāvena phalapradatvābhāvāt tata udgīthāderevātra phalavatkarmaugatayā phalavattvādutkṛṣṭatvamucyate tadā'pyudgīthādāvevādityādṛṣṭirvidheyā aṃgānaṃgasamabhivyāhāravatīnāṃ vidyānāmaṃgeṣvagadṛṣṭirūpatve pradhānakarmaprayuktāṃghāśrayatvāt| svatantraphalānāmapi tāsāṃ pṛthakprayuktirnakalpyeti prayuktilāghavādvaiparītye prayuktantarakalpanāgauravāt||
saṅgrahakārike||
yadvṛttaprādhamyaṃ yasmādādityamukhyeṣu|
phalasādhanakarmatvādudgīthādica tadutkṛṣṭam||
tasmādudgīthādidṛgādityādiṣu samādheyā|
maivamiha devataiva hi karmārādhyā tadutkṛṣṭā||
iti ādityādimattyādhikaraṇam||4||
āsīnassambhavāt||7||
dhyānācca||8||
acalatvañcāpekṣya||9||
smarantica||10||
yatraikāgratā tatrāviśeṣāt||11||
avasitamahaṃgraha prasaktānuprasaktaṃ|paravidyāviṣaya eva vicāritau'nuvartate|paravidyānāṃ pradhānaphalārthāpattimūlāvāvṛttyahaṃgrahau vicāritau| idānīmaṃgarūpapradānopakārāthāpattimalaugaviśeṣaścityate| paravidyāsvāsananiyamo'sti naveti saṃśaye nāsti niyāmakam| nacāsīnasyaivopāsanopayogyaikāgnyaṃ sambhavatīti vācyam| ṛtvikkartṛkāṃgāvabaddhopāsaneṣviva tiṣṭhato'pi tatsambhavāt| ṛtvijāṃ karmavidhyanusāreṇa tiṣṭhatāmapi tattaṃdaṃgāśrayopāsanādarśanāditi|
siddhāntastu - viśadatarāvabhāsarūpadarśanasamānākāratāpattayupayoginīnāṃ parabrahmāpāsanānāṃ nirantarasmṛtisantānarūpadhruvānusmṛtyātmatayā kratvaṃgāśrayopāsanāvaiṣamyāt acalatvasāmyamapekṣya " dhyāyatīva pṛthadhivī dhyāyīvāntarikṣa " mityādinirdeśanāt "upaviśyāsane yujyādyogamātmaviśuddhaye" iti smṛtaścāsīnaevopāsīta| śayānasya nidrāsambhavāt gacchatastiṣṭhatca dehadhāraṇaprayatnāpekṣaṇāt| āsīnasyāpi paścārdhadhāraṇaprayatno'stīti tannivṛttyapekṣāyāṃ sāpāśraya āsīta deśakālayoḥ paraṃ na niyamaḥ| ekāgratātiriktadeśakālaviśeṣāśravaṇāt| ekāgratānukūlo yo deśaḥkālaśca sa evopāsanasya| na tu tatra viśeṣo'sti| " same śucau śarkarāvahnivālukāvivarjite śītalāśrayādibhiḥ|mano'nukūle na tuḥ cakṣuḥ pūḍane guhānivātāśrayaṇe prayojaye " diti vacanamapyekāgratopayoginaṃ deśamāha na tu deśaviśeṣaṃ niyacchati|mano'nukūla iti vākyaśeṣāt|
saṃgrahakārike|
āsīna eva yogaṃ kuryādekāgratotpatteḥ|
sthitigatyoratiyatnācchayane nidrābhibhūteśca||
mano'nukūlatāmātraṃ deśakāleṣvapekṣyate|
na tārāraṇyatithyādi vākyaśeṣe tathā śruteḥ|
iti āsīnādhikaraṇam||5||
āprayāṇāttatrāpi hi dṛṣṭam||12||
pūrvāhṇādikālaniyamavadeva tāvatkālaparyantamupāsanākāryeti kāleyattānimasyāpyaśravaṇāt " sukṛtkṛte kṛtaśśāstrārtha " iti nyāyādekasminneva dine kāryā| na tu yāvadāyūṣam|" sa khalvevaṃ vartayanyāvadāyuṣaṃ brahmalokamabhisampadyate na ca pūnarāvartata " iti śrutiḥ karmaṇāmeva yāvadāyuṣamanuṣṭheyatvamāha| na tu vidyāyā pūrvapakṣaḥ| yāvadāyuṣaṃ karmānuṣṭhā karmānuṣṭhānasyāpunarāvṛttiphalakatvamucimānaṃ vidyāṃ prati svarūpopakāratvenaivopapādanīyamiti taduktisāmaryādvidyāyā api yāvadāyuṣamanuṣṭheyatvaṃ tatra
vivakṣitamunnīyata iti tadbalāt "prāyaṇāntarmoṃkāramabhidhyāyīte " ti śrutyantaradarśanācca yāvadāyuṣamanuṣṭheyeti siddhāntaḥ||
saṃgrahakārike|
upāsanamidaṃ kāryamakasminneva vāsare|
tāvatā'pi kṛtārthaṃ hi syādupāsanaśāsanam||
kāryamanvahamupakramaprabhṛyāśarīravilayādupāsanam|
yāvadāyuṣamiti śrutau tathā prāyaṇāntamiti ca śrutau vidheḥ||
iti āprayāṇādhikaraṇam||6||
tadadhigamaphattarapūrvāghayoraśleṣavināśo tadvyapadeśāt||13||
evaṃ vidyāyāmāvṛttyādiviśeṣā vicāritāḥ| athaitatpādārthabhūtaṃ pāpāśleṣavināśarūpaṃ phalaṃ vicāryata iti saṅgatiḥ| " tadyathā pūṣkarapalāśaāpo na śliṣyante evamevaṃvidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyata " iti " tadyatheṣīkātūlamagnau protaṃ pradūyetaivaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyanta " iti ca śrūyamāṇau pāpāśleṣavināśau brahmavidyāphale bhaviturmahato naveti abhuktakarmakṣayaśāstravirodhābhyāṃ saṃśaye pūrvapakṣaḥ| brahmavidyāphale tau| " nābhuktaṃ kṣīyate karma kalpakoṭiśatairapi " iti śāstreṇa karmaṇāṃ bhogaikanāśyatvāvagasyā tayorvidyāphalatvakalpanāyogāt| na ca tatsāmānyaśāstraṃ vidyāvināśa pāpātiriktakarmaviṣayaṃ syāditi vācyam| vidyāyāḥ pāpāśleṣavināṣārthatvena vidheraśrūtatayā tatsaṃkocakalpakābhāvāt | na ca tathā vidhikalpanaṃ brahmaprāptisādhanatvena vihitāyā vidyāyāḥ phalāntarānapekṣatvat pāpāśleṣavināśakṣavaṇasya vidyāpraśasārthatvopapatteriti||
siddhāntastu - vidyālābhe sati uttarapūrvāghayoraśleṣavināśau bhavataevā " tadyathā puṣkarapalāśa " ityādiśrutiṣu tathā vyapadeśāt| na ca brahmaprāptiphalakavidyārthavādastathā vyapadeśaḥ abhuktakarmakṣayaśāstrānuroghāditi vācyam| bhogaṃ vinā karmakṣayābhāve bhogārthe śarīraṃ grāhyaṃ tasminnapi śarīre karmāntarārjanamavarjanīyamtyanirmokṣāpattyā mokṣaśāstraireva pāpāśleṣavināśayorapekṣitatvāt| pāpāśleṣavināśābhuktakarmakṣayaśāstrayorbhinnaviṣayatve virodhābhāvācca| tathāhi| abhuktarmākṣayaśāstraṃ karmaṇāṃ phalajananaśakiṃta pratipādayati| pāpavināśāśleṣaśāstre tu prācīnakarmaṇāṃ phalajananasāmrthyavināśaktimāgāmikarmaṇāṃ phavajananasāmarthyotpattipratibandhaśakiṃta ca pratipādayataḥ|na ca tatra virodhaśaṅkāsti| agnijalayorauṣṇyatannivāraṇaśkitavadupapatteḥ| aśleṣavacanaṃ prāmādikapāpamātraviṣayam| "nāvirato duścaritā" dityādiśāstrairāprāyaṇamaharaharanuṣṭheyāyā vidyāyā duścaritaviratiniṣpādyatvāvagamāt| " apicetpātakaṃ kiṃcidyogi kuryātpramādataḥ| yogameva niṣeveta nānyatkarma samācare"diti smṛteśca| nanvidaṃ pāpaśleṣavināśadvayamupasanārambhaprathamadivase uta madhye athavā tadavasāna iti vivektavyam|madhye darśanasamānākārāpattisamaya iti vaviktameva "kṣīyantecāsya karmāṇi tasmindṛṣṭe " iti śrutyā| nanu pūrvai śarīrapātasamaye karmakṣaya uktaḥatra kathaṃ samayāntaroktiḥ| ucyate| bhagavadapritiḥ pāpaṃ| tatprītiviśeṣarūpāvaśleṣavināśau| tataśca darśanasamākārāpattisamaye prītiḥ paramātmā tatsamāptāvupāsakasyāghaṃ kṣamiṣya iti saṅkalpayatīti tadviṣayamidamadhikaraṇam| samāptau kṣāntāmiti saṅkalpayatīti tadviṣayaṃ pūrvādhikaraṇamiti na virodhaḥ||
saṃgrahakārike|
vidyodayānantarapūrvapāpayoraśleṣavidhvaṃsanavarṇanaṃ stutiḥ|
nābhuktikarmākṣayaśāstrabādhitaṃ vidyāphalatvapratipādanakṣamam||
vibhinnaśaktyoranayorvirudadhatānaśāstrayorasti mudhaikabādhanam|
amodhaśakterapi kiṃ na sambhavedvinājanmapratibandhakāraṇam||
iti tadadhigamādhikaraṇam||7||
itarasyāpyevamasaṃśleṣaḥ pātetu||14||
pāpātiriktakarmaviṣayaṃ syāditi vācyam| vidyāyāḥ pāpāśleṣavināśārthatvena vidheraśrutatayā
tatsaṃkocakalpakābhāvāt| na ca tathā vidhaikalpanaṃ brahmaprāptisādhanatvena vihitayā vidyāyāḥ phalāntarānapekṣatvāt pāpāśleṣavināśaśravaṇasya vidyāpraśaśārthatvopapattiriti||
siddhāntastu - vidyālābhe sati uttarapūrvāghayoraśleṣavināśau bhavataeva| " tadyathā puṣkarapalāśa " ityādiśrutiṣu tathā vyapadeśāt| na ca brahmaprāptiphalakavidyārthavādajastathā vyapadeśaḥ abhuktakarmakṣayaśāstrānurodhāditi vācyam| bhogaṃ vinā karmakṣayābhāve bhogārthaṃ śarīraṃ grāhyaṃ tasminnapi śarīre karmāntarārjanamavarjanīyamityanirmokṣāpattyā mokṣaśāstraireva pāpāśleṣavināśayorapekṣitatvāt| pāpāśleṣavināśābhuktakarmakṣayaśāstrayorbhinnaviṣayatve virodhābhāvācca| tathā hi| abhuktakarmākṣayaśāstraṃ karmaṇāṃ phalajananaśakiṃta pratipādayati| pāpāvināśāśleṣaśāstre tu prācīnakarmaṇāṃ phalajananasāmarthyavināśaśaktimāgāmikarmaṇāṃ phalajananasāmarthyotpattipratibandhaśakiṃta ca pratipādayataḥ| na ca tatra virodhaśaṅkā'sti| agnijalayorauṣṇyatannivāraṇāśaktivadupapatteḥ| aśleṣavacanaṃ prāmadikapāpāmātraviṣayaṃ| na tu
vināśavacanavat sarvapāpāviṣayam| "nāvirato duścaritā " dityādiśāśtrairāprāyaṇamaharaharanuṣṭheyāyā vidyāyā duścaritaviratiniṣpādyatvāvagamāt| " apicetpādakaṃ kicidyogi kuryātpramādataḥ| yogameva ni niṣeveta nānyatkarma samācāre " diti smṛteśca| nanvidaṃ pāpāśleṣavināśadvayamupāsanārambhaprathamadivase uta madhye athavā tadavasāna iti vivektavyam| madhye darśanasamānādhikārāpattisamaya iti viviktameva " kṣīyantecāsya karmāṇi tasmindṛṣṭe parāvara " iti śrutyā| nanu pūrva śarīrapātasamaye karmakṣaya uktaḥ atra kathaṃ samayāntaroktiḥ| ucyate| bhagavanadaprītiḥ pāpam| tatprītiviśṣarūpāvaśleṣavināśau| tataśca darśanasāmānādhikārāpattisamaye prītaḥ paramātmā tatsamāptāvupāsakasyāghaṃ kṣamiṣya iti saṅkalpayatīti tadviṣayamidamadhikaraṇam| samāptau kṣāntamiti saṅkalpayatīti tadviṣyaṃ pūrvādhikaraṇamiti na virodhaḥ||
saṃgrahakārike||
vidyodayānantapūrvapāpayoraśleṣavidhvaṃsanavarṇanaṃ stutiḥ|
nābhuktakarmākṣayaśāstrabādhitaṃ vidyāphalatvapratipādanakṣamam||
vibhinnaśaktyoranayorviruddhatānaśāstrayorasti mudhaikabādhanam|
amoghaśakterapi kiṃ na sambhavedvināśajanmapratibandhakāraṇam||
iti tadadhigadamādhikaraṇam||
itarasyāpyevamasaṃśleṣaḥ pātetu||14||
pāpavatpuṇyasyāpi vināśāśleṣādātideśisī saṃgatiḥ| tatreyamatideśanivartanīyā'dhikā śaṅkā| aśleṣavināśavacanayoḥ pāpagrahaṇāt na puṇyasyāśleṣavināśau| na ca " na jarā na mṛtyarna śoko na sukṛtaṃ na duṣkṛtaṃ sarve pāpmāno'to nivartanta " iti vacavanāntare pāpmaśabdasya puṇyasādhāraṇyādanyatrāpi vedānteṣu sthitaḥ pāpavācī śabdaḥ puṇyasādhāraṇaḥ| mumukṣunpratyaniṣṭaphalatvāviśeṣāditi vācyam| tathā niyamābhāvāt| sarve pāpmāna ityatra pāpmaśabdena gṛhītānāmapi jarā mṛtyuśokānāṃ " apahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoka " ityatra pṛthaggṛhītatvāt| evamihāpi pāpaśabdāsya pāpamātraparatvopapatteriti| evamadhikaśaṃkānivṛttistu - vedānteṣu kvacitkvacitpāpaśabdāsya pāpamātraparatve'pi sa iha sukṛtasādhāraṇa eva " na sasukṛtaṃ na duṣkṛtaṃ sarve pāpmāno'to nivartanta " iti vidyāmāhātmyāt eva sukṛtasyāpi nivṛttikṣanaṇāt tasya mumukṣuniṣṭaphalatvanyāyasāmyena mumukṣuśāstraprāmāṇyenacāśleṣasādhāraṇatvāditi| nanu viduṣo'pyupāsanamirvṛttaye vṛṣṭyannādyādiphalāni puṇyānyapekṣitāni| kathaṃ sarveṣāṃ puṇyānāṃ vināśaḥ| teṣāṃ vidyāniguṇadṛṣṭaphalānāṃ yāvaccharīradhāraṇamanuvṛttānāṃ śarīrapātasamaye'vaśiṣṭāṃśavināśa iti na virodhaḥ||
saṃgrahakārike||
sukṛtaṃ vihitaṃ hi vidyāyā na tadaśleṣalayau prapadyate|
ata eva tayā svaśabdato duratasyaiva viśiṣya tau śrutau||
vihitamapi tadvidyābhājāmaniṣṭaphalatvato
durutasadṛśaṃ dūre tyājyaṃ parānubhavārthinām |
tadubhayaparaśrutyantasthaṃ ca pāpmapadādikaṃ
tadiha niyataṃ tasyāpyaśleṣaṇaṃ salayaṃ tayā||
vṛṣṭyannādyādiphalaṃ yatpuṇyamapekṣitaṃ viduṣām|
yatra tadavaśiṣṭaṃ syāccharīrapāte layastasya
iti itarādhikaraṇam||
anārabdhakārye eva tu pūrve tadavadheḥ||15||
uktassukṛtaduṣkṛtayorvidyayā nāśaḥ kimaviśeṣaṇa utānārabdhakāryayoriti " sarve pāpmānaḥ praduṣyante tasya tāvadeva cira " miti śrutidvayavirodhāt saṃśaye sarvaśrutyavirodhādaśeṣayoḥ puṇyapāpayorvināśaḥ " tasya tāvadeva cira " miti śrutyuktaṃ tadanantaradehadhāraṇaṃ tu kulānacakrabhramaṇanyāyena saṃskārānuvṛttyā bhaviṣyatīti pūrvapakṣaḥ| saṃskārānuvṛttyācet
kṣīṇāyuṣāmaṣyavaniduṣāmapi tayā dehadhāraṇaṃ prasajyate| atto dehadhāraṇāparthe prārabdhe sukṛtaduṣkṛte vihāyānyeṣāṃ puṇyapāpānāṃ nidyayā vināśaḥ| evaṃ ca prārabdhavaśādeva viduṣāmapi sukhaduḥkhabhogopapattiḥ||
saṃgrahakārike||
aviśeṣaṇa vidyātassyāllayaḥ puṇyapāpayoḥ|
sarve pāpmāna iti hi śrutirāśakṣyate tathāḥ||
ārabdhakāryamapahāya tayorlayasya yattādeva ciramityavadhiḥ śruto'sti|
saṃskārato yadi śarīrabhṛtissamarthyā kṣīṇāyuṣāmaviduṣāmapi sā tadā syāt||
iti anārabdhakāryādhikaraṇam||
agnihotrāditu tatkāryāyaiva taddarśanāt||16||
ayo'nyapihyekeṣāmubhayoḥ||17||
yadeva vidyayeti hi||18||
itarasyāpyelamasaṃśleṣa ityadhikaraṇe vidyābalāt sukṛtasyāpyasaṃśseṣa uktaḥ| ato'gnihotrādīnāṃ nityanaimittikānāṃ svāśramadharmāṇāmapi sukṛtatvāviśeṣeṇa phalāśleṣādanicchato'nanuṣṭhānaṃ prāptam| na hi niṣphalaṃ kaściccirṣati| yadi nityanemittikānāṃ phalavattva syāt, tadānīmaśleṣavacanameva nirviṣayaṃ syāt, tadanīmaśleṣavacanameva nirviṣayaṃ syāt| tasya viṣayāntarakalpane'pi teṣāṃ phalamapi na paśāmaḥ| nacāprāyaṇamaharaharavicchedena vidyānuvṛttistatphalam| kamarṇa iva vidyāyā apyanutiṣṭhāsāmātreṇāvicchinnānuvṛttisiddheḥ| na ca vidyānuvṛttipratibandhakaduritanivṛtatistatphalam| vidyāprācīnaduritānāṃ vidyayā vināśāt viduṣastadanantaraṃ buddhipūrvakapāpacaraṇāprasakteḥ prāmādikasya yoganiṣevaṇena nivṛttyekteḥ| yadyapi nityanaimittikakānāmāśramadharmāṇāṃ parityāgo na yujyata iti pratyavāyaparihārārthamāśramadharmatvena kathañcidanuṣṭhānaṃ samarthyeta, tathā'pi vidyārthatvane teṣāmanuṣṭhānaṃ na sidhyatīti||
siddhāntastu - viduṣāpyāśramadharmatvena te vidyārthtavenāphyanuṣṭheyāḥ vidyārūpakāryāyaiva
tadupadeśāt| nanvasiddhastadupadeśaḥ, " yajñene " tyādiśrutervidyādayātprāk tādarthyena vidhānārthatopapatteḥ, " sa khalvedaṃ vartaya " nnityādiśruteparāśramadharmānuvṛttimātraviṣayatvopapatteriti cenna| udayahṛtaśrutidvayasyāpyantimapratyayaparyantavidyayarthakarmānuvṛtataviṣayatvasyāpi sambhave tathā saṅkocakalpanāyogāt| na ca naiṣphalam| yāvadvidyānuvṛttyanuṣṭheyatvena sahitasya vidyāsahakārikarmajātasya madhye buddhipūrvaṃtyāge satyayathāśāstrānuṣṭhānataḥ pratyavāyena cittadoṣe hi sati kadācidvidyānuvartāyiṣā vā vicchidyeta| antimapratyayavanirodhiniṣiddhānuṣṭhānarucervotpadyate| ato darśanasamānākāravidyodayānantaramapi yāvajjīvaṃ vdayārthakarmāṇyanuṣṭheyānīti| yadyagnihotrādisādhukṛtyā
vidyotpattyārthā vidyotpatteḥ prācīnaṃ ca vidyodayakāle sthitaṃ prārabdhaphalavyatiriktaṃ sarvaṃ sukṛtaṃ vidyayā vinaṣṭaṃ prārabdhaphalaṃ ca sukṛtaṃ bhogena nāśyaṃ, tarhi " suhṛdassādhukṛtyā " mityasya koviṣaya iti cet| ucyate - vidyotpattyarthāyāḥ agnihotrādisādhukṛtyāyāḥ vidyotpatteḥ prācīnāyāśca śādhikṛtyāyāḥ anyā api viduṣā kṛtā dehadhāraṇārthā vṛṣṭyannādyādiphalassādhukṛtyāstatatra yā prabalakarmāntarapratibandhāt prāgabhuktaphalā'sti sā tasyā viṣayaḥ| vidyārahitakarmaṇāṃ prabalakarmāntarapritabandhasambhavasya " yadeva vidyayā karotī " ti śrutyā'vagatatvāditi| atredaṃ vicāryate| - " itarasyāpyevamasamasaśleṣaḥ pāte " tviti sūtre vṛṣṭyānnādyādiphalāyāḥ sādhukṛtyāyāḥ bhuktaphalāvaśiṣṭāyāśarīrapātasamaye vināśa uktaḥ kathaṃ tasyāssuhṛtadgāmitvanucyate| atha viduṣi phalājanakatvemeva tatra tannāśo vivakṣitaḥ, tarhi vidyodayakāle puṇyāntaranāśo'pi tathaiva vaktuṃ śakya iti vidūṣāssarvasyā api sādhukṛtyāyāssuhṛdgāmitve vaktuṃ śakye kimiti " suhṛdassādhikṛtyā " mityasya vṛṣṭyannādyādiphalasādhaikṛtyāmātraviṣyatvena saṅkocaḥ kriyate| api ca " dviṣantaḥ pāpakṛtyā "; mityasya kaṃ viṣayaṃ dṛṣṭvā "suhṛdassādhu " mityasyaiva viṣayaviśeṣanirdhāraṇaṃ kriyate| vidyotpattyanantara bhāvanī pāpakṛtyā tasya viṣayaḥ syāditi cet na| tasyāmapyaśleṣavacanasattvāsāmyāt| yadi tatrāśleṣavacanaṃ viduṣi phalājanakatvamātrārthakamityadhyavasāyaḥ, tarhi viduṣi sādhukṛtyāyā aśaaleṣavacanamapi tathaivetyadhyavasituṃ śakyatvāt viśiṣṭasādhukṛtyāyāḥ gatyuktirnāpokṣitā| gatirapi prabalakarmātaparapratibaddhaphalaiva sādhukṛtyeti na saṅkocanīyam|
tadapratibaddhaphalāyā api sādhukṛtyāyāssambhavāt| yasya vidyāpratibandhakaduritānyalpāni tadbhūyastvaśaṅkāyā tannivattyarthamanuṣṭhitāni karmāṇi bhūyāṃsi tatra prārabdhapratibandhakavighnādhikasaṃkhyāni maṅgalānīca kārīrivadadattaphalānyavaśiṣyante upāsakasya vītarāgatve'pi bhramāttadabhyudayecchayā putrādibhiranuṣṭhaitāni taṭākarāmapratiṣṭhādīnyapi hi sambhāvyante| api ca tadadhigamādhikaraṇe vināśāśleṣaśāstrayorabhuktakarmākṣayaśāstreṇa virodhasamādhānārthe tayostasya ca bhinnaviṣayatvasamarthanāvasare vināśaśāstraṃ viduṣai prācīnakarmaṇāṃ phalajananasāmarthyavināśaśakiṃta aśleṣaśāstraṃ viduṣi phalajananasāmarthyotpattipritabandhaśakiṃta ca pratipādayatītyuktam| tatra svarūpavināśaḥ svarūpāśleṣaścoktaḥ| tata evāvagate'rthe punaḥ sādhukṛtyāyāḥ viṣayapraśnasya ko'vakāśaḥ| tasmātsarvamidamanupapannamiti| atredaṃ - prācīnasādhukṛtyāyā api kaścinmanyeta vināṣṭāyāstasyāssuhṛtsaṃkramaṇaśāstraviṣayatvaṃ nāstīti tadāśayamanurudhya suhṛstaṃkramaṇavākye viṣayapraśnaḥ tadāśayamanurudhyaiva sādhaikṛtyāntarāṇāṃ vanāse'pyantato vidvaddehadhāraṇārthatvenānuvṛttā vṛṣṭyādiphalāsādhukṛtyāstatīti tadviṣayatvoktiḥ| evameva sukṛtāntarasadbhāve'pi tadviṣayatāniktiḥ| evaṃ sati " dviṣantaḥ pāpakṛtyā " mityasyāpi yadyapi gatipraśnataduttarābhyāṃ bhāvyaṃ tulyanyāyatvāt, tathā'pi tasya viduṣā pramādena kṛtaṃ pramādenānavakāśena vā yadakṛtayoganiṣavaṇarūpaprāyaścitaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ tadviṣayo bhaviṣyatīti nyāyasāmyenonnetuṃ śakyatvāttdanuktiḥ| tadadhigamādhikaraṇoktasuhṛdviśatsaṃkramaṇaśāstraviṣayasadbhāve'pi yathāśrutavināśaśāstrārthaśraddhāsutaṭasthaśaṅkānurodhādevaṃ praśnattare iti sarvamavadātam||
saṃgrahakārike||
na śliṣyati cetsukṛṃ na kāryamevāgnihotrādi|
viduṣāmevaṃ vidyāveśadyārthe tadaharahaḥ kāryam||
yattu viduṣā kṛtaṃ syāddhṛṣṭyārogyādisiddhaye karma|
prabalapratibaddhaphalaṃ tasyaivasuhṛdgateruktiḥ||
iti agnihotrādyadhikaraṇam||
bhoganatvitare kṣapayitvā sampadyate||19||
ārabdhakāryayoḥ puṇyapāpayoḥ bhoganaiva vināśaḥ, na tu vidyayetyuktam| te kiṃ vidyāyoniśarīrāvasāna eveti niyamaḥ, uta śarīrāntarāvasāne'pītyaniyama iti saṃśaye vidyāyoniśarīrāvasāna eva " dhūtvā śarīramakṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahmalokamabhisambhavānī " ti " tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvannavimokṣya " iti ca śarīramātrāvādhiśravaṇāditi pūravapakṣaḥ|
prārabdhasyaikadehamātrabhogyatve tatheva, anekadehabhāgyatve tu tadavasāne puṇyapāpe iti tadanantarabrahmasampattiḥ| " dhūtvā śarīra " mityekavacanaṃ na vivakṣitam| " vidhūya pāpa " miti tatraiva darśanāt| " tasya tāvadeve " ti śrutau " yāvannavimokṣya " iti prārabdhakarmamokṣa evoktaḥ na tu vidyāyoniśarīramokṣaḥ| tadevaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ prāganuṣṭhitamabhuktaphalamanārabdhaphalañca puṇyapāparūpaṃ sarvaṃ karma vidyāmāhātmyādviduṣisvasvaphalājanakaṃ bhavati| vidyārambhottarakālamanuṣṭhitaṃ cca vidyārthakarmavyatiriktaṃ sarvaṃ tatra phalajananāsamarthaṃ bhavati tadidaṃ viduṣaṛ puṇyapāpaṃ sarvaṃ karma viduṣaḥ suhṛddviṣatossaṃkrāmati| prārabdhaṃtvekenānekena vā dehena bhuktaṃ kṣīyate| tadanantaraṃ brahmasampattariti sarvaṃ niravadyam||
saṃgrahakārike||
yasmindehe vidyājani stadavasāna eva muktissyāt|
dhūtvā śarīramiti yadyāvannavimokṣya iti ca yadadhītam||
tasminnanyasminvā yasminniśśeṣatāmeti|
prārabdhaṃ phalabhogaistaddehānte bhavenmuktiḥ||
dhūtvā śarīramityekavacanaṃ na vivakṣitam|
prārabdhasya vimokṣaśca vimokṣya iti kīrtitāḥ||
iti itarakṣapaṇādhikaraṇam||
iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulajaladhikaulastubha śrīmadadvaitavidyācārya śrīviśvajidyāji
śrīraṅgarājādhvarivarasunorappayadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturmatasāre nayamayukhamālikākhye dvitīyaparicchede caturthasyādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ||
||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||||
||śrīmate hayavadanaparabrahmaṇe namaḥ||
||caturthāsyādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ||
iha pāde viduṣo mūrdhanyānāḍyotkrāntirūpamarcirādigatyā brahmaprāpti hetutvena vidyāphalabhūtaṃ cintanīyam|
tatra mūrdhanyanāḍīpraveśaparyantotkrāntirvidvaviduṣossādhāraṇīti sā prathaṃ vicāryate| upāsanāsvarcirādigaticintāyāṃ
gatyupakramatvena tasyā api cintanīyatvāt|
vāṅmanasi darśanācchabdācca||1||
ataeva sarvāṇyanu||2||
chāndogye śrūyate - " asya somyepuruṣasya prayato vāṅmanasi sampadyate manaḥprāṇe prāṇastejasi tejaḥparasyāṃ
devatāyā " miti| atra kiṃ vāco manasi sampattiruta vāgavṛttiriti saṃśaya " satā somye tadā sampanno bhavatī "satyatra sampattiśabdasya layārthātvāvagamāt vācaḥ svāprakṛtau manasi svarūpalayāsambhavāt aprakṛtāvapi vṛttilayasya
iti pūrvapakṣaḥ| vāgvyāpāroparamānantaramapi manaḥpravṛttidarśanāt " vāṅmanasi sampadyata " iti śruteśca vācaeva manasi layaḥ| vākchaśabdasya vṛttilakṣaṇāyāṃ kāraṇābhāvāt| layastu vācaṛḥ vyāpāroparamapūrvakamanassaṃyogarūpa aupacāriko grāhyaḥ| vṛttipakṣe'pi vākprakṛtikavṛttinā manasi mukhyalayāsambhavāt vāci sampadyamānāyāmanyānyapi bāhyendriyāṇi manasi sampadyate| " indriyarmanasi sampadyamānai " riti śrutyantarāt||
saṃgrahakārike||
vāgindriyamaprakṛtau na manasi sampadyate mumukṣūṇām|
vāṅmanasīti hi vākyaṃ yojyaṃ vāgvṛttiviṣayatayā||
vāgeva sahānyendriyamasminsampadyate dṛśśabdāt|
sampāttiriha tu na layaḥ kiṃ tu vāyāpārarihatasaṃyogaḥ||
iti vāgadhikaraṇam||
tanmanaḥ prāṇauttarāt||3||
manaḥ prāṇa ityatra tu mukhya eva layaḥ " annamayaṃ hi somya manaḥ āpomayaḥ prāṇa " iti śrutyā mano'nnaśabdoktāyāḥ pṛthivyā vikāraḥ, prāṇastvapāṃ vikāra ityuktatvena manasaḥ svaprakṛtibhāvāpannasya prāṇaprakṛtibhāvapannāsvapsu mukhyalayasambhavāditi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| mano'haṅkāraprakṛtikaṃ, prāṇavāyūrākāśaprakṛtika iti tayornāsti kathamapi prakṛtivikṛtibhāvaḥ| tasmāt " annamayaṃhī " tyādiśrutau manasodanīyena prāṇasyādbhyaścāpyāyanamātraṃ vivakṣitamiti sakalabāhyendriyasaṃyuktaṃ manaḥ svavyāpāroparamapūrvakaṃ prāṇena saṃsṛjyata ityeva tadartho grāhyaḥ| manovṛttyuparamānantaramapi hi praṇavṛttirdṛśyata iti siddhāntaḥ||
saṃgrahakārike||
manastu prāṇe syāllayaeva prakṛtivikṛtitāsattāvat|
annamayaṃ hi manaśśrutamambhovikṛtistathā prāṇāḥ||
maivamahaṃkṛtigaganaprakṛtikayorannatoyābhyām|
āpyāyanameva tayorātastatrāpi saṃyogaḥ||
iti mano'dhikaraṇam||
so'dhyakṣe tadupagamādibhyaḥ||4||
prāṇaḥ kiṃ jīve sampadyate, uta tejasīte viśaye yathāśruti tejasyeva sampadyate. yathā vāṅmanasi manaśca prāṇa iti pūrvapakṣaḥ| vāhyāndriyamanassaṃyuktaḥ prāṇo jīve sampadyate| " evamevemamātmānamantakāle sarve prāṇā abhisamāyantī" ti tadupagamaśravaṇāt " tamutkrāmantaṃ prāṇo'nutkrāmatī " ti saprāṇajīvotkramaṇaśravaṇāt| " kasminnvahamutkrānta utkrānto bhaviṣyāmi kasminvā pratiṣṭhe pratiṣṭhāsyāmīti sa prāṇamasṛjate " ti prāṇasya jīvena
saṃyujya jīvena saha tejasi samptatirastīti " prāṇastejasī " tyasyāpi na virodhaḥ|| yamunāyā gaṅgayā saṃyujya sāgaraprāptāvapi hi yamunā sāgaraṃ prāpnotīti vyavahiyate||
saṃgrahakārike||
prāṇastejasi sākṣātsampadyata iti yathāśrutaṃ yuktam|
maivaṃ tasya tadānīṃ jīvopagamādikaśravaṇāt||
prathamaṃ yadi jīvamitastejasi sampadyate sa tatsahitaḥ|
evamapi śrutirucitā yathaiva sāgaramupaiti yamuneti||
iti adhyakṣādhikaraṇam||
bhūteṣu tacchrateḥ||5||
naikasamindarśayato hi||6||
jīvasyuktasya prāṇasya tejasi śrutā sampattiḥ kiṃ tejomātre, uta sarveṣu bhūteṣviti saṃśaye yathāśruti tejomātra iti pūrvapakṣaḥ| sarveṣu bhūteṣu sampadyate " pṛthivīmaya āpomayastejomaya " iti jīvasya sañcaratassarvabhūtamayatvaśruteḥ| na ca tejaḥ prabhṛtiṣu kramikasañcāreṇa tadupapattiḥ| " tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāmakarot " "nānāvīryāḥ pṛthakbhūtāstataste saṃhartiṃ vinā| nāśaknuvanprajāḥ straḥṣṭumasamāgamya kṛtsnaśaḥ|| sametyānyonyasaṃyogaṃ parasparasamāyāḥ| mahadādyā viśeṣāntahyaṇḍamutpādayanti ta " iti śrutismṛtibhyāmekaikasya kāryākṣamatadvapradarśanāt| atastejaśśabdo bhūtāntarasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ tejo'bhidhatta iti sarveṣu bhūteṣu sampattiriti siddhāntaḥ||
saṃgrahakārike||
sā sampattistejomātre prāṇasya jīvasahitasya|
anyeṣvapi sā bhūteṣvapekṣyate cetkrameṇa sā'pyastu||
mevaṃ bhūtagaṇasya pratyekasamarthatābhāvāt|
naikasminsā yukatā tasmātsarveṣu bhūteṣu||
iti bhūtādhikaraṇam||
samānāsṛtyupakramādamṛtatvañcānupoṣya||7||
tadāpītessaṃsāravyapadeśāt||8||
sūkṣmaṃ pramāṇataśca tayopalabdheḥ||9||
nopamardenātaḥ||10||
asyaivacopapatterūṣmā||11||
pratiṣedhāditi cenna śārīrātspaśṭohyekeṣām||12||
evamutkrāntiprakāro nirūpitaḥ| kimiyamutkrāntirvidvadaviduṣossamānā, utāviduṣa eveti saṃśaye satyaviduṣa eveti prāptam| " yadā sarve pramucyante kāmā ye'sya hṛdi sthitāḥ| atha martyo'mṛtobhavatyatra brahma samaśnuta " iti śruteriti| evaṃ prāpte ucyate - viduṣo'pi " tayordhvamāyannamṛtatvametī " ti nāḍīviśeṣarūpapraveśarūpagtayupakramātprāgutkrāntissamānā|
utkramaṇārthanāḍīpraveśadaśāyāmeva vidvadaviduṣorviśeṣaḥ śrūyate| " śatañṭaikāca hṛdayasya nāḍyastāsāṃ mūrdhānamabhinissṛtaikā| tayordhvamāyannamṛtatvameti viṣaavaṅṅanyā utkramaṇe ṅavanti " iti| viṣaavagiti nāḍīviśeṣaniyamābhāva ucyate| " eṣa ātmā niṣaakrāmati cakṣuṣo vā mūrdhno vā anyebhyo vā śarīradeśebya " iti hi śrutyantaram| evaṃ ca atraivāmṛtatvoktiśśarīrendriyasambandhamadagdhvaiva yadamṛtatvasattarapūrvāghāśleṣavināśasvarūpaṃ tadviṣayā " brahmasamaśruta " ityaktirapyupāsanākālabhavabrahmānubhavarūpabrahmaprāptiviṣayā| avaśyacaivamevābhyupeyam| arcirādimārgeṇabrahmaprāptiparyantaṃ dehasambandhalakṣaṇasaṃsārasya vyapadeśāt sūkṣmaśarīrānuvṛttaścandrasaṃvādarūpaprāṇenāvagamācca| śrūyate hi pathai candrasaṃvādo " yevaikecāsamāllokātprayanti candramasameva te sarve gacchatī " tyupaktamya " etadvaisvargasya lokasya dvāraṃ
yaschandramāḥ taṃ yaḥ pratyāha tamatisṛjate atha ya enaṃ na pratyāha vā siho vā matsyo vā parasvānmā puruṣovā anyovai teṣu sthāneṣu prayājāyate yathākarma yathāvidyaṃ tamāgataṃ pṛcchati ko'stīti taṃ prati brūyādityādi| ato " yadā sarve pramucyanta " iti śrutirna śarīrandriyarūpabandhoparamardaviṣayā sūkṣmaśarīrasyotkrasya jīvena gateścandarasaṃvādārthe tadantarapratipattāviti sūtroktanyāyenāvaśyavaktavytavāt| evaṃ viduṣo'pi sūkṣmaśarīrasyotkramaṇasattvādeva tasyotkramaṇonmukhasya " santāpayati svaṃ dehamāpātadalamastaka " miti śrutessarvadehavyāpyāgniśikhāyāḥ kvacidevopasaṃhriyamāṇatāvattadūṣmā kvacidevopalabhyate| nanu viduṣa utkrāntirnasaṃbhavati| yo kāmo niṣkāma āptakāma ātmakāmo na tasya prāṇā utkrāmanti brahamaiva san brahmāpyetī " ti tasyotkrāntipratiṣedhāt| na ca " na tasmātprāṇā utkrāmantī " ti māndhaayāndinapāṭhāt tasyeti kāṇvapāṭhe sambandhasāmānyavihitā ṣaṣṭhī " naṭasya śrṛṇotī " ti vadapādānārtheti mucyamānānnaviśliśyanta iti tadartha iti vācyam| śākhādvaye'pi tatpadasya śarīraparāmarśitatvāt| bṛhadāraṇyaka eva " yatrāyaṃ puruṣo mriyate udasmātprāṇāḥ krāmantyāhone " tyārtabhāgapraśnenetihovāca yājñavalkyo'traiva samavanīyante sa ucchvayatyādhmātho mṛtaśśeta iti prāṇotkrāntyapādānasyādhmānena vāyupūraṇena dṛtivaducchvasanasya śarīradharmasya kīrtanāt jīvātprāṇāmāmutkānteraprasaktatvena tatpratiṣedhānupapatteśca| ato viduṣaśśarīradutkrāntyabhāvādarcirādigatiścandharasaṃvādassarukagṛtaduṣkṛtavidhūnanamityetatsarvaṃ satyalokaprāptyarthavidyāviśeṣaphalaṃ na tu muktyarthabrahmavidyāphalamiti netavyam| brahmavido maraṇakāle kvācitkoṣmadarśanaṃ tu tejaśśabdoktasya sūkṣmadehasya paradevatāsampatteḥ prācīnatvenopapadyata iti ceducyate|
ārtabhāgapraśne śarīrādutkrāntirna pratiṣidhyate kiṃ tu śarīrādeva tatrotkrāntipratiṣedhasyāvidvādviṣayatvena aviduṣaḥ prāṇīnāṃ śarīrādutkrānteḥ pratiṣeddhumaśakyātvat| avidvadviṣayatvaṃ kathamavasīyata iti cet,utarasdarbhāt| uttarasaṃdarbhe
" yatrāsya puruṣasyamṛtasyāgniṃvāgapyeti vātaṃ prāṇaścakṣurīdityaṃ manaścandraṃdiśaśkṣatraṃ pṛthaivīṃ śarīramākaśamātmaiṣadhīrlomāni vanaspatīn keśā apsusohitaṃ ca retaśca nidhīyate kvāyaṃ tadāpuruṣo bhavatī" tyārtabāgapraśne yājñavalkyenārtabhāgena ca rahali sammantraṇena puṇyapāparūpaṃ karama tadāśrayatvena niścitamiti tadanantarasandarbhe śrutyaivapratipādanāt| catatra hi śrūyate " tauhotkramya mantrayāṃcakrāte tauhayadūcyatiḥ atha yatpraśaśaṃsatuḥ puṇyovaipuṇyena karmaṇā bhavati pāpaḥ pāpeneti " ti| na ca " apapunarmṛtyuṃjayatī " ti pūrvavākye sasārasaṃtaraṇarūpaṃ phalamucyate tena prakṛtividvadviṣayamutkrāntiprakatiṣedhasyāvasīyata iti vācyam| " agnirvai mṛtyussopāmanna " mityapāmagnyannatvopāsanāmuktvā hi tasya phalaṃ " apapunarmṛtyuñjīyatī " tyupacyate| tasmādagnijayaevātra mṛtyujayo na saṃsārataraṇaṃ jīvātprāṇānāmukrāntipaprasaktā na pratiṣedhyeti cet na| vidvadviṣaye " tasya tāvadeva cira "; miti śarīraviyogakālaeva viśleṣa iti śaṅkārūpaprasaktisattvāt| evaṃ prasaktyā viśiṣya viduṣo jāvītprāṇānāmutkrāntipratiṣedhenāviduṣastarhi jīvātprāṇānāmutkrāntirastimityavidvadviṣaye'pi śaṅkārūpaprasaktisattvācca| tasmādasti viduṣo'pyutkrāntiḥ| smaryate ca tasya mūrthanyayāḍyotkarāntiḥ " ūrdhvamekassthitasteṣāṃ bhitvā sūryamaṇḍasam| brahmalokamatikramyatena yāti parāṃ gati " miti nāḍīviseṣāṇāṃ madhye yassarūryamaṇḍasaṃ bhitvordhvaṃ sthaitaḥ tena mūrddhasambdhaināḍīviśeṣāṇānāṃ madhye'pi sūryamaṇḍalaparyantaprasṛtināḍīviśeṣaṇa brahmalokātītiṃ paramaṃ padaṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ| tasya yājñavalkyavacanasyodāharaṇena

saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
utkrāntirnāsti viduṣāṃ tatraivāmṛtatāśruteḥ|
natasya prāṇā iti ca tadutkrānterniṣedhanāt||
maivamastyeva teṣāmapyeṣā śrutiśu vañcitā|
āgatyupakramātteṣāṃ samānācetarairasā||
karmahāṇarūpātra teṣāmamṛtatā śrutā|
dehabandhadagdhvaiva brahmāptirabrahmāciṃtanam||
na tu tadubhayaṃ mukhyaṃ brahmarcirādipathena hi
sthitimadupari prāptaṃ tatraiva bandhavidhūnanam|
tadavadhivapūssūkṣmaṃ yenendusaṃvadanādikaṃ
vipuṣi viduṣaḥ kvācitkoṣmā ca yasya viniṣkrame||
prāṇotkrāntiniṣedhanaṃ na vapuṣaḥ kitvātmanaḥ prastutāt
brahmāvidviṣayārtabāgavihitapraśnottare tadyathā|

kiñcātra sphuṭamāmananti vacane mādhyandināḥ pañcamīm||

dehaviyoga teṣāmapi śaṅkerannato viyogamiti||
iti āsṛtyupakramādhikaraṇ||
tāni pare tathāhyāha||13||
sakaraṇagrāmassaprāṇaśśarīrendriyādhyakṣo jīva utkrāntau bhūtasūkṣmeṣu sampadyata ityuktam| saiṣā sampattirviduṣao vidyata ityāśaṅkya parihṛtaṃ ca| tāni jīvapariṣvaktāni, bhūtasūkṣmāṇi kiṃ yathākarma yathāvidhaṃ ca jīvena saha svakāryāya gacchantī, uta paramātmani sampadyanta iti cintyate| nanu " tejaḥ parasyāṃ devatīyā "
miti caturthavākyārthacinteyam| sā prāṇastejasītyantavākyatrayārthaviṣayādhikaraṇatrayānantarameva kartumucitā| caturthāvakyārthasyāpyutkrāntiviṣayasya gatyupakramātprācīnatvāt| evaṃ saprakārotkrāntivicārānantarameva seyumutkrāntirviduṣo'pi samāneti " samānācāsṛtyupakramā " dityadhikaraṇena nirūpaṇīyam| satyam| uktabhaṃgyāṃ prāksṅgatyā tasyāsyādhikaraṇasyātrāpyastītyākṣepikī saṅgatiḥ| brahmavidāmutkrāntipūrvikā gatirastīti pūrvādhikaraṇoktamayuktam| " teja parasyāṃ devatāyā " miti vāgādimanaḥ prāṇasahitasya sūkṣmadehasya sarvaprakṛtibhūtaparabrahmaṇi svarūpeṇaiva layapratipādanāt dehendriyādirahitasya candrasaṃvādādyasambhavena tadyuktāyā arcirādigateranyaviṣayasyāvaśyavyaktatvāditi| atraivaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ| madhye paramātmāsampattau sukhaduḥkhopabhogarūpakāryādarśanāttadupabhogarūpakāryānuguṇyena yathākarma yathāvidyaṃ gacchanti| tejaḥ parasyāmiti tu paramātmanassarvavastu sādhāraṇaṃ dhārakatvamanudyataiti||
siddhāntastu - prakṛtāni bhūtasūkṣmāṇi paramātmani sampadyante| tejaḥ parasyāṃ devatāyāmitihyāha śrutiḥ| śrutopapattayaṃ ca phalaṃ paramātmani sampattyā suṣuptipralayayoriva viśramahetustatatsaṃrgaviśeṣastraikasadhigamyaḥ| evaṃ " tejaḥ parasyāndevatāyā " mityasyāpūrvārthaparatvasambhavāt na sarvavastusādhāraṇadhārakatānuvādakatvaṃ kalpanīyamiti||
saṃgrahakārike||
jīvayuktāni bhūtāni gacchantyeva yathāphalam|
sampadyante phalābhāvānnamadhye paramātmani||
tejaḥ parasyāmiti tu sarvasādhāraṇīṃ vibhoḥ|
ādhāratāmanuvadatyeṣāmanyatra gacchatām||
maivaṃ tejaḥ parasyāmityuktasāphalyasiddhaye|
svīkāryā tatra sampattissuptipralayayoriva||
iti parasampattyadhikaraṇam||
avibhāgo vacanāt||14||
seyaṃ paramātmani sampattiḥ prākṛtapralayavat kāraṇāpattirūpā'stu| paramātmanaḥ sarvanaprakṛtitvena manādidevaiṣamyāditi prāpta ucyate| pṛthagvyavahārānarhasaṃsargarūpavibhāgamātrameva| " tejaḥ parasyā "mityatrāpi sampadyata ityasyānuṣaṅgātsya prāksaṃrgaviśṣācitvāklṛpteḥ| ihānuṣaktasyehārthabhede pramāṇābhāvāt| prākṛtapralayānantaraṃ mahāsargādāviva punassṛṣṭyavacanācca||
saṃgrahakārike||

evaṃ vidvadaviduṣossamānautkrāntiprakāra uktaḥ| gatyupakrame viduṣo mūrdhanyanāḍyotkranāntiniyamarūpo viśeṣa ucyate| atra mūrdhanyayā brahmanāḍyaiva viduṣo gatirnānyeṣāṃ tathā gatiriti niyantuṃ na śakyate| nāḍīnāmutkramaṇakleśakṣubhitahṛdayena jīvena vivektumaśakytavāt| na ca vidyānuṣṭhānakāle svayamīdṛkpradeśasthitayaitādṛśyā nāḍyā niṣkramya brahma prāpnavānītyanusndhānābhyāsapāḍavāt svaniṣkramanāḍīparijñānaṃ bhavet, utkramaṇakleśasya brahmāṇi viśramaṇena śāntyā vivecanakauśalamapi bhavediti vācyam| tathā'pila ghanasantamasāvṛtahṛdayāpavarakagatasya nirantarānekasūkṣmatamanāḍīsahasreṣu svaniṣkramaṇanāḍhyā vivektumaśkyātvāt
tasmāt " tayordhvamāya " nniti śrutiḥ kadācidyādṛcchikaṃ viduṣo mūrdhanyanāḍyā niṣkramaṇaṃ tasyāḥ stutyarthamanuvadatīti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ|
siddhāntastu - śatādhikayā mūrdhanyanāḍyaiva vidvānūtkrāmati nānya iti niyamassambhavatyeva| "tayordhvamāyanni"ti śruteḥ pākṣikānuvādatvakalpanā'yogāt | na ca nāḍīnāṃ durdarśatvam| " tasya haitasya hṛdayasyāgraṃ pradyotate tena pradyotenaiṣa ātmā niṣkrāmaṇa kāle hṛdayasyāgraprakāśaśravaṇādviduṣo vidyāsāmarthyasya vidyānuṣṭhānakāle tacacheṣagaticintanābhyāsasya vidyārādhaitaparamapuruṣaprasādasyacādhakasya sattvācca||
saṃgrahakārike||
nāḍyo durdarśāiti viduṣo na brahmanāḍikāniyamaḥ|
yādṛcchikatadgamanānuvādiyattattayordhvamiti maivam||
vidyārādhitabhagavadanugrahavibhāvitadvāraḥ|

raśmynusārī||17||
vidoṣo hayāt brahmanāḍyā nirgatasyādityaraśmīnanusṛtya tanmaṇḍalagatiḥ śrūyate| " yatraitadasmāccharīradutkrāmatyathaitaireva raśmibhirūrdhva ākramata " iti| ato raśmibhirevetyavadhāraṇamupapadyate naveti saṃśaye nopapadyate| niśimṛtasyādityaraśmyanuraṇāsambhāvat niśāsvanubhūyamānasyaṣmaṇa āgneyatvenāpyupapatteḥ| jyotiṣaprakriyayā candramaṇḍalparaviṣṭāssūryaraśmya eva candrakiraṇā ityaṅgīkāre'pi darśādirītriṣu teṣāmapyabhāvāt| na ca niśi mṛtasya viduṣo mūrdhanyanāḍyā niṣkrāntasya yāvadudayaṃ sūryaraśmipratīkṣaṇena ūrdhvagatirvācyā| rayāvatkṣipyenmanastāvadādityaṃ gacchedi " ti śaidhnyavacanavirodhāt| tasmādraśmivākyaṃ pākṣikānuvādakamityeva vācyamiti||
siddhāntastu - niśyapi vidvānsūryaraśmyanūsārī gacchita| grīṣmedivā saurātapoṣmaprakarṣestyeva niśai sikatādīṣūṣmadarśanena tasya sūryaraśmisambandhitvāvagatyā rātrāvapi tatsattvāvagamāt| evaṃ ca raśmivākyamapyarthavadbhavatīti||
saṃgrahakārike||
raśmyanusāri na gamanaṃ niyataṃ rajanīṣū tadabhāvāt|
śrutiretaireveti tu pākṣaikamanuvadati tallābham||
evaṃ satyavadhāraṇamanarthakatvaṃ prapadyate|
tasakmānniyataṃ rajaniṣvapi te santyuṣmadarśanālliṃgāt||
iti raśmyanusārādhikaraṇam||
niśineticenna sambhandhasya yāvaddehabhāvitvādarśayatica||
viduṣo niśimṛtasya brahmaprāptirasti naveti cintyate| nāstyeva| " divā ca śuklapakṣaśca uttrāyaṇameva ca| mumūrṣatāṃ praśastāni viparītaṃ tu garhita " miti niśāmaraṇasya ninditatvāt| ninditena ca paramapuruṣārthaprāptyayagāditi prāpta ucyate| viduṣo niśi mṛtasyāpi brahmaprāptirbhavatyeva| na tu niśāmaraṇanindayā garhitagatiprāptiḥ| karmasambandhasya vidyādehāvasānatvena bandhahetvābhāvāt| anārabdhakāryāṇāṃ prācīnakarmaṇāṃ vidyayā vināśāduttakarmaṇāmaśleṣāt prārabdhakāryasya karmaṇaścaramadehāvadhitvāt niśāmaraṇaṃ dṛśyate, teṣāṃ tadā prabalaprācīnaduṣkṛtakarmaṇāṃ phalonmukhatā phalabalātkalpanīyā||
saṃgrahakārike||

niśi mṛtasya viduṣo brahmaprāptihetunaiva dakṣiṇāyanamṛtasyāpi tasya sā siddhā| atideśādhikaraṇasyāsyeyamadhikā śaṅkā " ya evaṃvidvānudamayane pramīyate devānāmeva mahimānaṃ datvā''dityasya sāyujyaṃ gacchatyatha yo dakṣiṇe pramīyate pitṛṇāmeva mahimānaṃ gatvā candramasassāyujyaṃ salokatāmāpnotī " ti taittirīyāṇāmupaniṣadi śrūyate| tena viduṣo'pi dakṣiṇāyanamṛtasya candraṃ prāpya pitryabhogānanubhūya " yāvatsampātanuṣitve " ti śāstrānusāreṇa punarāvṛttirvavikṣitatetyavasīyate| tatra puruṣavidyāvasāne pūrvānūvākavihitabrahmavidyāvata eva hi uttaradakṣiṇāyanamaraṇyoḥ phalaviśeṣa uktaḥ| satyam| tatra brahmaprāptireva pritapipādayiṣitā " brahmaṇo mahimānamāpnotī " ti vākyaśeṣāt| tasmāt tatrottarāyaṇamṛtasyādityaloke divyabhogo dakṣiṇāyanamṛtsaya pitryabhogaśca mārge viśrāntihetutvena vivakṣita iti yojanīyam| anyathā viduṣa uttarāyaṇamṛtasyāpi brahmaprāptirnāstīti tatassiddhiprasaṅgāt| nanu viduṣo mumūrṣūnprati punakāvṛttimāvṛttimāvṛttiñcaiva yoginaḥ|
prayātā yānti taṃ kālaṃ vakṣyāmi bharatarṣabha|
agnirjyotirahaśśuklasṣaṇmāsā uttarāyaṇam|
tatra prayātā gacchanti brahma brahmavido janāḥ||
dhūmo rātristathā kṛṣṇaṣṇaṇmāsā dakṣiṇāyanam|
tatra cāndramasaṃ jyotiryogī prāpya nivartate||
śuklakṛṣṇe gatīhyete jagataśśāśvate mate| ekayā yātyanāvṛttimanyayā''vartate| punaḥ " iti| ato dakṣiṇāyanamṛtasya na brahmaprāptaḥ| ata eva bhīṣmasyottarāyaṇapratīkṣaṇamuktaṃ bhārata iti cet| ucyate| nātra mumūrṣunprati maraṇakālaviśeṣaḥ smaryate| kiṃ tu yogāniṣṭhaiḥ smartavyadevayānapitṛyāṇākhye gatī smaryate| tathāhyupasaṃhāra
" naite sṛtī pārthajānanyogī muhyati kaścana| tasmātsarveṣu kāleṣu yogyayukto bhavārjune " ti| " agnirjyotiḥ, dhūmo rātriri " ti ca ślokābhyāṃ devayānapitṛyānagatī pratyabhijñāyete| bhīṣmasya tu yogaprabhāvāt svapitṛprasādaprabhāvācca prāptaṃ svacchandamaraṇaṃ khyāpayitumuttarāyaṇapratīkṣaṇam| tasmādviduṣo dakṣiṇāyanamṛtasyāpi brahmaprāptistīti siddham||
saṃgrahakārike||
na tathā'pi dakṣiṇāyanaṃ mṛtasya sā tasya candrasāyujyokteḥ|
bhīṣmasya brahmavido'pyudagayanāgamanirīkṣaṇasyāpyukteḥ||
maivaṃ tasyāpi syātpūrvoktādbandhahetvabhāvādeva|
tasyaindavasāyujyaṃ kālaviśeṣātpathīndrutaḥ pūjāptiḥ||
ata eva vākyaśeṣe brahmaprāptistatra varṇyate tasya|
bhīṣmasya tatpratīkṣā māhātmyakhyāpanāyaiva||
iti dakṣiṇāyamanādhikaṇam||
iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulajaladhikāstaustubha śrīmadadvaitavidyācārya śrīviśvajidyājiśrīraṅgarājādhvaravarasunorapyadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturmatasāre
nayanamukhamālikākhye dvitīyapiracchede caturthādhyāyasya dvitīya pādaḥ||

||caturthādhyāyasya tṛtīya pādaḥ||
arcirādinā tatprathiteḥ||1||
brahmaprāptiphalo viduṣo mārga iha pāde cintyace| chāndogye tāvadupakramosalavidyāyāṃ - " arciṣamevābhisambhavantyarciṣo'haraha āpūryamāṇapakṣamāpūryamāṇapakṣādyān ṣaḍudaṅṅetimāsāṃstānmāsebhyassaṃvatsaraṃ saṃvatsarādādityamādityāccandramasaṃ candramasā vidyutaṃ tatpuruṣo mānavassa enānbrahyagamayatyeṣa devapatho brahmapatha etena
pratipādyamanā imaṃ mānavamāvarta nānartanta " iti śrūyate| bṛhadāraṇyake pañcāgnividadyāyāṃ - " terciṣamabhaiśambhavantyarchiṣo'harahna āpūryamāṇapakṣamāpūryamāṇapakṣādyān ṣaṇmāsāmudaṅṅāditya eti māsebhyo jevalokaṃ devalokokādītyamādityādvedyutaṃ vaidyutātpuruṣo'mānaca ssa etya brahmalokāngamayatī " ti| tatraiva puvaranyathā mārgaḥ śrūyate - " yadā vai puruṣo'smāllokātpraiti sa vāyulokamāgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā rathacakrasya svaṃ tena sa ūrdhva ākramate sa ādityamāgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihite yathālaṃtena sa ūrdhvaākramate sa candramasamāgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā duṃdubheḥ khaṃtenasaūrdhvaākramate sa lokamāgacchatyeśokamahimaṃ tasminvasati śāśvatīsmamā " iti śrūyate| kauṣītakināmupaniṣadi - " sa etaṃ devayānaṃ panthānamāpadyāgnilokamāgacchati sa vāyuloka sa varuṇalokaṃ sa ādityalokaṃ sa indralokaṃ sa prajāpatilokaṃ sa brahmaloka " miti mārgaḥ śrūyate| tatra punardaharavidyāyāṃ - " athaitaireva raśmibhirūrdhva ākramata " iti śrūyate| tatra saṃśayaḥ| kimarcirādika ekaeva mārga etābhiḥ śrutibhiḥ pratipādyate uta tasmādanyo'pi mārgaḥ pratipādyata iti mārgāniyama iti| aniyayama iti tāvatprāptam| mārgaparvabhedena ca bhedapratīteḥ| nacopasaṃhāreṇābedopapādanaṃ yuktam| " yatraitasmāccharīrādutkrāmatyathaitaireva raśmibhirūrdhva ākramata " ityavadāraṇavirodhāt| " sayāvatkṣipyenmanastāvadādityaṃ gacche " diti śaidhnyavacanavirodhāt| bhinnabhinnavidyāmnātānāṃ mārgaparvaṇāṃ brahmaguṇānāmivopasaṃhārānapekṣaṇāccaci prāpte ucyate| arcirādinaikenava mārgeṇa vidvān gacchati| tasyaiva sarvatra pratyabhījñānāt| āvadhāraṇasyāyogavyavacchedānuvādatvāt| ekasya vākyasya raśmiprāpaṇe taditaravyavacchede ca tātparyāyogāt| śaindhyavacanasya pitṛyāṇamārgāpekṣayopapatteḥ| " sa yāvatkṣipyenmana " ityasya kevalamatiśayoktirūpatvāt| chāndogyabṛhadāraṇyakayoḥ pañcāgnividyāyāṃ " tadya itthaṃ viduryeceme'raṇye śraddhātapa utyupāsata " iti " tadyaitthaṃviduryecāmī araṇye śraddhāṃ satyamupāsata " iti ca pañcāgnividāmiva vidyāntaraniṣṭhānāmapyārcirādikasyaiva mārgasyopadeśācca||
saṃgrahakārike||
bhinnā mārgā viduṣāmāmnātāstatra tatra vidyāyām|
vidyārūpaguṇabhedāttattatpūrvakramānyatvāt||
sarvo'rcirādiko mārgo'yaṃ pratyabhijñānāt|
tatsattve'pi ta sarvāvidyā naikā guṇānanyāt||
iti arcirādyadhikaraṇam||
vāyumabdādaviśeṣaviśeṣābhyām||2||
arcirādinaikenaiva mārgeṇa sarve vidvāṃso gacchantītyuktam|| tasminmārge chāndogā māsādityayorantarāle devalokamadhīyate| " māsebhyassaṃvatsaraṃ saṃvatsarādāditya " miti| vājasaneyinastu tayorantarāle devalokamadhaīyate| " māsebhyo devalokaṃ devalokādāditya " miti| mārgaikyāttāvubhāvapi ubhayatropasaṃhāryau| tayoḥ pañcamyabhihitasya śrautakramasya tulyatve'pi māsānantaraṃ lasaṃvatsaro niveśanīyaḥ| aharāpūryamāṇapakṣamāsānāmuttarottaramadhikakālānāmuttarottaraparvabhāvadarśanenanodagayanarūpaṣaṇmāsānantaraṃ niveśanaucityāt| atassaṃvatlarādūrdhvaṃ devaloka iti
niścīyate| atra ca vājasaneyino " yadā vai paruṣo'smāllokatpraiti sa vāyumāgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā rathacakrasya khaṃ tena sa ūrdhva ākramate sa ādityamāgacchatī " tyādityātpūrvaṃ vāyumadhīyate| kauṣitakinastu " sa etaṃ devayānaṃ panthānamāpadyāgnilokamāgacchati sa vāyuloka " mityagnilokaśabdaniridiśaṣṭādarciṣaḥ paraṃ vāyumadhīyate| tatra kauṣītakināṃ pāṭhakrameṇārciṣaḥ paratvena prāptasya vāyorvājasaneyināṃ " tenordhva ākramate sa ādityamāgacchatī " tyūrdhvaśabdanirdiṣṭaśrautakrameṇa pāṭhakramādbalīyasā''dityātpūrvaṃ niveśaḥ| evaṃ saṃvatsarādūrdhvamādityātpūrvaṃ vāyudevalokayoḥ prāptāvidaṃ cintyate| kiṃ vāyudevalokau bhinnāviti tau yatheṣṭakrameṇa vidvān gacchati| utābhinnāviti saṃvatsarādurdhvaṃ vāyuṃ santaṃ devalokaṃ gacchatīti| kiṃ yuktam| bhinnārthatvaprasiddherūrdhvaśabdena pañcamyācobhayorapi saṃvatsarādityāntarāle śrutikrameṇa prāpteva viśeṣābhāvācca yatheṣṭaṃ gacchati| na ca " yo|ḍayaṃ pavate eṣa devānāṃ graha " iti śravaṇānna vāyudevalokau bhinnāviti vācyam| " saṃvatsaraḥ sravgo loka " ityarthavādāntaraśravaṇāt saṃvastara eva devaloka ityapi kalpanāsambhavena māsādityayormadhye saṃvastaradevalokau niveśanīyau uta devalokavāyu ityatra vinigamābhāvaprasaṅgāditi| evaṃ prāpte ucyate| saṃvastarādūrdhva vidvānvāyuṃ gacchati| devalokaśabdena devānāṃ loki ityaviśeṣaṇoktasya " sa vāyumāgacchatī " ti viśaiṣaaya vāyurūpeṇa vidhānāt " vāyureva hi devānāṃ lokaḥ yo'yaṃ pavata " iti śravaṇāt| na ca " saṃvatsaraḥ svargo loka " itivattadarthamādamātraṃ divyānāṃ vimānānāṃ vāyupāśadhṛtatvena vāyoḥ stambhādivat devagṛhāntarbhāvopapatattyā svārathaparatvasambhavāt||
saṃgrahakārike||
śrutyaiva saṃvatsaradevalokayormāsebhya ūrdhva jhaṭiti prasaktayoḥ|
saṃvatsaro māsasamanvayādbhavedādau tadūrdhva niviśetacetaraḥ||
tatraiva māruto'pi prāpnotyūrdhvapadarūpayā śrutyā|
kauṣītakināmarcissamanantarapāṭhato balīyasyā||
tayorna pūrvottarastāniyāmakaṃ tato yatheṣṭaṃ viniveśanaṃ bhavet|
tadeva vokohyaviseṣamīritassavāyurevātra mitastathā śruteḥ||
śrutamapi devagṛhatvaṃ vāyoriva tatpurvamabdasyāpi|
stutadareva tadyathārthaṃ vimānanivahasya vāyupāśadhṛtatvāt||
iti vāyvadhikaraṇam||
taṭito'dhivaruṇassambandhāt||3||
kauṣītakināṃ " sa etaṃ devayānaṃ panthānamāpadyāgnilokamāgacchatī " tyādiśravaṇe agnilokaśabdasyārciḥparyāyatvena prāthamyavavigītam| tatra tadanantaraṣṭhitasya vāyossaṃvatsarādūrdhva niveśa uktaḥ| ādityāsyāpi tatra varuṇānantarapaṭhitasya pāṭhakramamabādhena devanalokadādityamiti vājasanekoktaśrutikramāddevalokaśabdābhihitāt vāyorupari niveśassiddhaḥ| varuṇendraprajāpatiṣu cintā - kiṃ teṣāṃ vāyorūrdhvaṃ niveśaḥ uta vidyuta ūrdhvamiti vāyorurdhvamiti tāvatprāptam| anyatra kvāpyavakāśābhāvāt| arcirādividyutparyantānāṃ sarveṣāmapi śrautakramavattvāttadanantaramamānavapuruṣasyaiva
brahmaprāpayitṛtvaśravaṇācca| tataśca sarvatrāvakāśābhāvena teṣāṃ niveśāprāptau tadupadeśāvaiyarthyāyāvaśyaṃ kva na śrautakrame bādhanīye " sa vāyulokaṃ sa varuṇaloka " miti pāṭhakrameṇa vāyoranantaraṃ varuṇo niveśanīyaḥ| kramibādhāt kramabādhasya nyāyyatvāt| evaṃ vāyvādityayośkṣautakramabādhe sati indraprajāpatī api varuṇānantaraṃ niveśanīyāviti| evaṃ prāpta ucyate - varuṇastāvadvidyuta ūrdhvaṃ niveśanīyaḥ| sambandhāt vidyuta upari sanīranīradarśanena nīrādhipatervarūṇasya tata uparibhāvaucityāt tadanantarabhindraprajāpyorniveśaḥ pariśeṣāt, " āgantukānāmante niveśa " iti nyāyācca| amānavapuruṣasya brahmaprāpakatvaṃ varuṇādivyavadhānena tatsāhityenavopapadyata iti na virodhaḥ||
saṃgrahakārike||
varuṇādyā niviśeranmadhye vāyośca bhānośca|
anyatrāpi niveśe śrautaḥ kaścitkramo bādhyaḥ||
ityupari niveśe bhavati sa enāniti śruterbādhaḥ|
upadiṣṭānāṃ teṣāṃ kvacinniveśca vaktavyaḥ||
tasmādvāyorūrdhva varuṇassyātta paṭhitatvāt|
tadanantaramitarāvapi vāyvādityakramasya bhagnatvāt||
maivaṃ vidyuta upari śrautakramabādhitasya varuṇasya|
sambandhādviniveśastaduparyavaśiṣṭayoryathāpāṭham||
vaidyutapuruṣasya na hi vyavadhivahīnamaśrutaṃ gamayatitṛtvam|
tena sa enāmiti śrutervihādhasya śaṅkā'sti||
iti varuṇādhikaraṇam||
ātivāhikāstalliṅgāt||4||
vaidyutenaiva tatastacchaØteḥ||5||
arcirādīnāṃ kramo nirūptaḥ| teṣāṃ svarūpaṃ cintyate| kimarcirādayo mārgacihnabhūtāḥ, uta bogabhūmayaḥ, athavā viduṣāmativoḍhāra iti tatra vananadīparvatādimārgacihnopadeśakalaukikavākyacchāyāpannavākyabodhyatvat| athavā bhogabhūmayaḥ eta eva lokaḥ| " yadahorātrāṇyardhamāsā ṛtavassaṃvatsarā " ityaharādīnāṃ lokatvavacanāt, kauṣītakiśākhāyāmagnilokamāgacchatīti lokaśabdaśravaṇācca| natvativoḍhāraḥ| amānavapuruṣasyaiva viśiṣyātivoḍhṛtvaśravaṇācceti| vidyutanantaramamānavapuruṣasyātivoḍhṛtvena varuṇādīnāmativoḍhṛtvāsambhāvācceti| evaṃ prāpte ucyate - viduṣāmativāhe paramapuruṣeṇa niyuktāḥ devatāviśeṣāḥ ete'rcirādayaḥ| " sa enānbrahma gamayatī " tyamānavapuruṣasya śrūyamāṇo yaḥ prāpyaprāpakabhāvasamndhaḥ sa eva pūrveṣvircirīdiṣvapi lasambandhaityavagamāt sāmānyato nirdiṣṭasya sambandhasya viśe,paryavasānāpekṣatvāt| nacācetanānāmātivāhikatvānupapattiḥ, arcirādīnāṃ tadabhimānidevatāparatvāt| yadyete sarve'pyānitivāhikāḥ tadā kathaṃ vidyutanantaraṃ śrutānāmindrādīnāmātivāhikatvopapattiḥ| tatrāmānavapuruśasyaivātivāhikatvaśravaṇaditicenmaivam| tatra varuṇādīnāmapi
tadanugrāhakatvenātivāhikakoṭyantarbhāvopapatteḥ||
saṃgrahakārike||
mārgacihnāni viduṣāṃ paṭhitā ye'rcirādayaḥ|
sarvatra lokaśabdādvā bhaveyurbhogabhūmayaḥ||
varṇyate hyativoḍhṛtvaṃ vaidyutasyopasaṃhṛtau|
tatassaeva sambandhaḥ pūrveṣāmapi sidhyati||
iti ātivāhikādhikaraṇama|
kāryaṃ bādarirasya gatyupapatteḥ||6||
viśeṣitatvācca||7||
sāmīpyāttu tadvayapadeśaḥ||8||
kāryātyaye tadadhyakṣaṇe sahātaḥ paramabhedhānāt||9||
smṛteśca||10||
paraṃ jaiminirmukhyatnāt||11||
darśanācca||12|
na ca kārye pratyabhisandhiḥ||13||
apratīkālasambanānnayatīti bādarāyaṇa ubhayathā ca doṣāttatkratuśca||14||
viśeṣañca darśayati||15||
gamayatṛnirūpaṇānantaraṃ gantṛrūpaṇamiti saṅgatiḥ| kimayayarcirādiko gaṇaḥ kāryaṃ hiraṇyagarbhamupāsīnānnayati utaḥ paraṃbrahmopāsarīnānatha paraṃbrahmopāsīnān pratyagātmānaṃ brahmātmatayopāsīnāṃśceti saṃśaye kāryamupāsīnānnayati| kāryaṃ hiraṇyagarbhamūpāsīnasyaiva gatyupapatteḥ| na hi paripūrṇaṃ paraṃ brahmopāsīnasya tatprāptaye deśānantaragatirupapadyate| nityaprāptatvāt| parabrahmaviṣayāvidyānivṛttimātrasya paravidyākāryātvāt| " puruṣo mānava etya brahmalokāngamayatī " ti, " prajāpatessabhāṃ veśma prapadya " iti ca bahuvacanāntena lokaśabdena lokaviśeṣeṣu tatrāpi prajāpatiśabdavācyababahiraṇyagarbhasabhāyāṃ vartamānaṃ hiraṇyagarbhamūpāsīnāneva nayatīti viśeṣitatvācca| " sa enānbrahma gamayatī " ti tasminnapuṃsakavaliṃgabrahmaśabdastu tasya parabrahmaprathamāpatyetvena tatsannikṛṣṭatvādupapadyate| arcarādinā hiraṇyagarbhaṃ prāptasya " tayordhvamāyannamṛtatvametī " imaṃ mānavamāvartaṃ nāvartanta "
iticāmṛtatvā punarāvṛttyuktiḥ kāryabrahmavokātyaye tadadhyakṣeṇa hiraṇyagarbheṇāvasitādhikāreṇa saha kāryabrahmalokātparasya parabrahmalokasya prāptiviṣayatvenopapadyate| " vedāntavijñānasuniścitārthāḥ saṃnyāsayogādyatayaśśuddhasatvāḥ| te brahmaloke tu parāntakāle paramṛtātparimucyanti sarve " iti śrutyā " brahmaṇā saha te sarve samprāpte pratisañcare| parasyānte kṛtātmānaḥ praviśanti paraṃ pada " miti smṛtyācca ca tathaiva pratipādanāt| athavā paraṃbrahmopāsīnāneva nayati| " sa enānbrahma magayatī " ti brahmaśabdasya tatra mukhyatvāt, parabrahmaṇassarvagatatve'pi viduṣastallokaviśeṣaṃprāptasyaiva karmatadvāsanādibandhanivṛttiriti paryaṅkavidyādiśāstrairavagamāt, " asmāccharīrātsamutthāya paraṃjyotirupasampadya svena rūpeṇāniṣpadyata " ityarcirādināla gatasya parabrahmaprāptiśravaṇācca| na ca " prajāpatessabhāṃ veśma prapadya " iti vidvadabhisandhipratipādanātkāryabrahmāptiśśaṅkanīyā| tatraiva " dhūtvā śarīramakṛtaṃ kṛtātmā brahmalokamabhisambhavānī " ti mantre " akṛtaṃ brahmaloka " miti viśeṣitatvena tadunurodhāt prajāpatiśabdasya yogena paramapuruṣaparatvopapatteḥ| evaṃ prapte ucyate - kāryamūsīnāneva nayatītivā paramīpāsīnāneva nayatīti vā na niyamaḥ| ubhayathāpi doṣāt| kāryamūsīnānnayatīti pakṣe "paraṃ jyotirupasaṃpadye "tyādikāḥ śrutayaḥ prakupyeyuḥ| paramevopāsīnānnayatīti pakṣe "tadya itthaṃ vidu "riti śrutiḥ prakupyet| sā hi śrutiḥ brahmātmakatvena jīvamūpāsīnānāmapyarcirādigatiṃ pratiktibhaṃgyā śarīrarūpācidviyuktajīvasvarūpopāsanāvidhānasya brahmāgnividāṃ brahmaprāptyayogāt| nacaivamapi tadayogastulyaḥ, "ātmetitumapagacchantī "tyādhikaraṇe brahmaṇo jīvaṃ pratyātmatvenānusandadhānasyaiva brahmaprāptisarādhanatvokteriti vācyam| brahmātmakatvena jīvānusandhāne'pi brahmaṇo jīvaṃ pratyātmatvānusandhānākṣateḥ lakṣmaṇapūrvaja ityato rāmānuja ityasyeva viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvamātraṃ hi tatra bhidyate| tasmāt hiraṇyagarbhādijīvameva ya upāsate kevalamacidvastu cidvastu ye vā brahmadṛṣṭyopāsate na tānpratīkālaṃbanānnayati arcirādiko gaṇaḥ| api tu paraṃbrahma jīvaviśeṣyatvena tadviśeṣaṇatvena vā ya upāsate tāneva nayati | ata eva " yāvannāmno gataṃ tatrāsya yathā kāmacāro bhavatī " tyādikā śrutirnāmādipratīkamupāsīnānāṃ brahmaprāptyartharcirādigatyanapekṣaṃ piramitaphalaviśeṣaṃ darśayatīti| anayā hi śrutyā devamanuṣyādi nāma yāvatsañcarati tāvatparyantameva nāma brahmeti pratīkālambanānāṃ yathākāmaṃ sañcāra ucyate| tena yatra " tathā vidvānnāmarūpādvimukta " iti śrutisiddho devādināmarūpavimokṣaḥ tatra sañcāro nāstīti jñāpyate| " vedāntavijñānasuniścitārthā " iti mantrastu hiraṇyagarbhopāsanayā'rcirādinā hiraṇyagarbhaṃ prāptasya tena saha brahmaprāpti na pratipādayati| kiṃ tu hiraṇyagarbhopāsanayā tallokaṃ prāptasya tatraiva kṛtabrahmopāsanasya svadehāvasāne brahmaprāptim| tasyahyayamarthaḥ - 
śravaṇamananādibhirniścitavedāntārthatatvā jitendriyāḥ puruṣadhaureyāḥ parabrahmaṇyeva loke vartamānāntanniṣṭhāssantaḥ parasya caramadehasyāntakāle avasāne parāmṛtāt parabrahmaṇassakāśāt tasyaiva prasādāt mucyanta iti| tasmādapratikālambanāneva nayatīti siddham||
saṃgrahakārike||
brahmāṇamupāsīnāngamayatyeṣo'rcirādigaṇaḥ|
tasya paricchinnatvādgatvā prāpyatvamupapannam||
na brahmopāsīnānvyāpitveneha tatprāpteḥ|
gamanasya vyarthatvādityevaṃ vādarirmena||
brahmaivopāsīnāngamayati tatra khalu tatpadaṃ mukhyam|
tallokaviśeṣasthaṃ gatvā prāpyamiti jaiminirmena||
brahmatadupāsate ye ye ca brahmātmakaṃ jīvam|
tānubhayānapi gamayati doṣātpakṣadvaye'pi pūrvokte||
bādarimate'rcirādiprāpyaṃ brahma śrutaṃ virudhye||
jaiminimatercirādiḥ pañcāgnividāṃ śruto na labhyeta||
ye vā hiraṇyagarbhaṃ yevā manaādyupāsate brahma dhiyā|
kevalamathavāvidhaitastasmātsarvānnanayatipratīkadṛśaste||
viśeṣaṇaṃ viśeṣyaṃvā kṛtvā brahmanijātmanaḥ|
upāsīnāngamayatītyevaṃ siddhāntapaddhatiḥ||
iti kāryādhikaraṇam||
||iti śrīmadbhāradvājakuladhaikaustubha śrūmadadvaitavidyācārya śrīviśvajidyāji
śrīraraṅgarādhvarivarasunorappayadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturamatasāre nayanamukha
mīlikākhye dvitadīyaparicchede caturthasyādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ||
||śrīmate hayavadanaparabrahmaṇe namaḥ||
caturdhādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ||
