muktaḥpratijñānāt||2||
ātmā prakaraṇāt||3||
" paraṃ jyotirapasampadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyata " ityatra devādirūpavatsādhyarupeṇābhiniṣpattirvivakṣitā uta svābhāvikarūpeṇāvirbhāva iti abhiniṣpattisvenaśabdābhyāṃ rūpasyābhiniṣpadyatvāyogāt| tasya suṣuptyādiṣu puruṣārthatvādarśanāt svena śabdāsyārthasiddhasvīyatvānuvādakatopapatteriti pūrvapakṣaḥ| svatassiddhasyāpyanādikarmabandharūpādikarmabandharūpāvidyātirohitasya tannivṛttyābhivyāktirūpeṇa sādhyatvasambhāvat abahatividyāyāmupapāditatvāt|
" yathā na kriyate jyotsnā malaprakṣālanānmaṇeḥ|
doṣaprahāṇānnajñānamātmanaḥ kriyeta tathā||
yathodapādakhananātkriyatena jalāmbaram|
sadeva nīyate vyaktimasatassambhavaḥ kutaḥ||
tathā heyaguṇadhvaṃsādavabodhādayo guṇāḥ| prakāśyante na janyante nityā evātmano hi te " iti śaunakasmaraṇācca| jñānānandādirūpasya karmabandhakṣayesati vikāsarūpāvanirbhāvo na svarūpata utpattiḥ| tathāsati svenaviśeṣaṇānarkyapattyā yena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate tas svīyatāyā arthasiddhatvena svena śabdasyāsādhyātvaparatāyā
vaktavyatvācceti siddhāntaḥ||
saṃgrahakārike||
āgantukaṃ kiñcidupaiti rūpaṃ mukto'bhiniṣpattadivaco'nurodhāt|
svarūpamātre pūruṣārthatāyāssuṣuptidṛṣṭemupalambhanācca||
trividagatavanmuktasyāgantukatvavitarkaṇāṃ
na hi viṣahate rūpesveneti tasya viśeṣaṇam|
api tadabhiṣpattiryuktaiva bandhavidhūnanā
dvikasati hi tajñjñānānandādi saṃkucitaṃ tadā||
iti sampadyāvirbhāvādhikaraṇam||
avibhāgena dṛṣṭatvāt||4||
kimayaṃ brahmaprāpto vidvān svātmānāṃ brahmaṇaḥ pṛthagbhūtamanubhavati, uta tadavibhaktimiti sāmyasāhitaparamasāmyakṣutibhyāṃ tattvamasyādikṣutibhiśca saṃśaye " so'śnute sarvānkāmānsaha| brahmaṇā vipaścite "ti,
" nirañjanaḥ sāmyupaitī " ti ca muktasya brahmaṇā saha sāhityaparamāsāmyaśravaṇāt pṛthagbhūtamevānubhavati| natvavibhaktam| avibhāgasyātivirodhāditi pūrvapakṣaḥ||
siddhāntastu - avibhaktamanubhavati| parabrahmaprāptayā nivṛttvamasyādisvātmano yathāvasthitasya svena dṛṣṭatvāt| svātmasvarūpaṃ hi tattvamasyādi śrutibhiḥ paramātmanaḥ taccharīratayā tatprakārabhūtamiti pratipāditam| tasmādavibāgena tataprakāratayaivānubhavati| sāhityaśrutistu kartṛsāmyaṃ karmasāmyaṃ vā pratipādayantī na prakāraprakāribhāvo'pi na paramasāmyena virudhyate| brahmānandānubhavādiguṇaiḥ paramasāmyasyaprakāraprakāribhāve'pi sattvāt| na ca sarvairapi guṇaissarvātmanā sāmyaṃ vivakṣitat| tathā sati vyāvartakadharmābhāvenābhedāpatteḥ| etena muktau paramasāmyaśravaṇāt prakāraprakāribhāvaśrutirbaddhāvasthāviṣayetyapītipūrvapakṣyāśayo nirastaḥ| sarvātmanā sāmyāsambhavāt|
tulādhṛtayoḥ svarṇaśilāśakalyoḥ paramasāmyoktāvivaṃ vivakṣitāṃśe atyantasāmyasattvāt||
saṃgrahakārike||
anubhavati svātmānaṃ muktaḥ paramātmanaḥ pṛthagbhūtam|
sāhityasāmyaviṣayaśrutyanusāreṇa tatkratunyāyāt||
evaṃ sati prakāraprakāribhāvādivaidharmyam|
baddhāvasthāmātreṇa tu muktau paramasāmyasampatteḥ||
maivaṃ tattvamasītyādyaparimitaśrutimitārthatatvasya|
dṛṣṭatvātsvaṃ brahmāvibhaktamanubhavati tatprakāratayā||
prakārabhutasyāpi syotsāhityaṃ bhogakrarmaṇi|
anyathaiva bhavetsāmyaṃ tena tasya vivakṣitam||
iti avibhāgādhikaraṇam||
brāhmeṇa jāminirūpanyālasādibhyaḥ||5||
cititmātreṇa tadātmakatvādityauḍulomiḥ||6||
evamapyupanyāsātpūrvaśrāvādavirodhaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ||7||
brahmaprāptasya svarūpāvirbhāva uktaḥ| sakenasvarūpeṇāvirbhāva iti śrutidvaidhyādvicāryate| tatra vijñānamātrarūpeṇeti tāvatpaprāptam| " sa yathā saindhavakhilyonantarobāhyaḥ kṛtsno rasaghanaevaṃ vā are'yamātmānantarobāhyaḥ kṛtsnaḥ prātrānadhana eve " ti tasya vijñānamātratvaśravaṇādapahatapātmatvādiśravaṇaṃ manaśśarīradarmapāpmatvādyanāśrayatvaparam| satyakāmatvasatyasaṅlpatvaśravaṇaṃ saṃsāradaśāyāṃ kadācitkasya
satyakātvādiparamiti| evaṃ prāptaucyate - brāhmeṇa daharavidyāyāṃ brahmaṇi śrutenāpahatapāpmādiguṇajātenāyamāvirbhāvaḥ
prajāpatividyāṃ jīve'pi tadupanyāsānmuktaveva jakṣaṇādiśruteśca| na prajñānaghanaevetyavadhāraṇavirodhaḥ| na hana tena vijñānātiriktarūpaniṣedhaḥ kriyate| kiṃ tu bāhyābhyantarabhedaṃ vinā sarvatra svaprakāśabodharūpatvamucyate| tatra dṛṣṭāntānuguṇyāt| na hi dṛṣṭānte saindhavaśakale sarvatra pradeśe lavaṇarasavyāptiṃ vinā rūpasparśādivyāvartane'pi tātparyamasti| tvagādipradeśabhedena cūtaphalāderiva pradeśabhedena nānārasatvaṃ nāstītyetāvadeva hi tatra vivakṣitam| atra śrutidvayānurodhāt karmabandharūpādividyākṣaye satyapahatapāpmatvanādiguṇajātena vikasitajñānasvarūpeṇacāyamāvirbhāva iti yuktam||
saṃgrahakārike|
brāhmeṇavirbhāvaṃ guṇāṣaaṭenāha muktasya|
jaiminirathauḍulomistamāha vijñānamātratayā||
ubhayavidhaśrutidṛṣṭerubhayātamatayeti siddhāntaḥ|
vijñānamātratātviha kārtsnyena svaprakāśatvam||
sā muktasyāmnātā nivartikā netaraguṇānām|
saindhavakhilyasya yathā rasaghanatādhavalatādīnām||
iti bāhyādhikaraṇam||
saṃkalpādeva tacchaØteḥ||8||
ataevacānananyādhipatiḥ||9||
rasaghana evetyavadhāraṇaṃ na pramāṇantarasiddhasya rūpāderiva saṅkalpādevetyavadhāraṇaṃ na lokasiddhasya vyāpārāntarasya nivartakamiti śaṅkayā saṅagatiḥ| prajāpatividyāyāṃ " jakṣatkrījanramamāṇaḥ strībhirvā yānairve " tyādinā pratipāditā brahmasampannasya stryādiprāptiḥ kiṃ saṅkalpamātrasādhyā uta vyāpārāntarasāpekṣeti saṃśaye jīvasaṅkalpānāṃ siddhasaṅkalpatayā prasiddheṣu sārvabhaumīdiṣvabhimatārthaniṣpādane vyāpārāntarāpekṣaiva tasya stryādiprāptiriti| evaṃprāpta ucyate - saṅkalpamātrasādhyā muktasya stryādiprāptiḥ| " sa yadi pitṛlokakāmo bhavati saṅkalpādevasya pitarassamuttiṣṭhanti sa yadi strīlokakāmo bhavati saṅkalpāde striyassamuttiṣṭhantī" tyīdisāvadhāraṇaśruteḥ, " prajñānaghanaeve " ti vadihāvadhāraṇavirodhiśrutyantarābhāvacca| na ca muktasyāpi jīvatvāviśeṣādrājādivat kadācinmoghasaṅkalpatāprasaṅgaḥ svarasādbhavatīti| tasyākarmavaśyatvarūpānanyādhipatiśravaṇāt||
saṃgrahakārike||
karmādhīnatvācca kadācitadsaṅkalpamoghatāyāḥ|
muktānāṃ jñātyādiprāptiryatnāntarāpekṣā||
saṅkalpamātrasādhyaṃ rājñāmapi na kvaciddṛṣṭam|
saṅakalpādeveti hyavadhāraṇabādhaikāśrutirnāsti||
tasmātsarveśvaravatteṣāṃ saṅkalpāmātrajā sṛṣṭiḥ||
iti saṅkalpādikaraṇam||
aśrāvaṃbādirāha hyevam||10||
śrāvaṃ jaiminirvikalpāmananāt||11||
dvadaśāhavaduṅayavidhaṃ bādarāyaṇo'taḥ||12||
tanvabhāve sandhyavadupapatteḥ||13||
bhāve jāgradvat|.14||
pradīpavadāveśastathā hi darśayati||15||
svāpyayasampattyoranyatarāpekṣamāviṣkṛtaṃ hi||16||
kiṃ muktasya dehendriyāṇi santi uta na santi| athavā yathāsaṅkalpaṃ santi na santi ceti viśaye sati na santyena| "na havai saśarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayorapahatirastyaśarīraṃ vāvasantaṃ na priyāpriye spṛśata "iti hyāha śrutiḥ| ata eva tasya śarīrātsamutthānamanantarameva vadati "eṣa samprasādo'smāccharīrātsamutthāye " tyādinā| na ca muktas svaśarīrasṛṣaaṭau tadarthapitrādisṛṣṭauvā'pekśā syāt| tadā tasya brahmānubhavādanyaparatvaṃ brahmaṇo niratiśayabhogyatvahāniśca prasajyeta| " sa ekadhā bhavati tridhā bhavatī " tyādyāmanānaṃtvarthavādamātram| aṇorjīvasya yugapadanekadehavyāptyayogāditi pūrvapakṣaḥ|
siddhāntastu - muktasyecchayā śarīriṇi santi na santi ca śrutidvayānurodhāt| yathā " dvādaśāhamṛddhikamā upeyuḥ, dvadaśāhena prajākāmaṃ yadaye " diti śrutidvayadarśanāt dvadaśāhaścasatrāhīnobhayātmakaḥ| nanu videhāvasthāyāṃ kathaṃ kāmabhogaḥ tadabhāve kathaṃ videhāvasthecchā| yadi manomātreṇa tadā bhogaḥ tadā kadā'pi dehāpekṣā na syāditi cet ucyate| dehābhāve svapnaivāsūkṣmabhogaḥ| tadbhāve jāgararūpaspaṣṭasthūlabhogaḥ| yadā muktasya śarīrādyupakaraṇāni santi tadā tāni tasya satyasaṃkalpatve'pi tenaiva sṛṣṭānīti nāsti niyamaḥ| kadācit svapna iva sarveśvarasṛṣaaṭaiḥ pitṛlokādibhirlīlārasaṃ bhuṅkte kadācijjāgaraiva svasṛṣṭaissarveśravasṛṣṭaiśca pitrādyupakaraṇailīlārasaṃ bhuṅkte| sarveśaavaro līlarthaṃ daśarathavasudevādipitṛlokādikamātmanassṛṣṭvā tairmanuṣyadharmalīlādikaṃ yathā bhuṅkte, tathā muktānāmapi svalīlāyai svapitṛlokādikaṃ svayameva sṛjantīti viśeṣaḥ| na caitāvatā muktasya brahmānubhavādanyaparatvaprasaṅgaḥ| na hi te svabhogalāṃpaṭyena pitrādyuparakaṇādi sṛjanti| kintu
savibhūtikabrahmānubhāvārthaṃ brahmavibhūtiviśeṣatayaiva sṛjanti| nanvevaṃ satiyadākabhūtikabrahmānubhūtāvutkarṣāpakarṣo syātāmiticet| naiṣadoṣaḥ| sarvasyāpi muktasya svasṛṣṭaparasṛṣṭātītināgatasakala vibhūtiviśiṣṭabrahmānubhūtyaṅgīkārāt| nacāṇorjīvasya yugapadanekadehādhiṣṭhānāsaṃbhavaḥ| saṃsāradaśāyaṃ sarvāṅāgīṇasukhānubhavopapādakaikaidehavyāptiyogyasyeva muktidaśāyamanekadehavyāptiyogyasya dharmajñānavikāsasya sadbhāvāt| "bālāgraśatabhāgasya śatadhākalpitasya ca| bhāgo jīvassavijñeyasyacānantyāya kalpata" iti muktau dharmajñānavikāsenāparicchedarūpānantśravaṇāt| nanu "prājñenātmanā saṃpariṣvakto na bāhyaṃ kiñcana vedanāntara"miti śrutyā muktasya jñānābhāva ucyate| kathaṃ tasya jñānavikāsassārvajñyañcopeyate| naiṣadoṣaḥ| tasyāḥ śrutessuṣuptimaraṇānyatarāvasthāviṣayasvāt| tayorevahi jñānābhāvaḥ prasiddhaḥ| "nāhakhalvayameva saṃpratyātmānaṃ jānātyayamahamasmīti no evemāni bhūtāni vināśamevāpīto bhavatī"ti suṣuptau etebhyo bhūtebhyassamutthāya tānyevānuvinaśyati na pretyasaṃjñāstī"ti maraṇe| muktautu sārvajñyaṃ spaṣṭamevocyate - "sarvaṃ hapaśyaḥ paśyati sarvamāpnoti sarvaśa" iti| tasmānumuktāḥ svecchayā sadehā videhāśca bhavantīti siddham|
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
apavargaṃ prāptānāṃ na santi dehāstathāśravaṇāt|
saṅkalpātpitrādessṛṣṭyuktyā'pi ca na tatsiddhiḥ||
dehe pitrādau vā bhavantyapekṣājuṣau yadi te|
teṣā brahmanubhavānadanyaparatvaṃ prasajyate||
brahmaṇi ca bhogyatāya niratiśayatvaṃ prahīyeta|
santi na santi ca dehāḥ svakīyasaṅkalpabhedatasteṣām||
pitrādyupakaraṇaissaha sṛjanti tānnasvabhogarasataste|
bhagavallīlāntargatimālocya sṛjanti kiṃ tu yathā||
yattasya daśarathādisraṣṭurlīleddaśī ddaṣṭā|
aṇavo'pi jīvadīpāścaitanyaissvaprabhārūpaiḥ||
yugapatsṛṣṭāndehānbahūnapi vyāpnuvanti tadā|
caitanyāni hi muktau prāksakucitāni karmagaṇaiḥ|
saṃkocakaprahāṇāvdyāptiṃ yāntīti siddhāntaḥ|
iti abhāvādhikaraṇam||5||
jagadvyāpāravarjaṃ prakaraṇādasannihitatvācca||17||
pratyakṣopadeśānniticennādhikārikamaṇḍalasthokteḥ||18||
vikārāvarti ca tathā hi sthitimāha||19||
darśayataścaivaṃ pratyakṣānumāne||20||
bhogamātrasāmyaliṃgācca||21||
anāvattiśśabdādanāvṛttiśśabdāt||22||
tadidaṃ muktasyaiśvaryaṃ sarveśvarasyeva jagavdyāpārasahitaṃ uta tadvarjamiti saṃśaye paramasāmyaśruterjagavdyāpārasahi - tamiti prāptam| na ca sṛṣciniyamanādi prakaraṇeṣu sarveṣvarasyaiva prakṛtatvāt muktānāmasannihitatvācca tasyaiva sṛṣṭyādivyāpāro na muktānāmiti vācyam| śyenaprakaraṇe śyenavadiṣorasannihitatve'pi "samānamitaracchyenene"ti sāmyavacanāttadīyavaiśeṣikāṅgānāmiṣāviva muktānāmīśvarasāmyavacanāttadīyasṛṣṭyādivyāpārāṇāṃ teṣu sidhyupapatteḥ| "imallokānkāmānnī kāmarūpyanuñcara"nniti muktānāṃ lokasañcāraśravaṇāt tāddaśasañcārasya tattallokagatavastuniyamanādilīlārthatvācca| na ca tattallokagataphalabhogārthaṃ tatsyāditi vācyam| brahmānandamanubhavato'pi viṣayāntaralipsayā sañcārabhyupagame tasya niratiśayatvabhaṅgāpatteḥ| na ca muktānāṃ jagatsṛṣṭyādisattve sarvathā sarveśvarasāmyāpatte - "na tatsamaścābhyadhikaśca ddaśyata" iti śrutivirodhaḥ syāt| ataḥ sāmyaṃ kenacideva rūpāntareṇeti kaṃkocanīyamiti vācyam| sāmyaśrutivirodhāttanniṣidhaśrutireva saṃsāriṣu brahmasāmyaniṣedhaviṣayeti saṃkocopapatteḥ| adhikāravākyāvagataphalavirodhena "ko hi tadveda yadyamuṣmilloke'sti vā navetī"yasyeva "paramaṃ sāmyamupaitī"ti phavavākyavirodhena sāmyaśruteranyathā nayanaucityācca| "kāraṇantu dhyeya" iti śruterjagatkāramatvaviśiṣṭabrahmopāsanasya tatkratunyāyena sṛṣṭyādivarjameva muktaiśvaryaṃ| jagatkāraṇatvasya brahmalakṣaṇatvaśrutvā jagatsraṣṭurekatvaśrutyā ca virodhāt| tasmādyathāprakaraṇaṃ sarveśvarasādhāraṇaṃ nikhilajagavdyāpāraḥ natvasannihitamuktasādhāraṇaṃ| sarvalokasañcāro'pi na niyamanārthaḥ| kiṃ tu tattallokagatabhogaprāptyarthaḥ| "kāmānnīkāmarūpī"ti viśeṣaṇāt| na ca muktasya janmavṛdhdyādivikāravat kṣudrabhogalipsayā lokaparibhramaṇe tadbhogyabrahmānandasya niratiśayatvahāniḥ| na hi saṃsārivat muktasya tattallokabhogānubhavo laukikasukhatayeṣyate| kiṃ tu mavibhūtikabrahmānandānupraviṣṭatayā| tatkratunyāyastvasādhāraṇopāsyadharmātiriktaviṣayaḥ| jagatkāraṇatvantu lakṣmīpatitvādivadasādhāraṇaṃ "tasmādvā etasmādātmana ākāśassambhūtaḥ" "apaeva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamapāsṛja"dityādityādiśrutismṛtibhyāṃ tathaiva pratipādanāt| ataḥ paramasāmyaśravaṇaṃ savibhūtikabrahmānandānubhavarūpabhogasāmyamātraviṣayam| "so'śnute sarvānkāmānsaha brahmaṇa vipaścite"ti tanmātraśravaṇaliṅgāt| evaṃ brahmāsādhāraṇaṃ jagavdyāpāraṃ muktāssatyasaṃkalpā api kathamāśaseran| te ca teṣāṃ satyasaṃkalpatvādiguṇāśca muktāvapi bhagavatparatantrā iti hi "tasya ca nityatvā"diti dvitīyādhyāyasūtreṇa pratipāditam| muktānāṃ satyasaṃkalpatā'pi bhagavatparicaraṇopayogisvadehapitrādyupakaraṇasṛṣṭimātraviṣayeti sarveśvarasya bhagavataḥ puruṣottamasyaiva jagatsṛṣṭyādivyāpāraḥ||
saṃgrahakārikāḥ||
aiśvaryaṃ muktibhājāmapinikhilajagavdyāpṛtivyāpakaṃ syādāmrātā brahmaṇā yatparamasaddaśatā satyasaṃkalpatā ca|
kāmānnīkārūpī sakalabhunasañcāraśālī svarāḍityādiṣṭe yattatopi sphuṭamiha cagatāntādadhīnyapratītiḥ||
maivaṃ brahmaprakaraṇagatāssṛṣṭisaṃhāramukhyāḥ
sambadhyante kathamapi na taissannidhānaprasaṅge|
tattallokaprathitaviṣayābhogabhogaikabhāvaṃ
naivācaṣṭe niyamapitṛtāṃ lokasañcārasūktiḥ||
heyāapi muktānāṃ vikāralokasthitā bhṛśaṃ bhogāḥ|
brahmavibhūtitayā syurbhogyāssavibhūtikaṃ tadanubhavati||
muktānāṃ yā satyasaṃkalpatoktā tatsaṃkalpādhīnasattaiva sā'pi|
tecānye ye tadguṇāssarva ete'pyāpadyante sarvadā tādadhīnyam||
evaṃ muktāvapyacidvastu tulyaṃ jīvāssarve śeṣataikasvabhāvāḥ|
iti jagavdyāpāravarjādhikaraṇam||6||
iti śrīmadbhāradvajākulajaladhikaustubha śrīmadadvaitavidyācārya śrīviśvajidyājiśrīraṅgarājādhvarivarasūnorappayadīkṣitasya kṛtau caturmatasāre nayamayūkhamālikākhye dvitīyaparicchede caturthasyādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ||
iti hayavadanaparabrahmaṇe namaḥ||

nayamayūkhamālikā samāptā||
